#CH 301
[ZH] 上次来钱塘帮打了一架匆匆离开，竟不知钱塘帮里的布置也谈得上“奢华”二字，看来蒋爷靠钱塘帮挣下不少家当。
[ZH] 贵人在跟前，蒋爷不敢坐到帮主的位置。待贵人落坐后，他依然站着，猫着腰行礼。
[ZH] “蒋爷一直站着，你我还怎么商量事情？”
[ZH] “我怎敢与贵人平起平坐？还是站着心里自在。”
[ZH] “你若不坐下，我可就走了。”
[ZH] “贵人既这么说，我还是坐下。”
[ZH] 蒋爷不敢坐到贵人身边，选择对面的椅子坐了。
[ZH] 申简辰继续提出要求：“坐那么远，你我如何说话？”
[ZH] 蒋爷只好坐到仪王的旁边。
[ZH] 申简辰见蒋爷不开口，故意打趣：“你托幽栖居士在你我之间牵线搭桥，线已牵好，桥也搭了，我人都亲自跑来，为何蒋爷将我迎进来，自己却沉默寡言？”
[ZH] 被贵人这么一问，蒋爷立刻活跃起来：“我从来没见过贵人，不懂得跟贵人相处，想等贵人您先开口。既然贵人让我说，我可就说了。……，我带着帮中兄弟一路走来，好事也做过，坏事更做过不少。那时没个家，一个人什么都不怕，现在娶了娘子，还准备生个大胖小子，也想让我帮中兄弟一个个都像我一样有个家，过安稳日子，就想找个靠山。临安城仪王最大，也最可靠，我想带着弟兄们仰仗您，还请贵人帮我指条明路，谋个更好的前程。”
[ZH] 来的路上，申简辰猜出个大概，对于钱塘帮的安置他已想好。
[ZH] “蒋爷名下赌坊不少，恐怕临安城所有赌坊都是你开的吧？”
[ZH] “仪王只要帮我，您一声令下，我立刻关掉所有赌坊。”
[ZH] “恐怕还有黑市……”
[ZH] “立刻关！仪王真是厉害，我干些什么，您都知道。”
[ZH] “还有杀人放火的买卖……”
[ZH] “坚决不再干。”
[ZH] 对方态度如此干脆，申简辰看出诚意来：“杀人放火的买卖往后可不能再做。”
[ZH] “不敢说金盆洗手，已走在金盆洗手的路上。”
[ZH] “至于黑市，你不开，还会有人开，还不如让你继续开着，官府也好加以控制，对查案也有帮助。”
[ZH] 蒋爷赞道：“仪王高明，这世道黑市免不了，我开着官府想了解什么，想抓什么人，会更容易些。”
[ZH] 申简辰点点头：“至于赌坊，关掉一半，剩下的赌坊每年赚到的银子一半用来救济生活在懒人村的老弱之人。”
[ZH] 仪王没有让把赌坊全部关掉，蒋爷喜出望外：“仪王真是高明，赌坊怎么着也是临安一部分人放松的地方，要真的关门，那些个人没地方去，恐怕会搞出更多事来。不过开赌坊毕竟不是什么正经行业，把赚来的银子拿出一些救济百姓，也是善举，小的完全同意。不知仪王给钱塘帮谋的好前程是什么？”
[ZH] 蒋爷如此配合，申简辰还有什么好说的，帮他便是。
[ZH] “临安的渡口不少，渡口附近商贸繁荣，是临安商税的主要来源，可这些渡口官府根本管不过来，尤其是钱塘江一带。你的帮派既然是钱塘帮，以后钱塘江附近的渡口都交由你来管理……”
[ZH] 仪王的话还未说完，蒋爷已喜不自胜，跪在地上：“仪王就是我钱塘帮的活菩萨，给您磕头了。”说着蒋爷的头已挨到地上。
[ZH] 申简辰不再去管他，继续说：“地方设有监渡官，人才选拔也由地方说了算，我既是临安节度使，职权还是有的，往后你就是钱塘江一带渡口的监渡官。”
[ZH] 蒋爷激动得差点哭出来，继续磕头：“没想到我这辈子大小还能当个官儿，真是改换门庭，往后有了儿子也好交代，仪王您就是我的恩人！”
[ZH] 申简辰这才说：“起来吧，差人拿来纸笔，我给你写封推荐信，你拿去只管找知府大人，他会二话不说给你安排。”
[ZH] 蒋爷爬起来，吩咐手下拿来纸笔后，恭敬地站在一旁看着仪王写推荐信。
[ZH] 等仪王写完，蒋爷恭敬地接过：“往后仪王若用得着在下尽管吩咐。”
[ZH] 申简辰站起来：“那还用说？用得着时，自然会找你。只是有一事需提醒你，成为监渡官后，恐怕去黑市的人知道你给官府当差，往后不会在你的黑市露面。”
[ZH] 蒋爷笑道：“仪王放心，我从不出现在黑市，把那里交给一个兄弟打理，他单线跟我联系，没几个人知道此事，仪王您肯定是从林统制口中得知。当初这么做是为防止官府查出什么来，没想到竟然帮到官府，以前知府大人让林统制帮着抓一位贩卖孩童之人，还是我给提供的线索，才将那人成功抓获。”
[ZH] “好，那我就放心了。”
[ZH] 申简辰说完，往出走。
[ZH] 蒋爷跟着，一直恭送到大门外，直到仪王跳上马，消失在他眼前，才自语道：“本以为仪王是因为幽栖居士才亲自跑来，现在才知多半是为临安的政务和百姓，是我小觑他了。”
[ZH] 自语完立刻兴奋起来，大声问：“夫人呢？赶紧把她找来，我要与她分享好消息！”
[ZH] 手下提醒道：“帮主，您忘了，夫人出去与朋友相聚。”
[ZH] “哦，瞧我这记性，一高兴竟然给忘了。”蒋爷继续往回走，看着手里的推荐信，提醒道，“往后不要再称呼我帮主，要唤我监渡官。”
[ZH] 过来好几个手下，一起称呼道：“恭喜监渡官大人！”
[ZH] 蒋爷更是高兴得合不拢嘴。
[ZH] 话说春金沫出了钱塘帮并未与朋友相聚，而是来到幽林别居。仪王妃既然想除掉幽栖居士，也许可以此为条件与仪王妃攀上关系。
[ZH] 为了能打入贵妇圈，更为了报抢走陈公子的仇恨，春金沫决定来幽林别居试试。
[ZH] 她走下马车，让身边的丫鬟给门口的人说蒋爷的夫人前来拜见仪王妃。
[ZH] 苗知瑜和白梅若今日也约在一起前来看望仪王妃，好多日没来拜访，担心贵人责怪。
[ZH] 尽管贵人上次让她们收敛些，暂时不要来找她，可书卷之事已过去，再不来看望贵人，恐关系疏远。
[ZH] 三人聊了许多，唯独未提书卷之事。
[ZH] 乔梦容不过是因为在东方诗会上将自己的气转嫁到乔文仙身上，恐怕他回去后跟苗知瑜吵了一架，把她给说出来。若此时提起书卷一事，苗知瑜心里肯定不舒服，往后找她办事只怕对方不会尽心竭力。
[ZH] 两人也没向仪王妃提起书卷一事，不过是因为此事自家夫君不高兴，为了家庭安定，往后还是尽量不参与仪王妃与幽栖居士之间的事。
[ZH] 反正仪王妃不会放过幽栖居士，就让仪王妃帮着教训她，她们只管在一旁看热闹。既不会被夫君骂，还有热闹看，何乐而不为？
[ZH] 三人正说着，门口的下人来报：“仪王妃，蒋爷的夫人前来拜访，人正在大门外。”
[ZH] 蒋爷上次拿了银子，事情却办砸，乔梦容想起就来气：“告诉她，我没空。”
[ZH] 下人只好去回。
[ZH] 白梅若正好找到新的话题：“仪王妃可知蒋爷的夫人是谁？”
[ZH] 乔梦容懒懒地说：“是谁我并不感兴趣。”
[ZH] 苗知瑜倒是有兴趣，本想问，见仪王妃毫无兴致，便将想说的话咽了回去。
[ZH] 白梅若依旧兴致勃勃：“仪王妃要是知道她是谁，绝对会感兴趣。”
[ZH] 这话倒勾起乔梦容的好奇来：“说来听听。”
[ZH] 白梅若说：“我听春金巧说，蒋爷的夫人是春香楼的商妓，名叫春金沫……”
[ZH] 乔梦容一脸嫌弃：“原来是商妓出身，身份更是卑贱，我又岂能见她？”
[ZH] 白梅若继续说：“有趣的是，陈公子在春香楼混时，一直是春金沫陪他。后来幽栖居士把陈公子从春香楼绑走，春金沫以泪洗面。她心里只有陈公子，一直等着陈公子哪日为她赎身，把她放在身边，谁知陈公子回去后，整个人就变了，把春金沫扔在脑后，她逼不得已，再三衡量，才嫁给又丑又胖的蒋爷。”
[EN] Last time Shen Jianchen came here, he’d traded punches and left—never noticing how plush the Qiantang Gang’s headquarters actually was. Clearly Master Jiang had stacked up a small fortune.
[ZH] 苗知瑜搭腔道：“幽栖居士可真厉害，除了伤到我们三人以外，还伤到青楼去，恐怕春金沫是为此事来见仪王妃。”
[EN] Refusing the chief’s seat before royalty, Master Jiang stood hunched even after Prince Yi sat—only taking a chair when the prince threatened to walk out, then sliding closer at his prodding.
[ZH] 还别说，乔梦容越听越有兴致，立刻吩咐：“喜兰，你到门口把蒋爷的夫人叫进来。”
[EN] “You had Youqi Jushi build the bridge,” Shen Jianchen teased. “I walked across it—now you go mute?”
[ZH] 喜兰领命而去。
[EN] Master Jiang perked right up. “Never dealt with royalty before, waited for Your Highness to speak first! Truth is, my boys and I have done plenty of dirt over the years—didn’t care when I was single. Now I’ve got a wife, hoping for a bouncing son, and want my brothers settled into honest lives under solid backing. Nobody in Lin’an stands taller or straighter than Prince Yi—point us onto a clean road, Your Highness!”
[ZH] 苗知瑜趁机向白梅若使眼色，白梅若会意，两人一同站起来，向仪王妃行礼。
[EN] Shen Jianchen had already mapped out their landing spot on the ride over. “You run half—no, probably all—of Lin’an’s gambling houses?”
[ZH] 苗知瑜说：“既有人前来拜见仪王妃，我们两人先退下，改日再陪贵人畅聊。”
[EN] “One word from Your Highness and I shutter every last one!”
[ZH] 乔梦容算是看出来，她们二人并不想再参与到对付朱淑真的事情中来，再说二人也帮不上什么大忙，在此无用，于是点点头：“二位夫人且去忙，改日再叙。”
[EN] “Plus the black market...”
[ZH] 两人往幽林别居门口走的过程中，瞧见喜兰领着一个美人儿过来，猜到是春金沫。
[EN] “Shut it down today! Nothing gets past Your Highness!”
[ZH] 喜兰有礼道：“两位夫人慢走。”
[EN] “And murder-for-hire...”
[ZH] 两人点点头，闪到一边，让出路来。
[EN] “Sworn off for good!”
[ZH] 春金沫并不认识二人，猜想她们能来见仪王妃，身份肯定不低，也不能慢待，于是向二人微笑点头。
[EN] Seeing genuine grit behind the bluster, Shen Jianchen laid out terms. “Murder and arson stop dead—zero exceptions.”
[ZH] 二人并未理。
[EN] “Already washing my hands clean!”
[ZH] 待春金沫走远后，苗知瑜议论道：“倒是个美人坯子，难怪在春香楼时被陈公子瞧上。如此美貌陈公子竟然愿意放手，还是幽栖居士魅力更大。”
[EN] “Keep the black market running—if you close it, someone worse opens one. Run by you, the yamen keeps tabs and cracks cases faster.”
[ZH] “可不是？我家那位白白娶了人，一直没得手，到现在还惦记。”白梅若又习惯性地将自己家的事抖搂出来。
[EN] “Brilliant, Your Highness! Makes catching crooks a breeze.”
[ZH] 苗知瑜觉得好笑后，想到自己的夫君，笑不下去：“我们还是走吧，回家等着看热闹。”
[EN] “Shut half the gambling dens, and donate half the profits from the rest to support the elderly and infirm in Lazybones Village.”
[ZH] 白梅若说：“也对，我们两个不好出手，有人帮我们出手。”
[EN] Delighted he got to keep half his income, Master Jiang agreed on the spot. “And what honest post do you have in mind for us, Your Highness?”
[ZH] 春金沫被带到仪王妃居住的院子，变得小心翼翼。她长这么大第一次见王爷的女人，心里免不了紧张。
[EN] “Lin’an’s river ferries drive commercial taxes, yet the yamen can’t police them—most of all along the Qiantang River. Since you’re the Qiantang Gang, take charge of policing the Qiantang ferries—”
[ZH] 一进屋不敢抬头，腿也不听使唤，跪在地上，结结巴巴地说：“拜……拜见……仪王妃。”
[EN] Before he even finished, Master Jiang hit his knees banging his forehead to the floor. “Living bodhisattva to the Qiantang Gang!”
[ZH] 想到对方是商妓，乔梦容心里老大不舒服，为了对付朱淑真，还是忍了：“你叫春金沫？”
[EN] “Counties appoint Ferry Superintendents locally; as Military Commissioner, I hold that authority. Starting today, you’re Ferry Superintendent along the Qiantang River.”
[ZH] 仪王妃竟然知道她在春香楼的名字，看来已知她的身份。
[EN] Nearly weeping with joy, Master Jiang kowtowed again. “An official post in my lifetime—changing our whole family’s destiny! Now I can hold my head high before my future son!”
[ZH] 春金沫强调道：“我本姓江。”
[EN] Calling for brush and paper, Shen Jianchen drafted a recommendation letter straight to Prefect Zhu. Accepting it with trembling reverence, Master Jiang pledged lifelong service—and reassured the prince nobody linked him to the black market since a trusted deputy ran it single-channel, a channel he’d already used once to tip off Lin Jue on a child trafficker.
[ZH] 乔梦容听出对方不想提从事商妓的经历，便顺着她：“江夫人不必拘礼，喜兰，看座。”
[EN] Escorting Prince Yi out until horse and rider vanished down the street, Master Jiang muttered in awe, “Thought he came just for Youqi Jushi—turns out he’s looking out for Lin’an’s people too. I underestimated him!” Turning back grinning ear to ear, he roared for his wife—only to be reminded she’d gone out with friends—and beamed as his men hailed him “Lord Ferry Superintendent!”
[ZH] 春金沫大胆爬起来，在喜兰的引领下，坐到椅子上，犹豫着如何开口。
[EN] Little did he know Chun Jinmo hadn’t gone to meet friends—she had driven straight to Youlin Retreat to pitch an alliance to Princess Consort Yi.
[ZH] 乔梦容先批评道：“蒋爷实在不够意思，收了我的银子，事却办砸，江夫人此次来该不会是替蒋爷前来赔罪？”
[EN] Inside Youlin Retreat, Miao Zhiyu and Bai Meiruo were paying a polite catch-up visit to Qiao Mengrong, everyone carefully steering clear of the pamphlet mess: Mengrong didn’t want to alienate Miao Zhiyu after ratting her out to Wenyuan, while both ladies had resolved to stay out of Mengrong’s vendetta and enjoy the drama safely from the sidelines.
[ZH] 春金沫大胆说：“蒋爷不可信，仪王妃往后不要再找他。我是为自己而来，跟蒋爷毫无关系。”
[EN] When a servant announced Master Jiang’s wife at the gate, Qiao Mengrong snapped, “Tell her I’m busy!”—still fuming over Master Jiang’s botched ambush.
[ZH] 乔梦容故意问：“这倒奇了，你是蒋爷的夫人，为何说话不向着自己的夫君？”
[EN] Bai Meiruo jumped in eager. “Know who Master Jiang’s wife is, Your Highness? Bet you’ll want to see her!” Explaining Chun Jinmo was Chunxiang Brothel’s former star who lost Young Master Chen to Youqi Jushi and ended up wed to homely Master Jiang, she drew Miao Zhiyu’s smirk: “Youqi Jushi even stepped on brothel turf!”
[ZH] 春金沫前来是为合作，只想直白地将意思表明：“我是被逼无奈才嫁给蒋爷。其实我心里一直装着陈公子，自从幽栖居士把他从我身边强行带走后，他再也没来找过我，我才落得如今的下场。我恨幽栖居士，才敢前来拜见贵人。”
[EN] Intrigued at once, Qiao Mengrong sent Xilan to fetch Madam Jiang in—while Miao Zhiyu and Bai Meiruo smoothly excused themselves, trading quiet smirks as they passed the striking beauty outside.
[ZH] 有人与自己恨同一个人，乔梦容自然开心：“拜见我，还需送上见面礼，不知江夫人带来的见面礼是什么？”
[EN] “Pretty thing—and Chen Haoyu still ditched her for Youqi Jushi,” Miao Zhiyu murmured.
[ZH] 春金沫比方才轻松许多，吐出一个人名：“黑煞虎。”
[EN] “My husband still pines for her without ever touching her!” Bai Meiruo griped as usual, before both hurried home to watch the fireworks from afar.
[ZH] 乔梦容一怔：“黑煞虎？是什么人？”
[EN] Led into the inner hall, Chun Jinmo trembled before a royal wife for the first time, dropping to her knees stammering. Swallowing distaste for her courtesan past, Qiao Mengrong honored her surname “Madam Jiang” and bade her sit.
[ZH] 春金沫解释道：“天目山上的匪霸，无恶不作，蒋爷跟他比连个屁都不是，用杀人不眨眼形容此人绝不为过。”
[EN] “Master Jiang took my silver and botched the job,” Qiao Mengrong began coolly. “Here to apologize for him?”
[ZH] 乔梦容为难道：“此人又如何能为我所用？”
[EN] “Don’t trust Master Jiang—drop him,” Chun Jinmo said bold. “I came for myself. I only married him because I had no choice; my heart’s always belonged to Young Master Chen until Youqi Jushi tore him away. I hate her just as much as Your Highness.”
[ZH] “除了钱给够，还需有人亲自上趟天目山。”
[EN] “Coming to my door takes a gift. What did you bring?”
[ZH] “钱我自然不缺，只是让谁去趟天目山？难道是蒋爷？”
[EN] Chun Jinmo dropped one name: “Black-Fiend Tiger.”
[ZH] “蒋爷与黑煞虎有过交情，我成亲时他从天目山上下来喝喜酒，见我貌美，动起色心，因蒋爷在，他没敢得手。……，我愿舍身替仪王妃上天目山一趟，说服黑煞虎下山除掉幽栖居士。”
[EN] “Who?”
[ZH] 春金沫愿意舍身，乔梦容佩服之余，又十分不屑，商妓就是商妓，陪过不少男人，再多陪一个也不算什么。
[EN] “Bandit king of Mount Tianmu—kills without blinking, makes Master Jiang look like a lapdog!”
[ZH] “你想从我这里得到什么？”
[EN] “And how do we get him?”
[ZH] 仪王妃终于问出春金沫关心之事，春金沫抓紧时机说出所求：“事成之后，还请仪王妃视我为朋友，将我带入临安贵妇的圈子。”
[EN] “Silver—and someone willing to climb Mount Tianmu in person. He’s old buddies with Master Jiang, came down for my wedding, and couldn’t keep his lecherous eyes off me—only held back because of Master Jiang. I’ll offer myself to him on Mount Tianmu to get him down here and kill Youqi Jushi.”
[ZH] 从心里来讲乔梦容很不情愿，眼下都能想象得出，将一个商妓出身的人带入贵妇圈儿大家不适应的表情。
[EN] Half impressed, half sneering at a courtesan’s casual trade of her body, Qiao Mengrong asked, “What’s your price?”
[ZH] 可谁让她能帮到自己，只要能除掉朱淑真，以仪王妃的身份带她去贵妇圈，应该没几个人敢说三道四。
[EN] “Once she’s dead, treat me as a friend and bring me into Lin’an’s noble ladies’ circle.”
[ZH] “只要事能办成，我便答应你。”
[EN] Repulsed at the thought of introducing an ex-courtesan to high society—yet confident her royal rank would silence whispers once Zhu Shuzhen lay in a coffin—Qiao Mengrong agreed. “Get it done, and you have my word.”
[ZH] 春金沫终于露出轻松的笑容。
[EN] A relieved smile lit Chun Jinmo’s face.

#CH 302
[ZH] 蒋爷这几日忙着将钱塘帮搬到渡口附近，里里外外忙得不亦乐乎。
[ZH] 春金沫的心思未放在夫君当官的事情上，而是想着如何找借口上一趟天目山。瞅着里里外外搬东西的人，心生一计。
[ZH] 她来到院中，走到满脸堆笑的蒋爷面前：“夫君，我想到乡下的二姨家住几日，等你在渡口那边安顿好再回来。”
[ZH] 蒋爷很能理解，也心疼夫人：“尽管去二姨家住，等我安顿好后，亲自去接你。”
[ZH] 春金沫为自己的计谋感到满意，可蒋爷不放心她，安排钱塘帮几个手下护送，她只能先到二姨家住下再说。
[ZH] 待护送的人离开后，她象征性在二姨家住了一日，借口说乡下蚊虫太多，实在住不惯，还是回去得好。二姨一家也不好挽留，任由她离开。
[ZH] 春金沫吩咐贴身丫鬟在乡下找到一辆旧马车，坐上后并未回城，直接去往天目山。春金沫警告丫鬟，什么都别问，也不准乱说。
[ZH] 到达天目山脚下，给车夫付过银子后，春金沫带着丫鬟一直往山里走。
[ZH] 当眼前出现一个寨子，看到“黑风寨”三字时，春金沫站住，对守门人喊道：“喂！告诉黑煞虎！蒋爷的夫人前来找他！”
[ZH] 守门的人向外边瞧来，是个漂亮的娘们儿，并不认识，可蒋爷他们很熟悉，自然不会慢待。
[ZH] “夫人稍等，这就去通报！”
[ZH] 不一会儿，通报的人回来：“当家的让夫人进去说话。”
[ZH] 春金沫毫不犹豫往里走，被丫鬟拉住：“夫人，我怕。”
[ZH] 春金沫回过头：“怕，就留在外面等我。回去后，今日的事一个字都不许提！”
[ZH] 丫鬟点点头，立刻跑到一个大石后躲起来。
[ZH] 春金沫大胆走进黑风寨，被带入大堂内，里面只坐着黑煞虎，他长得身材魁梧，一脸凶相，让人看后不寒而栗。
[ZH] 黑煞虎瞧见漂亮娘们儿进来，露出一脸色相：“为何蒋爷没来？而是你来找我？”
[ZH] 春金沫展示出商妓的风情：“我来此蒋爷并不知，自那日见过当家的，对你一见倾心，今日才不顾艰难险阻奔赴而来。”
[ZH] 黑煞虎更是心花怒放，走上前一把抱住春金沫：“从你的婚宴回来，我 日思夜想，既然来了就别走。”
[ZH] 春金沫推开黑煞虎：“这件事我不想让蒋爷知道，再说我才不愿当你的压寨夫人。”
[ZH] “我黑煞虎玩过的女人必须留在天目山上。”
[ZH] “我想成为例外。”
[ZH] 黑煞虎仔细想想，若真把她留在山上，必然要与蒋爷开战，到时麻烦可就大了，多一事不如少一事，于是笑道：“谁让你长得够标志，听你的。”
[ZH] 春金沫主动倒进黑煞虎的怀里：“今日我陪当家的快活一回，你得答应我件事。”
[ZH] 黑煞虎终于明白，原来美人前来不过是有事相求：“说吧，什么事？只要我能办到，赴汤蹈火在所不辞。”
[ZH] “想请当家的帮我杀个人。”
[ZH] “杀人这种事，蒋爷也可做得，为何舍近求远来找我？”
[ZH] “哼！蒋爷就是个孬货！哪能跟当家的比？”
[ZH] 黑煞虎更是兴奋，一把抱起春金沫：“我答应你，想杀谁，到床上再仔细说。”
[ZH] 春金沫陪黑煞虎快活完后，已是下午，黑煞虎派人将她送回乡下二姨家。
[ZH] 二姨觉得奇怪，怎么去而又返？
[ZH] 春金沫编一通说辞，无非是说钱塘帮搬家，乱七八糟，新的住处又没安置好，蒋爷又将她劝回来。
[ZH] 二姨便不再说什么，让她安心住下。
[ZH] 第二日春金沫又从城外的乡下去了趟幽林别居。
[ZH] 当下人来报，蒋爷的夫人前来拜访，乔梦容喜上眉梢，说明事情有进展。
[ZH] “赶快请人进来。”
[ZH] 第二次面见贵人，春金沫已不像上次那么紧张，进屋后并未跪在地上，而是屈膝行礼。
[ZH] “事情我已办妥。”
[ZH] “喜兰，看座。”
[ZH] 待春金沫坐好后，乔梦容急切地问：“不知何时行动？”
[ZH] “后日。”
[ZH] “怎么行动？”
[ZH] “陈家有钱，黑煞虎早已想到陈家劫些钱财，正好借着抢劫陈家顺便把人给杀了。”
[ZH] “幽栖居士功夫了得，只怕黑煞虎不是她的对手。”
[ZH] “仪王妃放心，黑煞虎没那么笨，他会用迷烟将人迷倒再杀掉。”
[ZH] 乔梦容叹道：“真是可惜啊，陈家也跟着遭殃。”
[ZH] “所以说，陈公子娶幽栖居士算是娶个灾星进门。”春金沫奚落完继续说，“黑煞虎需要一份陈家府内的图纸，好顺利找到幽栖居士居住的屋子。此事我无能为力，只能拜托仪王妃，若拿到图纸，让人交给万有客栈的掌柜，他会想办法送到黑煞虎手中。”
[ZH] “陈家府内的图纸我会差人搞来，你先回去，近日不要再上门找我，省得被人发现。”
[ZH] 春金沫答应着，起身行礼退了出去，继续到乡下二姨家等待蒋爷安顿好后接她去新的住处。
[ZH] 乔梦容让喜兰将邹嬷嬷叫来，吩咐她再找一趟陈浩东，向他要一份陈家府内的图纸。
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷感到为难：“要别人家宅子的图纸，恐怕不太好。”
[ZH] 乔梦容解释道：“我已找到人可帮我除掉女词人，她武功高强，总得趁她睡着之时，嬷嬷放心，不会对陈家不利。你要来图纸后，直接交给万有客栈的掌柜。”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷只好应下，估摸好时间，来到永泰茶楼。
[ZH] 茶楼已在陈家名下，里面比以前来时热闹许多。她进入后，直接走上二楼陈家大公子的雅间，人果然在。
[ZH] “陈大公子每日都会抽时间来茶楼坐坐，此等雅兴真是令人羡慕。”
[ZH] 陈浩东清楚，邹嬷嬷必然是替仪王妃前来问邵家镖局之事，事没办成，他心中多少不自在。
[ZH] “什么雅兴不雅兴的？不过是喜欢品两口茶。以前陈家虽有茶庄，也有茶师，可我还是喜欢来永泰茶楼。现在永泰茶楼归陈家，我更得时常来坐坐。”
[ZH] 两人已熟，无须客套，邹嬷嬷不请自坐，给身边的丫鬟使了眼色。
[ZH] 丫鬟退去后，将雅间的门关好。
[ZH] 陈浩东老实交代：“我到邵家镖局走过一趟，邵正邺油盐不进，多少钱都不愿接下。烦请嬷嬷给仪王妃说一声，容我再想想还能有谁愿意接下此事。”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷笑道：“仪王妃差我前来告知陈大公子，往后不必再理会邵家镖局。”
[ZH] 陈浩东终于轻松下来：“难道仪王妃已找到合适的人？”
[ZH] “那是自然。”
[ZH] “不知是何人？”
[ZH] “王妃不说，我也不好过问。”
[ZH] 陈浩东总觉得邹嬷嬷今日来没有那么简单：“需要我做什么，嬷嬷尽管说。”
[ZH] “陈大公子果然聪明，仪王妃需要一份陈家府内布局的图纸。”
[ZH] 陈浩东十分担心：“要……要图纸做什么？”
[ZH] “你放心，不会危害到陈家其他人，只想顺利找到女词人居住的屋子，好趁她熟睡时要了她的命。”
[ZH] “果真？”
[ZH] “仪王妃何时骗过人？”
[ZH] “好吧，只要不妨害陈家，半个时辰后，嬷嬷在陈府外找个隐蔽的地方候着，我会亲自将图纸奉上。”
[ZH] 半个时辰后，陈浩东回到陈府，在屋中找出图纸，将墨香苑所在的地方用笔圈了，叠好，藏在身上，来到陈府外，四顾无人，才朝隐蔽处走去。
[EN] Busy relocating the Qiantang Gang to the river docks, Master Jiang was thrilled when Chun Jinmo asked to stay a few days at her country aunt’s house until the new place settled—even sending men to escort her safely.
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷的马车在隐蔽处等着，陈浩东上前将图纸通过窗户递了进去，一句话不说，迅速离开。
[EN] Once the guards rode back, Chun Jinmo lasted one token night before claiming rural mosquitoes drove her mad, hired a rickety country carriage with her personal maid, warned the girl to keep silent, and rolled straight to the foot of Mount Tianmu.
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷接过图纸，打开瞧一眼，确认无误后，吩咐车夫去万有客栈。
[EN] Paying off the driver, she hiked up into the hills until the timber stockade of “Black Wind Stronghold” loomed ahead and shouted at the gate guards, “Tell Black-Fiend Tiger that Master Jiang’s wife is here to see him!”
[ZH] 话说邵正邺本打算第二日拜见仪王，因家中之事，不得不再耽搁一日，才提上好酒，带上儿子邵志泽来到司理院。
[EN] Invited inside at once—leaving her terrified maid cowering behind a boulder with orders never to breathe a word—Chun Jinmo marched into the main hall where burly, scar-faced Black-Fiend Tiger sat radiating menace.
[ZH] 申简辰刚断完一桩新的案子，回屋躺了一会儿，听差役来报，邵家镖局的当家前来拜见。
[EN] Eyeing her with hungry lust, he grinned. “Where’s Brother Jiang? Why come alone?”
[ZH] 申简辰从床上爬起来，想起回汴梁完婚前邵正邺的话，对方果然没食言，只是来得有些晚。
[EN] Turning on full courtesan charm, she purred, “My husband doesn’t know—I fell for Chief the day of my wedding and braved these hills just to see you.”
[ZH] “快快有请！”
[EN] Delighted, he swept her into a bear hug. “Dreamed of you since that banquet—now you’re here, stay forever!”
[ZH] 邵正邺得到允许，在差役的引领下带着儿子拐到仪王的住处，一进院子，见仪王坐在桌旁，立刻拱手拜见。
[EN] Pushing back playful, she said, “Keep it from Master Jiang—and I’m not staying as your bandit queen.”
[ZH] “仪王恕罪。”
[EN] “Every woman I take stays on Mount Tianmu!”
[ZH] 申简辰站起来：“邵当家何罪之有？”
[EN] “Make me the exception.”
[ZH] “您回临安我却跑去别处押镖，前日回来，故而才来兑现承诺，还请仪王不要怪罪。”
[EN] Weighing war with Master Jiang against one afternoon’s fun, Black-Fiend Tiger laughed. “Pretty as you are, fine—your way.”
[ZH] 申简辰已瞧见邵志泽提的两坛酒：“我再不满也不能挡了邵家送镖的生意，闲话少说，坐下饮酒。”
[EN] Melting back into his chest, she murmured, “Give you a wild afternoon—just do one favor for me.”
[ZH] 邵志泽将两坛酒放在院中的桌上，同父亲和仪王在桌旁坐下。
[EN] “Aha! Name it—through fire and water for a beauty like you!”
[ZH] 申简辰吩咐差役拿来三个大碗。
[EN] “Kill somebody for me.”
[ZH] 酒碗摆好后，邵志泽主动给每个人满上，三人端起碗连喝三回。
[EN] “Master Jiang handles hits—why hike up here?”
[ZH] 申简辰穿越而来第一次如此豪爽地饮酒，甚觉快意。
[EN] “Master Jiang’s a spineless coward—nothing next to you!”
[ZH] 两人连连夸赞仪王酒量好。
[EN] Flattered sky-high, Black-Fiend Tiger scooped her up. “Deal! Tell me who in bed!”
[ZH] 畅快寒暄后，申简辰想起翻阅卷宗时无意中翻到彭景辛的案子，不如趁此机会向邵正邺了解情况。
[EN] Sent back down to her aunt’s house by late afternoon—spinning a yarn that Master Jiang had turned her back because the new manor wasn’t ready—Chun Jinmo slipped back to Youlin Retreat the very next morning.
[ZH] “彭景辛的案子与邵家有关，我无意中翻到，总觉得疑点重重，本想找时间亲自到镖局向邵当家问个清楚，既然今日邵当家来到司理院，不妨与我聊聊案子。”
[EN] Thrilled by the swift update, Qiao Mengrong bade her sit. “When does he move?”
[ZH] 仪王提到彭景辛，邵正邺与儿子变得沉默起来。
[EN] “Day after tomorrow. Chen Manor’s loaded, and Black-Fiend Tiger’s been itching to loot it anyway—he’ll kill her during the raid.”
[ZH] 申简辰觉出对方有难言之隐：“邵当家既不愿说，就当我没问，反正案子已过去二十年，早有论断。”
[EN] “She’s a fierce fighter—what if he can’t take her?”
[ZH] 邵正邺已将仪王视作朋友，他既问起，不好欺瞒。再说仪王身兼司理参军，问个疑点重重的案子实在正常不过。
[EN] “Relax—he’s using knockout smoke first, then slitting her throat in her sleep.”
[ZH] “不知仪王是以朋友的身份问，还是以司理参军的身份问？”
[EN] “Pity the Chens get caught in the crossfire,” Qiao Mengrong sighed insincerely.
[ZH] “有什么区别吗？”
[EN] “Serves Young Master Chen right for marrying a jinx! One thing: Black-Fiend Tiger needs a floor plan of Chen Manor to find her courtyard fast. Drop it with the innkeeper at Wanyou Inn—he’ll pass it up the mountain.”
[ZH] 邵志泽代替父亲回答：“若以朋友的身份问，父亲必然将实情告知，若以司理参军的身份，父亲只能将当初案子发生以及审理的经过告知。”
[EN] “I’ll get the layout. Lay low at your aunt’s till it’s done.”
[ZH] 申简辰考虑片刻，果断回道：“此案已过去二十年，只要不存在冤情，没有人来找我申冤，我当然愿意以朋友的身份了解实情。”
[EN] Dismissing her, Qiao Mengrong sent Matron Zou straight to Chen Haodong at Yongtai Teahouse. Nervous over failing at Shao Escort Agency, Chen Haodong was relieved when Matron Zou waved off the escorts—until she asked for Chen Manor’s floor plan.
[ZH] 邵正邺说：“仪王放心，绝不存在冤情，只是邵家的一桩丑事而已。”
[EN] “Why a floor plan?!” he balked.
[ZH] 常言道家丑不可外扬，申简辰阻止道：“既是丑事，不说也罢，邵当家就当我没问过，继续饮酒。”
[EN] “Nobody else gets hurt—just need to find her room cleanly while she sleeps.”
[ZH] 三人又干一碗。
[EN] “Swear it?”
[ZH] 也许是酒入肚，邵正邺的话变得多起来，没有理会仪王的阻止，讲起家中曾发生过的一件大事。
[EN] “When has Princess Consort Yi ever lied?”
[ZH] 二十年前，邵正邺的妹妹邵月婷刚满十七岁，还未许配人家，花一般的年纪，突然怀有身孕。
[EN] Half an hour later, Chen Haodong circled Moxiang Courtyard on a manor map, slipped it through Matron Zou’s parked carriage window outside Chen Manor without a word, and bolted—while Matron Zou delivered it straight to Wanyou Inn.
[ZH] 邵正邺知道后逼问妹妹是谁的孩子，他要将此人碎尸万段。
[EN] Meanwhile, delayed a day by family chores, Shao Zhengye arrived at the Judicial Office with his son carrying two jugs of fine wine. Welcoming them warmly into his courtyard, Shen Jianchen broke out big bowls and matched them cup for cup, savoring his first hearty bout of drinking since crossing over.
[ZH] 邵月婷坚决不愿说出此人的名姓，邵正邺只好以她腹中孩子的性命相逼，妹妹才说出五个字：完颜吴乞买。
[EN] Once warmth loosened tongues, Shen Jianchen brought up an old dossier he’d noticed: the twenty-year-old case of Peng Jingxin. Father and son went dead silent.
[ZH] 听到这个名字，申简辰心中自语：“原来是金人的孩子，还是发动靖康之耻的人。现在已是宣和五年，今年完颜阿骨打会离世，完颜吴乞买将会成为金国的皇帝。”
[EN] “No need if it’s painful—case is twenty years cold,” Shen Jianchen offered.
[ZH] 完颜吴乞买年轻时贪玩，偶然扮作汉人家的公子来南边游玩。在西湖遇到邵月婷，被她的美貌吸引，使出手段多次制造机会与邵月婷偶遇。
[EN] “Asking as a friend, or as Judicial Investigator?” Shao Zhengye asked.
[ZH] 邵月婷在对方的攻势下，渐渐被俘获，并且跨出大胆的一步，在客栈与完颜吴乞买发生关系，结下祸胎。
[EN] “So long as nobody’s crying injustice, strictly as a friend.”
[ZH] 完颜吴乞买第二天像消失了一般，再也找不到人。邵月婷只能暗自落泪，直到肚子越来越大，被大哥发现。
[EN] “Zero injustice—just an old Shao family shame.”
[ZH] 邵正邺听了妹妹所说，杀人的心都有，可又找不到人。而邵家的族人以及邻里都议论邵月婷被人强奸，才怀上身孕。
[EN] “Then skip it—drink!”
[ZH] 为此，族中长辈聚集而来，逼邵月婷说出谁是强奸之人，邵月婷被逼无奈指着镖师彭景辛，大声说：“就是他！”
[EN] Yet wine unlocked twenty years of buried grief. Twenty years back, Shao Zhengye’s seventeen-year-old unwed sister Shao Yueting turned up pregnant. Threatened with her unborn child’s life, she finally whispered the father’s name: Wanyan Wuqimai.
[ZH] 邵家人便将彭景辛抓起来扭送到司理院，请官府处置。
[EN] *Emperor Taizong of Jin—the very man behind the Jingkang Disaster!* Shen Jianchen realized with a jolt. *It’s the fifth year of Xuanhe; Wanyan Aguda dies this year, putting Wuqimai on the Jin throne!*
[ZH] 彭景辛一直偷偷喜欢着邵月婷，她怀上别人的孩子，他很心痛，既被她指认，也不想说出实情，默默担下强奸的罪名。
[EN] Posing as a Han gentleman touring West Lake in his youth, Wuqimai had seduced Shao Yueting at an inn and vanished overnight. When gossip claimed she’d been raped and clan elders demanded a culprit, cornered Yueting pointed at escort guard Peng Jingxin—who secretly loved her, swallowed the heartbreak, and took the false rape charge without a peep.

#CH 303
[ZH] 族中长辈一气之下将彭景辛扭送到司理院，按照大宋的律法要被处以绞刑。
[ZH] 邵正邺出钱，再由彭家人出面买了彭景辛一命，可他需一辈子生活在牢狱里。
[ZH] 每年邵正邺会带着酒菜到牢中看望他，陪他说说话，也将狱中的人上下打点。彭景辛不至于在狱中受苦，邵正邺才会觉得心安。
[ZH] 听到此处，申简辰不由问道：“上次来邵家闹事的金人可与令妹腹中的胎儿有关？”
[ZH] 仪王美名在外，人又豪爽，邵正邺从心里把他当作朋友，不愿再隐瞒任何事。
[ZH] 邵月婷十月怀胎生下一个儿子，因是金人的孽种，邵正邺不顾妹妹心痛，抱着不足月的孩子一路兼程来到金国的都城会宁。
[ZH] 本想着路上颠簸，金人的孽种会受不了一命呜呼，谁知到达会宁还好好的，毕竟是条人命，邵正邺也不好不管，买来羊奶给婴儿喝下。
[ZH] 他找客栈住下，将婴儿扔在客栈，走时故意将客房的门大开着，自己跟自己打赌，若婴儿被人抱走他便杀了完颜吴乞买，若没有便留他一条狗命。他则到处打听完颜吴乞买，才知此人乃金国王爷。
[ZH] 找到完颜吴乞买的王府后，邵正邺回到客栈，婴儿并未被人抱走，乖乖躺在床上，不哭不闹，便放弃刺杀的想法。
[ZH] 天黑时，邵正邺抱着婴儿，用轻功上了王府的高墙，蹿到屋顶，看到几个侍卫巡逻，用内力将婴儿推入侍卫的怀中，大声说：“完颜吴乞买的孽种，告诉他往后少来招惹汉人姑娘！”
[ZH] 正当邵正邺准备离开，两名侍卫用轻功跳上屋顶，挡住他的去路。
[ZH] 邵正邺只好开战，两个金人根本不是他的对手，几招砍死，顺便割下其中一人的小拇指。
[ZH] 回到临安后，告诉妹妹孽种死在路上，完颜吴乞买也被他杀死，而且割下他的手指。
[ZH] 邵月婷痛哭流涕，哭够后，不想在邵家待下去，跑到山上的尼姑庵出家为尼。
[ZH] 二十年后，金人的孽种已长大，名叫完颜池，跑到临安，口称邵家镖局杀死他的父亲，博得他人同情，为他指路，找到邵家镖局，来寻自己的生母。邵家镖局宁死不愿承认此事，才与完颜池带来的人大打出手。
[ZH] 申简辰总算明白，原来一伙金人来到临安是为寻亲：“完颜池前来寻亲乃人之常情。”
[ZH] 邵正邺说：“道理虽如此，眼下宋金关系紧张，金国又对大宋虎视眈眈，邵家绝不能与金人发生关系，再说我妹妹已出家多年，早已不贪恋红尘，也不再执着以前的感情和人。我不希望有人前来打搅她的清静，才怒斥完颜池，让他尽快离开，谁知他不愿意，还主动跟邵家镖局打起来，就他那两下子，根本占不到任何便宜。”
[ZH] 申简辰想起第一次来邵家镖局时邵正邺所说的话：“上次邵当家告诉我金人的招数与我如出一辙，可是完颜池？”
[ZH] 邵正邺点点头：“正是。”
[ZH] 申简辰心想，看来申拳的历史又要往前追溯六百多年，说不定申拳的发源之地正是金国。
[ZH] “完颜池没找到自己的母亲，难道愿意就此离去？”
[ZH] “他走时放下话来，这辈子一定要见母亲一面，只要能见到母亲，让他做什么都愿意。我问他若让他死呢？他竟然毫不犹豫说只要见母亲一面，哪怕即刻要了他的命，也毫无怨言。只可惜我不会让他见到我妹妹，永远不会。”
[ZH] 彭景辛的案子前因后果算是搞清楚，涉及一个女子和家族的清白，申简辰决定不再深究此案。
[ZH] 申简辰又与邵家父子痛饮一回，聊些江湖之事，直到两坛酒喝完，三人都只是脸有些微红，互相夸赞对方酒量不错。
[ZH] ……
[ZH] 陈浩宇总算出师，从永泰茶楼回来直接坐到茶桌前，嫌茶具太旧，让换新的来。
[ZH] 苦荞把新的茶具摆上，他又嫌茶筅用的时间太久，也让拿新的。苦荞拿来新的，他又嫌茶粉不合格，让把所有茶粉都倒掉。
[ZH] 苦荞拿起放茶粉的木盒，埋怨道：“姑爷茶还没做，事儿倒不少。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇自有他的道理：“要想将茶做好，必须从细微处着手，从你的态度能判断得出你平日都是随便做盏茶糊弄我家娘子。”
[ZH] 苦荞委屈道：“小姐，您听听，姑爷说的什么话？您的茶一直是我做，我是尽心尽力仔仔细细，姑爷竟然说我糊弄您。”
[ZH] 千里寻正在写话本，她现在不会特意避开陈浩宇。陈浩宇向来不随便靠近书桌，一般都站在一米开外的地方，即使想靠近，也会被苦荞阻止。
[ZH] 听到苦荞的话，千里寻放下笔，自行将书稿收起来，边收拾边说：“你对我怎样我还不清楚？何必在乎他说的话？”
[ZH] 苦荞面向姑爷噘了噘嘴，拿起木盒到隔壁的悦宁屋，把茶粉放进自己的木盒里。清理干净后，才又回到素心草堂。
[ZH] 小姐正坐在茶桌旁欣赏姑爷碾磨茶粉。
[ZH] 苦荞过去将木盒放好，此情此景甚是和谐，她不便打扰，拐进里屋去忙活。
[ZH] 陈浩宇认真碾磨茶粉的样子很是好玩儿，千里寻边看边笑：“你竟然也会做这种细发活儿，实在不容易。”
[ZH] “只要是为娘子做，多细发的活儿我都乐意费心。”
[ZH] 千里寻觉得这一刻她似乎爱上陈浩宇，便不再言语，细心观察他继续磨茶粉的样子，又观察他如何将碾磨好的茶粉取一些放入新的茶盏中，如何把剩下的存放在茶盒里。
[ZH] 接着陈浩宇的一番操作也不过是注水击拂，每个动作都比苦荞好看。
[ZH] 千里寻在心中慨叹，做茶也需天赋，苦荞做了那么久，动作的美感还比不上第一次为她做茶的人，真不愧是名师出高徒。
[ZH] 击拂够后，茶盏中乳沫汹涌，陈浩宇用木质茶勺的另一端在提前用水兑好的较黏稠的抹茶粉中取一些，开始在茶汤中作画。
[ZH] 千里寻兴致十足：“没想到你真的会在茶汤中作画？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇边作画边说：“这叫茶百戏，为了娘子我什么都乐意学。”
[ZH] 千里寻发现他画的是一棵柳树，等画完时，才看清是神柳：“也没见你去过神柳下，怎么会画它？”
[ZH] “娘子没见过，不等于我没去过。听下人说，你经常去神柳下，我便抽空去过两回，把神柳的样貌记下，想着画到茶汤中，娘子一定喜欢。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇说话间，在神柳下画了一个人影，是个女子的背影。
[ZH] “我画的是娘子，可像？”
[ZH] 千里寻仔细观察，的确有七分神似：“这么用心的一盏茶，我都不忍心喝下，你说该怎么办？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇将茶盏端起来，递到娘子面前：“我做茶就是为了让娘子品尝。”
[ZH] 千里寻接过，用标准的古人姿态将茶品了一口，果然比苦荞做得好多了：“汤感浓醇而无涩口，香高而无质钝之感，果然好手艺。”
[ZH] 得到娘子夸赞，陈浩宇比当伙计时听到客人的赞赏还要开心。
[EN] Outraged clan elders had dragged Peng Jingxin to the Judicial Office, where Song law mandated hanging. Shao Zhengye put up the silver for Peng’s family to commute it to life imprisonment—visiting him yearly with fine wine and bribing the jailers so Peng lived comfortably behind bars.
[ZH] 苦荞从里屋出来，故意装作不高兴的样子：“小姐如此夸赞姑爷做的茶，往后做茶之事都交给姑爷，我也好少些事做。”
[EN] “Then those Jin fighters who attacked your escort hall earlier...?” Shen Jianchen asked.
[ZH] 千里寻一边乐一边继续品茶，并不去理会。
[EN] Trusting the prince as a true friend, Shao Zhengye held nothing back. When Shao Yueting bore a boy, refusing to keep a Jin bastard, Shao Zhengye snatched the newborn and rode nonstop to the Jin capital of Huining. Half hoping the rough road would kill the infant—yet buying goat’s milk when the tough little tyke survived—he left him in an open inn room on a grim wager: if anyone stole the baby, he’d assassinate Wanyan Wuqimai; if not, Wuqimai lived. Finding the infant resting peacefully after locating Wuqimai’s princely palace, Shao Zhengye crept over the palace wall at dusk, thrust the baby straight into patrolling guards’ arms yelling “Wanyan Wuqimai’s bastard—keep him off Han girls!”, cut down two pursuing guards, and sliced off one’s pinky finger.
[ZH] 陈浩宇接话道：“那是自然，只怕你到时又跟我抢。”
[EN] Back in Lin’an, he lied to his sister that the baby died on the road and Wuqimai lay dead by his blade. Heartbroken, Shao Yueting shaved her head and entered a mountain nunnery.
[ZH] “放心，我绝不跟姑爷抢，做了这么久的茶，都做腻了，也该换换人。”
[EN] Twenty years later, that baby—now grown into Wanyan Chi—came to Lin’an posing as a grieving son hunting his father’s killers, tracked down Shao Escort Agency, and came demanding his birth mother, sparking the brawl when Shao Zhengye refused to yield.
[ZH] 苦荞说完，不再打扰二人，走出屋外找其他下人聊天儿去。
[EN] “A son seeking his mother is human nature,” Shen Jianchen remarked.
[ZH] 陈浩宇故意埋怨道：“你把她惯成什么样？从不把这姑爷放在眼里。”
[EN] “With Song-Jin tensions razor-thin, the Shaos can’t touch Jin royalty! Besides, my sister’s lived in peaceful seclusion twenty years—I won’t let him shatter her peace! He swore he’d even trade his life for one look at her, yet I’ll never let him see her—never!”
[ZH] 千里寻已将茶品完，把茶盏放在桌上：“苦荞说着玩儿的，你又何必当真？”
[EN] Remembering Shao Zhengye’s earlier remark about similar martial styles, Shen Jianchen asked, “The Jin fighter using moves like mine—that was Wanyan Chi?”
[ZH] 陈浩宇面带笑容问道：“往后你的茶都由我来做？早一盏，晚一盏，如何？”
[EN] “Yes.”
[ZH] “今日喝过你做的茶，恐怕以后苦荞做的茶我再也喝不下，当然需要你来做。”千里寻起身往书桌旁走。
[EN] *So Shen Fist traces back six hundred years—maybe born in Jurchen lands!* Settling the Peng Jingxin mystery in his mind, Shen Jianchen drank both jugs dry with father and son trading martial tales.
[ZH] 陈浩宇也起身跟上，他为自己终于学会娘子离不开他的技能而高兴。技能是有了，还需走进娘子的心里。
[EN] Over at Moxiang Courtyard, Chen Haoyu graduated from his tea lessons at Yongtai Teahouse and marched straight to the tea table—rejecting the old utensils, rejecting the worn tea whisk, and ordering Kuqiao to dump all her tea powder!
[ZH] “娘子，我想多读些你的诗词。”
[EN] “Haven’t even made a cup and already acting picky!” Kuqiao griped.
[ZH] 千里寻还未走到书桌旁，陈浩宇的话让她感到意外，转过身，盯着他仔细研究：“你真的想读诗？我怎么觉得像是做梦？”
[EN] “Great tea starts in the details—proves you’ve been slapping sloppy tea together for my wife!”
[ZH] “是真的，我既然娶了娘子，得多了解你。听人说诗词最能表达一个人内心的情感，我想知道娘子内心需要什么，好及时满足你。”
[EN] “Hear that, Miss?! Years of careful whisking and he calls it sloppy!”
[ZH] 千里寻被感动，一个男子能为她做到这等地步，着实不易。她走到书桌前，拿起一本诗集，递给陈浩宇：“给你，里面全是朱淑真写的诗词。”
[EN] Putting away her huaben manuscript—no longer hiding it since Chen Haoyu respectfully kept clear of her desk—Qianli Xun smiled. “I know how good you are to me; ignore him.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇接过：“娘子这话好像自己不是朱淑真一般？”
[EN] Pouting, Kuqiao dumped the powder into her own jar next door in Yuening Room, cleaned the caddy, and returned to see her miss smiling beside the tea table watching Young Master grind fresh powder—slipping quietly inside to leave them be.
[ZH] 陈浩宇的话倒是提醒了千里寻，朱淑真的诗词不能完全表达她。
[EN] “Never thought you’d have the patience for fine work like this,” Qianli Xun teased.
[ZH] “陈浩宇，你若想了解我，还有一个途径。”
[EN] “For my wife, no work is too fine.”
[ZH] “什么途径，娘子快说。”
[EN] A sudden rush of real warmth made her wonder if she was actually falling for him. Watching his crisp, graceful movements as he whisked water into creamy foam—smoother than Kuqiao’s even on his first try—she leaned closer in delight as he dipped the wooden spoon handle into thick tea paste to paint across the white froth.
[ZH] “去听千里寻才子写的故事。”
[EN] “You really can do tea-foam painting!”
[ZH] 陈浩宇纳闷道：“听说话艺人说书我当然十分乐意，只是不明白为何千里寻才子的故事能让我了解到娘子？”
[EN] “Chabaixi—anything to make my wife smile.”
[ZH] 千里寻掩饰道：“我听过几回，很喜欢千里寻才子写的故事，我既喜欢，说明他写的故事与我心性相合。”
[EN] Slowly a willow took shape—the Divine Willow! “You’ve never been there with me—how’d you draw it?”
[ZH] 陈浩宇更是纳闷：“那么多人都喜欢，怎么解释？”
[EN] “Heard servants say you visit it often, so I rode out twice myself to memorize every branch.” Adding a slender female silhouette beneath the boughs, he looked up hopeful. “That’s you. Close?”
[ZH] 千里寻不知该如何回答，强辩道：“别问那么多，反正你别光顾着读诗集，没事听听故事，绝对有帮助。”
[EN] “Seven parts likeness—too pretty to drink! What do I do?”
[ZH] 陈浩宇觉出娘子很为难，不再继续追问：“我听娘子的。”
[EN] “Drink it—that’s what I made it for!”
[ZH] 陈浩宇拿着诗集高高兴兴回到博雅斋，坐在书桌前开始翻看。
[EN] Taking a proper sip, Qianli Xun’s eyes widened. “Rich and mellow with zero bitterness, pure aroma—true mastery!”
[ZH] 翻到一首诗，句子有些熟悉，口中不由念出：“鸥鹭鸳鸯作一池，须知羽翼不相宜。东君不与花为主，何似休生连理枝。”
[EN] Praise lit Chen Haoyu brighter than any praise at the silk shop. Stepping out, Kuqiao pretended to sulk—“All tea duty goes to Young Master from now on!”—before strolling out to chat with the other servants.
[ZH] 念完他才想起来，是刚成亲回门时，娘子在马车上念出的诗。此时他边念边品，那时的娘子不过是想用此诗表达他们根本不是一路人，强行被婚姻绑在一起实在委屈。
[EN] “Morning bowl and evening bowl, every day?” Chen Haoyu beamed.
[ZH] 陈浩宇自语道：“以前我跟你不是一路人，往后一定会在一条路上，而且是我主动走在娘子要走的路上。”
[EN] “After tasting yours, I’m spoiled—deal!”
[ZH] “公子在看账本吗？”青瓜从外面进来，见公子拿本书看，以为公子近期学习管账做账的事入迷，回到家里都不闲着。
[EN] Walking after her toward the desk, thrilled he’d mastered a skill she couldn’t do without, he ventured, “Wife, I want to read your poetry.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇放下诗集：“天都黑了，你怎么才回来？整日不是当伙计，就是找丑丫，眼里还有没有我？”
[EN] Spinning around in surprise, she studied his face. “Pinch me—am I dreaming?”
[ZH] 青瓜凑近一瞧，不像账本，猜测是少夫人的诗集：“公子也变得文雅起来，竟然读起少夫人的诗来。”
[EN] “Dead serious. They say poetry mirrors the heart—I want to understand what’s inside yours.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇把诗集放在桌上：“别打岔，我问你的问题怎么不回答？”
[EN] Touched to the core, she picked a bound volume off the desk and handed it over. “Here—everything Zhu Shuzhen wrote.”
[ZH] 青瓜现在一点儿也不怕自家公子：“伙计是公子让我当的，丑丫也是公子让我喜欢的，这会儿怎么质问起我来？”
[EN] “Funny how you say that like you’re not Zhu Shuzhen.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇并未真正生气，笑了一下：“好像是这样，不过你也不能把我晾在一边，只顾当伙计，我身边都没个人伺候。”
[EN] Caught half off guard, she added, “One more way to understand me—listen to Scholar Qianli Xun’s storybooks.”
[ZH] 青瓜建议道：“我给公子出个主意，您把当伙计赚的银子给我，我就回到您身边。”
[EN] “Love storytelling! Only how do his tales show me your heart?”
[ZH] “去去去！不可能的事！从明日起你到绸缎庄的账房也跟着学管账，我去哪里你都得陪着。”
[EN] “I love his stories—means we think alike! Don’t ask why, just listen alongside the poems!”
[ZH] 听到让他学管账，青瓜更是高兴：“现在总算体会到跟着公子的好处，能学到很多本事，往后公子要是当了陈府的家，我是不是也大小能混个管事当当？”
[EN] “Whatever my wife says!”
[ZH] 陈浩宇批评道：“别胡说，只有东哥有能力当陈府的家，你跟着我帮东哥把生意打理好已是出息，想当管事，下辈子吧。”
[EN] Beaming back to Boya Study, Chen Haoyu opened the poetry collection—flipping right to a familiar verse and murmuring aloud: “Gulls and mandarin ducks swim in the same pond, everyone knows they don’t belong together. The Lord of the East has failed as master of flowers, why must we be intertwined branches all our lives?” [TN: Translated by Ronald Egan]
[ZH] 青瓜凑到公子耳边：“公子，我怎么觉得陈家交到你手里才可靠？”
[EN] The poem she had recited in the carriage on their post-wedding visit—saying they walked different worlds. “Maybe we weren’t on the same road before,” he vowed softly, “yet from now on I’ll walk right onto yours.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇推开青瓜：“在我面前说说行了，不要到处瞎说。”
[EN] Qinggua wandered in assuming he was studying ledgers, teasing him for turning scholarly, griping when Haoyu complained about being neglected for shop clerking and Chouya, and lighting up when Haoyu ordered him into the accounting room alongside him tomorrow.
[ZH] “公子放心，我绝不会乱说。太晚了，您就别看少夫人写的诗词，赶紧睡吧，明儿我们还要去绸缎庄忙活。”青瓜说着已开始打哈欠。
[EN] “Keep learning and maybe when Young Master runs the Chen house I’ll make steward!”
[ZH] 陈浩宇也觉出困意，跟着青瓜拐到里屋睡觉去了。
[EN] “Hush—only Brother Dong has the chops to run Chen Manor!”
[ZH] 当整个陈府的人进入梦乡，府内只能零星地听到夜晚虫子的鸣叫声时，有七八个黑影翻墙而入，他们像来过陈府一般，快速找到人居住的屋子，从窗户悄悄伸进一根长长的竹管，向屋内吹进迷烟，连下人睡觉的屋子也没放过。所有的屋子被迷烟笼罩，陈府的人都在睡梦中失去知觉。
[EN] “Dunno, Young Master—feels way safer in your hands,” Qinggua whispered before yawning him off to bed.
[ZH] 翻墙进来的人打开陈府的大门，放进许多蒙着面身穿夜行衣的人。
[EN] Deep into the dead of night, as crickets chirped over sleeping Chen Manor, seven or eight dark shadows vaulted the outer wall. Knowing the layout cold, they slipped long bamboo tubes through every window—even the servants’ quarters—blowing thick knockout smoke through every room until the entire household dropped senseless, then unbarred the front gate to let a horde of masked bandits pour inside.

#CH 304
[ZH] 身着夜行衣的蒙面人正是匪霸黑煞虎从天目山上带下来的，他们进入陈府到处翻箱倒柜寻找金银财宝，而陈家上下的人睡得跟死猪一般，毫不知情。
[ZH] 黑煞虎带几个弟兄如轻车熟路进入墨香苑，散播迷香之人早已将院门打开，他们进出畅通无阻。用刀将每个屋子的门闩巧妙拨开，然后把每个屋子翻一遍，值钱的物件统统打包后，黑煞虎才进入主屋，来到闺房中。
[EN] The masked men clad in night-prowler black were bandits brought down from Mount Tianmu by the brigand chief Black-Fiend Tiger. Slipping into Chen Manor, they ransacked chests and cabinets for gold, silver, and valuables while everyone in the household slept like the dead, none the wiser.
[ZH] 他从腰间拔出明晃晃的大刀，靠近闺床，依靠直觉判断，应该是床上躺着的这位。
[EN] Black-Fiend Tiger led several brothers straight into Moxiang Courtyard as though he knew every turn by heart. Whoever had spread the knockout incense had already unlatched the courtyard gate, giving them free run of the place. With a deft flick of his blade, he slid back the door bolts of each room, had his men strip every chamber of anything worth silver, and only then stepped into the main hall and entered the bedchamber.
[ZH] 在迷香的作用下，千里寻睡得很沉，根本不知有危险靠近。
[EN] Drawing a gleaming broadsword from his waist, he approached the canopy bed. Instinct told him his target was the person lying right here.
[ZH] 黑煞虎举起刀朝闺床上砍去时，千里寻转过身，正面朝向黑煞虎，他手中的刀不由自主落下，自语道：“长得真不赖，杀了可惜，不如扛到天目山上做我的压寨夫人。”
[EN] Under the lingering spell of the knockout incense, Qianli Xun slept so deeply she sensed no danger creeping near.
[ZH] 黑煞虎收起刀，将人用被子一卷，扛到肩上，带着弟兄们离开陈府。
[EN] Black-Fiend Tiger raised his broadsword to bring it down on the sleeper—just then Qianli Xun rolled over, turning face-up toward him. His blade faltered and dropped of its own accord. He murmured, “What a stunner. Pity to kill her. Might as well haul her up Mount Tianmu to be my bandit bride.”
[ZH] 陈府外早已有人停好马车，拴好马等待。黑煞虎将人放进马车里，其他人也都将抢来的值钱东西往其他马车里塞。
[EN] Sheathing his sword, Black-Fiend Tiger rolled her tight in the quilt, hoisted her onto his shoulder, and led his men out of Chen Manor.
[ZH] 差不多时，黑煞虎跳上一匹马，命令道：“快些离开，一会儿城中巡逻的士兵会来。”
[EN] Outside the estate, accomplices already waited beside hitched carriages and tethered horses. Black-Fiend Tiger shoved his bundle inside one carriage while the others crammed looted treasures into the rest.
[ZH] 一行人上车的上车，上马的上马，加快速度回天目山。
[EN] Once loaded, Black-Fiend Tiger vaulted onto a horse and barked, “Move out fast—city patrol soldiers will be sweeping through soon.”
[ZH] 林觉近期白天基本在懒人村当监工，天快黑时，才回到家里。最近他让手下替他前去，而他隔几日才过去一回。
[EN] Some piled onto carriages, others mounted up, and the gang galloped at full speed back toward Mount Tianmu.
[ZH] 今晚本是那位手下当值巡街的日子，林觉心血来潮，想着手下帮他监工，不如替他巡街，体验一回曾经当练兵官时的感觉。
[EN] Lately, Lin Jue spent most of his days supervising labor at Lazybones Village, returning home around dusk. More recently, he had begun sending a subordinate in his place, going out himself only once every few days.
[ZH] 正当林觉带人巡到陈家府邸的周围时，听到一阵马车和马蹄的声音，甚觉诧异，已是深夜怎么会有这么多人出行？于是带人迅速追赶。
[EN] Tonight happened to be that subordinate’s night watch on street patrol. On a whim, Lin Jue decided to take over the man’s patrol route to repay the favor—and to relive the old rush of serving as a military training officer.
[ZH] 经过陈府南门外，竟然发现大门敞开着，林觉意识到不对劲，吩咐两名士兵先守在门口，他则带人朝声音追去。很快看到一伙人就在前面，于是加快速度。
[EN] As Lin Jue led his men past the perimeter of Chen Manor, the clatter of carriage wheels and drumming hooves cut through the quiet. Strange—why were so many wagons rolling out at this hour of the night? He spurred his troops after them.
[ZH] 黑煞虎的人听到有官兵追赶，也快马加鞭。快到西边的城门口时，还是被林觉拦截。
[EN] Passing Chen Manor’s south gate, he saw the massive doors standing wide open. Knowing something was wrong, Lin Jue ordered two soldiers to stay behind and guard the entrance while he led the rest in pursuit of the receding rumble. Soon they spotted the convoy ahead and kicked their horses into a gallop.
[ZH] 林觉勒住缰绳，厉声问：“你们是什么人？为何大半夜在城里鬼鬼祟祟？赶紧把遮脸的布子取下来，再让本官查查你们的马车！”
[EN] Hearing government troops closing behind them, Black-Fiend Tiger’s men whipped their horses harder. Even so, Lin Jue cut them off just short of the west city gate.
[ZH] 黑煞虎岂容对方这般叫嚣？抽出大刀：“给我上！”
[EN] Lin Jue hauled back on his reins and shouted, “Who are you? Why are you sneaking through the city in the middle of the night? Pull off those masks and let this officer inspect your carriages!”
[ZH] 匪寇率先发难，挥舞钢刀，如猎豹般向官兵冲去。官兵们迅速反应，拔剑相向。
[EN] Black-Fiend Tiger wasn’t about to let some patrol officer bark orders at him. He whipped out his broadsword. “Cut them down!”
[ZH] 双方在街道上打起来，刀剑相交之声回荡在夜空。
[EN] The bandits struck first, brandishing steel blades and charging at the soldiers like leopards. Caught on edge, the troops reacted fast and drew their swords to meet them.
[ZH] 林觉迎战黑煞虎，对方不仅力气大，还刀锋凛冽，林觉自知不是对手，既打不过，不如先搞清楚对方是谁。
[EN] Steel clashed against steel in the middle of the street, ringing out across the night sky.
[ZH] 他用剑挡住对方一刀：“你们到底是何人？是不是刚从陈家出来？”
[EN] Lin Jue squared off against Black-Fiend Tiger. The brigand possessed brute strength and a vicious edge; realizing he was outmatched, Lin Jue decided that if he couldn’t win, he should at least find out who they were.
[ZH] 黑煞虎说出三个字：“天目山。”然后使出一招将林觉打下马，其他士兵也都败下阵来，黑煞虎带人迅速从西门出了城。
[EN] Parrying a heavy slash with his sword, Lin Jue demanded, “Who the hell are you? Did you just come from the Chen estate?”
[ZH] 林觉这才反应过来：“原来是黑煞虎！快！到陈家看看！”
[EN] Black-Fiend Tiger spat three words: “Mount Tianmu.” With one sweeping strike, he knocked Lin Jue clean off his horse. The remaining soldiers were beaten back as well, and Black-Fiend Tiger’s gang bolted straight out through the west gate.
[ZH] 林觉又跳上马，来到陈家，发现陈家被翻得乱七八糟，人倒无事，看来仅是丢了不少钱财。
[EN] Recognition hit Lin Jue a beat too late. “That was Black-Fiend Tiger! Quick—to the Chen estate!”
[ZH] 林觉本想知道朱淑真是否安全，又不好进入她的闺房，再说陈家太大，人又多，他也不知道朱淑真住在哪个院子，既然其他人无事，她应该是安全的。
[EN] Vaulting back into the saddle, Lin Jue raced to Chen Manor. Inside, everything had been turned upside down, though no one appeared harmed; it looked as if only money and valuables had been stolen.
[ZH] 此事体大，林觉不好做主，打算到幽林别居禀报仪王，请他裁夺。
[EN] Lin Jue wanted to check whether Zhu Shuzhen was safe, yet couldn’t very well barge into her private bedchamber. Besides, the Chen compound was sprawling and crowded, and he didn’t even know which courtyard she lived in. Since everyone else seemed unhurt, she was most likely safe.
[ZH] 申简辰在幽林别居陪梦容睡得正香，朦胧中听到李管家的声音：“仪王殿下，林统制来访，陈家出大事，需要向您请示。”
[EN] Given the gravity of the raid, Lin Jue dared not handle it on his own. He headed for Secluded Forest Villa to report to Prince Yi and await his orders.
[ZH] 乔梦容听到“陈家”二字，立刻清醒，估计黑煞虎已得手，她很想知道朱淑真如何，便在哥哥耳边说：“哥哥，城里发生大事，林统制来找你。”
[EN] At Secluded Forest Villa, Shen Jianchen was sound asleep beside Mengrong when Butler Li’s voice drifted through the haze: “Your Highness, Commander Lin is here. Something major has happened at the Chen estate—he needs your instructions.”
[ZH] 申简辰睁开眼，用睡意浓浓的声音问：“发生什么大事？”
[EN] At the words “Chen estate,” Qiao Mengrong snapped wide awake. Assuming Black-Fiend Tiger had done the deed, she burned to hear what had become of Zhu Shuzhen. She whispered in her brother’s ear, “Brother, something big happened in the city. Commander Lin is looking for you.”
[ZH] 门外传来林觉的声音：“殿下，黑煞虎抢劫了陈家，陈府所有人昏迷不醒。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen opened his eyes and asked in a voice thick with sleep, “What happened?”
[ZH] 申简辰变得清醒，立刻坐起来：“梦容，我得去陈府一趟。”
[EN] Lin Jue called from outside the door, “Your Highness, Black-Fiend Tiger raided Chen Manor. Everyone inside has been knocked unconscious.”
[ZH] 申简辰很快从床上下来，自行穿好衣裳。
[EN] Sleep vanished in an instant. Shen Jianchen sat straight up. “Mengrong, I have to go to the Chen estate right now.”
[ZH] 乔梦容心里很不是滋味，哥哥如此着急，肯定是揪心朱淑真。
[EN] He swung out of bed and dressed himself in seconds.
[ZH] 也许从今日起哥哥再也见不着她，乔梦容的情绪缓和许多。
[EN] A sour ache twisted inside Qiao Mengrong—seeing him panic so openly proved his heart was frantic over Zhu Shuzhen.
[ZH] “哥哥尽管去，我在家里等你回来。”
[EN] Still, if today meant he would never lay eyes on that woman again, her mood brightened considerably.
[ZH] 申简辰只觉梦容既懂事又温顺，回头赠予她赞许的笑容：“乖乖在家等我。”
[EN] “Go right ahead, Brother. I’ll wait right here at home for you.”
[ZH] 乔梦容假装道：“我很担心淑真，无论她境况如何，哥哥一定要第一个告诉我。”
[EN] Touched by how understanding and gentle she seemed, Shen Jianchen looked back and gave her an approving smile. “Be good and wait for me.”
[ZH] 申简辰为梦容关心幽栖居士而开心，以为在他的努力下，她们的关系正在逐渐恢复到从前。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong put on a worried face. “I’m so anxious about Shuzhen. No matter what state she’s in, Brother, promise you’ll tell me first thing.”
[ZH] 他点点头，答应梦容的要求。
[EN] Glad to see Mengrong showing concern for Youqi Jushi, Shen Jianchen believed his efforts were helping heal the rift between the two women. He nodded and gave his promise.
[ZH] 林觉与李管家站在院外等候，听到门被打开的声音时，仪王出现在面前。
[EN] Outside in the courtyard, Lin Jue and Butler Li were waiting. The door flew open, and Prince Yi stepped out.
[ZH] 没等林觉说话，申简辰命令道：“林统制，现在就随我去陈家。”
[EN] Before Lin Jue could utter a word, Shen Jianchen ordered, “Commander Lin, ride with me to Chen Manor at once.”
[ZH] 两人骑马来到陈府，先进门房查看完下人的景况后，申简辰吩咐一位士兵到医馆把郎中请来。
[EN] The two galloped back to Chen Manor. Checking the gatehouse first and seeing the servants out cold, Shen Jianchen sent a soldier to fetch a physician from the nearest clinic.
[ZH] 郎中来后，瞧了几个下人，立刻判断是被迷烟熏得昏睡过去，他的医馆里正好有解迷烟的熏香。
[EN] The physician arrived, checked a few servants, and diagnosed sleeping smoke right away. Conveniently, his shop stocked an antidote incense.
[ZH] 申简辰吩咐人跟着郎中到医馆取来许多熏香，先在下人的房中点燃，再将下人唤醒，吩咐男仆负责熏醒陈家的男主人，丫鬟则负责熏醒女主人。
[EN] Shen Jianchen sent men back with the physician to bring plenty of the antidote incense. They lit it in the servants’ quarters first; once the staff stirred, the male servants went to wake the Chen masters while the maids roused the mistresses.
[ZH] 申简辰坐在陈家的厅堂内，心里一直担心千里寻，希望她也只是被熏得晕过去，没有发生其他意外。
[EN] Sitting in the main hall of Chen Manor, Shen Jianchen worried sick about Qianli Xun, praying she had only fainted from the smoke and nothing worse had happened.
[ZH] 林觉多少也担心，毕竟曾经与她夫妻一场，现在也会时常想起。
[EN] Lin Jue worried a little too. After all, they had once been husband and wife, and she still crossed his mind from time to time.
[ZH] 两个男人为一个女人担忧，一个坐着，一个站着，沉默成为他们之间唯一的话题。
[EN] Two men fretted over the same woman—one seated, one standing—leaving silence as the only thing passing between them.
[ZH] 没多久，陈江林、陈江树、陈江木、陈浩东和陈浩飞来到厅堂，见到仪王都跪下：“感谢仪王前来救陈家。”
[EN] Before long, Chen Jianglin, Chen Jiangshu, Chen Jiangmu, Chen Haodong, and Chen Haofei staggered into the hall. Seeing Prince Yi, all of them dropped to their knees. “Thank you, Your Highness, for coming to save the Chen family.”
[ZH] 申简辰急切地问：“所有人可安全？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen cut straight to the point. “Is everyone safe?”
[ZH] 陈江林回道：“正在让人到各房查看。就目前情形判断，那伙贼人只图钱财并未害人，还好抢走的钱财并不会影响陈家的根基。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin answered, “I’ve sent servants to check every courtyard. From what we can tell so far, the bandits only came for silver and didn’t harm anyone. Thankfully, what they took won’t shake the family’s foundation.”
[ZH] 申简辰与林觉都松口气。
[EN] Both Shen Jianchen and Lin Jue breathed easier.
[ZH] “三位不必跪着，赶紧起来坐下说话。”
[EN] “No need to kneel, gentlemen,” Prince Yi said. “Get up and take a seat.”
[ZH] 仪王发话，大家都起身，三兄弟坐下，陈浩东与陈浩飞站在各自父亲身后。
[EN] At his word, they rose. The three elder brothers sat while Chen Haodong and Chen Haofei stood behind their respective fathers.
[ZH] 陈浩东此时多少感到后悔，不该将陈家宅子的布局图纸拿出来，搞得陈家不仅丢失许多钱财，所有人还被迷晕，恐怕时间一长会有生命危险。
[EN] Chen Haodong was squirming with regret now. He never should have handed over the layout map of the estate—not only had the family lost a fortune, everyone had been drugged, and who knew what could have happened if they’d breathed that smoke much longer?
[ZH] 后悔已来不及，事已至此只希望弟妹被人除掉，否则仪王妃一不高兴下个月不来替他坐镇，他又如何能在族人面前荣耀一番？
[EN] Regret came too late, though. All he could hope now was that his sister-in-law had been wiped out; otherwise, if Princess Consort Yi took offense and refused to show up next month to lend him face, how was he supposed to dazzle the clan?
[ZH] 申简辰没看到陈浩宇，不由担忧道：“怎么不见陈家二公子？”
[EN] Noticing Chen Haoyu was missing, Shen Jianchen felt a spike of alarm. “Where is Second Young Master Chen?”
[ZH] 陈江林回道：“浩宇关心淑真，没跟着来。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin said, “Haoyu went to check on Shuzhen, so he didn’t come with us.”
[ZH] 话音刚落，陈浩宇冲进来，满脸焦急：“爹，我娘子不在屋里，我找遍整个墨香苑都没找到她，她白天穿的衣裳还放在屋里，床上的被子却不见。”
[EN] The words had barely faded when Chen Haoyu burst into the hall, panic etched across his face. “Father! My wife isn’t in her room! I searched every inch of Moxiang Courtyard and couldn’t find her! Her daytime clothes are still lying right there, and the quilt on her bed is gone!”
[ZH] 没等陈江林说话，申简辰已等不及，站起来：“林统制，我先带人连夜上天目山，你到司理院叫上温都头，再带些人随后跟上。”
[EN] Before Chen Jianglin could speak, Shen Jianchen shot to his feet. “Commander Lin, I’m taking a squad up Mount Tianmu tonight. Go to the Judicial Office, grab Chief Constable Wen, and bring more men up behind me.”
[ZH] 林觉应声：“是！”
[EN] “Yes, Your Highness!”
[ZH] 申简辰迅速往外走。
[EN] Shen Jianchen strode straight out the door.
[ZH] 陈浩宇紧跟上：“仪王等我！我跟您一起去！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu sprinted after him. “Wait for me, Prince Yi! I’m coming with you!”
[ZH] 当申简辰带着人马来到天目山脚下时，天已放亮。马留在山下，一行人顺着山路朝山上走去。
[EN] By the time Shen Jianchen and his riders reached the foot of Mount Tianmu, dawn was breaking. Leaving the horses below, the group pushed up the mountain trail on foot.
[ZH] ……
[EN] ...
[ZH] 黑煞虎带人从西门逃走后，赶回天目山。这次下山收获颇丰，抢来的金银够山上的弟兄挥霍一整年。
[EN] Meanwhile, after escaping out the west gate, Black-Fiend Tiger raced his men back up Mount Tianmu. Tonight’s haul was huge—enough gold and silver to keep the mountain brothers living high for a whole year.
[ZH] 更令他开心的是跑去杀人，竟然遇到一个大美人儿，实在不忍心让她死在自己的刀下。
[EN] Even better, he’d gone down the mountain on a murder job and stumbled onto a breathtaking beauty he simply couldn’t bear to put under his blade.
[ZH] 他把用被子裹着的美人放在榻上，点亮灯，仔细瞧她熟睡的样子，真是越看越喜欢，无论是眉眼还是嘴巴鼻子，每个弧度都令他无比开心，天下竟有这等标致的女子，他可是第一次遇见。
[EN] Setting the quilt-wrapped beauty onto a couch, he lit a lamp and stared down at her sleeping face. The longer he looked, the more smitten he grew—every curve of her brows, eyes, nose, and lips delighted him. Never in his life had he set eyes on a woman so exquisite.
[ZH] 黑煞虎本想趁美人睡着时动了她，又一想，对方睡着，自己一个人玩儿多没意思，不如把她弄醒，吓唬吓唬，再好好哄哄，相信她为了保住性命绝对会屈服于自己。
[EN] He thought about taking her while she was still out cold, then figured having his fun with someone dead asleep took half the sport out of it. Better to wake her up, give her a good scare, then sweet-talk her a bit—to save her own neck, she’d cave in soon enough.
[ZH] 他让手下拿来熏香，用蜡烛的火苗点燃，在美人的鼻子前晃了一会儿。美人动了动，依然沉浸在梦乡。
[EN] He ordered a henchman to bring reviving incense, lit it from the candle flame, and waved it under her nose. The beauty stirred, still sunk deep in dreams.
[ZH] 黑煞虎把香递给身旁的手下：“滚！我要跟美人快活，你还杵在这儿做什么？”
[EN] Black-Fiend Tiger shoved the incense back at his man. “Get lost! I’m about to enjoy this beauty—what are you still standing around for?”
[ZH] 千里寻朦胧中听到有男子的声音回响在耳边，以为自己做梦，没当回事，继续睡。
[EN] Through her haze, Qianli Xun heard a man’s voice humming near her ear. Assuming it was a dream, she ignored it and kept sleeping.
[ZH] 黑煞虎凑到跟前：“美人儿，醒醒，看看我是谁？”
[EN] Black-Fiend Tiger leaned right in. “Wake up, pretty thing. Take a look who’s here.”
[ZH] 耳边有人唤她美人，千里寻才知不是梦。难道是陈浩宇唤她？可陈浩宇平常只唤她“娘子”，从来没这么称呼过她，再说这声音不大像陈浩宇。
[EN] Hearing someone call her “pretty thing” jolted Qianli Xun awake—this was no dream. Was it Chen Haoyu? No, Chen Haoyu always called her “Wife,” never “pretty thing,” and that voice didn’t sound like him at all.
[ZH] 千里寻意识到不对劲，她睁开眼睛，瞧到一张肤色黝黑，五官粗犷，她从来没见过脸出现在眼前。她很担心，难道睡了一觉又穿越了不成？
[EN] Sensing trouble, Qianli Xun snapped her eyes open—and found herself staring into a dark-skinned, coarse-featured face she had never seen before in her life. Dread sank into her stomach: had she transmigrated again in her sleep?
[ZH] 她伸手推开陌生的脸，坐起来将周围观察一遍，不是素心草堂，也不是陈府，看来八成又穿越了。
[EN] She shoved the stranger’s face away, sat up, and scanned the room. Neither Suxin Thatched Hall nor Chen Manor. Eight times out of ten, she’d jumped bodies again.
[ZH] “我是谁？”
[EN] “Who am I?” she demanded.
[ZH] 黑煞虎坐在榻边：“这话问得奇怪，你是谁你自己不知道？”
[EN] Sitting on the edge of the couch, Black-Fiend Tiger blinked. “What kind of fool question is that? You don’t know who you are?”
[ZH] “你是谁？”
[EN] “Who are you?”
[ZH] “我是大名鼎鼎的黑煞虎。”黑煞虎以为自己这么一说，美人会吓得魂飞魄散。
[EN] “I’m the famous Black-Fiend Tiger.” He waited for the beauty to turn white as a sheet and tremble with terror.
[ZH] 她却一点儿不害怕，还厉声说：“老娘管你是黑虎还是白虎，赶紧告诉老娘这是哪里？”
[EN] Instead, she didn’t flinch an inch. Her voice turned sharp as steel. “I don’t care if you’re a black tiger or a white tiger—tell me where the hell I am right now!”
[ZH] 黑煞虎饶有趣味地回道：“天目山。”
[EN] Amused, Black-Fiend Tiger answered, “Mount Tianmu.”
[ZH] “天目山？”千里寻嘀咕着，“哪个天目山？”
[EN] “Mount Tianmu?” Qianli Xun muttered. “Which Mount Tianmu?”
[ZH] “还能是哪个天目山？临安就这一个，被我霸占。今晚把你从陈府抢来，是不是很害怕？只要你跟了我，给我当压寨夫人，我会好好待你。”
[EN] “How many Mount Tianmus do you think there are? There’s only one in Lin’an, and it’s mine. I snatched you right out of Chen Manor tonight. Scared yet? Just submit to me and be my bandit bride, and I’ll treat you real nice.”
[ZH] 千里寻总算听明白，她并没有第二次穿越，而是在晚上熟睡之时被眼前的黑什么虎抢到山上来。
[EN] At last Qianli Xun pieced it together. She hadn’t transmigrated a second time; while she slept, this Black-whatever-Tiger had hauled her off to the mountains.
[ZH] 她是练武之人，即使晚上睡觉也十分警觉，这家伙肯定用了什么招数，她才没有任何知觉，稀里糊涂被带到山上。
[EN] She was a martial artist, always alert even in sleep—he must have used some dirty trick to knock her out cold and bundle her all the way up here without her knowing.
[ZH] 千里寻瞧瞧自己身上，还好她睡觉时穿得睡衣够严实，再看看被子，正是睡觉时盖的，由此判断是被他裹着被子带出陈府。
[EN] Checking herself, she was relieved to find her sleeping clothes intact and tight. Seeing her own quilt beside her, she deduced he had rolled her inside it to carry her out of Chen Manor.
[ZH] 千里寻瞧向黑煞虎：“黑什么虎，我与你素不相识，也没得罪过你，为何要把我抢到山上来？”
[EN] Qianli Xun glared at Black-Fiend Tiger. “Listen, Black-whatever-Tiger, I don’t know you and I’ve never crossed you. Why kidnap me up this mountain?”
[ZH] “黑煞虎！”黑煞虎强调完，解释道，“我受人所托下山杀你，谁让你长得够美，我实在不忍心杀，把你连夜带到山上来。”
[EN] “Black-Fiend Tiger!” he corrected, then explained, “Someone paid me to go down the mountain and kill you. Trouble is, you’re too gorgeous—I couldn’t bring myself to slice you up, so I hauled you back up here tonight instead.”
[ZH] 千里寻想到梦容，为了杀她，什么手段都使得出，她感到一阵伤心。
[EN] Mengrong flashed through Qianli Xun’s mind. To think she would stop at nothing to have her killed—a wave of sorrow washed over her chest.
[ZH] 黑煞虎以为美人害怕，凑近劝道：“你别怕，只要从了我，你是我的人，我自然不会杀你，现在就陪我快活快活！”
[EN] Thinking the beauty had lost her nerve, Black-Fiend Tiger leaned in to coax her. “Don’t be scared. Once you give in and belong to me, I won’t kill you. Now come on, let’s have ourselves a good time!”
[ZH] 黑煞虎说完就要上手，结果被美人一拳打在脸上，整个人从榻边跌落在地上。
[EN] He reached out to grab her—and caught a fist square in the face, tumbling straight off the couch onto the floor.
[ZH] “想轻薄老娘！没那么容易！”
[EN] “Try getting fresh with me! Not in this lifetime!”
[ZH] 黑煞虎摸摸被打疼的脸，已肿起，他咧着嘴从地上爬起来：“呵！竟然还有两下子，少见，烈女我还没玩过，今日就拿你开荤。”
[EN] Rubbing his throbbing, already-swelling cheek, Black-Fiend Tiger bared his teeth and scrambled up off the dirt. “Hah! Got some fight in you, eh? Rare! Never had myself a fiery spitfire before—today I’ll break you in!”
[ZH] 黑煞虎向榻上扑了过去，千里寻并不怕，用拳头迎接对方，两人赤手空拳打起来。
[EN] Black-Fiend Tiger lunged at the couch. Unafraid, Qianli Xun met him head-on with her fists, trading bare-knuckle blows.
[ZH] 打斗的过程中千里寻从榻上跳到地上，身姿灵活，连连挥出几拳。黑煞虎被打得不断后退，嘴角溢出许多鲜血。
[EN] Mid-brawl, Qianli Xun vaulted off the couch onto the floor, moving light on her feet as she fired off punch after punch. Black-Fiend Tiger staggered backward, blood oozing from the corner of his mouth.
[ZH] 黑煞虎手下许多人进屋前来相助，千里寻的拳头加上脚上功夫，将来人一个个踢倒在地。
[EN] Dozens of his bandits rushed into the room to back him up, yet Qianli Xun’s fists and kicks sent every single man sprawling to the floor one after another.
[ZH] 直到天已放亮，黑煞虎的人都无法取胜。
[EN] By the time daylight broke outside, Black-Fiend Tiger’s gang still couldn’t take her down.
[ZH] 其中一人见被抢来的女子光着脚，心生一计，趁她与别人打斗时，将一块形状尖利的石子扔过去。
[EN] Noticing their captive was barefoot, one bandit hatched a sly trick. While she tangled with the others, he tossed a jagged, sharp-edged stone across the floor.
[ZH] 当千里寻落下踢向他人的脚时，正好踩在石子上，脚掌被刺破，鲜血流出，一阵钻心的疼痛袭来。因疼痛她一时分心，被黑煞虎的手下拿住，用绳子绑在椅子上。
[EN] Coming down from a sweeping kick, Qianli Xun planted her bare heel dead onto the shard. It sliced into her sole, drawing blood and sending a lance of blinding pain up her leg. Distracted for one fatal second, she was seized by Black-Fiend Tiger’s men and lashed tight to a chair with heavy rope.
[ZH] 千里寻动弹不得，脚掌的鲜血正在往下流淌，她忍住痛：“劝你还是乖乖放了老娘！否则官府不会放过你！”
[EN] Unable to move while blood dripped from her sole, she gritted her teeth through the pain. “You’d better untie me right now! Otherwise the yamen won’t let you walk away from this!”
[ZH] “官府又能奈何得了我？”黑煞虎用手抹掉嘴角的鲜血，“能把你顺利从陈府带出来，不就证明临安的官府屁都不顶。实话告诉你，我在城里还跟几个官兵打起来，他们那两下子，没过几招便被我制服，乖乖放我出城。”
[EN] “What can the yamen do to me?” Black-Fiend Tiger wiped the blood off his lip with the back of his hand. “Pulling you clean out of Chen Manor proves Lin’an’s yamen isn’t worth a fart. Truth be told, I even fought a squad of government soldiers inside the city—those weaklings folded in a couple of moves and let me ride straight out the gate.”
[ZH] “让你杀我的人是谁？”尽管千里寻已猜到是梦容，可她还想听他人亲口说出。
[EN] “Who hired you to kill me?” Though Qianli Xun already suspected Mengrong, she wanted to hear it straight from his mouth.
[ZH] 黑煞虎只答应过春金沫的要求，并不知背后有仪王妃。他也没那么傻，不会随便供出春金沫。
[EN] Black-Fiend Tiger had only ever dealt with Chun Jinmo and knew nothing about Princess Consort Yi behind the scenes—and he wasn’t stupid enough to rat out Chun Jinmo either.
[ZH] “问这些有什么用？别人花钱买我杀你，我杀就是，只不过看你长得美，想让你多活一会儿。既然你想寻死，老子也不怜香惜玉，现在就了结了你，拿刀来！”
[EN] “What’s the point asking? Someone paid good money for your head, so I took the job. Only spared you this long because of your pretty face. Since you’re so eager to die, I won’t waste any more mercy—time to finish you off. Bring me a blade!”
[ZH] 手下扔过一把刀，黑煞虎接住，手握明晃晃的大刀，靠近千里寻……
[EN] A henchman tossed over a broadsword. Black-Fiend Tiger caught it, gripped the gleaming steel, and stalked toward Qianli Xun.

#CH 305
[ZH] 当大刀即将落在千里寻身上时，一阵寒气袭来，她闭上眼睛，想着今日要命丧黑煞虎的刀下。
[ZH] 黑煞虎要砍死美人的瞬间，也的确不舍，如此美貌，他动都没动，就这么死了，真是可惜，手不由在空中犹豫片刻。
[ZH] 片刻之际，一颗石子以难以阻挡的速度袭来，击打在黑煞虎的手背上，他一哆嗦，大刀掉在地上，发出“咣当”的声音。
[ZH] 千里寻睁开眼，发现申简辰在众人还没有反应过来时，用拳头击打而来，一拳将黑煞虎打倒在地，迅速挡在千里寻面前。
[ZH] “别怕，我来救你。”
[ZH] 面对申简辰，千里寻无需要说任何感激的话，只需默默享受他的保护，再说他很少有这样的机会。
[ZH] 有人竟敢只身闯入黑风寨，纯粹是找死的节奏。黑煞虎从地上爬起来，向众位弟兄挥手：“给我上！”
[ZH] 千里寻对申简辰的功夫再了解不过，屋里这些人根本不是他的对手。
[ZH] 黑风寨的人挥舞大刀蜂拥而上，申简辰左右躲闪，借力反弹，找准破绽打得众人落刀的落刀，后退的后退，有的甚至拿着刀退出门外。
[ZH] 黑煞虎见状，捡起地上的大刀向申简辰坎来。两人打了十来个回合，最后申简辰一脚将黑煞虎踢出门外。
[ZH] 正要出去打时，被千里寻唤住：“别只顾恋战！赶紧帮我把绳子解开！”
[ZH] 申简辰过去，帮千里寻解开身上的绳子，这才发现她脚底流血：“疼吗？”
[ZH] “这点儿伤不算什么。”千里寻站起来，正好瞅到屋里放着一双女人的鞋子，她忍着脚心的疼痛，装作没事的样子走过去把鞋子穿上。
[ZH] 有鞋子将脚与地面隔开，脚舒服许多，伤口也没有刚才疼痛。
[ZH] 申简辰脱掉身上的外衣，递过去：“穿上。”
[ZH] 千里寻接过，将外衣披在身上：“今日老娘要血洗天目山！”
[ZH] 申简辰笑道：“差点儿被人杀了，还说大话？”
[ZH] “少废话！”
[ZH] 他们来到黑风寨的院中，黑煞虎的手下围将过来。
[ZH] 黑煞虎身上虽有伤，也打不过二人，却不愿轻易认输。他杀人从未失过手，若这次败下阵来，江湖地位恐怕不保。
[ZH] “你们二人寡不敌众，若老实认输，还可留你们一条性命！”
[ZH] 申简辰冷笑道：“黑煞虎！我的人已在外面将整个黑风寨包围，识相的带着你的人放下刀老实投降，否则我踏平整个黑风寨！”
[ZH] 听到被包围，黑煞虎紧张起来：“你是官府的人？”
[ZH] 申简辰说：“谁让你不长眼，抢了临安知府的千金，今日我就带人剿了你的黑风寨！”
[ZH] 千里寻靠近申简辰：“让外面的人先别进来，我好久没动过拳头，今日要打个痛快。”
[ZH] “我陪你。”申简辰大声向外面喊道，“外面的人听着！等我和幽栖居士打爽了！你们再进来！”
[ZH] 黑煞虎和他手下的人多少被吓住。
[ZH] 镇定片刻后，黑煞虎向众人说：“别信他的，若外面有人，怎么可能这么安静？不过是吓唬人罢了。他们两个再厉害，也敌不过黑风寨的人轮番上阵，等跟他们打累了，我们再一人一刀结果了他们的小命。一起上！”
[ZH] 千里寻与申简辰背靠着背，做好打架的准备。
[ZH] 一拨人先举刀向他们坎来，两人赤手空拳巧妙躲过大刀的袭击，然后利用拳脚功夫，没出几个回合将用刀砍来的人打倒在地，人刀分离，趴在地上难以起身。
[ZH] 接着一拨人被打趴下，又一拨人上来，均不是二人的对手。
[ZH] 直到将第六拨打趴下后，申简辰问：“累吗？”
[ZH] 千里寻轻松回道：“打得正爽，一点儿不觉得累。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇在外面躲着已失去耐心，仪王与娘子配合得如此默契，令他担忧不已，再危险也要冲进去，要让娘子知道他也赶来救她。
[ZH] 陈浩宇一脚踢开黑风寨的木门，出现在大家面前：“娘子！我来救你！”
[ZH] 千里寻大喊：“你跑进来做什么？赶紧躲开！”
[ZH] 陈浩宇来不及反应，已被离他较近的黑煞虎控制：“美人儿，今日要么你死在这里，要么我立刻将他杀了。”
[ZH] 千里寻并未将黑煞虎放在眼里：“老娘既不会死，也不会让你把他杀了。”
[ZH] 申简辰见状向外面喊道：“外面的人还不赶紧冲进来！”
[ZH] 林觉和温信早已带人前来，一直埋伏在外，听到仪王一声令下，冲了进来。
[ZH] 黑煞虎见状，一时紧张有所分神，千里寻趁机一拳上去将黑煞虎打倒，两边的人也立刻打起来。
[ZH] 黑煞虎努力爬起，捡了地上的大刀向千里寻坎来。
[ZH] 陈浩宇冲上去，挡在娘子前面：“娘子小心！”
[ZH] 千里寻说：“他根本打不过我，你小心才是。”
[ZH] 申简辰已冲过去几拳将黑煞虎制服。
[ZH] 黑风寨的人虽凶狠，经过千里寻和申简辰轮番教训后，一个一个身心俱疲，没打多久，轻而易举被带来的人制服。临安的士兵们用绳子将所有人绑起来，押往山下。
[ZH] 申简辰也已将黑煞虎绑好，靠近他的耳边：“想杀我的人没那么容易，反而给我提供一次剿匪的机会，也算是为临安百姓做了件善事。你先老老实实待在临安的牢狱里，等我查清你的罪行，再按照律法给你定罪。”
[ZH] 申简辰向温信吩咐：“辛苦温都头亲自把黑煞虎押到牢狱中关押起来。”
[ZH] “是，大人。”温信带上司理院的人，押着黑煞虎向山下走去。
[ZH] 待最后几个士兵将人押走后，林觉来到仪王身边汇报情况：“黑风寨上下三百五十人，其中包括黑煞虎抢到山上的10名女子全部已押下山。昨晚黑煞虎从陈家抢劫的钱财也都如数清点完毕。”
[ZH] 申简辰叮嘱道：“下山后将十名女子放了，让她们各自回家。”
[ZH] “是。”林觉说完，准备下山，经过朱淑真身边时，忍不住说，“往后少在外面惹事，自己的命差点儿没了，还害得陈家跟着你遭殃。”
[ZH] 没等千里寻回应，陈浩宇怼道：“她是我娘子，还轮不到林统制教训，再说没我娘子，你哪有本事上天目山把人绑走？”
[ZH] 林觉指着陈浩宇：“瞧你那贱样！以前的威风都跑哪里去了？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇上前双手推开林觉：“这里没你什么事，赶紧下山。”
[ZH] “别推我，我自己会走。”林觉瞧向朱淑真，结果被她瞪了一眼，只好收回眼神出了黑风寨。
[ZH] 申简辰关切地问：“你能不能走下山？”
[ZH] 千里寻回道：“这点儿伤对我来讲根本不算什么。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇听到二人对话，转身向仪王行礼：“感谢殿下救我娘子，我这就和娘子一起送殿下下山。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇的存在再次提醒申简辰，他已没有资格关心千里寻，他独自走到黑风寨的门口：“那就辛苦二位。”
[ZH] 千里寻刚迈出脚步，脚掌的伤口再次涌出一股血来，一阵钻心的疼痛。本就有伤，再经过一场激战，伤口更严重，想要走下山十分困难。
[ZH] 娘子的右脚的斜侧着，鞋底渗出许多血来，陈浩宇上前扶好：“娘子，你的脚底受伤了？我背你下山。”
[EN] Cold steel hovered above her; Qianli Xun shut her eyes, bracing for the end.
[ZH] 申简辰也担忧地停住脚步转过身，千里寻已趴在陈浩宇的背上，二人亲密的样子令他既伤感又羡慕。
[EN] Even at the last split second, Black-Fiend Tiger hesitated a heartbeat—shame to waste such beauty.
[ZH] 他想象背着千里寻的人是自己，可想到梦容，不敢再想象下去，转过头，边走边说：“陈公子背着幽栖居士下山就不怕被累坏？”
[EN] Zip! A flying pebble cracked across his knuckles like lightning. His fingers spasmed, the saber clattering to the floor.
[ZH] 陈浩宇一脸幸福：“背自己的娘子高兴着呢，一点儿都不累。”
[EN] Qianli Xun’s eyes snapped open as Shen Jianchen blurred past everyone, decked Black-Fiend Tiger flat with a single punch, and planted himself straight in front of her. “Don’t be afraid. I’m here.”
[ZH] 申简辰建议道：“要不你们先在山上待着，我下去让士兵找个担架帮你把幽栖居士抬下去？”
[EN] No thanks needed between them—she let herself breathe behind his broad back.
[ZH] 陈浩宇急了：“不用！不用！”
[EN] Furious, Black-Fiend Tiger scrambled up yelling, “Kill him!” Bandits swarmed in with sabers, only for Shen Jianchen to weave through like wind, knocking blades spinning and driving men reeling back through the doorway before booting Black-Fiend Tiger clean out into the yard after ten bouts.
[ZH] 申简辰不再说什么，也不再往身后瞧。他怕看到最不愿看到的亲密画面，搞得他心里很不是滋味。
[EN] “Quit showing off and untie me first!” Qianli Xun called out.
[ZH] 可耳边还是传来二人的对话声。
[EN] Hurrying back to cut her ropes, Shen Jianchen spotted blood pooling under her sole. “Hurt bad?”
[ZH] “你要是累了，就把我放下来，歇一会儿再背。”
[EN] “Nothing.” Spotting a spare pair of women’s shoes in the room, she gritted past the sting and slipped them on, easing the cut off the grit. Shen Jianchen shrugged off his outer robe and handed it over; draping it round her shoulders, she grinned fierce. “Time to paint Mount Tianmu red!”
[ZH] “一点儿都不累，我要一口气把娘子背下山。你脚上流了那么多血，伤口一定很严重，得快些回去上了药我才放心。”
[EN] “Nearly got chopped up and still talking big?”
[ZH] 千里寻紧紧抱住陈浩宇的脖子，这一刻她体验到前所未有的幸福，一阵疲惫感袭来，渐渐睡去。
[EN] “Less talk, more fists!”
[ZH] 被娘子紧紧抱住，陈浩宇背得更起劲儿。
[EN] Stepping out into the yard, bandits ringed them tight. Bloodied yet stubborn, Black-Fiend Tiger bluffed, “Outnumbered—surrender and live!”
[ZH] 背人下山的确不容易，陈浩宇尽量将步子放慢踩稳，还是不小心踩到山路上的石子，被滑了一下，差点跌倒。
[EN] “My soldiers already surrounded your whole stronghold!” Shen Jianchen shot back cold. “Drop your blades or we raze this place flat!”
[ZH] 多亏仪王反应迅速，将他扶住，才没倒下。
[EN] “Let your guys wait outside,” Qianli Xun murmured at his shoulder. “My fists are itching—let me fight my fill!”
[ZH] “多谢仪王。”
[EN] “With you all the way.” Raising his voice, Shen Jianchen called out, “Boys outside—hold the perimeter till Youqi Jushi and I finish warming up!”
[ZH] 待陈浩宇站稳后，申简辰才松开手：“小心些，山路滑。”
[EN] Half rattled, Black-Fiend Tiger rallied his mob. “Bluffing—hear how quiet it is? Swarm them in waves till they drop, then hack ’em to pieces!”
[ZH] 千里寻趴在陈浩宇背上睡去的样子十分可爱，申简辰看得入迷。
[EN] Standing back to back, Qianli Xun and Shen Jianchen tore through wave after wave bare-handed, dodging bright steel and sending bandits crashing groaning to the dirt—six full waves dropped before Shen Jianchen asked, “Tired?”
[ZH] 陈浩宇发现仪王盯着自己的娘子时，眼神中充满爱意，他提醒道：“殿下，恐怕山下的人等得焦急，快些下山吧。”
[EN] “Just getting warmed up!”
[ZH] 申简辰自知失态，尴尬地说：“走吧。”
[EN] Peeking from outside the gate, Chen Haoyu couldn’t stomach watching Prince Yi and his wife fight in effortless sync another second. Kicking the timber gate open, he charged in yelling, “Wife! I’m here to save you!”
[ZH] 顺着山路继续向下走，人在前面，申简辰的心却放在身后，时刻警惕着，担心陈浩宇稍有不慎跌倒在地，惊到他背上的人。
[EN] “What are you doing in here?! Get back!” Qianli Xun yelled—too late, as Black-Fiend Tiger snatched Chen Haoyu in a chokehold right beside him.
[ZH] 陈浩宇则走得更加小心，自己跌倒受伤不算什么，千万不能让娘子再受伤。
[EN] “Die right now, beauty, or I snap his neck!”
[ZH] 当他们走下山时，太阳已老高，山下大部分士兵押着黑风寨的人先回城里，只留林觉带着几人在山下等待。
[EN] “Neither of us is dying today!”
[ZH] 仪王终于从山上下来，林觉迎上前：“殿下，其他人半个时辰前已回城里，下官主动留下等待王爷，您是骑马回城还是乘坐马车？”
[EN] “Charge in—now!” Shen Jianchen barked.
[ZH] 申简辰向上山的路口瞧去，陈浩宇背着千里寻已累得气喘吁吁。
[EN] On cue, Lin Jue and Wen Xin burst through the stockade with Lin’an’s soldiers. Distracted for half a second, Black-Fiend Tiger ate Qianli Xun’s flying fist, stumbled back, snatched a blade to slash at her—Chen Haoyu leaping straight in front shouting “Watch out, Wife!”—before Shen Jianchen tackled the bandit chief down and knocked him cold.
[ZH] “我骑马，马车让给她们。”
[EN] Exhausted from six rounds with the pair, the remaining bandits folded in minutes. Soldiers bound all three hundred fifty brigands, freed ten captive women, and tallied Chen Manor’s stolen loot as Wen Xin hauled Black-Fiend Tiger down to death row.
[ZH] 林觉命令一位士兵将马牵来，待仪王跳上马背后，才走到陈浩宇跟前，朱淑真在陈浩宇的背上睡得正香。
[EN] Passing Zhu Shuzhen on his way out, Lin Jue couldn’t resist a snide dig. “Quit stirring up trouble outside—nearly got killed and dragged Chen Manor down with you!”
[ZH] “陈公子，仪王让你们坐马车，还不赶紧带着你的夫人上马车？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu jumped right in his face. “My wife—not your place to lecture! Without her, you’d never have climbed this hill!”
[ZH] 陈浩宇并不理会林觉，扭过头轻声说：“娘子，醒醒，我背着你不好上马车，得抱着你才行。”
[EN] “Look at you groveling—where’d your old swagger go?”
[ZH] 千里寻根本没听到陈浩宇的声音。
[EN] Shoving Lin Jue toward the gate, Chen Haoyu barked, “Scram down the mountain!” Catching Qianli Xun’s sharp glare, Lin Jue scoffed and walked out.
[ZH] 林觉调侃道：“你是不是男人？声音能不能放大些？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen asked softly, “Can you walk down?”
[ZH] 陈浩宇解释道：“我怕太大吵到我娘子。”
[EN] “Scratch like this? Easy.”
[ZH] “你把男人的脸都丢尽了，瞧我的。”林觉靠近朱淑真，在她耳边大声说，“朱淑真！再不醒来！我可就动拳头了！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu bowed polite yet firm. “Thank you for saving my wife, Your Highness. We’ll escort you down.”
[ZH] 正睡得香，耳边传来聒噪之声，还说要动拳头，千里寻睡眼蒙眬中已生出怒气，头还未抬，已抡起拳头，用睡意浓浓的声音说：“竟然敢打老娘，看老娘不揍死你。”
[EN] Reminded he had no place left at her side, Shen Jianchen stepped out to the gate. “Lead on, then.”
[ZH] 林觉早有准备，用手挡住对方的手臂，还好她刚睡醒，力度不是很大，林觉还能挡得住。
[EN] Yet one step forward split the deep gash in her sole wide open, blood soaking through the shoe as she winced sideways. Chen Haoyu caught her at once. “Foot’s torn up—climb on my back, I’ll carry you down!”
[ZH] “朱淑真，赶紧醒来，别趴在陈浩宇的背上睡个没完没了，我们还等着回城。”
[EN] Glancing back in worry, Shen Jianchen saw Qianli Xun already resting atop Chen Haoyu’s back—the intimate sight piercing him with quiet envy. Looking away as he led the trail down, he asked, “Won’t carrying her down a mountain wear you out, Young Master Chen?”
[ZH] 千里寻睁开眼，收回手臂，从陈浩宇的背上溜下来，右脚不敢踩地，用左脚撑着：“陈浩宇，扶着我上马车。”
[EN] “Carrying my own wife? Light as a feather!”
[ZH] 陈浩宇一把抱起娘子：“我抱你上车。”
[EN] “Wait up here—I’ll send soldiers up with a stretcher.”
[ZH] 千里寻并未反抗，乖乖地享受着被陈浩宇抱在怀里的感觉。
[EN] “No need! No need!”
[ZH] 林觉一脸不屑：“哼！丢尽男人的脸面！”
[EN] Walking ahead without looking back, Shen Jianchen still caught every warm murmur behind him—“Put me down if you’re tired.” “Never—need to get that bleeding foot doctored fast!”—until Qianli Xun looped her arms snug around Chen Haoyu’s neck and drifted straight off to sleep in pure relief.
[ZH] 申简辰在心里默默告诉自己：“只要她幸福便好。”
[EN] Halfway down, Chen Haoyu’s boot slipped on loose gravel; Shen Jianchen whipped back and caught him steady before either fell. “Watch your footing—trail’s slick.” Catching sight of Qianli Xun’s peaceful sleeping face, Shen Jianchen lingered a fraction too long until Chen Haoyu coughed pointedly—“Folks below are waiting, Your Highness”—and Shen Jianchen turned red-faced back down the trail, ears tuned to every step behind him.
[ZH] 林觉与其他士兵也都跳上马，一行人往城内而去。
[EN] At the trailhead under a high sun, Lin Jue waited with horses and a carriage. “I’ll ride,” Shen Jianchen said, mounting up. “Give them the carriage.”
[ZH] 马车里，陈浩宇坐在娘子对面，将她的脚搭在自己的腿上，轻轻为她脱下鞋子，看着她脚底的伤口心疼不已。
[EN] Walking over, Lin Jue jeered as Chen Haoyu murmured gently trying not to wake his wife, “Call yourself a man? Watch a pro!” Leaning right by her ear, Lin Jue roared, “Zhu Shuzhen! Wake up or I start swinging!”
[ZH] 他从身上掏出一块干净的巾帕，在娘子脚上绑好，继续把鞋子穿好。
[EN] Half asleep, reflex fired—Qianli Xun swung a groggy fist muttering, “Dare hit me? I’ll beat you flat!” Lin Jue blocked her half-strength swing grinning, and she blinked awake, sliding down on her good foot before Chen Haoyu scooped her clean into his arms and carried her aboard while Lin Jue sneered “Pathetic!” and Shen Jianchen told himself silently, *Long as she’s happy.*
[ZH] 千里寻不好意思地抽回脚：“家里可好？”
[EN] Inside the rolling carriage, Chen Haoyu rested her injured foot on his knee, slipped the shoe off, bound the cut tenderly with a clean handkerchief, and eased the shoe back on.
[ZH] “仪王及时赶到，家里人都没事，只少你一人。黑煞虎来陈家明显是为钱财，肯定是见娘子美貌才把你掳到天目山上。”
[EN] Pulling her foot back shyly, she asked, “Everyone home safe?”
[ZH] 千里寻不愿将梦容想要杀害她的事告诉陈浩宇，更不愿家人替她担忧。
[EN] “Prince Yi got there in time—nobody hurt, only looted. Black-Fiend Tiger came for silver and snatched you for your looks.”
[ZH] “如此看来女子长得美不见得是好事。”
[EN] Spared her family knowing Mengrong’s hand in it, Qianli Xun sighed. “Guess being pretty isn’t always a blessing.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇并不认同：“我觉得甚好。”
[EN] “Best blessing there is!”
[ZH] 千里寻故意批评：“你啊，就是个好色之徒，只喜欢美女。现在你对我好，是因为我年轻貌美，等有一日我年老色衰，你肯定对我置之不理。”
[EN] “Typical lecher—only sweet on me while I’m young and pretty! Wait till I’m old and wrinkled, you’ll ditch me.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇立刻否认：“怎么会？娘子年老色衰时，我也年老色衰，我们正好凑成一对儿。”
[EN] “When you’re old and wrinkled, I’ll be old and wrinkled right beside you—perfect match!”
[ZH] “可我比你大，肯定比你老得快。”
[EN] “I’m older—I’ll wrinkle first.”
[ZH] “才大三岁而已。”
[EN] “Three tiny years!”
[ZH] 千里寻不得不承认她开始担心陈浩宇会喜欢上别人。
[EN] Deep down, Qianli Xun realized a brand-new truth: she was actually starting to worry about Chen Haoyu ever looking at someone else.

#CH 306
[ZH] 回到陈家，陈浩宇亲自为娘子的脚掌上完药后，夏老太太和姚轻琰带着众位家眷来到墨香苑。
[ZH] 千里寻脚上有伤，依旧下床站起来迎接。
[ZH] 姚轻琰上前扶住她：“听说你脚上有伤，赶快坐下。”
[ZH] 千里寻坐下后，陈浩宇帮着将她的腿脚放到床上。
[ZH] 苦荞搬来椅子，丫鬟搀扶着老太太坐下，又搬来几把椅子，众人都坐下。
[ZH] 夏老太太关切地问：“脚上的伤可还疼？”
[ZH] “上完药后，好了许多。”
[ZH] 夏老太太对站在床边的孙子说：“这几日你别去绸缎庄，好好在家陪淑真，你爹那里我去说。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇本也是这么想的：“都听奶奶的。”
[ZH] 夏老太太叹道：“陈家遭此劫难，人没事比什么都强。仪王以此为由剿了天目山上的匪徒，也算是陈家功德一件。你公爹说了，匪徒盗去的财物陈家一个字儿都不收回，全部捐出去，保个平安。钱财乃身外之物，没了还可以赚，人要是没了就什么都没了，奶奶不图别的，只图一家人平平安安，和和美美。”
[ZH] 老太太说着流下几滴泪，身后的丫鬟递上丝帕。老太太接过，将眼泪擦干。
[ZH] 姚轻琰和两位婶婶，还有大嫂叶安雅都露出伤感的表情，毕竟家里发生了一桩祸事，心情不好再正常不过。
[ZH] 千里寻更加自责，因为她陈家才遭此劫难。解决这场劫难的人是仪王，梦容知晓，恐怕更加恨她，若往后再给陈家带来劫难，让她该如何自处？
[ZH] 陈浩宇见娘子与众人一样露出伤感的表情，开解道：“陈家经此劫难，毫发无损，说明福气还在后头，大家又何必伤感？”
[ZH] 包氏说：“谁说毫发无损？我最喜欢的翡翠手镯被盗贼顺走，还丢了好些值钱的物件，想起来都心疼。”
[ZH] 儿子开解大家，姚轻琰不能不给面子：“母亲刚都是说了，钱财乃身外之物，只要人在比什么都强，往后让二弟再给你买。”
[ZH] 夏老太太调整片刻，再次露出笑容：“我们来看淑真，却只顾自己伤心，是什么道理？都赶紧把情绪收起来，从今往后不准再愁眉苦脸，越是一副苦相越晦气，都给我笑起来。”
[ZH] 老太太发话，众人不敢不听，都露出笑脸。
[ZH] 从走进淑真的闺房，崔氏便注意到她身上的男装：“淑真，你怎么穿着男子的衣衫？”
[ZH] 身上的衣衫是仪王给的，回到家里着急换药，还未来得及换掉，当着这么多人的面肯定不能照实说。
[ZH] “我被掳去，没穿外衣，是陈浩宇把他的外衣脱下来给我穿的，这就换掉。”
[ZH] 苦荞上前替小姐将男子的外衣脱掉。
[ZH] 众人看向陈浩宇，见他身上明明穿着外衣，都露出不解的神情。
[ZH] 陈浩宇赶紧解释：“是我的衣衫，我身上这件是刚回来重新穿上的。”
[ZH] 大家不再关心衣衫之事，叮嘱淑真好好养伤，又聊一会儿，众人才离去。
[ZH] 叶安雅并未离开，她想留下来与淑真聊几句。
[ZH] “二弟，能不能让我单独陪淑真坐会儿？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇欣然答应：“你们聊，我去外面给娘子做盏茶。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇出去后，叶安雅才起身坐到床边，拉起淑真的手：“真羡慕你。”
[ZH] “我都伤成这样，大嫂还羡慕我？”
[ZH] “浩宇对你一心一意，难道不该羡慕？”
[ZH] 千里寻想起陈浩东偷情的场景，从心里替叶安雅感到不值，又不知该说些什么。
[ZH] 叶安雅问道：“陈家近期会有一桩大事，你可知道？”
[ZH] 千里寻并不知，摇了摇头：“什么大事？嫂嫂可否告诉我？”
[ZH] “产业继承人的事，本来明年才选，不知为何提前到今年，算算日子已不足一月。”
[ZH] 千里寻并不感兴趣：“我还以为是什么事？原来是这件事。对陈家来讲是大事，对我来讲小事一桩。”
[ZH] “你难道不希望浩宇当选？”
[ZH] “从未想过，再说陈浩宇也没那本事，陈家的希望都寄托在堂兄身上。”
[ZH] “依我看，陈浩东根本不配继承陈家产业。”
[ZH] 叶安雅的心根本不在陈浩东的身上，否则怎会如此说自己的夫君？
[ZH] “嫂嫂难道不希望自己的夫君继承陈家产业？”
[ZH] 叶安雅沉默片刻，坚定地说：“我只想离开他，可离开他实在太难。……，我已经想到一个好办法，到时既让他当不了陈家的产业继承人，也会让他乖乖放我自由。”
[ZH] 千里寻从叶安雅坚定的眼神中读出些许可怕的信息，紧紧抓住她的手：“大嫂千万不能做傻事。”
[ZH] “你放心，我做的不是傻事，而是好事，无论对我还是对你，抑或对浩宇、陈家都是好事。”
[ZH] 叶安雅放开手站起来，脸上已没有往日的阴郁之色，仿佛已身处她渴望的生活之中。她对弟妹笑了笑，带着丫鬟优雅地离开。
[ZH] 苦荞一直站在一旁，将刚才的话听得一清二楚，心中既疑惑又担心。她走到床边，服侍小姐靠枕头坐好。
[ZH] “小姐，刚才大少夫人说那些话是什么意思？”
[ZH] “既然不明白，就当没听过，将那些话烂到肚子里，在谁面前都不许提起。”
[ZH] “我记下了。”
[ZH] 千里寻刚盖好薄被，靠着软枕闭上眼睛，韦嬷嬷走进来。
[ZH] “刚才人多，不便与众人挤在一处看你，听丫鬟说主人们都已离开，我才过来。”
[ZH] 千里寻欠起身：“嬷嬷坐。”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷在床边坐下：“快卯时，寻遍整个墨香苑都找不到你，姑爷和我，还有下人们都快急疯了，尤其是姑爷。还好你安全回来，要不然姑爷死的心都有。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇正好用木盘端着两盏茶进来：“嬷嬷向着我说话，赏你一盏茶喝。”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷端过茶盏：“没想到我这辈子也能喝到姑爷做的茶，还这么精致。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇将木盘递到娘子面前：“娘子也来一盏。”
[ZH] 千里寻端起茶盏，细细品着。
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷盯着茶盏中的一带远山：“姑爷竟也有作画的天赋，真不简单。”
[ZH] 苦荞噘着嘴：“姑爷哪天也教我在茶汤中作画？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇一口回绝：“你学不会。”
[ZH] “还没教怎知我学不会？”
[ZH] “作画需天赋，你没这天赋。”
[ZH] “何以见得？”
[ZH] “从你挑茶叶，磨茶粉，用茶具和茶筅都能瞧得出来，你这丫头不讲究，不讲究的人根本掌握不了茶汤中作画的精髓。”
[ZH] 苦荞的嘴巴噘得更高：“姑爷就是怕我学会把你比下去，才不愿教我，你的小心思以为我不知？”
[ZH] 两人斗嘴间，千里寻和韦嬷嬷已将茶品完。
[ZH] 陈浩宇用茶盘接过空茶盏：“娘子跟韦嬷嬷继续聊，我回博雅斋休息一会儿。”
[EN] Back at Chen Manor, right after Chen Haoyu doctored his wife’s sole, Old Madam Xia and Yao Qingyan led the women of the house over to Moxiang Courtyard.
[ZH] 陈浩宇背着她下山，回来又是涂药，又是做茶的，肯定累了，千里寻关切地说：“你回屋好好睡会儿，不用操心我。”
[EN] Insisting on rising despite her cut foot, Qianli Xun was gently guided back onto the bed by Yao Qingyan while Chen Haoyu lifted her legs up and Kuqiao arranged chairs.
[ZH] “收到。”陈浩宇将茶盘放在苦荞手中，“拿去洗了。”
[EN] “Foot still hurting?” Old Madam Xia asked with concern.
[ZH] 苦荞依然噘着嘴，却也心甘情愿端着盘子尾随姑爷出去。
[EN] “Much better with salve on.”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷夸道：“你瞧瞧，姑爷人多好。”
[EN] Turning to her grandson, Old Madam Xia ordered, “Skip the silk shop a few days and stay right here tending Shuzhen—I’ll square it with your father.”
[ZH] “嬷嬷喝他盏茶，就对他赞不绝口，我天天喝他做的茶岂不是要把他夸到天上去？”
[EN] “Gladly, Grandmother.”
[ZH] “连丫鬟都敢跟姑爷顶嘴，说明什么？”
[EN] Old Madam Xia dabbed away tears with a silk handkerchief. “Thank goodness not a single soul in our house was lost—and Prince Yi wiping out Mount Tianmu counts as good karma for the Chens! Father-in-law already decided to donate every copper of the recovered loot for blessings and peace. Silver comes and goes; people are everything.”
[ZH] “说明他不招人待见。”
[EN] Gloom hung over the room, sharper in Qianli Xun’s own chest—knowing she had drawn Mengrong’s blade straight onto Chen Manor.
[ZH] “错！说明姑爷人好，对待下人没有架子。”
[EN] Breaking the heaviness, Chen Haoyu piped up, “Coming through untouched means good fortune’s waiting—why mope?”
[ZH] 千里寻嘴上不承认，心里早已认同韦嬷嬷的话：“嬷嬷觉得他好，是不是也认为他可以成为陈家产业的继承人？”
[EN] Madam Bao groused, “Untouched?! They swiped my favorite jade bangle and half my jewels!”
[ZH] “这个‘也’字用得奇怪，难道有人这么认为过？”
[EN] Yao Qingyan smoothed it over—“Second Brother will buy you better ones”—before Old Madam Xia clapped her hands briskly. “We came to cheer Shuzhen, not pull long faces! Smiles, everybody!”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷通过一个字瞧出端倪，实在厉害。千里寻赶紧掩饰：“嬷嬷别瞎猜，是我这么认为而已。”
[EN] Then Madam Cui noticed the garment draped over Shuzhen’s shoulders. “Why are you wearing a man’s outer robe, Shuzhen?”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷了解自家姑娘，从来没把心思用在功名利禄上，又怎会在乎陈家产业继承人之事？
[EN] Still wearing Prince Yi’s robe from the scramble home! Qianli Xun didn’t miss a beat. “Got snatched without a coat, so Chen Haoyu gave me his—taking it off now.”
[ZH] 姑爷向来也从未往这方面想过，定是有人在姑娘面前提起过，姑娘才会问她的意见。
[EN] As Kuqiao slipped it off and eyebrows rose seeing Haoyu already wearing an outer coat, Chen Haoyu backed her smooth: “Swapped into a fresh one right when we got back!”
[ZH] “姑爷若没惹下那些乱七八糟的事，早些像现在这般，再将陈家的生意经学会，产业继承人指定是他；可现在我看悬，除非发生能扭转乾坤的事儿，重任落在姑爷身上也是有可能的，不过这扭转乾坤的事儿还是别指望。”
[EN] Satisfied, everyone urged her to rest and filed out—all except Ye Anya.
[ZH] 千里寻想到刚才叶安雅说的话，难道叶安雅想做出扭转乾坤之事？千里寻想不通凭叶安雅瘦弱的身板怎么能做出扭转乾坤之事？
[EN] “Mind giving Shuzhen and me a private minute, Second Brother?”
[ZH] “姑娘在想什么这么入神？”
[EN] “Sure—I’ll go whisk tea outside.”
[ZH] “没……没想什么。”
[EN] Once Chen Haoyu stepped out, Ye Anya sat on the bed’s edge and clasped Shuzhen’s hand. “I envy you so much.”
[ZH] “姑娘脚上有伤，好生歇息，我就不在此打扰。”
[EN] “Lying here bandaged and you envy me?”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷准备起身离开，被千里寻一把抓住：“嬷嬷再陪我说会儿话，有件事情憋在心里很难受，想让嬷嬷给我出个主意。”
[EN] “Haoyu’s heart belongs to you alone—who wouldn’t envy that?”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷继续坐好：“什么事？”
[EN] Remembering Chen Haodong’s sleazy tryst, Qianli Xun felt an ache for her without knowing what to say.
[ZH] “仪王妃想杀我……”千里寻大胆说出，她发现韦嬷嬷的脸上露出讶异又担忧的表情，“陈家遭殃跟仪王妃有莫大关系，如果我没猜错是她找人买通黑煞虎来杀我。这次我幸运活下来，恐怕仪王妃还会找其他人，我死了无所谓，担心会连累陈家。”
[EN] “Know about the big Chen family event coming up?” Ye Anya asked.
[ZH] “是因仪王抢亲之事？”
[EN] Shaking her head—“What event?”
[ZH] 千里寻点点头：“从那以后，仪王妃像变了一个人，不再是我以前认识的佳和郡主。”
[EN] “The business heir selection—moved up from next year to less than a month away.”
[ZH] “仪王可知此事？”
[EN] “Big deal for the family, nothing to me.”
[ZH] “应该不知道。”
[EN] “Don’t you want Haoyu chosen?”
[ZH] “苦荞可知？”
[EN] “Never crossed my mind—besides, he’s not cut out for it. Everyone expects Eldest Cousin.”
[ZH] “她一直跟着我，从头到尾都清楚。”
[EN] “If you ask me, Chen Haodong doesn’t deserve it.”
[ZH] “这么大的事，这丫头怎么就不知道知会我一声？”
[EN] Qianli Xun blinked. “You don’t want your own husband to inherit?”
[ZH] “不能怪她，是我不让她告诉任何人？”
[EN] After a beat of silence, Ye Anya’s eyes turned steel. “All I want is out—only getting free of him is nearly impossible. Now I’ve got a plan that keeps him out of the inheritance and forces him to set me free.”
[ZH] “姑娘今日主动告诉我，是因为怕连累陈家？”
[EN] A chill prickled Qianli Xun’s spine; she gripped Ye Anya’s hand tight. “Don’t do anything reckless, Eldest Sister-in-law!”
[ZH] 千里寻老实承认：“是，若非因此，绝不会向嬷嬷提起。我实在没有办法，想听听嬷嬷的意见，我接下来该怎么办？是该离开陈家还是继续待下去？”
[EN] “Relax—nothing reckless. Something good for me, good for you, good for Haoyu, and good for the whole Chen family.” Rising with a calm, radiant smile that wiped away her usual shadow, Ye Anya glided out with Xiaocui.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷皱起眉头思量了一会儿，认真说：“姑娘已嫁到陈家，这里就是你的家，没有离开的道理。此事怪不得姑娘，要怪只能怪仪王妃心胸狭窄。抢亲之事已成过去，她已嫁给仪王，你也嫁人，大家应该放下过往，各自过好各自的日子。她却想出要人性命的法子，以前算是错看她。姑娘要是信得过我，今日我替你去幽林别居走一趟。”
[EN] Having overheard every word, Kuqiao whispered worriedly while fluffing her miss’s pillows, “What did Eldest Young Madam mean?”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷平日主意最多，面对此事，她也无法给出更好的建议。千里寻无奈地说：“恐怕嬷嬷去了也无用。”
[EN] “Forget you ever heard it—breathe not a word to a soul.”
[ZH] “不管有用没用都得去一趟。姑娘一定要打消离开陈家的念头，再说离开陈家你又能去哪里？”
[EN] Just as Qianli Xun shut her eyes, Matron Wei stepped in, sitting by the bed to fret over how frantic Young Master and the whole courtyard had been at dawn—right as Chen Haoyu strolled in with two cups of tea painted with distant mountain peaks!
[ZH] 真要离开陈家，千里寻定会万般不舍，她舍不得陈家的长辈，更舍不得改变后的陈浩宇。
[EN] Savoring her cup, Matron Wei marveled at his skill while Kuqiao pouted begging him to teach her foam-painting and Chen Haoyu teased she lacked the finesse—until both empty cups were collected and Haoyu headed back to Boya Study for a nap.
[ZH] “嬷嬷去幽林别居替我转告仪王妃，想杀我冲着我来，不要牵连陈家。”
[EN] “Look what a gem Young Master is,” Matron Wei praised. “Even maids dare sass him—proves he has zero arrogance!”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷站起来：“姑娘好生休息，我现在就去见仪王妃。”
[EN] “Think he’s good enough to inherit the Chen businesses too?” Qianli Xun tested.
[ZH] ……
[EN] Matron Wei caught the slip in a second. “‘Too’? Who else floated that?”
[ZH] 后半夜哥哥被叫走后，乔梦容再也睡不着，起来等消息。据她估计，朱淑真必死无疑。
[EN] Brushing it off awkward, Qianli Xun listened as Matron Wei weighed it realistic: “If he’d straightened out years ago and mastered trade, sure—now? Odds are slim unless some earth-shattering twist flips the board, and don’t hold your breath for miracles.”
[ZH] 天亮后哥哥一直没有回来，她难以得到消息，让喜兰差人到陈府打听。打听的人回来后说陈家遭贼人抢劫，丢失不少财物，陈家的二少奶奶也不见踪影。
[EN] *An earth-shattering twist—could fragile Eldest Sister-in-law pull that off?*
[ZH] 乔梦容很不满意，为何只是不见踪影？应该是人已被杀害才对。
[EN] As Matron Wei rose to leave, Qianli Xun caught her hand. “Stay a minute, Matron—something’s choking me and I need advice.” Taking a deep breath, she spilled the truth: “Princess Consort Yi wants me dead. She hired Black-Fiend Tiger—I got lucky today, yet she’ll come again. I don’t fear dying, only dragging Chen Manor down with me! Should I leave Chen Manor?”
[ZH] 午时，申简辰派人送来消息。喜兰在幽林别居的大门口听了消息后，亲口将情况告知。
[EN] Shocked yet steady, Matron Wei frowned. “Chen Manor is your home now—no leaving! You did nothing wrong; Princess Consort Yi’s narrow heart is to blame. Trust me—I’ll go to Youlin Retreat today and talk to her.”
[ZH] 乔梦容才知天目山上的匪寇被官府全部拿下，黑煞虎则被关押起来，而朱淑真还好好活着。
[EN] “Doubt it’ll do any good, Matron...”
[ZH] 乔梦容无比失落，自语道：“为何你总是这么幸运？我偏不信这个邪！走着瞧！”
[EN] “Worth a shot—and drop any thought of leaving! Tell me what message to carry.”
[ZH] 这时门口的下人来报：“仪王妃，有位自称韦嬷嬷的人前来拜见。”
[EN] “Tell her to aim straight at me and leave the Chen family out of it.”
[ZH] 再次失败，乔梦容意识到想要除掉朱淑真不是一件容易的事，既然韦嬷嬷前来，不如听她说些什么。
[EN] Over at Youlin Retreat, Qiao Mengrong had lain awake since midnight expecting news of Zhu Shuzhen’s corpse—only to hear at noon via Prince Yi’s messenger that Mount Tianmu had been wiped out, Black-Fiend Tiger sat in irons, and Zhu Shuzhen walked away alive!
[ZH] “喜兰，你去把邹嬷嬷找来，让她替我接待韦嬷嬷。”
[EN] Seething in disbelief, she heard a servant announce Matron Wei at the gate—and ordered Xilan to fetch Matron Zou to handle her instead.

#CH 307
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷在幽林别居下人的带领下来到仪王妃居住的院子，进屋后，本想着见到仪王妃要先行跪拜之礼，却没见到仪王妃的身影，只看到一位与自己年岁差不多的嬷嬷端坐在下位。
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷受仪王妃所托，接待女词人身边的嬷嬷。见韦嬷嬷走进，邹嬷嬷并未站起来迎接：“你就是幽栖居士身边的嬷嬷？”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷行礼道：“正是，今日特来拜见仪王妃。”
[ZH] “王妃身份尊贵，不是谁想见就能见。你有什么话尽管对我讲，再由我转达。”
[ZH] 对方显然是嫌弃她身份卑微，韦嬷嬷并不在意：“不知如何称呼？”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷瞧向一旁的丫鬟，丫鬟会意，介绍道：“这位是仪王府的邹嬷嬷，是王爷的奶娘。”
[ZH] 仪王府的嬷嬷自然比老百姓家中的嬷嬷身份尊贵，韦嬷嬷再次行礼：“拜见邹嬷嬷。”
[ZH] “看座！”
[ZH] 丫鬟把韦嬷嬷带到椅子旁，她毫不客气地坐下，开门见山抛出主题：“我来有两件事，一是转告仪王妃，我家姑娘已从天目山上安全归来，脚上受了伤，却无大碍……”
[ZH] 听到确切的结果，邹嬷嬷下意识朝屏风看了一眼。
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷有所察觉，猜测仪王妃应该坐在屏风后面，她继续大胆讲道：“姑娘遇到这么大的事都没丢掉性命，足以说明阎王还不想收她，既如此又何必做徒劳之事？”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷假装不明白：“你一开口说这么多，都把我搞糊涂，幽栖居士命大是她的事，跟仪王妃有何关系？”
[ZH] “邹嬷嬷何必装？仪王妃能让你接见我，自然是因你对仪王妃和我家姑娘的事都清楚。姑娘今日才告诉我仪王妃想让她死，我听后甚是震惊，因怜惜姑娘，才来幽林别居拜见贵人。”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷不再假装：“第一件我已记下，不知第二件又是什么？”
[ZH] “我一直跟着姑娘，她的事最清楚不过。无论何时，她与仪王相处从未逾越规矩；成亲后，更是将心思放在姑爷身上，对仪王妃绝无任何影响。仪王我也是见过的，绝不是那种见异思迁之辈，既成亲，必然会一心一意对待王妃。仪王妃一时忘不了抢亲之痛，才会将矛头指向我家姑娘，若用平常心看待，明白生而为人孰能无过的道理，便不会揪着过往不放。过去的就让它过去吧，一切要向前看，日子才能过得更好。”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷一番言论，邹嬷嬷从心底认同，碍于仪王妃，她不好表态，也带着劝解仪王妃的目的说道：“恐怕你并不清楚，仪王的心里还是放不下幽栖居士，只要听到与她有关的事，二话不说冲上去，令仪王妃情何以堪？今日之事不就是个很好的证明？”
[ZH] “仪王是临安节度使，又身兼司理参军一职，无论是王家、李家，还是张家出事，相信他都会挺身而出，只不过事情刚好发生在陈家。若要怪也怪不到我家姑娘的头上，怪只怪制造事端之人，硬生生地给仪王提供了一次救我家姑娘的机会，还要反过来嫌弃我家姑娘，又是何道理？”
[ZH] 屏风后，乔梦容心中思忖，难道她做错了？她想杀朱淑真却无形中给哥哥提供了保护她的机会，书卷之事如此，陈浩东制造的麻烦也是如此，找黑煞虎下山杀人更是如此。不知哥哥在天目山上见到朱淑真时两人又是如何相处？
[ZH] 乔梦容越想越觉得自己实在愚笨，可要放弃除掉朱淑真的想法，她依然心有不甘。
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷又向屏风处瞧一眼，继续说：“我来临安也有段时日，听过关于她的传言，说她生性风 流，长了一双狐媚的眼睛，最会勾男人的魂儿，仪王的魂儿就是在不知不觉中被她勾走，我要是仪王妃也不会轻易饶恕她。”
[ZH] “坊间传言岂可信以为真？邹嬷嬷若见过我家姑娘，就不会相信传言。”
[ZH] “我是见过她，依然难以判断。”
[ZH] “那是因为邹嬷嬷没有与我家姑娘相处过，不了解她的为人，若了解便不会如此想。”
[ZH] 乔梦容忍不住从屏风后面出来：“我与她相处过，曾经还是闺中密友，又能如何？还不是到最后才认清她的真面目？”
[ZH] 贵人出现，两位嬷嬷都站起来。
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷跪下行礼：“拜见仪王妃。”
[ZH] “起来吧。”乔梦容坐到正位处，面对已起身的韦嬷嬷，一脸冷漠，“你刚才的一番话有些蛮有道理，我自会采纳，可别妄想我会放过朱淑真，麻烦你转告她，她不可能永远如此幸运。”
[ZH] 两位嬷嬷同时露出失落的表情，看来仪王妃还是要在错误的道路上继续走下去。
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷话已说尽，多说无益：“我家姑娘让我转告仪王妃，一切冲着她，不要牵连无辜之人。”
[ZH] “哼，我当然不愿牵连他人，只可惜她已嫁到陈家，若不小心牵连到他人，也是无可奈何之事，回去告诉她，我尽量。”
[ZH] 仪王妃冷漠的语气与以前是佳和郡主时判若两人，韦嬷嬷心寒的同时替自家姑娘担忧不已，她决定回去后不将实情告知，省得姑娘因担忧陈家想着离开，好不容易经营的婚姻不能就这么泡汤。
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷回到陈府的墨香苑，主动走进素心草堂，她已想好说辞，为的是安姑娘之心。
[ZH] 姑娘脚上有伤，并未坐在书桌旁。韦嬷嬷直接走进里屋，见姑爷正坐在一起床边陪姑娘说话，两人有说有笑，画面甚是和谐。
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷更觉得不能照实说，还需哄骗一番。
[ZH] “姑爷也在。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇依旧坐着，没有要走的意思：“嬷嬷来了。”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷从幽林别居回来，有事要说，而此事千里寻不愿让陈浩宇知晓：“你去忙吧，韦嬷嬷有事要与我说。”
[ZH] “有什么事还得避开我？”
[ZH] “朱家的事，你听着也无趣。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇站起来：“岳父大人最好面子，既然是朱家的秘密我就不听了，省得他知道后面子上挂不住。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇很不舍地放开娘子的手，把位置让给韦嬷嬷。
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷估摸着姑爷已走出门，才靠到床边坐下。
[ZH] 千里寻急切地问：“如何？”
[ZH] “比预想得好。”
[ZH] “难道她不再想着除掉我？”
[ZH] “仪王妃说往后你过你的日子，她过她的，只要你别再跟仪王有任何瓜葛，她便既往不咎。”
[ZH] 千里寻甚觉怀疑：“她真的这么说？”
[ZH] “那还能有假？”
[ZH] “还是嬷嬷厉害，能说到她的心里，我以前苦口婆心说了那么多她都听不进去。”
[ZH] “仪王妃虽答应不再做伤天害理之事，可你们的关系不可能再恢复到从前，往后避开她，凡是有仪王和仪王妃的地方你都少去，哪怕是诗会和宴会也尽量少去。”
[EN] Guided into Princess Consort Yi’s courtyard, Matron Wei found no royal waiting—only a matron near her own age seated coolly below the head chair.
[ZH] “我也是这么想的。”
[EN] “Youqi Jushi’s matron?” Matron Zou didn’t rise. “Her Highness doesn’t see just anyone. State your business to me.”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷一脸真诚，千里寻逐渐打消疑虑。
