You are His Majesty’s own son—how can you live in a dump like this? If that isn’t smearing soot across His Majesty’s face, what is?”
[ZH] 申简辰依旧不说一句话，平静的面色下压着一腔怒气，要不是看在历史迟早会收拾昏君和奸臣，他绝对会将梁师成一拳打成肉饼。
[EN] Still Shen Jianchen kept silent, locking a surge of fury behind an unruffled mask. Were it not for knowing history would settle scores with this foolish emperor and his crooked lackey sooner or later, he would have pounded Liang Shicheng into mincemeat right there.
[ZH] “我按照官家的意思，已选好新的司理参军，明日前来上任，还请仪王让出位置，回去只当个王爷，就当您还病着，什么都别理会。”
[EN] “Following His Majesty’s wishes, I’ve already chosen a new Judicial Investigator to take office tomorrow. Please vacate the post, return home, and just be a prince—pretend you’re still sick and stay out of everything.”
[ZH] 梁师成终于站起来，行礼道：“言尽于此，怎么做，殿下应该心知肚明。我一路颠簸而来，甚是辛苦，回别馆休息。仪王若有事，可差人到城北的柳岸别馆找我，告辞。”
[EN] Liang Shicheng rose at last and offered a bow. “I’ve said all that needs saying; Your Highness knows very well what to do. The road down here wore me out, so I’ll head back to my lodge to rest. Should Your Highness need anything, send word to Liu’an Villa in the north of the city. Farewell.”
[ZH] 申简辰并未理会。
[EN] Shen Jianchen offered no reply.
[ZH] 梁师成走后，他又默默坐了一会儿，才起身走出司理院，连向韩谷和温信道别的心情都没有，步行离开。
[EN] After Liang Shicheng left, he sat alone a little longer before standing and walking out of the Judicial Office. Too drained inside even to bid goodbye to Han Gu or Wen Xin, he left on foot.
[ZH] 他在街上一个人走了许久，想着喧嚣的街市会让自己心情好一些，可街市的热闹更令他觉出内心的落寞。
[EN] He wandered the streets alone for a long time, hoping the bustle of the market would lift his spirits, yet the noisy crowd only deepened the hollow ache inside him.
[ZH] 本是为寻找千里寻来到临安，人找着，却得不到，便想着不能白来一趟，不如做些有意义的事，既可打发时间，亦可不辜负穿越而来的身份。怎奈在一个颠倒是非的时代，想做些事难之又难。
[EN] He had come to Lin’an looking for Qianli Xun. Finding her yet being unable to have her, he had figured he might as well do some real good—both to pass the time and to make his transmigration count for something. Yet in an upside-down era where right meant wrong, trying to get anything done felt like pushing uphill through mud.
[ZH] 他忽然觉得自己很失败，没有得到想要的人，还被官家的口谕逼得什么事都做不成，难道今后只能当一个闲散的王爷？再跟梦容生一堆孩子？
[EN] All at once he felt like an utter failure. He hadn’t won the woman he wanted, and now His Majesty’s decree blocked him from doing anything useful. Was he really stuck playing a leisurely prince from here on out—and raising a houseful of children with Mengrong?
[ZH] 若身边的人是心中所爱，申简辰会觉得闲散的日子肯定是幸福的。可身边的人并不是他爱的人，什么都不做就这样闲散下去，日子会过得毫无乐趣。
[EN] If the person beside him were the woman he loved, a quiet, carefree life would have been bliss. With someone he didn’t love, drifting through empty days with nothing to do sounded lifeless beyond words.
[ZH] 不知走了多久，听到文渊的声音：“怎么一个人在街上闲逛？”
[EN] He had no idea how far he’d walked when Wenyuan’s voice called out, “Why are you strolling down the street all by yourself?”
[ZH] 申简辰站住，顺着声音瞧去，文渊坐在马车里，探出个脑袋。
[EN] Shen Jianchen halted and looked toward the voice. Wenyuan was leaning his head out of a carriage window.
[ZH] “你一个人？”
[EN] “Are you alone?”
[ZH] “一个人。”
[EN] “Alone.”
[ZH] “能不能陪我喝两杯？”
[EN] “Care to join me for a couple of drinks?”
[ZH] “有什么不可以的？”乔文仙走下马车，“不远处有家酒肆，我们去那里坐坐。”
[EN] “Why not?” Qiao Wenxian climbed down from the carriage. “There’s a wine shop just ahead. Let’s sit inside.”
[ZH] 两人来到酒肆，选择了一处靠窗户的位置，要了壶皇都春，各自满上。
[EN] They picked a table by the window, ordered a pot of Capital Spring, and poured each other full cups.
[ZH] 乔文仙问：“听说知府大人换人了，可是真的？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian asked, “I heard the Prefect was replaced. Is it true?”
[ZH] “没错，都是因为我。”申简辰落寞地端起酒杯一饮而尽。
[EN] “It is—and all because of me.” Shen Jianchen picked up his cup gloomily and drained it.
[ZH] “怎么能怪你？我倒觉得自从你来后，临安的官府才变得无比正常。”
[EN] “How could anyone blame you? If you ask me, Lin’an’s yamen only started functioning properly after you stepped in.”
[ZH] 申简辰苦笑一下：“一个不正常的时代想做些正常的事太难，从今往后，我什么都不做，只做一个闲散的王爷，回到刚来临安时的状态。只是我的心态已不同，那时还有希望，现在连希望都没有。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen gave a wry smile. “Trying to do normal, decent work in an abnormal age is near impossible. From now on, I’m done trying. I’ll just be a leisurely prince, right back where I started when I first reached Lin’an—except my state of mind is different now. Back then, I still had hope. Now there’s none left.”
[ZH] 乔文仙安慰道：“怎么没有？有仪王妃陪着，你们再有个孩子，孩子就是希望。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian tried to comfort him. “How can you say you have none? You have Princess Consort Yi at your side. Once you have a child together, that child becomes your hope.”
[ZH] 提到孩子，申简辰想起乔文仙快要当爹的事：“听说你的一妻一妾已有身孕，不久要当爹，心情如何？”
[EN] At the mention of children, Shen Jianchen remembered Qiao Wenxian was about to become a father himself. “I heard both your wife and your concubine are expecting. You’ll be a father soon—how does that feel?”
[ZH] “心情自然与以往不同，充满期待。”
[EN] “Different from before, of course. Full of anticipation.”
[ZH] 申简辰瞧着文渊脸上的笑容，想起他当初爱得死去活来的样子，恍如隔世：“真羡慕你，娶了不爱的人，还能如此享受。”
[EN] Looking at the easy smile on Wenyuan’s face, Shen Jianchen thought back to how lovesick the man had once been—it felt like another lifetime. “I envy you. You married someone you didn’t love and still manage to enjoy life like this.”
[ZH] 乔文仙饮完一杯酒，给仪王满上：“人生哪有圆满？想通了自然会接受老天送到身边的人和物，若想不通，只能是自己折磨自己。你要像我一样，既然无法拒绝，接受了，抱着享受生活的姿态，人不要总是自己为难自己。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian drained a cup and refilled the prince’s. “Who ever gets everything they want in life? Once you make peace with it, you accept whatever Heaven puts in your hands. Refuse to make peace, and you only torture yourself. Be like me: if you can’t refuse what’s dealt to you, take it and choose to enjoy what you have. Never make yourself your own worst enemy.”
[ZH] “说得有道理。”申简辰端起酒杯，“往后我既不是节度使，也不再去司理院，空闲时间很多，一定要多来幽林别居坐坐，陪我说说话，最好把三位公子叫上。”
[EN] “Fair enough.” Shen Jianchen lifted his cup. “Now that I’m neither Military Commissioner nor going back to the Judicial Office, I’ll have time to burn. Make sure you drop by Secluded Forest Villa often to keep me company—and bring the other three young masters along.”
[ZH] 两人干了一杯，乔文仙说：“你既变得闲散，我自然会时常去打扰，只是三位公子已变成两位，往日景况已不复。”
[EN] They clinked and emptied their cups. Qiao Wenxian sighed, “Since you’re at leisure again, of course I’ll come bother you. Only those three young masters are down to two now—things aren’t what they used to be.”
[ZH] 申简辰想起曹班与心爱之人离开临安：“我竟然忘了曹公子的事。还别说，多少羡慕他，敢于跨出你我不敢跨出的一步，带着心爱的人远走他乡。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen recalled Cao Ban eloping with the woman he loved. “I’d almost forgotten about Young Master Cao. You know, I envy him a little—having the guts to take the step neither you nor I dared take, running off to start over with the woman he loves.”
[ZH] “仪王不知，他之所以那么大胆，无非是家中兄弟多，不用担心无人照料父母。我如果也像他一样有好几个兄弟，当初肯定比他勇敢。”
[EN] “You don’t know the half of it, Your Highness. He only dared pull something that bold because he has plenty of brothers at home to look after his parents. If I had brothers like him, I’d have been braver than he was back then.”
[ZH] 申简辰想到自己，那时的他是想带着千里寻离开，她却不同意，坚决步入婚姻：“我更佩服叶家姑娘，不仅逼得陈浩东休了她，还放下所有，毅然决然跟着曹公子离开。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen thought of his own past attempt—he had tried to sweep Qianli Xun away too, yet she had turned him down flat and chosen marriage. “I admire Miss Ye even more. Not only did she push Chen Haodong into divorcing her, she walked away from everything and followed Young Master Cao without looking back.”
[ZH] “的确值得人佩服，可也付出代价。为了几个妹妹的名声，她与叶家断绝关系。即便如此，还是影响到妹妹们，听说本来上门提亲的人不少，自从发生这件事，几乎没有人上门提亲。所以做任何选择有好有坏，就看选择的人是怎么想的，是想顾全大局还是只顾及个人幸福。”
[EN] “Admirable, yes—though she paid a price for it. To shield her younger sisters’ reputations, she severed all ties with the Ye family. Even so, it hurt her sisters’ prospects; plenty of matchmakers used to call at the Ye gate, and since that happened, almost nobody comes proposing. Every choice cuts both ways—it all depends on whether someone thinks about the bigger picture or only personal happiness.”
[ZH] 乔文仙说着，脸上露出落寞的神色：“她选择顾全大局，逼得我不得不放弃个人幸福。她现在跟陈浩宇在一起看上去蛮好，我在外人眼里也生活圆满，证明她当初的选择没有错。”
[EN] A shadow of sadness crossed Qiao Wenxian’s face as he spoke. “She chose the bigger picture, which forced me to let go of my own happiness. She seems content enough with Chen Haoyu now, and to outsiders my own household looks complete. I suppose that proves her choice wasn’t wrong.”
[ZH] 申简辰想到自己，人生的选择往往也存在着许多无奈，有时不必选，老天已替人选好，就像架在他头上的圣旨。
[EN] Shen Jianchen thought of himself. Often enough, a person didn’t even get to make the choice—Heaven shoved it onto their lap, just like an imperial edict hanging over his neck.
[ZH] 乔文仙举起重新添满的酒杯：“什么都别想，既已踏上选择的道路，勇敢走下去，哪怕是一条错误的道路，也得走下去，因为对你我而言没有回头路。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian raised his freshly poured cup. “Stop overthinking it. Once you’ve stepped onto a road, walk it through with your chin up—even if it turns out to be the wrong road, you still have to keep walking, because for men like you and me, there’s no turning back.”
[ZH] 两人聊了许久，喝了整整三壶皇都春，文渊已醉得不省人事，申简辰才稍微有些酒意。
[EN] They talked on and on, polishing off three whole pots of Capital Spring. By the time they parted, Wenyuan was dead drunk while Shen Jianchen felt only a mild buzz.
[ZH] 二人分别后，申简辰步行出了城，来到神柳下。因一直在司理院忙，他好久没有来看望神柳。
[EN] After splitting up, Shen Jianchen strolled out through the city gate toward the Divine Willow. Tied up at the Judicial Office, he hadn’t come to visit the tree in ages.
[ZH] 神柳上的红绸已被全部取下，它看上去轻松自在。已入秋，神柳的叶子正在枯萎。一阵风吹来，散落下许多枯萎的柳叶。
[EN] Every red silk ribbon had been stripped from its branches, leaving the willow looking light and unburdened. Autumn had arrived, and its leaves were withering; a gust swept past, shaking loose a shower of dry foliage.
[ZH] 秋风拂面，几片神柳的叶子从申简辰的面颊摩擦而过，落在地上即将干枯的草丛中。
[EN] The crisp breeze brushed Shen Jianchen’s cheeks with drifting willow leaves that settled into the yellowing grass below.
[ZH] 已过中秋，月亮没有那么圆满，一轮半月慢慢爬上高空，缓缓地想要穿过神柳的枝头。
[EN] Mid-Autumn had come and gone, so the moon hung half-full, climbing high into the night sky as if inching slowly through the willow’s boughs.
[ZH] 来到神柳下，申简辰更加确定对千里寻的爱从未减退过，因得不到反而更加浓烈。
[EN] Standing beneath the Divine Willow, Shen Jianchen knew with absolute certainty that his love for Qianli Xun had never faded—being denied her only made it burn fiercer.
[ZH] 带着这样的心情不好回幽林别居见梦容，若流露太多，会让她的心情变差，再做出不理智的行为来，受伤的只能是他心爱之人。
[EN] Carrying a heart like this, he had no business facing Mengrong back at Secluded Forest Villa. If too much showed on his face, her mood would sour, she might do something reckless again, and the one who’d end up bleeding would be the woman he loved.
[ZH] 申简辰决定在神柳下多坐一会儿，既可散去酒气，亦可整理心情。
[EN] Shen Jianchen decided to linger under the willow a little longer—both to let the wine wear off and to pull himself together.
[ZH] 他走到石头上坐下，背靠在神柳粗壮的树干上，想着个人的心事，醉意渐渐袭来，他竟然不知不觉睡去……
[EN] Walking over to a boulder, he sat down and leaned his back against the willow’s thick trunk. Lost in his own thoughts, drowsiness crept over him, and without meaning to, he drifted off to sleep.

#CH 314
[ZH] 一阵秋风吹来，申简辰在梦中感受到一股寒意。他睁开眼睛，月亮已穿过神柳的枝头。
[ZH] 该是回去的时候，要不然梦容会担心。
[ZH] 申简辰从冰凉的石头上坐起来，再瞧一眼月光下的神柳，才穿过池塘，向密林走去。
[EN] A gust of autumn wind swept over him, sending a chill straight into Shen Jianchen’s dream. Opening his eyes, he saw the moon had already cleared the branches of the Divine Willow.
[ZH] 迟迟不见哥哥回来，乔梦容甚是焦心。
[EN] Time to head back, or Mengrong would fret.
[ZH] 天还亮着时，白风一个人回来。
[EN] He pushed himself up off the cold boulder, cast one last look at the willow bathed in moonlight, and walked past the pond into the dense woods.
[ZH] 乔梦容问他：“为何一个人回来？哥哥呢？”
[EN] With her brother still missing so late, Qiao Mengrong had grown frantic with worry.
[ZH] 白风说：“王爷不在司理院，我以为他回幽林别居。”
[EN] Earlier, before dusk, Bai Feng had returned all by himself.
[ZH] 乔梦容对白风越来越不满，好长一段时间不见他，见着后向他打听那些日子做些什么，他只说王爷安排了重要的事情，便什么都不说。今日还不顾哥哥，一个人跑出去玩儿。
[EN] “Why are you back alone?” Qiao Mengrong had demanded. “Where is my brother?”
[ZH] “你既是哥哥的近身侍卫，就该随身跟着，却一个人跑出去，以后若再这样，我就让哥哥辞了你，重新找个侍卫。”
[EN] “His Highness wasn’t at the Judicial Office, so I figured he’d come back to Secluded Forest Villa.”
[ZH] 白风第一次见仪王妃生气，赶紧说：“我去神柳下找找。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong’s irritation with Bai Feng had been mounting. After vanishing for a stretch and refusing to say what he’d been doing beyond “important business for His Highness,” now he’d gone off playing around and left her brother unattended.
[ZH] 白风去神柳下时，天还没黑，仪王还未来此，他失望而归，免不了被仪王妃数落一顿。为了赎罪，他跑到大门口，坐在石墩上等王爷回来。
[EN] “You’re his personal bodyguard—you’re supposed to stay right by his side, not wander off on your own! Pull this again and I’ll have my brother dismiss you and hire someone else!”
[ZH] 等了许久，终于看到王爷的身影，显然是从密林里出来。
[EN] Never having seen Princess Consort Yi flare up like that, Bai Feng hurried to say, “I’ll go check under the Divine Willow.”
[ZH] 白风迎上去：“王爷，您是不是跑去看神柳？”
[EN] When Bai Feng went to the willow, daylight hadn’t faded and Prince Yi wasn’t there yet. Returning empty-handed earned him a sharp scolding. To make up for it, he planted himself on a stone stool right outside the main gate to wait.
[ZH] 申简辰“嗯”了一声，继续往回走。
[EN] After a long stretch, he spotted Prince Yi emerging from the woods.
[ZH] 白风紧跟上：“奇怪，我去过神柳下找您，根本没见着。”
[EN] Bai Feng scrambled to meet him. “Your Highness! Did you go to see the Divine Willow?”
[ZH] 没等王爷回答，白风已找到答案：“肯定是我去找时，您还没到神柳下。我真笨，应该再去找一回。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen let out a low grunt and kept walking.
[ZH] 想到仪王妃，白风继续说：“王爷安全回来，仪王妃总算可以安心。”
[EN] Bai Feng trotted at his heels. “Weird—I checked under the willow for you and didn’t see a soul!” Before the prince answered, Bai Feng supplied his own explanation. “You must not have reached the willow yet when I looked. Silly me—I should’ve gone back for a second look.” Remembering the princess consort, he added, “At least Princess Consort Yi can stop worrying now that you’re back safe.”
[ZH] 申简辰任凭白风自语，一句都懒得搭理。他回到屋中，梦容衣着整齐，显然是一直等着他回来。
[EN] Shen Jianchen let him chatter on without replying to a word. Reaching his quarters, he found Mengrong still fully dressed, having waited up for him.
[ZH] 乔梦容见哥哥出现，冲上前抱住他：“哥哥去了哪里？害我担心许久。”
[EN] The instant he stepped inside, Qiao Mengrong threw her arms around him. “Where did you go, Brother? You scared me half to death!”
[ZH] 申简辰照实说：“我跟文渊在酒肆多饮了几杯，又在神柳下坐了一会儿，才回来晚些。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen answered honestly, “I had a few too many drinks with Wenyuan at a wine shop, then sat under the Divine Willow for a bit, which made me late.”
[ZH] 乔梦容终于放心，只要哥哥不是跟朱淑真相会便好，她放开哥哥：“司理院忙，哥哥为何有时间与乔公子饮酒？还是在酒肆？”
[EN] Relief washed over Qiao Mengrong—as long as he hadn’t been sneaking off to meet Zhu Shuzhen, she could breathe easy. Letting go of him, she asked, “Isn’t the Judicial Office keeping you busy? Why did you have time to drink with Young Master Qiao—and in a tavern at that?”
[ZH] 申简辰尽量让自己脸上的笑容显得轻松些：“从今往后我不再是临安节度使，也不会去司理院，有大把的时间陪你，是不是很开心？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen forced an easy smile onto his face. “From today on, I’m no longer Military Commissioner of Lin’an, and I won’t be going back to the Judicial Office either. Now I’ll have all the time in the world to spend with you. Doesn’t that make you happy?”
[ZH] 乔梦容并未表现出开心的样子，而是面露疑惑：“发生了什么？”
[EN] Instead of looking pleased, Qiao Mengrong frowned in puzzlement. “What happened?”
[ZH] 申简辰走向一边，背对着梦容：“朱晞颜被一位名唤李克的人替代，已不是临安知府；官家让我只当个闲散的王爷，我也乐得逍遥自在。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen stepped aside, turning his back to her. “Zhu Xiyan’s been replaced as Prefect of Lin’an by someone named Li Ke. His Majesty wants me to stay a leisurely prince, and honestly, I’m glad to be free of it.”
[ZH] 乔梦容已顾不上哥哥的职位：“官家派谁来临安处理此事？”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong cared nothing for her brother’s lost title. “Who did His Majesty send to Lin’an to handle this?”
[ZH] “还能有谁？近身伺候官家之人，梁师成。”
[EN] “Who else? His Majesty’s personal favorite—Liang Shicheng.”
[ZH] 申简辰若转过身，他定能注意到梦容脸上露出意外的笑容；可他没有，根本无法领会此时乔梦容内心的盘算。
[EN] Had Shen Jianchen turned around, he would have caught an unexpected smile curve across Mengrong’s lips. Since he didn’t, he had no clue what wheels were turning inside her head.
[ZH] “梁内侍会不会在临安待段时日？”
[EN] “Will Imperial Attendant Liang be staying in Lin’an for a while?”
[ZH] “官家派他前来扭转临安府衙的风气，必然会待段时日。”
[EN] “His Majesty sent him to set Lin’an’s prefecture office back in line, so yes, he’ll be here a stretch.”
[ZH] “哥哥可知他住在哪里？”
[EN] “Do you happen to know where he’s staying, Brother?”
[ZH] 申简辰这才转过身：“为何打听他的住处？”
[EN] That made Shen Jianchen turn around. “Why ask where he lives?”
[ZH] 乔梦容掩饰道：“不过是为哥哥，想上门拜访他，再给他塞些银子让他多在官家面前替哥哥美言，说不定还会恢复临安节度使一职。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong covered fast. “Just for your sake, Brother. I thought I might pay him a visit, slip him some silver, and get him to put in good words with His Majesty—maybe restore your post as Military Commissioner of Lin’an.”
[ZH] 申简辰只觉梦容实在单纯，哪里懂得官场的复杂和当今官家的昏庸：“不必再想着巴结他，我现在只想当一个闲散的王爷。”
[EN] Thinking Mengrong far too naive to grasp official politics or His Majesty’s folly, he shook his head. “Don’t waste your time buttering him up. Right now I just want to live as a leisurely prince.”
[ZH] 没能从哥哥的口中获得梁内侍落脚之处，乔梦容决定明日派邹嬷嬷出去打探一番。
[EN] Unable to pry Liang’s address out of her brother, Qiao Mengrong decided she’d send Matron Zou out to investigate first thing tomorrow.
[ZH] ……
[EN] ...
[ZH] 天亮后，乔梦容陪哥哥用过早饭，见哥哥带白风在院中练拳法，不好将邹嬷嬷请来说事，担心被哥哥听出些端倪来，便带着喜兰亲自去找邹嬷嬷。
[EN] The next morning, after breakfast with her brother, Qiao Mengrong saw him drilling Bai Feng in fist forms in the courtyard. Afraid her brother might overhear if she summoned Matron Zou here, she brought Xilan along and walked straight to Matron Zou’s quarters.
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷刚用过早饭，打算去拜见仪王妃，顺便陪她聊聊天儿。
[EN] Matron Zou had just finished eating and was getting ready to pay her morning respects and chat with Princess Consort Yi.
[ZH] 自从黑煞虎被抓后，仪王妃再未提过要杀女词人的事，邹嬷嬷很欣慰，认为仪王妃已改变想法，能有这样的结果再好不过。
[EN] Since Black-Fiend Tiger’s arrest, the princess consort hadn’t breathed another word about killing the poetess. Matron Zou felt deeply relieved, believing her mistress had finally let it go.
[ZH] 刚走出屋门，见仪王妃走进院子。邹嬷嬷迎上去：“我刚要去见你，你就来了。”
[EN] Stepping out her door, she ran straight into Princess Consort Yi entering the yard. Matron Zou greeted her warmly. “I was just heading over to see you, and here you are.”
[ZH] 乔梦容拉起邹嬷嬷的手：“屋里说话，我有事要拜托嬷嬷。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong took Matron Zou’s hand. “Let’s talk inside. I need your help with something.”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷担心仪王妃再提刺杀女词人的事，在屋中坐下后，小心问：“王妃有什么重要的事？”
[EN] Worried she might bring up assassinating the poetess again, Matron Zou sat down inside and asked cautiously, “What important matter does Your Highness have in mind?”
[ZH] 为了不再引起他人怀疑，乔梦容打算连邹嬷嬷都瞒着：“昨晚哥哥回来，告诉我官家不让他再当临安节度使，只当个闲散的王爷。他提起此事时看上去很轻松，我却能感觉到哥哥心里并不好受，所以，我想帮帮他。”
[EN] To avoid arousing suspicion, Qiao Mengrong kept the truth even from Matron Zou. “Last night Brother told me His Majesty stripped him of the Lin’an Military Commissioner post and told him to be an idle prince. He acted unbothered when he told me, yet I can tell it hurts him inside. I want to help him.”
[ZH] “王妃是弱女子，如何帮得了王爷？”
[EN] “You’re a delicate lady, Your Highness—how could you help Prince Yi?”
[ZH] “哥哥说官家派梁内侍来临安处理此事，梁内侍会在临安住段时日，我想亲自去拜访他。梁内侍贪财，多给他塞些钱财，让他在官家面前替哥哥说些好话，节度使的位置还会是哥哥的。”
[EN] “Brother said His Majesty sent Imperial Attendant Liang to handle things and that he’ll be staying in Lin’an for a spell. I want to call on him myself. He’s greedy for money—if I grease his palms with enough silver, he’ll speak well of Brother before His Majesty, and the Military Commissioner post will come right back to him.”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷觉得此法可行，毕竟两位贵人的婚事能提前也是靠梁内侍帮忙。
[EN] Matron Zou thought it sounded plausible enough; after all, Imperial Attendant Liang had helped move the royal wedding forward too.
[ZH] “王爷可知王妃的想法？”
[EN] “Does His Highness know what you’re planning?”
[ZH] “不能让哥哥知道，他拉不下脸面，等我把事情办成，再告诉哥哥，给他一个惊喜。”
[EN] “He mustn’t find out. He’s too proud to stoop to it. Once I pull it off, I’ll surprise him.”
[ZH] “王妃如此关心王爷令人感动，需要我做什么尽管说。”
[EN] “It moves me to see how devoted you are to Prince Yi. Tell me whatever you need done.”
[ZH] “要拜访梁内侍，需打听他暂住何处，我不方便出去打探，还得拜托嬷嬷。”
[EN] “To call on Imperial Attendant Liang, I need to know where he’s staying. I can’t go snooping myself, so I’ll have to trouble you.”
[ZH] “小事一桩，交给我，不出一个时辰便打听到。”
[EN] “Nothing to it—leave it to me. I’ll find out within one shichen.”
[ZH] ……
[EN] ...
[ZH] 快用午饭时，邹嬷嬷从城里回来。挨到用完午饭后，假装叫仪王妃散步消食，顺利躲过仪王的关注。
[EN] Matron Zou returned from the city just before noon. Waiting until lunch was over, she suggested a stroll to help Princess Consort Yi digest, giving them clean cover away from Prince Yi’s ears.
[ZH] 两人在幽林别居的小径中并肩而行，感觉仪王不会尾随而来后，邹嬷嬷才说：“梁内侍住在城北的柳岸别馆。”
[EN] Walking side by side along a path inside Secluded Forest Villa until sure Prince Yi wasn’t trailing them, Matron Zou spoke. “Imperial Attendant Liang is staying at Liu’an Villa in the north of the city.”
[ZH] 乔梦容叹道：“没想到城北最大的柳岸别馆竟然是梁内侍的。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong sighed. “To think the grandest villa in northern Lin’an belongs to Imperial Attendant Liang.”
[ZH] “王妃猜猜，我从谁那里打探到梁内侍的住处？”
[EN] “Care to guess who told me where he’s staying, Your Highness?”
[ZH] “谁？”
[EN] “Who?”
[ZH] “林统制的夫人白梅若。”
[EN] “Commander Lin’s wife, Bai Meiruo.”
[ZH] 乔梦容面露不满：“哼！林觉就是棵墙头草，才一日功夫就开始巴结梁内侍，连他的住处都打听得一清二楚。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong scowled. “Hmph! Lin Jue’s nothing but a weathervane! Barely one day in town and he’s already sucking up to Imperial Attendant Liang—knows his exact address and everything.”
[ZH] “人在官场混，有此等想法再正常不过。王爷从小不受官家重视，得了临安节度使的职位，在许多人看来是官家开始重视他，一个个才会把他放在眼里前来巴结拜访。现在职位没了，往后幽林别居会更加冷清。”
[EN] “That’s how men climb in officialdom, nothing surprising about it. His Highness never stood high in His Majesty’s favor growing up. Getting made Military Commissioner of Lin’an looked like a sign His Majesty had started valuing him, which is why everyone lined up to flatter him. Now the title’s gone, and Secluded Forest Villa will turn colder and emptier than ever.”
[ZH] “哥哥再怎么着也是官家的儿子。”
[EN] “No matter what, Brother is still His Majesty’s son.”
[ZH] “是官家的儿子没错，可官家的儿子太多，总有亲疏之别，官 场之人眼睛亮着呢，最会察言观色，也最会见风使舵，王爷为他们带不来任何利益，又何必在他身上浪费时间？”
[EN] “True, he’s the emperor’s son, yet His Majesty has so many sons—some favored, some neglected. Officials have sharp eyes; they read the wind and follow profit. If His Highness can’t bring them advancement, why would they waste time on him?”
[ZH] “为了哥哥，我需快些拜访梁内侍，现在就去。”
[EN] “For Brother’s sake, I have to visit Imperial Attendant Liang right away. I’m going now.”
[ZH] 乔梦容已无心散步，带着喜兰往回走。
[EN] Losing all interest in walking, Qiao Mengrong turned straight back with Xilan.
[ZH] 来到屋内，哥哥百无聊赖地躺在床上。
[EN] Returning to their bedchamber, she found her brother stretched out listlessly on the bed.
[ZH] 乔梦容挨着床边坐下：“哥哥，我需出去一趟，几位临安的贵妇邀请我参加宴会，不好不去。”
[EN] Sitting on the mattress edge, Qiao Mengrong said, “Brother, I have to step out for a bit. A few noblewomen in Lin’an invited me to a gathering, and it wouldn’t be polite to refuse.”
[ZH] 申简辰坐起来：“我送你去。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen sat up. “I’ll escort you there.”
[ZH] 乔梦容哪里敢让哥哥送她，拒绝道：“哥哥还是好好休息，有喜兰陪着即可，再说都是些妇人，哥哥去不大合适。”
[EN] The last thing Qiao Mengrong wanted was her brother coming along. She refused quickly. “Stay and rest, Brother. Having Xilan with me is plenty—besides, it’s all married women, it wouldn’t look right for you to come.”
[ZH] “我只送你到门外，不进去。”
[EN] “I’ll only see you to the gate. I won’t go in.”
[ZH] “哥哥之前总是忙里忙外，辛苦着呢，还是好好休息，等下次有这等机会，肯定让哥哥送我。”
[EN] “You’ve been running yourself ragged lately. Stay and catch your breath. Next time an invitation comes up, I’ll let you take me.”
[ZH] 申简辰感觉出乔梦容并不想让他护送，便不再强求，继续躺下：“快去快回。”
[EN] Sensing Qiao Mengrong really didn’t want him tagging along, Shen Jianchen gave up and lay back down. “Go and come back soon then.”
[ZH] 乔梦容点点头，带喜兰坐上马车。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong nodded and boarded the carriage with Xilan.
[ZH] 马车启动后，乔梦容问：“银票可带够？”
[EN] Once the wheels rolled, Qiao Mengrong asked, “Did you bring enough silver notes?”
[ZH] “带够了，共两万两。”
[EN] “Yes, Your Highness—twenty thousand taels of silver in all.”
[ZH] “我的积蓄基本已拿出，只要能成功，花多少银子都值得。”
[EN] “That’s practically every ounce of savings I have. Still, as long as it works, no price is too high.”
[ZH] 马车进入城内，穿过闹市，来到北边，停在柳岸别馆外。
[EN] The carriage passed through the city gate, threaded through bustling markets, and halted outside Liu’an Villa in the northern quarter.
[ZH] 乔梦容带着忐忑的心情走到大门口，让喜兰上前敲门。
[EN] Heart fluttering with nerves, Qiao Mengrong walked up to the grand gate and had Xilan knock.
[ZH] 开门的下人问：“您找谁？”
[EN] A servant opened the door. “Who are you looking for?”
[ZH] 喜兰答道：“麻烦通报一声，仪王妃前来看望梁内侍。”
[EN] Xilan answered, “Kindly announce that Princess Consort Yi is here to call on Imperial Attendant Liang.”
[ZH] 听到是仪王妃，下人不敢怠慢：“贵人稍等，我这就去通报。”
[EN] Hearing “Princess Consort Yi,” the servant dared not stall. “Wait a moment, noble lady. I’ll announce you at once.”
[ZH] 门又被关上。
[EN] The gate clicked shut again.
[ZH] 不多时，那位下人将门开大，行礼道：“内侍说了，请仪王妃进去说话。”
[EN] Moments later, the servant swung the doors wide and bowed. “The Imperial Attendant says to invite Princess Consort Yi inside.”
[ZH] 乔梦容多少有些不爽，她怎么着也是官家的儿媳妇，算是皇家人。梁内侍再得官家信任说到底也是个奴才，竟然不亲自出来迎接，足以说明他根本没把哥哥放在眼里。
[EN] A spark of irritation pricked Qiao Mengrong. Like it or not, she was His Majesty’s daughter-in-law, part of the imperial clan; trusted or not, Liang was still a palace servant—yet he hadn’t bothered coming out to greet her. Clearly he held her brother in zero regard.
[ZH] 乔梦容能明显感觉出哥哥的地位已回到病着时，她安慰自己既然前来求人，还是别计较那么多。
[EN] She could already feel her brother’s standing slipping straight back to his sickly days, though she reminded herself not to pick fights when she had come asking a favor.
[ZH] 柳岸别馆比幽林别居大得多，可以媲美京城的仪王府。乔梦容更是心酸，官家身边的奴才比皇子在临安住得还要好，证明他比哥哥更受官家重用，还比哥哥有钱。
[EN] Liu’an Villa dwarfed Secluded Forest Villa—it rivaled Prince Yi’s own manor back in the capital. Bitter resentment tightened her chest: an emperor’s attendant lived better in Lin’an than an imperial prince, proving he commanded more favor and far deeper pockets than her brother.
[ZH] 带来的两万两银票，也不知梁内侍可会放在眼里？想起哥哥病着时，每次梁内侍前来传话，她会塞些银两，数额不大，他都会尽数收下，乔梦容才安心一些。
[EN] Would twenty thousand taels of silver even catch his eye? Remembering how eagerly Liang had pocketed every small bribe she slipped him during her brother’s sick days whenever he brought messages, Qiao Mengrong felt a little steadier.
[ZH] 下人将乔梦容带进别馆的屋内，乔梦容自认为身份尊贵，对方不迎接她也就罢了，她更没有向对方行礼的必要。
[EN] Led into an inner chamber, Qiao Mengrong held her chin high; if he wouldn’t greet her at the door, she saw no reason to curtsy to him either.
[ZH] 她走进屋内，直直地站立着，等待梁内侍拜见她。
[EN] She stood straight inside the hall, waiting for Imperial Attendant Liang to pay his respects.
[ZH] 只见梁内侍披头散发，身着居家的衣衫，斜靠在榻上。榻旁坐着一位丫鬟正在给他捏腿；还有一位丫鬟跪在地上，手中托着果盘，给梁内侍喂葡萄吃。
[EN] Instead, she saw him lounging sideways on a couch in loose indoor robes, hair hanging unbound. One maid sat beside the couch massaging his legs; another knelt on the floor holding a fruit platter, feeding him grapes one by one.
[ZH] 乔梦容心中更来气，如此装扮和姿态见她，显然没把她放在眼里。既然来了，只能先忍着。
[EN] Anger flared hot in Qiao Mengrong’s chest. Receiving her dressed and sprawled like that screamed disrespect. Still, having come this far, she forced herself to swallow it.
[ZH] 她向喜兰示意，喜兰开口说：“梁内侍，仪王妃特来看望您。”
[EN] At her nod, Xilan spoke up. “Imperial Attendant Liang, Princess Consort Yi has come expressly to call on you.”
[ZH] 梁师成早已用余光瞥见仪王妃站在面前，他故意装作没看见的样子。听到喜兰提醒，才坐起来，向两位丫鬟摆手让她们离开。
[EN] Liang Shicheng had spotted her from the corner of his eye the second she walked in, pretending not to notice until Xilan spoke. Only then did he sit up and wave the two maids out.
[ZH] “仪王妃驾到，有失远迎，快请入座。”
[EN] “Princess Consort Yi graces us—forgive me for failing to greet you at the gate. Please, take a seat.”
[ZH] 对方虽坐起来，却没有起身迎接她的意思，乔梦容心中骂着梁内侍实在无礼，也只好乖乖落座，谁让她有求于人。
[EN] Even seated upright, he didn’t bother standing to welcome her. Cursing his insolence under her breath, Qiao Mengrong sat down anyway—what choice did a petitioner have?
[ZH] “梁内侍要不要去换身衣衫？”
[EN] “Should Imperial Attendant Liang go change into proper clothes?”
[ZH] 梁师成笑道：“在汴梁，私下里与官家畅聊时，我也是如此装扮，官家都不介意，仪王妃又何必介意？”
[EN] Liang Shicheng chuckled. “Back in Bianliang, when I chat privately with His Majesty, I dress just like this. If His Majesty doesn’t mind, why should Princess Consort Yi?”
[ZH] 乔梦容无话可说，心中感慨，一位异姓之人竟有机会与官家身着居家服饰一起聊天儿；哥哥明明是官家的亲生儿子，却从来没有这等机会。难怪从她进来，梁内侍一直敢对她无礼。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong choked on her reply. An outsider with no royal blood sat around casually dressed chatting with the emperor, while her brother—His Majesty’s own flesh and blood—had never known such closeness. No wonder this eunuch dared treat her like dirt.
[ZH] 梁师成见仪王妃不言语，主动说：“仪王妃送到汴梁的书信我已收到，因要亲自来临安一趟，故而没回信，望贵人见谅。”
[EN] Seeing her bite her tongue, Liang Shicheng shifted to business. “I received the letter Princess Consort Yi sent to Bianliang. Since I was coming down to Lin’an myself, I didn’t write back—trust Your Highness forgives the oversight.”
[ZH] 提到重要的事，乔梦容忘记刚才所有的计较：“梁内侍不必客气，不知我在书信上提到的事内侍可能办到？”
[EN] Hearing what mattered most, Qiao Mengrong swept aside every slight. “No need for apology, Imperial Attendant Liang. Can you handle the matter I wrote about in that letter?”
[ZH] 梁师成充满自信：“天下还没有我办不成的事。”
[EN] Liang Shicheng smiled with oily certainty. “There isn’t a thing under Heaven I can’t pull off.”
[ZH] 乔梦容庆幸自己终于找对人，她向身后唤道：“喜兰。”
[EN] Glad she’d picked the right man at last, she called behind her. “Xilan.”
[ZH] 喜兰领会，走上前，将手中精致的锦盒呈上。
[EN] Understanding, Xilan stepped up and presented the exquisite brocade box in her hands.
[ZH] 梁师成看着锦盒，心中明白仪王妃是想用金钱收买他。他接过锦盒，打开瞧一遍，还真不少，又将锦盒盖好，放在榻上。
[EN] Liang Shicheng eyed the box, knowing well enough she meant to buy him. Opening the lid for a glance, he saw the stack inside was hefty indeed, snapped it shut, and set it beside him on the couch.
[ZH] “仪王妃恐怕不只为一件事而来？”
[EN] “Surely Princess Consort Yi didn’t come for just one matter?”
[ZH] “还是内侍厉害，一下子猜中，除了信中所说，还为哥哥而来，希望内侍大人多在官家面前美言，恢复哥哥临安节度使的职位。”
[EN] “Nothing slips past you, Lord Attendant. Aside from what was in my letter, I also came for my brother. I hope you’ll speak kindly of him before His Majesty and restore his post as Military Commissioner of Lin’an.”
[ZH] 梁师成将仪王妃上下打量一遍：“是仪王让你来找我的？”
[EN] Liang Shicheng looked her up and down from head to toe. “Did Prince Yi send you to me?”
[ZH] “不是，哥哥他并不知。”
[EN] “No. My brother knows nothing about this.”
[ZH] 梁师成用贪娈的眼神在乔梦容的身上继续打量：“让你的身边的丫鬟出去，我想单独与仪王妃说。”
[EN] Greedy eyes crawling over Qiao Mengrong’s figure, Liang Shicheng said softly, “Tell your maid to wait outside. I want to speak with Princess Consort Yi alone.”
[ZH] 喜兰呈上锦盒后，已回到仪王妃身后。见梁内侍如此说，请示道：“王妃？”
[EN] Having delivered the box, Xilan had stepped back behind her mistress. Hearing his command, she glanced down. “Your Highness?”
[ZH] 乔梦容转过头：“你先出去待会儿。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong turned her head. “Step outside for a little while.”
[ZH] 喜兰只好往外走，心里充满担忧。
[EN] Full of misgiving, Xilan had no choice but to walk out.
[ZH] 见丫鬟出去，梁师成终于站起来，光着脚，走到门前，将门关好。
[EN] The instant the maid left, Liang Shicheng finally stood—barefoot—walked to the door, and shut it tight.
[ZH] 屋内的光线一下子变得暗下来，莫名的恐惧感袭上心头，乔梦容站起来：“梁内侍为何要把门关上？”
[EN] Dimness swallowed the room. Cold dread crept up Qiao Mengrong’s spine, and she rose to her feet. “Why are you shutting the door, Imperial Attendant Liang?”
[ZH] 梁师成将门闩插好后，转过身：“关上门，你我的密谋才不会被人听去。”
[EN] Sliding the heavy bolt home, Liang Shicheng turned back. “Shut the door, and nobody overhears what you and I are plotting.”
[ZH] 乔梦容松口气，可依然充满担忧。她瞧着梁师成再次走到榻前，拿起放有银票的锦盒，来到她跟前。
[EN] She let out a tense breath, though unease still gnawed at her as she watched him walk back to the couch, pick up the brocade box of silver notes, and step right up to her.
[ZH] “仪王妃还是将锦盒收回，你给的银子虽不少，可我现在最不缺的就是银子。”
[EN] “Take your brocade box back, Princess Consort Yi. You brought plenty of silver, true—only right now, silver is the very last thing I lack.”
[ZH] 乔梦容不敢去接锦盒，小心问：“内侍难道不愿帮我？”
[EN] Afraid to touch the box, Qiao Mengrong asked carefully, “Are you refusing to help me, Lord Attendant?”
[ZH] 梁师成将锦盒放进乔梦容的怀里：“帮还是要帮，就看仪王妃愿不愿给我想要的？”
[EN] Liang Shicheng shoved the box straight into her arms. “Help? Oh, I’ll help—depends on whether Princess Consort Yi is willing to give me what I want.”
[ZH] “内侍……内侍想要什么？”
[EN] “What… what does the Lord Attendant want?”
[ZH] “我虽不是真正的男人，可也喜欢女人，尤其喜欢成了亲的女人，若仪王妃愿意陪我快活一回，让我做什么都可以。”
[EN] “I may not be a real man, yet I still enjoy women—married women most of all. If Princess Consort Yi keeps me happy for one afternoon, I’ll do whatever you ask.”

#CH 315
[ZH] 手中的锦盒落在地上，乔梦容不由自主向一旁退去：“在汴梁时，你不是很喜欢银子吗？”
[EN] The brocade box slipped from her hands and clattered onto the floor. Qiao Mengrong backed away in revulsion. “Back in Bianliang, you loved silver well enough!”
[EN] Liang Shicheng stalked closer. “Back then you weren’t married—you had zero appeal to me. I only took your silver to keep you calm. Now you’re wed, prettier than ever, and you walked right into my lap begging for help. Think I’d let a prize like you slip away?” Reaching out, he stroked Qiao Mengrong’s cheek.
[ZH] 梁师成慢慢逼近：“你那时未成亲，对我毫无吸引力，为了让你安心，我才收下银子。终于等到你成亲，而且越来越美，又求到我门上，我岂能轻易放过？”梁师成边说边伸手去摸乔梦容的脸。
[EN] She slapped his hand away in disgust, her voice shaking. “I… I am Princess Consort Yi! How dare you make… make such a filthy demand of me!”
[ZH] 乔梦容厌恶地推开他的手，声音有些颤抖：“我……我是……仪王妃，你……胆敢向我……提出……这等要求！”
[EN] “I don’t even put Prince Yi in my eyes—why would I care about his consort?”
[ZH] “仪王我都不放在眼里，何况是仪王妃？”
[EN] “I’m leaving! I don’t want your help anymore!” Qiao Mengrong bolted toward the door.
[ZH] “我……我要走，不再……求你帮忙。”乔梦容向门口闪去。
[EN] Liang Shicheng cut straight across her path. “Think I’d ever let a chance this good walk out the door?”
[ZH] 结果被梁师成堵在面前：“这么好的机会我怎么可能轻易放你走？”
[EN] Desperate, she threatened him. “Touch me, and my brother… my brother will never let you off!”
[ZH] 乔梦容继续吓唬：“你若……动我，哥哥不会……不会轻易放过你！”
[EN] “You won’t tell Prince Yi—you’re terrified he’ll look at you with disgust if he finds out. Just you and me know. No third soul will ever hear a word, so why put up this coy act?”
[ZH] “你不会告诉仪王，因为你担心仪王听后嫌弃你。此事你知我知，不会有第三个人知道，又何必扭扭捏捏？”
[EN] Putting every ounce of strength into her arms, Qiao Mengrong shoved Liang Shicheng aside and lunged for the door. Quick as a snake, he grabbed her arm and wrenched her straight into his chest.
[ZH] 乔梦容使出全身的力气推开梁师成，向门口冲去。梁师成快速上前，一把抓住她的手臂，将她拽入怀中。
[EN] “You’re not escaping my grip.”
[ZH] “你逃不出我手掌心。”
[EN] She thrashed to break free, yet one arm clamped tight around her throat while his other hand groped across her body, pinning her helpless.
[ZH] 乔梦容想要逃离，可对方一只手臂勒住她的脖子，另一只手摸在她的身上，想要逃根本不可能。
[EN] Tears welled hot in her eyes. “Please… let me go…”
[ZH] 泪水开始在眼中打转：“求你……放过我。”
[EN] “While Prince Yi lay sick, I worried you’d wind up living a chaste widow’s life—looking all pure and untouched like ice. I even considered having someone take your maidenhead first so you’d taste sweeter for me later. Who knew Prince Yi would suddenly recover and you’d beg to move up the wedding? How could I say no? Two words from me swayed His Majesty, and you got your wedding right on cue. That night you married, I lay awake thrilled half to death, picturing you as my own bride. Care to guess why I came all the way down to Lin’an?”
[ZH] “仪王病着时，我一直担心你将来会为仪王守寡，总是一副冰清玉洁的样子，还想过不如找个人先动了你，我吃着才香。谁知仪王竟然好了，你还要求提前成亲，我怎能不答应？三言两语说动官家，你才跟仪王顺利成亲。你们成亲那晚，我激动了好久，就像你要成为我的新娘一般。你可知我为何来临安？”
[EN] Never in her wildest nightmares had Qiao Mengrong imagined the man had been stalking her all along. Fury at her own blindness burned her throat—she had walked step by step straight into the wolf’s jaws.
[ZH] 乔梦容怎么都没想到，原来对方蓄谋已久。她后悔自己为何不早些看出，一步一步羊入虎口。
[EN] Sick with dread and regret, she couldn’t choke out a word.
[ZH] 悔恨和害怕搞得她没有心情回答梁师成的问题。
[EN] “All for you! I whispered in His Majesty’s ear, spiced up everything Prince Yi was doing in Lin’an, got His Majesty steaming mad, and got myself sent down here to clean house. I knew the second you heard I was in town, you’d come running to me. So restoring Prince Yi’s post? Nothing more than a single word from my lips.”
[ZH] “都是为了你，我才向官家进言，又将仪王在临安的所作所为添油加醋一番，官家听后很生气，派我来临安整治风气。我早已猜到，你知道我来临安，必然会找我。因此，仪王想官复原职不过是我一句话的事。”
[EN] Knowing she was the very reason her brother lost his post tore fresh tears down Qiao Mengrong’s cheeks. “How did you know what my brother was doing in Lin’an?”
[ZH] 原来是自己害了哥哥，乔梦容更加难过，泪水夺眶而出：“你怎知哥哥在临安做些什么？”
[EN] “Hehehe… I’ve got eyes inside Lin’an’s prefecture office writing me letters on everything happening here. Did you think I wouldn’t know?”
[ZH] “呵呵……，临安府衙有我的人，时常书信向我汇报临安的情况，我岂会不知？”
[EN] Hands roaming wildly over her body, Liang Shicheng trapped her as Qiao Mengrong stared hopelessly at the bolted door and scanned the room—no escape anywhere.
[ZH] 梁师成在她身上上下其手，乔梦容厌恶的同时，瞧向紧紧关闭的大门，再扫视一遍周围，想要逃跑已不可能。
[EN] Blaming herself twisted straight into murderous hatred for Zhu Shuzhen. That woman had driven her corner by corner into this hell. Zhu Shuzhen had to die!
[ZH] 她埋怨自己的同时，更恨朱淑真，认为是她一步一步将自己逼到今日境地，她必须死！
[EN] “Can you truly kill her for me?”
[ZH] “你真的能帮我杀了她？”
[EN] Hearing the fight go out of her voice, Liang Shicheng kissed her cheek. “Keep me happy today, and I guarantee I’ll scatter her soul to the winds.”
[ZH] 梁师成见仪王妃的口气不再那么强硬，在她脸上亲了一口：“只要你今日陪我快活一回，我保证让她魂飞魄散。”
[EN] “Don’t lie to me!”
[ZH] “不许骗我！”
[EN] “Why would I ever lie to a beauty who’s haunted my dreams?”
[ZH] “我怎么会骗让我魂牵梦绕的美人？”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong squeezed her eyes shut as two heavy tears rolled down her cheeks. “Fine… I agree. Only swear to me you’ll never breathe a single syllable of today to my brother.”
[ZH] 乔梦容闭上双眼，两颗泪珠滚落下来：“好……我答应你，但你必须答应我，今日之事绝不能在哥哥面提起一个字。”
[EN] Loosening his grip, Liang Shicheng stripped off her outer robe. “Relax. Prince Yi will never know a thing.”
[ZH] 梁师成松开手臂，为乔梦容褪去外衣：“放心，仪王永远不会知道今日之事。”
[EN] With her outer garment pooling on the floor, he stepped back, walked to the table, lifted a wine pot in one hand and a cup in the other, poured himself wine, and barked, “Get in here!”
[ZH] 外衣已掉落在地上，梁师成不再亲自动手，他走到桌前，一手端起酒壶，一手拿只酒杯，给自己斟满酒：“来人！”
[EN] Four women in skimpy, revealing clothes stepped out at once.
[ZH] 竟然出现四名女子，衣着暴露。
[EN] Liang Shicheng ordered, “Strip every stitch off her—leave her stark naked—and make her dance for me. Nothing I enjoy more than sipping wine while watching women dance.”
[ZH] 梁师成命令道：“给她把衣裳脱了，要一丝 不挂，在我面前舞一曲，我最喜欢边饮酒边欣赏女子跳舞。”
[EN] Tears streaming down her face, Qiao Mengrong stood numb while the four women peeled off her clothes, then forced herself to sway and twist like them in a degrading dance.
[ZH] 乔梦容已泪流满面，任由四名女子脱去她身上的衣裳，然后学着那四名女子扭动身姿，跳着屈辱的舞蹈。
[EN] Liang Shicheng didn’t even sit; prowling around her with a leering grin, he drank wine and feasted his eyes.
[ZH] 梁师成并未坐下，绕着周围，面露色相，一边饮酒，一边欣赏。
[EN] When the pot ran dry, he flung pot and cup crashing onto the floor. At once the four women stopped, grabbed Qiao Mengrong, and pinned her flat onto the boards.
[ZH] 待一壶酒饮完，梁师成将酒壶和酒杯扔在地上。四名女子立刻停下，将乔梦容控制住，压倒在地。
[EN] Part of her wanted to fight, yet everything had already gone too far—she’d been stripped bare before his eyes anyway. All she could do was shut her eyes tight.
[ZH] 乔梦容想反抗，可一切已经开始，她已被对方看光，再反抗又有何意义？只能闭上眼睛。
[EN] “Hold her arms and legs down steady,” Liang Shicheng ordered, stalking closer. “I’m no whole man, true, yet I’ve got my own way of finding bliss. I’m going to kiss you from head to toe, and everywhere my lips touch, I’ll drive a needle in. The worse it hurts, the louder you scream, the happier I get—and I won’t stop until I reach pure heaven.”
[ZH] “你们四个将他的手脚控制好。”梁师成命令完，向乔梦容逼近，“我不是真正的男人，可我有自己获得快乐的方式，我会吻遍你的全身，凡是我吻过的地方，都会扎上一针，你疼得越厉害，声音喊得越大，我会越开心，什么时候我到达飘飘 欲仙的境界，才会作罢。”
[EN] At the word “needle,” Qiao Mengrong’s eyes flew open. Seeing Liang Shicheng looming over her like a demon clutching a steel needle between his fingers, cold sweat broke out across her skin. “Please! Spare me! Let me go! Wuu…”
[ZH] 听到要用针扎，乔梦容吓得睁开眼睛，她看到梁师成像恶魔一样，手中捏着一根针，向她走来。她吓出一身冷汗，央求道：“求求你！放过我吧！放过我！呜呜……！”
[EN] Crouching beside her, Liang Shicheng spoke in a gentle, chilling purr. “We’ve come this far—why on earth would I stop now? Let this needle show you the might of a man. Scream when it stings; the louder, the better.”
[ZH] 梁师成已蹲在她身旁，用和蔼的声音说：“既已开始，岂有放过你的道理？我会用手中这根针让你感受到男人的雄风，疼了就喊出了，声音越大越好。”
[EN] “You… monster!”
[ZH] 乔梦容骂道：“你……变态！”
[EN] “Curse all you like! I used to be a proper boy till palace life left me neither man nor woman. Living in a twisted world, I learned to twist along with it. If you want someone to blame, curse His Majesty, not me.”
[ZH] “骂得好！我本是好男儿，一入宫，却变得不男不女。生活在一个变态的时代，我只能顺着潮流改变自己，你要骂，就骂官家，不要骂我。”
[EN] With that, Liang Shicheng pressed his lips to Qiao Mengrong’s shoulder—followed instantly by a piercing stab that tore a shriek from her throat: “Aaah!”
[ZH] 梁师成说完，亲吻在乔梦容的肩上，接着她感受到一阵刺痛，不由自主大声喊叫着：“啊！……”
[EN] Closing his eyes to savor the rush of her scream, Liang Shicheng leaned down, kissed another spot, and ruthlessly drove the needle in again.
[ZH] 梁师成闭上眼睛享受了一会儿乔梦容的尖叫声带来的快感，再次吻去，然后又狠心刺下……
[EN] Outside, hearing her mistress’s cries, Xilan pounded frantically on the door. “Your Highness! What’s wrong? Open the door! Open up!”
[ZH] 喜兰在外面听到仪王妃喊叫的声音，上前使劲敲门：“仪王妃！你怎么了？快开门啊！快开门啊！”
[EN] A guard stepped over and pressed a naked blade against Xilan’s neck. “Keep yelling and I’ll slit your throat!”
[ZH] 一名侍卫过来，用刀架在她的脖子上：“再喊！小心我杀了你！”
[EN] The razor-sharp edge grazed her skin; one slip would draw blood. Terrified, Xilan froze.
[ZH] 明晃晃的刀刃挨着脖子，不小心会被刺破，喜兰吓坏，不敢再喊。
[EN] “Wait quietly in the courtyard and stay out of the Lord Attendant’s fun!”
[ZH] “乖乖待在院中，不要随便打扰内侍大人快活！”
[EN] Forced back at swordpoint, Xilan retreated into the yard while the guard sheathed his blade and kept watch over her.
[ZH] 喜兰在侍卫的威逼下，走到院中。
[EN] Piercing screams continued pouring through the walls, each louder than the last, twisting Xilan’s stomach into knots. Hearing the guard’s words about “fun,” she guessed well enough what awful fate Princess Consort Yi was suffering inside.
[ZH] 侍卫才收起手中的剑，站在一旁看着喜兰。
[EN] Crouching on the flagstones, Xilan clapped both hands over her ears, yet her mistress’s shrieks cut straight through anyway.
[ZH] 里面不断传出仪王妃的喊声，一声比一声大，每一声听得喜兰担忧不已。刚才侍卫提到“快活”二字，喜兰大略已猜到仪王妃恐怕要失身于梁内侍。
[EN] After what felt like ages, the screaming died down. A little while later, the door bolt finally slid back.
[ZH] 她蹲在地上，捂住耳朵，可还能清晰地听到仪王妃的尖叫声。
[EN] Xilan scrambled up and stared at the doorway.
[ZH] 不知过了多久，声音没了，再过了一会儿，终于听到开门的声音。
[EN] Princess Consort Yi stumbled out. Her clothes were buttoned neat enough, yet her hair hung tangled, her face looked white as chalk, and her eyelids burned red and swollen—she swayed like a ghost ready to collapse.
[ZH] 喜兰站起来，看向门口。
[EN] Xilan rushed up to catch her. “Your Highness! What happened?”
[ZH] 仪王妃从里面走出来，衣衫虽穿得整齐，头发却凌乱，面色苍白，眼皮红肿，一副失魂落魄的样子，仿佛站都站不稳。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong mumbled blankly, “I want to go home. Take me home right now…”
[ZH] 喜兰冲上去，扶好她：“王妃，您怎么了？”
[EN] “Right away, Your Highness.” Seeing her mistress shaken to her wits’ end, Xilan’s heart ached with dread.
[ZH] 乔梦容念叨着：“我要回家，快带我回家……”
[EN] Halfway across the yard, one of the scantily clad women trotted up behind them holding out the brocade box. “The Lord Attendant says Princess Consort Yi performed wonderfully—and he doesn’t want a single one of your silver notes.”
[ZH] “我这就带王妃回去。”仪王妃显然被惊吓到，喜兰担忧不已。
[EN] “I want to go home, I want to go home…” Qiao Mengrong kept repeating.
[ZH] 她扶着仪王妃刚走到院中，身后走来一名衣着暴露的女子，递上锦盒：“内侍说了，仪王妃表现很好，至于银票他一张都不会要。”
[EN] Snatching the box, Xilan half-carried her mistress out of Liu’an Villa.
[ZH] “我要回家，我要回家……”乔梦容继续念叨着。
[EN] Inside the carriage, Xilan smoothed down her mistress’s tangled hair, weeping softly. “Had I known, I never would have brought Your Highness to meet Imperial Attendant Liang.”
[ZH] 喜兰迅速接过锦盒，扶着仪王妃出了柳岸别馆。
[EN] Tear streaks staining her deathly pale cheeks, Qiao Mengrong murmured in a hollowed voice, “Don’t tell Brother.”
[ZH] 马车上，喜兰帮仪王妃整理好头发，流泪道：“早知如此，我不该陪王妃见梁内侍。”
[EN] Wiping her eyes, Xilan promised, “Never. If we told him, I’m afraid… His Highness might turn away from you in disgust.”
[ZH] 乔梦容苍白的脸上挂着泪痕，看上去毫无生气，用缺乏力度的声音说：“别告诉哥哥。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong longed to wail out loud, yet fearing the coachman would hear, she bit down and swallowed the sobs—shaking violently from head to toe inside.
[ZH] 喜兰一边抹眼泪，一边答道：“我定然不会说，只怕说了王爷……王爷会嫌弃王妃。”
[EN] Panic-stricken, Xilan wrapped her arms around her mistress, mistress and maid clutching each other and trembling together inside the swaying carriage.
[ZH] 乔梦容想大声哭出来，担心被车夫听到，努力忍住，哭声憋到体内，震得整个身体都跟着颤抖。
[EN] Back at Secluded Forest Villa, Qiao Mengrong forced herself to walk steadily. Supported by Xilan, she stopped outside the courtyard where her brother slept. After a long pause, she whispered, “Take me to my old courtyard.”
[ZH] 喜兰甚是担忧，抱住仪王妃，主仆两个在车内缠成一团，颤抖着身体。
[EN] Understanding why, Xilan didn’t argue a word.
[ZH] 回到幽林别居，乔梦容努力表现得与往常一样。在喜兰的搀扶下，来到哥哥居住的院子门口，她停住脚步，片刻后说道：“回我以前的小院。”
[EN] Once inside her old room, Qiao Mengrong finally let out a whimper of pain and told Xilan to fetch salve for her skin.
[ZH] 喜兰大略明白仪王妃的心意，没有说任何反对的话。
[EN] When Xilan helped her out of her clothes and saw dozens of tiny needle punctures speckling her shoulders and back, her jaw nearly hit the floor. She had assumed Imperial Attendant Liang had simply forced himself on her—never imagining the depraved cruelty behind those screams.
[ZH] 回到小院的屋内，乔梦容才喊疼，让喜兰找来药，给身上涂抹。
[EN] “Don’t… don’t breathe a word,” Qiao Mengrong choked out. “Not even to Matron Zou.”
[ZH] 当喜兰褪去仪王妃的衣衫，看到她的肩头和背上有很多针眼儿，几乎傻眼。本以为仪王妃不过是被梁内侍糟蹋，没承想对方如此心狠，难怪她在外面听到一声声痛苦的喊叫声。
[EN] Weeping, Xilan nodded and dabbed salve over every red puncture mark. “Your Highness, never go near Imperial Attendant Liang again for that poetess’s sake. He’s pure scum!”
[ZH] “别……别说出去，连邹嬷嬷都不能告诉。”
[EN] “Never again. Not ever. He gave me his word he’ll kill her—she has to die! Otherwise what did I endure all this for…”
[ZH] 喜兰含泪答应，将药涂抹在每个针眼儿处：“王妃往后不要再为女词人的事找梁内侍，他就是个混蛋。”
[EN] Unable to finish, Qiao Mengrong ignored Xilan’s hands still tending her back, flung herself face-down onto the bed, buried her face in the pillow, and broke into racking sobs.
[ZH] “不会了，再也不会，梁内侍已答应我会杀了她。她必须死！否则我岂不是白白……”
[EN] ...
[ZH] 乔梦容已说不下去，顾不上喜兰正在涂抹药物，趴在床上，将头埋进枕头里，痛哭起来……
[EN] When night fell and Mengrong still hadn’t returned, Shen Jianchen grew uneasy and started toward the main gate to check.
[ZH] 天已黑，还没见梦容回来，申简辰很是担心，准备到大门口瞧瞧。
[EN] Just then Xilan walked up and curtsied. “Your Highness, Her Highness came back feeling unwell and wishes to rest quietly in her old courtyard for a few days.”
[ZH] 却见喜兰前来，她行礼道：“王爷，王妃从外面回来后，身体不适，想近期住在以前的院子静养些日子。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen frowned in concern. “Let me go look at her.”
[ZH] 申简辰担忧地说：“我去瞧瞧。”
[EN] Xilan stepped in his path. “Her Highness is already asleep, Your Highness. Go tomorrow.”
[ZH] 喜兰阻止道：“王妃已睡下，王爷明日再去。”
[EN] Suspicion flickered across his mind. “Did something happen to her while she was out?”
[ZH] 申简辰感到奇怪：“王妃是不是在外遇到什么事？”
[EN] Xilan lied smoothly. “Nothing at all—just a common women’s ailment, a discharge issue that leaves her uncomfortable. She wants to sleep in separate quarters for a few days until she feels better, then she’ll keep you company again.”
[ZH] 喜兰掩饰道：“没有，不过是女子身上容易有的带下之症，王妃感到不舒服，才要与王爷分房住些日子，等好些再陪王爷。”
[EN] “In that case I won’t disturb her. Take good care of her.”
[ZH] “既如此我就不去打扰她，你好生伺候。”
[EN] “Yes, Your Highness.”
[ZH] “是。”
[EN] Breathing a quiet sigh of relief, Xilan curtsied and slipped away.
[ZH] ﻿喜兰松口气，行礼后离开。
[EN] ...
[ZH] 第二日，梁师成衣冠楚楚来到幽林别居拜见仪王。
[EN] The very next day, Liang Shicheng turned up at Secluded Forest Villa dressed in crisp, formal robes to pay a call on Prince Yi.
[ZH] 申简辰稳坐正位，黑着脸接见了他。
[EN] Shen Jianchen received him from the seat of honor with a thundercloud on his face.
[ZH] 梁师成简单行礼后，不请自坐，显然没将仪王放在眼里。
[EN] Offering the briefest bow, Liang Shicheng helped himself to a chair uninvited, showing open contempt for the prince.
[ZH] “仪王可知我为何前来拜见？”
[EN] “Does Your Highness know why I’ve come calling?”
[ZH] “梁内侍不必与本王打哑谜，有什么事尽快说。”
[EN] “Spare me the riddles, Imperial Attendant Liang. State your business and be quick about it.”
[ZH] “先恭喜仪王。”
[EN] “First, congratulations, Your Highness.”
[ZH] “何喜之有？”
[EN] “What is there to congratulate?”
[ZH] “仪王可继续担任临安节度使一职，您先安心担着，等我回到京城会在官家面前多多美言，不愁官家不会收回口谕。”
[EN] “Your Highness may keep serving as Military Commissioner of Lin’an. Take up the post calmly for now—once I return to the capital, I’ll put in plenty of good words before His Majesty, and he’s sure to rescind his verbal decree.”
[ZH] 重要的官职被一个奸臣随口说没就没，说有就有，朝堂上的官家已昏庸到何等地步？申简辰才不愿替昏君做事。
[EN] A key post stripped away on a crooked attendant’s whim, then handed right back the next day—how far gone was the emperor sitting on the dragon throne? Shen Jianchen had zero desire to lift a finger for an incompetent sovereign.
[ZH] “哼！原来是此事。梁内侍不必费心，本王是仪王，仪王的名头可比临安节度使的职位尊贵得多，我岂能把小小的职位放在眼里？麻烦你回到汴梁告诉父皇，我只喜欢当个闲散的王爷。”
[EN] “Hmph! So that’s all. Save yourself the trouble, Imperial Attendant Liang. I am Prince Yi—my title as an imperial prince outranks some petty Military Commissioner of Lin’an ten times over. Think I care about a minor post? Carry word back to Bianliang for my imperial father: I prefer staying a leisurely prince.”
[ZH] 仪王的态度令梁师成感到意外，他猜测昨日仪王妃求他不过是一厢情愿而已。
[EN] Prince Yi’s blunt refusal caught Liang Shicheng off guard; apparently, Princess Consort Yi’s begging yesterday had been entirely her own idea.
[ZH] “仪王看得开，令人佩服，既如此临安节度使的职位就让给他人。不过我回到汴梁仍然会在官家面前替仪王美言，谁让仪王能娶到一位好王妃。”
[EN] “How remarkably detached Your Highness is—truly admirable. Very well, the Lin’an Military Commissioner post can go to someone else. Still, once back in Bianliang, I’ll praise Your Highness to His Majesty anyway—seeing how fortunate you were to wed such an extraordinary princess consort.”
[ZH] 申简辰警惕地问：“什么意思？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen sharpened his gaze. “What is that supposed to mean?”
[ZH] “哦，我的意思是仪王病着时，她对仪王照顾有加，令人感动，此等女子世间少有，因为她我自然会帮仪王。”梁师成掩饰完，站起来行礼，“下官告退。”
[EN] “Oh, nothing—only how touching her devotion was while you lay sick. A woman like that is rare in this world. For her sake, naturally I’ll help Your Highness.” Smoothly covering his slip, Liang Shicheng stood and bowed. “Your servant takes his leave.”
[ZH] 申简辰从梁师成的解释中琢磨不出任何意味，继续黑着脸。待对方离开后，叮嘱白风：“告诉李管家，此人再来就说我不在。”
[EN] Making no sense of the man’s cryptic remark, Shen Jianchen kept his dark scowl. Once Liang left, he instructed Bai Feng, “Tell Butler Li: if that man shows up again, say I’m not home.”
[ZH] “是。”白风答应完，忙着出去向李管家叮嘱。
[EN] “Yes, Your Highness.” Bai Feng hurried off to pass the order along.
[ZH] 当喜兰听说梁内侍来到幽林别居时，将此消息告诉仪王妃，她躺在床上一脸恐惧，指着门。
[EN] When word reached the inner quarters that Imperial Attendant Liang had come to Secluded Forest Villa, Xilan mentioned it to her mistress. Lying in bed, Qiao Mengrong went pale with terror and pointed straight at the door.
[ZH] “快去！把门关好！别让他进来！”
[EN] “Hurry! Shut the doors! Don’t let him inside!”
[ZH] 喜兰匆忙出去将院门和屋门都关好，回到屋内安慰道：“他只是找王爷，不会来打扰王妃。”
[EN] Xilan scrambled to bolt both the courtyard gate and the bedchamber door before rushing back to soothe her. “He only came to see His Highness, Your Highness—he won’t bother you.”
[ZH] 乔梦容担忧道：“他会不会将昨日的事告诉哥哥？你快去打听他找哥哥是何事？”
[EN] Panic clawed at Qiao Mengrong. “What if he tells Brother what happened yesterday? Go find out right now what he wanted with Brother!”
[ZH] 喜兰答应后，匆忙出去，正好碰到白风。
[EN] Agreeing, Xilan hurried outside and bumped right into Bai Feng.
[ZH] “白风！干什么去？”
[EN] “Bai Feng! Where are you off to?”
[ZH] 听到喜兰的呼唤，白风站住回过身：“去找李管家。”
[EN] Hearing his name, Bai Feng stopped and turned. “Looking for Butler Li.”
[ZH] 喜兰靠近后问：“听说梁内侍来找王爷，王妃担心，让我过来问问。”
[EN] Stepping closer, Xilan asked, “I heard Imperial Attendant Liang came to see His Highness. Her Highness is worried and sent me to ask what it was about.”
[ZH] “让王妃别担心，梁内侍不过是来请王爷继续担任节度使一职，王爷没答应，他就走了。王爷还让我叮嘱李管家，往后梁内侍来幽林别居，就说他不在。”
[EN] “Tell Her Highness not to worry. Imperial Attendant Liang just came to ask His Highness to take back the Military Commissioner post, and His Highness turned him down flat, so he left. His Highness even told me to have Butler Li turn Liang away next time he comes calling.”
[ZH] 没有提到昨日之事，喜兰安心许多：“你快去吧。”
[EN] Nothing about yesterday—thank Heaven! Xilan let out a huge breath. “Go on then.”
[ZH] 白风走后，喜兰也快步回到小院，将情况给仪王妃说了。
[EN] Once Bai Feng trotted off, Xilan hurried back to the small courtyard to tell Princess Consort Yi.
[ZH] 乔梦容不再担忧，继续躺好。哥哥根本瞧不上节度使的职位，她一半为此事而献身，实在不值，眼泪不由滚落下来。
[EN] Her fear easing, Qiao Mengrong lay back down. So her brother couldn’t care less about the Military Commissioner title—meaning half the reason she had sacrificed her own body had been thrown away for nothing. Hot tears spilled down her cheeks.
[ZH] 忽然觉得腹内疼痛，乔梦容的身体不由蜷缩起来。
[EN] All at once, sharp cramps tore through her lower belly, doubling Qiao Mengrong over.
[ZH] 喜兰见状问道：“您怎么了？”
[EN] Alarmed, Xilan rushed to the bed. “What’s wrong, Your Highness?”
[ZH] “不知怎的，腹中甚是疼痛，帮我揉揉。”
[EN] “I don’t know—my belly hurts terribly! Rub it for me!”
[ZH] 喜兰掀开被子，准备去帮仪王妃揉腹部时，却看到仪王妃的下身流出许多血来，将衣裤和褥子浸透。
[EN] Throwing back the quilt to massage her mistress’s stomach, Xilan froze at the sight: dark red blood was pooling from beneath Princess Consort Yi, soaking straight through her trousers and the mattress pad.
[ZH] 喜兰不由惊道：“我的妈呀！怎么会流出好多血？我去找王爷！”
[EN] Xilan shrieked in terror, “Mother of Heaven! Where did all this blood come from?! I’m getting His Highness!”

#CH 316
[ZH] 喜兰奔跑着来到仪王的院子，冲进屋内，跪下哭道：“呜呜！王爷！王妃的下身流出许多血！您快去瞧瞧！”
[ZH] 申简辰二话不说冲出屋外，正好碰见白风回来，命令道：“快去城里把大夫请来！”
[ZH] 王爷焦急地离开，白风根本来不及问，迅速跑出幽林别居，驾上马车去请大夫。
[EN] Xilan sprinted straight to Prince Yi’s courtyard, burst into the room, and dropped to her knees sobbing. “Wuu! Your Highness! Princess Consort Yi is bleeding heavily down below! Hurry and look at her!”
[ZH] 到达小院门口，申简辰才想起邹嬷嬷懂些医术，对身后的喜兰说：“快去！把邹嬷嬷叫来！”
[EN] Without saying a word, Shen Jianchen dashed out the door, colliding with Bai Feng on his way back. “Ride into town and fetch a physician—now!”
[ZH] 喜兰匆忙答应完，立刻去找邹嬷嬷。
[EN] Seeing the prince so frantic, Bai Feng asked no questions, sprinted straight out of Secluded Forest Villa, hitched a carriage, and raced for town.
[ZH] 当申简辰进入闺房，梦容躺在床上流泪，面色惨白。
[EN] Reaching Mengrong’s courtyard gate, Shen Jianchen remembered Matron Zou knew a bit of medicine and called over his shoulder to Xilan, “Go fetch Matron Zou right away!”
[ZH] 他上前坐在床边，抓起梦容的手关切地说：“大夫一会儿就到，有我在什么都不用怕。”
[EN] Xilan gasped out a “Yes!” and bolted off.
[ZH] 腹部的疼痛感已减弱，下身流出那么多血，乔梦容的确被吓着，她抱住哥哥：“哥哥，我是不是要死了？”
[EN] Stepping into the bedchamber, Shen Jianchen saw Mengrong lying pale as paper with tears rolling down her face.
[ZH] 申简辰安慰道：“不会，有哥哥在，你不会有事。”
[EN] Sitting down on the edge of the bed, he gripped her hand warmly. “A physician will be here any moment. I’m right here—don’t be afraid.”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷已进来，到床前掀开被子，瞧到褥子上的一滩血迹，已有不好的预感。她从仪王手里接过王妃的手，将手指搭在手腕上，感受一会儿脉息，才把王妃的手腕轻轻放下，面色凝重。
[EN] The abdominal cramps had eased, yet all that blood terrified Qiao Mengrong. She clung to him. “Brother… am I going to die?”
[ZH] 申简辰问：“嬷嬷可看出什么？”
[EN] “No,” Shen Jianchen soothed her. “With me here, nothing’s going to happen to you.”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷叹口气：“我仅懂些皮毛，还需大夫来了再做判断。”
[EN] Matron Zou rushed in, lifted the quilt, and saw the dark puddle soaking the mattress pad—her heart sank. Gently taking the princess consort’s wrist from Prince Yi, she pressed her fingers to the pulse for a long moment before laying her hand back down with a grim expression.
[ZH] 乔梦容感觉到邹嬷嬷担心她难过才不愿说出：“嬷嬷说吧，不必顾及我，大夫来后，依旧会知道病情，无论是什么结果我都能接受。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen asked, “Can you tell what’s wrong, Matron?”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷才大胆做出判断：“只怪我近期疏忽，没有时常帮王妃诊脉，竟不知她已有身孕……”
[EN] Matron Zou sighed. “I only know the basics. We’ll have to wait for the physician to be certain.”
[ZH] 听到有身孕，乔梦容闪着泪光的双眼中多出些许笑意：“我竟然有身孕？是好事，嬷嬷又何必难以启齿？”
[EN] Sensing Matron Zou was holding back to spare her feelings, Qiao Mengrong said weakly, “Speak plainly, Matron. Don’t worry about upsetting me—once the physician gets here, I’ll hear the truth anyway. Whatever it is, I can take it.”
[ZH] “听我把话说完。”邹嬷嬷尽量将语气放得缓和些，“王妃吃了几个月的药，终于怀上孩子，本是好事，可不知怎会小产？下身才会流出许多血，等会儿大夫来，再让他瞧瞧。”
[EN] Only then did Matron Zou speak out. “It’s my fault for getting careless lately and not checking your pulse regularly. I had no idea you were with child…”
[ZH] 刚有的笑容立刻消失，乔梦容抱住自己的脑袋，像是自语：“怎么会这样？怎么会这样？怎么会这样？……”
[EN] “With child?” A spark of joy flickered through Qiao Mengrong’s tear-rimmed eyes. “I’m pregnant? That’s wonderful news—why couldn’t you bring yourself to say it, Matron?”
[ZH] 申简辰将梦容抱在怀里：“没事的，等大夫来了，开些药，帮你调理好身体，还会怀上孩子。”
[EN] “Let me finish, Your Highness.” Matron Zou kept her voice gentle. “After months of taking medicine, getting pregnant was indeed a blessing. Yet somehow you’ve miscarried—that’s why you’re bleeding so heavily. When the physician arrives, let him examine you properly.”
[ZH] 哥哥安慰的话语再次令乔梦容充满希望，她放下手：“真的吗？”
[EN] The fragile smile vanished instantly. Qiao Mengrong clapped both hands around her head, murmuring as if to herself, “How could this happen? How could this happen? How could this happen…”
[ZH] 申简辰肯定地点点头：“真的，哥哥什么时候骗过你？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen pulled Mengrong into his arms. “It’s going to be alright. Once the physician prescribes tonics to nurse your body back to health, we can still have children.”
[ZH] 乔梦容再次流下眼泪，抱着哥哥哭起来：“呜呜……！”
[EN] His gentle words lit a flicker of hope again. Lowering her hands, she asked, “Really?”
[ZH] 喜兰跪在地上也哭起来：“呜呜！王爷，都怪我没照顾好王妃，呜呜……！”
[EN] Shen Jianchen nodded with certainty. “Really. When has Brother ever lied to you?”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷站在一旁也抹着眼泪：“行了，别跪着，赶紧拿些布子垫在王妃身下。”
[EN] Tears spilled fresh as Qiao Mengrong threw her arms around him and wept aloud. “Wuu…!”
[ZH] 喜兰匆忙起身，找来布子，在王妃身下刚垫好。
[EN] Kneeling on the floor, Xilan broke down sobbing too. “Wuu! Your Highness, it’s all my fault for not taking proper care of her! Wuu…”
[ZH] 这时，白风请来的大夫提着药箱从外面进来。
[EN] Matron Zou dabbed her eyes beside them. “Enough kneeling and crying—hurry and fetch clean cloths to slip under Her Highness!”
[ZH] 申简辰赶紧让出位置，喜兰端来凳子。大夫急忙坐好开始诊脉。诊完脉后，得出的结论与邹嬷嬷说的一模一样。
[EN] Scrambling up, Xilan grabbed fresh cloths and had just tucked them beneath her mistress when Bai Feng ushered in the physician carrying his medicine chest.
[ZH] 得到大夫的确诊，乔梦容心酸不已，好不容易怀上哥哥的孩子，就这么没了，她觉得老天实在不公。
[EN] Shen Jianchen yielded his spot beside the bed while Xilan set down a stool. Taking a seat, the physician felt her pulse—and confirmed every word Matron Zou had guessed.
[ZH] 大夫从医箱里拿出一瓶丸药递给喜兰：“取出一丸让王妃用温水服下，血便可止住。”
[EN] Hearing the physician’s verdict made Qiao Mengrong’s heart ache unbearably. To finally carry her brother’s child only to lose it like this—how could Heaven be so cruel?
[ZH] 喜兰接过药，倒杯温水，到床边伺候王妃将药服下。
[EN] Pulling a vial of pills from his chest, the physician handed it to Xilan. “Give Her Highness one pill with warm water; it will stop the bleeding.”
[ZH] 大夫则坐到桌前开药方，边开边说：“我开的这方子可帮王妃清除女子胞内的残留之物，先服用三日，三日后，我再来诊脉。”
[EN] Xilan took the vial, poured warm water, and helped her mistress swallow the medicine.
[ZH] 待梦容服下丸药后，申简辰再次坐到床边，关切地问：“可好些？”
[EN] Moving to the table to write a prescription, the physician explained, “This formula clears any lingering residue inside the womb. Take it for three days, and I’ll return to check her pulse again.”
[ZH] 乔梦容点点头：“好多了。”
[EN] Once Mengrong swallowed the pill, Shen Jianchen sat back down by the bed and asked softly, “Feeling any better?”
[ZH] 申简辰拿起床边放着的巾帕帮梦容擦掉眼泪。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong nodded. “Much better.”
[ZH] 乔梦容埋怨自己太傻竟然不知道已有身孕，她想不通为何怀上后孩子会轻易小产？
[EN] Picking up a handkerchief from the bedside, Shen Jianchen gently wiped away her tears.
[ZH] 她带着疑惑向正在写药方的大夫问：“大夫，能否诊出我已有多久的身孕？”
[EN] Blaming herself for being too foolish even to notice she was expecting, Qiao Mengrong couldn’t understand why the baby had slipped away so easily.
[ZH] 大夫写好药方后递给一旁的邹嬷嬷：“按照方子抓药，还是一日两次。”
[EN] Turning toward the physician writing at the table, she asked, “Doctor, can you tell how far along I was?”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷接过，出去安排人抓药。
[EN] Finishing the prescription, the physician handed it to Matron Zou. “Have this filled—twice daily, same as before.”
[ZH] 大夫这才说：“回仪王妃，根据刚才诊脉的景况，我推算，您大概已有一个多月的身孕。”
[EN] Matron Zou took the slip and hurried out to send someone for the herbs.
[ZH] 乔梦容继续问：“难道是我的身体出现问题孩子才没保住？”
[EN] Only then did the physician reply, “To answer Princess Consort Yi: judging from your pulse, you were a little over one month along.”
[ZH] 大夫否认道：“仪王妃服用我开的药，体寒之症已好，按说怀上胎儿后不会轻易小产。据我推测，您是不知自己有身孕，动作太过剧烈，才没保住胎儿。”
[EN] “Was something wrong with my body that made me lose the child?”
[ZH] 乔梦容想起昨日在柳岸别馆为梁内侍跳舞，然后又被他一针一针扎在身上，她因疼痛撕心裂肺地喊叫，同时身体不断扭动，恐怕是因此才导致小产。
[EN] “No, Your Highness. After taking my earlier prescriptions, your internal cold had cleared; by rights, once conceived, a pregnancy should hold steady. My guess is that, unaware you were expecting, you engaged in overly violent exertion, which dislodged the fetus.”
[ZH] 悔恨、恐惧，加上痛苦，乔梦容的情绪显得很不稳定，再次抱住自己的脑袋，从靠着的枕头上溜下，背对着哥哥抽泣起来，边抽泣边说：“我恨自己！我恨自己！我恨自己！……”
[EN] Yesterday’s nightmare crashed straight back over Qiao Mengrong: dancing naked at Liu’an Villa before Imperial Attendant Liang, then screaming in agony and thrashing under his needle one stab after another—no wonder she had lost the baby!
[ZH] 申简辰见状，急切地问：“梦容，你怎么了？”
[EN] Guilt, terror, and torment snapped something inside her. Clasping her head again, she slid down off her propped pillows, turned her back to her brother, and wept uncontrollably, chanting over and over, “I hate myself! I hate myself! I hate myself…!”
[ZH] 乔梦容根本不理会哥哥，只顾一味自语：“我恨自己！我恨自己！……”
[EN] Alarmed, Shen Jianchen asked, “Mengrong, what’s wrong?”
[ZH] 大夫上前想要继续诊脉，乔梦容的手一直抱着脑袋不愿松开。
[EN] She ignored him completely, lost in her loop: “I hate myself! I hate myself…!”
[ZH] 申简辰一边哄，一边把梦容的手从她头上挪开，为防止梦容再次抽回手，便一直将她的手抓住。
[EN] When the physician stepped forward to feel her pulse again, Qiao Mengrong kept her arms locked tight around her head.
[ZH] 大夫上前将搭脉，感受片刻后说：“仪王妃心神不宁，近期不要受任何刺激，否则会导致神志不清。”
[EN] Coaxing her gently, Shen Jianchen pried her hands away and held her wrist firm so she couldn’t yank it back.
[ZH] 申简辰甚是诧异：“怎么会这样？”
[EN] The physician rested his fingers on her pulse for a moment, then drew back. “Her Highness’s mind is severely rattled. Keep any shocks or agitation away from her for the foreseeable future, or she risks losing her wits completely.”
[ZH] 因诧异，抓住梦容手臂的力度不由减弱。梦容趁机抽回手，继续抱住脑袋自语。
[EN] Shen Jianchen stared in shock. “How could it be that serious?”
[ZH] 大夫也难以判断，还是给出猜测：“仪王妃应该是受到惊吓，接着又失去孩子，心中难以接受，才会出现此等症状，小人调理妇人的疾病尚可，对神志方面的疾病并不擅长。”
[EN] Distracted by surprise, his grip loosened—and Mengrong wrenched her wrist free, clutching her head and muttering once more.
[ZH] “惊吓？”申简辰想不通，昨日梦容出去时还好好的，难道是外出参加宴会受到惊吓？
[EN] Unable to say for certain, the physician offered his best guess. “Her Highness must have suffered a severe fright, followed right away by losing the child—it’s more grief than her heart can bear. I can mend women’s physical ailments well enough, though disorders of the mind lie outside my skill.”
[ZH] “喜兰！”
[EN] “A fright?” Shen Jianchen couldn’t fathom how—Mengrong had left yesterday morning in fine spirits. Could something have terrified her at that ladies’ banquet?
[ZH] 喜兰立刻跪，她猜到王爷要问昨日之事，吓得连眼泪都不敢再流。
[EN] “Xilan!”
[ZH] “昨日仪王妃外出到底受到什么惊吓？”
[EN] Xilan dropped straight to her knees, guessing he was about to question her about yesterday and too terrified even to keep crying.
[ZH] 喜兰不敢照实说，担心仪王知道昨日之事会不要仪王妃：“没……没有受到任何惊吓，或许……或许是因为昨晚做噩梦给吓着，对，王妃昨晚做噩梦被吓醒，浑身是汗。”
[EN] “Tell me—what frightened Princess Consort Yi while she was out yesterday?”
[ZH] 申简辰难以置信，又问不出其他原因：“起来吧，好好照顾王妃。”
[EN] Dare she breathe the truth and risk His Highness casting her mistress aside? Xilan stammered, “N-nothing frightened her out there! Maybe… maybe it was a nightmare last night! Yes, yes—Her Highness woke up drenched in cold sweat from a terrible nightmare!”
[ZH] 大夫没有要走的意思，好像还有话要说，申简辰见状问道：“大夫可还有事？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen couldn’t quite buy it, yet saw no point pressing further right now. “Get up and look after your mistress properly.”
[ZH] 大夫行礼道：“王爷，借一步说话。”
[EN] Noticing the physician lingering as if holding something back, Shen Jianchen asked, “Is there something else, Doctor?”
[ZH] 两人来到屋外，大夫才大胆说：“怕王妃再受刺激，我不敢当着她的面说，可又需将实情告知。王妃能怀上孩子已是难得，头胎没保住，往后再难有身孕。”
[EN] The physician bowed. “A word outside, Your Highness.”
[ZH] 申简辰心里很不是滋味，仅仅是为梦容。她悉心照料赵朴，对方却离她而去；跟他这位重生的哥哥成亲，他心里却装着别人；好不容易怀孕，孩子没了，又因受到惊吓心神不宁。
[EN] Stepping outside the door, the physician spoke low. “I dared not say it in front of Her Highness for fear of shocking her further, yet you deserve the full truth. Conceiving this child was already hard-won; now that she’s miscarried her first pregnancy, it will be near impossible for her to conceive again.”
[ZH] 申简辰决定不管梦容变得如何，都要好好待她。
[EN] A heavy ache settled in Shen Jianchen’s chest—purely out of pity for Mengrong. She had nursed Zhao Pu hand and foot, only for Zhao Pu to vanish from the world; she married his reborn body, yet his heart belonged to another; she finally conceived, only to lose the baby and crumble under some sudden trauma.
[ZH] “您考虑周到，本王甚是感谢。不知临安哪位大夫擅长治疗神志方面疾病？还请指点一二。”
[EN] Right then Shen Jianchen resolved that, no matter what became of Mengrong, he would care for her and never abandon her.
[ZH] 大夫说：“城南有位郭大夫，擅长此等疾病，等王妃身体恢复，可将他请来诊治。”
[EN] “You were thoughtful to spare her. Thank you. Tell me, which physician in Lin’an excels at treating ailments of the mind?”
[ZH] “多谢。”申简辰谢过后，向屋内大声说，“喜兰，把大夫的医箱拿出来。”
[EN] “A Physician Guo in the southern quarter specializes in such cases. Once Her Highness regains her strength, you can send for him.”
[ZH] 喜兰提着医箱出来，脸上挂着泪痕。
[EN] “Much obliged.” Calling toward the doorway, Shen Jianchen ordered, “Xilan, bring out the doctor’s chest.”
[ZH] 申简辰走进屋内，梦容依旧抱着头，不断自语：“我恨自己，我恨自己……”
[EN] Xilan carried the chest outside, tear tracks still glistening on her cheeks.
[ZH] 申简辰坐在床边，俯下身，从背后抱住梦容：“不要恨自己，你很好，都是我的错。”
[EN] Stepping back inside, Shen Jianchen found Mengrong still hugging her head, whispering, “I hate myself, I hate myself…”
[ZH] 听到哥哥的安慰，乔梦容不再自语，放下抱在头上的双手，转过身，抱住哥哥：“哥哥答应我，别再喜欢她，只爱我一人。”
[EN] Sitting beside her, he leaned over and wrapped both arms around her from behind. “Stop blaming yourself. You’ve done nothing wrong—every bit of this is on me.”
[ZH] 为了不刺激到梦容，申简辰犹豫了一会儿，将自己想象成真正的赵朴：“我是赵朴，赵朴从来只爱梦容。”
[EN] At his comforting voice, her muttering stopped. Lowering her hands, she turned and buried her face against his chest. “Promise me, Brother—stop loving her. Love only me.”
[ZH] 乔梦容紧紧地抱住哥哥：“哥哥没骗我？”
[EN] Desperate not to set her off again, Shen Jianchen hesitated a heartbeat, then slipped into the role of the real Zhao Pu. “I am Zhao Pu, and Zhao Pu has only ever loved Mengrong.”
[ZH] “赵朴从来没骗过梦容。”想到床上的褥子已被血浸透，申简辰劝道，“我抱你去我们的屋子住，让喜兰把被褥重新更换一下。”
[EN] She squeezed him tighter. “You aren’t lying to me, Brother?”
[ZH] 乔梦容沉浸在刚才哥哥的话语中，点头答应。
[EN] “Zhao Pu has never lied to Mengrong.” Seeing the mattress pad beneath her stained dark with blood, he added softly, “Let me carry you back to our room while Xilan changes the bedding here.”
[ZH] 申简辰掀开被子，抱起梦容。刚走出几步，乔梦容想起身上的针眼儿，想到被针刺的过程，蜷缩在哥哥怀里，身体轻微哆嗦着。
[EN] Still soaking in his words, Qiao Mengrong nodded yes.
[ZH] “不……不……我不去哥哥的屋子，我就要……就要住这里。”
[EN] Drawing back the quilt, Shen Jianchen scooped her into his arms. Within three steps, memories of the needle punctures and sharp stabs flared across her skin; curling tight inside his embrace, she trembled slightly.
[ZH] 乔梦容的手拽住哥哥的衣衫，像是要拽住他的脚步一般。
[EN] “N-no… no… I’m not going to Brother’s room… I want… I want to stay right here.”
[ZH] 王爷抱起王妃时，喜兰已进来，开始收拾床铺，将血染红的被褥撤去，换上新的。
[EN] Her fingers clutched at his tunic as if trying to anchor his feet in place.
[ZH] 还没完全换好时，听到王妃的声音，反应过来，停下手中的活儿上前说：“王爷，就让王妃住在这里吧？她现在适合一个人住着静养，王爷若放不下王妃，可每日过来陪伴。”
[EN] Xilan had already walked back in to strip the blood-soaked bedding and lay fresh sheets. Hearing her mistress’s voice, she stopped mid-task and stepped forward. “Your Highness, why not let Her Highness stay here? She needs quiet to rest alone right now. Whenever you’re worried about her, you can come sit with her every day.”
[ZH] 喜兰说得有道理，申简辰不再继续往外走：“你快些把床铺收拾好，再帮王妃将身上清理干净，换身衣裳。”
[EN] Seeing the sense in that, Shen Jianchen set her gently back down. “Finish making up the bed quickly, clean Her Highness up, and change her into fresh clothes.”
[ZH] 很快，喜兰按照王爷的要求将一切收拾完毕。
[EN] Before long, Xilan had everything tidied and spotless.
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷用盘子端着一碗熬好的汤药进来：“白侍卫就是厉害，快马加鞭从城里最近的药房把药抓好，又快马加鞭赶回来，我才能及时让丫鬟将药煎好。”
[EN] Matron Zou carried in a tray bearing a steaming bowl of decoction. “Guard Bai doesn’t mess around—he rode full gallop to the nearest pharmacy in town and straight back, so I got the maids brewing this in no time.”
[ZH] 喜兰从盘子里端了药，来到床边。
[EN] Xilan lifted the bowl from the tray and brought it to the bedside.
[ZH] 申简辰正好与邹嬷嬷有话要说，主动让出位置。
[EN] Wanting a private word with Matron Zou anyway, Shen Jianchen stepped aside to yield his spot.
[ZH] 在喜兰伺候梦容吃药时，申简辰向邹嬷嬷示意出去说话。邹嬷嬷放下盘子，跟着王爷来到院中。
[EN] While Xilan spoon-fed the medicine to Mengrong, Shen Jianchen caught Matron Zou’s eye and gestured toward the yard. Setting down the tray, Matron Zou followed him outside.
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷问道：“王爷要吩咐什么？还需避开王妃？”
[EN] “What orders does Your Highness have that must be kept from Her Highness?”
[ZH] 申简辰叹口气：“唉，大夫说梦容在外受到惊吓，加之孩子没了，才心神不宁，若再受到刺激，恐怕会神志不清。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen let out a sigh. “The physician says Mengrong suffered a fright outside, and coupled with losing the child, her spirit is dangerously shaken. Any further shock could unhinge her mind.”
[ZH] “什么？怎么这么严重？”
[EN] “What? How did it get this grave?”
[ZH] “昨日梦容出去时还好好的，回来后躲在屋里不出来，我想应该是受到惊吓。喜兰不愿照实说，嬷嬷帮我私下问问底发生了什么？”
[EN] “Yesterday she went out perfectly fine, then came home and shut herself in here refusing to come out. Something must have terrified her. Xilan won’t tell me the truth—find a moment in private to get the real story out of her.”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷心中一惊，仪王妃昨日为王爷之事跑去见梁内侍，难道是在柳岸别馆受到惊吓？
[EN] Matron Zou’s heart skipped a beat. Yesterday Princess Consort Yi had gone to see Imperial Attendant Liang on Prince Yi’s behalf—had something happened at Liu’an Villa?
[ZH] “好，我帮王爷问问。”
[EN] “Understood. I’ll ask her for Your Highness.”
[ZH] “梦容这会儿好了许多，我避开片刻，嬷嬷好找机会询问喜兰。”
[EN] “Mengrong looks a bit calmer now. I’ll step out for a while so you have an opening to question Xilan.”
[ZH] 申简辰走出小院，留下邹嬷嬷一人站在院中想着刚才仪王说的话，怎么都无法相信一夜之间王妃会变得心神不宁。
[EN] Leaving the small courtyard, Shen Jianchen left Matron Zou standing in thought, struggling to fathom how the princess consort could unravel overnight.
[ZH] 她走进屋，喜兰还在一勺一勺伺候王妃吃药，她除了脸色苍白一些，看上去与正常人没有什么不同。
[EN] Walking back inside, she saw Xilan still spooning broth-dark medicine to Her Highness. Pale though she looked, she seemed no different from normal.
[ZH] 喜兰将最后一勺药喂完，用巾帕在王妃的嘴边轻沾几下，放好碗后，扶王妃躺下。
[EN] Feeding the final spoonful, Xilan dabbed the corners of her mistress’s mouth with a cloth, set the bowl aside, and eased her back onto the pillows.
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷上前试着问：“王妃，现在感觉如何？”
[EN] Matron Zou stepped closer and tested gently, “How are you feeling now, Your Highness?”
[ZH] 乔梦容像没听见似的，没有给予任何回应。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong stared blankly, offering no answer at all.
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷继续试着问：“王妃，可好些？”
[EN] Matron Zou tried again. “Your Highness, feeling any better?”
[ZH] 乔梦容这才将目光看向邹嬷嬷：“嬷嬷说什么？”
[EN] Only then did Qiao Mengrong turn her eyes toward her. “Did you say something, Matron?”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷担心泪水流出来被仪王妃瞧见，赶紧转过身：“没……没说什么，王妃睡会儿，养养精神。”
[EN] Fearing Her Highness would catch the tears brimming in her eyes, Matron Zou turned away fast. “N-nothing. Get some sleep and build back your strength, Your Highness.”
[ZH] 当她擦干眼泪，转过身时，仪王妃已闭上眼睛。
[EN] By the time she wiped her face dry and turned back, Princess Consort Yi had shut her eyes.
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷扯了一下喜兰的衣角，示意她出去说话。
[EN] Matron Zou tugged Xilan’s sleeve and motioned her outside.
[ZH] 两人出去后，将门从外面关好。
[EN] Stepping out, they shut the door behind them.
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷盯着喜兰厉声问：“老实交代，你跟随王妃去柳岸别馆到底发生了什么？”
[EN] Matron Zou fixed Xilan with a stern glare. “Confess every word! What happened at Liu’an Villa when you went with Her Highness?”
[ZH] 喜兰吓得跪下哭起来：“呜呜，嬷嬷，仪王妃不让我说，呜呜……！”
[EN] Dropping straight to her knees, Xilan burst into tears. “Wuu… Matron, Princess Consort Yi forbade me to breathe a word! Wuu…”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷将喜兰扶起来：“别哭，省得吵醒王妃。她的事我尽知，告诉我又何妨？也好多个人出主意。”
[EN] Matron Zou hauled Xilan up off the dirt. “Quit wailing before you wake Her Highness! I already know all her business—telling me won’t hurt, and two heads are better than one.”
[ZH] 喜兰一个人守着仪王妃的秘密心里也觉沉重，不如告诉邹嬷嬷，也好有个人帮着分担。
[EN] Carrying her mistress’s secret alone had crushed Xilan’s chest; sharing it felt like dropping half a mountain.
[ZH] 她止住哭声，小声说：“梁内侍就是个畜生，他不要银票却要王妃，还在她身上扎下许多针眼儿，我待在外面只听到王妃撕心裂肺的哭喊声，却无可奈何。王妃回来后怕王爷知道才躲到小院住，谁知今日竟然小产，恐怕也是昨日的经历所致。”
[EN] Choking back her sobs, she whispered, “Imperial Attendant Liang is an animal! He refused the silver notes and demanded Her Highness instead—even stabbed needles all over her skin! Standing outside, all I could hear were her bloodcurdling screams, and there wasn’t a thing I could do! She hid in this courtyard afterward terrified His Highness would find out. Who knew she’d miscarry today—yesterday’s ordeal must have caused it!”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷悔恨不已：“都怪我，为何要帮着打听梁内侍的住处？还支持王妃亲自登门。”
[EN] Bitter regret washed over Matron Zou. “Blame me! Why did I ever track down Liang’s address for her, and back her going there in person?”
[ZH] 喜兰叮嘱道：“该说的已说，还望嬷嬷不要将实情告知王爷，给王妃留些体面。”
[EN] Xilan pleaded urgently, “I’ve told you everything, Matron—please, whatever you do, don’t tell His Highness! Leave Her Highness a shred of dignity.”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷既怨自己，又心疼王妃，决定替王妃瞒着：“你回去守着王妃，悉心伺候，我去找王爷，给他一个解释。”
[EN] Torn between self-reproach and heartache for her mistress, Matron Zou resolved to keep the secret. “Go back in and watch over Her Highness attentively. I’ll go give His Highness a story.”
[ZH] 喜兰急忙拦住：“嬷嬷是要将实情告知吗？”
[EN] Xilan caught her arm. “You aren’t going to tell him the truth, are you?”
[ZH] “放心，一个字都不会说。王爷让我打听，总得给他一个合理的解释。”
[EN] “Relax—not a single syllable. He asked me to look into it, so I owe him a believable explanation.”
[ZH] “对了，还有一事需叮嘱，昨日王妃回来住进小院，我骗王爷王妃有带下之症，嬷嬷可别穿帮。”
[EN] “Oh—one more thing! When Her Highness moved into this courtyard yesterday, I lied to His Highness that she had a feminine discharge ailment. Don’t give me away.”
[ZH] “把心放在肚子里，为了王爷和王妃，我知道该怎么说。”邹嬷嬷叹口气，抹着眼泪出了小院。
[EN] “Set your heart at ease. For His Highness’s sake and hers, I know what to say.” Matron Zou sighed, wiped her cheeks, and walked out of the courtyard.

#CH 317
[ZH] 找到仪王时，他正站在木质高台上眺望神柳的方向。
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷顺着台阶爬到高处：“王爷，我问过喜兰，她担心被您批评没敢照实说，昨日仪王妃的确受到惊吓。”
[ZH] 申简辰转过身急切地问：“被什么吓着？”
[EN] She found Prince Yi standing atop the wooden platform, gazing off toward the Divine Willow.
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷已想好说辞：“参加完宴会，仪王妃到街上逛了一会儿，遇到两人打起来，动了刀子，还闹出人命来，才被吓着。您不记得，王妃自小胆子小，见不得打打杀杀，尤其见不得死人的场面。”
[EN] Matron Zou climbed the steps to join him. “Your Highness, I questioned Xilan. She was afraid you’d scold her, which is why she held back—Princess Consort Yi really did suffer a terrible fright yesterday.”
[ZH] 申简辰信以为真，他不熟悉小时候的梦容，更不会怀疑邹嬷嬷：“都怪我，如果亲自去接她，就不会发生这等事。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen spun around anxiously. “What scared her?”
[ZH] “王爷不必自责，一切都是命数，我只希望王爷和王妃以后都好好的。”邹嬷嬷说着眼泪又流了出来，王爷是骗过去，可王妃已自食其果，若王爷知道，恐怕会嫌弃。
[EN] Matron Zou rolled out her rehearsed story smoothly. “After the ladies’ gathering, Her Highness strolled down the street for a bit and stumbled right into two men hacking at each other with knives—one even died on the spot. You wouldn’t remember, Your Highness, yet since childhood Her Highness has been fainthearted; she can’t stand blades and bloodshed, especially seeing someone killed.”
[ZH] “嬷嬷不必难过，无论梦容变成什么样，我都不会舍她而去。”申简辰又看向神柳，仿佛在和心爱之人告别，“等梦容的身体恢复后，我打算回汴梁，已不是临安节度使，又何必赖在临安不走？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen bought every word. Having no memory of Mengrong’s childhood, he never doubted Matron Zou for a second. “It’s my fault. If I’d gone to pick her up myself, none of this would have happened.”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷十分认同：“王爷有此想法再好不过，汴梁的仪王府才是王爷的家。”
[EN] “Don’t blame yourself, Your Highness—everything runs by fate. All I pray for is that you and Her Highness stay well from here on.” Tears welled in Matron Zou’s eyes again; she’d fooled the prince, yes, yet Her Highness had brought ruin on herself—if His Highness ever learned the truth, he might loathe her forever.
[ZH] ……
[EN] “Don’t cry, Matron. No matter what happens to Mengrong, I’ll never cast her aside.” Shen Jianchen looked back toward the Divine Willow as if bidding farewell to the woman he loved. “Once Mengrong recovers, I plan to return to Bianliang. I’m no longer Military Commissioner of Lin’an anyway—what’s the point hanging around here?”
[ZH] 正当陈家茶庄的伙计忙碌时，林觉带着两名练兵官和许多士兵将茶庄包围。林觉则独自一人走入茶庄内。
[EN] Matron Zou nodded warmly. “You couldn’t make a wiser choice, Your Highness. Prince Yi’s Manor in Bianliang is your true home.”
[ZH] 陈江木正好在茶庄，见林统制带兵而来，甚是不解，迎上前：“林统制怎么有空来茶庄？还带这么多人？”
[EN] ...
[ZH] 以前朱晞颜在位，为巴结朱晞颜，林觉自然与陈家三位老爷保持良好的关系。现在朱晞颜不是临安知府，林觉也不会再将陈家当回事，拿出统制的架子：“有人向官府反映，陈家售卖假茶，新上任的李知府派我带人前来查验。”
[EN] Meanwhile, at the Chen family tea shop, while clerks bustled behind the counter, Lin Jue marched up with two military training officers and a platoon of soldiers to surround the building, then strode inside alone.
[ZH] 陈江木稍微适应了一会儿才想通林觉的变化，立刻示意管事拿银子。
[EN] Chen Jiangmu happened to be at the shop. Seeing Commander Lin arrive with armed troops, he stepped forward in bewilderment. “What brings Commander Lin to our tea shop with so many men?”
[ZH] 管事从柜台上拿出一袋银子，给了陈江木。
[EN] Back when Zhu Xiyan held office, Lin Jue bent over backward flattering all three Chen brothers to curry favor with the prefect. Now that Zhu Xiyan was out, Lin Jue dropped all pretense and put on his sharpest official airs. “Someone reported to the yamen that the Chen family is peddling counterfeit tea. Newly appointed Prefect Li sent me to seal and inspect the shop.”
[ZH] 陈江木接过，靠近林觉，悄悄塞进他的手中：“陈家茶庄在临安这么久，口碑如何，林统制还不清楚？恐怕是有人诬陷。”
[EN] It took Chen Jiangmu a beat to grasp the sudden change in tone, then he signaled the shop manager right away.
[ZH] 林觉接过银子，装进怀里，立即态度转变，拉着陈江木到一旁说话：“售卖假茶只是借口，我劝你们陈家还是到梁内侍跟前走动走动，否则明日我还得去绸缎庄说你们售卖假货。”
[EN] Pulling a pouch of silver from beneath the counter, the manager passed it to Chen Jiangmu.
[ZH] 陈江木不解：“为何梁内侍要针对陈家？”
[EN] Stepping close to Lin Jue, Chen Jiangmu slipped the pouch discreetly into his hand. “The Chen tea shop has traded in Lin’an for years—surely Commander Lin knows our reputation better than anyone? Somebody must be slandering us.”
[ZH] 林觉解释道：“具体什么原因我也说不准，只知梁内侍爱财，恐怕是想从陈家捞些钱财。”
[EN] Feeling the hefty pouch, Lin Jue tucked it straight into his robe—his attitude flipped in an instant. Drawing Chen Jiangmu aside, he murmured, “Counterfeit tea is just an excuse. Take my advice: go pay your respects to Imperial Attendant Liang sooner rather than later, or tomorrow I’ll be marching into your silk shop claiming you sell fake goods too.”
[ZH] 陈江木拱手道：“感谢林统制提点，我这就回去与大哥商议此事。”
[EN] Chen Jiangmu blinked. “Why is Imperial Attendant Liang targeting the Chen family?”
[ZH] 林觉拿了银子心满意足，带人离开。
[EN] “Can’t say the exact reason—only that Imperial Attendant Liang loves silver. Most likely he wants to squeeze a little profit out of the Chens.”
[ZH] 出发前，李知府叮嘱过，让他前去整顿陈家各处的生意，目的是逼得陈家主动前去拜见梁内侍。
[EN] Chen Jiangmu cupped his hands. “Thank you for the tip, Commander Lin. I’ll go talk this over with my eldest brother at once.”
[ZH] 李知府刚来，许多情况不了解，以为需大动干戈，殊不知他林觉出马两下便将事办好。他不敢与李知府多费口舌，主要是他还没摸清楚李知府的秉性，怕哪句话说错得罪人。
[EN] Pocketing his bribe with a pleased grin, Lin Jue marched his soldiers away.
[ZH] 把事办好才最重要，林觉相信不出今日，陈家肯定会去柳岸别馆拜访梁内侍。
[EN] Before setting out, Prefect Li had instructed him to shake down the Chen family’s shops until they came crawling to pay homage to Imperial Attendant Liang. Fresh in Lin’an and unfamiliar with local ways, Prefect Li expected a drawn-out fuss—little knowing Lin Jue could wrap it up cleanly in two moves. Lin Jue hadn’t argued with Prefect Li either; not knowing the new prefect’s temper yet, keeping his mouth shut kept him out of trouble.
[ZH] 陈江木匆忙回到陈府，将茶庄发生的事告知大哥陈江林。
[EN] Getting results mattered most. Lin Jue bet the Chen family would be knocking on Liu’an Villa’s door before nightfall.
[ZH] 陈江林仔细分析，也认为梁内侍不过是为钱财。可对方的胃口到底有多大？该带多少银票前去？陈江林不免犯难。他决定先向朱晞颜请教一番，毕竟他在官场混了许久，以前也曾巴结过梁内侍，应该了解他。
[EN] Hurrying back to Chen Manor, Chen Jiangmu told his eldest brother Chen Jianglin everything that happened at the tea shop.
[ZH] 当陈江林来到朱府时，朱晞颜正在书房写诗，无政务烦心，他这两日算是体会到无官一身轻的妙处。
[EN] Thinking it through, Chen Jianglin agreed Liang Shicheng likely just wanted silver—yet how huge was the man’s appetite? How much in silver notes should they bring? Stumped, he decided to consult Zhu Xiyan first; as a veteran official who had once buttered up Imperial Attendant Liang himself, his in-law would know the man’s measure.
[ZH] 亲家前来拜访，朱晞颜自然撂下笔热情接待。
[EN] Arriving at Zhu Manor, Chen Jianglin found Zhu Xiyan in his study composing poetry. Free from official headaches, the former prefect had spent the last two days savoring how light a man felt without a black gauze cap weighing on his head.
[ZH] 两人寒暄几句，陈江林说了林觉带人来茶庄一事。
[EN] Seeing his in-law arrive, Zhu Xiyan set down his brush and welcomed him warmly.
[ZH] 朱晞颜叹道：“总算看清林觉，当初不该把淑真嫁给他，应该直接嫁给浩宇。以前我在位时，天天拜见我，现在已两日未登门，听说又去巴结新来的李克。我不在位，没法护着陈家，他才敢带人闹事。”
