[EN] After brief pleasantries, Chen Jianglin recounted Lin Jue’s raid on the tea shop.
[ZH] 陈江林说：“官 场向来如此，人走茶凉。我倒觉得亲家现在挺好，不用操那么多心，逍遥自在。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan sighed. “At last I see Lin Jue’s true colors. I never should have married Shuzhen to him—I ought to have wed her straight to Haoyu from the start. While I held office, he called at my gate every single day. Two days out of office and he hasn’t shown his face once—I hear he’s already fawning over Li Ke instead. Because I can’t shield the Chen family anymore, he dares come make trouble at your door.”
[ZH] 朱晞颜“呵呵”笑道：“的确，为官时总觉得这也放不下，那也放不下，被别人逼得不得不放下时，才觉得什么都可以放下。我还真要好好感谢梁内侍，是他让我认清自己，原来我更喜欢现在的生活。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin shook his head. “That’s officialdom for you—the tea goes cold the moment the guest leaves. Honestly, In-law, you look much better off now, free of worries and living at ease.”
[ZH] 提到梁内侍，陈江林说出今日来此的目的：“林觉虽不再把陈家当回事，可走时也提点了几句。他说梁内侍贪财，恐怕是想从陈家得些钱财，我想明日带浩宇拜访梁内侍，只是不知该带多少银两前去，还望亲家指点一二。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan chuckled. “True enough. When you hold office, you think you can’t let go of this or that. Only when someone forces your hand do you realize you can let go of all of it—and breathe easier. Truthfully, I ought to thank Imperial Attendant Liang for helping me see I prefer life just like this.”
[ZH] “梁内侍的确贪财，而且胃口越来越大，恐怕几万两难以打发。”朱晞颜伸出五个指头，“得这个数。”
[EN] At the mention of Liang, Chen Jianglin stated his purpose. “Even though Lin Jue no longer shows us deference, he dropped a hint before leaving: Imperial Attendant Liang is greedy and wants a cut from the Chen family. Tomorrow I plan to take Haoyu to call on him, only I haven’t a clue how much silver to bring. Give me some guidance, In-law.”
[ZH] “五十万两？”
[EN] “Greedy doesn’t cover half of it—and his appetite keeps swelling. A few tens of thousands of taels won’t satisfy him.” Zhu Xiyan held up five fingers. “At least this much.”
[ZH] 朱晞颜点点头：“对。”
[EN] “Five hundred thousand taels?”
[ZH] “胃口的确大，就当破财消灾。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan nodded. “Yes.”
[ZH] “五十万两是多，可也值。”
[EN] “What a monster appetite! Well—call it buying peace to ward off disaster.”
[ZH] “亲家为何如此说？”
[EN] “Five hundred thousand taels of silver is steep, yet worth every coin.”
[ZH] 朱晞颜解释道：“梁内侍深得官家信任，在汴梁只手遮天，陈家用五十万两与他疏通关系，往后还愁汴梁的生意做不大？”
[EN] “How so, In-law?”
[ZH] 此事陈江林不是没想过，毕竟像梁内侍这等人也不是人人能够巴结得上，有些人即使想送钱也没有门路。人家主动派人上门来提点，说明瞧得上陈家。
[EN] “Liang Shicheng holds His Majesty’s absolute trust—one hand of his covers the sky in Bianliang. Use five hundred thousand taels to grease a connection with him, and you’ll never have to worry about expanding Chen businesses into the capital.”
[ZH] 可陈江林总觉得此事没有那么简单，他不敢奢望能跟梁内侍攀上关系为陈家开拓汴梁的市场，只求这尊大神拿了银子不要再找陈家的麻烦。
[EN] Chen Jianglin had actually considered that angle. Not just anyone could buy their way into Liang’s circle; plenty of merchants had silver and nowhere to send it. Sending a signal through Lin Jue meant Liang at least considered the Chens worth his time.
[ZH] “希望如亲家所言。”
[EN] Still, an odd unease nagged at Chen Jianglin. He didn’t dare dream of cracking the Bianliang market through Liang—he only prayed the lofty demon would pocket the silver and leave his family in peace.
[ZH] 回到陈府，陈江林派人把陈浩宇叫回，让去的人说家中有大事需要带他一起前去处理。
[EN] “Let’s hope it turns out just as you say, In-law.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇已经能独当一面，他每日会到陈家的店铺、茶楼、酒庄、饭庄等各处走一遭。陈江林说了，等儿子再学一年，他便回家什么都不管，每日只陪姚轻琰。
[EN] Back at Chen Manor, Chen Jianglin sent word summoning Chen Haoyu home, saying urgent family business required his presence.
[ZH] 陈浩宇也在想，他得赶紧生个儿子，等自己的儿子长大成人，也将手中的重任交了，他才能每时每刻陪伴娘子，与她共享天伦之乐。
[EN] Nowadays Chen Haoyu handled business on his own, making daily rounds through the family’s shops, teahouses, wine shops, and restaurants. Chen Jianglin had promised that after one more year of training, he himself would retire completely to spend every day with Yao Qingyan.
[ZH] 想到已答应娘子为她守身如玉两年，陈浩宇只好将生儿子的想法搁置一边。算算日子，还有不到一年半的时间，他很期待那一日快些到来。
[EN] Chen Haoyu had his own plans too: hurry up and have a son, raise him to take over the reins, and then spend every waking hour with his own wife in domestic bliss.
[ZH] 待陈浩宇回到家中，陈江林将今日发生的事说了，让儿子带够五十万两银票与他一同去拜见梁内侍。
[EN] Then he remembered his promise to keep himself chaste for her for two years—sighing, he shelved the thought of a son for now. Counting on his fingers, less than a year and a half remained, and he counted down the days with quiet longing.
[ZH] 一切准备好后，父子二人乘坐马车来到柳岸别馆。
[EN] Once Chen Haoyu reached home, Chen Jianglin explained the situation and told him to pack five hundred thousand taels in silver notes to accompany him to Liu’an Villa.
[ZH] 向门口的下人说明情况后，得到梁内侍允许，陈浩宇亲自抱着装有银票的箱子跟在父亲后面走入别馆内。
[EN] With everything ready, father and son rode their carriage straight to Liu’an Villa.
[ZH] 在下人的引领下来到会客厅，梁师成衣冠楚楚坐在正位品茶。
[EN] After stating their business at the gate and receiving permission, Chen Haoyu carried the wooden chest of silver notes himself behind his father as they stepped inside.
[ZH] 下人说：“内侍大人，陈家父子已到。”
[EN] Led into the reception hall, they saw Liang Shicheng sitting upright in proper robes at the seat of honor, sipping tea.
[ZH] 没等梁内侍说话，陈江林跪下，陈浩宇见父亲跪下，也抱着箱子跪下。
[EN] The attendant announced, “Lord Attendant, Master Chen and his son have arrived.”
[ZH] “拜见内侍大人。”
[EN] Before Liang could speak, Chen Jianglin dropped to his knees; seeing his father kneel, Chen Haoyu knelt beside him, chest still cradled in his arms.
[ZH] “快快请起。”梁师成放下茶盏，面露假笑，“给陈家父子看座。”
[EN] “We pay our respects to the Lord Attendant.”
[ZH] 父子二人起来，在下人的引领下坐在一旁的椅子上。
[EN] “Up, up, quickly,” Liang Shicheng said, setting down his tea bowl with a painted smile. “Seats for Master Chen and his son.”
[ZH] 梁师成向下人挥手，示意他离开。
[EN] Father and son rose and took seats along the side.
[ZH] 梁师成先是一番夸赞：“早听说陈家是临安首富，为临安百姓做了不少事，算得上义商，等我回到汴梁定要在官家面前替陈家美言几句。”
[EN] With a flick of his fingers, Liang Shicheng dismissed the attendant.
[ZH] 陈江林一时还听不出梁内侍的言外之意：“能得梁内侍夸赞，陈家面上有光。首富不敢当，不过是老百姓给的虚名而已。官家治理的天下一向太平，陈家才能有机会做些买卖。既挣了银子，自然要懂得回馈官家的百姓，只当是为官家做些事。”
[EN] He opened with generous praise. “I’ve long heard the Chen family are the wealthiest merchants in Lin’an—and righteous merchants at that, doing much good for the local people. When I return to Bianliang, I’ll be sure to put in a good word for your family before His Majesty.”
[ZH] 梁师成心中赞叹，陈家老爷实在会说话，他一时找不出毛病，目光不由落在陈浩宇的身上：“这位是？”
[EN] Not catching the hidden hook yet, Chen Jianglin bowed his head modestly. “Praise from Imperial Attendant Liang honors our house beyond measure. ‘Wealthiest in Lin’an’ is merely empty flattery from neighbors. Only because His Majesty governs a peaceful realm can the Chens trade at all. Having earned a modest living, giving back to His Majesty’s subjects is simply doing our small bit for the throne.”
[ZH] 陈江林赶紧介绍：“我的儿子陈浩宇？”
[EN] Liang Shicheng marveled silently—*this old merchant knows how to talk*; not a single flaw to poke at. His gaze shifted onto Chen Haoyu. “And this young gentleman is?”
[ZH] “可是他娶了朱晞颜的女儿，也就是江南有名的女词人？”
[EN] Chen Jianglin hurried to introduce him. “My son, Chen Haoyu.”
[ZH] “正是。”
[EN] “Isn’t he the one who married Zhu Xiyan’s daughter—that famed Poetess of Jiangnan?”
[ZH] 梁师成继续夸赞：“陈公子好福气。”
[EN] “The very same.”
[ZH] 面对夸赞，陈浩宇不知该如何回应。陈江林悄悄给儿子使眼色，陈浩宇意识到是该献上银票的时候。
[EN] Liang Shicheng smiled slyly. “Young Master Chen is blessed with rare good fortune.”
[ZH] 他抱着木箱起身，来到梁师成面前：“内侍大人从汴梁来到临安一路辛苦，小小礼物不成敬意，还望大人笑纳。”
[EN] Caught off guard by the compliment, Chen Haoyu didn’t know what to say. Seeing his father’s subtle glance, he realized it was time to present the chest.
[ZH] 梁师成盯着眼前陈浩宇递上来的木箱，从大小判断，应该装了不少银票，少说也有几十万两，他的确动心。想到仪王妃将他伺候得挺开心，既然答应了，再多银子也不能要，还是往后再想办法从陈家手中把银子搞来。
[EN] Standing with the wooden box in his hands, he stepped before Liang Shicheng. “You traveled far from Bianliang to Lin’an, Lord Attendant. A trifling token to show our respect—please accept it with a smile.”
[ZH] “呵呵呵……，让陈家费心，礼物还是收回去，我替官家办事，就是替百姓办事，岂能随意收取百姓的礼物？”
[EN] Eyeballing the chest held out before him, Liang Shicheng judged by its size it held a staggering stack of silver notes—easily hundreds of thousands of taels. Tempted he certainly was. Yet remembering how thoroughly Princess Consort Yi had serviced him yesterday, a deal was a deal; no matter how sweet the silver, he couldn’t take it today. He’d squeeze the Chens dry another day.
[ZH] 陈浩宇一时愣住，一路而来从未想过是这等结果，托着木箱的手已伸出，伸着也不是，收回也不是。
[EN] “Hehehe… you Chens are far too thoughtful, though take your gift back. Serving His Majesty means serving the people—how could I pocket gifts straight out of ordinary citizens’ hands?”
[ZH] 陈江林也一时懵住，以为梁内侍嫌钱少，于是站起来行礼道：“若礼物不合内侍大人心意，陈家会重新准备。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu froze on the spot. Never in a hundred guesses had he expected this—stuck holding the chest mid-air, awkward whether he held it out or pulled it back.
[ZH] 梁师成站起来，将陈浩宇托着的木箱推回：“礼物我不要，人我却要见。”
[EN] Stunned too, Chen Jianglin thought the eunuch deemed the sum too stingy. Jumping up, he bowed deep. “If this humble gift falls short of your expectations, Lord Attendant, the Chen family will gladly prepare more.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇收回木箱，大胆问：“不知内侍大人想见何人？”
[EN] Rising from his seat, Liang Shicheng pushed the wooden chest firmly back into Chen Haoyu’s hands. “I don’t want your gift. I want to meet someone.”
[ZH] “你的夫人，江南第一女词人。”
[EN] Pulling the chest back, Chen Haoyu forced himself to ask, “Whom does the Lord Attendant wish to see?”
[ZH] 父子两个甚是诧异。
[EN] “Your wife—the foremost Poetess of Jiangnan.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇赶紧拒绝：“小人的娘子虽是女词人，可也是足不出户的妇人，不值得大人接见。”
[EN] Father and son stared in shock.
[ZH] 陈江林也说：“淑真是女子，没见过什么大场面，恐内侍大人见了会失望。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu refused fast. “Though my wife writes ci poetry, she’s merely a retiring housewife who rarely steps past her gate—hardly worthy of your audience, Lord Attendant.”
[ZH] 父子两个一起推脱，梁师成不满道：“给你们两日时间，两日后若不让女词人来见我，我不敢保证陈家能继续成为临安首富，恐怕不出几日，陈家在临安以及各地的生意再难做下去。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin chimed in, “Shuzhen is only a woman who has never seen grand society. Were you to meet her, Lord Attendant, you’d only be disappointed.”
[ZH] 梁内侍如此威胁，恐怕淑真见梁内侍凶多吉少，父子二人不愿说出答应的话。
[EN] Seeing both men dodge him together, Liang Shicheng’s face hardened. “I’ll give you two days. If the poetess doesn’t show up at my door in two days, I won’t guarantee how long the Chens stay Lin’an’s richest family. Give it a few more days after that, and every Chen enterprise across Lin’an and beyond can close its doors for good.”
[ZH] 梁师成见父子二人不说话，对外喊道：“送客！”
[EN] Hearing that cold threat, both men knew meeting Liang Shicheng could cost Shuzhen her life—neither dared breathe a word of agreement.
[ZH] 梁师成甩了甩衣袖拐到屏风后面，留下父子二人继续傻站着。
[EN] Seeing them silent, Liang Shicheng shouted toward the doors, “See our guests out!”
[ZH] 直到下人进来说：“二位请回。”
[EN] With a sharp flick of his sleeve, he vanished behind a standing screen, leaving father and son standing there stunned.
[ZH] 两人才反应过来，陈浩宇继续抱着木箱跟在父亲身后。
[EN] Only when an attendant stepped in saying, “Please leave, sirs,” did they snap out of it, Chen Haoyu still clutching the chest as he trailed behind his father.
[ZH] 父子二人坐在马车里沉默许久，心中思忖着为何梁内侍非要见淑真？
[EN] Inside the carriage, silence hung heavy for a long stretch as both tried to fathom why Liang Shicheng insisted on seeing Shuzhen.
[ZH] 陈浩宇没忍住问道：“爹，为何梁内侍不贪财，反而要见我娘子？”
[EN] Unable to hold it in, Chen Haoyu burst out, “Father, why would Imperial Attendant Liang turn down all that silver and demand to see my wife instead?”
[ZH] 陈江林给出一个他认为比较合理的解释：“官家历来喜欢文人才子，最是重视写诗作词之人，恐怕因为这个梁内侍才提出面见淑真。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin offered the most sensible guess he could find. “His Majesty has always cherished literary men and poets—he prizes writers of shi and ci above most. Likely that’s why Imperial Attendant Liang asked to meet Shuzhen.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇难以认可：“想见我娘子，举办诗会即可，只要给东方公子说一声，凭东方诗会的影响，我怎么可能不让娘子参加？可他却要单独见我娘子，只怕另有所图。爹，梁内侍该不会是看上我娘子？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu shook his head hard. “If he merely wanted to meet her, all he had to do was host a poetry banquet! One word to Young Master Dongfang, with his salon’s clout, and could I possibly stop my wife from attending? Why demand to see my wife alone? He’s clearly up to something crooked! Father—could Imperial Attendant Liang have designs on my wife?”
[ZH] 陈江林立刻否认：“他是内侍，算不上真正的男人，应该不会对女子动心。即便他还有那心思，在京城什么姑娘没见过？不至于跑到临安来抢别人家的娘子，传出去有失体面。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin dismissed the thought at once. “He’s a palace eunuch, not even a whole man—why would he feel desire for a woman? Even if he kept some twisted itch, what beauties hasn’t he seen in the capital? He’d hardly travel all the way to Lin’an just to snatch another man’s wife and make himself a laughingstock.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇还是担心：“我娘子人长得美，又有才华，谁见了都容易动心，我绝对不能让她去见梁内侍。还有两日时间，一定能想出办法，既能保住陈家生意，也能避免娘子面见梁内侍。”
[EN] Still, Chen Haoyu’s gut twisted with dread. “My wife is gorgeous and brilliant—anyone who lays eyes on her risks losing his head. No matter what, I won’t let her walk through his door! We have two days. We’ll find a way to save the family’s business and keep my wife far away from Liang Shicheng.”
[ZH] 陈江林叮嘱道：“此事先瞒着大家，我找你岳父大人商议一下，你回家将银票放好后去拜见仪王，看他有没有办法。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin instructed, “Keep this quiet from the household for now. I’ll go consult your father-in-law right away. Take the silver notes home, lock them up safe, then ride out to see Prince Yi and ask if he has a way.”
[ZH] 从心里来讲，娘子的事陈浩宇是一万个不愿麻烦仪王，可像梁内侍那样的人，除了官家，也只有仪王能够撼动。
[EN] In his heart of hearts, Chen Haoyu hated turning to Prince Yi over his wife’s troubles—yet against a monster like Liang Shicheng, aside from His Majesty, only Prince Yi had any chance of shaking him.
[ZH] 陈家的马车先去朱府，将陈江林放下后，继续回陈府。
[EN] First the Chen carriage stopped at Zhu Manor to drop off Chen Jianglin before rolling home.
[ZH] 一日之内，陈江林第二次前来拜见，朱晞颜甚觉纳闷。
[EN] Seeing Chen Jianglin return a second time in one day baffled Zhu Xiyan.
[ZH] “亲家怎么又来？快过来坐，一会儿留下吃晚饭。”
[EN] “Back again so soon, In-law? Come sit—stay for dinner later.”
[ZH] 陈江林坐下，顾不上其他，急忙说：“我和浩宇带着五十万两银票前去拜访梁内侍，那么多银票他竟然不要，反而提出要见淑真，根本不符合常理，我与浩宇十分担心，让他去找仪王帮忙，我则前来与亲家商量一下。”
[EN] Sitting down, Chen Jianglin wasted zero breath. “Haoyu and I took five hundred thousand taels in silver notes to call on Imperial Attendant Liang. He wouldn’t even touch all that silver—instead he demanded to see Shuzhen! Nothing about it makes sense. Haoyu and I are worried sick. I sent him to ask Prince Yi for help while I came to talk it over with you.”
[ZH] 朱晞颜气得一拳砸在桌上：“实在可恶！”桌上的茶盏跟着晃动几下，茶水溢出杯中，两人都没有心情理会。
[EN] Furious, Zhu Xiyan slammed a fist onto the table. “Outrageous!” The tea bowls rattled so hard tea sloshed over the rims, though neither man noticed.
[ZH] 朱晞颜继续说：“我曾听人说过，梁内侍好女色，尤其喜欢成过亲的女子，他竟然将算盘打到我女儿头上，是可忍孰不可忍！”
[EN] “I’ve heard rumors before—Liang Shicheng craves women, and worst of all, he has a sick taste for married women! Now he’s eyeing my own daughter?! How dare he!”
[ZH] 看来浩宇猜得没错，陈江林更是担忧：“既如此，绝不能让淑真去见他。亲家，你说该如何是好？”
[EN] So Haoyu’s worst fear was dead on! Panic gripped Chen Jianglin. “Then under no circumstances can we let Shuzhen go see him! In-law, what on earth do we do?”
[ZH] 朱晞颜说：“天快黑，此时登门拜访不甚合适，明日一早我亲自前去找梁师成，当面问问他为何要见我女儿？”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan’s eyes narrowed. “Dusk is falling; calling on him tonight looks wrong. First thing tomorrow morning, I’ll go face Liang Shicheng myself and demand to know why he insists on seeing my daughter!”
[ZH] 陈江林站起来拱手道：“拜托亲家。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin stood and cupped his hands. “I’m counting on you, In-law.”
[ZH] “一家人不说两家话。”朱晞颜也没有心情再提留人用晚饭的事，亲自将陈江林送至门外，然后坐在屋内思忖明日见到梁师成该如何应对。
[EN] “We’re one family—no need for such words.” With no heart left for dinner invitations, Zhu Xiyan saw Chen Jianglin straight to the gate, then paced back inside to plan how he would confront Liang Shicheng at dawn.

#CH 318
[ZH] 陈浩宇回到陈府，到兴隆阁将一箱子银票交给方管事后，急急匆匆赶往幽林别居。
[ZH] 到达时天已黑，门口的下人说，这个点儿仪王正陪王妃用晚饭，让他在门外稍候。
[EN] Back at Chen Manor, Chen Haoyu handed the chest of silver notes to Steward Fang at Xinglong Pavilion, then hurried straight out to Secluded Forest Villa.
[ZH] 为了让梦容心情好些，申简辰在她的屋内用饭。
[EN] Night had fallen by the time he arrived. A gatekeeper informed him Prince Yi was taking dinner with Princess Consort Yi and told him to wait outside.
[ZH] 喜兰负责将王妃扶起，待王妃靠好后，给她一勺一勺地喂。
[EN] Hoping to lift Mengrong’s spirits, Shen Jianchen ate in her room.
[ZH] 哥哥陪在屋内，乔梦容是知道的。因身体原因，更因昨日的经历，她提不起精神与哥哥说话。
[EN] Xilan helped prop Princess Consort Yi upright against the pillows, then fed her patiently one spoonful at a time.
[ZH] 想到大夫说梦容不能再受任何刺激，申简辰尽量不说话，他担心哪句话说错会加重梦容的病情。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong knew her brother sat beside her, yet between her weakened body and yesterday’s horrors, she couldn’t muster the energy to speak a single word to him.
[ZH] 用完晚饭后，丫鬟将碗筷撤去，申简辰才坐到床边，声音尽量放得温柔：“晚上要不要我留下陪你？”
[EN] Remembering the physician’s warning against any agitation, Shen Jianchen kept silent too, terrified one careless remark might worsen her state.
[ZH] 乔梦容的表情本来有些愣，听哥哥这么一问，她使劲儿摇头：“不，哥哥别陪我，快回你的屋去。”
[EN] After dinner, once the maids cleared the dishes, Shen Jianchen sat on the bed’s edge and kept his voice feather-soft. “Would you like me to stay with you tonight?”
[ZH] 梦容的表现的确与以往不同，申简辰感到心酸，在梦容的脸上轻轻吻了一下。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong’s blank gaze flickered. At the question, she shook her head violently. “No! Don’t stay with me, Brother—go back to your own room!”
[ZH] 感受哥哥的吻，乔梦容本应开心，可她想起梁内侍如何吻在她的脸上和身上，恐惧再次袭来，抱着头缩成一团，颤抖着。
[EN] Seeing her behave so unlike herself pained Shen Jianchen. Leaning forward, he pressed a light kiss onto her cheek.
[ZH] 申简辰见状关切地问：“梦容，你怎么了？是不是害怕？”
[EN] At one time, a kiss from her brother would have sent Mengrong soaring. Instead, memories of Imperial Attendant Liang kissing her face and body hit like lightning; clutching her head, she curled into a tight ball, trembling uncontrollably.
[ZH] 乔梦容不断重复：“我要回家，我要回家……”
[EN] Alarmed, Shen Jianchen asked, “What’s wrong, Mengrong? Are you frightened?”
[ZH] “你已经回到家里。”
[EN] She chanted over and over, “I want to go home, I want to go home…”
[ZH] 听到哥哥的提醒，乔梦容安静下来，放下手臂，不再缩成一团。
[EN] “You are already home.”
[ZH] 申简辰很自责，刚才不该亲 吻她。见梦容状态好些，扶她躺下，给她盖好被子才离开。
[EN] At his gentle reminder, Qiao Mengrong quieted, lowered her arms, and uncurled slowly.
[ZH] 刚回到自己的院子，白风前来禀报：“王爷，陈浩宇跑来找您，在大门外等了一会儿。”
[EN] Blaming himself for kissing her without thinking, Shen Jianchen waited until she looked calmer, helped her lie back down, tucked the quilt snugly around her, and stepped out.
[ZH] “让他进来。”申简辰的心情很不好，官职要不要无所谓，梦容突然变成这样，让他始料未及。
[EN] No sooner had he reached his own courtyard than Bai Feng reported, “Your Highness, Chen Haoyu came looking for you—he’s been waiting outside the gate for a while.”
[ZH] 他不想回屋，站在院子，吹着秋风，希望秋风能一扫他心头的阴霾。
[EN] “Let him in.” Shen Jianchen was already in a foul mood; losing an official post meant nothing, yet Mengrong’s sudden collapse caught him completely off guard.
[ZH] 陈浩宇走进来，二话不说跪在地上：“仪王，求求您救救我娘子，救救陈家。”
[EN] Too restless to go indoors, he stood out in the yard letting the crisp autumn wind blow through him, hoping it might clear the gloom sitting on his chest.
[ZH] 申简辰已不顾上心头的阴霾：“她怎么了？”
[EN] Striding in, Chen Haoyu dropped straight to his knees on the dirt without greeting. “Prince Yi! I beg you—save my wife, save the Chen family!”
[ZH] “梁内侍找陈家麻烦，我爹想到破财消灾的办法，与我带着银票前去拜访。结果他连五十万两银票都不要，只要求见我娘子，肯定没安好心，还说给陈家两日时间，若两日后不让我娘子去见他，陈家所有的生意别想做下去。我没有办法，才来请王爷帮忙。”
[EN] All personal gloom vanished instantly. “What happened to her?”
[ZH] “她可知此事？”
[EN] “Imperial Attendant Liang started harassing our businesses. Father thought we could buy peace, so we carried silver notes to call on him—only he turned down five hundred thousand taels flat and demanded to see my wife instead! He’s clearly up to no good! Worse, he gave us two days: if my wife doesn’t go see him in two days, every Chen shop is finished! Out of options, I came begging Your Highness for help.”
[ZH] “还不知，不能告诉我娘子，她若知道肯定会冲到柳岸别馆揍梁内侍，只怕到时会更麻烦。”
[EN] “Does she know?”
[ZH] “我这就去会会梁师成。”申简辰撇下跪在地上的陈浩宇，带着白风出了幽林别居。
[EN] “Not yet—and we can’t breathe a word to her! Knowing her, if she finds out, she’ll march straight into Liu’an Villa and beat Liang Shicheng black and blue, making everything ten times worse!”
[ZH] 仪王毫不犹豫答应，按说陈浩宇应该高兴才对，可他根本高兴不起来。仪王碰到他娘子的事如此心急，足以证明他心里还装着他的娘子。
[EN] “I’ll go pay Liang Shicheng a visit myself.” Leaving Chen Haoyu kneeling in the dirt, Shen Jianchen swept straight out of Secluded Forest Villa with Bai Feng at his heels.
[ZH] 陈浩宇在地上坐了一会儿，才起身回府。
[EN] By rights, seeing Prince Yi jump up without hesitation should have cheered Chen Haoyu—yet his heart only sank heavier. How desperately Prince Yi reacted the second anything touched his wife proved just how deeply the man still held her in his heart.
[ZH] 申简辰与白风各骑一匹骏马向城内飞奔……
[EN] Chen Haoyu sat stunned on the flagstones for a long moment before climbing to his feet and heading home.
[ZH] 到达柳岸别馆外，白风将马拴在隐蔽处，与王爷一起用轻功翻墙而入。
[EN] Meanwhile, Shen Jianchen and Bai Feng galloped two swift steeds straight toward the city.
[ZH] 见一伙侍卫经过，两人躲在树后。通过侍卫的衣着判断，应是从汴梁皇宫带来的。能将官家身边的侍卫带到临安，足以证明官家有多信任这位奸臣。
[EN] Outside Liu’an Villa, Bai Feng tethered the horses out of sight, and the two used light-foot technique to vault over the perimeter wall.
[ZH] 申简辰本可光明正大见梁师成，可若那样梁师成会有所准备，便很难看出他的真实面目，也难以判断他逼迫陈家只为见幽栖居士的真实目的。
[EN] Spotting a squad of patrol guards approaching, they ducked behind a cluster of trees. One glance at the guards’ uniforms told Shen Jianchen they had come straight from the Bianliang palace—bringing imperial guards all the way to Lin’an proved just how deep His Majesty’s trust in this crooked eunuch ran.
[ZH] 为避开巡逻侍卫，两人瞅准时机，用轻功上了屋顶。他们在屋顶穿梭，听到一阵乐曲的声音，判断梁师成肯定在传来乐曲声的房中。
[EN] Shen Jianchen could have marched up to the front door openly, yet doing so would put Liang Shicheng on guard, hiding his true colors and his real motive for forcing the Chen family to hand over Youqi Jushi.
[ZH] 他们来到传出乐曲之音的屋顶，掀开几片瓦，透过屋顶的缝隙向里瞧，果不其然梁师成坐在屋内，边饮美酒，边欣赏舞曲。
[EN] Evading the patrols, the two slipped onto the rooftops the first clear chance they got. Moving silently across the tiles, they caught the drift of music floating up from below and guessed Liang Shicheng must be inside that hall.
[ZH] 跳舞的是一群衣着暴露的女子，比春香楼的商妓穿得还露骨。
[EN] Reaching the roof above the music, they lifted two clay tiles and peered down through the gap. Sure enough, Liang Shicheng lounged indoors, sipping fine wine while watching dancers perform.
[ZH] 白风小声嘀咕：“已入秋，都不怕冷，穿得那么少。”
[EN] The women dancing wore sheer, skimpy garments far bolder than even the courtesans at Chunxiang Brothel.
[ZH] 申简辰没有搭话，继续观察。
[EN] Bai Feng muttered under his breath, “It’s already autumn—aren’t they freezing wearing next to nothing like that?”
[ZH] 一曲舞跳完后，梁师成挥手让乐师退去，剩下几名衣着暴露的女子。
[EN] Shen Jianchen didn’t answer, keeping his eyes locked below.
[ZH] “把人带进来。”
[EN] When the number ended, Liang Shicheng waved the musicians out, leaving only the scantily clad women behind.
[ZH] 梁师成话音刚落，门被打开，走进一名女子。
[EN] “Bring the newcomer in.”
[ZH] 白风惊讶之余，小声说：“我认得，她是林统制的妾室，原本也是商妓，叫什么来着？”
[EN] At his order, the door swung wide and a woman stepped inside.
[ZH] 申简辰小声回道：“春金巧。”
[EN] Startled, Bai Feng whispered, “I recognize her! She’s Commander Lin’s concubine—the former courtesan! What was her name again?”
[ZH] “对，春金巧，她怎么会在这里？”
[EN] “Chun Jinqiao,” Shen Jianchen murmured back.
[ZH] 白风的问题也正是申简辰心中的疑惑，两人继续向屋内瞧。
[EN] “Right, Chun Jinqiao! What on earth is she doing here?”
[ZH] 只听梁师成说：“还是林统制最懂事，前途无量啊。”
[EN] Bai Feng’s question echoed Shen Jianchen’s own bewilderment as both kept watching below.
[ZH] 春金巧被送来伺候梁内侍，心中自然不快，可架不住林觉一番好言相劝。
[EN] Downstairs, Liang Shicheng chuckled, “Commander Lin really knows how to read the room—bright future ahead for that boy.”
[ZH] 林觉说，像梁内侍那样的人，林家所有家财拿出来贿赂，人家未必看在眼里，送女人的办法再好不过。
[EN] Chun Jinqiao hadn’t been thrilled about servicing Imperial Attendant Liang either, yet Lin Jue’s smooth talking had won her over.
[ZH] 春金巧问，为何不送白梅若偏偏选她？
[EN] As Lin Jue put it, emptying the entire Lin treasury wouldn’t even make a dent in Imperial Attendant Liang’s eyes; sending a woman was the smartest ticket forward.
[ZH] 林觉解释道，白梅若样貌比不上春金巧，床上功夫更与她没法比。只要把梁内侍伺候好，不仅他林觉的仕途会好起来，就连春金巧本人也会受益匪浅，这么好的事为何不去？
[EN] When Chun Jinqiao asked why he didn’t send Bai Meiruo instead of her, Lin Jue flattered her flat out: Bai Meiruo couldn’t hold a candle to her face, let alone her skill in bed. Once she kept Imperial Attendant Liang thoroughly pleased, not only would his own career shoot sky-high, she’d reap endless rewards too—so why pass up a golden deal?
[ZH] 春金巧问对她有什么好处？林觉说往后她在临安想有多尊贵就有多尊贵，连新来的知府大人见了她恐怕都得礼让三分，更别说临安那些贵妇，往后肯定不敢嘲笑她商妓的身份。
[EN] Asking what she’d get out of it, she heard Lin Jue promise she’d become the most respected woman in all of Lin’an—even the new Prefect would step aside politely at the sight of her, and those snooty noblewomen who looked down on her courtesan past wouldn’t dare sneer again.
[ZH] 春金巧一想，以前是商妓时陪的人不少，现在多陪一个也没什么，重要的是好处多多，主君还满心支持。于是被林觉送到柳岸别馆外，然后只身一人前来献身。
[EN] Thinking it over, Chun Jinqiao figured she’d entertained plenty of men in her brothel days anyway; one more wouldn’t kill her, especially with juicy rewards waiting and her own master pushing her straight through the door. So Lin Jue had dropped her outside Liu’an Villa, leaving her to walk in alone and offer herself up.
[ZH] 梁师成话音刚落，春金巧开始发挥商妓的本事，主动将外衣脱掉，露出香肩，用柔美的眼神挑 逗梁师成：“内侍大人想让奴家如何陪您？尽管吩咐。”
[EN] The moment Liang Shicheng finished speaking, Chun Jinqiao switched into full courtesan mode: sliding off her outer robe to bare smooth shoulders, she cast a coy, sultry glance his way. “Tell your servant how you’d like to be entertained tonight, Lord Attendant.”
[ZH] 梁师成满脸淫笑：“呵呵呵……，第一次见像你这么主动的，如若每个陪我的女子都像你这样该多好，会省去我不少麻烦。”
[EN] Liang Shicheng grinned lewdly. “Hehehe… first time I’ve met one so eager. If every woman who waited on me were half as cooperative as you, it’d save me a world of bother.”
[ZH] 屋顶处，白风轻声骂道：“不要脸，就不怕被林统制知道？”
[EN] Up on the roof, Bai Feng hissed quietly, “Shameless hussy! Aren’t you scared Commander Lin will find out?”
[ZH] 申简辰说：“难道没听出来？是林觉把她送来的。”
[EN] “Didn’t you hear?” Shen Jianchen murmured. “Lin Jue sent her here himself.”
[ZH] 白风惊讶地张大嘴巴，半天才合上。他今日算是见识有些人为个人利益什么事都能做得出，甚至连自己的女人都能心甘情愿往外送。
[EN] Bai Feng’s jaw dropped open and stayed stuck for three seconds. Tonight he truly learned how low men could sink for promotion—some would happily gift-wrap their own women and hand them over.
[ZH] 梁师成说：“既然你已经脱去外衣，不如把衣裳全脱掉，学着她们为我舞上一曲。”
[EN] “Since you’ve taken off your outer robe,” Liang Shicheng said, “strip the rest off too and dance a tune for me like those girls.”
[ZH] “这有何难？我在春香楼时，为取悦顾客，不知跳过多少回，肯定比她们几个跳得好，只是从来没体验过不穿衣裳跳的感觉，今日不妨体验一回。”春金巧夹着嗓子说完，开始一件一件脱。
[EN] “What’s hard about that? Back at Chunxiang Brothel, I danced more rounds than I can count—I’ll outdance all of them. Only I’ve never tried dancing stark naked before; tonight seems a fine time to try.” Squeezing her voice syrupy-sweet, Chun Jinqiao peeled off one garment after another.
[ZH] 梁师成双眼泛着红光，笑得甚是开心：“好！好！真是个难得的尤 物！”
[EN] Eyes flashing red with excitement, Liang Shicheng laughed aloud. “Good! Good! What a rare little treasure!”
[ZH] 白风不好意思起来：“我还没成亲，还是不看得好。”
[EN] Bai Feng squirmed uncomfortably. “I’m not even married yet—better not watch this.”
[ZH] 申简辰懒得理会白风，继续向内瞧，他担心梁师成提出见幽栖居士也是为了见不得人的目的。
[EN] Ignoring Bai Feng, Shen Jianchen kept staring closely, fearing Liang Shicheng’s demand to see Youqi Jushi hid some equally depraved scheme.
[ZH] 白风挪开目光后，见王爷看得仔细，经不住好奇心驱使，继续看去。
[EN] After looking away for a heartbeat, Bai Feng couldn’t fight his curiosity and peeked back down anyway.
[ZH] 春金巧已开始跳舞，浑身上下一丝不挂，看得白风既害羞又心热，脸不由变得滚烫起来，脑子里浮现出苦荞的身影。
[EN] Chun Jinqiao was now dancing completely naked. Watching made Bai Feng blush furiously and grow flustered, his mind suddenly flashing to Kuqiao’s face.
[ZH] 本是教春金巧跳舞的几名女子见她跳得更好，一个个退到一边。
[EN] Seeing her far outshine them, the other dancers stepped back one by one.
[ZH] 梁师成起身走到春金巧跟前，用贪婪的眼神盯着她身上的每一寸肌肤。
[EN] Liang Shicheng rose and walked straight up to Chun Jinqiao, greedy eyes devouring every inch of her bare skin.
[ZH] 春金巧一点儿不扭捏，绕着梁内侍扭动腰肢，时不时用挑 逗的眼神勾 引他。见梁内侍双眼冒出红光，认为火候已差不多，顺势倒在梁内侍的怀里：“内侍大人，让奴家到床上好好陪您。”
[EN] Not one bit shy, Chun Jinqiao swayed her hips circling around him, tossing provocative glances until she saw his eyes glow scarlet. Guessing the iron was hot, she melted limp into his chest. “Lord Attendant, let me wait on you properly in bed.”
[ZH] 令她想不到的是，梁内侍脸色一沉，将她推开：“你是笑话我不是男人？”
[EN] To her shock, Liang Shicheng’s face darkened and he shoved her straight off. “Mocking me for not being a whole man?!”
[ZH] 春金巧立刻醒悟，眼前这位的确不是男人，这种人她没伺候过，不小心说错话，需尽快挽回。
[EN] Chun Jinqiao snapped alert instantly. Right—this man wasn’t intact; she’d never served a eunuch before and had slipped up badly. She had to smooth it over fast.
[ZH] 见内侍大人坐回榻边，春金巧跟上去，倒在梁师成怀里，勾住他的脖子：“在奴家眼里谁都比不上内侍大人，您想让奴家怎么做，奴家都乐意。”
[EN] As the eunuch sat back on the couch, Chun Jinqiao glided over, sank onto his lap, and hooked her arms around his neck. “Nobody in my eyes comes close to you, Lord Attendant. Tell me whatever you want—I’ll gladly do it.”
[ZH] 梁师成伸手抓过榻边的一个盒子，打开，从里面拿出一根针：“瞧，这是什么？”
[EN] Reaching beside the couch, Liang Shicheng grabbed a small case, snapped it open, and lifted out a needle. “Look—know what this is?”
[ZH] 春金巧不以为然：“一根针，内侍大人为何让我看它？”
[EN] Chun Jinqiao blinked lightly. “A needle. Why show me that, Lord Attendant?”
[ZH] 梁师成捏着针，像欣赏一件宝贝一样：“它能带给我快乐，对我来讲很重要。”
[EN] Holding the needle between two fingers as though admiring a priceless jewel, he murmured, “This brings me pleasure—means the world to me.”
[ZH] 春金巧依然不懂：“一根普通的针而已，哪能跟我比？我才能带给内侍大人真正的快乐。”
[EN] Still clueless, she pouted playfully. “It’s just an ordinary sewing needle—how could it compare to me? Only I can bring you true bliss.”
[ZH] “它是普通，可若用它扎在你的身上，你叫的声音越大，我会越开心。”
[EN] “Ordinary, yes—until I sink it into your skin. The louder you scream, the happier I get.”
[ZH] 春金巧脸上的笑容顿失，原来内侍大人爱好特殊，她看一眼那针觉得浑身上下似乎已有种刺痛的感觉。
[EN] The smile froze dead on Chun Jinqiao’s face. So the Lord Attendant had *that* kind of twisted hobby—one glance at the glint of steel already sent phantom pricks tingling across her skin.
[ZH] “怎么？不乐意？”
[EN] “What? Unwilling?”
[ZH] 春金巧挤出笑容：“怎么会？我前来就是陪内侍大人快活，只是这种方式会不会有些残忍？要不换一种？我当初在春香楼没白干，学下不少让男人快活的手段，您要不试试？觉得哪种好我就用哪种。”
[EN] Forcing a smile back, Chun Jinqiao cooed, “How could I be? I came here to please you! Only… isn’t that a bit cruel? Why not try another way? I didn’t spend all those years at Chunxiang Brothel for nothing—I picked up plenty of tricks for pleasing men. Care to test a few and see which you like best?”
[ZH] “你学的手段都比不上我选的这种。”梁师成说着，亲在春金巧的肩上，她还没反应过来，针已刺在肩头。
[EN] “None of your tricks hold a candle to mine.” Leaning down, Liang Shicheng kissed Chun Jinqiao’s shoulder—and before she could blink, drove the needle straight in.
[ZH] 冷不丁被刺一针，春金巧“啊”了一声，心中骂着梁内侍，嘴上却说：“既然内侍大人已扎了奴家，奴家就陪大人乐一乐。但您要答应奴家，今日之后让我成为临安最尊贵的女人，让那些个贵妇都看我的脸色，而不是我看她们的脸色。我还要成为林觉的正室，让白梅若当妾室。”
[EN] Stung out of nowhere, Chun Jinqiao gasped, “Ah!” Cursing him black and blue inside, she purred aloud, “Since you’ve already pricked me, Lord Attendant, I’ll play along! Only promise me one thing: after tonight, make me the highest-ranking woman in Lin’an—make those noble ladies watch *my* mood instead of me watching theirs! And make me Lin Jue’s primary wife, knocking Bai Meiruo down to concubine!”
[ZH] “这有何难？我只有一个要求，你把我紧紧抱住，我每扎一针，你就使劲儿喊，喊得声音越大我会越开心。”
[EN] “Easy enough. I have just one condition: hold me tight, and every time I jab you, scream as loud as you can. Louder you scream, happier I am.”
[ZH] 春金巧紧紧抱住梁内侍，将头埋进他的怀里，做好被扎的准备。
[EN] Wrapping both arms tight around Liang Shicheng, Chun Jinqiao buried her face against his chest and braced herself.
[ZH] 梁师成很满意，继续向春金巧白皙的肌肤上亲去，接着又是一针。
[EN] Pleased, Liang Shicheng kissed another patch of her pale skin and jabbed in again.
[ZH] 因疼痛春金巧自然喊出声来，为了快些结束，疼痛之余，她用上技巧，喊出像夜间野猫叫春的声音，又大又骚情，搞得梁师成因为她的一声喊叫享受半天，她也能少挨几针。
[EN] Pain forced a cry out of her—yet wanting to wrap this up fast, she worked her brothel polish into the scream, letting out a loud, sultry yowl like an alley cat in heat. That single shriek sent Liang Shicheng into half an eternity of bliss, sparing her several extra stabs.
[ZH] 如此变态的行为申简辰实在欣赏不下去，在房顶上大声说：“梁内侍可真快活啊！”
[EN] Unable to stomach the sickening spectacle a second longer, Shen Jianchen boomed straight down from the roof, “My, Imperial Attendant Liang sure knows how to have a good time!”
[ZH] 刚要扎下第三针，听到传来仪王的声音，还是在屋顶，恐怕屋内的一切已被仪王偷 窥到。
[EN] Caught mid-raise for a third jab, Liang Shicheng heard Prince Yi’s voice from overhead—meaning everything in this room had just been spied on.
[ZH] 梁师成放下针，对衣着暴露的女子说：“给她穿好衣裳带到里屋去。”
[EN] Dropping the needle, Liang Shicheng snapped at the half-naked women, “Throw her clothes on and haul her into the inner room!”
[ZH] 女子们拿起衣裳披在春金巧的身上。
[EN] The women scrambled to drape garments over Chun Jinqiao.
[ZH] 梁师成趁机抓住一位女子的手腕，小声说：“你从后门出去，告诉侍卫瞅准时机守在门外。”
[EN] Seizing one girl by the wrist, Liang Shicheng hissed in her ear, “Slip out the back door and tell the guards to wait outside for my signal.”
[ZH] 女子点头，拉起春金巧进入里屋。
[EN] Nodding, the woman hurried Chun Jinqiao into the inner chamber.
[ZH] 春金巧在心中埋怨，那声音出现得太不是时候，说不定自己再挨一针就完事，这下搞得待会儿还要重新开始，多挨几针已避免不了。
[EN] Chun Jinqiao silently fumed: *What rotten timing! One more poke and I’d have been done! Now we’ll have to start all over again later—no dodging extra holes now!*
[ZH] 梁师成整理好居家的衣衫，站起来向屋顶问道：“仪王既然来了，为何不大大方方些？却躲在屋顶偷 窥我的隐私，是几个意思？”
[EN] Adjusting his loose robe, Liang Shicheng stood and called up toward the rafters. “Since Prince Yi dropped by, why hide on the roof peeking at my private affairs? What’s the meaning of this?”
[ZH] 门已被踢开，申简辰带着白风出现在梁师成面前。
[EN] With a sharp bang, the hall door flew kicked inward, and Shen Jianchen strode in with Bai Feng beside him.
[ZH] “我若大大方方前来，岂能看到一出好戏？又怎能了解梁内侍特殊的癖好？”
[EN] “If I’d walked through your front door like a gentleman, how would I ever catch such a grand show—or learn about Imperial Attendant Liang’s special little hobbies?”
[ZH] “我不是真正的男人，无法体验仪王能体验到的快乐，也只好另辟蹊径，仪王又何必用正常人的眼光诟病下官。”
[EN] “I’m not a whole man, Your Highness—I can’t taste the pleasures a prince enjoys, so I find my own path. Why look down your nose at me like some self-righteous saint?”
[ZH] 申简辰懒得关注对方的特殊癖好，警告道：“你动其他人我不管，可若想动幽栖居士，我的拳头可不是吃素的。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen couldn’t care less about the man’s sick fetishes. His voice dropped to ice. “Touch whoever else you like and I won’t blink—lay one finger on Youqi Jushi, and my fists aren’t made of bean curd.”
[ZH] 梁师成笑道：“仪王的意思是仪王妃我可动得？”
[EN] Liang Shicheng gave a soft laugh. “Does Your Highness mean Princess Consort Yi is fair game then?”
[ZH] 申简辰迅速卡住梁师成的脖子：“再敢胡说一句，小心我扭断你的脖子！”
[EN] In a flash, Shen Jianchen clamped one hand around Liang Shicheng’s throat. “Say one more filthy word and I’ll snap your neck clean off!”
[ZH] “开个玩笑，仪王又何必当真？”
[EN] “Just a joke—why take it so seriously, Your Highness?”
[ZH] “再警告你一遍，不准打幽栖居士的主意！否则我现在就要了你的命！”
[EN] “One last warning: keep your hands off Youqi Jushi, or I take your life right here and now!”
[ZH] 申简辰话音刚落，从窗户射进一支箭，若不出差错，他必然受伤。白风眼尖，瞅到箭向王爷而来，迅速挡住，那箭正好伤在他的身上，虽不是要害处，却流出不少鲜血，白风疼得倒在地上。
[EN] Scarcely had the threat left his mouth when an arrow whistled straight through the window—dead on course for Shen Jianchen. Sharp-eyed Bai Feng spotted the shaft in time and lunged in front, taking the arrow square in his own body. Not a lethal spot, yet blood poured fast as Bai Feng crumpled to the floor with a groan.
[ZH] 申简辰已顾不上梁师成，一把推开他，转身抱住白风：“你怎么这么傻？”
[EN] Dropping Liang Shicheng, Shen Jianchen shoved him aside and dropped to catch Bai Feng. “Why did you do something so foolish?!”
[ZH] 白风笑笑说：“我没事，王爷小心。”
[EN] Bai Feng forced a crooked grin. “I’m fine… watch out, Your Highness.”
[ZH] 一群侍卫从外面进来，个个手拿利刃。
[EN] Guards wielding naked blades flooded through the doorway from outside.
[ZH] 梁师成被仪王推到榻上，顺势坐下，用不紧不慢的语气说：“幽栖居士是陈家的儿媳，王爷再喜欢她也得不到，又何必因她得罪下官？”
[EN] Shoved back onto the couch, Liang Shicheng settled himself casually and drawled, “Youqi Jushi is a married Chen daughter-in-law, Your Highness. No matter how badly you want her, you can’t have her—why make an enemy of me over her?”
[ZH] 申简辰将白风扶到一旁的椅子上坐下，对面前的侍卫说：“你们是官家身边的侍卫，今日怎能用手中的剑对准皇子？本王现在命令你们把梁师成抓起来！”
[EN] Helping Bai Feng onto a chair nearby, Shen Jianchen faced the ring of guards. “You men belong to His Majesty’s personal guard—how dare you point blades at an imperial prince? I command you right now: arrest Liang Shicheng!”
[ZH] 一名侍卫说：“官家让我们保护梁内侍安全，并未吩咐我等来临安要听仪王指挥！”
[EN] One guard answered stiffly, “His Majesty ordered us to protect Imperial Attendant Liang—nowhere did he command us to take orders from Prince Yi in Lin’an!”
[ZH] 梁师成大笑起来：“哈哈哈……！仪王可听到？官家最是明辨是非，知道什么人该保护。既然官家让你们保护我的安全，愣着干什么？还不快把仪王抓起来！”
[EN] Liang Shicheng burst into loud laughter. “Hahaha! Hear that, Your Highness? His Majesty knows right from wrong—knows exactly who deserves protecting! Since His Majesty told you to guard my safety, what are you standing around for? Seize Prince Yi!”

#CH 319
[ZH] 侍卫们手握明晃晃的剑一起攻向仪王。
[EN] Swords glinting in the lamplight, the imperial guards closed in on Prince Yi from every side.
[EN] Undaunted, Shen Jianchen unleashed the Shen Fist counters designed specifically against armed blades, plunging straight into the fray.
[EN] Every one of His Majesty’s palace guards had come up through brutal drills—each man could handle several Shen Jianchens on raw skill. Swarmed from all angles, Shen Jianchen held his own smoothly at first, yet as the barrage kept hammering in, keeping up grew tougher by the breath.
[EN] Seeing his master starting to slip into disadvantage, Bai Feng tried to jump in—only every twitch sent blood gushing fresh from the arrow wound, leaving him half-paralyzed with pain.
[EN] Even pinned down, Shen Jianchen gritted his teeth and fought hard, knocking several guards flat to the floor.
[ZH] 申简辰并未将众人放在眼里，他施展出申拳中用来对付兵刃的拳法与侍卫们对决起来。
[EN] Watching Prince Yi battle like a cornered lion terrified Liang Shicheng. Desperate to end it fast, he waited for an opening and pressed a hidden catch beside his couch.
[ZH] 官家身边的侍卫一个个经过严苛训练，对付几个申简辰不在话下，一群人围上来打他一人，刚开始应对起来尚觉轻松，打了一阵渐渐觉出困难来。
[EN] With a metallic clatter, an iron cage plummeted straight down from the ceiling rafters.
[ZH] 白风见王爷逐渐处于弱势，很想去帮他，可他稍微一动，带箭的伤口流出许多血来，疼得他难以施展四肢。
[EN] While Shen Jianchen focused hard on parrying incoming blades, every guard suddenly leaped wide clear.
[ZH] 尽管困难，申简辰依然硬撑着，还是将几个侍卫打趴下。
[EN] Spotting it first, Bai Feng shouted, “Your Highness! Look out!”
[ZH] 梁师成见仪王英勇，难以制服，心中甚是胆怯。为了迅速拿下仪王，他瞅准时机，轻轻按动榻边的机关，一个铁制的笼子从屋顶掉落下来……
[EN] Too late—before Shen Jianchen could dodge, the heavy iron bars slammed around him, trapping him tight with zero room to swing a fist.
[ZH] 正当申简辰专注对付侍卫时，众人突然向一旁撤去……
[EN] Sauntering over to the cage, Liang Shicheng offered a mock bow. “Forgive the offense, Your Highness. Please stay as my guest at Liu’an Villa for a few days. Once I wrap up an important piece of business, I’ll see you safely back to Secluded Forest Villa.”
[ZH] 白风见状，大声提醒：“王爷！小心！”
[EN] “What are you planning?!”
[ZH] 申简辰还未反应过来，已被铁笼牢牢罩住，拳头无法再施展出去。
[EN] “Best not ask, Your Highness—and stay out of the poetess’s affairs.”
[ZH] 梁师成走到铁笼跟前行礼道：“仪王，得罪了，下官请您在柳岸别馆住些时日，等办完重要的事定将您安全送回幽林别居。”
[EN] “If you lay a single hand on her, the second I get out I swear I’ll never let you live!”
[ZH] “你到底要做什么？”
[EN] Ignoring him, Liang Shicheng walked over to a cabinet, pulled out a drawer, grabbed a vial of wound medicine, and tossed it into Bai Feng’s lap.
[ZH] “仪王最好不要过问，也少管女词人的事。”
[EN] “Tend your scratch, Guard Bai, and wait nicely on His Highness.”
[ZH] “你若敢动她！我出去绝不会放过你！”
[EN] Leading his guards out the front door, Liang Shicheng ordered the women hiding in the back out through the rear, then had every door and window boarded solid and even the roof tiles patched—leaving only one narrow slit in a window to slide through bowls of rice and water.
[ZH] 梁师成不再理会仪王，走到一个柜子跟前，拉开抽屉取出一瓶药，扔进白风的怀里。
[EN] Bai Feng groaned, “Don’t panic, Your Highness. Once I stop the bleeding, the two of us together can tip this iron cage over.”
[ZH] “白侍卫治好伤后，好好伺候仪王。”
[EN] Yanking the arrow straight out, Bai Feng hissed as blood spurted, peeled open his robe, and dumped Liang’s medicine powder straight onto the gash.
[ZH] 梁师成带着侍卫从前门出去，躲在里屋的女子都从后门出去，然后让人将前后门及窗户堵死，就连屋顶也修补好，只在窗户上留出一处方便送水送饭的空隙。
[EN] Testing the bars himself, Shen Jianchen threw every ounce of strength against the cage—it didn’t budge a hair. “This thing weighs at least a thousand catties. Even both of us together couldn’t shift it.”
[ZH] 白风说：“王爷别着急，等我把血止住，我们一起合力将铁笼子掀倒。”
[EN] Once the bleeding stopped cleanly, Bai Feng buttoned his shirt and limped up to the bars. “What do we do now?”
[ZH] 白风说着拔出身上的箭，一股血喷涌而出，他忍着疼，解开衣衫，将梁师成给的药倒在伤口上。
[EN] Shen Jianchen sank onto the floorboards. “Get some sleep tonight. We’ll figure something out at daybreak.”
[ZH] 申简辰仔细观察罩着自己的铁笼，使出全身的力气试图挪动它，可铁笼纹丝不动：“估计铁笼约有上千斤，合你我二人之力根本挪不动。”
[EN] Bai Feng could tell just looking at him how frantic—and helpless—his master felt underneath.
[ZH] 白风已给伤口上好药，血立刻止住，他穿好衣裳，走到铁笼跟前：“该怎么办？”
[EN] In another wing of the villa, Liang Shicheng pushed the whole clash clean out of his head and went straight back to his twisted amusement. Chasing status and vengeance, Chun Jinqiao grit her teeth through every needle prick, screaming loud on cue.
[ZH] 申简辰坐在地上：“先休息一晚，等明日再想办法。”
[EN] Satisfied at last, Liang Shicheng sent Chun Jinqiao home to Lin Manor in his own carriage.
[ZH] 白风能看得出，王爷既着急又无奈。
[EN] Gifting Chun Jinqiao to Imperial Attendant Liang was easily the lowest stunt Lin Jue had ever pulled, yet for his own career he had swallowed his pride and done it. After dropping her outside Liu’an Villa, he had returned to Lin Manor and tossed sleeplessly all night in Zhulan Residence.
[ZH] 另一个屋子内，梁师成早已将刚才的事忘得一干二净，继续寻欢作乐。春金巧为了名声和地位，忍着针刺之痛，大声喊叫着。
[EN] Bai Meiruo knew about it too, of course. At first she cursed Lin Jue as a spineless beast for pimping out his own woman—then thought twice and brightened right up: he’d handed over a *concubine*, not his primary wife! That proved in her husband’s eyes a primary wife held sacred status no outsider could touch. Thrilled, she slept like a log, never even noticing what hour Chun Jinqiao crept home.
[ZH] 梁师成满足后，派人用自己的马车将春金巧送回林府。
[EN] Paying no mind to the raw punctures stinging across her back, Chun Jinqiao marched straight into Zhulan Residence upon returning. A lamp still burned inside, proving Lin Jue hadn’t slept. She pushed straight through the door.
[ZH] 把春金巧送给梁内侍是林觉迄今为止做过的最出格的事，为了个人前程，也只能出此下策。他亲自将春金巧送到柳岸别馆后，回到林府，在竹兰居辗转难眠。
[EN] Startled half out of his skin, Lin Jue tumbled straight off the couch and scrambled up. “Y-you’re back already? Don’t tell me Imperial Attendant Liang didn’t like you!”
[ZH] 白梅若自然也知道此事，开始时她骂林觉是畜生，连自己的女人都往外送；很快她想通，林觉送的是妾室而非正室，说明在夫君的眼里正室就是正室，不容他人玷污。一高兴便早早入睡，春金巧几时回到林府她根本不知。
[EN] Chun Jinqiao sat down by the table. “Lift my shirt and look at my back—you’ll understand everything.”
[ZH] 春金巧无心顾及背上的针眼儿带来的疼痛感，回到林府直奔竹兰居。灯还亮着，说明林觉并未睡着，她推门而入。
[EN] Stepping over, Lin Jue peeled back her collar and stared. “What on earth poked you like that?! How could he be so vicious?!”
[ZH] 林觉见状从榻上滚落下来，再从地上爬起：“你……怎么回来了？梁内侍该不会是不喜欢你？”
[EN] Only then did the throbbing ache really hit Chun Jinqiao. “Stop gawking, get me some medicine, and I’ll tell you while you rub it on.”
[ZH] 春金巧坐到桌旁：“掀开我的衣裳，看看我背上便什么都清楚。”
[EN] Fetching ointment, Lin Jue smeared salve gently across her bare back while listening to her account. Finished, he sighed guiltily. “You suffered terribly tonight.”
[ZH] 林觉走上前，掀开春金巧背部的衣裳，向内一瞧：“被什么扎的？怎么这么狠？”
[EN] Pulling her clothes back on, Chun Jinqiao smirked. “Still, he agreed to everything I asked. As long as he pulls through for me, a few pokes were worth it.”
[ZH] 春金巧这才感到一阵疼痛：“快帮我上药，慢慢说给你听。”
[EN] Lin Jue’s sliver of guilt vanished instantly. “I’m relieved you’re back safe. Go to your room and rest—time I finally caught some sleep too.”
[ZH] 林觉取了药，在春金巧露出的背部边涂抹药物边听她描述过程。听完，愧疚道：“让你受委屈了。”
[EN] Remembering Prince Yi barging into Liu’an Villa, Chun Jinqiao added, “Wait—one more thing you ought to hear.”
[ZH] 春金巧将衣裳穿好：“不过他已答应我的要求，只要他能帮到我，这委屈也算受得值。”
[EN] Drowsy already, Lin Jue waved it off. “Too sleepy. Tell me tomorrow.”
[ZH] 林觉的愧疚感顿时消失：“你回来我也放心，赶紧回屋休息，我也该睡个安稳觉。”
[EN] “Even if it’s about Youqi Jushi?”
[ZH] 春金巧想到仪王闯入柳岸别馆的事：“还有一事需说给你听。”
[EN] Lin Jue snapped right awake. “What about her?”
[ZH] 困意袭来，林觉懒得再听别的事：“困了，明儿再说。”
[EN] “Prince Yi stormed Liu’an Villa tonight and kicked up a huge brawl—only to get locked up by Imperial Attendant Liang instead! Guess who he came charging in for?”
[ZH] “若是关于幽栖居士呢？”
[EN] “Zhu Shuzhen?!”
[ZH] 林觉果然清醒许多：“她能有什么事？”
[EN] “Well, I heard Prince Yi roaring at Liang never to touch Youqi Jushi. Chances are Imperial Attendant Liang wants the poetess dancing stark naked too so he can poke a few needles into her.”
[ZH] “仪王今晚闯入柳岸别馆大闹一场，最后反而被梁内侍关起来，你猜他是为谁而去？”
[EN] “Dirty old lecher!” Lin Jue slammed a fist onto the table. “No wonder he sent me to squeeze the Chen family—so *this* was his real game!”
[ZH] “难道是为朱淑真？”
[EN] “Oh? Planning to march back and murder Imperial Attendant Liang now? If you’re that righteous, why deliver me to his doorstep tonight?”
[ZH] “反正我听到仪王警告梁内侍不准动幽栖居士，恐怕梁内侍也想看女词人不穿衣裳跳舞，然后在她身上刺几针。”
[EN] Pulling his fist back, Lin Jue muttered, “If my career weren’t on the line, I’d knock every tooth out of his mouth!”
[ZH] “老色鬼！”林觉一拳砸在桌上，“难怪让我带人找陈家的麻烦，原来是为此事。”
[EN] “Save it! You only bark tough behind closed doors. Put Imperial Attendant Liang right in front of your nose and you wouldn’t dare let out a fart—you’d hold it in till your belly burst! Why talk big?”
[ZH] “怎么？你还想杀了梁内侍不成？若如此，又何必今晚把我送去？”
[EN] Lin Jue shut his mouth. If not even Prince Yi could stop the eunuch, what could *he* do? Besides, Zhu Shuzhen wasn’t his wife anymore—none of his concern. His own advancement came first.
[ZH] 林觉收起拳头：“要不是为前程着想，我定打得梁内侍满地找牙！”
[EN] ...
[ZH] “行了，你也就是在家里威风威风，梁内侍若在你面前，你连个屁都不敢放，还得憋回去难受自己，又何必说大话？”
[EN] Early the next morning, skipping breakfast altogether, Zhu Xiyan rode straight to Liu’an Villa.
[ZH] 林觉不再言语，仪王都办不成的事，他又能如何？再说朱淑真已不是他的人，他也犯不着去管，还是个人前程重要。
[EN] Guessing at once why Zhu Xiyan had shown up, Liang Shicheng refused to waste breath arguing and had servants usher him straight into a side room.
[ZH] 第二天一大早，朱晞颜顾不上吃早饭，直奔柳岸别馆。
[EN] The instant Zhu Xiyan stepped inside, guards pressed blades to his throat, trussed him tight with rope, and stuffed a gag into his mouth.
[ZH] 朱晞颜前来拜访，梁师成猜到是为陈家的事，他不想跟朱晞颜多费口舌，吩咐下人将朱晞颜带进一个屋内。
[EN] One guard announced flatly, “Imperial Attendant Liang invites you to stay a few days. Once his business is done, he’ll send you back to Zhu Manor.”
[ZH] 朱晞颜刚进屋，几个侍卫用刀架在他的脖子上，迅速用绳子将他捆了，口中塞上布子，
[EN] Locking the door from outside, the guards left him stranded.
[ZH] 一名侍卫说：“梁内侍请你住上几日，等事情办完，再放你回朱府。”
[EN] Helpless, Zhu Xiyan sank down thinking, *I fear my daughter won’t escape this time.*
[ZH] 侍卫将他留在屋内，把门从外面锁上。
[EN] ...
[ZH] 朱晞颜无可奈何，心想，看来女儿在劫难逃。
[EN] When Prince Yi failed to return by morning after slipping out the night before, Matron Zou panicked—yet dared not breathe a word to Princess Consort Yi for fear of triggering another collapse.
[ZH] 仪王晚上出去，到天亮人还没回来，邹嬷嬷担心不已，又不敢让仪王妃知道，怕她因担心王爷受到刺激。
[EN] Thankfully Her Highness barely stirred from bed anymore. Ordering Xilan to stick to a rehearsed lie—that His Highness had been summoned to the yamen on urgent matters and would stay there several days—kept the peace.
[ZH] 还好仪王妃基本躺在床上，不怎么出来，只要叮嘱好喜兰，骗仪王妃说王爷被请去府衙处理重要的事，会在府衙住上几日。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong half-doubted the excuse, yet felt secretly glad her brother was away; it bought time for the scabbing needle marks on her skin to fade cleanly before he came back.
[ZH] 乔梦容虽不大相信，可也庆幸哥哥不在，省得他发现自己身上已结痂的针眼儿，她希望等针眼儿处恢复正常后哥哥再回来。
[EN] After waiting a whole day with zero word, Chen Jianglin and Chen Haoyu checked Zhu Manor and Secluded Forest Villa separately—only to learn neither Zhu Xiyan nor Prince Yi had ever come home!
[ZH] 等了一日，陈江林和陈浩宇等不来消息，两人分别去朱府和幽林别居打探，才知朱晞颜和仪王自从出去后一直未归。
[EN] The Zhu household panicked like ants on a hot skillet, while Matron Zou at Secluded Forest Villa grew desperate enough to beg Chen Haoyu to scour the city for Prince Yi.
[ZH] 朱家人不知因何缘故，急得像热锅上的蚂蚁；幽林别居的邹嬷嬷也急得不行，拜托陈浩宇到处打听仪王的下落。
[EN] Out of choices, father and son returned to Liu’an Villa for answers.
[ZH] 父子二人不得已来到柳岸别馆打听。
[EN] Liang Shicheng sent one guard out to the gate to tell them, “Both Prince Yi and Zhu Xiyan are staying here as guests for a few days. The instant Imperial Attendant Liang lays eyes on the poetess and feels pleased, both men walk free unharmed.”
[ZH] 梁师成派一名侍卫来到门口，告诉他们：“仪王与朱晞颜都在柳岸别馆做客，要住上几日。等见到女词人，内侍大人一高兴便会将二人安全送回。”
[EN] Then the guard added a cold warning: “Our Lord says you have one day left. Fail to deliver her on time, and you can kiss every Chen shop across the empire goodbye.”
[ZH] 侍卫还警告道：“内侍大人说了，还有一日时间，若陈家不按时将人送来，别再想将各地的生意继续做下去。”
[EN] Staggering back to Chen Manor beaten and hollow, father and son kept the women in the dark and slipped straight into Old Madam Xia’s room, dismissed the maids, shut the door, and laid everything bare.
[ZH] 父子二人垂头丧气回到陈府，瞒着各自的女人，来到夏老太太房中，让伺候的丫鬟退下，关上门，将事情告知。
[EN] After the initial shock, Old Madam Xia turned icy calm. “So Imperial Attendant Liang means to back the Chens into a wall with no way out. Fine—then I’ll make a trip to Liu’an Villa myself.”
[ZH] 夏老太太震惊之后，变得冷静：“梁内侍这是要逼得陈家进退两难。既如此，我亲自到柳岸别馆走一趟。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin stepped in front of her. “Mother, not even Prince Yi or my in-law could budge him—what can you do? I’m terrified he’ll lock you up right alongside them!”
[ZH] 陈江林拦道：“母亲，仪王和亲家去了都没用，您去了又有何用？只怕他把您老人家也给关起来。”
[EN] “If I’m going, I’ve got something that will put him right in his place.” Old Madam Xia raised her voice toward the door. “Come in!”
[ZH] “我能去，自然有拿住他的手段。”夏老太太说完，向外喊道，“来人！”
[EN] A maid pushed the door open. “Your orders, Old Madam?”
[ZH] 一位丫鬟推门而入：“老太太有何吩咐？”
[EN] “Go to the inner chamber and fetch the large purple pouch buried at the bottom of my pearwood chest.”
[ZH] “去，到里屋把压在梨花木箱子底下的紫色大荷包取来。”
[EN] Father and son exchanged bewildered stares as the maid slipped inside.
[ZH] 看着丫鬟进入里屋，父子两个一头雾水。
[EN] Moments later she carried out a bulky purple brocade pouch and placed it in Old Madam Xia’s hands.
[ZH] 丫鬟进去没多久，手里捧着一个紫色的大荷包出来，送到老太太面前。
[EN] Opening the drawstrings, Old Madam Xia pulled out a folded letter and a jade pendant carved with a name. Studying the characters carefully, she tucked both back inside.
[ZH] 夏老太太接过，将荷包打开，从里面取出一封折好的信和一个玉佩，玉佩上刻着一个名字，夏老太太仔细看一遍，又把东西放回荷包。
[EN] “You two—come with me to call on Imperial Attendant Liang.”
[ZH] “你们父子两个陪我一同去拜见梁内侍。”
[EN] ...
[ZH] 从前天晚上起，千里寻总觉得陈浩宇心情不佳，连着两个早晨给她做茶时，也没有往日话多。
[EN] Ever since two nights ago, Qianli Xun had noticed Chen Haoyu acting off. Two mornings straight, when he came to whisk tea for her, his usual chatter had vanished.
[ZH] 当千里寻接过茶盏问他为何看上去不高兴时，陈浩宇用生意上的事既多又麻烦掩饰过去，然后匆忙出了门。
[EN] Taking the tea bowl and asking why he looked so glum, she only got brushed off with vague excuses about “too many tangled business headaches” before he bolted out the door.
[ZH] 千里寻觉得纳闷，透过窗户瞧见青瓜，迅速出门将他叫住：“青瓜，你去哪里？”
[EN] Puzzled, Qianli Xun spotted Qinggua passing outside the window, hurried out, and flagged him down. “Qinggua, where are you rushing off to?”
[ZH] 青瓜说：“回少夫人，公子让我今日去永泰茶楼接待一位客商，把香料的生意给谈下来。”
[EN] “To Yongtai Teahouse, Young Madam. Young Master told me to meet a merchant alone today and close the spice deal.”
[ZH] “陈浩宇不去吗？”
[EN] “Chen Haoyu isn’t going?”
[ZH] “公子说让我一个人谈，他这两日有其他事忙。”
[EN] “He told me to handle it myself—said he’s tied up with other urgent business these past couple of days.”
[ZH] “什么事？”
[EN] “What business?”
[ZH] “公子没说，我不大清楚。”
[EN] “He didn’t say, so I’m really not sure.”
[ZH] 什么都问不出，千里寻只好说：“你去吧，别耽误正事。”
[EN] Getting nowhere, Qianli Xun sighed, “Go on then, don’t miss your meeting.”
[ZH] 青瓜答应一声后离开去忙生意上的事。
[EN] Qinggua bowed and hurried off on business.
[ZH] 千里寻心中犯嘀咕，准备向婆婆打听一下。
[EN] Suspicion itching inside her, Qianli Xun decided to ask her mother-in-law instead.
[ZH] 来到婆婆的屋子时，发现婆婆也正坐在屋内纳闷。
[EN] Walking into Yao Qingyan’s room, she found her mother-in-law sitting there looking equally baffled.
[ZH] 姚轻琰觉得夫君这两日看着怪怪的，还一副魂不守舍的样子，今日一早用饭时也没往常话多，显得心事重重。问他怎么了，陈江林只说生意上的事让人焦心而已。
[EN] Yao Qingyan had noticed her husband acting strange for two straight days—distracted, spaced out, barely speaking a word over breakfast this morning with gloom written across his face. Pressed on what was wrong, Chen Jianglin had only mumbled about work pressures.
[ZH] 昨日一大早出去，很晚才回来，今日有事又一大早出去，还紧皱眉头。以前生意上也遇到过问题，夫君还从未如此过，难道这次遇到更大的麻烦？
[EN] Out at dawn, back late at night, out at dawn again today with brows knotted tight—even during previous trade crises, her husband had never looked like *this*. Had disaster struck?
[ZH] 正想着，淑真前来。姚轻琰招呼她坐下，开口便问：“你可听浩宇说起生意上的事？”
[EN] Just then Shuzhen walked in. Greeting her warmly, Yao Qingyan asked at once, “Did Haoyu mention anything troubling about business?”
[ZH] 千里寻摇摇头：“没跟我说起，从昨日回来，他看上去一点儿都不开心，该不会是生意上发生了什么大事？婆婆可听公爹说起过？”
[EN] Qianli Xun shook her head. “Not a word—though he’s looked miserable ever since coming home yesterday. Could something major have happened with the shops? Did Father-in-law say anything to you?”
[ZH] “你公爹也是从昨晚起看着心情沉重，我问他，他也不细说。”
[EN] “Your father-in-law’s looked heavy-hearted since last night too, and won’t tell me details either.”
[ZH] “看来是生意上的事，要不两人怎么都是一副模样。婆婆，要不去问问老太太？”
[EN] “It’s got to be business—otherwise why would both of them wear the exact same face? Should we ask Grandmother?”
[ZH] “昨日我们也陪过老太太，她看上去什么都不清楚，问了也是白问。不过也该到陪老太太聊天儿的时间，你我正好一同过去。”
[EN] “We sat with Old Madam yesterday and she seemed completely in the dark—asking won’t tell us much. Still, it’s time for our morning visit with her anyway. Let’s go together.”
[ZH] 婆媳两个携手往老太太的院子而去，走到半道上，看到各自的夫君搀着老太太迎面走来。
[EN] Walking arm in arm toward Old Madam Xia’s courtyard, the two women rounded a bend—and ran straight into father and son escorting Old Madam Xia toward the gate!
[ZH] 两人迎上去，姚轻琰先开口：“你们两个怎么回来了？要带母亲去哪里？”
[EN] Intercepting them, Yao Qingyan asked first, “Why are you two back—and where are you taking Mother?”
[ZH] 夏老太太编起瞎话：“生意上的事，有个老客户提出要见我，他们父子二人才回来请我出山，你们两个赶紧回屋去，今日陪我说话的事取消，也给老二和老三媳妇说一声。”
[EN] Old Madam Xia spun a smooth lie. “Just business. An old client insisted on seeing me in person, so father and son came to drag this old bone out of retirement. You two run along back to your rooms—skip sitting with me today, and tell Second and Third Daughters-in-law too.”
[ZH] 千里寻故意问：“要不奶奶带上我，也让我见见世面？”
[EN] Testing the waters, Qianli Xun smiled brightly. “Why not take me along, Grandmother? Let me broaden my horizons!”
[ZH] 夏老太太立马拒绝：“你是女词人，好好在家写诗作词，商场上抛头露面的事不适合你，老实在家待着。”
[EN] Old Madam Xia shot it down fast. “You’re a poetess—stay home writing verse and lyrics. Showing your face in merchant squabbles doesn’t suit you. Stay put indoors.”
[ZH] 婆媳两个从各自夫君沉重的表情中感觉出不对来，但又说不出哪里不对。
[EN] One look at the grim tightness on both men’s faces told the two women something smelled fishy—only neither could pin down what.
[ZH] 三人离开后，千里寻将婆婆送回屋，带上苦荞套好马车，吩咐车夫尾随刚刚出发的陈家马车。
[EN] Once the trio rolled away, Qianli Xun escorted her mother-in-law back to her rooms, grabbed Kuqiao, had a carriage hitched, and ordered her coachman to tail the Chen carriage that had just pulled out.
[ZH] 苦荞不解地问：“小姐，为何要偷偷摸摸？”
[EN] Mystified, Kuqiao asked, “Why are we sneaking behind them like spies, Miss?”
[ZH] “不知为何，总觉得他们有事瞒着我和婆婆，放心不下，才想出尾随的办法。”
[EN] “Don’t ask me why—I just know they’re hiding something big from Mother-in-law and me. I won’t rest easy until I see what it is.”
[ZH] “我这两日没见到白风，心里总觉得很慌，以前从没有这种感觉，您说他是不是出了什么事？”
[EN] “I haven’t seen Bai Feng for two whole days either, and my chest feels tight with dread for no reason. Do you think something happened to him?”
[ZH] “应该不会，他是仪王的侍卫，能出什么事？恐怕是因为仪王这两日心情不好，他忙着安慰，才没来找你。”
[EN] “Probably not. He’s Prince Yi’s bodyguard—who’d touch him? Prince Yi’s been in a rotten mood these past two days, so Bai Feng’s probably stuck cheering him up and couldn’t slip over.”
[ZH] “仪王为何会心情不好？”
[EN] “Why’s Prince Yi in a rotten mood?”
[ZH] “听说他既当不成节度使，也不能在司理院做事，心情自然不好。”
[EN] “Word is he lost both the Military Commissioner post and his spot at the Judicial Office—anyone would be moody.”
[ZH] “唉，没想到像仪王这么尊贵的人也会被人拿捏。”
[EN] “Sigh… to think someone as noble as Prince Yi still gets pushed around like a pawn.”
[ZH] “官家迟早也会被拿捏，何况仪王？”
[EN] “Even His Majesty will get pushed around sooner or later, let alone a prince.”
[ZH] 千里寻掀开马车的帘子，向外瞧，从街上的店铺判断是往城北去。她放下帘子，推开马车的门，瞧向前面的马车，小声提醒车夫：“别跟得太紧，小心被发现。”
[EN] Peeking out past the curtain, Qianli Xun judged by the street signs they were heading north. Dropping the curtain, she slid the front panel slightly and murmured to the driver, “Don’t tail too close or they’ll spot us.”
[ZH] 车夫放慢速度，缓缓地跟着。
[EN] The driver eased off the whip and kept a cautious gap.
[ZH] 关好车门后，千里寻不再与苦荞聊天儿，闭上眼睛想着自己的心事。
[EN] Shutting the panel, Qianli Xun stopped chatting with Kuqiao and closed her eyes in thought.
[ZH] 她发现感情的确可以培养，跟陈浩宇成亲已六七个月，培养得还不错。照这个速度发展下去，总有一日当她想起申简辰时，体会到的感情一定只是朋友间才有的情谊。
[EN] She was beginning to see that affection really could grow over time. Six or seven months married to Chen Haoyu, and things between them had warmed nicely. At this rate, someday when Shen Jianchen crossed her mind, it might feel like nothing more than old friendship.
[ZH] 自黑煞虎的事后，生活变得平静许多，千里寻认为一定是梦容良心发现不再找她麻烦。
[EN] Since the Black-Fiend Tiger ordeal, life had turned remarkably peaceful—surely Mengrong had finally found her conscience and decided to leave her in peace.
[ZH] 她甚至想再过几年，与陈浩宇有了自己的孩子，梦容与仪王肯定早已有孩子，那时大家带着各自的孩子见面，一定会将往事抛之脑后，说不定关系还可恢复到从前。
[EN] She even pictured a few years down the road: she’d have children with Chen Haoyu, Mengrong and Prince Yi would have theirs, and one day both families might run into each other with kids in tow, laugh off old scars, and maybe even become close again.
[ZH] 千里寻正畅想着美好的未来时，马车停下，从外面传来车夫谨慎的声音：“少夫人，老太太和老爷的马车停在柳岸别馆门口，我已把马车停到隐蔽处，您尽管放心下来。”
[EN] Right in the middle of that sweet daydream, the carriage rolled to a stop. From outside came the coachman’s hushed voice: “Young Madam, Old Madam and Master’s carriage just pulled up outside Liu’an Villa. I’ve parked around the corner out of sight—you’re safe to step down.”

#CH 320
[ZH] 夏老太太带着儿子和孙子下了马车后，敲开柳岸别馆的大门，告诉开门的下人，只需报上“梁师慧”的名字。
[ZH] 梁师成正坐在亭子里听曲儿，听得极其入神，用手在大腿上拍着节奏，跟着唱曲的人轻哼着。
[ZH] 下人来到跟前，不敢大声打搅，靠近他，在耳边说出那个名字。
[ZH] 梁师成立刻睁开眼，示意唱曲儿的退下，问道：“你没听错？”
[ZH] “小人听得真切，来人是位老太太，说的就是这个名字。”
[ZH] “老太太？”梁师成的眼睛不由变得湿润，他起身，急急忙忙向门口迎去。
[ZH] 陈浩宇和陈江林见梁师成亲自迎出来，表情显得十分激动，仿佛是见到许久未见的亲人一般，两人甚是惊讶。
[ZH] 眼前站着一位银发老太，拄着拐杖。梁师成希望从她的面容中看到熟悉的样貌，可惜什么都没看出，他还是亲切地唤出：“姐姐，是你吗？”
[ZH] 夏老太太心想，梁师慧的名字还真管用，赶紧回道：“让内侍大人失望，我不是您的姐姐梁师慧，我是陈家人，不过我与师慧妹妹交往甚密，她在我这里留了些东西，让我亲手交给她的弟弟。”
[ZH] 父子两个才算明白老太太手中的王牌是什么。
[ZH] 梁师成感到失望的同时，清楚对方前来不过是为陈家和女词人的事。她手中有姐姐留下的东西，自然知道姐姐的下落，还需请入屋内。
[ZH] “原来如此，请到屋内说话。”
[EN] After stepping down from the carriage with her son and grandson, Old Madam Xia knocked on the main gate of Liu’an Villa and told the servant who answered to report one name alone: “Liang Shihui.”
[ZH] 千里寻让苦荞坐在马车里等她，她则隐蔽好身形，偷偷朝大门口瞧，发现陈浩宇和公爹陪着奶奶站在柳岸别馆的大门口。不一会儿出来一人，说了会儿话，三人才被请入别馆内。千里寻猜测，出来迎接的人应该就是梁师成。
[EN] Inside a garden pavilion, Liang Shicheng sat engrossed in a musical performance, tapping the beat against his thigh and humming low along with the singer. The servant approached, wary of disturbing him aloud, leaned in, and murmured the name into his ear.
[ZH] “他们为何来此？难道陈家的生意与梁师成有关？”
[EN] Liang Shicheng’s eyes snapped open. He waved the singers away and asked, “Are you certain you heard right?”
[ZH] 千里寻决定偷偷进去查探明白。
[EN] “Your humble servant heard every word clear as day. An elderly woman is at the gate, and that is the exact name she gave.”
[ZH] 她抬头瞧了瞧柳岸别馆的院墙，不算太高，用轻功上去很容易。于是启动轻功，顺利落入院内。
[EN] “An elderly woman?” Moisture welled in Liang Shicheng’s eyes. He rose and hurried out to meet her at the gate.
[ZH] 还好有一排树挡着，不容易被人发现。透过树与树之间的空隙，清楚地窥探家人跟着梁师成一直向里走。她迂回着悄悄尾随，时刻警惕地观察着四周。
[EN] Chen Haoyu and Chen Jianglin stared in astonishment as Liang Shicheng came out in person, his face brimming with emotion, as though greeting long-lost kin.
[ZH] 众人拐过叠石假山后，她轻手轻脚靠近假山，正打算跟上，一队侍卫巡逻经过，她谨慎躲好。
[EN] Before him stood a silver-haired old woman leaning on a walking cane. Liang Shicheng searched her features for some trace of a familiar face, yet found none. Even so, he called out in a tender voice, “Older Sister, is that you?”
[ZH] 侍卫的队伍中最后一位感觉到有人影闪过，停下来，抽出剑，向假山后靠近。
[EN] That name worked like a charm, Old Madam Xia thought. She hastened to reply, “I hate to disappoint you, Imperial Attendant Liang. I am not your older sister, Liang Shihui. I am from the Chen family. However, Sister Shihui and I were close confidantes, and she left a few things with me to place into her younger brother’s own hands.”
[ZH] 千里寻担心被发现，屏住呼吸身体紧贴假山。
[EN] Only then did father and son understand what trump card the old madam had been holding.
[ZH] 等拿着剑的手出现在面前，她迅速抓住那只手，将侍卫拽到假山后，没等人反应过来，将其打晕，然后用轻功飞身到屋顶。
[EN] Disappointment washed over Liang Shicheng, and he knew at once they had come on account of the Chen family and the poetess. Still, since she held his sister’s belongings, she had to know what had become of her. He would have to invite them inside.
[ZH] 身居高处，清楚地看到三人被带进一个屋内。院中有侍卫巡逻，不好跟踪，千里寻决定还是躲在屋顶偷听。
[EN] “I see. Please come inside so we may talk.”
[ZH] 她踩着屋顶的瓦片，像蜻蜓点水般穿越屋顶……
[EN] Meanwhile, Qianli Xun had told Kuqiao to wait in their carriage while she stayed out of sight and peered toward the villa’s front gate. She spotted Chen Haoyu and her father-in-law standing there beside Grandmother. Moments later, a man came out, exchanged a few words with them, and invited all three inside. Qianli Xun guessed the man who had come to greet them must be Liang Shicheng.
[ZH] 正好经过申简辰被关的屋顶，白风听到声音，提醒道：“王爷，屋顶有人。”
[EN] “What are they doing here?” she wondered. “Does the Chen family’s business have ties to Liang Shicheng?”
[ZH] 申简辰早已听到，瞧着周围结实的铁笼：“希望能有人帮我杀了梁师成。”
[EN] Determined to slip inside and find out, she glanced up at the courtyard wall of Liu’an Villa. Not too high—an easy hop with her light-foot technique. Gathering her breath, she launched herself up and landed inside without a hitch.
[ZH] 又一想，历史上记载梁师成死在宋钦宗之手，楼顶上穿过的那人不可能杀了他。
[EN] A row of trees provided good cover, keeping her out of view. Peering through the gaps between the trunks, she had a clear sight of her family trailing Liang Shicheng deeper into the estate. Taking a roundabout route, she shadowed them on silent feet, her eyes scanning every corner in constant alert.
[ZH] 申简辰安慰自己：“他还有三年的寿命，我会耐心等到那一日。”
[EN] The group rounded a rockery of stacked boulders. She crept up to the stones, about to follow, when a squad of patrolling guards marched past. She tucked herself out of sight with care.
[ZH] 千里寻来到众人所在的屋顶，轻轻趴下，挪开瓦片，将耳朵靠近，大略能够听到里面的声音。
[EN] The last guard in the file caught a flicker of movement, halted, drew his sword, and edged toward the back of the rockery. Fearing discovery, Qianli Xun held her breath and pressed her body flat against the rock. The instant the sword-bearing hand came into view, she snatched his wrist, yanked him behind the boulders, and knocked him out cold before he could react. Then she sprang onto the roof with her light-foot technique.
[ZH] 众人在屋内落座后，梁师成急切地问：“老人家可知我姐姐人在哪里？”
[EN] From her perch above, she watched the three of them enter a building below. With guards patrolling the yard, tailing them on the ground was too risky, so she chose to eavesdrop from the rooftops. Skimming across the roof tiles as light as a dragonfly touching water, she glided from eave to eave.
[ZH] 夏老太太叹口气：“唉，五十多年前，她已离开人世，走之前，拜托我将这个亲手交给她的弟弟，还告诉我她的弟弟进了宫，伺候宫里的贵人。”
[EN] Her path carried her right over the building where Shen Jianchen was held captive. Catching the faint sound, Bai Feng alerted him, “Your Highness, someone is on the roof.”
[ZH] 夏老太太边说边将紫色大荷包递上。
[EN] Shen Jianchen had heard it already. Eyeballing the sturdy iron cage enclosing him, he muttered, “Here’s hoping whoever it is will do me the favor of killing Liang Shicheng.”
[ZH] 下人接过，送至梁内侍手中。
[EN] On second thought, history recorded that Liang Shicheng met his end at the hands of Emperor Qinzong, so whoever had just crossed the roof could never be the one to kill him. Shen Jianchen consoled himself. “He has three years of life left. I have the patience to wait for that day.”
[ZH] 他颤抖着手打开荷包，取出信，展开用心念了一遍，已是泪眼婆娑，再取出刻有姐姐名字的玉，更确信都是姐姐的遗物。
[EN] Reaching the roof above the room where the others sat, Qianli Xun eased onto her stomach, shifted a tile aside, and pressed her ear to the gap. She could make out most of what was being said below.
[ZH] “难怪我一直寻找姐姐没有任何结果，原来她早已离开人世。可你为何不将手中之物早些交给我？”
[EN] Once everyone had taken their seats inside, Liang Shicheng pressed without delay, “Elder Madam, do you know where my sister is?”
[ZH] 夏老太太解释道：“不是没想着找您，去过好几次京城，宫门口的人说根本没有此人。后来内侍大人得到官家重用，才渐渐听到您的名号，又担心找您会被认为陈家想攀高枝，便一拖再拖。要不是为家中之事，恐怕我这辈子都不会将荷包拿出来。”
[EN] Old Madam Xia let out a sigh. “Alas, she passed from this world over fifty years ago. Before she went, she asked me to place this into her younger brother’s own hands, telling me he had entered the palace to serve the nobles within.”
[ZH] “我姐姐葬在何处？”
[EN] As she spoke, she held out a large purple silk pouch. A servant received it and carried it to Imperial Attendant Liang.
[ZH] “夏家陵园。”
[EN] With trembling hands, he undid the pouch and drew out the letter. He unfolded it and read every line with full attention, his eyes swimming with tears by the end. When he pulled out the jade piece carved with his sister’s name, all doubt vanished—these were indeed his sister’s keepsakes.
[ZH] “你是怎么认识我姐姐的？”
[EN] “No wonder all my years of searching turned up nothing,” he said. “She has been gone all this time. Yet why did you not bring these things to me sooner?”
[ZH] “我那会儿还未出嫁，来到街上，见一姑娘跪在街边卖自己，我甚是心疼，主动将她买回家，这位姑娘便是内侍大人的姐姐梁师慧。我与师慧甚觉投缘，结为姐妹，让她待在夏家。我成亲后，父母便认她做女儿，想着日后也给她寻一个好婆家，谁知她后来得了怪病，根本治不好，不久离开人世。离开前给了我那个荷包，嘱咐我她有一个亲弟弟，被迫入宫，让我帮她找到人，亲手把荷包给了，今日总算帮她完成遗愿。”
[EN] Old Madam Xia explained, “It is not that we never tried to find you. We went to the capital several times, only for the guards at the palace gates to tell us no such person existed. Later, once you won His Majesty’s favor and your name began to spread, I worried that seeking you out would look as though the Chen family were trying to curry favor with the mighty, so I kept putting it off. Had it not been for our family’s current trouble, I doubt I would have brought this pouch out in my lifetime.”
[ZH] 梁师成更是心痛不已，眼泪“吧嗒，吧嗒”往下掉：“姐姐比我大八岁，我们从小没了父母，生活艰辛，她既当爹又当娘照顾我，我不想她辛苦，主动净身进了宫，就是希望她有好日子过，可等我有能力让她过上好日子时，却找不到她……”
[EN] “Where is my sister buried?”
[ZH] 梁内侍开始抽泣，以情动人的效果已拉满。夏老太太顺势跪下，陈浩宇和陈江树也跟着跪下。
[EN] “In the Xia family cemetery.”
[ZH] “请梁内侍看在我与师慧妹妹有过一段情谊，对陈家高抬贵手，放过淑真。”
[EN] “How did you come to know my sister?”
[ZH] 千里寻听到这里，总算明白公爹与陈浩宇愁眉不展之事原来是为自己。她与梁内侍从未有过交集，为何梁内侍要针对她？
[EN] “I was not yet married back then. Out on the street one day, I saw a young woman kneeling by the roadside offering to sell herself. My heart went out to her, so I bought her and brought her home. That young woman was your sister, Liang Shihui. Shihui and I hit it off from the start, swore sisterhood, and kept her right there in the Xia household. After I wed, my parents adopted her as their own daughter, hoping to find her a good husband in time. Who could have known she would fall prey to a strange, incurable illness and pass away not long after? Before she died, she gave me that pouch, told me she had a younger brother who had been forced into the palace, and begged me to find him and deliver it into his own hands. Today, at long last, I have fulfilled her dying wish.”
[ZH] 她自然想到梦容，难道梦容还不愿放过她？才请到一位权高位重之人？
[EN] Grief tore at Liang Shicheng, fat tears plopping down one after another. “My sister was eight years older than me. We lost our parents as children and scraped by in misery. She raised me as both father and mother. I could not bear to watch her toil, so I had myself castrated and entered the palace of my own accord, hoping to give her a comfortable life. Yet by the time I had the means to give her that life, I could not find her anywhere...”
[ZH] 为了进一步作出判断，千里寻继续认真听。
[EN] Imperial Attendant Liang began to sob. The appeal to his emotions had reached its peak. Seizing the moment, Old Madam Xia dropped to her knees, and Chen Haoyu and Chen Jianglin knelt right behind her.
[ZH] 梁内侍止住抽泣声，抬起眼，用手中的帕子擦着眼泪：“老人家快快请起，你有恩于我姐姐，我自然不会动陈家。看在你的面子上，我还会帮陈家拓展在汴梁的生意，往后有我在，谁也动不了陈家，”
[EN] “For the sake of the bond I once shared with Sister Shihui, I beg you, Imperial Attendant Liang, to show mercy to the Chen family and spare Shuzhen.”
[ZH] 三人听后自然开心，齐声称谢：“多谢内侍大人！”
[EN] Listening from above, Qianli Xun grasped at last that the trouble weighing down her father-in-law and Chen Haoyu was all about her. She had never crossed paths with Imperial Attendant Liang—why on earth was he targeting her? Her thoughts jumped straight to Mengrong. Did Mengrong still refuse to let her go, going so far as to enlist someone of such immense power? To be sure of her hunch, Qianli Xun kept listening with rapt attention.
[ZH] 父子两个将夏老太太扶起来继续坐好，心里无不认为淑真这下算是安全。
[EN] Imperial Attendant Liang checked his sobs, lifted his gaze, and dabbed his tears with a handkerchief. “Please rise at once, Elder Madam. You showed great kindness to my sister, so of course I will not lay a finger on the Chen family. On your account, I will even help the Chens expand their business in Bianliang. From now on, with me standing behind you, no one will dare touch the Chen family.”
[ZH] 谁知梁师成继续说：“至于女词人，我还是想见她一面。你们放心只是见一面，没有别的意思。”
[EN] Overjoyed at his words, the three of them gave thanks in unison. “Thank you, Imperial Attendant Liang!”
[ZH] 三人脸上的笑容瞬间消失。
[EN] Father and son helped Old Madam Xia back to her seat, all three convinced that Shuzhen was safe now.
[ZH] 陈江林认为如果只是见一面又何必大动干戈？
[EN] Yet Liang Shicheng went on, “As for the poetess, I would still like to meet her once. Rest assured, it is just a meeting—nothing more.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇忍不住说道：“内侍大人若想见我家娘子，明日我陪她一起登门拜访。”
[EN] The smiles vanished from all three faces in an instant. If all he wanted was a simple meeting, Chen Jianglin thought, why go to such drastic lengths?
[ZH] 梁师成红红的眼圈衬着微笑，看上去很不协调：“不必陈公子亲自送她前来，既然我不会再拿陈家做文章，两日时间取消，今日未时，林统制会到陈家将女词人请到柳岸别馆做客。”
[EN] Unable to hold back, Chen Haoyu spoke up. “If Imperial Attendant Liang wishes to see my wife, I will accompany her to pay a formal call tomorrow.”
[ZH] 听到此处，千里寻更加确定与梦容有关，恐怕她到柳岸别馆走一趟凶多吉少。
[EN] The smile paired with Liang Shicheng’s red-rimmed eyes looked unsettling and out of place. “There is no need for Young Master Chen to escort her in person. Since I will no longer make trouble for the Chen family, the two-day deadline is off. Today during the hour of wei, between one and three in the afternoon, Commander Lin will call at Chen Manor to invite the poetess to Liu’an Villa as my guest.”
[ZH] 千里寻很想冲下去将梁师成打得稀巴烂，想到陈家长辈对她有情有义，若直接下去，只怕会牵连陈家。再说别馆内有许多侍卫，恐怕也占不到多少便宜，于是迅速用轻功飞檐走壁离开。
[EN] Hearing that, Qianli Xun felt even more certain Mengrong was behind this. A trip to Liu’an Villa would bode far more peril than good. Part of her itched to drop straight down and beat Liang Shicheng to a pulp. Still, the Chen elders had treated her with loyalty and warmth; storming in now would only drag the Chen family into disaster. Besides, the villa teemed with guards, so the odds were not in her favor. Using her light-foot technique, she bounded across the eaves and walls and sped away.
[ZH] 陈江林本想说两句，被母亲阻止，只听夏老太太说：“既然梁内侍执意要见我孙媳，未时恭候大驾。”
[EN] Inside, Chen Jianglin opened his mouth to protest, but his mother stopped him. Old Madam Xia spoke up instead. “Since Imperial Attendant Liang insists on meeting my granddaughter-in-law, we shall await his envoy at the hour of wei.”
[ZH] 离开柳岸别馆后，三人又乘坐马车原路返回。
[EN] After leaving Liu’an Villa, the three boarded their carriage and rode back the way they had come.
[ZH] 马车上，陈浩宇担忧地问：“奶奶，您真的想让梁内侍到陈家带走我娘子？”
[EN] Inside the carriage, Chen Haoyu asked in alarm, “Grandmother, do you really intend to let Imperial Attendant Liang’s people come to Chen Manor and haul my wife away?”
[ZH] 老太太解释道：“你把奶奶看成什么人？梁内侍一心想见淑真，你们父子说什么都没用，还不如赶紧回家想办法。现在已无两难之选，只剩下保护淑真一桩难事，应付起来倒也轻松。离未时还有一个时辰，我想好了，回去后让淑真到乡下躲躲，林觉来陈家找不到人，只能空手而归。”
[EN] The old madam explained, “What sort of person do you take your grandmother for? Imperial Attendant Liang has his heart set on seeing Shuzhen. Nothing you or your father said would have swayed him—better to hurry home and devise a plan. We no longer face an impossible choice between the family and her; now our sole challenge is protecting Shuzhen, which is far easier to handle. We still have a full shichen—two hours—before the hour of wei. I have thought it through. Once we get back, we will send Shuzhen into hiding in the countryside. When Lin Jue arrives at Chen Manor and finds no one there, he will have no choice but to leave empty-handed.”
[ZH] 陈江林担忧道：“只怕找不到淑真，梁内侍又会怪罪陈家。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin frowned in worry. “I fear that if he cannot find Shuzhen, Imperial Attendant Liang will take it out on the Chen family all over again.”
[ZH] “我对她姐姐有恩，他绝不会为难陈家。还好我没有早早把荷包送给他，否则今日又如何能救陈家？”夏老太太多少对自己曾经的决定感到庆幸。
[EN] “I was his sister’s benefactress. He will never bring harm to the Chens. Thank goodness I did not hand that pouch over years ago, or how else could I have saved this family today?” Old Madam Xia felt a measure of relief over her past choice.
[ZH] 凭着对母亲的了解，陈江林做出判断：“我猜母亲之所以后来没找梁内侍送荷包，肯定有原因；或者说，母亲是想选择合适的时机拿出。”
[EN] Knowing his mother well, Chen Jianglin hazarded a guess. “I suspect Mother had another reason for holding back the pouch all those years—or perhaps you were waiting for the right moment to bring it out.”
[ZH] 夏老太太赞道：“你说对一半，的确另有原因。起初是没找到人，只能搁置；后来听说了他，可他已得官家重用，成为百姓口中诟病的奸臣，我不愿陈家与此人扯上任何关系，才把荷包藏起来，本以为永远不会送到他手中，可他为难陈家，不得已才拿出。”
[EN] Old Madam Xia nodded in approval. “You are half right. There was indeed another reason. At first I could not track him down, so I set it aside. By the time word of him reached me, he had risen in His Majesty’s favor and become a treacherous official reviled by the common people. Wanting no ties whatsoever between the Chen family and a man like that, I hid the pouch away. I thought I would never deliver it into his hands, yet when he backed the Chens into a corner, I had no choice.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇担忧道：“现在陈家与梁师成扯上关系，慢慢大家会知道，只怕陈家将来被人诟病。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu voiced a fresh worry. “Now that the Chen family has a connection to Liang Shicheng, people will find out sooner or later. I am afraid our family will be condemned for it down the line.”
[ZH] 老太太叮嘱道：“你们父子二人记着，今日是为陈家和淑真才去攀这层关系，往后老老实实护着陈家现有的根基，不要想着往汴梁拓展生意，也不要求到梁内侍门下，只有如此才能保陈家平安。”
[EN] The old madam gave them a strict warning. “Both of you remember this well. Today we leaned on that tie only to save the Chen family and Shuzhen. Going forward, keep your heads down and guard the foundation the Chens already have. Do not dream of expanding into Bianliang, and never go begging at Imperial Attendant Liang’s door. Only then will our family stay safe.”
[ZH] 两人齐声回答，一个说“儿子记下”，一个说“孙儿记下”。
[EN] The two answered together, one saying, “Your son will remember,” and the other, “Your grandson will remember.”
[ZH] 三人回到府中时，千里寻到家有一段时间。她已做好打算，要只身面对即将发生的事，哪怕死，她也不怕。已经死过一回，多活一段时日已是幸运，她倒要瞧瞧梁师成想要将她如何？
[EN] By the time the three returned to the manor, Qianli Xun had been home for a while. Her mind was made up: she would face whatever was coming on her own. Even death did not scare her. She had died once already, so every extra day she had lived was a stroke of luck. Let her see what Liang Shicheng thought he could do to her.
[ZH] 为不引起淑真怀疑，夏老太太和陈江林没有一起来墨香苑，他们把重任交给陈浩宇。
[EN] To avoid arousing Shuzhen’s suspicions, Old Madam Xia and Chen Jianglin stayed away from Moxiang Courtyard, leaving the crucial task to Chen Haoyu.
[ZH] 陈浩宇装作若无其事的样子，一进院子开始大喊：“娘子！我回来了！有件事必须跟你说！”
[EN] Putting on an air of casual ease, Chen Haoyu stepped into the courtyard and called out at the top of his lungs, “Wife! I’m back! There’s something I have to tell you!”
[ZH] 还没等他进屋，娘子出现在他面前：“什么事？”
[EN] Before he even reached the doorway, his wife stepped out in front of him. “What is it?”
[ZH] 陈浩宇尽量表现得轻松自在：“因生意需要，要到乡下走一趟，娘子待家里无事，不如陪我一起去？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu did his best to sound breezy and carefree. “Business requires me to make a trip out to the countryside. You have nothing keeping you at home anyway—why not come along with me?”
[ZH] “去乡下的宅子住吗？”
[EN] “Are we going to stay at the country house?”
[ZH] “不是那个乡下，有些远，路上得走两天两夜。”
[EN] “Not that place. This one is farther out—two full days and nights on the road.”
[ZH] 原来陈浩宇是想带她逃走，恐怕是长辈想到的办法。
[EN] So Chen Haoyu meant to spirit her away. That had to be the elders’ plan.
[ZH] “太远了，我不想去。”
[EN] “Too far. I’m not going.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇装不下去：“不去也得去，这是命令。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu dropped the act. “You’re going whether you want to or not. That’s an order.”
[ZH] “笑话，你能命令我？”
[EN] “What a joke. Since when can you give me orders?”
[ZH] “这件事我说了算，容不得你做主。”为了娘子，陈浩宇已不管不顾，他大声喊道，“苦荞！”
[EN] “On this matter, I call the shots, and you don’t get a say.” Casting all caution aside for his wife’s sake, Chen Haoyu shouted, “Kuqiao!”
[ZH] 苦荞从屋里出来：“姑爷，何事？”
[EN] Kuqiao emerged from the room. “Young Master, what is it?”
[ZH] 陈浩宇的声音不由自主显得有些着急：“给娘子收拾些衣物！立刻！马上！我要带她去乡下！”
[EN] Panic crept into Chen Haoyu’s voice despite himself. “Pack some clothes for your mistress! Right now! This instant! I’m taking her to the countryside!”
[ZH] 苦荞不明所以，还是回屋收拾。
[EN] Clueless as to what was going on, Kuqiao went back inside to pack all the same.
[ZH] 陈浩宇又开始喊：“红豆！”
[EN] Next, Chen Haoyu bellowed, “Hongdou!”
[ZH] 红豆听到喊声，从西边跑过来：“姑爷有何吩咐？”
[EN] Hearing him shout, Hongdou ran over from the west side of the yard. “What are your orders, Young Master?”
[ZH] “把其他下人都叫齐！护送我和娘子去乡下！”
[EN] “Gather every last servant! You’re all escorting me and your mistress to the countryside!”
[ZH] 红豆更是不明所以，见小姐站在一旁不言语，只好去做。
[EN] Even more bewildered, Hongdou glanced at her mistress standing off to the side in silence, then went off to obey.
[ZH] 陈浩宇自语着：“对，差点儿忘了韦嬷嬷。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu muttered to himself, “Right—almost forgot Matron Wei.”
[ZH] 他正要跑到后院找人，被娘子从背后抱住。
[EN] He was about to bolt toward the back courtyard when his wife wrapped her arms around him from behind.
[ZH] 第一次被娘子如此拥抱，陈浩宇的内心产生莫名的幸福感。想到林觉快要带人上门，他无法再沉迷下去。
[EN] Held like this by his wife for the first time, Chen Haoyu felt an inexplicable rush of happiness flood his chest. Yet the thought of Lin Jue arriving with his men any minute snapped him out of the spell.
[ZH] “娘子，我要去找韦嬷嬷，你松开一会儿。”
[EN] “Wife, I need to go find Matron Wei. Let go for a moment.”
[ZH] 千里寻并未松开：“韦嬷嬷不在后院，她一早带着两个下人出去了。”
[EN] Qianli Xun kept her arms right where they were. “Matron Wei isn’t in the back courtyard. She took two servants and went out first thing this morning.”
[ZH] “只好不带她，我们先走。”
[EN] “Then we’ll have to leave without her. Let’s go first.”
[ZH] “为何这么着急？”
[EN] “What’s the big rush?”
[ZH] “没办法，生意上的事有些急。”
[EN] “No choice—some urgent business came up.”
[ZH] “我知道，不是生意上的事。梁内侍要见我，你不放心，才想让我到乡下避一避。”
[EN] “I already know it has nothing to do with business. Imperial Attendant Liang wants to see me, and because you’re worried sick, you want to spirit me off to the countryside to hide.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇想不通娘子怎么会知道真相？依旧掩饰道：“你从哪里听到这种说法？梁内侍是什么人？怎么可能提出见你？再说他即使提出要见你，见一见又何妨？你别瞎猜，单纯是生意上的事。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu could not fathom how his wife knew the truth, yet he kept trying to cover it up. “Where did you hear such nonsense? Who do you think Imperial Attendant Liang is? Why would a man like him ask to see you? Besides, even if he did, what would be the harm in meeting him? Stop letting your imagination run wild—it really is just business.”
[ZH] “你跟奶奶和公爹去柳岸别馆时，我一直跟在后面。你们在屋里说的话我在屋顶已听得明白。”千里寻放开陈浩宇，“你都说了，见一见又何妨？那我就去见他一见。”
[EN] “I tailed you, Grandmother, and Father-in-law all the way to Liu’an Villa. I was up on the roof and heard every word you said inside.” Qianli Xun let go of him. “You said it yourself—what’s the harm in a meeting? Fine, then. I’ll go meet him.”
[ZH] 已瞒不下去，陈浩宇转过身急切地说：“你既已知道实情，也不必瞒你。梁内侍提出见你，绝非那么简单。为了此事，他派人到茶庄找事，还把仪王和岳父大人关起来，你前去只怕有危险。我听爹讲，岳父大人说梁内侍好色，尤其喜欢成了亲的女子，我又怎能让你前去涉险？”
[EN] Realizing the charade was over, Chen Haoyu spun around, urgency pouring from his voice. “Since you already know the truth, there’s no point hiding it. Imperial Attendant Liang’s demand to see you is nowhere near that simple. To force your hand, he sent men to stir up trouble at our tea shop, and he has both Prince Yi and my father-in-law locked up. Going to him puts you in grave danger. Father told me your dad said Liang Shicheng is a lecher—with a special taste for married women. How could I ever let you walk into that trap?”
[ZH] 千里寻冷笑道：“就他？一个奸臣，想好老娘的色，没那么容易，老娘一拳打得他脑袋开花！”
[EN] Qianli Xun let out a cold laugh. “Him? A treacherous toad like that thinks he can get his hands on me? Not a chance! One punch from me and I’ll crack his skull open like a melon!”
[ZH] 娘子毫不畏惧的神情，令陈浩宇欣慰，他还是劝道：“与其硬碰硬，不如躲起来让他找不到你，也许慢慢就忘了。”
[EN] Seeing his wife’s fearless expression eased his heart a fraction, yet he still tried to reason with her. “Rather than clashing with him head-on, why not go into hiding where he can’t find you? Give it time, and maybe he’ll lose interest and forget.”
