[ZH] 千里寻只觉陈浩宇想得太过简单，连仪王和父亲朱晞颜都被梁师成关起来，恐怕不见到她，梁师成不会罢休，躲起来又有何用？
[EN] Qianli Xun thought Chen Haoyu was being far too naive. Liang Shicheng had locked up both Prince Yi and her father, Zhu Xiyan. Until he laid eyes on her, he would never back down. What good would hiding do?
[ZH] “躲起来根本不是办法，梁师成想找到我有的是办法。我已想好，大大方方去见他，看他能将我如何？”
[EN] “Hiding solves nothing. A man like Liang Shicheng has plenty of ways to hunt me down. I’ve made up my mind. I’ll walk right in and face him in the open—and see what he thinks he can do to me.”

#CH 321
[ZH] “不！我不会让你去见他，现在就跟我走！”陈浩宇拉起娘子往外走，“再耽搁下去会来不及的！”
[ZH] 苦荞将衣物收拾好，背着两个包袱出来；红豆也已将下人们叫来。他们以为小姐打算现在离开，也都跟上。
[ZH] 走出两步，千里寻甩开陈浩宇的手臂：“躲得过今日，躲不了明日，只怕我躲下去，会连累陈家……”
[EN] “No! I’m not letting you go see him—pack up and come with me right now!” Chen Haoyu grabbed his wife’s arm and pulled her toward the door. “Any longer and it’ll be too late!”
[ZH] 陈江林、姚轻琰与夏老太太不放心，赶来看个究竟，听到淑真的话，明白她已知实情。
[EN] Having packed bundles of clothes, Kuqiao emerged carrying two packs while Hongdou led the rest of the servants into the hall, assuming their mistress meant to flee at once.
[ZH] 没等淑真说完，夏老太太强调道：“目前还不会连累到陈家，你只管躲起来，其他的事长辈们自会应对。”
[EN] Two paces out, Qianli Xun wrenched her arm free of Chen Haoyu’s grip. “Dodge him today, and I can’t dodge him tomorrow! Worse, if I run off into hiding, it’s the Chen family that gets dragged down…”
[ZH] 姚轻琰上前劝道：“淑真，快跟着浩宇走吧，一会儿林统制把人带来，你想走都走不了。”
[EN] Too worried to stay inside, Chen Jianglin, Yao Qingyan, and Old Madam Xia hurried into the courtyard just in time to catch her words—realizing she already knew the whole truth.
[ZH] 陈江林也说：“梁内侍已经说了，不会动陈家，你大胆躲起来，等梁内侍离开临安后再回来。”
[EN] Before she finished, Old Madam Xia cut in firmly, “It won’t hurt the Chen family right now! Just go into hiding—leave your elders to deal with everything else.”
[ZH] 长辈待她有情有义，千里寻更不愿连累陈家。她走上前向长辈行礼：“梁师成现在不动陈家，不等于以后不会，我更不能为了自己不顾家人。既然事情因我而起，由我来解决。”
[EN] Stepping up, Yao Qingyan pleaded, “Listen to your father-in-law and go with Haoyu! Once Commander Lin marches soldiers here, you won’t be able to slip out!”
[ZH] 夏老太太继续劝道：“你既嫁到陈家就是陈家人，陈家有义务保护你，不需要你只身犯险。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin added, “Imperial Attendant Liang already promised not to move against the Chens. Hide away safely until Liang leaves Lin’an, then come right back home.”
[ZH] 千里寻却说：“我既是陈家人，更应懂得保护陈家……”
[EN] Their fierce loyalty warmed Qianli Xun’s heart—making her all the more determined never to endanger them. Stepping forward, she bowed deeply to her three elders. “Liang Shicheng isn’t touching the Chen family *yet*, which doesn’t mean he won’t later. I can’t save my own neck by abandoning my family. This mess started because of me—let me settle it.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇强调道：“有我在，不需要你来保护，快跟我走！”
[EN] Old Madam Xia insisted, “The moment you married into this house, you became a Chen! It’s our duty to protect you—we don’t need you walking into a tiger’s den alone!”
[ZH] 陈浩宇再次拉起娘子的手，千里寻第二次甩开，用轻功落在素心草堂门口。
[EN] “Because I am a Chen,” Qianli Xun answered steady and clear, “it’s my duty to protect this family too…”
[ZH] “我意已决，不必再劝，既是我惹下的祸，由我来承担。”千里寻走进屋内，“苦荞！还不快进屋把门关上！”
[EN] “With me around, nobody needs *you* doing the protecting!” Chen Haoyu snapped, grabbing her wrist again. “Come with me—now!”
[ZH] 苦荞虽不知发生了什么，从眼前的景况推测小姐肯定是遇到大麻烦。她也希望小姐逃走，可又不能不听话，迅速回屋，将门关上。
[EN] Shaking free a second time, Qianli Xun leaped gracefully onto Suxin Thatched Hall’s doorstep using light-foot technique.
[ZH] 陈浩宇慢了一步，跑到门前时，苦荞刚好把门关好。
[EN] “My mind is made up—stop trying to talk me out of it. I brought this trouble down; I’ll shoulder it.” Stepping inside, she barked, “Kuqiao! Get in here and shut the door!”
[ZH] 他敲打着门：“娘子！开开门！千万不能去！你去了，若有个三长两短，我该怎么办？”
[EN] Though still foggy on all the details, Kuqiao could tell from the panic outside that grave danger had struck. Part of her wanted her mistress to flee too, yet she dared not disobey—scrambling inside, she slammed the doors shut.
[ZH] 里面传出娘子的声音：“陈浩宇，天下好姑娘多的是，我若有事，你可另娶一位，肯定会比我好。”
[EN] A heartbeat behind her, Chen Haoyu reached the porch just as the bolt slid home.
[ZH] 陈浩宇很难过，手扶着门，人却不由自主溜下去，直到坐在地上，才向里面大声说：“你从来没真正爱过我，才会如此无情，想要舍我而去，是也不是？”
[EN] He pounded hard against the wood. “Wife! Open up! Don’t go—you mustn’t! If anything happens to you, how am I supposed to go on living?!”
[ZH] 屋内，千里寻坐在书桌旁，琢磨着陈浩宇的问题，很想告诉他，她多少已对他动情，愿意给他生孩子，愿意与他白头偕老。
[EN] From behind the door came his wife’s calm voice. “Chen Haoyu, this world is full of wonderful girls. If something happens to me, go marry another one—she’ll treat you ten times better than I ever did.”
[ZH] 因担心陈浩宇为了她不顾陈家，不顾他作为产业继承人肩负的责任，千里寻违心地回道：“你既知道，又何必问我？”
[EN] Heart torn apart, Chen Haoyu rested both palms flat against the door and slid straight down until he sat crumpled on the steps, shouting hoarsely at the panels, “You never truly loved me, did you?! That’s why you’re so cold—ready to throw me aside without blinking! Tell me I’m right!”
[ZH] 陈浩宇不愿相信娘子所说，敲打着门：“不！你骗我！肯定是骗我！”
[EN] Inside, seated at her desk, Qianli Xun mulled over his raw cry. How desperately she wanted to tell him she *had* started caring—that she was willing to bear his children, willing to grow old beside him till their hair turned white.
[ZH] 三位长辈见此状况，深知拗不过淑真，一起走到门前。
[EN] Yet terrified Chen Haoyu would throw away his entire family and his birthright as heir just to gamble for her life, she forced out a cruel lie. “If you already know the answer, why bother asking?”
[ZH] 夏老太太劝道：“浩宇，既然淑真意已决，我们只能顺着她。别难过，淑真有功夫，梁内侍不敢将她怎样。若梁内侍敢动她，我拼了这条老命也会护着她。”
[EN] Refusing to believe it, Chen Haoyu hammered his fists against the door. “No! You’re lying to me! I know you’re lying!”
[ZH] 陈浩宇的声音中略带哭腔：“连仪王拿梁内侍都没办法，我们又能做什么？”
[EN] Watching from the yard, the three elders knew nothing on earth could bend Shuzhen once her mind locked. Walking over together, Old Madam Xia put a hand on her grandson’s shoulder. “Haoyu, since Shuzhen has decided, we have to let her handle this her way. Don’t break down—Shuzhen knows martial arts; Liang Shicheng won’t dare bully her easily. And if that eunuch lays a finger on her, I’ll risk this old life of mine to shield her.”
[ZH] 王管家急急匆匆来报：“老太太！大老爷！不好了！林统制带兵将陈家包围！说要把二少夫人带走！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu’s voice broke into a choked sob. “Even Prince Yi couldn’t beat Liang Shicheng—what chance do we stand?!”
[ZH] 未时还没到，林觉便带人前来，看来梁内侍是担心人逃走。事已至此，只能面对。
[EN] Just then Butler Wang sprinted into the courtyard, gasping for breath. “Old Madam! Eldest Master! Disaster! Commander Lin has surrounded Chen Manor with troops—demanding we hand over Second Young Madam!”
[ZH] 陈浩宇迅速从地上爬起来，向门外跑去；三位长辈顾不上其他，也跟着出去。
[EN] Not even the Hour of Wei yet, and Lin Jue was already at the gates—clearly Liang Shicheng feared his prey might bolt. No escaping this now.
[ZH] 外面安静下来，苦荞才大胆问：“小姐，梁内侍要见您，该不会是因为仪王妃？”
[EN] Scrambling off the steps, Chen Haoyu sprinted toward the main gate with the three elders hurrying right behind him.
[ZH] “八成是。”
[EN] Once the yard outside fell quiet, Kuqiao finally found her voice. “Miss… is Imperial Attendant Liang demanding to see you because of Princess Consort Yi?”
[ZH] “既如此您更不该去见他。”
[EN] “Eight times out of ten, yes.”
[ZH] “既如此我更该去，与梦容的恩怨也该有个了结。”
[EN] “Then you really shouldn’t go see him!”
[ZH] “小姐打算如何了结？”
[EN] “All the more reason to go. This score between Mengrong and me has dragged on long enough—time to settle it.”
[ZH] “还没想好，走一步看一步。”
[EN] “How do you plan to settle it, Miss?”
[ZH] 千里寻站起来走向门口。
[EN] “Haven’t figured that out yet. One step at a time.”
[ZH] 苦荞跪下央求道：“小姐，您不能去，您若去了只怕会有生命危险。”
[EN] Rising, Qianli Xun walked toward the door.
[ZH] 千里寻转身将苦荞扶起：“凭我的功夫谁能害得了我？安心待着，天黑后我准回来。”
[EN] Kuqiao dropped to her knees and clutched her hem. “You can’t go, Miss! Walk through that door and you might never come back alive!”
[ZH] 千里寻推开门，走出屋外。
[EN] Turning around, Qianli Xun lifted Kuqiao gently to her feet. “With my fists, who on earth could put me down? Stay calm and wait here—I promise I’ll be back after dark.”
[ZH] 红豆、丑丫、阿升、三宝等人都站在门外。
[EN] She pulled open the door and stepped out.
[ZH] 见小姐出来，红豆带头说：“小姐，林统制手下的人没多少功夫，不如带着我们几个打出去？现在离开还来得及。”
[EN] Hongdou, Chouya, Asheng, Sanbao, and the others stood waiting in a tight cluster right outside.
[ZH] 千里寻看着眼前的几名下人，当初因失恋心情不好，为了快些从失恋中走出，才训练他们练习千里拳。她从未想过让他们练拳是为了有一日为自己以身涉险，红豆的建议她根本不会采纳。
[EN] Stepping up first, Hongdou urged, “Miss, Commander Lin’s soldiers don’t know real martial arts! Take us along and we’ll fight our way straight out—there’s still time to escape!”
[ZH] “你们几个有几斤几两我还不清楚？与外面的士兵打斗的确不在话下，可经不住林觉手下人多，一波一波上来总有招架不住的时候。还是老实在墨香苑待着，没有姑爷和长辈说的那么严重，我去去就回。”
[EN] Looking at the loyal servants she had trained herself, Qianli Xun felt a lump in her throat. Back then, nursing a broken heart, she had taught them Qianli Fist just to sweat out her frustration—never dreaming the day would come when they’d volunteer to throw their lives down for her. There was zero chance she’d take Hongdou’s offer.
[ZH] 下人们听小姐说得轻松，他们又不甚了解实情，便信以为真。
[EN] “Think I don’t know how much weight you lot carry?” she said lightly. “Hand-to-hand against those patrol soldiers? Sure, you’d hold your own. Yet Lin Jue has waves of troops—keep swinging long enough and anyone runs out of gas. Stay put right here in Moxiang Courtyard. It’s nowhere near as grim as Young Master and the elders make out. I’m just popping out and coming right back.”
[ZH] 阿升说：“小姐既这么说，我们也就放心。”
[EN] Hearing how breezy their mistress sounded—and knowing next to nothing of the danger—the servants relaxed right away.
[ZH] 千里寻见几个下人被她哄住，继续装作轻松的样子：“愣着干什么？还不赶快散了，墨香苑的活儿还多着呢，等我回来看到谁偷懒，绝不轻饶！”
[EN] Asheng grinned. “If Miss says so, then we’re not worried!”
[ZH] 下人们四散而去，各自去忙活。
[EN] Glad her bluff worked, Qianli Xun kept up the casual front. “What are you all standing around gawking for? Scatter! Moxiang Courtyard’s got plenty of chores waiting—catch anyone slacking when I get back and I won’t let them off easy!”
[ZH] 苦荞跟出来：“小姐，我陪您去门口。”
[EN] Chuckling, the servants dispersed to their tasks.
[ZH] 千里寻阻止道：“今日你别跟着，回屋点炷香守着，祈祷我快些回来，我向你保证，等点完八炷香肯定会回来。”
[EN] Kuqiao trailed at her heels. “Let me at least walk you to the front gate, Miss.”
[ZH] “真的吗？”
[EN] Qianli Xun stopped her. “Not today. Go back inside, light incense, and pray I come back quick. I give you my word—by the time eight sticks of incense burn down, I’ll walk back through that door.”
[ZH] “信不信随你。”
[EN] “Truly, Miss?”
[ZH] “我信，我信，这就回去点香，每一炷我都会虔诚祈祷。”
[EN] “Believe it or don’t.”
[ZH] 看到苦荞兴冲冲进屋去点香，千里寻自语道：“总得给你些希望，省得你非要跟着我去。”
[EN] “I believe you! I believe you! I’ll go light the first stick right now and pray hard over every single one!”
[ZH] 解决完麻烦的下人，千里寻只身一人向大门外走去。
[EN] Watching Kuqiao hurry indoors cheerfully to fetch incense, Qianli Xun murmured to herself, “At least give you something to hold onto, so you don’t insist on tagging behind me.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇第一个跑到大门口，见林觉身着统制的官服，一副道貌岸然的样子，陈浩宇一拳打在林觉的脸上。
[EN] With the pesky servants safely sidetracked, Qianli Xun strode alone toward the main gate.
[ZH] “你个混蛋！敢带人来包围陈家，看我不打死你！”
[EN] First to reach the gate, Chen Haoyu spotted Lin Jue standing outside in crisp military commander’s robes looking thoroughly pleased with himself—and without a second thought drove a fist straight into Lin Jue’s face!
[ZH] 林觉冷不丁被陈浩宇打了一拳，气不打一处来：“陈浩宇！你敢殴打官员！小心我治你的罪！”
[EN] “You bastard! Daring to march soldiers around the Chen estate—I’ll beat you to death!”
[ZH] “就你那一副小人嘴脸！还敢自称官员？我打的就是你！”
[EN] Blindsided by the punch, Lin Jue exploded in rage. “Chen Haoyu! Assaulting a court official—watch me haul you in for treason!”
[ZH] 陈浩宇又使出一拳，被林觉挡住：“朱淑真呢？赶紧让她出来！”
[EN] “With that slimy rat face of yours, you dare call yourself an official?! *You’re* the exact bastard I’m punching!”
[ZH] “你别想把我娘子带走！”陈浩宇又向林觉打去。
[EN] Chen Haoyu swung a second punch, which Lin Jue caught easily in his palm. “Where’s Zhu Shuzhen? Bring her out right now!”
[ZH] 两人对打了几下，林觉将陈浩宇打得退出几步：“就你那两下子还有脸跟我打？”
[EN] “Over my dead body!” Chen Haoyu lunged again.
[ZH] 三位长辈跟了过来，姚轻琰扶住儿子。
[EN] Trading two quick blows, Lin Jue shoved Chen Haoyu stumbling backward several paces. “With pathetic skills like yours, you still dare pick a fight with me?”
[ZH] 老太太关切地说：“打他又有何用？要见淑真的人又不是他？”
[EN] Arriving just behind, Yao Qingyan caught her son’s arm while Old Madam Xia stepped forward sharply. “What good does hitting *him* do? He isn’t even the one demanding Shuzhen!”
[ZH] 陈浩宇说：“我看见他就来气。”
[EN] “Just looking at his face makes my blood boil!” Chen Haoyu snarled.
[ZH] 陈江林上前有礼道：“林统制别跟浩宇一般见识，未时还没到，你怎么这么早过来？”
[EN] Stepping forward politely, Chen Jianglin said, “Don’t take Haoyu to heart, Commander Lin. It isn’t even the Hour of Wei yet—what brings you here so early?”
[ZH] 林觉整理好身上的官服：“内侍大人担心出现意外，着急慌忙派人告诉我要早些过来，我不敢耽搁，便早早前来。朱淑真呢？赶紧让她随我去见内侍大人，只要她乖乖听话，说不定对她对你们陈家都有好处。”
[EN] Straightening his ruffled uniform, Lin Jue replied coldly, “Imperial Attendant Liang worried something might go wrong and rushed word to bring her early. I dared not delay. Now where is Zhu Shuzhen? Have her come meet the Lord Attendant quietly. If she plays nice, who knows—it might turn out well for her *and* the Chen family.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇大声怼道：“你转告梁内侍，我娘子不会去柳岸别馆见他，永远不会！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu shouted back, “Go tell Liang Shicheng my wife isn’t setting one foot inside Liu’an Villa—not ever!”
[ZH] 林觉毫不示弱：“内侍大人说了，他念夏老太太的恩情，不会将陈家怎样；倘若陈家不愿交出女词人，他会将恩情暂且放在一边，该怎么收拾陈家便怎么收拾。若陈家乖乖将女词人交出，恩情还是恩情，往后他便是陈家最大的靠山。”
[EN] Lin Jue didn’t flinch an inch. “The Lord Attendant says he still honors his debt to Old Madam Xia and won’t touch a hair on the Chen family—unless the Chens refuse to hand over the poetess. In that case, old favors go straight out the window, and he’ll crush your house however he pleases. Hand her over nicely, though, and the favor stays intact—from here on out, he’ll be the strongest patron backing the Chen clan!”
[ZH] 门口发生大事，也惊动陈家其他人。
[EN] The commotion at the front gate drew the rest of the household running.
[ZH] 陈江树和陈江木去陈家的铺子忙活，因此不在家；陈浩飞为准备来年的殿试，近期到学堂跟着老师学习，甚是刻苦，故而人也不在家中。
[EN] Chen Jiangshu and Chen Jiangmu were out tending the family’s shops; Chen Haofei was away grinding hard at his academy under his tutor ahead of next spring’s palace examination.
[ZH] 只剩包氏和崔氏，她们来到门口，正好听到林觉说的话。
[EN] That left only Madam Bao and Madam Cui, who hurried up just in time to catch Lin Jue’s speech.
[ZH] 崔氏虽不知具体发生了什么，但林统制的话她是听到心里。儿子刻苦攻读无非是为考取功名，若朝中有靠山，岂不更容易？她走到母亲跟前，提醒道：“母亲，绝对不能得罪梁内侍。”
[EN] Though clueless about the background, Madam Cui absorbed every word about backing. Her son was studying himself blind for official rank—with a powerful court patron behind them, wouldn’t promotion come easy? Stepping right up beside Old Madam Xia, she whispered urgently, “Mother! Whatever we do, we must never offend Imperial Attendant Liang!”
[ZH] 夏老太太狠狠瞪了崔氏一眼：“你懂什么？”
[EN] Old Madam Xia shot Madam Cui a blistering glare. “What do *you* know?!”
[ZH] 包氏问道：“到底发生了什么事？”
[EN] Madam Bao asked plaintively, “What on earth is going on here?”
[ZH] 眼下的景况没有人有心情回答包氏的问题，她见无人搭理自己，更是伤感，自从儿子被关进大牢，她只觉自己在陈家的地位不如从前。
[EN] Nobody had the patience to explain things to Madam Bao; left ignored again, a fresh sting of bitterness pricked her chest—ever since her son landed in prison, her standing inside this manor had shrunk to nothing.
[ZH] 陈浩宇对林觉说道：“陈家不需要梁内侍那样的靠山！”
[EN] “The Chen family doesn’t need a patron like Imperial Attendant Liang!” Chen Haoyu roared at Lin Jue.
[ZH] 林觉警告道：“念在以往陈家对我不错，你刚才说的话我就当没听见。还是快些将朱淑真交出来，否则别怪我翻脸不认人。”
[EN] Lin Jue warned smoothly, “Seeing how decent the Chens always were to me, I’ll pretend I didn’t hear that. Hand over Zhu Shuzhen right now, or don’t blame me when I drop all pleasantries!”
[ZH] 陈江林被儿子感染，也想男人一回：“林统制想带走淑真，先从我父子的尸体上爬过去再说！”
[EN] Fired up by his son’s backbone, Chen Jianglin stepped forward like a lion. “If Commander Lin wants to take Shuzhen, step over my corpse and my son’s first!”
[ZH] 夏老太太也说道：“还有我这把老骨头！”
[EN] Old Madam Xia planted her cane firm. “And over these old bones too!”
[ZH] 姚轻琰也不示弱：“还有我！淑真是我儿媳，我不能弃她不顾！”
[EN] Yao Qingyan stood shoulder to shoulder with them. “And me! Shuzhen is my daughter-in-law—I’m not abandoning her!”
[ZH] 林觉一时被惊得愣住，陈家人如此待朱淑真，难怪她乐意嫁给陈浩宇，还乐意跟他过日子。想想当初自己待她的景况，自愧不如，怪不得她会使尽浑身解数想要离开他。
[EN] Lin Jue froze dumbstruck. Look at how fiercely the entire Chen household stood behind Zhu Shuzhen—no wonder she had married Chen Haoyu so willingly and settled right into life with him. Comparing that to how wretchedly *he* had treated her made shame burn his throat; no wonder she had moved Heaven and Earth to escape his house.
[ZH] 很快林觉回过神来，向外喊道：“还不快些进来？将陈家人拿了！”
[EN] Snapping back to duty, Lin Jue barked outward, “What are you waiting for?! Storm in and arrest the whole Chen family!”
[ZH] 进来几名士兵，还未到陈家人跟前，被千里寻连拳带脚打倒在地。她站稳后，质问道：“林觉！你找的人是老娘，又何必为难我的家人？”
[EN] Soldiers surged through the gateway—only to be sent flying backward by Qianli Xun’s fists and kicks before getting anywhere near the Chens. Planting her feet steady, she glared straight at Lin Jue. “Lin Jue! I’m the one you came for—why bully my family?!”
[ZH] “我的家人”四个字令林觉心里老大不爽，朱淑真嫁过自己一回，从来没拿他当家人看待。仔细推算她嫁到陈家满打满算也就六七个月，跟当初嫁到林家的时日差不多。同样的时日与他闹和离，却亲切地将陈家人当亲人看待，嫉妒之情油然而生。
[EN] “My family.” Those two words grated like gravel down Lin Jue’s spine. During their marriage, Zhu Shuzhen had never once looked at him or his kin as *family*. Counting the months, she’d barely been at Chen Manor six or seven months—about the same stretch she’d stayed with the Lins! Same span of time: with him she’d fought tooth and nail for *heli* (mutual divorce), yet these Chens she called *my family* with fierce devotion. Bitter jealousy coiled inside his gut.
[ZH] “既然来了，老老实实跟我去见内侍大人。”
[EN] “Since you’re out here, come peacefully to see the Lord Attendant.”
[ZH] 千里寻不想再多言，直接往出走。
[EN] Wasting no more breath, Qianli Xun walked straight forward.
[ZH] “娘子！”身后传来陈浩宇的声音。
[EN] “Wife!” Chen Haoyu called desperately from behind.
[ZH] 千里寻站住，装出若无其事的表情后转过身：“我很快会回来，安心在家等我。”
[EN] Qianli Xun halted, pasted an unbothered smile onto her face, and turned back. “I’ll be home soon. Wait quietly for me inside.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇再次提出要求：“不要去！”
[EN] “Don’t go!” Chen Haoyu pleaded again.
[ZH] 千里寻面带笑容，没有做出回应。她扫视一眼家人，奶奶、公爹和婆婆，他们眼中充满担忧以及阻止她的渴望。
[EN] Smiling faintly without replying, she swept her gaze across her family—Grandmother, Father-in-law, Mother-in-law, every pair of eyes begging her to stay.
[ZH] 千里寻本想跪下向三位长辈磕头，感谢他们几个月来带给她的亲情。可若跪下，他们肯定怀疑她会一去不复返，恐怕拼了命也要阻止她前去，到时既没救下自己，也会把陈家搭进去，得不偿失。
[EN] For a second she wanted to drop to her knees and kowtow to the three elders to thank them for every ounce of warmth they’d shown her these past months. Yet kowtowing would tip them off she didn’t expect to return, and they’d throw their lives in her path to stop her—ruining both her and the Chens for nothing.
[ZH] 千里寻狠心转过身，往大门外走去。
[EN] Hardening her heart, Qianli Xun turned around and walked straight out the main gate.
[ZH] 陈浩宇和家人想要靠近。
[EN] Chen Haoyu and his family lunged after her.
[ZH] 林觉一挥手，许多士兵过来，手举利刃，像一堵墙一样挡在他们面前。陈家众人只能眼睁睁地目睹淑真走出陈府，走上马车。
[EN] With one wave of Lin Jue’s hand, soldiers snapped naked swords into a gleaming wall barring the gateway. Helpless, the Chen household could only watch Shuzhen cross the threshold and climb into the waiting carriage.
[ZH] 陈浩宇向外大声喊道：“娘子！我等你回来！不管怎样！你都必须给我回来！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu yelled with everything in his lungs, “Wife! I’m waiting right here! No matter what happens, you *have* to come back to me!”
[ZH] 千里寻在马车里听到陈浩宇的声音，心中说道：“陈浩宇，如若我能顺利回家，便取消两年约定，早些与你圆房。”
[EN] Inside the carriage, hearing his voice carry clear across the courtyard, Qianli Xun promised silently: *Chen Haoyu—if I walk out of this alive and make it home safe, I’m canceling our two-year deal. We’ll consummate this marriage right away.*
[ZH] 马车已启动，林觉坐在对面，一直盯着朱淑真，她还是那么美，难怪梁内侍点名要见她。
[EN] As the carriage rolled forward, Lin Jue sat opposite her, eyes glued to Zhu Shuzhen. She still looked breathtaking—no wonder Imperial Attendant Liang had singled her out by name.
[ZH] 林觉多少有些不舍和后悔：“你这么主动前去，就不怕梁内侍对你起歹心？”
[EN] A twinge of reluctance—even regret—tugged at Lin Jue’s chest. “Walking in there on your own like this… aren’t you scared Liang Shicheng has filthy plans for you?”
[ZH] 千里寻瞪了林觉一眼：“什么是老娘主动？明明是你带着人包围陈家，逼得老娘不得不跟你上了马车。”
[EN] Qianli Xun shot him a scorching glare. “Who walked in on her own?! You’re the one who surrounded Chen Manor with troops and forced me onto this carriage!”
[ZH] “你以为我愿意？我也是身在官场不得已，梁内侍和新来的李知府哪一个我敢得罪？”
[EN] “Think I wanted to? A man in officialdom has no choice—can I afford to cross Imperial Attendant Liang or the new Prefect Li?”
[ZH] “哼！别把自己说得那么可怜，你不过是趋炎附势之辈，老娘最瞧不上你这号人！”
[EN] “Hmph! Spare me the sob story! You’re nothing but a spineless bootlicker chasing power—people like you make me sick!”
[ZH] 被朱淑真说中，林觉毫不在乎：“别把我说得这么不堪，这世道有几个人不趋炎附势？恐怕屈指可数。”
[EN] Hit dead on the mark, Lin Jue shrugged carelessly. “Paint me ugly all you want. How many folks in this world *don’t* chase power and clout? Count them on one hand.”
[ZH] 千里寻又“哼”了一声，懒得再理会林觉。
[EN] Qianli Xun let out another cold snort and looked away, done wasting words on him.
[ZH] “喂，看在我们两个曾经夫妻一场，好心提醒你，梁内侍那方面很变态，喜欢用针扎人，你得小心些。”
[EN] “Hey,” Lin Jue spoke up awkwardly. “Seeing as we were husband and wife once, take one friendly warning: Imperial Attendant Liang is sick in the bedroom—loves poking women with needles. Watch yourself.”
[ZH] 千里寻不甚理解：“哪方面？”
[EN] Qianli Xun blinked. “Sick where?”
[ZH] “亏你都是成过两次婚的人，这种话都听不明白？”
[EN] “Married twice and you can’t even follow plain talk?”
[ZH] 千里寻明白过来：“这种人老娘见一个打一个。”
[EN] Catching his drift, Qianli Xun curled her lip. “Pigs like that? Every one I meet, I flatten!”
[ZH] “他可是梁内侍，官家最信任的人，你打他就不怕他治你的罪？”
[EN] “He’s Imperial Attendant Liang—His Majesty’s favorite! Touch him and aren’t you scared he’ll slap a capital charge on your head?”
[ZH] “只有像你这种趋炎附势之人才会把奸臣放在眼里，老娘才不怕他。”
[EN] “Only spineless bootlickers like you wet themselves over corrupt eunuchs. I don’t scare that easily.”
[ZH] 林觉奚落道：“别以为你拳头厉害就天不怕地不怕，告诉你，在权势面前你的拳头一文不值；我劝你实在不行让他扎几针，既保住自己，也保住陈家，何乐而不为？”
[EN] Lin Jue sneered back. “Think strong fists make you untouchable? Wake up—against real power, your fists aren’t worth a copper coin! Take my advice: if worst comes to worst, grit your teeth and let him stick a few needles in you. Keeps you alive and keeps the Chen family safe—why fight it?”
[ZH] 千里寻骂道：“闭上你的臭嘴！再胡说，小心老娘连你一起打！”
[EN] “Shut your filthy mouth!” Qianli Xun snapped. “Yabber one more word of trash and I’ll clobber you too right here in this carriage!”

#CH 322
[ZH] 官兵离开后，陈浩宇二话不说，走出大门，套好马车追赶而去。
[EN] Once the troops marched off, Chen Haoyu didn’t pause a heartbeat—bolting out the gate, he hitched a carriage and raced after them.
[EN] Still baffled, Madam Cui asked, “Sister-in-law, why on earth would Imperial Attendant Liang want to meet Shuzhen?”
[ZH] 崔氏不解地问：“大嫂，为何梁内侍要见淑真？”
[EN] Unable to guess the real reason, Yao Qingyan only murmured bleakly, “I fear this trip spells disaster for her.”
[ZH] 具体原因姚轻琰也难以猜测，只说道：“淑真这一去恐怕凶多吉少。”
[EN] Madam Bao chimed in, “Shuzhen barely sets foot outside the manor—she couldn’t possibly have offended Imperial Attendant Liang! Why did Commander Lin show up with an army hauling her off like a criminal?”
[ZH] 包氏判断道：“淑真整日待在府中，根本不可能做出得罪梁内侍的事，为何林统制带那么多人前来像抓犯人一般将她带走？”
[EN] Too heavy-hearted to answer, everyone stayed silent. Feeling sidelined again, Madam Bao swallowed another bitter lump—her standing in this household really had hit rock bottom when nobody even bothered answering her questions.
[ZH] 大家很难回答包氏的问题，都沉默着。包氏再次伤感，她的家庭地位不比从前，问出什么话都无人搭理。
[EN] Staring down the empty street for a long while, Old Madam Xia sighed at last. “What’s done is done. Worrying won’t help—leave it to Heaven now. Go back inside.”
[ZH] 盯着门口呆看了半晌，夏老太太才说：“事已至此，担心也无用，只能听天由命，回吧。”
[EN] Slowly the crowd dispersed back inside the manor to wait for whatever storm was coming.
[ZH] 众人都散去，回家等待即将到来的一切。
[EN] Outside Liu’an Villa, Lin Jue watched Zhu Shuzhen step alone through the tall gates. Once the doors shut behind her, he let out a long breath, shaking off an unwanted pang of worry and regret, and led his soldiers away.
[ZH] 柳岸别馆的门口，林觉目睹朱淑真只身进入，等大门关上后，他叹口气，疏散掉内心的担忧和不舍，才带兵离开。
[EN] By the time Chen Haoyu arrived, the gates stood locked tight. He hammered on the wood, yelling until his throat went raw: “Let my wife out! Let my wife out!” Not a single voice answered from inside.
[ZH] 陈浩宇到达时，大门已紧闭。他使劲敲打着，大喊：“放了我娘子！放了我娘子！”根本无人应答。
[EN] Spent and hoarse, Chen Haoyu slumped down against the front doors, refusing to budge until his wife walked back out.
[ZH] 喊累后陈浩宇坐在门口，靠着大门，他要在此等待娘子从里面走出来。
[EN] Inside, a servant led Qianli Xun into a cavernous chamber and vanished, slamming the door shut behind him with a heavy *bang*. Without even glancing back, Qianli Xun swept the room with sharp, wary eyes.
[ZH] 千里寻被一位下人带进一个屋子，下人出去后，门“嘭”的一声关上。她并未回头，警惕地观察着屋内的一切。
[EN] Almost bare of furniture, the vast room looked weirdly empty—except for sheer pink silk curtains hanging floor to ceiling layer after layer ahead, hiding whatever lay beyond as warm, humid mist curled out from behind the drapes.
[ZH] 屋子很大，没有什么家具，显得空荡荡的。眼前自上而下垂下一层一层的粉色薄纱，遮挡住视线，从薄纱后面时不时散发出热气。
[EN] *Could Imperial Attendant Liang be taking a bath behind those curtains?* Qianli Xun wondered.
[ZH] 千里寻猜想：“难道梁内侍正在薄纱后沐浴？”
[EN] Then giggles drifted through the mist—definitely more than one woman, several by the sound of it.
[ZH] 传来一阵女子的笑声，听上去不止一名女子，应该有好几个。
[EN] Curiosity getting the better of her, Qianli Xun parted layer after layer of damp pink gauze until thick steam billowed around her—and there lay a sprawling indoor bathing pool. Amid rolling hot vapor, a handful of young women bobbed like lotus blooms in mist, laughing and splashing one another.
[ZH] 好奇心驱使下，千里寻掀开一层一层的薄纱，直到水雾之气越来越浓时，一个大水池出现在眼前，温热的水雾在水池中翻滚着，几个女子就像是水雾中的莲花，一个一个露出笑脸，互相嬉戏。
[EN] *Why on earth did Liang have his servant bring me here?*
[ZH] 千里寻心想：“梁内侍为何让下人将我带到此处？”
[EN] Before she could work it out, a head surfaced from the water—straggly soaked gray-streaked hair plastered around an aged, lecherous face stretched in a slimy grin. Lunging his hands at the splashing girls, he cackled, “Felt every inch of you underwater already—now whichever one I catch gets a big kiss!”
[ZH] 还没想明白时，从水里探出个脑袋，一头湿发，一张苍老的脸，一脸猥琐的笑容，伸出淫爪去抓几个女子，口中说着：“刚才在水底已将你们摸了个遍，现在我抓到谁，谁就得被我亲。”
[EN] Knowing all too well what needle torture waited if he caught them, the girls darted expertly through the pool, laughing brightly as if playing a game so Liang wouldn’t suspect their dread.
[ZH] 几个女子都清楚，一旦被梁内侍抓住，要受针刺之苦，一个个都在水中躲避着。为了不引起梁内侍怀疑，她们一边笑，一边躲，如同在水池中玩乐一般。
[EN] Well practiced at dodging, they slipped right past Liang Shicheng’s groping claws every time. Missing grab after grab, Liang’s temper was just starting to sour when through the swirling steam he spotted a striking beauty standing straight on the pool’s edge—and his lewd grin snapped back wider than ever.
[ZH] 女子们深谙此道，都能轻松躲过梁师成那双淫手。梁师成几次落空，即将恼羞成怒时，透过泛起的雾气，发现岸边站着一位美人，他的脸上再次露出淫笑。
[EN] “Since a fresh beauty’s arrived, I’ll let you lot off for now!”
[ZH] “既有美人前来，暂时放过你们。”
[EN] Swimming to the steps, Liang Shicheng climbed out wearing a single soaked silk tunic clinging to his skin, dripping beside his plastered wet hair.
[ZH] 梁师成游到岸边，从水中爬出来。他身上穿着一层衣衫，湿答答的，与他披散的湿发甚是搭配。
[EN] The girls climbed out right after him, standing there drenched and shivering without daring to change.
[ZH] 几个女子也从水中出来，她们并未着急换衣裳，就那么湿漉漉地站在梁师成身边。
[EN] Revulsion curling her lip, Qianli Xun looked straight away from the repulsive old creep.
[ZH] 千里寻很不屑地瞧着眼前的奸臣，一副猥琐的表情实在令人作呕，她将目光移到一边。
[EN] “Are you Zhu Shuzhen, the Poetess of Jiangnan?”
[ZH] “你就是江南女词人朱淑真？”
[EN] *As if the real Zhu Shuzhen ever ran into filth like this!* Stuck wearing her name, though, Qianli Xun answered coolly, “Close enough.”
[ZH] 千里寻心想朱淑真哪经历过这些？可顶着她的名头不得不承认：“算是。”
[EN] “Why aren’t you kneeling before this official?”
[ZH] “为何见本官不跪拜？”
[EN] “I kneel to Heaven, Earth, my parents, wise rulers, and loyal ministers. Which category does Imperial Attendant Liang fit into? If none of the above, spare yourself the breath.”
[ZH] “我跪天跪地跪父母跪明君忠臣，不知梁内侍属于哪一类？若都不是，还请不要强求。”
[EN] Stung by her razor-sharp insult, Liang Shicheng nearly flared up—yet admiring her lovely face, he swallowed his irritation for the moment.
[ZH] 梁师成被对方隐晦辱骂，本应生气，看在她长相娇美的份上，先不予计较。
[EN] “Know why I summoned you here?”
[ZH] “你可知我为何要见你？”
[EN] “If you don’t tell me, Lord Attendant, how would I know?”
[ZH] “大人不说我又怎知？”
[EN] Stepping closer across the wet tiles, he purred, “Think hard—who have you offended?”
[ZH] 梁师成向女词人靠近一些：“好好想想你得罪过何人？”
[EN] Mengrong flashed across Qianli Xun’s mind, though she refused to name her. “I live upright and aboveboard. I haven’t wronged a soul.”
[ZH] 千里寻想到梦容，却不愿承认：“我做人做事坦坦荡荡，从未得罪过任何人。”
[EN] “Hmph! Offending people without even realizing it only makes you deserve death faster. Someone hired me to put you in the ground.”
[ZH] “哼，得罪人而不自知，死有余辜，有人请我杀了你。”
[EN] “Even with all your power, Lord Attendant, you can’t just murder someone whenever you feel like it.”
[ZH] “即使梁内侍位高权重，也不能说杀人就杀人。”
[EN] “Cooking up an excuse to execute someone takes me less effort than turning over my palm.”
[ZH] “找个杀人的理由对我来讲易如反掌。”
[EN] “Oh? And what charge do you plan to pin on me?”
[ZH] “梁内侍打算找什么理由杀我？”
[EN] Instead of answering right away, Liang Shicheng changed tack smoothly. “I used to buy that old line about talented women being plain—never guessed you’d boast brilliance *and* a face and figure that make my chest itch just looking at you! Word is you’ve wed twice—first to Lin Jue, now to the Chens. Truth be told, I’ve never tasted what a twice-married woman feels like. Keep me happy one time today, and not only do I spare your life, I send you safely home to Chen Manor.”
[ZH] 梁师成没有着急说出理由，自顾言他：“我本以为女子有才便无貌，没想到你既有才华，还长了一副好皮囊，让我瞅着心里痒得难受。听说你成过两次婚，第一次嫁给林觉，第二次嫁到陈家。我还从来没体验过成过两次婚的女人是什么感觉，你若今日能陪我快活一回，我保证不杀你，还会把你安全送回陈府。”
[EN] Qianli Xun shot back without blinking, “Hmph! I’d drop dead on the spot before entertaining scum like you!”
[ZH] 千里寻果断拒绝：“哼！老娘就是死，也不会陪你这种人快活！”
[EN] Choking down anger, Liang Shicheng coaxed, “One afternoon of fun to buy back a whole life—quite the bargain.”
[ZH] 梁师成忍着怒气劝道：“快活一次换回一条命，很值得。”
[EN] Qianli Xun shoved a fist straight under his nose. “Got the guts to fight me one-on-one? Beat me first, then we’ll talk bargains!”
[ZH] 千里寻将拳头伸到梁师成面前：“有本事跟老娘打一架，打败老娘再来谈值或不值。”
[EN] Liang Shicheng flinched backward as the wind of her punch slapped his face and blew his wet locks straight back.
[ZH] 梁师成朝后闪去，感受到气流向面部冲击而来，就连湿发也被吹起。
[EN] Catching his balance several paces back, he hissed, “Since you’re begging for a grave, why waste my breath—”
[ZH] 待湿发平稳后，梁师成后退出几步：“既然你想找死，我又何必奉劝……”
[EN] Before the last word finished leaving his tongue, Qianli Xun’s flying kick caught him square and sent him splashing headfirst back into the pool! The soaking girls shrieked and scattered to the far wall.
[ZH] 话还未说完，千里寻一脚将梁师成踢入水中，其他女子吓得躲到一边。
[EN] Breaking the surface sputtering, Liang Shicheng roared, “Guards! Seize her!”
[ZH] 梁师成浮出水面，向外大喊：“来人！给我抓住她！”
[EN] Vaulting light across the water’s edge, Qianli Xun drove a solid fist straight into his face, then landed smooth as a feather on the dry tiles just as the doors burst open and armed imperial guards poured in clutching swords.
[ZH] 千里寻借助轻功，使出一拳，打在梁师成的脸上，然后稳稳地落在水池边。门已打开，冲进许多侍卫，手执利剑。
[EN] Face throbbing where she’d clocked him—not even daring to wipe it with soaked sleeves—Liang Shicheng screeched, “Attempted murder of a court official! Grab her! Haul her back to Bianliang for the Court of Judicial Review—put her under the executioner’s blade!”
[ZH] 梁师成的脸被打伤，不敢用湿手去摸，命令道：“她谋杀朝廷命官！把她抓起来！带回汴梁！由大理寺审问！定她一个死罪！”
[EN] Swords clashed against bare fists as brutal combat erupted around the steaming pool.
[ZH] 侍卫们与千里寻打斗起来，场面甚是激烈。
[EN] At the start, Qianli Xun dodged and counterstruck with effortless grace, knocking men down left and right—yet wave after fresh wave of imperial guards kept pouring in nonstop.
[ZH] 刚开始时，她游刃有余，应付自如，却经不住对方一波一波攻击。
[EN] Unlike sloppy mountain bandits on Mount Tianmu, these palace troops moved like seasoned killers trained to perfection.
[ZH] 这群侍卫经过严苛训练，与天目山上的匪盗比起来更难对付。
[EN] After flooring seven or eight waves straight, exhaustion dragged at her limbs while razor-sharp blades flashed closer from every side. Still she fought on with everything she had.
[ZH] 应付完七八拨人后，疲惫感袭来，加之对方个个手拿利剑，打斗的困难加大，可她依然努力应对。
[EN] Standing waist-deep in the pool watching the melee, Liang Shicheng marveled inside—*this poetess actually fights like a demon!*
[ZH] 梁师成站在水池里，盯着岸上打斗的场面，心中叹道，女词人的功夫竟如此厉害！
[EN] Half a shichen into the brawl, Qianli Xun’s arms felt like lead and her breaths turned ragged. Seeing her falter, Liang Shicheng crowed in delight, “Take her down!”
[ZH] 打了半个时辰后，女词人开始力不从心，梁师成心中暗喜，大声说：“给我抓住她！”
[EN] One split second of exhaustion cost her: a blade sliced across her back, drawing hot blood. As fiery pain blinded her for a breath, seven or eight sword points locked tight around her throat.
[ZH] 因疲惫千里寻稍有失神，被一个侍卫用剑刺破背部，鲜血流出，一阵疼痛袭来时，七八柄剑已架在她的脖子周围。
[EN] Spent beyond fighting, thinking that if Mengrong wanted her dead so badly she might as well hand her the win today, Qianli Xun shut her eyes. “If you’ve got the spine, kill me right now!”
[ZH] 她太累，无心反抗，既然梦容想让她死，今日不如满足她。千里寻闭上双眼：“有本事现在就杀了老娘！”
[EN] Climbing back out of the pool with half his face already bruised purple-black, Liang Shicheng offered smugly, “Last chance. Agree to keep me happy, and you walk free.”
[ZH] 梁师成已从水中出来，顶着半边乌青的脸，劝道：“再给你最后一次机会，若答应陪我，便放了你。”
[EN] Snapping her eyes wide, Qianli Xun spat straight into his face! “Ptui! Not even if I die right here!”
[ZH] 千里寻睁开眼，将一口唾液吐在梁师成的脸上：“呸！老娘就是死！也不会答应你！”
[EN] Saliva landed dead across his bruised cheek, stinging the fresh welt like acid. Wincing, Liang scrubbed at it with a drenched sleeve—only making the sting burn worse. Twisted with pain and fury, he roared, “Tie her up! Lock her in a prisoner cart and ship her to Bianliang right now!”
[ZH] 唾液正好落在乌青的脸上，梁师成感到伤口被蜇得刺痛，忍不住用湿答答的衣袖去擦，结果伤口见水更不舒服，他露出难受的表情：“把她绑起来，关进囚车，现在就出发送往汴梁。”
[EN] Guards hauled over heavy ropes and lashed her wrists and shoulders tight. Before they dragged her out, Qianli Xun asked evenly, “Why wait? Why not kill me on the spot?”
[ZH] 侍卫找来绳子将千里寻捆了，准备押她走时，千里寻问道：“为何不立刻杀我？”
[EN] Nursing his throbbing cheek, Liang Shicheng gave a venomous laugh. “Oh, don’t worry—you’ll die soon enough! As a court official, I need a clean charge and proper procedure before chopping heads. And look what you just handed me: *attempted murder of an imperial official*—straight to the chopping block! Let the Court of Judicial Review stamp your death warrant in Bianliang so your death stays nice and legal!”
[ZH] 梁师成忍着面部的疼痛冷笑道：“放心，迟早会杀你。我是朝廷官员，杀人总需有个名头，还应走正常程序。名头已有：谋杀朝廷命官，已够斩首之罪。还需把你送到大理寺定罪，也好名正言顺杀你。”
[EN] Qianli Xun spat straight at the floor. “Bah! Corrupt dog! Doing filthy deeds while still setting up a virtue arch for yourself! Think anyone falls for it? History will remember every rotten crime you ever pulled!”
[ZH] 千里寻唾骂道：“呸！狗官！干坏事还要给自己立牌坊！殊不知根本没用！后世的人只会记住你的罪行！”
[EN] “Get her locked in that prisoner cart—*now*!” Liang Shicheng shrieked.
[ZH] 梁师成大喊着：“还不赶紧把她关进囚车！”
[EN] Outside the gates, hours ticked past with zero sign of his wife, yet Chen Haoyu refused to leave.
[ZH] 陈浩宇在外面等了许久，不见娘子出来，他仍旧不愿离开。
[EN] Slumped gloomily by the wall, he jerked his head up at the creak of iron-rimmed wheels.
[ZH] 正当他愁眉不展时，听到车轮碾压道路的声音。
[EN] Out through Liu’an Villa’s side gate rolled a wooden prisoner cart—only unlike ordinary cages, heavy black cloth draped every side from top to bottom, hiding whoever sat inside while Villa guards marched tight around it.
[ZH] 他抬起头，从柳岸别馆的侧门出来一辆车，像是一辆囚车，与其他囚车不同的是，上面套着黑色的布子，根本看不到里面有没有人，而守卫囚车的人正是柳岸别馆的侍卫。
[EN] Jumping up, Chen Haoyu bolted straight in front of the escort. “Why hasn’t my wife come out yet?!”
[ZH] 陈浩宇冲上去，拦住侍卫：“我娘子为何还没出来？”
[EN] A guard kicked him hard in the stomach, knocking him sprawling. “High-priority convict inside! Get out of the road!”
[ZH] 侍卫一脚将陈浩宇踢开：“车上有重要犯人！还不快闪开！”
[EN] Sitting bound behind the black drapes, cloth stuffed tight between her jaws, Qianli Xun couldn’t utter a sound—and even if she could, she would sooner vanish silently than watch Chen Haoyu throw his life away fighting armed guards.
[ZH] 千里寻坐在用黑布遮盖的囚车里，她的口中塞着布子，无法发出声音。即使能出声，她宁可默默离开，也不愿陈浩宇为了她以身涉险。
[EN] Groaning through the sharp ache in his ribs, Chen Haoyu watched the black-draped cart roll slowly out of sight. A wild fear flickered—could *she* be inside? Then he shook his head: prisoner carts hauled heinous killers and traitors; how could his sweet, gentle wife ever be stuffed inside something like that?
[ZH] 陈浩宇被踢疼，忍了一会儿，目送着囚车渐渐远离他的视线。他怀疑过，里面会不会是自己的娘子？又一想，怎么可能？关在囚车里的人往往十恶不赦，她的娘子既美丽又善良，怎么可能在里面？
[EN] Dragging himself back to the front gate, Chen Haoyu sat down again to wait.
[ZH] 陈浩宇再次坐在柳岸别馆的门口耐心等待……
[EN] Night fell pitch-dark, and still no Shuzhen walked out.
[ZH] 直到天黑，娘子也没出来。
[EN] Panic boiling over, Chen Haoyu pounded the heavy gates with both fists. “Let my wife out! Let my wife out…!”
[ZH] 陈浩宇急了，使劲儿敲打门：“放我娘子出来！放我娘子出来！……”
[EN] On the fifth roar, the side door finally cracked open.
[ZH] 喊到第五声时，门被打开。
[EN] Lunging forward, Chen Haoyu grabbed the servant’s collar. “Talk! Where is my wife?!”
[ZH] 陈浩宇抓住开门的下人，急切地问：“快说！我娘子在哪里？”
[EN] “The Lord Attendant told me to pass word,” the servant muttered, “your wife tried to assassinate a court official—heinous treason! She’s under Court of Judicial Review jurisdiction now, already on the road to Bianliang. If you want any chance of saving her neck, it’ll take five hundred thousand taels of silver at minimum.”
[ZH] 下人说：“内侍大人让我告诉你，你娘子谋杀朝廷官员，罪大恶极，需交大理寺审理，已经在去汴梁的路上，如若想救人，最少也得五十万两银子。”
[EN] “Liar!” Chen Haoyu swung a wild fist, knocking the servant straight to the dirt.
[ZH] 陈浩宇抡起拳头朝下人打去：“你胡说！”
[EN] “Hitting me won’t help!” the servant yelped from the ground. “Better hurry home and gather silver instead!”
[ZH] 下人被打倒在地：“打我有什么用？还不赶紧回去准备银子？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu bellowed through the open doorway, “Wife! Wife! Where are you?!”
[ZH] 陈浩宇大声向里喊：“娘子！娘子！你在哪里？”
[EN] No wife answered—only a squad of armed guards surged forward.
[ZH] 没有将娘子喊出来，却出现许多侍卫。
[EN] The lead officer barked, “The poetess left Liu’an Villa hours ago! Want her, chase all the way to Bianliang!”
[ZH] 为首的说：“女词人已不在柳岸别馆！想找人去汴梁！”
[EN] The black-draped prisoner cart flashed back into Chen Haoyu’s mind like lightning. Half out of his wits, he sprinted back to his carriage and screamed at the driver, “Chase north toward Bianliang—fast!”
[ZH] 陈浩宇想起那辆套着黑布的囚车，像疯了一样，冲出去，对车夫喊道：“朝汴梁方向追！”
[EN] Horses galloped hard through Lin’an’s dark streets, burst out through the north gate, and pounded north along the highway.
[ZH] 马车载着陈浩宇迅速飞驰过临安的街头，从北门出去，一直向北追赶……
[EN] Miles down the dark road, the carriage slowed and rolled to a halt.
[ZH] 也不知追了多久，马车渐渐缓慢下来。
[EN] Throwing the door wide, Chen Haoyu snapped, “Why are we stopping?!”
[ZH] 陈浩宇推开车门，大声质问：“为何停下？”
[EN] The coachman turned anxiously. “Young Master, go much farther and we cross straight out of Lin’an prefecture! Even if you ride to Bianliang after Young Madam, you’ve got to tell the family first and pack properly! Wait—outside Liu’an Villa I heard them say five hundred thousand taels to Imperial Attendant Liang could spare Young Madam’s life! Saving her neck comes first—go back, settle the silver with him tonight, and *then* set out for Bianliang!”
[ZH] 车夫无奈地说：“公子，再追就该出临安地界，您要是想去汴梁找少夫人，也该回去给家人说一声，准备充分再出发。在柳岸别馆外时，我听到里面的人说只要给梁内侍五十万两银子便可保住少夫人的命。保命要紧，还是回去先把此事办了再去汴梁。”
[EN] Blinded by panic, Chen Haoyu hadn’t been thinking straight—the coachman’s blunt words snapped sense back into his head. Staring down the black, empty highway with zero trace of any cart ahead, even catching up alone unarmed wouldn’t accomplish a thing. Better race home and buy Liang off with silver first.
[ZH] 陈浩宇一时心急失了盘算，车夫的话将他点醒。他朝漆黑的远处望去，根本看不到囚车的影子，这么追下去不是办法，即使自己一路追到汴梁又能如何？还是先回去用银子搞定梁内侍。
[EN] “Turn around!”
[ZH] “回去吧。”
[EN] At his nod, the driver whipped the horses round for home.
[ZH] 得到公子允许，车夫扭转马头。
[EN] ...
[ZH] 苦荞守在素心草堂一炷一炷地点香，共点了八炷后，并未见小姐归来。终于明白小姐不过是骗她而已，她开始小声抽泣：“小姐，您一定要安全回来。”
[EN] Back inside Suxin Thatched Hall, Kuqiao sat lighting one stick of incense after another—counting straight to eight, yet no Miss walked through the door. Understanding at last her mistress had tricked her, she broke into quiet sobs. “Miss… you *have* to come back safe…”
[ZH] 苦荞继续点起一炷香，边哭边祈祷：“小姐一定没事，一定会没事……”
[EN] Lighting a ninth stick, she whispered through tears, “Nothing will happen to Miss… she’ll be fine, I know she will…”
[ZH] 两炷香后天已黑，也不见小姐的身影。苦荞再也无心点香，坐在地上哭起来：“呜呜……！”
[EN] Two more sticks burned down into pitch night, still without a shadow of her mistress. Dropping the joss sticks, Kuqiao crumpled onto the floor and wept out loud. “Wuu…!”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷从外面回来，听到苦荞的哭声，走进屋内，不见姑娘的身影，只有苦荞一人坐在地上哭。
[EN] Returning from errands outside, Matron Wei heard sobbing, pushed open the door, saw zero trace of her young lady, and found Kuqiao weeping alone on the floorboards.
[ZH] “你怎么哭起来？姑娘呢？”
[EN] “Why are you crying like that? Where is Miss?”
[ZH] 苦荞抬起泪眼：“小姐被林觉带去见梁内侍，到现在还没回来，呜呜……！”
[EN] Kuqiao raised red, flooded eyes. “Commander Lin took Miss away to see Imperial Attendant Liang, and she still isn’t back! Wuu…!”
[ZH] “见梁内侍？梁内侍为何要见姑娘？”
[EN] “See Imperial Attendant Liang?! Why on earth would Liang want to see her?!”
[ZH] “呜呜，肯定是仪王妃让梁内侍害小姐，他才差人上门逼迫陈家，小姐不愿连累陈家，主动去见梁内侍。呜呜，小姐说我只要点到第八炷香她就回来，可我点了整整十炷香，她还没回来。呜呜……！”
[EN] “Wuu… it’s got to be Princess Consort Yi putting Liang up to hurting Miss! He pressured the Chen family, and Miss went willingly so she wouldn’t drag them down! Wuu… she promised she’d be back after eight sticks of incense, and I’ve burned ten whole sticks already and she still isn’t home! Wuu…!”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷听罢，瘫坐在椅子上：“只怕姑娘已遇难，都怪我，今日不该出去忙活。”
[EN] Matron Wei slumped onto a chair, face drained of color. “Heaven forbid—something awful must have befallen her! Blame me for going out today of all days!”
[ZH] 红豆带着其他下人走进来，带头说：“韦嬷嬷，我们几个商量好，今晚冲进柳岸别馆将小姐救出来，就是死，我们也不怕。”
[EN] Hongdou marched in with the rest of the trained servants right behind her. Stepping forward, Hongdou declared fiercely, “Matron Wei, we talked it over—tonight we’re storming Liu’an Villa to break Miss out! Even if it costs our lives, we don’t care!”
[ZH] 丑丫说：“小姐待我们好，还教我们功夫，她现在遇难，我们不能不管她。”
[EN] Chouya cried, “Miss treated us like family and taught us martial arts—she’s in trouble, we can’t just sit on our hands!”
[ZH] 其他人都说：“是啊，必须救小姐！”
[EN] Every servant echoed together, “Right! We have to save Miss!”
[ZH] 姑娘培养的十名下人有情有义，韦嬷嬷甚是感动。可她深知姑娘不想任何人为她以身涉险，更明白十个下人虽有些功夫，可若去营救姑娘恐怕只会成为他人砧板上的肉。
[EN] Seeing such fierce loyalty among the ten servants Qianli Xun trained moved Matron Wei deeply. Yet knowing her mistress would never want anyone throwing their life away for her—and that ten half-trained servants storming a minister’s guards meant walking straight onto a butcher’s block—she kept her head.
[ZH] “你们若真为姑娘好，就老实待着，别辜负她一片心意，否则她的心思岂不白费？姑娘现在面对的人不是山贼匪寇，而是位高权重之人，他在官家面前随便说句话陈家就会遭灭门之灾，何况你们几人？若你们去了只会让人找到更多错处，不仅连累姑娘，还会连累陈家。”
[EN] “If you truly care about Miss, stay right where you are and don’t waste everything she sacrificed! You aren’t facing mountain thugs tonight—you’re up against a man high enough to whisper one word in His Majesty’s ear and wipe out the entire Chen clan! Even *one* slip from you lot only hands him an excuse to destroy both Miss *and* the Chens!”
[ZH] 阿升说：“如此看来的确是我们考虑不周，可总不能什么都不做？”
[EN] Asheng muttered grimly, “Put that way, we didn’t think it through… still, we can’t just sit here doing nothing!”
[ZH] “什么都不做，就是帮姑娘。”韦嬷嬷站起来，命令道，“你们都老实待着，不许走出陈府，此事陈家人自会处理，我现在就去找老爷和夫人。苦荞，看着他们！”
[EN] “Doing nothing *is* helping Miss!” Standing firm, Matron Wei ordered sharply, “Nobody steps one foot outside Chen Manor! Let the Chen masters handle this—I’m going to Eldest Master and Madam right now. Kuqiao, keep watch over them!”
[ZH] 待韦嬷嬷出去后，阿美问道：“苦荞姐姐，我们真的什么都不做吗？”
[EN] Once Matron Wei hurried out, Amei whispered, “Sister Kuqiao… are we really just sitting still?”
[ZH] 苦荞说：“听韦嬷嬷的，她与我们一样关心小姐。”
[EN] “Listen to Matron Wei,” Kuqiao sniffled. “She worries about Miss every bit as much as we do.”

#CH 323
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷还没走出墨香院，青瓜从外面回来，见到韦嬷嬷，急切地问：“听门口的下人说少夫人被官兵带走，到底出了什么事？”
[EN] Matron Wei hadn’t even stepped out of Moxiang Courtyard when Qinggua hurried back from town and caught her. “I just heard from the gatekeepers that troops hauled Young Madam away—what on earth happened?”
[EN] Without answering, Matron Wei let out a heavy sigh and walked past.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷没有回答，叹口气离开。
[EN] Qinggua rushed straight indoors to ask Chouya instead.
[ZH] 青瓜只好进屋找丑丫打听。
[EN] At the eldest master’s courtyard, servants told Matron Wei that Master and Madam had gone to Old Madam’s rooms, so she hurried there instead.
[ZH] 来到大老爷的院子，下人说大老爷和夫人去了老太太的屋子，韦嬷嬷又向老太太的院子拐去。
[EN] Shown inside Old Madam Xia’s chamber, she found the entire Chen family gathered in silence, every face grim and drawn. Clearly they had huddled together late into the night worrying over her young lady.
[ZH] 当她被允许进入老太太的屋子后，发现陈家所有人坐在里面，个个面色凝重，沉默不语。不用猜便知他们大晚上聚在一起，不过是替自家姑娘担心。
[EN] Deeply moved, Matron Wei bowed low to the elders. “Thank you all for losing sleep over Miss—let this servant thank every elder of the Chen household on her behalf.”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷怀着感激之情上前向主人们行礼：“姑娘的事让大家费心，我替她谢过陈家所有长辈。”
[EN] Old Madam Xia sighed, “Shuzhen married into our house; naturally we worry for her. Pity the Chens hold no official rank to stand up to an imperial attendant—all we can do is sit and wait.”
[ZH] 夏老太太说：“淑真嫁到陈家就是陈家人，自然要为她费心。可惜陈家无权无势，拿梁内侍没办法，只能坐着等。”
[EN] Matron Wei asked urgently, “Do Prince Yi and Master Zhu know about this?”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷问道：“仪王与朱家老爷可知此事？”
[EN] Chen Jianglin answered bleakly, “Both know—both are locked up inside Liu’an Villa right now. Imperial Attendant Liang didn’t even give Prince Yi face, let alone our in-law.”
[ZH] 陈江林解释道：“都已知，现在被关在柳岸别馆。梁内侍连仪王的面子都不给，何况亲家。”
[EN] Dread gripped Matron Wei. “If Madam Zhu finds out, she’ll faint dead away from weeping!”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷担忧道：“朱家夫人若知道此事，还不得哭晕过去。”
[EN] Yao Qingyan said softly, “We’ve kept it from Madam Zhu and Wenxin for now. They know Master Zhu is being detained, though nothing about Shuzhen.”
[ZH] 姚轻琰说：“此事瞒着亲家夫人，包括文心，她们只知梁内侍将亲家公关起来，并不知淑真的事。”
[EN] “Best hide it as long as we can.” Having come hoping for some plan, yet seeing the whole family helpless, Matron Wei scanned the room and noticed the bridegroom missing. “Where is the Young Master?”
[ZH] “能瞒一时是一时。”韦嬷嬷本想前来讨个主意，见众人如此，想必已毫无办法。她再将众人瞧一遍，发现姑爷不在，问道，“怎么没见姑爷？”
[EN] Madam Bao volunteered quickly, “Right after Shuzhen got taken away, Haoyu chased after her to Liu’an Villa.”
[ZH] 包氏帮着回答：“淑真被带走后，浩宇跟着去了柳岸别馆。”
[EN] Too sick at heart for chatter, Matron Wei sank quietly onto an empty side chair to wait alongside everyone else.
[ZH] 因心情沉重，韦嬷嬷不想说任何话，坐到一旁空的椅子上，与大家一起面色凝重等待有关姑娘的消息。
[EN] Stung that Matron Wei ignored her comment, Madam Bao fumed silently—*just a glorified maid putting on airs like a master, looking down her nose at me too!*
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷没有回应，包氏心里不舒服。心想，韦嬷嬷不过是有些身份的下人，竟然也学起主人来瞧不起自己。
[EN] Roughly half a shichen later, Chen Haoyu stumbled back through the doors, pale and unhinged, dropping straight to his knees before them.
[ZH] 大约过了半个多时辰，陈浩宇终于回来，跌跌撞撞失魂落魄地出现在众人面前，跪在地上。
[EN] “Father… Grandmother… save my wife! She’s… Imperial Attendant Liang locked her in a prisoner cart and sent her to Bianliang…”
[ZH] “爹……奶奶……，救救我娘子，她……她被梁内侍用囚车送去汴梁……”
[EN] Shock rippled through the room.
[ZH] 众人一听，顿觉事态严重。
[EN] Chen Jianglin gasped, “On what charge?!”
[ZH] 陈江林吃惊地问：“定得什么罪？”
[EN] “Attempted murder… attempted murder of a court official…”
[ZH] “谋杀……谋杀朝廷官员。”
[EN] Yao Qingyan burst into sobs. “My poor daughter-in-law! How could fate be so cruel?! Going to meet Liang once and getting slapped with a capital charge! Wuu…!”
[ZH] 姚轻琰哭起来：“可怜的儿媳妇啊！怎么这么命苦？去见一趟梁内侍就被定了这等罪！呜呜……！”
[EN] Every woman in the room reached for her handkerchief.
[ZH] 在场的女人们都开始抹眼泪。
[EN] Wiping a tear, Matron Wei asked tightly, “Is there any way to save her?”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷擦完一滴泪后，问道：“可还有救？”
[EN] That was why Chen Haoyu had raced back. “Imperial Attendant Liang says saving her takes five hundred thousand taels of silver at minimum!”
[ZH] 陈浩宇回来就是为此事，赶紧说：“梁内侍说若想救人，最少也得五十万两银子。”
[EN] A spark of hope flared back across every face, drying tears fast.
[ZH] 众人又看到希望，不再难过。
[EN] Chen Jianglin decided instantly, “Tomorrow we pack the five hundred thousand taels in silver notes and go straight back to Liu’an Villa!”
[ZH] 陈江林立刻说：“明日把五十万两银票带上再去趟柳岸别馆。”
[EN] ...
[ZH] 第二日，陈江林与陈浩宇像上次一样带着五十万两银票拜见梁内侍。
[EN] The very next morning, Chen Jianglin and Chen Haoyu brought the chest of five hundred thousand taels in silver notes back to Liu’an Villa.
[ZH] 梁师成毫不客气将银票收下，说些客套话：“女词人的罪过不小，按律当斩，看在你们陈家出手阔绰，等我回到汴梁定会上下打点，将女词人的罪行减轻，保住她的性命。”
[EN] This time Liang Shicheng swept the silver notes straight into his sleeve without blinking, rattling off oily assurances: “The poetess committed a grave crime—by law, execution. Still, seeing how generous you Chens are, once I return to Bianliang I’ll pull strings up and down to lighten her sentence and keep her head on her shoulders.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇本想问为何他的娘子来一趟柳岸别馆就得了谋杀朝廷官员的罪名，话到嘴边又咽了回去。他担心问得越多，对娘子越不利。
[EN] Chen Haoyu burned to demand how visiting Liu’an Villa once turned into “attempted murder of a court official,” yet bit his tongue hard—worried pushing too far would only hurt his wife worse.
[ZH] 父子二人被下人送出后，都觉得实在可笑，竟然要把希望寄托在害人的人身上。可陈家已被逼得无路可走，只好出此下策。
[EN] Shown out by servants, father and son felt sick at the absurdity: forced to pin all their hopes on the very villain who’d framed her. Backed into a corner with zero road left, what choice did the Chens have?
[ZH] 两人走到柳岸别馆的门口时，侍卫押着朱晞颜走来，他们瞧见后站在原地等候。
[EN] Reaching the villa’s front gate, they spotted guards marching Zhu Xiyan outside, so they waited.
[ZH] 朱晞颜被送至门口，侍卫警告道：“内侍大人说了，女词人谋杀朝廷官员，已被押往汴梁，你若再来寻事，别怪他不顾往日情谊。”
[EN] Tossed onto the street, Zhu Xiyan heard a guard bark, “Our Lord says the poetess attempted murder of a court official and is already en route to Bianliang. Come stirring trouble again, and don’t blame him for forgetting past favors!”
[ZH] 听到女儿被定下杀头的罪名，朱晞颜哭道：“可怜的淑真啊！”
[EN] Hearing his daughter carried a death-penalty charge broke Zhu Xiyan down on the spot. “My poor Shuzhen!”
[ZH] 陈江林上前安慰：“亲家别难过，先回家报个平安。我和浩宇已打点过梁内侍，他答应回到汴梁会上下疏通，减轻淑真的罪行。”
[EN] Stepping forward to steady him, Chen Jianglin whispered, “Take heart, In-law—go home first and let your household know you’re safe. Haoyu and I have already greased Liang’s palms; he promised to pull strings in Bianliang to commute Shuzhen’s sentence.”
[ZH] 朱晞颜并未责怪亲家没将女儿照顾好，他深知陈家必然会为淑真的事尽心尽力。他更了解梁师成，此人不达目的不罢休，任凭陈家怎么保护淑真，梁师成都有办法达到目的。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan didn’t dream of blaming the Chens—he knew they’d moved heaven and earth for her. Worse, he knew Liang Shicheng: a viper who never rested till he got his way, impossible for merchants like the Chens to outmaneuver.
[ZH] “梁师成只手遮天，我等拿他毫无办法，只能回去等待消息。”
[EN] “Liang Shicheng blocks out the sky—there’s nothing men like us can do except go home and wait for word.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇说：“岳父大人放心，我会亲自去汴梁盯着娘子的事。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu promised firmly, “Rest easy, Father-in-law. I’m going to Bianliang myself to watch over my wife’s case.”
[ZH] 朱晞颜满意地点点头。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan nodded with heavy gratitude.
[ZH] 三人走后，梁师成来到关仪王的屋外，让人打开大锁。
[EN] ...
[ZH] 梁师成走进后，先向坐在铁笼内的仪王行礼：“委屈殿下，今日便放您出去。”
[EN] Once the three left, Liang Shicheng strolled back to the room holding Prince Yi and ordered the heavy padlock unlocked.
[ZH] 申简辰站起来，厉声问：“快说！你将她怎么了？”
[EN] Stepping inside, he bowed mockingly toward Shen Jianchen sitting inside the iron cage. “Sorry for keeping Your Highness cooped up—we’re letting you out today.”
[ZH] 梁师成依然一副恭敬的样子：“我请女词人来不过是想与她畅聊诗词，她敬酒不吃吃罚酒，竟然想杀我。谋杀朝廷官员可是死罪，我只能派人用囚车将她送去汴梁。”
[EN] Springing upright, Shen Jianchen snapped, “Speak! What did you do with her?!”
[ZH] 申简辰一拳砸在铁笼上。
[EN] Maintaining his polite mask, Liang Shicheng sighed, “I merely invited the poetess over to discuss poetry—yet instead of appreciating kindness, she tried to murder me! Attempting to kill a court official carries a death sentence, so I had no choice but to ship her to Bianliang in a prisoner cart.”
[ZH] 梁师成装出关心的样子：“殿下要爱惜自己，别砸坏了手。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen slammed a fist hard into an iron bar.
[ZH] 梁师成说完，走到墙边。墙上有一个匕首形的凹槽。他从袖中取出一把匕首放进凹槽里，铁笼开始缓缓向上移动。
[EN] Feigning sympathy, Liang Shicheng clucked, “Mind your hands, Your Highness—wouldn’t want you bruising yourself.”
[ZH] 白风心想：“难怪我按榻上的机关毫无用处，找遍整个房间都没用，原来启动的关键是他袖中的匕首。”
[EN] Walking to the wall, he stopped before a dagger-shaped groove, drew a blade from his sleeve, and slid it into the slot. Slowly the heavy iron cage creaked upward toward the rafters.
[ZH] 等到能够从铁笼中出来时，申简辰迅速将梁师成推出门外。
[EN] *No wonder pressing the couch catch and tearing the whole room apart didn’t move a single bar,* Bai Feng thought—*the trigger was a dagger tucked up his sleeve!*
[ZH] 侍卫们围上来，白风警惕地盯着每个侍卫，负责保护好王爷。
[EN] The instant enough clearance opened beneath the bars, Shen Jianchen ducked out and shoved Liang Shicheng sprawling straight through the doorway into the yard.
[ZH] 梁师成笑道：“殿下还是省省吧，侍卫多得是，你根本杀不了我。”
[EN] Guards snapped blades up in a tight circle while Bai Feng hovered tense beside his master, ready to shield him.
[ZH] “打你一顿也可暂时解我心头之恨！”申简辰说完，命令道，“白风，抵挡一会儿，等我打够了再走。”
[EN] Scrambling up on the couch outside, Liang Shicheng smirked, “Save your breath, Your Highness—I’ve got guards swarming everywhere; you’ll never kill me.”
[ZH] “是！王爷！”白风的伤没完全好，倒也无大碍，帮王爷抵挡一会儿应该不成问题。
[EN] “Giving you a sound thrashing will do plenty to cool my rage!” Shen Jianchen barked. “Bai Feng, hold them back till I’ve beaten my fill!”
[ZH] 申简辰一拳将梁师成打倒，然后骑在他身上，左右拳开弓，在他的身上和脸上狂打一气。
[EN] “Yes, Your Highness!” Though still nursing his arrow wound, Bai Feng had plenty of fight left to buy his master time.
[ZH] 梁师成向侍卫们喊着：“快来救我！快来救我！……”
[EN] With one crushing punch, Shen Jianchen floored Liang Shicheng, straddled his chest, and hammered him left and right across face and ribs without holding back an ounce.
[ZH] 白风以灵巧的身姿竭尽全力阻挡侍卫，为王爷争取更多揍梁师成的时间。
[EN] Liang Shicheng shrieked, “Help! Get him off me! Help…!”
[ZH] 打斗激烈，他的伤口隐隐作痛，实在顶不住时，提醒道：“王爷！我快顶不住！”
[EN] Darting smoothly on light feet, Bai Feng deflected blade after blade, buying his prince extra breaths to pound the eunuch raw.
[ZH] 申简辰迅速用一拳将梁师成打晕，大喊一声：“走！”
[EN] Mid-brawl, Bai Feng’s wound ripped hot with pain; feeling himself about to buckle, he yelled, “Your Highness! I can’t hold much longer!”
[ZH] 二人用轻功从侍卫中脱身，待落地站稳后，申简辰大声说：“等他醒来，帮本王警告梁师成，若幽栖居士有个三长两短，我定让他死无葬身之地！”
[EN] Delivering one final knockout punch that left Liang Shicheng cold out, Shen Jianchen shouted, “Go!”
[ZH] 白风对着手执利剑的侍卫举起拳头，做出找打的动作，等王爷警告完众人，他跟上王爷扬长而去。
[EN] Vaulting cleanly over the guards’ heads with light-foot technique, both landed outside the wall. Turning back, Shen Jianchen roared across the courtyard, “Tell Liang Shicheng when he wakes up: if anything happens to Youqi Jushi, I’ll bury him without a grave!”
[ZH] ……
[EN] Shaking a fist tauntingly at the baffled swordsmen, Bai Feng dashed away after his prince.
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷见仪王消失两日后安全回来，甚是欣慰。可想到仪王妃，她又高兴不起来。
[EN] ...
[ZH] “王爷不在这两日，王妃按时吃药，身体好了许多，神志却越来越不清楚。要么发呆，要么说些大家听不懂的话，有时笑，有时又哭。”
[EN] Seeing Prince Yi return safe after vanishing for two days flooded Matron Zou with relief—only the state of Princess Consort Yi crushed that joy right away.
[ZH] “难道她受了什么刺激？”
[EN] “While you were gone these two days, Your Highness, Her Highness took her medicine on schedule and her body mended nicely—yet her wits keep slipping worse. Either she stares blankly into space or babbles nonsense nobody understands, laughing one second and weeping the next.”
[ZH] “应该不会，她每日待在幽林别居，哪里都没去。”
[EN] “Did something trigger another shock?”
[ZH] 申简辰来到梦容的屋子，她躺在床上睡得很香。申简辰在床边坐下，默默守着。
[EN] “Hardly—she stayed inside Secluded Forest Villa all day and never stepped out.”
[ZH] 熟睡中的乔梦容做了一个梦，她梦见自己来到一个奇怪的地方，到处金光闪闪，然后哥哥出现在她面前，唤着她的名字：“梦容！梦容！”
[EN] Walking to Mengrong’s chamber, Shen Jianchen found her sleeping peacefully on the bed and sat quietly on the edge watching over her.
[ZH] 梦中的她向哥哥走去，用手摸着哥哥的脸：“哥哥，是你吗？”
[EN] Deep in sleep, Qiao Mengrong dreamed she walked into a strange realm glowing bright gold everywhere—and right before her stood her brother, calling her name: “Mengrong! Mengrong!”
[ZH] “是我，我才是你的哥哥。”
[EN] Hurrying forward in her dream, she cupped his face in both hands. “Brother… is it really you?”
[ZH] 乔梦容抱住哥哥：“哥哥，你不要喜欢淑真，只爱我好吗？”
[EN] “It’s me. I’m your real brother.”
[ZH] “我的心里从来只有你，即使死了，心里也只有你。”
[EN] Throwing her arms around him, Qiao Mengrong cried, “Brother, don’t love Shuzhen—love only me, please?”
[ZH] “哥哥别乱说，你没死，你还好好活着。”
[EN] “My heart has never held anyone besides you. Even dead, it holds only you.”
[ZH] 乔梦容怀中抱着的哥哥很快消失，只留下声音：“我才是你的哥哥，他不是……”
[EN] “Don’t talk nonsense, Brother—you aren’t dead, you’re right here alive!”
[ZH] 乔梦容从梦中惊醒，睁开眼睛，口中唤出：“哥哥！你别走！”
[EN] Yet the brother in her arms dissolved like mist, leaving only a fading echo: “I am your brother. He isn’t…”
[ZH] 申简辰凑上前：“哥哥没走，现在回来了。”
[EN] Jolting awake from the nightmare, Qiao Mengrong snapped her eyes open and cried, “Brother! Don’t leave me!”
[ZH] 乔梦容开始流泪：“你不是我哥哥，你不是我哥哥，……”
[EN] Shen Jianchen leaned closer. “I’m not leaving—I’m back now.”
[ZH] 申简辰抓起她的手：“我是，当然是。”
[EN] Tears spilled instantly down Qiao Mengrong’s cheeks. “You aren’t my brother! You aren’t my brother…!”
[ZH] 乔梦容甩开他的手，爬起来，抱起枕头，挨到床角，一副惊恐的样子：“你不是我哥哥，不是我哥哥，……”
[EN] Reaching for her hand, he insisted gently, “I am. Of course I am.”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷说：“唉，从昨日起就变成这样，王妃还说要回汴梁找王爷，还不断地说她错了，让人看着难受。”
[EN] Flapping his hand away, she scrambled backward hugging a pillow tight against the far corner of the bed, eyes wide with terror. “You’re not my brother! Not my brother…!”
[ZH] 梦容的精神状态变得比两日前严重，申简辰厉声问道：“喜兰，老实说，我不在这两日到底发生了什么？为何王妃的病情会加重？”
[EN] Matron Zou sighed softly. “She’s been like this since yesterday, Your Highness—even begged to go back to Bianliang to look for you, repeating over and over that she was wrong. Breaks the heart to watch.”
[ZH] 喜兰跪下，老实交代：“昨日奴婢伺候王妃去茅厕，王妃出来时，还说身体里排出好些异物，觉得一身轻松。让奴婢陪她在园子里走走。走着走着，听到两个丫鬟议论，说王妃以后再也不能生孩子。王妃听后，当时没有任何表现，只要求回屋睡觉，醒来后就成这样。”
[EN] Alarmed by how sharply she had worsened in two days, Shen Jianchen snapped, “Xilan! Tell me the truth—what happened while I was gone? Why did Her Highness suddenly lose her mind like this?!”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷生气道：“为何昨日不告诉我此事？”
[EN] Xilan dropped to her knees trembling. “Yesterday your servant helped Her Highness to the latrine. Coming out, she even remarked her belly felt much lighter after passing clots and asked me to walk her around the garden for fresh air. Walking along the path, we overheard two maids gossiping—saying Princess Consort Yi could never bear children again for the rest of her life. Right then Her Highness didn’t blink or cry—just asked to go back to bed and sleep. When she woke up… she was like this.”
[ZH] 喜兰辩解道：“嬷嬷没问，我便没提。”
[EN] Matron Zou flared up. “Why didn’t you tell me yesterday?!”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷命令道：“你起来，告诉李管家，把那两个爱嚼舌的下人赶出去！”
[EN] Xilan pleaded weakly, “You didn’t ask, Matron, so I didn’t dare bring it up…”
[ZH] 喜兰回声“是”，起身去安排。
[EN] “Get up and tell Butler Li to throw those two loose-tongued wenches out of the estate immediately!”
[ZH] 申简辰瞧着靠在床角抱着枕头的梦容，她仿佛已经不认识他。
[EN] “Yes, Matron!” Scrambling up, Xilan hurried out to see it done.
[ZH] “白风！”
[EN] Watching Mengrong huddled in the corner clutching her pillow like a frightened stranger who didn’t even recognize him, Shen Jianchen called out sharply, “Bai Feng!”
[ZH] 白风从外面进来：“王爷有何吩咐？”
[EN] Bai Feng stepped inside. “Your orders, Your Highness?”
[ZH] “去城里把擅长治疗神志方面疾病的郭大夫请来。”
[EN] “Go into town and bring back Physician Guo, the specialist in mental disorders.”
[ZH] 等白风将郭大夫请来时，乔梦容已安静许多，她躺在床上，两只眼睛睁得老大，可又不知她在看着什么。
[EN] By the time Bai Feng returned with Physician Guo, Qiao Mengrong had calmed down, lying flat on her back with eyes stretched wide open, staring blindly at nothing at all.
[ZH] 郭大夫诊完脉后，叹口气：“我医术有限，治不了此病，开些安神之药，先吃着。建议你们去汴梁找大夫治，汴梁是京城，名医多。”
[EN] After taking her pulse, Physician Guo shook his head with a sigh. “My skill falls short—I cannot cure an affliction this deep. I’ll write a calming tonic to settle her spirits for now, though I advise taking her back to Bianliang. The capital has far better master physicians.”
[ZH] 申简辰本就打算回汴梁，现在千里寻已被囚车押往汴梁，他在临安待着没有任何意义，必须去汴梁救她。
[EN] Shen Jianchen had already meant to head back to Bianliang—and now that Qianli Xun had been hauled there in a prisoner cart, staying in Lin’an made zero sense. He had to go to Bianliang and save her.
[ZH] 等大夫写完药方离开后，申简辰对邹嬷嬷说：“明日就回汴梁。”
[EN] Once the physician wrote the prescription and left, Shen Jianchen told Matron Zou, “We leave for Bianliang tomorrow.”
[ZH] 他吩咐白风去趟陈家，告诉陈浩宇，若想救他的娘子，可结伴而行。
[EN] Then he sent Bai Feng straight to Chen Manor to tell Chen Haoyu that if he wanted to save his wife, they could travel north together.
[ZH] 白风来到陈府南门口时，乔家的马车停在一边，乔文仙带着南双站在门口向里张望。
[EN] ...
[ZH] 他正想上前打招呼时，陈浩宇从大门走出。
[EN] Reaching Chen Manor’s south gate, Bai Feng saw the Qiao carriage parked nearby, with Qiao Wenxian and Nan Shuang standing outside peering eagerly toward the entrance.
[ZH] 乔文仙二话不说冲上前开打，边打边说：“早知淑真嫁给你会这样！我就该阻止她跟你成亲！”
[EN] Just as Bai Feng walked up to greet them, Chen Haoyu stepped out the main gate.
[ZH] 陈浩宇并未还手，像失去知觉一般任由乔文仙在他身上捶打。
[EN] Without saying a word, Qiao Wenxian charged straight up and started swinging at him, yelling between blows, “If I’d known marrying you would bring Shuzhen to this, I never would’ve let you wed her!”
[ZH] 乔文仙那两下子根本不会伤到陈浩宇，打累后，他用双手晃着陈浩宇，大声问：“你打算怎么办？”
[EN] Standing numb, Chen Haoyu didn’t even lift a hand to block, letting Qiao Wenxian hammer away against his chest.
[ZH] 陈浩宇回道：“我打算去汴梁救她。”
[EN] Not that Qiao Wenxian’s soft scholarly fists hurt much; panting once he tired out, he seized Chen Haoyu’s shoulders and shook him hard. “What are you going to do?!”
[ZH] “算你还像个男人。”乔文仙推开陈浩宇，“我在汴梁待过一段时日，多少认识些人，回去后，我会把这些人的名字和住址一一写好，你前去只要报上我的名字，他们自会接见你，说不定还能帮到淑真。要不是家里的一妻一妾有身孕，我也会随你去汴梁。”
[EN] “I’m going to Bianliang to save her,” Chen Haoyu answered flatly.
[ZH] 陈浩宇将感激之情汇聚为两个字：“多谢。”
[EN] “At least you still act half a man.” Letting go, Qiao Wenxian added, “I lived in Bianliang a spell and know a few influential folks there. Once I get home, I’ll write down every name and address for you. Show up mentioning my name, and they’ll grant you a hearing—might even help get Shuzhen out. If my wife and concubine weren’t both pregnant right now, I’d ride north with you myself.”
[ZH] 白风走过来：“两位公子，我家王爷说了，他明日回汴梁，若陈公子想去汴梁救幽栖居士，可随我家王爷同行。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu condensed every ounce of gratitude into two words. “Thank you.”
[ZH] 乔文仙高兴道：“你跟着仪王会多一重保障，到汴梁也不用担心人生地不熟，救起淑真来更容易些。”
[EN] Stepping over, Bai Feng announced, “Gentlemen—my master bids me inform you he sets out for Bianliang tomorrow. If Young Master Chen wishes to save Youqi Jushi, you’re welcome to ride along with His Highness.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇也觉甚好：“辛苦白侍卫，帮我回复仪王殿下，明日不见不散。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian brightened at once. “Riding with Prince Yi gives you solid protection—and you won’t be groping blind in Bianliang. Makes saving Shuzhen far easier!”
[ZH] 得到确切答复，白风并不着急走，而是向里瞧。
[EN] Seeing the wisdom in it too, Chen Haoyu nodded. “Thank you for bringing word, Guard Bai. Tell His Highness I’ll meet him bright and early tomorrow without fail.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇立刻明白，向门口的下人说：“去把苦荞叫出来。”
[EN] With a firm answer in hand, Bai Feng lingered anyway, craning his neck past the gateway.
[ZH] 白风很开心：“谢谢陈公子。”
[EN] Catching on fast, Chen Haoyu called to a gatekeeper, “Go tell Kuqiao to come out.”
[ZH] 乔文仙继续向陈浩宇嘱咐事情，他毕竟与朝中官员有所接触，还需将知道的以及该注意的事向陈浩宇说个明白，省得他到汴梁跟个无头苍蝇一般。
[EN] Bai Feng grinned wide. “Many thanks, Young Master Chen!”
[ZH] 南双则凑到白风跟前：“你要回汴梁？”
[EN] Drawing Chen Haoyu aside, Qiao Wenxian rattled off careful instructions on court etiquette and which officials to tread lightly around so he wouldn’t stumble blindly around the capital.
[ZH] “对，明日就走。”
[EN] Meanwhile, Nan Shuangsidled over beside Bai Feng. “Heading back to Bianliang?”
[ZH] “我们好久没见，见过苦荞后，来乔家找我，咱们好好聊聊。”
[EN] “Yep—leaving tomorrow.”
[ZH] “好。”
[EN] “Haven’t caught up in ages. Once you finish talking with Kuqiao, drop by Qiao Manor and let’s have a real chat.”
[ZH] 叮嘱完后，乔文仙带着南双离开。
[EN] “Deal.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇则向白风拱手致谢后回府收拾东西。
[EN] Finishing his briefing, Qiao Wenxian departed with Nan Shuang.
[ZH] 苦荞终于出来，见到白风，眼泪不由流下来，哭道：“我家小姐被囚车拉去汴梁，生死未卜，该怎么办？”
[EN] Cupping his hands gratefully to Bai Feng, Chen Haoyu hurried back inside to pack.
[ZH] 白风安慰道：“王爷说明日回汴梁，如果我没猜错，他是为救幽栖居士才会这么快决定回汴梁。我来是告诉陈公子明日可与王爷同行。刚才乔公子还答应帮陈公子介绍些重要人物。所以，你不必担心，幽栖居士肯定没事。”
[EN] At last Kuqiao hurried out the gate. One look at Bai Feng, and tears burst right down her cheeks. “My Miss was hauled off to Bianliang in a prisoner cart—no telling if she’s dead or alive! What do we do?!”
[ZH] 苦荞露出笑容：“真的？”
[EN] Bai Feng hurried to reassure her. “His Highness leaves for Bianliang tomorrow—and if I’m not wrong, he’s only rushing north this fast to rescue Youqi Jushi! I just came to invite Young Master Chen to ride with us, and Young Master Qiao even promised letters introducing big shots in the capital! Don’t cry—Youqi Jushi’s going to be fine!”
[ZH] “有这么多人关心她救她，怎么会有事？”
[EN] Hope lit a tearful smile on Kuqiao’s face. “Really?”
[ZH] “这两日你去了哪里？”
[EN] “With this many people tearing down Heaven to save her, how could anything go wrong?”
[ZH] “跟王爷一起被关在柳岸别馆。”
[EN] “Where were you these past two days?”
[ZH] “你没事吧？”
[EN] “Locked up inside Liu’an Villa with His Highness.”
[ZH] “没事。”白风怕苦荞问出身上的伤来，害她担心，赶紧转移话题，“要不你也跟着一起去汴梁？”
[EN] “Are you hurt?!”
[ZH] 苦荞二话不说答应：“好，我去，为了小姐我也要去。”
[EN] “Not a scratch!” Terrified she’d ask about his wound and worry sick, Bai Feng switched subjects fast. “Why don’t you come along to Bianliang with us?”
[ZH] “太好了，我回去求王爷把你带上。”
[EN] Kuqiao agreed without blinking. “I’m coming! For Miss, I’d go anywhere!”
[ZH] “为何要仪王带我？我家公子即可。”
[EN] “Brilliant! I’ll go beg His Highness to bring you along!”
[ZH] “对对！给陈公子说说即可，看我高兴得都忘了你现在是陈家的丫鬟。”
[EN] “Why ask Prince Yi? My Young Master is going too!”
[ZH] 两人也只是片刻高兴，想到小姐的事，苦荞依旧愁眉不展。
[EN] “Oh—right, right! Just ask Young Master Chen! Haha, got so excited I forgot you’re a Chen maid now!”
[ZH] 白风又安慰一会儿，才与苦荞分别，跑去乔家找南双聊天儿。明日就要回汴梁，也不知何日还能见到他，走之前朋友之间还需阔别一番。
[EN] Their flicker of cheer faded fast as worry for Qianli Xun returned. After soothing her a bit longer, Bai Feng parted ways with Kuqiao and hurried off to Qiao Manor for one last catch-up with Nan Shuang before leaving town indefinitely.

#CH 324
[ZH] 白风来到乔府找南双，一问才知乔公子带他去了幽林别居。
[EN] Arriving at Qiao Manor looking for Nan Shuang, Bai Feng learned Young Master Qiao had taken him straight out to Secluded Forest Villa—so back Bai Feng rode.
[ZH] 白风只好往回赶。
[EN] Reaching Secluded Forest Villa, he found Nan Shuang sitting at the foot of the wooden platform while Prince Yi and Young Master Qiao sat drinking and talking up top, facing toward the Divine Willow.
[ZH] 回到幽林别居，他发现南双坐在木质高台下，王爷与乔公子则坐在高台之上，面向神柳的方向饮酒聊天儿。
[EN] Plopping down beside Nan Shuang, Bai Feng complained, “Just rode all the way to Qiao Manor looking for you, and here you are sitting in my backyard!”
[ZH] 白风挨着南双坐下：“我刚去乔府找你，结果你跑到幽林别居。”
[EN] “Been waiting here a good while already.”
[ZH] “我坐在这儿等了你好一会儿。”
[EN] “You’re my only real friend in Lin’an—hate leaving you behind.”
[ZH] “我在临安就你一个朋友，要离开还真有些舍不得。”
[EN] “Why not ask Prince Yi to stay then?”
[ZH] “要不给仪王说说别走了。”
[EN] “No chance. His Majesty stripped his Military Commissioner post—what’s left keeping him here? Besides, with Youqi Jushi hauled to Bianliang in a prisoner cart, His Highness has zero heart left for Lin’an…”
[ZH] “王爷肯定不会答应，他已不是临安节度使，待在这里有何意义？再说幽栖居士出事，被囚车拉去汴梁，王爷肯已无心在临安待下去……”
[EN] “Prince Yi really is faithful to the bone—still can’t let go of Youqi Jushi after all this time.”
[ZH] “仪王对幽栖居士可真是一往情深，到现在都放不下。”
[EN] “Keep your voice down.” Tapping a finger against his temple, Bai Feng whispered, “Princess Consort Yi’s head isn’t right anymore. Local doctors can’t cure her, so His Highness has to take her to Bianliang for treatment.”
[ZH] “小声些。”白风提醒完，用手指指脑袋，声音有意放低，“仪王妃这里出问题，临安的大夫治不了，王爷要带她到汴梁医治。”
[EN] “Lost her marbles?”
[ZH] “脑袋坏掉了？”
[EN] “Out of her senses.”
[ZH] “神志不清。”
[EN] Nan Shuang’s mouth fell open. “How on earth did that happen?!”
[ZH] 南双惊得嘴巴张得老大：“怎么会这样？”
[EN] “Some big fright, plus grief—and she just snapped.”
[ZH] “好像是受到惊吓，加上伤心，就成这样。”
[EN] “Whoa! Being scared too hard can wreck your head? Better watch myself from now on!” Shaking off the shock, Nan Shuang grinned shyly. “My master says I get to marry Youfan next spring—wonder if you’ll be back in Lin’an by then?”
[ZH] “啊！被吓着也会变这样？那我以后可得小心些。”南双惊叹完，想到自己的事，“公子说明年开春让我娶幼凡，不知道那会儿你能不能来临安？”
[EN] “Congrats first off—though no telling if I’ll be back by spring.”
[ZH] “先恭喜你，能不能来临安很难说。”
[EN] “What about you and Kuqiao? When’s the wedding?”
[ZH] “你跟苦荞打算什么时候成亲？”
[EN] “Beats me. With Youqi Jushi in trouble, how could Kuqiao even think about marrying? And with Princess Consort Yi like this, I wouldn’t dare bother His Highness about my own plans—have to keep pushing it off.”
[ZH] “不知道，幽栖居士出事，苦荞哪有心情谈成亲的事？仪王妃又变成那样，我更不好向王爷提自己的事，只能继续拖着。”
[EN] Nan Shuang sighed in relief. “Lucky my master didn’t marry Youqi Jushi after all—otherwise he’d be the one sweating bullets right now, and my wedding would be pushed off too!”
[ZH] 南双庆幸道：“多亏我家公子当初没娶幽栖居士，要不现在就换我家公子为难，我的婚事也会受到影响。”
[EN] Irritated by Nan Shuang’s smug look, Bai Feng snapped, “Don’t think your master’s living some dream life—he only looks fine on the outside.”
[ZH] 白风对南双一脸庆幸表示不满：“别以为你家公子现在真好，不过是表面而已。”
[EN] “Still beats marrying someone hauled off to Bianliang in a prison cage!”
[ZH] “不管怎样都比娶个被人用囚车拉到汴梁的人强。”
[EN] Bai Feng opened his mouth to curse Nan Shuang out, then bit it back knowing how long it might be before they met again—besides, who could blame Nan Shuang for thinking like an ordinary bloke? Youqi Jushi’s life really had been bumpy as cobblestones, dragging his master along for the ride.
[ZH] 白风本想骂南双两句，想到分别后再难见面，不想跟他计较，再说南双的想法也正常。幽栖居士命运坎坷，搞得王爷也跟着受牵连。
[EN] “Knock it off—don’t talk about Youqi Jushi like that. She’s a good soul, just hit some rough luck.”
[ZH] “行了，别这么说幽栖居士，她人很好，只是经历坎坷些。”
[EN] The two friends sat chatting over ordinary trifles, masking the sting of parting beneath casual banter.
[ZH] 两人继续聊着自己关心的话题，用看似普通的话语表达着离别之情。
[EN] ...
[ZH] 高台上，申简辰与乔文仙也一边饮酒一边表达离别之情。
[EN] Up on the platform, Shen Jianchen and Qiao Wenxian drank steadily, trading farewells of their own.
[ZH] “世事难料，你才来临安一年多，又要回去，我多少有些不舍。”
[EN] “Who could have guessed how fast things turn? Barely over a year in Lin’an, and you’re already heading back. Feels hard watching you go.”
[ZH] 申简辰回想起他刚来临安时与文渊一同住在幽林别居的情景：“我也是，可天下没有不散的筵席。”
[EN] Remembering those early months sharing Secluded Forest Villa with Wenyuan, Shen Jianchen sighed. “Same here—yet no feast lasts forever.”
[ZH] “是因为淑真吗？”
[EN] “Is it because of Shuzhen?”
[ZH] “不完全是，但因她回汴梁的想法更坚决，时间也提前。”
[EN] “Not *only* because of her—though she’s why my mind locked firm and why we’re leaving early.”
[ZH] 乔文仙沉默片刻后，得出自己的结论：“我现在终于明白你比我更爱她。”
[EN] After a quiet pause, Qiao Wenxian spoke softly. “At last I see you love her deeper than I ever did.”
[ZH] “我对她不只是一个‘爱’字那么简单。”
[EN] “With her, it goes well beyond a single word like *love*.”
[ZH] “是什么？”
[EN] “What is it then?”
[ZH] 申简辰摇摇头：“不知道，只觉得她走到哪里我必须去哪里，否则都不知道自己活着是为了什么。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen shook his head. “Can’t even explain it. Only know wherever she goes, I have to go—otherwise I wouldn’t even know why I’m drawing breath.”
[ZH] “她已是陈浩宇的娘子。”
[EN] “She’s Chen Haoyu’s wife now.”
[ZH] “所以我要带着陈浩宇一起去汴梁救她。”
[EN] “Which is why I’m taking Chen Haoyu to Bianliang to help save her.”
[ZH] “救出来后，她会跟着陈浩宇回临安，难道你还会回来？”
[EN] “And once you rescue her, she’ll ride back to Lin’an with Chen Haoyu. Are you planning to come back too?”
[ZH] 申简辰望着远处的神柳：“神柳在临安，她若也在临安，我迟早还会回来。”
[EN] Gazing toward the distant Divine Willow, Shen Jianchen murmured, “The Divine Willow stands in Lin’an. If she returns to Lin’an too, sooner or later I’ll be back.”
[ZH] 这个话题太过沉重，乔文仙想换一个：“仪王妃可好？”
[EN] Too heavy a topic to linger on, Qiao Wenxian steered away. “How is Princess Consort Yi holding up?”
[ZH] “她很不好。”
[EN] “Not well at all.”
[ZH] “什么意思？”
[EN] “What do you mean?”
[ZH] “她现在神志不清，总是傻笑，说些让人很迷惑的话。”
[EN] “Her mind’s slipped—keeps giggling blankly and muttering things nobody makes sense of.”
[ZH] “怎么会这样？”
[EN] “How did it come to this?”
[ZH] “她在外受到惊吓，好不容易有身孕，孩子又没了，她很伤心，又知道自己不能再生孩子，受到刺激，整个人变得不是笑就是哭，要么说些我听不懂的话。”
[EN] “Suffered a fright while out, finally conceived only to miscarry, got crushed by grief, then learned she can’t bear children again. All those blows shattered her—now she alternates between laughing and weeping or spouting gibberish.”
[ZH] 乔文仙感叹道：“没想到才几日，你也遇到不顺心的事。汴梁名医多，兴许有人能医好仪王妃的病。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian shook his head in pity. “Who’d have thought trouble would hit you too in just a few days? Bianliang boasts master physicians—someone there might cure Her Highness.”
[ZH] “我也这么想，许多事加在一起，必须回汴梁。”
[EN] “That’s my hope too. Everything piled together leaves no choice but going back to Bianliang.”
[ZH] ……
[EN] ...
[ZH] 第二天一大早，陈浩宇带着苦荞和青瓜来到幽林别居与仪王汇合。
[EN] Bright and early the next morning, Chen Haoyu rode up to Secluded Forest Villa with Kuqiao and Qinggua to meet Prince Yi’s party.
[ZH] 当马车在幽林别居门口停下时，他发现大门口站着许多人，应该都是来为仪王送行。
[EN] Pulling up outside the gate, he found a sizable crowd gathered to see Prince Yi off.
[ZH] 仪王走出大门时，身后跟着邹嬷嬷和仪王妃。众人都发现仪王妃看上去神情呆呆的，不如往日灵动。
[EN] Prince Yi stepped through the gate trailed by Matron Zou and Princess Consort Yi. Even bystanders couldn’t miss how glazed and vacant the princess consort’s eyes looked, devoid of her old spark.
[ZH] 待女眷上了马车后，众人才上前与仪王话别。
[EN] Once the women climbed inside their carriage, well-wishers stepped up one by one to bid Prince Yi farewell.
[ZH] 朱晞颜第一个说话：“殿下为小女之事费心，实在令我感动，我无以为报，给贵人磕头以表感谢。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan spoke first. “To see Your Highness go to such lengths for my daughter’s sake moves me beyond words. Having nothing worthy to repay you with, let me kowtow in gratitude.”
[ZH] 朱晞颜说着就要跪下，被仪王扶住：“我救幽栖居士心甘情愿，无须你跪我。你若跪了，我反而心中不安。”
[EN] As Zhu Xiyan dropped toward his knees, Shen Jianchen caught his arms. “I’m saving Youqi Jushi of my own free will—no need for you to kneel to me. If you kneel, it only sits wrong on my conscience.”
[ZH] 众人都明白仪王的意思。
[EN] Everyone standing by understood perfectly what Prince Yi meant.
[ZH] 陈浩宇坐在马车里听到仪王的话，心中不满：“当众表达，根本没把我放在眼里。”
[EN] Watching from inside his carriage, Chen Haoyu’s jaw clenched tight: *Saying that right in front of everybody—doesn’t put me in his eyes at all!*
[ZH] 等着与仪王话别的人多，朱晞颜不好多占用时间：“贵人忙，我上马车跟贤婿说两句。”
[EN] Seeing a line waiting to speak with the prince, Zhu Xiyan excused himself politely. “You’re busy, Your Highness. Let me climb into my son-in-law’s carriage for a word.”
[ZH] 征得仪王同意后，朱晞颜才走上陈浩宇的马车。
[EN] With Prince Yi’s nod, Zhu Xiyan stepped inside Chen Haoyu’s carriage.
[ZH] 第二个说话的是蒋爷：“殿下，能否借一步说话？”
[EN] Next up stepped Master Jiang. “Your Highness, a word in private?”
[ZH] 监渡官的职位虽不高，大小也是个官儿。蒋爷没有因为梁师成的权势避开，申简辰甚是欣慰，点头同意。
[EN] Though Ferry Superintendent was only a minor post, it was still a government post. Seeing Master Jiang brave Liang Shicheng’s wrath to show up warmed Shen Jianchen’s heart; he nodded readily.
[ZH] 二人来到一边，申简辰故意问：“你来给我送别，就不怕新来的知府大人找你麻烦？”
[EN] Stepping aside together, Shen Jianchen teased lightly, “Coming to send me off—aren’t you afraid the new Prefect will make life miserable for you?”
[ZH] 蒋爷笑着说：“我这个人没什么优点，就是讲义气，您帮过我，哪怕当不成监渡官，也不能忘恩负义。”
[EN] Master Jiang grinned. “Only virtue I’ve got is sticking by friends. You helped me out before—even if it costs me my post as Ferry Superintendent, I don’t turn my back on a debt.”
[ZH] “算你有良心。”
[EN] “Got a real conscience on you.”
[ZH] “我来一是为王爷送别，二是想提醒王爷，幽栖居士的事可能与仪王妃有关，是我娘子猜的。您也知道，她跟仪王妃一起做过对不起幽栖居士的事，对仪王妃有一定了解。我想来想去，觉得她说得有道理，才借今日送别之机将心中的疑虑告知王爷。”
[EN] “Came for two reasons, Your Highness: first, to wish you safe travels; second, to drop a hint—my wife suspects what happened to Youqi Jushi ties back to Princess Consort Yi. You know she and Her Highness pulled a dirty stunt against Youqi Jushi once before, so she reads Her Highness pretty well. Thinking it over, her hunch rings true—wanted to tell you straight before you left.”
[ZH] 蒋爷发现仪王的表情已不对，赶紧说：“下官就说这些，祝仪王一路顺风，告辞。”
[EN] Noticing Prince Yi’s expression stiffen instantly, Master Jiang wrapped up fast. “That’s all I had to say, Your Highness. Smooth roads north—farewell!”
[ZH] 蒋爷离开后，邵正邺和儿子邵志泽提着两坛酒过来。
[EN] After Master Jiang stepped away, Shao Zhengye and his son Shao Zhize walked up carrying two jars of wine.
[ZH] 邵正邺说：“两坛好酒是我送给仪王路上喝的。”
[EN] “Two jars of fine vintage for Your Highness to drink along the road,” Shao Zhengye offered warmly.
[ZH] 申简辰不好继续沉浸在对梦容不满的情绪中，拱手道：“感谢邵当家。白风！将邵当家的心意收下！”
[EN] Pushing down the dark churn stirred by Master Jiang’s warning, Shen Jianchen cupped his hands. “Thank you, Master Shao! Bai Feng, take Master Shao’s gift!”
[ZH] 白风走过来从邵志泽的手中接过两坛酒放进已套好的马车里。
[EN] Bai Feng stepped forward, accepted both jars from Shao Zhize, and stowed them neatly inside the hitched carriage.
[ZH] 邵家镖局的一位手下用盘子端着三碗酒过来。
[EN] An escort guard from the Shao Family Escort Agency followed with a tray holding three bowls of wine.
[ZH] 邵正邺说：“仪王请，一切尽酒中。”
[EN] “Drink up, Your Highness,” Shao Zhengye beamed. “Everything goes unsaid in a bowl of wine!”
[ZH] 三人端起酒碗，碰在一起，然后一口气喝完，邵正邺和邵志泽发出爽朗的笑声。
[EN] Clinking bowls together, the three men drained theirs dry in one gulp, Shao Zhengye and his son bursting into hearty laughter.
[ZH] 申简辰不由自主跟着笑起来，暂时将刚才的不快搁置一边。他很感谢邵家父子，他们的笑声可以令人忘却所有烦恼，重新燃起面对一切的勇气。
[EN] Before he knew it, Shen Jianchen laughed along with them, setting his heaviness aside for a moment. Grateful to father and son, he marveled how one hearty laugh of theirs could sweep away gloom and rekindle nerve to face whatever lay ahead.
[ZH] 笑够后，邵正邺与儿子拱手道：“祝仪王一路顺风，告辞。”
[EN] Once the laughter settled, Shao Zhengye and his son cupped their hands. “Safe journey, Your Highness—farewell!”
[ZH] 还剩下司理院的人，韩谷和温信带领几名差役前来送别。
[EN] Last in line stood the Judicial Office crew—Han Gu and Wen Xin leading several constables forward.
[ZH] 没等他们过来，申简辰主动走过去。
[EN] Before they could cross the grass, Shen Jianchen strode straight out to meet them.
[ZH] “多谢二位前来相送。”
[EN] “Thank you both for coming to see me off.”
[ZH] 两人带领差役跪在地上：“拜见仪王！”
[EN] The two officers dropped to their knees alongside every constable. “We pay our respects to Prince Yi!”
[ZH] “都是自己人，何必跪拜？快起来。”
[EN] “We’re all brothers here—why kneel? Get up quickly!”
[ZH] 众人起身后，韩谷说：“才几日工夫，我等已开始怀念仪王在司理院的日子。新来的司理参军只知收敛钱财，普通老百姓的事根本不管。”
[EN] Rising, Han Gu sighed, “Only a few days gone and we already miss Your Highness running the Judicial Office. The new Judicial Investigator cares only for lining his pockets—wouldn’t lift a finger for common folk.”
[ZH] 温信说：“新来的司理参军也是梁内侍的人，都头的差事没法干下去，我准备做完今日的差事离开司理院回乡下老家种地，做什么都比跟着现在的司理参军强。”
[EN] Wen Xin spat, “Liang’s lapdog too! Can’t stomach staying Chief Constable under him anymore—soon as I finish today’s shift, I’m quitting the Judicial Office and heading home to farm. Anything beats serving a crooked boss.”
[ZH] 韩谷说：“我也打算回乡下，现在的司理参军了解我们与仪王的情谊，处处排挤，继续做下去没有任何意义。”
[EN] “I’m heading home to the countryside too,” Han Gu nodded. “The new guy knows how close we stood with Your Highness and squeezes us every turn. Zero point sticking around.”
[ZH] “怪我影响了二位。”
[EN] “Hate seeing you both dragged down because of me.”
[ZH] 韩谷说：“我等从未如此想过，怪只怪朝廷腐败，奸臣当道，容不下为民请命之人。即使您贵为王爷，也无可奈何。”
[EN] “Never crossed our minds, Your Highness,” Han Gu protested. “Blame a rotten court and treacherous ministers who can’t tolerate honest men speaking for the people. Not even an imperial prince could hold the line.”
[ZH] 温信说：“这样的朝廷我等又何必为它做事？还不如回家独善其身。”
[EN] Wen Xin added fiercely, “Why serve a court like that? Better go home and tend our own integrity.”
[ZH] “说得好！达则兼善天下，穷则独善其身。在即将没落的时代，做好自己，已是不易。”
[EN] “Well said! ‘When successful, benefit all under Heaven; when thwarted, cultivate one’s own virtue in solitude.’ [TN: Translator’s rendering] In a fading age, keeping your own hands clean is no small thing.”
[ZH] 韩谷和温信对仪王的活法无不认同。
[EN] Both Han Gu and Wen Xin nodded firmly at the prince’s words.
[ZH] 马车上，朱晞颜向陈浩宇叮嘱了好些事，最重要的莫过于他在汴梁的人脉，让陈浩宇去后，多多拜访，说不定这些人会成为救淑真的关键。
[EN] ...
[ZH] 陈浩宇一一记在心里，加上昨晚乔文仙派南双送来的名单和地址，他认为救出娘子应该不成问题。
[EN] Inside the carriage meanwhile, Zhu Xiyan pressed crucial instructions onto Chen Haoyu—most importantly a list of his own contacts in Bianliang to call on right after arriving, any one of whom might hold the key to freeing Shuzhen.
[ZH] 猛然想到姐姐陈文心，关切地问：“我姐姐和岳母可好？”
[EN] Memorizing every detail alongside the names and addresses Qiao Wenxian had sent via Nan Shuang last night, Chen Haoyu felt confident they stood a solid shot at rescuing his wife.
[ZH] 朱晞颜叹道：“昨日我从柳岸别馆回到家里，将淑真的事说了。你岳母因伤心一病不起；文心既为淑真担忧，还得照顾婆婆，也好不到哪里去；景元和我是男人，都得撑着。现在大家把一切希望寄托在你去汴梁这趟，希望能顺利救下淑真。”
[EN] Suddenly remembering his sister Chen Wenxin, he asked anxiously, “How are my sister and Mother-in-law holding up?”
[ZH] “梁内侍收下陈家那么多银子，应该不会为难我娘子。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan let out a ragged sigh. “Coming home from Liu’an Villa yesterday, I told them what happened to Shuzhen. Your mother-in-law collapsed sick in bed from heartbreak.
