Between worrying over Shuzhen and nursing her mother-in-law, Wenxin’s barely holding together herself. Jingyuan and I have to stay strong as men. Every last hope in both households rests on your trip to Bianliang to bring Shuzhen home.”
[ZH] “我太了解梁师成，贪财好色两头占。恐怕第一次送钱时，他没要是为好色，后来淑真去一趟，他没得逞，还被淑真揍了一顿，才将她抓起来，顺便找个罪名惩治她。再次要陈家的钱财，肯定是因舍不得五十万两银子。钱已在他手中，会不会帮很难说，不要对他抱太大期望。”
[EN] “Liang took so much silver from our family—surely he won’t torment my wife too badly.”
[ZH] “这么说五十万两银子打水漂了不成？”
[EN] “I know Liang Shicheng inside out: greedy for silver *and* hungry for women, wanting both ends at once. First time you brought silver, he turned it down because lust came first. When Shuzhen marched in, refused him, and even beat him up, he locked her up and slapped a crime on her head out of spite. Only when you came back the second time couldn’t he bear turning down five hundred thousand taels a second time. Now that the money’s in his pouch, no telling if he’ll lift a finger—don’t pin too much hope on *him*.”
[ZH] “自然是打水漂，我们还无处辩驳。”
[EN] “So five hundred thousand taels went straight down the drain?!”
[ZH] “梁师成就是个混蛋！官家为何要重用这种人？”
[EN] “Straight down the drain—with nowhere on earth to appeal.”
[ZH] “官家昏庸，自然会重用奸臣。”
[EN] “Liang Shicheng is pure scum! Why would His Majesty ever put a monster like him in power?!”
[ZH] 陈浩宇露出疑惑的表情：“岳父大人以前为官时，每次对官家赞美有加，为何现在态度大变？”
[EN] “An incompetent emperor naturally surrounds himself with treacherous officials.”
[ZH] “为官时身不由己，自然会那么说，其实我心里跟明镜似的，什么都清楚。唉，人有时心里想的和嘴上说的是两回事。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu stared in surprise. “When you still served in office, Father-in-law, you sang His Majesty’s praises every chance you got—how’d your tune flip overnight?”
[ZH] 陈浩宇打趣道：“岳父大人现在说话可心口一致？”
[EN] “In office, a man’s hands are tied—you say what the role demands, even when your heart sees clear as glass. Funny how what men think inside and what leaves their mouths are two completely different things.”
[ZH] “那是自然。”朱晞颜向车窗外瞧，仪王正准备上马车，“行了，就说这些，最后再叮嘱一遍，我给你说的那些人，一个都别放过，定要登门拜访。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu teased lightly, “So are your mouth and heart saying the same thing right now, Father-in-law?”
[ZH] “记下了。”
[EN] “Every single word.” Glancing out the window, Zhu Xiyan saw Prince Yi stepping toward his carriage. “Enough talk. One last reminder: every single contact I gave you—don’t miss a soul. Knock on every single door.”
[ZH] 朱晞颜从马车里下来，听到一阵疾驰而来的马车声。众人也都看去，待马车停下后，东方尘泽与安明轩从马车里下来。
[EN] “Got it.”
[ZH] 二人直接走向仪王，拱手行礼。
[EN] Just as Zhu Xiyan stepped down, the thunder of hooves and carriage wheels rolled up fast. All eyes turned as a carriage halted and Dongfang Chenze and An Mingxuan stepped down.
[ZH] 东方尘泽说：“昨日乔兄派人告知，才知殿下今日启程汴梁，我们二人便匆匆赶来为殿下送别。”
[EN] Hurrying straight up to Prince Yi, both men cupped their hands and bowed.
[ZH] 申简辰收回刚跨上马车的脚：“感谢二位公子前来相送。”
[EN] “Young Master Qiao sent word yesterday that Your Highness departs for Bianliang today,” Dongfang Chenze said warmly, “so we rushed out to bid you farewell.”
[ZH] 安明轩说：“殿下一走，临安会冷清许多。”
[EN] Pulling his foot back down from the carriage step, Shen Jianchen smiled. “Thank you both for coming all this way to see me off.”
[ZH] “安公子言重，我对临安来讲不过是过客，无足轻重。”
[EN] An Mingxuan sighed, “Lin’an will feel much emptier with Your Highness gone.”
[ZH] 东方尘泽说：“不管别人怎么看，在我等眼中，仪王对临安百姓来讲意义重大。”
[EN] “You flatter me, Young Master An. To Lin’an, I was only ever a passing traveler—no loss at all.”
[ZH] “感谢二位公子看得起我。”
[EN] “No matter what anyone else says,” Dongfang Chenze replied earnestly, “in our eyes, what Your Highness did meant everything to the people of Lin’an.”
[ZH] 马车在一旁等着，两位公子不好再说什么，齐声道：“祝仪王殿下一路顺风！”
[EN] “I appreciate your high regard.”
[ZH] 两人与剩下的人一起目送仪王和陈浩宇的马车离开。
[EN] With horses hitched and waiting, neither friend kept him longer, calling together, “Fair winds all the way, Your Highness!”
[ZH] 共三辆马车，乔梦容、邹嬷嬷和喜兰同乘一辆；陈浩宇带着青瓜和苦荞同乘一辆；申简辰和白风同乘一辆。
[EN] Together with everyone else, they watched Prince Yi’s and Chen Haoyu’s carriages roll away.
[ZH] 马车行走在路上，没有人再说道别的话，申简辰想起蒋爷所说，对梦容不满的情绪再次袭上心头。
[EN] Three carriages moved out in convoy: Qiao Mengrong, Matron Zou, and Xilan in one; Chen Haoyu with Qinggua and Kuqiao in the second; Shen Jianchen and Bai Feng in the third.
[ZH] 书卷之事和千里寻被带到天目山上，因人无碍，他原谅了梦容，甚至认为错在自己。若这次的事也因梦容而起，恐怕他再也无法原谅她。
[EN] As the wheels rolled down the highway, farewells faded behind them, and Master Jiang’s warning crept straight back into Shen Jianchen’s mind, darkening his mood toward Mengrong once more.
[ZH] 离开幽林别居有段路程后，半道上被乔家人拦住。乔守仁带着钱氏和米氏，还有文渊前来送别。
[EN] Over the forged scrolls and the Mount Tianmu kidnapping, because Qianli Xun had come out unscathed, he had forgiven Mengrong—even blamed himself. Yet if she had engineered *this* disaster too, he doubted he could ever forgive her again.
[ZH] 昨日已与仪王话别，因父母执意要来，作为儿子乔文仙必须跟随。
[EN] Some distance beyond Secluded Forest Villa, a cluster of figures flagged down the convoy—Qiao Shouren waiting alongside Madam Qian, Concubine Mi, and Wenyuan to say goodbye.
[ZH] 申简辰走下马车，乔家人都上前拜见。
[EN] Though Wenyuan had already seen Prince Yi off yesterday, his parents insisted on coming, leaving him no choice as a dutiful son.
[ZH] 乔守仁带头说：“听文渊说殿下要回汴梁，我便在此候着，送您一程。”
[EN] Climbing down from his carriage, Shen Jianchen received the Qiao elders’ bows.
[ZH] 申简辰扶住乔守仁：“我走后，外公与外祖母，还有米姨娘定要保重身体，等文渊的孩子出生，你们便可享怡孙之乐。”
[EN] Steadying Qiao Shouren’s arms, Shen Jianchen urged gently, “Once I leave, Grandfather, Grandmother, and Aunt Mi must take good care of yourselves. Soon as Wenyuan’s children arrive, you can enjoy bouncing your grandchildren on your knees.”
[ZH] 申简辰的话说得乔家人甚为喜悦。
[EN] That warmed every Qiao face at once.
[ZH] 乔守仁不好太过高兴，毕竟仪王之所以回汴梁是因没了临安节度使的职位。昨日还听文渊说仪王妃变得神志不清，乔守仁替仪王难受了好一阵。
[EN] Still, Qiao Shouren couldn’t fully smile—knowing Prince Yi was only returning to Bianliang stripped of his Military Commissioner post, and having heard from Wenyuan yesterday that Princess Consort Yi had lost her wits, Qiao Shouren had lain awake hurting for the prince.
[ZH] 神志方面的疾病难以医治，仪王另娶或者是纳妾在所难免。既如此不如将藏在心中的事以及疑惑告知仪王。
[EN] Mental ailments rarely healed; sooner or later, Prince Yi would likely wed again or take a concubine anyway. Better to get the heavy secret off his chest now.
[ZH] 乔守仁并不想让文渊知晓，对钱氏说：“你带他们二人去那边待会儿，我有话跟仪王说。”
[EN] Not wanting Wenyuan to hear, Qiao Shouren told Madam Qian, “Take those two over yonder for a moment—I need a private word with Prince Yi.”
[ZH] 三人来到一边，远远瞅着乔守仁靠近仪王，像说悄悄话一般。乔文仙感到纳闷，父亲到底跟仪王说些什么？钱氏大略能猜到，此事定与仪王妃有关。
[EN] Strolling aside, the three watched from afar as Qiao Shouren leaned close to murmur in the prince’s ear. Wenyuan frowned, wondering what his father could be whispering about, though Madam Qian guessed easily enough it concerned Princess Consort Yi.
[ZH] 陈浩宇并未下马车，坐在车里盯着两人，他们说话声音不大，根本听不清说些什么。
[EN] Staying inside his carriage, Chen Haoyu stared sharply at the pair, too far to catch a single syllable.
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷本想下马车与乔家人寒暄几句，看着目光呆滞的仪王妃，她毫无心情。透过马车的窗户见乔守仁单独与仪王说悄悄话，不由担心起来，希望他不要将仪王妃的事告诉王爷。
[EN] Matron Zou had thought about climbing down to exchange pleasantries with the Qiaos, yet one glance at Princess Consort Yi’s vacant stare drained every ounce of heart from her. Seeing Qiao Shouren pull Prince Yi aside in private out the window sent a cold knot twisting in her gut—praying silently he wouldn’t spill Her Highness’s secret.
[ZH] 待家人走到一边后，乔守仁小声对仪王说：“有件事一直憋在我心里没敢告诉殿下，是怕影响殿下与仪王妃之间的感情，可自从天目山上的匪盗抢了陈家后，我心里就发怵。现在女词人被随便寻个理由抓走，我不得不说……”
[EN] Once his family stepped out of earshot, Qiao Shouren lowered his voice. “I’ve swallowed something for months, afraid saying it might ruin things between Your Highness and Princess Consort Yi. Yet ever since Mount Tianmu bandits raided the Chens, my heart’s turned cold with dread. Now that the poetess’s been hauled off on some trumped-up charge, I can’t keep quiet anymore…”
[ZH] 申简辰已猜到，主动说出答案：“外公怀疑一切是梦容所为？”
[EN] Already guessing the rest, Shen Jianchen finished calmly, “Grandfather suspects Mengrong is behind all of it.”
[ZH] 乔守仁变得轻松许多：“原来仪王已知道。”
[EN] Relief washed over Qiao Shouren’s shoulders. “So Your Highness already knew.”
[ZH] “以前的事很确定，这次的事暂时还不能确定。外公为何会怀疑梦容？”
[EN] “About the earlier plots, yes. This time I can’t be certain yet. Why did Grandfather suspect Mengrong?”
[ZH] 乔守仁照实说：“殿下跟仪王妃从汴梁完婚后回来，邹嬷嬷来过乔府一趟，替仪王妃找乔家想办法让女词人消失。杀人的事乔家从未做过，我哪敢应下？就向邹嬷嬷推荐了蒋爷。后来担心了许久，还好没听到蒋爷杀害女词人的事，否则我良心难安。”
[EN] Qiao Shouren held nothing back. “Right after you and Princess Consort Yi returned from your wedding in Bianliang, Matron Zou came calling at Qiao Manor on Her Highness’s behalf—asking our house to find a way to make the poetess disappear. The Qiao family has never touched murder! How could I take that on? So I pointed Matron Zou toward Master Jiang instead. I spent months sweating in fear afterward—thank Heaven word never came that Master Jiang killed the poetess, or my conscience would never let me sleep!”
[ZH] 申简辰已能够确定，千里寻被梁内侍迫害定与梦容有关。邹嬷嬷竟然也知道此事，还私下帮助梦容，恐怕喜兰也什么都清楚。他后悔以前因自责轻易原谅梦容，搞得千里寻遇到更大的麻烦。
[EN] Now Shen Jianchen knew with absolute certainty: Liang Shicheng’s trap around Qianli Xun traced straight back to Mengrong. Worse, Matron Zou knew everything and had actively aided her—likely Xilan knew the whole story too! Fool that he had been to forgive Mengrong out of guilt last time, landing Qianli Xun straight in mortal peril!
[ZH] 与乔家人道别后，申简辰一路上一言不发，往后他与梦容的情分算是走到尽头。
[EN] Bidding the Qiaos goodbye and climbing back aboard, Shen Jianchen spoke not a single word the rest of the road—whatever thread of affection still lingered between him and Mengrong had snapped clean for good.

#CH 325
[ZH] 三日后，被打伤的脸好了许多，为尽快回到汴梁处理女词人的事，梁师成准备在临安逗留四五日，处理好该处理的事。
[ZH] 头等重要的事，需更换曾经与仪王走得近，又不懂得贿赂自己的人。
[ZH] 临安大小官员闻风纷纷上门投其所好，要么送钱，要么送女人。许多官员因此保住头上的乌纱帽。
[ZH] 总有些冥顽不灵的，不送钱也不送女人，还主动卸了官职。对于这类人，梁师成只会嘲笑他们不懂得顺势而为。
[ZH] 自然也处理到蒋爷的头上，甚至知道他在仪王离开那日跑去送行。
[ZH] 蒋爷为保住好不容得到的官职，找春香楼的妈妈寻来三位商妓，扮作人妻，送给梁内侍。
[ZH] 梁内侍夸蒋爷懂事，将职位继续保留，警告他，往后不能与仪王走得太近。
[ZH] 蒋爷嘴上答应着，心中却不以为然。
[ZH] 临安受益最大的人便是林觉和春金巧。
[ZH] 林觉直接官升两级，成为诸军都统制，统管临安所有军权。
[ZH] 春金巧后来又去过两次柳岸别馆，将梁内侍陪得甚好，是所有陪过的女人中最令内侍大人满意的。
[ZH] 梁师成以自己的名义为她举办了一场宴会。为巴结梁内侍，临安的官员无不携带家眷前来参加。
[ZH] 梁师成在宴会中大言不惭，声称春金巧是临安女子的表率，不仅人长得美，还心地善良。
[ZH] 不知从哪里找来两个穷苦人，给些钱财，让他们当着众人说一说春金巧是如何帮助他们。
[ZH] 两名穷苦人按照提前教好的，当着所有人的面将春金巧夸得连她都觉得自己好得令人难以置信。
[ZH] 然后又夸春金巧身为妾室却能与正室和睦相处，为家庭和谐做出贡献。
[ZH] 白梅若迫于梁内侍的权势，当着众人将春金巧在林府如何与她姐妹相待，如何顾大局识大体的事例随便编几个，再添油加醋一番。说完她感到反胃，差点吐出来。
[ZH] 一连好几天都有种想呕吐的感觉，找大夫诊脉后，才知已有一个月的身孕。因为孩子保住她正室的身份，否则春金巧借着现在的势头夺走正室不在话下。
[ZH] 经过这场宴会，春金巧荣耀一番，冷眼旁观临安的贵妇如何向她谄媚。心想，终于在瞧不上她的人面前扬眉吐气一回。
[ZH] 最令她失望的是，代替白梅若成为正室的想法泡汤。
[ZH] 梁内侍说，从古至今还没有正室健在妾室登堂入室的先例。其他事他都能办到，唯独这件事不能违反从古至今的惯例。
[ZH] 春金巧求林觉帮忙，林觉也感到为难。结果白梅若有了身孕，春金巧的希望彻底泡汤。还好林觉看在梁内侍的面子上，答应她往后在林家可与白梅若平起平坐，春金巧才不再提任何要求。
[ZH] 春金巧看似荣耀，背地里却被临安贵妇骂得一无是处。
[ZH] 白梅若与苗知瑜相约在永泰茶楼，一个刚有身孕，一个则挺着三四个月大的肚子。两人见面先是一番恭喜，毕竟有了身孕，女人在家中的地位会有所提升是件值得高兴之事。
[ZH] 两人在雅间落座后，品着茶师做好的茶调侃起来。
[ZH] 白梅若说：“一个商妓出身的妾室，只是皮肉卖得好，就想抢走正室的位置，实在不知天高地厚。可惜老天不给她机会，不仅梁内侍没答应，我又偏偏有了身孕。”
[ZH] 苗知瑜故意说：“你在宴会上将她夸得跟朵花似的，怎么这会儿又嫌弃？”
[ZH] 白梅若反问道：“你知道原因，又何必故意问？”
[EN] Three days later, Liang Shicheng’s bruised face had healed considerably. Eager to return to Bianliang and settle the matter of the female ci poet, he planned to linger in Lin’an for four or five days to wrap up his business there.
[ZH] 苗知瑜放下茶盏，叹口气：“唉，自从梁内侍来到临安，风气大变，这等不知廉耻的女人竟然骑到我们头上，昨日我向她行礼时浑身不自在。”
[EN] At the top of his list was replacing any official who had been close to Prince Yi yet failed to offer him a bribe.
[ZH] 白梅若催促道：“快给我说说别人都是怎么骂她的？也让我高兴高兴。”
[EN] Word traveled fast. Officials big and small across Lin’an flocked to his door, catering to his tastes with silver or women. Many kept the black gauze caps on their heads that way.
[ZH] “还不都是些污秽之词，什么‘贱货’‘破鞋’之类，连林都统也跟着挨骂。大家都不敢明目张胆骂，只在背地里发泄发泄。昨日我跟夏果在一起聊天儿，她也骂个不停，说春金巧丢了天下女人的脸。”
[EN] There were always a few stubborn souls who offered neither coin nor women and resigned their posts of their own accord. Liang Shicheng only mocked them for failing to ride the tide of power.
[ZH] “骂得好！”白梅若想起幽栖居士，高兴不起来，“现在我终于明白为何幽栖居士会离开林家，一个连自己的女人都往外送的男人实在靠不住。往后我不会再对他有任何期望，只做两件事，一是保住正室的位置，二是把孩子生下来，好好教育，不要让孩子像他爹那样。”
[EN] Master Jiang did not escape scrutiny either; Liang even knew he had gone to see Prince Yi off on the day of his departure. To hold onto the post he had worked so hard to gain, Master Jiang went to the madam of Chunxiang Brothel, fetched three commercial courtesans dressed as married women, and presented them to Imperial Attendant Liang.
[ZH] 苗知瑜慨叹不已：“幽栖居士才是女子中的楷模，她被梁内侍定了莫须有的罪名，恐怕是她不畏强权所致。”
[EN] Liang praised Master Jiang for knowing how the world worked, let him keep his post, and warned him to stay clear of Prince Yi from now on. Master Jiang agreed to his face, while scoffing in private.
[ZH] “听我夫君说，梁内侍喜欢成过亲的女子，应该是看上幽栖居士，她不从，才定下罪名。”
[EN] The biggest winners in Lin’an were Lin Jue and Chun Jinqiao. Lin Jue leaped two ranks at once to become Commander-in-Chief of All Garrisons, holding total military authority over Lin’an.
[ZH] “所以说她才是女子中的楷模。”
[EN] Chun Jinqiao visited Liu’an Villa twice more and pleased Imperial Attendant Liang better than any other woman he had summoned. Liang Shicheng went so far as to throw a banquet in her name, and to curry favor with him, every official in Lin’an brought his family.
[ZH] “可我总觉得没有那么简单。”
[EN] At the banquet, Liang Shicheng boasted without shame that Chun Jinqiao was a paragon among Lin’an women, blessed with both beauty and a kind heart. He paid two paupers from somewhere to testify before the crowd about how she had helped them. Coached beforehand, the pair lavished praise on Jinqiao until even she found her own goodness hard to believe.
[ZH] 苗知瑜立刻领会：“你是说与仪王妃有关？”
[EN] Next, they praised her for living in harmony with the primary wife despite being a concubine, keeping peace within the household. Under pressure from Liang’s power, Bai Meiruo forced herself to invent a few tales before everyone about how she and Jinqiao treated one another like sisters and how Jinqiao always kept the bigger picture in mind, laying the flattery on thick. Once she finished speaking, her stomach churned so hard she nearly threw up.
[ZH] 白梅若点点头：“我们两个跟着仪王妃也做过几次对付幽栖居士的事，那时我就觉得仪王妃绝不会放过她。”
[EN] For days afterward, nausea dogged her. When she called a physician to take her pulse, she learned she was one month pregnant. The child saved her place as primary wife; otherwise, riding this wave of favor, Jinqiao could have seized that seat with ease.
[ZH] “可我听文渊说仪王妃回汴梁前已神志不清，应该不会是她。”
[EN] After the banquet, Jinqiao basked in glory, watching the noblewomen of Lin’an fawn over her with cool eyes. At last, she thought, she had held her head high before the very people who used to look down on her.
[ZH] “以前见她好好的，怎么会神志不清？”
[EN] Her one bitter disappointment was seeing her dream of replacing Bai Meiruo as primary wife fall through. Imperial Attendant Liang told her there had never been a precedent since ancient times for a concubine to take the primary seat while the primary wife lived. He could grant anything else, he said, just not a breach of that age-old rule.
[ZH] “听说受到惊吓后，又小产失去孩子伤心不已，再受到刺激，才出现神志方面的疾病。”
[EN] Jinqiao begged Lin Jue to help, leaving him torn, until Meiruo’s pregnancy ended that hope for good. Out of deference to Liang, Lin Jue promised Jinqiao she would stand on equal footing with Meiruo in the Lin household from then on, and Jinqiao dropped her demands.
[ZH] 白梅若端起茶盏若有所思地品了一会儿，感慨道：“多亏你我及时收手，没有跟着仪王妃错下去，否则害人又害己。”
[EN] To all appearances, Jinqiao shone in glory, yet behind closed doors the noblewomen of Lin’an cursed her up and down.
[ZH] “看来你还是认为幽栖居士的事与仪王妃有关，若真有关系，的确是罪有应得。”
[EN] Bai Meiruo met Miao Zhiyu at Yongtai Teahouse, one newly pregnant and the other already showing at three or four months. After exchanging congratulations on a pregnancy that strengthened a woman’s standing at home, they sat in a private booth, sipped tea prepared by the tea master, and began trading barbed remarks.
[ZH] “此事有没有关系难以断定，可天目山之事一定与仪王妃有关。”
[EN] “A concubine out of a brothel, good at nothing but selling her flesh, actually thought she could snatch the primary wife’s seat,” Bai Meiruo said. “She doesn’t know her own place. Too bad Heaven shut the door on her. Not only did Imperial Attendant Liang refuse, I went and got pregnant too.”
[ZH] “听林都统说的？”
[EN] “You praised her to the skies at the banquet like some rare blossom,” Miao Zhiyu teased. “Why look down on her now?”
[ZH] 白梅若摇摇头：“直觉。你我都有身孕，为了即将出生的孩子，不能再像以前那样。”
[EN] “You know full well why. Why ask?”
[ZH] “我发现你比以前成熟不少。”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu set down her tea bowl and sighed. “Ever since Imperial Attendant Liang set foot in Lin’an, decency has gone to the dogs. A shameless woman like that riding roughshod over us! Yesterday when I had to bow to her, my skin crawled.”
[ZH] “很多事情看开，自然成熟。以前嫉妒幽栖居士占据我夫君的心。现在想想，林觉算个什么东西，不值得我为他嫉妒别人，况且还是幽栖居士这样不畏惧梁内侍的人。”
[EN] “Tell me how the others curse her,” Meiruo urged. “Give me something to smile about.”
[ZH] “头一次听你直呼林都统的名讳，看来你真的变了。其实我也不再怨幽栖居士。我与她一早相识，最了解她的性情，因文渊才嫉妒她，不愿与她成为闺蜜。现在连文渊的孩子都有了，还嫉妒什么？放下嫉妒才想明白许多事，感激她当初离开文渊成全我。设身处地想一下，那时她肯定很难过。”
[EN] “Nothing but filth. Calling her a ‘cheap slut,’ a ‘worn shoe,’ and dragging Commandant Lin through the mud alongside her. Nobody dares curse aloud, of course, so everyone vents behind her back. When I chatted with Xiaguo yesterday, she cursed nonstop too, saying Chun Jinqiao disgraced women everywhere.”
[ZH] “等幽栖居士安全回到临安，你我一定要上门拜访，我想跟她成为闺中密友。”
[EN] “Good!” Meiruo’s smile faded as her thoughts turned to Youqi Jushi. “Now I understand why Youqi Jushi left the Lin household. A man who hands his own woman out to another cannot be trusted. I expect nothing from him anymore. From now on I care about two things only: holding my seat as primary wife, and raising this child well so it doesn’t turn out like its father.”
[ZH] “一言为定。”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu sighed in admiration. “Youqi Jushi is the true model for women. That trumped-up charge Imperial Attendant Liang pinned on her must have come because she refused to bow to his power.”
[ZH] 两个女人统一想法，又慨叹一会儿人生，再将春金巧骂上一回，才各自归家。
[EN] “My husband told me Liang likes married women. He must have set his sights on Youqi Jushi, and when she refused, he framed her.”
[ZH] ……
[EN] “Which proves all the more what a model she is.”
[ZH] 离开临安的时日里，无论是在路上还是在客栈住下，申简辰都没理会过梦容。
[EN] “Still, I suspect there is more to it.”
[ZH] 乔梦容神志不清，呆呆傻傻，时笑时哭。哥哥的表现对现在的她来讲已无任何意义。
[EN] Miao Zhiyu caught her meaning at once. “You think Princess Consort Yi had a hand in it?”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷和喜兰一路上悉心照顾仪王妃外，都发现仪王的变化。
[EN] Meiruo nodded. “You and I joined her a few times to move against Youqi Jushi. Even then I knew Princess Consort Yi would never let her off.”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷心想，恐怕王爷猜到女词人遭遇的事与王妃有关，她已做好回到汴梁后被王爷兴师问罪的准备。
[EN] “Wenyuan told me she lost her mind before leaving for Bianliang. It couldn’t be her.”
[ZH] 一路同行，陈浩宇才知仪王妃的状况。他好奇到底是什么原因会让一个正常人变成那样？仪王看上去不怎么高兴，搞得陈浩宇不敢向仪王问起。他纳闷，为何一路上仪王不怎么理会仪王妃？
[EN] “She seemed fine before. How did she lose her mind?”
[ZH] 陈浩宇认为，恐怕因仪王妃变傻，仪王才不愿搭理她。他变得更加担心，仪王妃变成那样，仪王会不会抢他的娘子？
[EN] “I heard she suffered a bad fright, then miscarried, and the grief and shock pushed her over the edge.”
[ZH] 他决定到达临安后，先不依靠仪王。乔文仙和岳父朱晞颜介绍的人已够厉害，帮忙救娘子应该不成问题。
[EN] Bai Meiruo lifted her bowl, sipped in silence, and let out a long breath. “Good thing you and I pulled back in time instead of following her down that road. Otherwise we would have ruined others and ourselves alike.”
[ZH] ……
[EN] “So you still believe Youqi Jushi’s trouble ties back to Princess Consort Yi? If it does, she brought this ruin on herself.”
[ZH] 十来日后，一行人终于到达汴梁。
[EN] “I can’t be sure about this time, yet Mount Tianmu was her doing without a doubt.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇想寻客栈住下，申简辰不允许，强行要求他住在仪王府。
[EN] “Commandant Lin told you?”
[ZH] 陈浩宇虽不情愿，还是勉为其难跟着往仪王府而去。
[EN] Meiruo shook her head. “Woman’s instinct. We’re both carrying children now. For their sake, we can’t act as we did before.”
[ZH] 来到仪王府时天色已晚，没办法立刻去忙娘子的事，先暂且住下，明日再按照名单一一拜访。
[EN] “You’ve grown up a lot.”
[ZH] 等众人安顿好，用过晚饭，乔梦容也已睡下，申简辰吩咐白风将邹嬷嬷和喜兰唤来，他有话要问。
[EN] “Once you let go of things, you grow up on your own. I used to resent Youqi Jushi for holding my husband’s heart. Looking back now, what is Lin Jue worth? Not worth my envy of anyone, least of all a woman fearless enough to defy Liang Shicheng.”
[ZH] 白风完成任务，跑去找苦荞，屋内只留下王爷、邹嬷嬷和喜兰。
[EN] “That’s the first time I’ve heard you call Commandant Lin by his plain name. You really have changed. Truth be told, I don’t hold a grudge against Youqi Jushi anymore either. I knew her long before and understood her temper best. Only because of Wenyuan did I turn jealous and refuse to be close to her. Now that I’m carrying Wenyuan’s child, what is there left to envy? Once that jealousy faded, everything became clear. I’m grateful she walked away from Wenyuan and made room for me. Putting myself in her shoes, she must have hurt terribly back then.”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷猜到王爷所为何事，一进屋，跪在地上。喜兰见状，也跟着跪下。
[EN] “When Youqi Jushi makes it back safe to Lin’an, let’s pay her a visit together. I want her as a close friend.”
[ZH] 仪王坐在书案前，没有劝她们起来的意思。
[EN] “Agreed.”
[ZH] “嬷嬷既跪下，应该知道我要问什么。不准再有任何隐瞒，把事情从头到尾详细说清楚。”
[EN] Having reached the same mind, the two women sighed a bit more over life, cursed Chun Jinqiao one last round, and went their separate ways home.
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷老实交代：“自从仪王妃知道王爷喜欢女词人，她心中难安，加之王爷前去抢亲搞得王妃颜面无存，她便要我一同去临安。开始王妃只想毁了幽栖居士江南女词人的美名，结果没有成功，还是因为王爷您，您还利用东方诗会替女词人正名，王妃心中难过，才想除掉她。找乔老爷帮忙，他没答应，然后与陈浩东合作，各取所需，才有了陈家的麻烦和蒋爷之事；再后来又与蒋爷的夫人合作有了天目山上的事……”
[EN] Along the road out of Lin’an, whether traveling by day or resting at inns by night, Shen Jianchen ignored Qiao Mengrong. She stayed lost in her fog, vacant and simple-minded, laughing one moment and crying the next. His cold shoulder meant nothing to her now.
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷的说辞还是与事实有些出入，她拜访乔府时，仪王妃已起杀心，之所以如此说，是希望王爷意识到他的影响，从而不要太过怪罪已神志不清的仪王妃。
[EN] As Matron Zou and Xilan tended the Princess Consort along the route, both saw the change in Prince Yi. Matron Zou suspected he had guessed her mistress’s hand in the poetess’s plight, and braced herself for his reckoning once they reached Bianliang.
[ZH] 申简辰打断邹嬷嬷：“嬷嬷年长，人生阅历丰富，应该知道对错，为何不知规劝梦容？却成为她的帮凶？”
[EN] Traveling alongside them, Chen Haoyu learned of Princess Consort Yi’s condition and wondered what could have broken a sane woman like that. Seeing Prince Yi’s dark mood kept him from asking, though he marveled that the prince paid his wife no mind at all. Haoyu assumed Prince Yi shunned her because she had gone simple, which fed his dread that the prince might try to take his own wife instead. He resolved that once they reached Bianliang, he would avoid leaning on Prince Yi; the high officials Qiao Wenxian and Zhu Xiyan had recommended should carry enough weight to save her.
[ZH] “开始我是可怜仪王妃，王爷病着时她对王爷照顾有加，任谁见了都会感动，何况我是天天见？王爷起死回生，不记得与王妃之间的感情，心里还有了别人，我比谁都理解王妃内心的痛苦，才答应帮她。后来我渐渐发现女词人安于自己的生活，没有非分之想，也劝过王妃，可她像鬼迷心窍一般，一心想除掉女词人，我也无可奈何，只好继续帮她。”
[EN] After more than ten days on the road, the party reached Bianliang at last.
[ZH] 申简辰问出最关键的问题：“幽栖居士此次遇难，可与仪王妃有关？”
[EN] Haoyu looked to take rooms at an inn, but Shen Jianchen refused and insisted he stay at Prince Yi’s Manor. Haoyu went along reluctantly. By the time they arrived, dusk had fallen, leaving no time to visit anyone on his list until the next day.
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷与喜兰都不敢言语。
[EN] Once everyone settled in, ate supper, and Mengrong fell asleep, Shen Jianchen sent Bai Feng to summon Matron Zou and Xilan. Bai Feng delivered the message and slipped off to find Kuqiao, leaving only the prince, the matron, and the maid inside.
[ZH] “喜兰，你老实交代，那日与仪王妃出去参加宴会是不是骗我？你们根本没有去参加宴会，而是去了柳岸别馆。”
[EN] Guessing what awaited her, Matron Zou knelt as soon as she stepped in, and Xilan dropped down beside her. Seated behind his desk, Prince Yi made no move to bid them rise.
[ZH] 喜兰吓得头挨在地上：“回……回王爷，是去参加宴会。”
[EN] “Since you knelt on your own, Matron, you must know what I mean to ask,” Shen Jianchen said. “No more hiding anything. Tell me the whole truth from start to finish.”
[ZH] 申简辰一拳砸在桌上：“再胡说我就让人把你拉出去打五十大板！”
[EN] Matron Zou confessed. She told how Princess Consort Yi grew frantic after learning of his love for the poetess and losing face when he crashed the wedding, bringing Matron Zou to Lin’an to ruin Youqi Jushi’s name. When that failed—and he used Dongfang’s poetry banquet to restore her honor—Mengrong’s heartbreak hardened into murder. Master Qiao refused to help, so she struck a deal with Chen Haodong, setting off the Chen family troubles and the plot with Master Jiang, followed by her alliance with Master Jiang’s wife that led to Mount Tianmu.
[ZH] 喜兰吓得浑身哆嗦：“王爷……王爷……饶命！”
[EN] Matron Zou slanted the truth slightly—Mengrong had wanted blood even before visiting Qiao Manor—hoping the prince would feel the weight of his own role and spare a broken woman his wrath.
[ZH] 申简辰要求道：“想让我饶命，就老实交代。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen cut her off. “You are older, Matron, seasoned in the ways of the world. You knew right from wrong. Why fail to counsel Mengrong and become her accomplice instead?”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷也被吓坏，劝喜兰：“已瞒不下去，老实说吧。”
[EN] “At first I pitied her,” Matron Zou answered. “When Your Highness lay sick, she nursed you night and day. Anyone seeing it would have been moved, let alone me watching every hour. When you returned from death’s door, forgot your bond with her, and set your heart on another, nobody felt her torment more than I, so I helped her. Later I saw Youqi Jushi kept to her own life with no improper designs, and I tried to dissuade the Princess Consort, yet she acted possessed, bent on killing her. What choice did I have?”
[ZH] 喜兰哭了起来：“呜呜，王爷，我说，我都老实交代，呜呜……”
[EN] Shen Jianchen drove straight to the vital question. “Did Princess Consort Yi cause Youqi Jushi’s current disaster?”
[ZH] 喜兰一五一十将事情经过说明……
[EN] Both Matron Zou and Xilan kept silent.
[ZH] 申简辰气得将书案掀翻：“我定要亲手宰了梁师成！”
[EN] “Xilan, speak the truth. Did you lie to me that day about attending a banquet? You never went to a banquet. You went to Liu’an Villa.”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷和喜兰第一次见仪王发怒，两人吓得不行，浑身瑟瑟发抖。
[EN] Xilan pressed her forehead to the floor, terrified. “Y-Your Highness, we did go to a banquet.”
[ZH] 申简辰控制好怒气，用冷漠的声音说：“往后仪王妃交由你们照料，看在我病着时她照顾有加，我依然会养着她，也会找大夫替她诊治，但别再指望我会去看她。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen slammed his fist onto the desk. “One more lie and I’ll have you dragged outside for fifty strokes of the heavy bamboo!”
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷和喜兰离开后，申简辰心情不好，他走出屋，打算在府内散散心。
[EN] Shaking from head to toe, Xilan begged for mercy. Shen Jianchen demanded the truth, and Matron Zou urged her to confess since they could hide it no longer. Bursting into tears, Xilan poured out every detail of what happened.
[ZH] 在临安时，心情不好都会去看望神柳。仪王府没有神柳，只有萧瑟的秋风和到处飘零的枯叶，已是深秋时节，风中自带寒意。
[EN] Enraged, Shen Jianchen overturned the desk. “I’ll butcher Liang Shicheng with my own hands!”
[ZH] 申简辰反而感觉甚好，充满寒意的秋风能让他变得冷静，头脑也变得更加清晰。
[EN] Having never seen him erupt like that, both women trembled in terror.
[ZH] 以前他想着如何照顾梦容，如何安心与她过下去，如何与她白头偕老。现在只剩下一桩事，便是救千里寻，他反而有种莫名的轻松感。
[EN] Shen Jianchen reined in his fury and spoke in an icy voice. “From this day on, the two of you will look after Princess Consort Yi. Out of regard for how she cared for me while I was sick, I will continue to support her and summon doctors to treat her. Just don’t expect me ever to lay eyes on her again.”
[ZH] 时不时有王府的侍卫从身旁经过，都会向他恭敬地行礼，口称：“仪王殿下。”
[EN] After they withdrew, Shen Jianchen stepped outside to walk off his foul mood. In Lin’an he used to go to the Divine Willow; here there was no willow, only biting late-autumn wind and dead leaves scattering across the yard. Oddly, the chill helped clear his head.
[ZH] 穿越成为堂堂的王爷，竟然拿一个奸臣没办法，实在可悲。关键在于龙椅上坐着的那位，他决定明日面见官家，请求他吩咐大理寺放了幽栖居士。
[EN] Before, his thoughts had been tangled with how to care for Mengrong, settle into marriage, and grow old with her. Now only one goal remained: saving Qianli Xun. That clarity brought a strange relief.
[ZH] 打定主意后，申简辰仍旧没有回去睡觉的想法，继续踩着落叶散心。
[EN] Every now and then passing guards bowed and greeted him as Prince Yi. To transmigrate into an imperial prince only to be helpless before a treacherous eunuch was pathetic, and the root of the problem sat on the dragon throne. He resolved to see His Majesty the next morning and request an order for the Court of Judicial Review to free Youqi Jushi.
[ZH] 走着走着传来白风和苦荞说话的声音。
[EN] With his mind made up, he stayed out crunching over fallen leaves instead of turning in. Ahead came the voices of Bai Feng and Kuqiao.
[ZH] “我在汴梁长大，对这里很熟，你想去哪里逛，尽管说。”
[EN] “I grew up in Bianliang and know every corner,” Bai Feng said. “Tell me wherever you want to go.”
[ZH] “小姐的事还没着落，我哪有心情逛？”
[EN] “With Miss still in trouble, how could I be in the mood to stroll around?”
[ZH] “不用担心，有王爷和陈公子，幽栖居士肯定没事。等把幽栖居士救出来，能不能答应我件事？”
[EN] “Don’t worry. With His Highness and Young Master Chen here, Youqi Jushi will be fine. Once we get her out, promise me one thing?”
[ZH] “什么事？”
[EN] “What thing?”
[ZH] “去我以前住的地方，我想让邻居和认识的人都瞧一瞧，我白风也快有媳妇。”
[EN] “Come see my old neighborhood. I want the neighbors and everyone I grew up with to see that Bai Feng is about to take a wife.”
[ZH] 苦荞害羞起来，故意说：“我才不去。”
[EN] Kuqiao flushed shyly and said she wouldn’t go.
[ZH] “你害羞的样子真好看，能不能让我亲一下？”
[EN] “You look adorable when you’re shy. Let me steal a kiss?”
[ZH] “天这么黑你怎么能看到人家害羞？”
[EN] “How can you see me blushing in pitch dark?”
[ZH] “感觉的，我要亲了，可别推我。”
[EN] “I can feel it. I’m kissing you now—don’t push me away.”
[ZH] 再听不到两人说话的声音，申简辰脑补着二人抱在一起亲热的情景，他很羡慕。白风和苦荞虽是下人，却能与喜欢的人在一起，而他……
[EN] Silence fell over their spot. Picturing them holding each other in the dark, Shen Jianchen felt a pang of envy. Servants though they were, Bai Feng and Kuqiao got to be with the one they loved, while he had no such luck.
[ZH] 为了不打扰一对有情人，申简辰拐到别处，走着走着，见有人迎面而来，借着月光，看清是陈浩宇的身影。
[EN] Not wanting to intrude on the pair, Shen Jianchen turned down another path—and caught sight of Chen Haoyu walking toward him in the moonlight.

#CH 326
[ZH] 陈浩宇在仪王府的客房住下，因担心娘子，恨不得连夜登门拜访那些重要人物。
[ZH] 大晚上的，甚是不便。他躺在床上辗转难眠，起身穿好衣裳打算在仪王府到处走走消磨时光。
[ZH] 夜色下，有人跟他一样走在王府的小径中，走近才发现是仪王。陈浩宇快走几步上前打招呼：“仪王殿下。”
[ZH] 申简辰客气地问：“陈公子可住得习惯？”
[ZH] “王府什么都不缺，住的屋子也舒适，下人们看在仪王的面子上对我处处照顾，跟在家里没什么区别。”
[ZH] “那便好。明日我会为幽栖居士的事面见官家，无论如何也要将她救出来。”
[ZH] “多谢仪王。”陈浩宇多少感到不舒服，“我明日也会去拜访文渊和岳父大人介绍的朝中重臣，说不定不用仪王出面便可救出我娘子。”
[ZH] 申简辰感觉出陈浩宇的戒备：“你我分头行动，无论用哪种方法，将人救出来才最重要。”
[ZH] “仪王殿下说得极是。”
[ZH] 两人因同一个女人的事偶然遇见，又说了明日同样要忙的事，很快他们都不知该与对方说些什么。
[ZH] 陈浩宇行礼道：“我有些困，先回去睡，不打扰殿下。”
[ZH] 申简辰没有说任何话，目送着陈浩宇的身影消失在黑暗中。他仍旧没有困意，继续没有目的地走着。
[ZH] 不知不觉来到梦容居住的院子外，申简辰驻足站立一会儿，回忆梦容与他在一起的点点滴滴……
[ZH] 他感到十分惋惜，心中自问：“难道因为我心里装着他人才逼得曾经单纯善良的梦容变得残忍？”申简辰不愿再将所有责任归咎到自己身上。
[ZH] 梦容让他太过失望，失望到不想继续迁就她，不愿再培养他们之间的感情，一切到此为止。
[ZH] 他要竭尽全力营救千里寻，无论付出任何代价都不会放弃。哪怕救出千里寻后她毅然决然跟着陈浩宇走，申简辰也在所不惜。
[ZH] 第二日，陈浩宇在仪王府简单用过早饭，带着青瓜，用马车拉上礼物，按照名单上记好的名字和住址，一家一家挨门挨户拜访。
[ZH] 申简辰自然也不闲着，吩咐白风不必跟着，负责将汴梁的名医请来为仪王妃诊治。
[ZH] 白风很开心，他可借请名医的机会带苦荞逛街。
[ZH] 申简辰安排完，独自骑马来到西华门外，下马后，拿出仪王的腰牌向守门的侍卫出示。
[ZH] 侍卫看过后，还给仪王，吩咐一旁的侍卫帮仪王把马牵了，恭敬地说：“这会儿官家应该在紫宸殿，殿下想要拜见还需征得楚国公同意。”
[ZH] “楚国公？”申简辰对宫中之人依然不熟悉，只怪他匆忙穿越而来，又匆忙去了临安，对官家身边的朝臣知之甚少。
[ZH] 侍卫解释道：“就是童太师。”
[ZH] 申简辰才略微反应过来：“童贯？”
[ZH] 侍卫回道：“正是。”
[ZH] “为何我见官家还需他同意？”
[ZH] “殿下有所不知，自从梁内侍去了临安，官家才吩咐楚国公伴驾，只因官家十分信任楚国公，而且与他无话不谈。”
[ZH] 申简辰很无奈，赵朴作为皇子，想见自己的亲爹竟如此困难，还需一个奸臣同意：“烦请禀告楚国公，十三子仪王前来拜见官家。”
[ZH] “殿下不必跟我等客气，再说我等也没机会面见楚国公。您只管进，先在偏殿等候片刻，小人先去内侍府，找位小内侍前去请示。”
[ZH] 申简辰走进宫内，被一名侍卫带到一座没有名字的偏殿等候。偏殿应是专供有身份之人等待消息的临时休息之所，布置得倒也舒适，茶水、果品、糕点齐备，一旁还有宫女伺候。
[ZH] 申简辰坐在殿内耐心等待……
[ZH] 约莫半个时辰后，一位小内侍前来，先行礼。
[ZH] “小的帮仪王殿下走了一趟，楚国公请示官家后，让小的前来转达。若殿下是为女词人之事，就不必面圣。一来官家政务繁忙，二来，梁内侍还未回到临安，此事与他有关，需等他回来方可处理。楚国公还说，官家提醒殿下，既然从临安回来，往后老实待在王府，别到处乱跑，也少管他人的闲事。”
[ZH] 几句话将申简辰打发，他心中甚是不满，又无可奈何。
[ZH] 出了皇宫，申简辰不想回王府，一个人牵着马在街上瞎逛，来到一家茶肆附近，听到说话艺人正在说千里寻写的话本。心想，她写的话本竟也传到汴梁。
[ZH] 见不到人，不如听听话本消磨时光。
[ZH] 茶肆的伙计将马牵了，申简辰找地方坐下，要壶茶，尽量拿出耐心，边品茶边听话本。
[ZH] 千里寻现在应该被关在大牢里，也不知那种环境她能否适应？里面会不会很脏？会不会有狱卒欺负她？
[ZH] 申简辰越想越担心，故事没听几句，一盏茶没喝完，付了银子，迅速离开，骑上马一路打听大理寺的位置。
[ZH] 来到大理寺后，大理寺卿陈大人听说仪王前来，带领相关人员出来迎接，将仪王请进大理寺内。
[ZH] 申简辰第一次来大理寺，无心到处观察，未等落座，急切地说：“本王前来打听刚刚被送到汴梁交由大理寺审理的江南女词人幽栖居士，不知此案审理得如何？”
[EN] Settled into a guest room at Prince Yi’s Manor, Chen Haoyu had wanted to call on those influential officials that very night, though late-night visits were impossible. Tossing and turning in bed, he got dressed and wandered the manor grounds to pass the hours.
[ZH] 陈大人回道：“有劳仪王挂怀，女词人是两日前来到汴梁，现关押在狱中。官家吩咐，等梁内侍回来后再由大理寺审理此案。”
[EN] In the dark, another figure strolled along the garden path. Drawing closer, Haoyu recognized Prince Yi and hurried forward to greet him.
[ZH] “梁内侍何日归来？”
[EN] “Are the quarters to your liking, Young Master Chen?” Shen Jianchen asked politely.
[ZH] “如果下官没猜错，内侍大人已在回汴梁的路上。”
[EN] “The manor lacks nothing, and the room is comfortable. Out of deference to Your Highness, the servants look after me well. It feels no different from home.”
[ZH] 申简辰仔细推算时间，梁师成到汴梁恐怕至少需七八日：“有件事拜托陈大人。”
[EN] “Good. Tomorrow I will seek an audience with His Majesty about Youqi Jushi and get her out no matter what.”
[ZH] “殿下请说。”
[EN] “Thank you, Your Highness.” Still uneasy, Haoyu added, “Tomorrow I will also visit the high court officials Wenyuan and my father-in-law recommended. With luck, we may free my wife without troubling Your Highness.”
[ZH] “我想去狱中探望幽栖居士，不知可否方便？”
[EN] Sensing his guard, Shen Jianchen said, “We will work from both ends. Whichever path works, getting her out is all that matters.”
[ZH] 陈大人笑道：“您是仪王，凭您的腰牌，便可出入汴梁的牢狱。”
[EN] “Your Highness speaks the truth.”
[ZH] “多谢陈大人提醒。”申简辰埋怨自己穿越而来拥有尊贵的身份却不懂得利用，还跑来求一位大理寺卿，真是浪费时间。
[EN] Brought together by one woman and having spoken of tomorrow’s errands, the two men ran out of words. Haoyu bowed, excused himself to sleep, and disappeared into the night.
[ZH] 不过还是有所得，起码进一步确定想要救千里寻还需等到梁师成回到汴梁。
[EN] Still wide awake, Shen Jianchen kept walking until he found himself outside Mengrong’s courtyard. Stopping for a moment, memories of their time together surfaced. With a bitter pang, he wondered if keeping another woman in his heart had pushed a once innocent, gentle girl into cruelty. Refusing to shoulder every shred of blame, he let go. She had disappointed him too far; he would make no more allowances and try no longer to mend what lay broken.
[ZH] 申简辰离开大理寺后，安心回仪王府。
[EN] He would stake everything on saving Qianli Xun, even if she chose to walk straight back to Chen Haoyu the moment she walked free.
[ZH] 陈浩宇出去一整天，到晚上才失落而归，整个人看上去很疲惫。
[EN] At daybreak, Haoyu ate a quick breakfast, packed gifts into a carriage, and set out with Qinggua to visit every name and address on his list one by one.
[ZH] 他一进仪王府，来到仪王的屋子，“扑通”跪下：“仪王，求求您，一定要救我娘子，我只能靠您。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen kept busy too, telling Bai Feng to stay behind and bring Bianliang’s top doctors to examine Princess Consort Yi—an errand Bai Feng gladly used as an excuse to take Kuqiao out shopping.
[ZH] 晚饭刚摆上，申简辰拿起筷子还未来得及开动，被陈浩宇这么一闹，已无心夹菜，放下筷子。
[EN] Riding alone to Xihua Gate, Shen Jianchen dismounted and showed his Prince Yi waist token to the guards. After inspecting it and returning it, one guard had another take the prince’s horse and bowed. “His Majesty should be in Zichen Hall right now, Your Highness. To seek an audience, you will need the approval of the Lord of Chu.”
[ZH] “昨晚我当着你的面已表过态，同样的话不想说第二遍。青瓜，把陈公子扶起来，与我一同用晚饭。”
[EN] “Lord of Chu?” Shen Jianchen knew little of court titles, having transmigrated in haste and left for Lin’an soon after.
[ZH] 今日一天算是白白浪费，没有一个人愿意管娘子的事，陈浩宇一着急竟忘了仪王对他娘子的事最是用心。青瓜将公子扶起，又扶他在仪王对面坐好。
[EN] “Grand Preceptor Tong,” the guard clarified.
[ZH] “怎么？今日吃了闭门羹？”
[EN] “Tong Guan?”
[ZH] “唉，我按照提前写好的名单一一拜见，有的人直接将我拒之门外，有的则好言相劝，说梁内侍的事他们都说不上话，只能求官家，现下剩仪王您这一条路。您今日可见到官家？”
[EN] “The very man.”
[ZH] “官家不愿见我，他告诉我需等梁师成回到汴梁后再说。”
[EN] “Since when do I need his consent to see His Majesty?”
[ZH] “他何时回汴梁？”
[EN] “You may not know, Your Highness. Since Imperial Attendant Liang went to Lin’an, His Majesty ordered the Lord of Chu to attend him personally. His Majesty trusts him implicitly and confides in him on everything.”
[ZH] “还需等七八日。”
[EN] Helpless that an emperor’s son needed a corrupt favorite’s permission to see his own father, Shen Jianchen told the guard to report that his thirteenth son, Prince Yi, sought an audience.
[ZH] “这么久？我娘子岂不是还要在狱中关七八日？牢狱里肯定又脏又臭，她怎么受得了？”
[EN] “No need to stand on ceremony with us, Your Highness, and we have no standing to face the Lord of Chu anyway. Please step inside and wait in the side hall. I will go to the Office of Imperial Attendants and have a junior eunuch pass word up.”
[ZH] “明日我带你去狱中见她。”
[EN] Shown into an unnamed side hall built for high-ranking visitors—furnished comfortably with tea, fruit, pastries, and palace maids—Shen Jianchen waited in silence.
[ZH] 陈浩宇的脸上终于露出笑容：“真的能见到我娘子？”
[EN] About an hour later, a junior attendant entered and bowed. “I went on your behalf, Your Highness. After consulting His Majesty, the Lord of Chu bade me relay this: if you come regarding the female poet, there is no need for an audience. First, His Majesty is weighed down with state affairs; second, Imperial Attendant Liang has not yet returned to Bianliang, and since this case concerns him, it must wait for his return. Furthermore, the Lord of Chu said His Majesty reminds you that now you are back from Lin’an, you should stay quietly inside your manor, stop roaming about, and stay out of other people’s affairs.”
[ZH] “我大小也是个王爷，汴梁的牢狱还是进得。”
[EN] Dismissed in a few curt sentences, Shen Jianchen left the palace seething yet powerless. Unwilling to go straight home, he led his horse aimlessly through the streets until the sound of a storyteller outside a teahouse caught his ear—reciting one of Qianli Xun’s huaben tales. Even here in Bianliang, her stories had spread.
[ZH] 陈浩宇拿起筷子：“跑了一整天，没吃几口饭，现在才感到肚子饿。”
[EN] Unable to see her, he sat down at the teahouse, ordered a pot of tea, and forced himself to listen. Yet thoughts of her locked inside a filthy prison, tormented by jailers, soon broke his patience. Leaving his cup unfinished, he tossed down silver, mounted up, and asked directions to the Court of Judicial Review.
[ZH] 申简辰再次拿起筷子陪陈浩宇吃起来，他从来没想过有一日能与陈浩宇同桌吃饭，而且还是家常的晚饭。
[EN] Informed of Prince Yi’s arrival, Chief Minister Chen came out with his staff to welcome him inside. Shen Jianchen dispensed with pleasantries before even sitting down. “I came to ask about the southern poetess Youqi Jushi, brought here from Lin’an for your court’s review. Where does the case stand?”
[ZH] 按说他们是情敌，见面应该打起来才对。面对陈浩宇，申简辰讨厌不起来，唯一有的只是羡慕，羡慕他轻而易举得到他想要得到的人。
[EN] “Thank you for your concern, Your Highness,” Chief Minister Chen answered. “She arrived two days ago and is held in the prison. His Majesty ordered us to hold the trial until Imperial Attendant Liang returns.”
[ZH] 申简辰边吃边想，如若老天眷顾他，再给他一次可以得到千里寻的机会，他定然不会放过。
[EN] “When will he be back?”
[ZH] 他在心里问自己：“这样的机会还会有吗？”
[EN] “If I am not mistaken, he is already on the road to Bianliang.”
[ZH] 申简辰想起神柳下女尼送他的那句“守得云开见月明”，难道是想暗示他与千里寻还有机会？
[EN] Counting the days, Shen Jianchen judged it would take at least seven or eight more days. “I have a favor to ask, Minister Chen. May I visit her in prison?”
[ZH] 瞧着眼前陈浩宇吃得津津有味，申简辰内心叹口气，女尼不过是为了安慰他，才说些充满希望的话。千里寻已嫁给陈浩宇，他又那么爱她，一口一个娘子，叫得既肉麻，又令人羡慕。只要婚姻还在，千里寻永远不会属于他。
[EN] Chief Minister Chen smiled. “You are Prince Yi. With your waist token, the gates of every prison in Bianliang open to you.”
[ZH] 申简辰不由叹口气：“唉。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen felt foolish for begging a minister when his own rank held that authority. Still, he had learned what he needed: nothing could move until Liang Shicheng arrived. Thanking the minister, he rode back to his manor.
[ZH] 陈浩宇抬眼，见仪王吃个饭都神情落寞，难道是被娘子的事给愁的？
[EN] That evening, Haoyu trudged back exhausted and defeated. Finding Shen Jianchen just lifting his chopsticks for supper, he dropped straight to his knees. “Your Highness, please, you must save my wife! You are my only hope left!”
[ZH] “仪王叹气，该不会是因为我娘子的事不好办？”
[EN] His appetite gone, Shen Jianchen set his chopsticks down. “I told you my stance last night. I won’t repeat myself. Qinggua, help Young Master Chen up and have him dine with me.”
[ZH] 被对方误会，申简辰赶紧解释：“别瞎猜，幽栖居士的事的确不好办，也不至于难到让我叹气。”
[EN] Too frantic after a wasted day to remember Prince Yi’s own devotion to her, Haoyu let Qinggua pull him into the opposite chair.
[ZH] 来到汴梁后，陈浩宇本不想欠仪王的人情。一天的经历，使他不得不将希望寄托在仪王身上，他的回答令他安心许多。想到明日能见娘子，吃饭的速度不由加快。
[EN] “Shut out at every door?” Shen Jianchen asked.
[ZH] 晚饭过后，白风跟着王爷练完拳法，王爷累了，他像往常一样伺候他宽衣。
[EN] Haoyu sighed. “I visited every single name on the list. Some refused to see me outright; others gave gentle excuses that nobody can challenge Imperial Attendant Liang, only His Majesty. You are the only path left. Did you see His Majesty today?”
[ZH] 令白风感到奇怪的是，王爷从外面回来一直没问起仪王妃的事。他担心王爷嫌弃仪王妃，想要舍弃她，试探地说：“王爷，今日我请来两位大夫上门给王妃瞧病。”
[EN] “He refused me. Told me to wait until Liang Shicheng returns to Bianliang.”
[ZH] 申简辰淡淡地问：“大夫诊断的结果如何？”
[EN] “When will that be?”
[ZH] 王爷还是关心王妃，白风松口气：“两位大夫诊完脉都说治不好，还说他们的医术在汴梁街头算是最好的，若他们医不好，只能找宫里的御医诊治，若御医也治不好，恐怕仪王妃这辈子只能如此。”
[EN] “Another seven or eight days.”
[ZH] 申简辰淡淡地说：“明日你去趟太医局，请位医术好的太医前来。”
[EN] “So long! My wife will be stuck in that filthy, stinking cell for another week or more. How will she bear it?”
[ZH] “太医局在宫里，会让我进去吗？我只是王爷的侍卫，太医会不会给我面子？”
[EN] “Tomorrow I will take you inside to see her.”
[ZH] “去请太医，又不是见官家。你是我的侍卫，拿出腰牌，他们自然给你面子。”
[EN] Hope broke across Haoyu’s face. “I can really see her?”
[ZH] “对啊，我有腰牌，从来没用过，这次终于派上用场。”
[EN] “I’m still a prince. Getting into Bianliang’s prisons is well within my power.”
[ZH] 王爷已躺到床上，白风仍旧问：“王爷今日回来为何不去看望仪王妃？”
[EN] Relief awakened Haoyu’s hunger; he picked up his chopsticks and began eating. Watching his rival tuck into dinner, Shen Jianchen reflected on how strange it felt to share an ordinary meal with the man who held what he wanted most. He didn’t hate Haoyu—only envied him, wondering silently if Heaven would ever grant him a second chance, as the nun under the Divine Willow had hinted with her words about waiting for the clouds to part and reveal the moon. Listening to Haoyu talk of “my wife,” he thought the nun had only meant to comfort him, and let out a low sigh.
[ZH] 申简辰翻过身，背对着白风：“不该你操心的事别瞎操心。”
[EN] Haoyu looked up in alarm. “Why sigh, Your Highness? Is her case too hard to fix?”
[ZH] 白风不再瞎问，将灯熄灭，走出屋，把门从外面关好。
[EN] “Don’t jump to conclusions. It’s tricky, not hopeless.”
[ZH] ……
[EN] Relieved, Haoyu ate faster, eager for tomorrow’s visit.
[ZH] 汴梁的牢狱建在城东最偏僻处，从周围的景况能想象到狱中的环境根本好不到哪里去。
[EN] After supper, Shen Jianchen sparred with Bai Feng until tired, then let Bai Feng help him undress. Noticing the prince hadn’t asked once about his wife, Bai Feng tested the waters. “Your Highness, I summoned two physicians to examine the Princess Consort today.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇跟随仪王下了马车，因眼前的景况再次担忧娘子。他让青瓜从车上拿来两个包袱，一个里面是吃的，一个里面是用的。一会儿就要见到娘子，陈浩宇多少感到激动。
[EN] “What did they say?” Shen Jianchen asked evenly.
[ZH] 申简辰向牢狱大门口的狱卒亮出腰牌：“本王听说从临安来的女词人关在牢狱中，特来探望，还请前面带路。”
[EN] “Both said they could not cure her. They claim they are among the finest street physicians in Bianliang, so if they fail, only the palace’s imperial physicians might stand a chance. If even they cannot cure her, she may stay like this for life.”
[ZH] 狱卒看一眼腰牌，面色变得恭敬：“原来是仪王殿下，小的这就为您前面带路。”
[EN] “Go to the Imperial Medical Bureau tomorrow and summon a skilled imperial physician.”
[ZH] 狱卒叮嘱其他人将大门口看好，带领仪王向里走。
[EN] “The bureau is inside the palace. Will the guards let an attendant in?”
[ZH] 门口的人把陈浩宇和青瓜挡住：“你们两个不许进！”
[EN] “You aren’t asking to see His Majesty. Show my waist token and they will oblige.”
[ZH] 申简辰停下脚步，转过身：“他们随我而来，难道也进不得？”
[EN] “Right! I have a token I’ve never used. Tomorrow it earns its keep.”
[ZH] 狱卒立刻笑眯眯地说：“进得进得，只是上面有规定。不过仪王您来，可以破例多带一位。”
[EN] Once Shen Jianchen lay down, Bai Feng ventured one more question. “Why didn’t you go visit the Princess Consort after returning today?”
[ZH] 陈浩宇从青瓜手里接过两个包袱，背在自己肩上：“带我一个。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen rolled over, facing the wall. “Stop meddling in what isn’t your concern.”
[ZH] 青瓜退出门外，伸长脖子望着公子与仪王进入牢狱的院子，铁门被打开，二人进去后，铁门又重重关上。
[EN] Taking the hint, Bai Feng blew out the lamp, stepped out, and pulled the door shut.
[ZH] 牢狱中时不时传来难闻的气味，进入眼帘的都是些女犯人，每个狱室中的犯人都蓬头垢面，衣衫褴褛。
[EN] Bianliang’s prison stood in the loneliest corner of the east quarter, bleak enough from outside to warn of the squalor within. Following Prince Yi out of the carriage, Haoyu asked Qinggua to fetch two bundles of food and supplies.
[ZH] 陈浩宇想象着娘子的样子，她会不会也像她们一样？
[EN] At the gate, Shen Jianchen flashed his waist token. “I heard the poetess from Lin’an is held here. Lead the way.”
[ZH] 再向里面走一些，狱卒恭敬地说：“仪王殿下，女词人关在这里。”
[EN] The jailer’s manner flipped at once to deference, ordering the gate guards to watch the entrance while he escorted the prince inside. When guards moved to block Haoyu and Qinggua, Shen Jianchen turned back. “They came with me. Are they barred too?”
[ZH] 申简辰急切地说：“把门打开。”
[EN] The jailer beamed. “Not at all, though rules allow only one extra guest for Your Highness.”
[ZH] 狱卒掏出牢房的钥匙，打开锁子。马上要见到娘子，陈浩宇既紧张又兴奋。
[EN] Haoyu slung both bundles onto his own shoulders, leaving Qinggua to wait outside as the heavy iron gates slammed behind them.
[ZH] 当锁子打开后，申简辰上前一把推开牢房的门。
[EN] A foul stench hung over the corridor lined with women’s cells, where disheveled prisoners in rags stared out. Haoyu dreaded seeing his wife like them. Deeper inside, the jailer unlocked a cell, and Shen Jianchen pushed the door open.
[ZH] 里面正如他想象的那样简陋，一张床，一个低矮的桌子，床上铺着草席。千里寻坐在床边，手脚戴着铁链，面色苍白，看到他进来，面露喜色，站起来：“你怎么来了？”
[EN] Inside sat only a wooden bed topped with a straw mat and a low table. Qianli Xun sat on the bed’s edge, pale, iron chains shackling her wrists and ankles. At the sight of Shen Jianchen, delight lit her face, and she rose. “Why are you here?”
[ZH] 接着看到陈浩宇背着两个包袱进来，眼中闪着泪光。
[EN] Then she spotted Haoyu behind him with two bundles on his back, and tears welled in her eyes.
[ZH] ﻿“娘子，你受苦了。”
[EN] “My wife, look how you’ve suffered!”
[ZH] 千里寻的双眼也不由变得湿润：“你怎么也跑来？”
[EN] Her own eyes grew moist. “Why did you come all this way too?”

#CH 327
[ZH] 陈浩宇顾不上其他，上前抱住娘子：“娘子。”眼泪不由夺眶而出。
[ZH] 千里寻很自然地抱住陈浩宇，手上的铁链跟着叮叮当当地响：“没想到还能见到你，家里人可好？”
[ZH] 二人抱在一起，申简辰心里不舒服，又没有任何理由将他们分开，他转过身不去看他们。只要不看，心里应该会舒服一些，可却清清楚楚听到二人的对话。
[ZH] “家里人都很好，只是担心你。”
[ZH] “都怪我，惹下事，搞得家人不得安宁。”
[ZH] “家里人和我从来没怪过你，相反，因为此事全家人更认为我娶了位好娘子，来汴梁前一再叮嘱我一定要将娘子救出来。”
[ZH] 从陈浩宇的话中，千里寻听出其中的意味。正因为她被梁师成定罪，才向众人证明她的清白。陈家人，包括陈浩宇在内才会高看她。
[ZH] 挨到陈浩宇身上的包袱，千里寻问：“你怎么还背着包袱？难道里面是给我带的东西？牢里什么都用不上，也不让用。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇放开娘子，将背着的包袱取下，放在铺着草席的床上：“不过是吃的和用的，狱卒看在仪王的面子上允许将东西留下。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇本想继续抱住娘子，娘子却拖着铁链，向仪王行礼：“殿下为草民之事费心费力，草民无以为报，只能以礼相谢。”
[ZH] 申简辰转过身：“你我之间何必言谢？今日见过你，总算放心。暂时还没法救你出去，需等梁师成回到汴梁后大理寺才能启动你的案子，到时我会竭尽全力将你救出。暂且在狱中待些时日，我会叮嘱狱卒对你多加照顾。”
[ZH] 仪王的言辞中每一句饱含着个人情感，陈浩宇心中不爽。需依靠仪王救娘子，他不敢得罪贵人，又不能憋着不说话：“仪王若真想让狱卒照顾我娘子，不如先让人把她手脚上的铁链去掉，也好松快些。”
[ZH] 申简辰二话不说向外喊道：“来人！”
[ZH] 开门的狱卒过来：“仪王殿下有何吩咐？”
[ZH] “把幽栖居士手上和脚上的铁链打开。”
[ZH] 狱卒为难道：“她是重犯，若出意外，小的担不起责任。”
[ZH] “出了什么事由我担着，你尽管打开。”
[ZH] 狱卒无奈，仪王的话不得不听，上前掏出钥匙，将千里寻手上和脚上的铁链打开，起身说道：“殿下，探望的时间快到，您有什么话需快些说。”
[ZH] 被狱卒催促，申简辰与陈浩宇反而不知该说些什么。
[ZH] 千里寻活动活动手脚，轻松地说：“这下松快许多，今日我终于体会到有位王爷做朋友的好处。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇再次上前抱住娘子：“你真傻，要是早些跟我去乡下躲避，也不至于有今日。”
[ZH] 千里寻轻拍着陈浩宇的背部：“躲起来不过是迟几日蹲大牢，说不定还会连累陈家。现在不挺好？只要家里都好，陈家的生意没有受到影响，我吃点儿苦不算什么。再说都是我惹下的事，无论什么结果都需我承担。”
[ZH] 申简辰问：“难道你知道梁师成要求见你的原因？”
[ZH] 除了梁师成好色，陈浩宇想不出还会有什么原因。仪王的问题显然包含着其他说法，他放开娘子：“梁师成好色成性，除贪恋娘子美色外，还能有什么原因？”
[ZH] 千里寻并未回答陈浩宇，而是向仪王发问：“难道你已知？”
[ZH] 申简辰点点头：“你被送到汴梁后我才得知。”
[ZH] “梦容可好？”
[ZH] “她做错事，已尝到恶果，精神变得失常。”
[ZH] “精神失常？怎么会这样？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇在跟前，申简辰不好将详细经过说明：“我刚说过，她做错事，因错误的行为才导致精神失常。”
[ZH] 千里寻不好再问下去。
[ZH] 显然娘子和仪王都知道其他原因是什么，他们不明说，陈浩宇很是着急。想问时，外面的狱卒再次提醒：“仪王殿下，时间到。”
[ZH] 申简辰看一眼千里寻，目光中充满心疼和不舍，他没有说道别的话，转身走出牢房。
[ZH] 陈浩宇第三次抱住娘子：“还有什么原因？能不能告诉我？”
[ZH] 千里寻不愿说出梦容的事，再次轻拍陈浩宇的背部：“哪有其他原因？仪王走了，你也快去，狱卒等着锁牢房的门。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇恋恋不舍地放开娘子：“你先在狱中待几日，我和仪王定会救你出去。”
[ZH] 狱卒继续催促：“时间已到，还请公子快些出来。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇终于放开娘子的手，走出牢房，目睹牢房的门被锁上，狠狠心，转身离开。
[ZH] 牢狱外，陈浩宇向仪王追问：“刚才殿下与我娘子提到的其他原因是什么？”
[ZH] 申简辰装作不知：“什么原因？你的问题倒把我搞糊涂。”
[ZH] 仪王往马车旁走，陈浩宇紧跟上。
[ZH] 两人在马车里坐定后，陈浩宇仍不死心，继续追问：“殿下装傻充愣，我可不傻，你明明问我娘子是不是知道梁师成要见她的原因，快告诉我，到底还有什么原因？”
[ZH] 申简辰根本不愿说，他要替梦容保留最后一点儿尊严，除此之外，还有其他。
[EN] Chen Haoyu brushed everything else aside and swept his wife into an embrace, tears spilling from his eyes. Qianli Xun hugged him back naturally, the iron chains clinking between them.
[ZH] 刚才在牢房里，他与千里寻之间的对话只有他们听得懂，陈浩宇无法领会。申简辰很享受这种感觉，又岂会将实情告知从而破坏只属于他和千里寻之间的感觉？
[EN] “I never thought I’d see you here,” she said. “Is everyone at home well?”
[ZH] “即使有，我也不会告诉你，还是不要再问。”
[EN] Standing beside them, Shen Jianchen felt a dull ache in his chest. Having no right to part them, he turned his back so he wouldn’t have to look, though every word reached his ears clearly.
[ZH] 人家是王爷，救娘子还要仰仗他，陈浩宇不好再追问下去。想到刚才在牢房里，娘子与仪王之间仿佛只有他们二人的那段对话，心里老大不舒服。
[EN] “Everyone is fine, just sick with worry over you.”
[ZH] 回到仪王府时，白风正送宫中的太医往外走。
[EN] “It’s my fault for stirring up trouble and costing the family their peace.”
[ZH] 太医见到仪王，也是恭敬行礼。
[EN] “Nobody blames you—not me, not anyone at home. Quite the opposite. Because of this, everyone respects you even more as a worthy wife. Before I left for Bianliang, they told me over and over to bring you back safe.”
[ZH] 仪王并未向太医问起仪王妃的病情，陈浩宇心里有不好的预感，恐怕仪王又会将目标盯向自己的娘子。
[EN] Qianli Xun caught the deeper meaning behind his words. By standing up to Liang Shicheng and taking his false charge, she had proved her innocence to the Chen household beyond doubt. Feeling the bundles against his shoulders, she asked, “Why carry packs in here? You can’t use anything inside a prison.”
[ZH] 倒是白风，送走太医后，快步跟上仪王，主动汇报诊断的结果。
[EN] Haoyu let go, slipped the bundles off, and laid them on the straw mat. “Just food and daily things. Out of respect for Prince Yi, the jailer let them stay.”
[ZH] “王爷，宫中的太医医术的确好，诊治完，将仪王妃得病的原因和病症说得一字不差。唉，只可惜太医也治不好，还说仪王妃的病容易遗传，恐怕是王妃的家人有此病史，才过继到王妃身上。若仪王妃生下孩子，多半也会染上此病，有朝一日受到外因诱发同样会犯病。”
[EN] He reached to hug her again, only for Qianli Xun to step back, chains dragging, and drop into a formal bow before Shen Jianchen. “Your Highness has spent great care and effort on this humble subject’s behalf. I have no way to repay you save this bow of gratitude.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇在一旁听了，很想笑白风，两句话的事，却说那么多。见仪王面色凝重，他不敢露出笑意，否则会被认为是幸灾乐祸。
[EN] Shen Jianchen turned around. “Why speak of thanks between us? Seeing you with my own eyes sets my mind at ease. I cannot get you out just yet—the Court of Judicial Review won’t open your case until Liang Shicheng returns to Bianliang. Once he arrives, I will do everything in my power to free you. Bear with it a few days more. I will see that the jailers look after you.”
[ZH] 正好来到路口处，陈浩宇打算回房歇息，行礼道：“殿下，我回房了。”
[EN] Every word carried personal devotion, souring Haoyu’s mood. Needing the prince’s help yet unable to swallow his pride completely, he cut in. “If Your Highness wants the jailers to care for my wife, start by taking off her wrist and ankle chains so she can breathe easier.”
[ZH] 申简辰点头回礼后，朝另一边拐去，边走心中边想着赵朴与梦容的事。
[EN] Without hesitation, Shen Jianchen called out for the guard. When the jailer stepped up, the prince ordered him to unlock Youqi Jushi’s shackles.
[ZH] 梦容是赵朴在路边捡回来的妹妹，根本没见过她家里的人，无法得知梦容的家人有没有同样的病史。太医既如此说，搞不好梦容的家人真的有此类病史。
[EN] “She is a capital prisoner, Your Highness,” the jailer hedged. “If something happens, this lowly servant cannot bear the blame.”
[ZH] 他想起在柳岸别馆的屋顶偷看梁师成用针扎春金巧的场景……
[EN] “If anything happens, I will bear it. Unlock them.”
[ZH] 同样的经历，春金巧无事，梦容却出事。她不像商妓会应付男人，自然会害怕，便诱发出遗传的疾病，加上痛失孩子，又知道自己不能再有身孕，病情自然加重。
[EN] Powerless to refuse a prince, the jailer took out his keys, freed her wrists and ankles, and stood up. “Your time is almost up, Your Highness. Say what you must quickly.”
[ZH] 申简辰难以想象当时梁师成用针在梦容的身上扎下时她痛苦的样子，他也不愿去想，冷冷地说：“转告邹嬷嬷，既然治不好，便不要再治。”
[EN] Hurried by the guard, both men fell strangely tongue-tied. Qianli Xun rolled her shoulders and flexed her ankles. “Much better! Today I finally see the perks of having a prince for a friend.”
[ZH] 王爷变得如此冷漠，白风一时难以接受，愣愣地站在原地，忘记进屋伺候王爷。
[EN] Haoyu pulled her back into his arms. “You fool. If you had fled to the countryside with me earlier, none of this would have happened.”
[ZH] 苦荞正好过来：“白风，你愣着做什么？”
[EN] She patted his back gently. “Hiding would only have delayed my arrest and dragged the Chen family down too. Isn’t this better? As long as everyone at home is safe and the family business untouched, a little hardship means nothing. I brought this on myself, and I’ll answer for it.”
[ZH] 白风才回过神来，把苦荞拉到一边：“自从仪王妃病了，我总感觉王爷开始嫌弃她，仪王妃真可怜。”
[EN] “Wait—do you know why Liang Shicheng demanded to see you?” Shen Jianchen asked.
[ZH] 小姐不让将仪王妃害她的事告诉任何人，苦荞从未向白风提起过。白风可怜仪王妃，苦荞很想向他说明，话到嘴边，想起小姐的话，不敢说出口，只好表现出不满：“是可怜，可我家小姐更可怜。”
[EN] To Haoyu, the answer seemed obvious—Liang’s lechery. Hearing an unspoken layer in the prince’s question, he let go of his wife. “Liang Shicheng is a shameless lecher after her looks. What other reason could there be?”
[ZH] “幽栖居士再怎么着也是正常人，王爷和陈公子正想尽办法营救。可仪王妃的病已治不好，王爷也不再去看望她，两相对比，还是仪王妃可怜。”
[EN] Instead of answering Haoyu, Qianli Xun looked at Shen Jianchen. “You already found out?”
[ZH] “可怜之人必有可恨之处。”
[EN] He nodded. “Only after they sent you off to Bianliang.”
[ZH] “苦荞，你怎能这么说？”
[EN] “How is Mengrong?”
[ZH] 白风不知实情，一再维护仪王妃，苦荞很虽生气也能理解：“我才懒得管仪王妃的事，今日仪王去狱中见我家小姐，我想进去问问小姐的境况。”
[EN] “She did wrong and reaped the bitter fruit. She has lost her mind.”
[ZH] 苦荞丢下白风，朝仪王的屋子走去。
[EN] “Lost her mind? How did that happen?”
[ZH] 白风追上去拦住她：“王爷心情不好，不要去打扰，想了解情况可以去问陈公子。”
[EN] With Haoyu standing right there, Shen Jianchen kept his answer guarded. “As I said, she acted wrongly, and the consequences broke her mind.”
[ZH] 苦荞拍拍自己的脑袋：“看我这脑子，一心想着你，光想到问仪王，竟忘记我家姑爷也去了，这就去问他。”
[EN] Taking the hint, Qianli Xun asked no more.
[ZH] 苦荞迅速跑开，白风才进屋陪王爷。
[EN] Watching the two of them trade coded words he couldn’t follow left Haoyu frantic. Before he could press for answers, the jailer called time. Shen Jianchen rested one long look on Qianli Xun, full of pain and reluctance, then walked out in silence.
[ZH] ……
[EN] Haoyu hugged her a third time. “What other reason? Tell me.”
[ZH] 七八日的时间本不算长，可对申简辰和陈浩宇来讲度日如年。
[EN] “What other reason could there be?” she replied, patting his back. “His Highness already left. Hurry out so the jailer can lock up.”
[ZH] 好不容易过了八日，申简辰到宫中打探梁内侍的消息，结果说他还需三日才能回到汴梁。
[EN] Haoyu pulled away at last, promising that he and Prince Yi would free her, and stepped out as the cell door clanged shut.
[ZH] 然后又是度日如年地等待。
[EN] Outside the prison, Haoyu chased after Shen Jianchen. “What was that other reason you and my wife spoke of?”
[ZH] 三日后，申简辰又去宫中，既没见到官家也没见到梁内侍，只有一位小内侍前来传话。
[EN] “What reason? You’re baffling me.”
[ZH] “仪王殿下，内侍大人让我转告您，女词人谋杀朝廷官员罪大恶极，大理寺已将此案审理，十日后斩首。还请殿下以后不要再来宫中为此事打扰官家和内侍大人。”
[EN] Haoyu followed him right into the carriage and pressed harder. “Don’t play dumb with me, Your Highness. I’m no fool. You asked straight out whether she knew why Liang Shicheng summoned her. Tell me what else happened!”
[ZH] 听到这样的结果，申简辰差点晕倒。他踉跄着走出宫外，骑上马，往大理寺而去。
[EN] Shen Jianchen refused to speak, both to guard the last shred of Mengrong’s dignity and to keep that secret thread shared solely with Qianli Xun intact. “Even if there were one, I wouldn’t tell you. Stop asking.”
[ZH] 来到大理寺，大理寺卿陈大人恭敬地接待了仪王。
[EN] Dependent on the prince to save his wife, Haoyu swallowed his frustration, though that private exchange inside the cell left a bitter taste in his mouth.
[ZH] 还未等仪王说明来由，陈大人主动说：“殿下肯定是为女词人的事而来，昨日梁内侍回到京城，先来到大理寺，要求将女词人处死，下官不敢不从。再说她谋杀的人可是内侍大人，官家也因此勃然大怒，她必须死。”
[EN] Back at Prince Yi’s Manor, Bai Feng was just seeing an imperial physician out the gate. The doctor bowed respectfully to the prince, who walked straight past without asking a word about his wife—sending a chill down Haoyu’s spine.
[ZH] 申简辰责怪自己为何要晚进宫一日？他依然不愿放弃，请教道：“她不能死，还请陈大人帮本王出个主意。”
[EN] Once the doctor left, Bai Feng hurried after Shen Jianchen to report. “The palace physicians truly know their craft, Your Highness! He named the exact cause and symptoms of the Princess Consort’s illness down to the word. Sadly, he can’t cure her either. He said this sickness tends to run in bloodlines—someone in her family likely passed it down to her, and any child she bore might inherit it and break down under outside strain too.”
[ZH] 陈大人叹口气：“唉，除了官家开口饶她不死，没有其他更好的办法。”
[EN] Listening nearby, Haoyu almost laughed at how Bai Feng turned two sentences into a speech, though he kept a straight face lest Prince Yi think him gloating. At the path fork, Haoyu bowed and headed back to his room.
[ZH] 申简辰谢过陈大人后，离开大理寺，一路上都是一副垂头丧气的模样。
[EN] Shen Jianchen turned down the opposite path, brooding over Zhao Pu’s sister. Zhao Pu had picked Mengrong up off the roadside as a child; nobody knew her birth family or their ailments. If the imperial physician spoke the truth, perhaps her bloodline carried that flaw after all.
[ZH] 白风骑马跟着王爷，他也很难过，担心苦荞知道结果会哭得死去活来。
[EN] He pictured Liang Shicheng driving needles into Chun Jinqiao at Liu’an Villa. Both women endured the same torture, yet Jinqiao shrugged it off while Mengrong—unversed in handling men—shattered from terror, waking the dormant illness, followed by losing her unborn child and learning she might never conceive again. Shen Jianchen refused to dwell on her agony during those hours and spoke coldly to Bai Feng. “Tell Matron Zou: since it cannot be cured, stop all treatment.”
[ZH] 回到仪王府，两人刚走到正院的中央，陈浩宇带着青瓜和苦荞迎上来。仪王失落的表情让他产生不好的预感，还是充满希望地问：“殿下，事情办得如何？”
[EN] Shocked by the prince’s chill, Bai Feng stood frozen until Kuqiao walked up and asked what was wrong. Pulling her aside, he murmured, “Ever since the Princess Consort fell ill, His Highness acts like he wants nothing to do with her. Poor lady.”
[ZH] 申简辰照实说：“昨日梁师成回到汴梁，已让大理寺定案，十日后要将幽栖居士斩首。”
[EN] Bound by her mistress’s order never to tell a soul about Mengrong’s plots, Kuqiao held her tongue, though anger flared in her chest. “Pitiable, yes. My miss is far worse off.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇只觉双腿一软，差点倒下，还好被青瓜扶住。
[EN] “At least Youqi Jushi has her wits about her, and His Highness and Young Master Chen are moving heaven and earth to save her. Princess Consort Yi can never be cured, and His Highness won’t even visit her. Who’s more to be pitied?”
[ZH] “什么？斩首？”陈浩宇硬撑着走上前，抓住仪王，“你不是王爷吗？不是官家的儿子吗？为何连个人都救不了？呜呜……，娘子！”
[EN] “Anyone so pitiful must have something hateful in her to bring it on.”
[ZH] 苦荞已泪流满面，伤心地哭起来：“呜呜……，小姐，呜呜……”白风走到苦荞身边，抱住她，让她靠着自己哭个够。
[EN] “Kuqiao! How can you say that?”
[ZH] 青瓜也十分难过，问道：“仪王，难道二少夫人真的没救了？”
[EN] Though frustrated that Bai Feng didn’t know the truth, Kuqiao understood his ignorance and changed the subject, heading toward the prince’s rooms to ask after her mistress. Bai Feng caught up and told her to ask Chen Haoyu instead, since Prince Yi was in a black mood. Laughing at herself for thinking only of Bai Feng and forgetting her young master had gone too, Kuqiao darted off.
[ZH] 申简辰说：“只有一个办法。”
[EN] Seven or eight days crawled past like years. At last Shen Jianchen rode to the palace for word, only to learn Liang Shicheng needed three more days. Another three agonizing days crept by. When Shen Jianchen went back, neither His Majesty nor Liang saw him; a junior attendant came out instead.
[ZH] 众人都用泪眼充满希望地盯着仪王。
[EN] “His Majesty’s Attendant bade me inform Your Highness: the poetess’s crime of attempting to murder a court official is heinous. The Court of Judicial Review has tried and closed the case. She will be beheaded in ten days. Do not trouble His Majesty or the Attendant over this matter again.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇追问道：“快说，还有什么办法？”
[EN] Nearly collapsing on the spot, Shen Jianchen staggered out to his horse and galloped to the Court of Judicial Review.
[ZH] 申简辰说：“只要官家开口饶她不死，她便可获救。”
[EN] Chief Minister Chen received him respectfully and answered before the prince spoke. “Your Highness must be here for the poetess. Imperial Attendant Liang arrived in the capital yesterday and came straight to this court demanding her execution. We dared not disobey. Besides, attempting to murder him infuriated His Majesty. She must die.”
[ZH] 众人听后，又开始哭起来，这哪是什么办法？纯粹是再次告诉大家已无人能救。
[EN] Blaming himself for waiting an extra day to check the palace, Shen Jianchen begged the minister for any way out. Chief Minister Chen only sighed: nothing short of His Majesty’s own word could spare her.
[ZH] “白风，照顾好仪王府和陈公子，不许跟着我。”申简辰命令完，转身向门外走。
[EN] Shen Jianchen rode back to the manor crushed, with Bai Feng trailing gloomily behind, dreading how Kuqiao would take the news.
[ZH] 白风上前拦住：“王爷要去哪里？”
[EN] In the main courtyard, Haoyu, Qinggua, and Kuqiao rushed up. One look at the prince’s face foretold disaster, yet Haoyu clung to hope. “How did it go, Your Highness?”
[ZH] 申简辰没有回头：“去宫中找官家，请他同意饶幽栖居士不死。”
[EN] “Liang Shicheng reached Bianliang yesterday and had the court close the case. Youqi Jushi is set to be beheaded in ten days.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇立刻说：“我跟你一起去！”
[EN] Haoyu’s knees buckled; Qinggua barely caught him. Haoyu lurched forward and grabbed Shen Jianchen’s robe. “Beheaded?! Aren’t you an imperial prince? Aren’t you His Majesty’s son? How can you fail to save one person?!”
[ZH] “你没有资格进宫，去了起不到任何作用，安心待在仪王府，等我的消息。”申简辰再次走出仪王府，骑上马向皇宫而去。
[EN] Kuqiao burst into loud sobs, collapsing against Bai Feng’s chest as he held her tight. Pale with grief, Qinggua asked if any hope remained.
[ZH] 到达皇宫后，他直接来到紫宸殿外，不由分说跪在地上。申简辰已决定，他要跪到官家同意为止。
[EN] “There is only one way,” Shen Jianchen said. “If His Majesty speaks the word to spare her life, she lives.”
[ZH] 若官家不同意，他便跪死在紫宸殿外。千里寻要被斩首，他活着还有什么意义？
[EN] Fresh tears broke out; to them, asking the Emperor for mercy sounded no different from saying nobody could save her.
[ZH] 早已有人将此事报告给梁内侍。
[EN] “Bai Feng, look after the manor and Young Master Chen. Do not follow me.” Shen Jianchen turned for the gate, telling Haoyu—who begged to come—that a commoner had no right inside the palace and should wait at the manor.
[ZH] 梁师成到殿外瞧了一眼，心中冷笑道：“现在知道我的厉害，打我那日怎么就没想到你也有下跪的一天？哼！还扬言女词人若有个三长两短，让我死无葬身之地。十日后她就要被斩首，你是王爷，又能奈我何？”
[EN] Riding straight into the palace, Shen Jianchen walked to the stone steps outside Zichen Hall and dropped to his knees. He had made his choice: he would kneel without rising until His Majesty relented—or until death took him, since life without Qianli Xun held no meaning anyway.
[ZH] 紫宸殿内，赵佶正站在桌前写字，一旁有宫女伺候。
[EN] Word soon reached Liang Shicheng. Stepping outside to gloat over the kneeling prince, Liang sneered to himself: had the prince imagined this day when beating him in Lin’an? Ten days from now the poetess’s head would roll, prince or no prince.
[ZH] 梁师成走进来，禀告道：“官家，仪王殿下正跪在殿外。”
[EN] Inside Zichen Hall, Zhao Ji stood at a long table practicing calligraphy while palace maids waited beside him. Liang entered and reported that Prince Yi was kneeling outside.
[ZH] 赵佶继续写字：“为女词人的事？”
[EN] “For the female poet?” Zhao Ji asked, never pausing his brush.
[ZH] 梁师成回道：“正是。”
[EN] “Yes, Your Majesty.”
[ZH] “喜欢跪就让他跪着，跪累了自然会回去。”赵佶越发写得轻松。
[EN] “If he likes kneeling, let him kneel. Once his knees ache enough, he’ll go home.”
[ZH] 梁师成凑到跟前，赞道：“官家的字写得挺拔秀丽，独具韵味，笔画瘦劲，似铁画银钩，刚硬中尽显细腻与灵动。”
[EN] Liang leaned closer in praise. “Your Majesty’s brushstrokes stand tall and slender, keen as iron hooks and silver cords, uniting steel strength with nimble grace!”
[ZH] 赵佶沉浸在溢美之词中，根本没将仪王跪在殿外之事放在心上。
[EN] Basking in flattery, Zhao Ji dismissed his kneeling son from his mind.

#CH 328
[ZH] 仪王已两日未回府，所有人担心不已。
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷与喜兰照顾完仪王妃，时不时到前面打听王爷有没有归来。
[ZH] 陈浩宇与白风决定去皇宫问问。他们来到西华门外，向守门的侍卫打听，才知仪王在宫里跪了两日。
[ZH] 两人请求进入宫中，被侍卫厉声呵斥：“你们以为皇宫是什么地方？谁想进就能进？”
[ZH] 白风拿出腰牌：“我是仪王的侍卫，难道也进不得？”
[ZH] 守门的侍卫说：“前几日你来请太医，只需去外殿，自然进得；今日不行，仪王正跪在紫宸殿外，那可是内殿，你们两个要身份没身份，要官职没官职，根本没资格进。”
[ZH] 白风收起腰牌，哄骗道：“你放我进去，我还是去找太医。”
[ZH] 守门的侍卫劝道：“找借口进去有什么用？即使进了外殿，也进不了内殿，内殿的宫门也有人把守。还是赶紧回去吧，仪王毕竟是皇子，官家不会将他怎样。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇想趁机冲进去，侍卫抽出身上佩戴的宝剑挡在面前，他只好退回。
[ZH] 白风说：“只要王爷好着我便放心，陈公子，你我根本做不了什么，还是回去等消息吧？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇不愿离去：“要回你回，我守在宫门口等消息。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇将宫门外观察一遍，找个地方跪下，仪王能做到的事他也能。
[ZH] 白风很无奈：“你喜欢跪就跪着，我回去让青瓜给你捎件暖和的披风来。唉，也不知我家王爷跪在里面冷不冷？有没有水喝？有没有吃东西？”
[ZH] 白风将宫墙瞧一遍，目测用轻功能轻松进入，才安心坐上马车离开，他打定主意，等天黑后悄悄潜入宫中。
[ZH] ……
[ZH] 申简辰一直跪在紫宸殿外，粒米未进，滴水未沾。白天还好些，到了晚上，深秋的寒风吹来，冷得全身打颤。
[ZH] 再饿再冷他从未想过放弃跪下去的想法，他已想好，要么跪死在殿前，要么触动官家，只要他稍微对自己产生怜悯之情，千里寻便有获救的希望。
[ZH] 跪了足足两日，膝盖已麻得失去知觉，官家都没有任何反应，梁师成也未出现。申简辰担心自己的做法根本不灵，又一想只剩下这一个办法，便继续心安理得地跪着。
[ZH] 天又变黑，冷风袭来，不由打了个寒颤。寒颤过后，他感觉有披风披在身上。转头看去，原来是白风。
[ZH] “你怎么会进来？”
[ZH] 白风见王爷一脸疲惫，嘴唇干得起了一层皮。他从腰间取下水囊递给王爷：“里面是热水，一路过来还有些温度，王爷凑合着喝。”
[ZH] 申简辰顾不上追问，接过水囊，咕咚咕咚喝起来，直到所有水下肚，才觉舒服许多。
[ZH] 白风蹲在王爷身边悄悄说：“王爷，我是偷偷溜进来的，不能久留，这包糕点给你，饿了偷偷吃上一块。”
[ZH] “我不需要，快些回去，省得被发现害我前功尽弃。”申简辰将糕点推回，也将空的水囊递给白风，然后将披风取下，放进白风的怀里，“平白无故多件披风会被怀疑，快走吧。”
[ZH] 白风只能将披风和糕点收回：“王爷既然不要，我把糕点和披风送给宫外跪着的陈公子。”
[ZH] “陈浩宇跪在宫外？”
[ZH] “是的，跟王爷一样傻。”
[ZH] 传来皇宫侍卫走动的声音，申简辰催促道：“赶紧走，小心被宫中侍卫发现。”
[ZH] 白风迅速启动轻功离开。
[ZH] 皇宫侍卫经过时，白风已消失得无影无踪，他们并未发现异常，只是朝仪王看了几眼，佩服他竟然能跪这么久。
[ZH] 皇宫外，青瓜站在一旁陪着陈浩宇：“公子跪在宫外有什么用？不如跟我回去吧？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇不这么认为：“如果娘子顺利被救下，起码知道有我一份功劳。”
[ZH] “您又何必跟仪王争着看谁能吃苦？少夫人已嫁给您，仪王再能吃苦，把人救出来，少夫人还得跟您回临安。”
[ZH] “道理是这个道理，只怕她因此心里更放不下仪王。”
[ZH] 白风已神不知鬼不觉出现在两人面前，他把手中装有糕点的布袋塞进陈浩宇怀里。
[ZH] “我家王爷不吃，送陈公子。”
[ZH] 白风又把披风递给青瓜：“给陈公子再披上一件，晚上冷。”
[ZH] 青瓜好奇地问：“难道仪王连披风都不要？”
[ZH] 白风一脸担忧：“我家王爷担心前功尽弃，只把水囊里的水喝了，什么都不要。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇把装有糕点的布袋递给青瓜，又把青瓜早已给他穿好的披风取下：“仪王不要，我也不要，统统拿回去。”
[ZH] 青瓜接在手中劝道：“公子，您没必要跟仪王学，他是表演给官家看，您再表演也没人看，不要为难自己。”
[ZH] “我是表演给自己看，只有这样才能心安。”
[ZH] “哪有自己演戏给自己看的道理？”
[EN] Two full days passed with no word of Prince Yi returning. Back at the manor, panic spread. Between tending Princess Consort Yi, Matron Zou and Xilan hurried to the front courtyard hour after hour for news.
[ZH] “无论你说什么，我都不会要，快跟白风回去，别在这里烦我。”
[EN] Unable to sit still, Chen Haoyu and Bai Feng rode to Xihua Gate. When they asked the guards inside, they learned Prince Yi had been kneeling outside Zichen Hall for two days straight.
[ZH] 青瓜对白风说：“你回去吧，我要留下陪我家公子。”
[EN] “Let us inside!” they pleaded.
[ZH] 白风担心王爷不愿离去：“既然你不走，我也留下，今晚睡马车里，就当陪我家王爷。”
[EN] “What do you take the imperial palace for?” a guard snapped. “Think anyone can wander in?”
[ZH] ……
[EN] Bai Feng flashed the waist token. “I’m Prince Yi’s personal guard. Surely I can go in?”
[ZH] 天刚蒙蒙亮，上朝的马车经过西华门外，朝中官员见有人跪在宫外，甚是稀奇，上前问明缘由，才知与仪王跪在紫宸殿外原因一致。
[EN] “Two days ago you came for the imperial physicians, who sit in the outer court,” the guard replied. “Today is different. Prince Yi is kneeling outside Zichen Hall in the inner palace. With neither rank nor office, neither of you may step inside.”
[ZH] 早朝结束后，再从宫中出来时，人依然跪着。经过的官员有的唏嘘，有的嘲笑，有的劝陈浩宇跪着无用，大理寺已定罪，不可能挽回。
[EN] Bai Feng tucked the token away and tried bluffing that he needed a physician again, only for the guard to advise him against wasting breath: even past the outer gate, another watch guarded the inner palace. As an imperial son, Prince Yi wouldn’t come to serious harm anyway.
[ZH] 紫宸殿内的官家怎么都没想到，仪王为了女词人如此坚持，三日不吃不喝身体会出现问题，不由担忧起来，吩咐身边的小内侍出去瞧瞧，送些喝的吃的。
[EN] When Haoyu moved to rush past, the guards drew their swords, forcing him back. Seeing no way in, Bai Feng offered to go wait at the manor, yet Haoyu refused to leave. Scanning the open ground outside Xihua Gate, Haoyu dropped to his knees as well—if Prince Yi could kneel, so could he. Sighing at his stubbornness, Bai Feng promised to send Qinggua back with a warm cloak, secretly sizing up the palace wall to confirm he could vault over it once night fell.
[ZH] 小内侍拿着吃食和水来到仪王面前，见仪王耷拉着脑袋，整个人看上去很难受。
[EN] For two days and nights outside Zichen Hall, Shen Jianchen neither ate a grain of rice nor drank a drop of water. By day the chill was bearable, yet late-autumn night winds cut to the bone until his whole body shook and his knees went dead numb. Still, no sign came from His Majesty or Liang Shicheng. Doubt crept in, yet knowing no other path remained, he held firm.
[ZH] 小内侍说：“仪王殿下，官家让我给您送水和吃的来，您好歹吃些喝些。”
[EN] As dusk deepened into dark on the second night, a gust set him shivering—and then a warm cloak settled over his shoulders. Turning his head, he found Bai Feng crouched beside him.
[ZH] 申简辰硬撑着没有倒下，他睁开眼睛：“告诉官家，除非饶幽栖居士不死，否则我长跪不起，不吃不喝，直到咽下最后一口气。”
[EN] “How did you get in here?”
[ZH] 小内侍拿着东西回到紫宸殿，将仪王的原话回给官家。
[EN] Seeing his master’s gaunt face and cracked, peeling lips, Bai Feng unhooked a waterskin. “Hot water, Your Highness. Still warm from the ride. Drink up.”
[ZH] 赵佶说：“去把梁内侍唤来。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen gulped down every drop before sighing in relief. Whispering that he had slipped over the wall and couldn’t stay long, Bai Feng pressed a bundle of pastries into his hands for when nobody looked.
[ZH] 不多时，梁师成来到紫宸殿拜道：“官家可是为仪王之事？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen pushed the pastries back, handed over the empty waterskin, and pulled off the cloak. “Take them back. If anyone catches me warm and fed, all this kneeling goes to waste. Hurry before you’re spotted.”
[ZH] 赵佶笑道：“还是你最了解朕，仪王毕竟是朕的儿子，今日已是第三日，再这么跪下去，恐怕性命不保……”
[EN] With no choice, Bai Feng gathered the things. “If you won’t take them, I’ll bring them to Young Master Chen kneeling outside the gate.”
[ZH] 梁师成猜到官家的意思，自己再得官家信任，也是臣子；外面那位官家再不喜欢也是官家的亲生儿子。三日不吃不喝跪着，官家不理会，已给足他面子，没必要再计较。
[EN] “Chen Haoyu is kneeling outside?”
[ZH] “臣的事是小事，仪王的事才是大事，官家想怎么处理臣绝无异议。”
[EN] “Yes. Just as foolish as Your Highness.”
[ZH] 赵佶起身，走到梁师成跟前，拍着他的肩膀：“既然内侍大人性命无忧，不如饶女词人不死？朕答应你死罪可免，活罪怎么个受法由你来定。”
[EN] Hearing boots approaching, Shen Jianchen waved him off. Bai Feng vanished into the night air via light-foot skill an instant before the patrol passed. Spotting nothing amiss, the guards only glanced at the prince in quiet awe at his endurance.
[ZH] 梁师成自然不会放过好机会：“那就让她在牢中待上几年，方可泄臣心头之恨。”
[EN] Outside Xihua Gate, Qinggua hovered anxiously over Haoyu. “What good does kneeling out here do, Young Master? Come back with me.”
[ZH] 赵佶想想说：“甚好，朕同意。”
[EN] “If my wife makes it out alive, at least she’ll know I bore a share of this too,” Haoyu muttered.
[ZH] 梁师成还是不甘心，想要将事情搅得更乱些：“官家，您有没有想过为何仪王为女词人之事会在殿外长跪不起？”
[EN] “Why compete with Prince Yi over who can suffer more? She’s already married to you. No matter how much he endures, once she’s freed she goes back to Lin’an with you.”
[ZH] 赵佶疑惑道：“为何？”
[EN] “True enough, yet I dread she’ll find it even harder to let him go.”
[ZH] “臣去临安才知，仪王甚是喜欢女词人，她成亲那日还跑去抢亲，搞得人尽皆知。”
[EN] Bai Feng materialized silently beside them and dropped the cloth bag of pastries into Haoyu’s lap. “His Highness refused to eat them. They’re yours.” He handed Qinggua the extra cloak for the night cold.
[ZH] “竟有这等事？”
[EN] Learning that Prince Yi had touched only the water, Haoyu shoved the pastries back to Qinggua and shrugged off the cloak Qinggua had wrapped around him earlier. “If Prince Yi won’t take them, neither will I! Take everything back.”
[ZH] “臣听太医说仪王妃精神失常，再难医好，仪王身边总需有人陪伴，不如将女词人趁机赐给仪王作侧妃？”
[EN] “Prince Yi is putting on a show for His Majesty,” Qinggua argued. “Nobody inside can even see you! Why torment yourself?”
[ZH] “好主意，也不枉仪王为她跪了几日的情分。不过免去女词人的死罪总不能以仪王在外跪了许久为由，需找个合适的理由。”
[EN] “I’m putting on a show for my own conscience. It’s the only way my heart rests easy.”
[ZH] 梁师成想到好主意：“臣替官家想到两个理由：其一，可说臣大度，因性命无忧，不愿再与女词人计较，令其在狱中忏悔改过；其二，我朝历来重视文人，女词人才华卓越，官家爱才惜才，同意饶她不死。”
[EN] “Who ever heard of putting on a show for yourself?”
[ZH] “甚好！还需让她写首词，朕好以此做文章。”赵佶点头认可，走到桌案前坐下，“朕曾读过她的诗词，阴柔有余，气概不足，毕竟是闺阁女子，不能要求太高。她的诗词境界是差了些，技巧倒是上乘，连朕都自愧不如。辛苦内侍大人派人到狱中走一趟，告诉女词人，她若能写出一首没有一个字的诗词来，又能让朕拍案叫绝，便饶她不死。”
[EN] “Save your breath. Go back with Bai Feng and stop bothering me.”
[ZH] “臣遵命。”
[EN] Refusing to leave his master, Qinggua stayed—and Bai Feng stayed too, sleeping in the carriage through the night for Prince Yi’s sake.
[ZH] 梁师成拜别官家后走出紫宸殿，来到仪王面前，装模作样拜道：“仪王殿下，别跪了，回去吧，女词人的命我要了有何用？殿下喜欢就给殿下。”
[EN] At first light, officials riding into Xihua Gate for morning court stared at Haoyu kneeling on the stones, and stared again when leaving after court adjourned. Some sighed, some snickered, and some urged him to go home since no power could overturn a closed sentence from the Court of Judicial Review.
[ZH] 申简辰不敢相信自己的耳朵，睁开眼，抬起头，一脸疲惫地问：“你没骗我？”
[EN] Inside Zichen Hall, Zhao Ji never expected Prince Yi’s resolve to hold through three days without food or drink. Fearing the boy’s life might actually slip away, he sent a junior attendant outside with water and food.
[ZH] “不敢欺瞒殿下，官家已答应饶她不死，我还要派人去狱中为官家讨要诗词。”
[EN] Head drooping and strength fading, Shen Jianchen forced his eyes open when the attendant spoke. “Tell His Majesty this: unless Youqi Jushi’s life is spared, I will kneel until my dying breath, touching neither food nor drink.”
[ZH] 听到这话，申简辰再也撑不下去，倒在地上。
[EN] Hearing the exact message, Zhao Ji summoned Liang Shicheng.
[ZH] 梁师成招手叫来两名侍卫：“将仪王殿下扶到宫外，找辆马车送回仪王府。”
[EN] “Your Majesty sent for me over Prince Yi?” Liang asked, bowing.
[ZH] 两名侍卫将仪王扶到宫外，白风正坐在马车的边缘，一眼瞧见王爷，冲上去：“感谢两位大哥，把王爷交给我。”
[EN] Zhao Ji smiled. “You know me best. He is my own son after all. Three days now—if he keeps kneeling like this, his life may be in real danger.”
[ZH] 两名侍卫见外面又有人又有马车，省去他们不少麻烦，便将仪王交给白风。
[EN] Liang read his master at once. Favorite though he was, he remained a servant, whereas the man outside was blood of the Emperor’s loins. Letting the prince kneel three days unheeded had already honored Liang’s face enough. “My grievance is trifling; His Highness’s safety comes first. Whatever Your Majesty decides, I obey without complaint.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇见仪王从宫中出来，也从地上爬起来，人还没站稳，腿又酸又疼，朝下倒去，还好被青瓜扶住。
[EN] Rising to pat Liang’s shoulder, Zhao Ji said, “Since you came through unscathed, why not spare her death? I promise you: spare her life, yet you decide what living punishment she endures.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇急切地问：“仪王！我娘子可救下？”
[EN] Liang seized the opening. “Then lock her in prison for a few years, to ease the outrage in my heart.”
[ZH] 仪王并未回答，应该是饿得没有力气。陈浩宇不好再追问，适应了一会儿，能站稳时，膝盖又疼得难受，被青瓜搀扶着在宫外找块石头坐下。
[EN] “Good. Approved.”
[ZH] 青瓜撩开公子的裤子，膝盖已跪伤。还好他有先见之明，提前给身上装了瓶治伤的药，拿出来给公子涂抹上。
[EN] Still eager to stir the pot, Liang added smoothly, “Has Your Majesty wondered why Prince Yi risks death kneeling outside your hall for a poetess?”
[ZH] 陈浩宇说：“先别管我，到马车里给仪王涂上，他膝盖上的伤肯定比我严重。”
[EN] “Why?”
[ZH] 青瓜很快替公子涂完药，到马车跟前，从窗户将药瓶递进去：“白风，我这儿有药，给仪王涂上。”
[EN] “When I visited Lin’an, I learned Prince Yi adores her—even crashed her wedding day, till the whole city knew of it.”
[ZH] 白风接过药瓶，开始为王爷涂药。
[EN] “Such a thing happened?”
[ZH] 申简辰在马车里靠好，拿起水囊喝水，第一次觉得靠在马车里静坐的状态无比舒服。喝完水后，主动要求道：“有吃的吗？快给我些。”
[EN] “What is more, the imperial physicians report Princess Consort Yi has lost her wits beyond all cure. A prince needs someone beside him. Why not bestow this poetess upon Prince Yi as a secondary consort?”
[ZH] 白风停下涂药的动作，将一旁装有糕点的袋子递给王爷：“看来幽栖居士已无性命之忧。”
[EN] “A fine idea! At least his days on those stones won’t go unrewarded. Still, we cannot cite his stubbornness as our reason for pardoning a capital crime. We need a fitting pretext.”
[ZH] 申简辰顾不上回答，打开布袋，拿出糕点大口吃着。平常他不大动的糕点，此刻吃起来竟如此美味。
[EN] Liang had two ready. “First, declare that since I escaped fatal harm, I chose magnanimity and asked only that she repent in prison. Second, our dynasty treasures men and women of letters; out of love for rare talent, Your Majesty spares her.”
[ZH] 几块糕点下肚，申简辰觉得精神许多，吩咐道：“回府。”
[EN] “Excellent! Have her compose a ci poem too, so I have grounds to praise her gift.” Returning to his desk, Zhao Ji reflected, “I have read her verses before—delicate and wistful in plenty, though lacking heroic sweep. Still, for a woman of the inner chambers, one cannot ask too much. Her depth falls slightly short, yet her craft is peerless—even I feel humbled beside it. Trouble yourself to send someone to the prison and tell her: if she can compose a lyric without a single written word that still makes me slap the table in amazement, I will spare her life.”
[ZH] 白风已帮王爷涂完药，向外喊道：“陈公子！王爷让回府！”
[EN] “Your servant obeys.”
[ZH] 青瓜也将陈浩宇扶上马车。
[EN] Stepping out of Zichen Hall, Liang bowed with mock humility before Shen Jianchen. “Get up and go home, Your Highness. What use do I have for the poetess’s life? Since you fancy her, she is yours.”
[ZH] 马车启动后，陈浩宇再次问：“我娘子可有救？”
[EN] Barely trusting his ears, Shen Jianchen lifted his exhausted face. “Are you telling the truth?”
[ZH] “若没救下她的性命，我怎么可能出来？”
[EN] “Would I dare deceive Your Highness? His Majesty agreed to spare her and is sending someone to fetch a poem from her.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇忍不住哭了起来：“呜呜……！”
[EN] Relief cut the last thread holding him upright; Shen Jianchen slumped onto the flagstones. Liang waved two guards over to carry him out to his waiting carriage.
[ZH] 申简辰面露无力的笑容：“你哭的样子实在难看，还是不要再哭。”
[EN] Seeing Prince Yi emerge, Bai Feng ran up to take over from the guards. Haoyu scrambled to his feet too, only for his sore, cramped legs to give way until Qinggua caught him.
[ZH] 陈浩宇被逗乐，又是哭，又是笑的。
[EN] “Did you save my wife?!” Haoyu gasped, breathless.
[ZH] ……
[EN] Too starved to speak, Shen Jianchen stayed silent. Qinggua helped his young master onto a roadside stone, rolled up his trousers over raw knees, and reached for a bottle of salve he had brought along.
[ZH] 梁师成派两名小内侍来到狱中，在千里寻被关押的牢房内将官家的要求说了，让狱卒将纸笔在小木桌上摆好。
[EN] “Leave mine for a moment,” Haoyu insisted. “Put it on Prince Yi first. His knees must be twice as bad.”
[ZH] 一名内侍命令道：“赶快写，我等还急着拿给官家预览。”
[EN] After quickly tending Haoyu’s knees, Qinggua passed the bottle through the carriage window to Bai Feng, who set to work on the prince. Leaning back against the cushions, Shen Jianchen drained his waterskin, relishing the simple bliss of sitting upright again, then asked for food.
[ZH] 三日前，千里寻已知自己必死无疑，突然之间又能继续活着，倒令她喜出望外。
[EN] Bai Feng passed over the bag of pastries, beaming that Youqi Jushi must be safe. Shen Jianchen bolted down pastries that he barely touched on normal days, felt his strength return, and ordered the driver home.
[ZH] 两位小内侍说，写首词能救她性命，开始她难以置信，仔细想来，也许是因为仪王的原因，也不知他做了什么，才救下她的性命？
[EN] As the carriage rolled, Haoyu climbed aboard and asked again, “Is she really saved?”
[ZH] 千里寻已想好写哪首词。
[EN] “Would I have left the palace if she weren’t?”
[ZH] 官家的要求本是为难，在她看来却也十分有趣，朱淑真的诗词中偏偏就有这么一首。不同的是朱淑真用它表达相思之情，而她却要借助这首词挽救性命。
[EN] Haoyu burst into loud sobs, then laughed through his tears as Shen Jianchen smiled weakly and told him he looked hideous crying.
[ZH] 只见她提笔在空白的纸张上画下许多圈圈和一些黑点来……
[EN] Meanwhile in prison, two junior attendants set brush and paper on the low wooden table in Qianli Xun’s cell, relayed the Emperor’s command, and urged her to write fast.
[ZH] 一旁的两位小内侍瞧见都一脸懵，不知女词人画的是什么符号？官家的要求也是奇特，不能有一个字，还怎么写诗作词？可怜女词人无奈到乱涂乱画的地步。
[EN] Resigned to death three days ago, Qianli Xun felt joy surge through her chest. Doubtless Prince Yi had moved mountains for her. Better yet, the Emperor’s impossible riddle suited her perfectly: Zhu Shuzhen actually had a famous piece built from nothing but circles and dots—written for longing originally, now pressed into service to save her neck.
[ZH] 画完后，千里寻将纸张拿起，站起来，递给一位小内侍，顺便问道：“小内侍可知仪王殿下的状况？”
[EN] Dipping the brush, she filled the sheet with circles and black dots. The two attendants gaped, bewildered by how anyone could compose verse without words, thinking the poor poetess reduced to scribbling nonsense.
[ZH] 小内侍回道：“仪王殿下为你的事在紫宸殿外跪了快三日，官家才松口，梁内侍也才派我等前来。”
[EN] Handing the sheet over, Qianli Xun asked softly how Prince Yi fared. One attendant told her Prince Yi had knelt outside Zichen Hall for nearly three days until His Majesty relented.
[ZH] 果然与仪王有关，千里寻慨叹自己欠他的越来越多，恐怕这辈子无法再偿还，只能等来世。若真有来世，她希望两人相遇后没有任何阻碍，顺顺利利走到一起。
[EN] Hearing that, Qianli Xun sighed quietly inside. Her debt to Shen Jianchen grew deeper by the day—a debt she could never clear in this life, only pray to repay in the next, when nothing would stand between them.

#CH 329
[ZH] 紫宸殿内，赵佶展开小内侍呈上的纸张，瞧着上面又是圈圈又是点点，一时不解：“没有一个字，倒是符合要求，可画的是什么？”
[ZH] 他招呼梁师成：“你也过来瞧瞧，猜猜到底是什么名堂？”
[ZH] 梁师成靠近官家，向纸上瞧去，也不知是何意：“官家不让她写一个字，她也只能靠乱涂乱画糊弄，应当治她一个欺君之罪。”
[ZH] 赵佶似乎有所领悟：“朕总觉得圈圈点点中自有诗意，来人！”
[ZH] 小内侍上前行礼：“官家有何吩咐？”
[ZH] “再去狱中一趟，就说朕允许她把画中表达的诗意写出来。”
[ZH] 官家如此说，梁师成不敢再强调“欺君之罪”。
[ZH] 等小内侍匆匆从狱中将写好诗句的纸张拿回呈上后，赵佶将两张纸放在一起，盯着写有字的纸张赞道：“蝇头小楷写得不错，娟秀而典雅，看得出女词人秀外慧中。”
[ZH] 再看题目“圈儿词”，赞道：“妙！难怪画那么多圈圈。梁内侍，你来读词，朕负责欣赏她画的圈圈点点，看女词人解得如何？”
[ZH] 梁师成从桌案上拿起写有字的纸张念道：
[ZH] 相思欲寄无从寄，画个圈儿替。
[ZH] 话在圈儿外，心在圈儿里。
[ZH] 单圈儿是我，双圈儿是你。
[ZH] 你心中有我，我心中有你。
[ZH] 月缺了会圆，月圆了会缺。
[ZH] 我密密加圈，你须密密知我意。
[ZH] 还有数不尽的相思情，我一路圈儿圈到底。
[ZH] 梁师成念完批判道：“这哪是词？连个词牌名都没有。要说是诗，也不符合诗的规矩，句子也是有长有短，参差不齐。下官认为是个四不像。”
[ZH] 赵佶却说：“梁内侍批判得不无道理，可在朕看来朱淑真写得甚妙。朕让她写词，必然在她自己看来是词。我朝文人依据词牌名写词已是惯例，有人打破惯例有何不可？朕看中的便是她心思巧妙和写诗作词的技巧，真是个难得的人才。还请梁内侍将这首词宣扬出去，再到大理寺走一趟，就说朕因女词人才华出众饶她不死。”
[ZH] “臣这就去办。”梁师成再对女词人和仪王不满，也不敢违抗官家的旨意，“将女词人在狱中关多久，还需官家定夺。”
[ZH] “朕本想关她五六年，既然她才华斐然，仪王又对她一往情深，关到宣和七年吧，待中秋团圆之日再将她放出来。”
[ZH] 梁师成仔细推算，几乎两年时间，心中甚是满意：“臣这就去办，不过，还需官家写下一道圣旨，臣好去仪王府告知仪王官家将女词人赐给他。”
[ZH] 赵佶二话不说，提笔写下圣旨，盖上玉玺。
[ZH] 梁师成到大理寺传完官家的旨意后，带着圣旨来到仪王府。
[ZH] 申简辰带领众人跪下接旨。
[ZH] 只听梁师成展开圣旨念道：“奉天承运，官家诏曰：朕虽身居皇位，爱才惜才之心从未更改。朱淑真谋杀朝廷官员，本应斩首，朕念其才华难得，特免死罪，判其入狱，于宣和七年中秋佳节放回。朕观朱淑真温婉聪慧，与仪王甚是匹配，故将其赐予仪王。着陈浩宇速速回临安解除婚约，不得有误。钦此！”
[ZH] 娘子被免去死罪，陈浩宇一阵开心，可听到要将娘子赐予仪王，还逼他解除婚约，想要反抗，被仪王一把拉住，小声警告：“若不想死，先老实接旨。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇愤然跪下，与仪王一起磕头谢恩：“草民接旨！”
[ZH] 仪王则口称：“儿臣接旨！”
[ZH] 梁师成将圣旨放在仪王的手中：“仪王殿下好艳福，仪王妃刚一病，又得一美人，真是羡煞旁人，紫宸殿外长跪不起总算有所回报。”
[ZH] 接过圣旨后，申简辰带领众人站起。
[ZH] 想到梁师成迫害梦容，心中自有怒气，为了不破坏好不容易争得的局面，只能忍了，然后向白风使眼色。
[ZH] 白风收到王爷的眼神，悄悄离开人群。
[ZH] 梁师成又看向陈浩宇，他显然难以接受圣旨的内容。
[ZH] 梁师成装作安慰的样子：“天下女子千千万，陈公子不要再跟仪王争，仪王抢亲那会儿就该拱手相让，要不今日哪有这等事？还是乖乖回临安把婚约解除了，重新娶一位娘子，依然有好日子过。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇憋着怒气一言不发。
[ZH] 梁师成见仪王不搭理他，拱手道：“殿下忙，下官还要回去交差。”
[ZH] 梁师成刚走出仪王府门外，陈浩宇再也忍不住，一拳打向仪王：“我今日终于明白，你帮我娘子是为了得到她！是也不是？”
[ZH] 申简辰用掌挡住陈浩宇打来的一拳：“我若说不是，你可信？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇又抡起另一个拳头：“我才不信！你就是个伪君子！”
[ZH] 二人打起来，其他人想上前劝说，结果仪王挡住陈浩宇另一拳，命令道：“你们都退下！这是我跟陈浩宇之间的事！最好不要插手！”
[ZH] 青瓜为公子感到伤心的同时，担心公子吃亏，跪在地上求道：“仪王殿下，您千万不要伤我家公子，他的心里只有少夫人，已容不下其他女子，突然来一道圣旨，公子肯定难以接受……”
[ZH] 陈浩宇大声说：“青瓜，你起来，滚得越远越好！我今日哪怕是被打死也要与仪王打一架！”
[EN] Inside Zichen Hall, Zhao Ji unfolded the sheet covered in circles and dots and frowned. “Not a single written character, so it meets the rule—yet what has she drawn here?”
[ZH] 青瓜还是跪在地上：“不！我不滚！”
