[EN] “Word along the road. Better safe than sorry—and I’ve meant to move my household south for years. A friend already bought farmland for me in Yangzhou; if not now, when? Without a poem, go seek someone else.”
[ZH] 听到这话，白风和喜兰感到失落，仪王妃并未彻底好。看来还需用神医开的药好好调养，方可彻底痊愈。
[EN] “Wait, Great Physician!” Qianli Xun stepped in his path. “Those white chrysanthemums were so lovely they stay in the mind. Let me compose a poem on ‘White Chrysanthemum’ as a gift for you.”
[ZH] 申简辰倒觉得现在的梦容无比正常，好感萌生许多，他走到跟前：“我虽不是你的哥哥，只要你乖乖的，我会替他照顾你一辈子。”
[EN] Intrigued, Liu Miaoshou paused. “Good! Recite it. If it passes muster, I’ll stay in Yanjing a few extra days to treat your friend personally. If not, my disciple stays behind instead.”
[ZH] “谢谢你。”乔梦容脸上露出笑容，看向淑真，“我不是躺在床上，就是待在屋里，好闷，现在就陪我逛街？”
[EN] “You won’t be disappointed,” Qianli Xun promised, recalled the lines, and recited:
[ZH] 千里寻点头答应：“听你的。”
[EN] “Swirling autumn hues hold thick the tender dew;
[ZH] 喜兰伺候仪王妃换好衣裳，扶着她走出客房外。
[EN] The keen west wind keeps pure the young frost.
[ZH] 乔梦容挣脱喜兰，上前拉起淑真的手：“我想和你牵着手逛街。”
[EN] Count it no common bloom beside the eastern hedge—
[ZH] 千里寻乖乖答应：“好。”
[EN] Fresh and clean, it once gathered the Moon Palace’s fragrance.” [TN: Translator’s rendering.]
[ZH] 两人不顾他人，牵着对方的手，一起走下楼，走出客栈。
[EN] Turning the lines over silently, Liu Miaoshou burst into praise. “What rare talent! Under your brush, white chrysanthemums gleam heavy with dew, glowing with tender feeling against the autumn wind—and that closing phrase, ‘the Moon Palace’s fragrance,’ captures both the season and their unworldly purity! Talent like yours deserves a seat of honor in the world of letters.”
[ZH] 喜兰快步跟上。
[EN] Unable to hold back, Shen Jianchen spoke up. “Perhaps you don’t know, Physician Liu—the young lady before you is none other than the southern poetess Zhu Shuzhen.”
[ZH] 白风请示道：“王爷，要不要跟上？”
[EN] Liu Miaoshou looked her up and down in wonder. “You are the famed poetess of Jiangnan—Youqi Jushi herself?”
[ZH] 申简辰说：“远远跟着，好护她们周全。”
[EN] “The very same,” she replied. “Please look kindly on our long journey and treat my friend.”
[ZH] 白风提醒道：“幽栖居士多厉害，哪需要我们保护？”
[EN] “To meet the southern poetess today is rare fortune! For Youqi Jushi’s sake, I’ll linger a few days in Yanjing. Today I must see my family off toward Yangzhou first; tomorrow morning I will call on you myself. Where are you staying?”
[ZH] 申简辰瞪了白风一眼，没说一句话，也下了楼。
[EN] “Yunlai Inn,” Shen Jianchen answered, giving his surname as Shen when asked.
[ZH] 白风自语道：“别以为我不知道，是王爷自己想跟幽栖居士逛街。”
[EN] True to his word, Liu Miaoshou arrived at Yunlai Inn first thing the next morning with his medicine chest. Taking Mengrong’s pulse and studying her closely, he declared with certainty, “Besides me, nobody alive could cure this.”
[ZH] 步行来到街上，申简辰一直跟在身后，保持一定的距离。他顾不上关注大街上背着包袱出城的人，一心放在前面两位女子的身上。
[EN] Joy lit everyone’s faces. Liu Miaoshou asked for a room at the inn to perform three days of acupuncture, and Shen Jianchen told Bai Feng to cover every expense and book him quarters downstairs.
[ZH] 她们牵着手，比往常任何时候都显得亲密，画面实在美好。不用看正面便知两人正在开心地笑。此刻她们的关系已恢复到从前，申简辰希望时光永远停留在这一刻。
[EN] Over the next three days, Liu Miaoshou applied needles to Mengrong’s head on schedule. Dull as she was, she seemed to sense she was being treated and sat quietly without crying or fussing.
[ZH] 白风没有王爷那么多闲情逸致，他更关注街上背着包袱匆忙出城的人。他还看到小摊小贩的数量减少了一半，有一些店铺也已关门，如此看来燕京城要发生大事。
[EN] Watching, Qianli Xun felt hope mixed with dread that a healed Mengrong might hate her anew. She resolved to head straight back to Lin’an once the three days ended. Glancing at Shen Jianchen’s absorbed profile, she prayed silently that after they parted in three days, Mengrong would recover and the two of them would grow old together in peace.
[ZH] 白风拦住一位背着行李的妇人：“大婶，为什么很多人像逃难似的？到底发生了什么？”
[EN] Sensing her eyes on him, Shen Jianchen looked over—only for her to snap her gaze away, making him grin to himself that she still wouldn’t admit her feelings.
[ZH] 大婶急切说：“金人要进城了，快逃吧，只怕到时连命都没了。”大婶说完，匆匆离开。
[EN] By the end of the third day, Mengrong no longer begged for silk flowers or babbled nonsense, though her gaze remained vacant. Leaving a prescription to be taken for six months until full recovery, Liu Miaoshou collected his fee and hurried south after his family.
[ZH] 白风靠近仪王：“王爷，金人要来，还是尽快离开燕京吧？”
[EN] Sitting on the bed, Qianli Xun took Mengrong’s hand. “You’ll recover soon, though I can’t stay to see that day. I’m going home to Lin’an today. Take care of yourself—when we meet again, let’s still be the closest of friends.”
[ZH] “今日陪她们逛街，明日回。”申简辰并不当回事，他实在不想破坏眼下的气氛。
[EN] Mengrong sat blankly without a twitch. Letting go, Qianli Xun stood before Shen Jianchen. “I’m leaving. Look after Mengrong.”
[ZH] 白风不好再说什么，继续跟着。他想不通，街上卖东西的人越来越少，仪王妃和幽栖居士有什么好逛的？还那么起劲儿？
[EN] He caught her arm at once. “In such a rush to leave me?”
[ZH] 千里寻感受着两年多来久违的友情，心思并未放在时不时擦肩而过逃亡百姓的身上。梦容说着时而正常，时而充满傻气的话语，她都爱听，只因那些话语没有一句充满怨恨。
[EN] She didn’t pull free, speaking evenly. “Her recovery is underway. It’s time I went home so my family stops worrying.”
[ZH] “为什么街上很多人背着包袱？像是要离开？”
[EN] “Can’t bear to stay away from Chen Haoyu?” he asked bluntly right in front of Bai Feng and Xilan.
[ZH] 千里寻不想梦容害怕，编了一个善意的谎言：“估计都是像我们一样暂时来燕京的人，办完事打算回家。等你逛完街，我们也快些离开。”
[EN] “I married him. My place is at the Chen manor in Lin’an.”
[ZH] “不，我不想走，我喜欢燕京。”
[EN] Sick with impatience over his master keeping silent about the decree, Bai Feng blurted out, “Your Highness, the Imperial Decree—”
[ZH] “为什么？燕京比不上临安，更比不上汴梁。”
[EN] “Shut your mouth! Nobody asked you!” Reluctantly, Shen Jianchen let go of her arm. “Go if you must. Before long, I’ll come to Lin’an for you.”
[ZH] “因为有你陪我。”
[EN] “Why bother?” Hardening her heart, Qianli Xun walked toward the door.
[ZH] 千里寻被感动：“恐怕往后我陪你的时间并不多。”
[EN] Just as she reached the threshold, Mengrong’s voice rang out behind her—clear and normal as before her illness. “Shuzhen! Don’t leave… all right?”
[ZH] “为何？”
[EN] Joy froze Qianli Xun in place. Turning back, she hurried to the bed. Mengrong looked up with slightly sluggish eyes. “Don’t go, Shuzhen. It’s our first time in Yanjing—come walk the streets with me, please?”
[ZH] 千里寻站住，盯着梦容郑重地说：“因为我不想破坏属于你的幸福。”
[EN] Tears sprang to Qianli Xun’s eyes. “All right. I won’t go. I’ll walk with you.”
[ZH] 乔梦容一脸委屈：“我总感觉好久好久没见到你，你好像做了一件让我伤心的事，我很难过，可又想不起来是哪件事。”
[EN] Delighted, Xilan cried out that the Princess Consort was cured, though Shen Jianchen stayed sober when Mengrong looked right at him and murmured, “Why do you look identical to my brother? Too bad you aren’t him. Brother went up to heaven, waiting for me there.”
[ZH] 千里寻担心梦容会想起往事，小心问：“若我真的做过让你伤心的事，你会原谅我吗？”
[EN] Seeing she still needed the six months of medicine, Bai Feng and Xilan sank back down, yet Shen Jianchen stepped forward gently. “Even if I am not your brother, as long as you’re good, I’ll look after you for him my whole life.”
[ZH] 乔梦容宛然一笑：“一定是因为我病了，才会胡思乱想，你怎么会做伤害我的事？即使你真的做了，我也会原谅你。因为在我的记忆中，你是我唯一的闺中密友。”
[EN] Mengrong smiled, thanked him, and told Shuzhen she was sick of beds and locked rooms and wanted to go out right now.
[ZH] 千里寻的双眼再次湿润。
[EN] Once Xilan dressed her, Mengrong slipped away from her maid and laced her fingers through Qianli Xun’s. “I want to hold hands while we walk.”
[ZH] 乔梦容靠近她，悄悄说：“身后那人根本不是我哥哥，还要冒充他，你说他是不是骗子？”
[EN] Trailing with Xilan, Bai Feng asked whether they should follow. Telling him to keep a safe distance to guard them, Shen Jianchen ignored Bai Feng’s teasing reminder about Youqi Jushi’s fists and headed down behind them.
[ZH] 千里寻含泪说：“他是仪王赵朴，是你的哥哥。”
[EN] Out on the street, Shen Jianchen kept pace far enough back, ignoring streams of citizens hurrying toward the city gates with bundles, eyes fixed solely on the two women ahead walking hand in hand like old times—wishing time could freeze right there.
[ZH] “连你也骗我。”乔梦容说完，看到不远处一个卖糖葫芦的，“我想吃糖葫芦，我们一人买一个吧？”
[EN] Less dreamy, Bai Feng noticed half the stalls gone and shops shuttered. Stopping a woman lugging baggage, he learned Jin troops were marching into the city!
[ZH] 不等淑真答应，乔梦容像个快乐的孩子一般，跑到卖糖葫芦的跟前。喜兰追上去，付了银子。
[EN] “Your Highness, the Jin are coming—let’s leave Yanjing now!” Bai Feng urged.
[ZH] 卖糖葫芦的人将两串糖葫芦递给乔梦容，她拿着一串咬了一口：“真甜。”
[EN] “We walk with them today and leave tomorrow,” Shen Jianchen dismissed him, unwilling to shatter the moment.
[ZH] 然后举起另一串朝淑真招呼：“快过来！这个给你！”
[EN] Puzzled as Bai Feng fell silent, Qianli Xun gently fibbed when Mengrong asked why folks carried bundles, claiming they were out-of-towners heading home.
[ZH] 千里寻走上前，接过糖葫芦，也吃上一口，赞道：“你买的糖葫芦就是好吃。”
[EN] “I don’t want to leave,” Mengrong murmured. “I like Yanjing because you’re with me.”
[ZH] 乔梦容朝后面的申简辰瞥上一眼：“我怎么感觉他老是看你，他会不会是喜欢上你？”
[EN] Moved, Qianli Xun stopped and spoke earnestly. “I won’t have much time to stay by you from now on, because I don’t want to spoil your happiness.”
[ZH] 千里寻担心梦容试探她，迅速解释：“怎么会？难道你忘了，我已经成亲。”
[EN] Looking hurt, Mengrong whispered, “I feel like I haven’t seen you in ages… like you did something that broke my heart, though I can’t remember what.”
[ZH] “成亲？”乔梦容像是回想着以前的事，“你什么时候成亲的？我怎么一点儿印象都没有？我只记得乔公子喜欢你，难道你嫁给他？”
[EN] Tense, Qianli Xun asked softly, “If I really did hurt you once, would you forgive me?”
[ZH] 原来神医扎针的效果是让梦容忘记很多事，难怪她会如此真诚地对待她。
[EN] Mengrong smiled sweetly. “Only my sickness making me imagine things. How could you ever hurt me? Even if you did, I’d forgive you—in my memory, you’re my only bosom friend.”
[ZH] “想什么呢？陪我继续逛街。”乔梦容拉起淑真的手，两人边吃糖葫芦边逛。
[EN] Tears blurred Qianli Xun’s eyes again. Leaning closer, Mengrong whispered that the man behind them was an impostor pretending to be her brother, then brightened at a vendor selling candied hawthorns and darted over like a child while Xilan paid.
[ZH] 当他们来到街市中心时，四周传来马蹄声。
[EN] Biting one skewer (“So sweet!”), Mengrong held the second out to Shuzhen, then glanced back at Shen Jianchen. “Why does he keep staring at you? Could he be falling for you?”
[ZH] 千里寻心想：“不好！难道金军已入城？”
[EN] “How could that be? Did you forget I’m married?”
[ZH] 只见街上的行人开始四处逃窜，小商贩快速收起摊位，商铺也立刻关了大门。
[EN] “Married? When did you marry? All I remember is Young Master Qiao liking you—did you marry him?”
[ZH] 申简辰走上前，指着另一条街：“那边的街道没有马蹄声，应该安全，我们尽快过去。”
[EN] Realizing the physician’s needles had wiped parts of her memory clean—explaining her sudden warmth—Qianli Xun let Mengrong pull her along down the avenue eating candied hawthorns.
[ZH] 几人快步走到另一条街，来到一个废弃的屋子跟前，申简辰吩咐道：“白风，你和喜兰带仪王妃在废弃的屋子里躲会儿。”
[EN] Then thunderous hooves shattered the market square!
[ZH] 白风问：“王爷要做什么？”
[EN] Jin troops inside the city! Pedestrians screamed and scattered; peddlers grabbed stalls and bolted; heavy shop doors slammed shut.
[ZH] “这么大的历史事件，我又岂能错过？”申简辰转向千里寻，“要不要一起去瞧瞧？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen rushed up, pointing toward an empty side street free of hoofbeats. Hurrying down it to an abandoned house, he ordered Bai Feng and Xilan to hide inside with Mengrong.
[ZH] 千里寻点头同意：“说不定我们两个还能一起干掉几个金人。”
[EN] “What are you doing, Your Highness?” Bai Feng asked.
[ZH] 白风不好再阻拦，推开废弃屋子的门。乔梦容一脸懵懵懂懂，却也知道发生了大事，任由众人安排。
[EN] “Think I’d miss witnessing a moment of history this huge?” Shen Jianchen asked Qianli Xun. “Care to watch with me?”
[ZH] 见三人已躲好，千里寻和申简辰一起用轻功跳上街边的屋顶，他们蹲在屋顶向主街瞧去。
[EN] “Might even knock off a few Jin soldiers while we’re at it,” she grinned.
[ZH] 金军的大部队不费一兵一卒已进入主街。
[EN] As the other three slipped behind the door, Shen Jianchen and Qianli Xun vaulted cleanly onto the rooftops overlooking the main avenue.
[ZH] 管理燕京的官员和将领拿着降书来到主街跪下，手举降书，口称：“魏王在上，下官愿携上下官员与燕京将领归顺金国。”
[EN] Below, the Jin vanguard marched straight in without shedding a drop of blood. Yanjing’s Song officials and commanders dropped to their knees in the street, lifting a surrender document. “Your servant presents the officials and garrison commanders of Yanjing in submission to the Jin!”
[ZH] 坐在马背上的完颜宗望很是满意：“若我大金所到之处宋人皆如你一般知趣，岂不是天下太平？燕京本就是金人从辽国手中夺得，又被大宋的皇帝要回去，给就给了，谁让盟约在先。只可惜是我大金的国土要也要不走，今日照样还得回到金人的手中。”
[EN] Mounted on horseback, Wanyan Zongwang smiled down. “If every Song town knew its place like you, the world would know peace! We took Yanjing from the Liao and returned it to your Emperor out of treaty honor—yet land that belongs to Great Jin returns to Jin hands sooner or later.”
[ZH] 有金人跳下马从官员的手中接过降书，送至完颜宗望手中。他展开仔细瞧一遍：“态度诚恳，言辞恳切，既已是金国子民，都起来吧。”
[EN] A Jin soldier took the document up to Wanyan Zongwang, who skimmed it and bade the new subjects of Jin rise.
[ZH] 申简辰小声骂道：“一群卖国贼！”
[EN] “Pack of traitors!” Shen Jianchen hissed on the roof.
[ZH] 千里寻说：“官家昏庸，他们不投降才怪。”
[EN] “With an emperor that muddled, no shock they surrender,” Qianli Xun muttered.
[ZH] 这时，大宋官员的身后传来一阵笑声：“哈哈哈……！尔等都是一群卖国贼！我今日就是战死在此！也绝不投降！”
[EN] Just then a wild laugh rang out behind the kneeling officials: “Hahaha! Nothing but traitors, every last one of you! Better to die fighting right here than ever surrender!”

#CH 335
[ZH] “看那穿着像是一位将领，如此骨气令人钦佩。”申简辰赞叹完，担忧道，“这么多金人，只怕他是以卵击石。”
[EN] “That man in armor looks like a general. I admire his spine,” Shen Jianchen said, then worry crept into his voice. “So many Jin soldiers. He's throwing an egg against a rock.”
[ZH] “不如帮帮他？”
[EN] “Why don't we help him?”
[ZH] “自然要帮，先观察一会儿，再伺机而动。”
[EN] “Of course we will. Let's watch a moment, then move when there's an opening.”
[ZH] 两人继续蹲在屋顶观察事态发展。
[EN] The two stayed crouched on the roof, watching how things unfolded.
[ZH] 完颜宗望一脸不屑，向身后招招手：“给我拿下！”
[EN] Wanyan Zongwang's face twisted in contempt. He waved to the men behind him. “Take him down!”
[ZH] 大宋投降的官员及其他将领闪到一边。
[EN] The surrendered Song officials and the other generals stepped aside.
[ZH] 金人的士兵围上来，将有骨气的大宋将领包围。大宋将领抽出身上的宝剑，与金人打斗起来。
[EN] Jin soldiers closed in, surrounding the Song general who still had his backbone. He drew the sword at his waist and fought back.
[ZH] 金兵面目狰狞，挥舞刀枪，步步紧逼。大宋将领毫不畏惧，大喝一声，如猛虎般冲向敌人。剑影闪烁，与金人的兵器碰撞出点点火花。还好他身形矫健，左劈右砍，一时间金兵竟不能近身。
[EN] The Jin soldiers' faces were ugly with malice. They swung sabers and spears, pressing in step by step. The Song general did not flinch. He let out a roar and charged like a cornered tiger. Sword flashes met their weapons in showers of sparks. He moved lightly, slashing left and right, and for a while not one Jin soldier could get near him.
[ZH] 然而，金兵人数众多，渐渐地，大宋将领开始力不从心。一个金兵趁机从背后偷袭，长枪狠狠刺中他的后背。将领闷哼一声，嘴角溢出鲜血。但他依然屹立不倒，反手一剑将偷袭的金兵斩杀。
[EN] But there were too many of them. Bit by bit, his strength began to fail. One Jin soldier stole behind him and drove a spear hard into his back. The general grunted, blood spilling from the corner of his mouth. Still he did not fall. He whirled and cut the attacker down with a backhand stroke.
[ZH] 将领强忍着伤痛，继续奋力拼杀。每一次挥剑都带着无尽的愤怒与不屈，仿佛要将侵略者全部击退。
[EN] He swallowed the pain and kept fighting. Every swing carried his fury and his refusal to yield, as if he could drive every invader back with the blade alone.
[ZH] 金兵不断涌来，将领的处境愈发危险……
[EN] More Jin soldiers poured in. His chances grew thinner by the moment...
[ZH] 千里寻用轻功向下跳，申简辰见状，立刻跟上。
[EN] Qianli Xun sprang down from the roof with light-footed skill. Shen Jianchen saw him go and followed at once.
[ZH] 两人从天而降落在将领身边，三拳两脚帮他打退围攻的金兵，一左一右抓住大宋的将领，用轻功带着他离开被围攻之地。
[EN] The two dropped from the sky beside the general, drove off the surrounding Jin with a few punches and kicks, then caught him one on each side and carried him clear of the siege.
[ZH] 完颜宗望正要派人前去追赶，听到一个声音：“我去追！”
[EN] Wanyan Zongwang was about to send men in pursuit when a voice rang out. “I'll go!”
[ZH] 完颜池正好从另一个方向骑马赶来，瞧见打斗后有人带走大宋将领，他并未看清营救之人的样貌。完颜池的身边是陆远峰，他也骑着马，身后跟着一群金人的士兵。
[EN] Wanyan Chi rode up from another direction. He had seen someone carry the Song general away after the fight, but could not make out their faces. Lu Yuanfeng rode at his side, a band of Jin soldiers trailing behind.
[ZH] “你既已进城，叔叔就派你去捉拿反贼。”完颜宗望向身后骑在马上的将领吩咐，“跟着小王爷！抓到三人后就地正法！”
[EN] “Since you've entered the city, Uncle will send you after the rebels,” Wanyan Zongwang told the mounted general behind him. “Follow the young prince! Catch all three and execute them on the spot!”
[ZH] 众人回道：“是！”
[EN] “Yes!” the men answered.
[ZH] 千里寻和申简辰用轻功带着大宋将领飞身屋顶，迅速从屋顶跳进先前的街道，来到乔梦容等人躲避的废弃屋子。
[EN] Qianli Xun and Shen Jianchen carried the Song general onto the roofs and dropped quickly into the street they had watched from before, reaching the abandoned house where Qiao Mengrong and the others were hiding.
[ZH] 申简辰吩咐白风带梦容和喜兰从一旁的小巷子迂回着回客栈等他们。
[EN] Shen Jianchen told Bai Feng to take Mengrong and Xilan back to the inn by a side alley and wait for them there.
[ZH] 白风带着二人离开废弃的屋子，刚拐进小巷子，听到金人追来的马蹄声和脚步声。
[EN] Bai Feng led the two out of the abandoned house. They had just turned into the alley when hoofbeats and running feet came after them.
[ZH] 梦容被吓得抱住脑袋蹲下，一副惊恐的表情：“我怕，我怕，我怕……”
[EN] Mengrong crouched down, clutching her head, her face full of terror. “I'm scared, I'm scared, I'm scared...”
[ZH] 喜兰安慰道：“王妃别怕，巷子很安全，我们这就回客栈。”
[EN] “Don't be afraid, Princess. The alley is safe. We'll be at the inn in no time,” Xilan comforted her.
[ZH] 白风说：“跟我走。”
[EN] “Follow me,” Bai Feng said.
[ZH] 喜兰将仪王妃扶起，跟上白风。
[EN] Xilan helped Princess Yi to her feet and hurried after him.
[ZH] 他们顺着巷子往出走，巷子很长，也不知通向哪里。白风想打听，可巷子里除了他们三人再找不到其他人影，两边的住户也都紧闭屋门。
[EN] They made their way along the alley. It ran on and on, and there was no telling where it led. Bai Feng tried to ask, but there was no one in sight but the three of them, and every door along both sides was shut tight.
[ZH] 白风主动敲响一户人家的屋门，根本无人回应，他带着仪王妃和喜兰继续顺着巷子朝前走。
[EN] Bai Feng knocked at one house himself. No one answered. He led Princess Yi and Xilan farther down the alley.
[ZH] 走着走着，前面出现七八个金兵挡住去路。
[EN] They had gone some way when seven or eight Jin soldiers appeared ahead, blocking the path.
[ZH] 喜兰吓得抱住仪王妃。
[EN] Xilan grabbed Princess Yi in fear.
[ZH] 白风朝身后说：“有我在，别怕，你先带仪王妃躲远些。”
[EN] “I'm here. Don't be afraid. Take the Princess and hide,” Bai Feng said over his shoulder.
[ZH] 喜兰扶着仪王妃沿原路往回走，白风已与金人打斗起来。
[EN] Xilan helped Princess Yi back the way they had come while Bai Feng charged into the Jin.
[ZH] 听到打打杀杀的声音，乔梦容更加害怕，抱头蹲在地上，口中念叨着：“哥哥救我，哥哥救我……”
[EN] The sounds of fighting made Qiao Mengrong more frightened still. She crouched with her head in her hands, mumbling, “Brother, save me. Brother, save me...”
[ZH] 仪王妃蹲着不走，喜兰俯身劝道：“王妃，我们快些离开，否则会没命。”
[EN] Princess Yi stayed crouched and refused to move. Xilan bent over her. “Princess, we have to go. If we stay, we'll be killed.”
[ZH] 乔梦容使劲儿摇头：“不，不，我要等哥哥来救我。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong shook her head hard. “No. No. I'll wait for my brother to save me.”
[ZH] 喜兰心想，往后退还有金人，逃也逃不走，不如等白风把那些金人打退，好从巷子出去。于是陪仪王妃蹲下，观察白风与金人打斗的景况。
[EN] Xilan thought: behind them were Jin soldiers too, so running was no use. Better to wait until Bai Feng drove them off, then slip out of the alley. She crouched down with Princess Yi and watched the fight.
[ZH] 白风的身手不赖，七八个金人对付起来不成问题。见此情景，喜兰轻松许多，用期待的目光盯着白风与金人对打，祈祷他尽快取胜。
[EN] Bai Feng handled himself well. Seven or eight men were nothing he couldn't manage. Seeing that, Xilan's fear eased. She watched him with hope in her eyes, praying he would win quickly.
[ZH] 白风拳脚厉害，七八个金人不是他的对手，可也无法在几招之内解决，仍需花费时间和精力应付一番。
[EN] Bai Feng's fists and feet were fierce. The seven or eight Jin were no match for him, yet he could not finish them in a few moves. It still took time and effort.
[ZH] 经过几番搏斗，白风赤手空拳打倒几人，又夺走对方手中的刀剑，如虎添翼，向剩下几人轮番砍去，直指要害，金人全部倒在巷子内。
[EN] After several exchanges, Bai Feng knocked a few down bare-handed, then tore the sabers and swords from their hands. With steel in his grip he struck at the rest, aiming at vital spots, until every one of them lay in the alley.
[ZH] 白风扔了刀剑，向喜兰说：“扶好王妃，快些跟我走。”
[EN] Bai Feng dropped the weapons. “Hold the Princess steady. Come with me, quickly,” he told Xilan.
[ZH] 喜兰说：“王妃，听话，快跟我走。”
[EN] “Princess, listen to me. Come quickly,” Xilan urged.
[ZH] 金人被打死，乔梦容不再害怕，乖乖地站起来。两人跟上白风，继续在巷子里穿梭。
[EN] With the Jin dead, Qiao Mengrong was no longer afraid. She got to her feet obediently. The two followed Bai Feng through the alley.
[ZH] 七拐八拐，终于看到巷口，白风还没来得及高兴，只觉光线明亮的巷口变得暗淡许多，更多金兵拿刀剑慢慢向三人靠近。
[EN] They twisted and turned until the mouth of the alley came in sight. Before Bai Feng could feel relief, the bright opening darkened. More Jin soldiers, blades in hand, were closing in on the three of them.
[ZH] 白风吓出一身冷汗，他一个人根本对付不了这么多人，向身后的喜兰急切地说：“我先顶着！你带王妃快跑！”
[EN] Cold sweat broke out over Bai Feng. He could not hold off so many alone. “I'll stop them! Take the Princess and run!” he said urgently to Xilan.
[ZH] 已容不得喜兰继续害怕，拉起仪王妃就跑。
[EN] There was no time left for Xilan to be afraid. She pulled Princess Yi up and ran.
[ZH] 跑出几步，乔梦容站住，继续蹲下，蜷缩着：“我要等哥哥救我，等哥哥救我……”
[EN] After a few steps, Qiao Mengrong stopped and crouched again, curling into a ball. “I'll wait for my brother to save me. I'll wait for my brother...”
[ZH] 喜兰哄骗道：“王妃的哥哥就在前面，与其等他来，不如我们去找他？”
[EN] “The Princess's brother is just ahead,” Xilan coaxed. “Instead of waiting for him, why don't we go find him?”
[ZH] 乔梦容信以为真，站起来与喜兰继续跑。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong believed her and stood, running on with Xilan.
[ZH] 金兵人多，以白风一人之力，根本阻挡不了，在他打斗之际，已有许多人向喜兰和乔梦容追赶而去。
[EN] There were too many Jin. Bai Feng could not hold them all. Even as he fought, many broke past him and chased after Xilan and Qiao Mengrong.
[ZH] 白风想要转身去阻止，一分神，被金军一刀砍在背部，又一脚将他踢倒在地，许多金兵进入巷子，有的甚至从白风的身上踩过。
[EN] Bai Feng tried to turn back, but the moment his attention wavered a saber cut into his back and a kick sent him sprawling. Jin soldiers poured into the alley, some even stepping over his body.
[ZH] 待金兵走完后，白风努力爬起来站稳，启动轻功，落在金人的面前，因身上有伤，加上疲于应对，差点儿没站稳。他忍着痛稳住下盘：“要想……往前走……需过我这一关。”
[EN] When they had passed, Bai Feng struggled up and steadied himself. He leapt ahead with light-footed skill and landed in front of the Jin. His wounds and his exhausted state nearly made him stumble. He swallowed the pain and planted his feet. “If you want to go forward... you go through me first.”
[ZH] 金兵根本没将白风放在眼里，几人拿起刀剑向白风砍去。白风将其中一人一拳打倒，夺走对方手中的剑，向其他人杀将而去。
[EN] The Jin barely spared him a glance. Several raised their blades and cut at him. Bai Feng knocked one down with a punch, took his sword, and went after the others.
[ZH] 怎奈敌不过众人之力，身上又被砍了几刀，白风再也撑不住，倒在地上，眼睁睁看着金人向仪王妃和喜兰追去。
[EN] But he could not beat them all. Two more cuts landed. His strength gave out and he fell, forced to watch as the Jin chased Princess Yi and Xilan.
[ZH] 乔梦容和喜兰是女子，平时又缺乏锻炼，跑得并不快，很快被金兵追上。一个金兵一刀刺穿喜兰的后背，她连喊疼的机会都没有，倒在地上，只说出四个字：“王妃……快跑……”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong and Xilan were women and unused to running. They were soon caught. One Jin thrust a saber through Xilan's back. She had no time even to cry out. She fell and got out four words. “Princess... run...”
[ZH] 乔梦容被吓坏，蹲下摸着喜兰的脸：“你怎么了？你怎么了？”
[EN] Mengrong crouched beside her, stroking her face. “What's wrong? What's wrong?”
[ZH] 喜兰毫无反应，乔梦容抱住脑袋缩成一团：“哥哥救我！哥哥救我！”
[EN] Xilan did not answer. Mengrong hugged her knees and curled up tight. “Brother, save me! Brother, save me!”
[ZH] 只听一人金兵说：“小妞长得不赖，不如先奸后杀。”
[EN] One of the Jin soldiers said, “Not a bad looking girl. Let's have her before we kill her.”
[ZH] 其他人都露出淫笑：“呵呵呵……！”
[EN] The others grinned. “Heh, heh, heh...”
[ZH] “我要去找哥哥，我要去找哥哥……”乔梦容念叨着站起来就要跑。
[EN] “I want my brother. I want my brother...” Qiao Mengrong mumbled, getting to her feet to run.
[ZH] 金兵已挡在她的面前：“往哪里逃？”
[EN] A Jin soldier stepped in front of her. “Where do you think you're going?”
[ZH] 乔梦容闪到一边，结果正好撞在一个金兵的身上，对方趁机抓住她：“想逃没那么容易！”
[EN] She darted to one side and ran straight into another soldier, who grabbed her. “You won't get away that easy!”
[ZH] 乔梦容使劲推抓住自己的金兵，怎奈她力气小，根本没用。她用脚踢向对方的裤裆，金兵没忍住，松了手，直喊疼，乔梦容趁机逃离。
[EN] She pushed at him with all her might, but she was too weak. She kicked him in the groin. He let go with a yelp of pain, and she broke free.
[ZH] 金兵根本没将她放在眼里，看着她仓皇逃走的样子都大笑不止：“哈哈哈……！”
[EN] The Jin laughed at her scrambling escape. “Ha, ha, ha...!”
[ZH] 乔梦容根本无法逃脱，又有人挡住她的去路：“美人，往哪里逃？”
[EN] She could not get away. Another soldier blocked her. “Where to, beauty?”
[ZH] 乔梦容无路可逃，惊恐地盯着对方：“我哥哥会杀了你。”
[EN] With nowhere left to run, she stared at him in terror. “My brother will kill you.”
[ZH] 挡住她的金兵说：“你哥哥？哈哈哈……！来了我也不怕，你乖乖地陪我们玩玩儿，若玩儿高兴了，说不定我们不杀你，饶了你的性命。”
[EN] “Your brother?” the soldier laughed. “Ha, ha, ha! I'm not afraid of him. Be a good girl and play with us. If we enjoy ourselves, maybe we'll spare your life.”
[ZH] 听到“杀”字，乔梦容十分害怕，蹲下，抱住头浑身颤抖：“别过来，别过来，别过来……”
[EN] At the word “kill,” Qiao Mengrong was terrified. She crouched, clutched her head, and shook all over. “Don't come near. Don't come near. Don't come near...”
[ZH] 那金兵笑着上前将乔梦容扛起，在其他人的嬉笑声中踢开一户人家的门，这户人家显然人已搬走，屋里只剩下简单的家具和一盏油灯。
[EN] The soldier laughed, picked her up, and kicked open the door of a house amid the others' jeering. The place had clearly been abandoned. Only a few plain pieces of furniture and an oil lamp remained.
[ZH] 乔梦容一边拍打着金兵，一边大叫：“放开我！放开我！”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong beat at him and screamed. “Let me go! Let me go!”
[ZH] 那金兵进屋将她扔在木榻上，满脸淫笑就要扑上去。又进来一人，将那金兵拉住：“怎能只顾你开心？也得让我品尝一下美人的好。”
[EN] He carried her inside and threw her onto a wooden couch, a filthy grin spreading over his face as he moved to pounce. Another man came in and held him back. “Why should you have all the fun? Let me taste the beauty too.”
[ZH] 第一个金兵说：“需有个先来后到。”
[EN] “There's an order to this,” the first soldier said.
[ZH] 第二个说：“我先来，你待会儿。”
[EN] “I'll go first. You can wait,” said the second.
[ZH] 第一个金兵十分不满：“凭什么？是我把她扛进来的。”
[EN] “Why should you?” the first demanded. “I carried her in here.”
[ZH] 第二个说：“凭我级别比你高。”
[EN] “Because I outrank you,” said the second.
[ZH] 第一个金兵一拳打去，两人互相打起来。
[EN] The first threw a punch. The two began to fight.
[ZH] 第一个打不过第二个，装作认输的样子：“不打了，不打了，既然你级别比我高，你先来。”
[EN] The first was losing. He pretended to give in. “All right, all right. You outrank me, so you go first.”
[ZH] “算你识相！”第二金兵松了手，向乔梦容走去，一脸淫笑。
[EN] “Smart choice.” The second let go and walked toward Qiao Mengrong, leering.
[ZH] 乔梦容害怕极了，在木榻上向后退缩。
[EN] She was too frightened to move, shrinking back on the wooden couch.
[ZH] 第二个金兵还未靠近时，却倒在她面前。原来是第一个金兵偷偷在身后用剑将他刺死。
[EN] Before he could reach her, he fell flat at her feet. The first soldier had crept up behind him and run him through with a sword.
[ZH] 有人死在面前，乔梦容更害怕，她惊恐地盯着金兵手中沾有血迹的宝剑，脑中闪过被梁师成用针刺向她身上的情景，悔恨和屈辱使得她大哭起来：“呜呜呜……！”
[EN] A man dead at her feet made her more afraid still. She stared at the bloody blade in his hand. The memory of Liang Shicheng's needle striking her flashed through her mind. Shame and regret broke her into loud sobs. “Wuwu...”
[ZH] “别害怕，我不会杀你。”金兵哄了两句，将剑放在一旁的桌上，“等我把他的尸体挪到一边，再与你快活。”
[EN] “Don't be scared. I won't kill you,” the soldier soothed her, laying the sword on a nearby table. “Once I've moved his body, we'll have our fun.”
[ZH] 乔梦容并不去理会金兵搬尸体的过程，她一边哭，一边盯着带有血迹的剑，淑真大婚那日，哥哥抢亲时，她冲向利刃的一刻在脑中闪过……
[EN] She paid no attention to him dragging the corpse. She wept, her eyes fixed on the bloody blade. The day of Shuzhen's wedding, when her brother had stormed in to take her back and she had rushed toward the sharp steel—it all came back to her...
[ZH] 乔梦容不再哭泣，走下木榻，走向利刃，拿起它：“哥哥，我想你……”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong stopped crying. She stepped down from the couch, walked to the blade, and picked it up. “Brother, I miss you...”
[ZH] 她用剑刺向自己的腹部，脸上露出笑容：“哥哥，梦容来找你……”
[EN] She drove the sword into her own belly, a smile on her face. “Brother, Mengrong has come for you...”
[ZH] 金兵将尸体搬到一边，一转身发现美人倒在地上，剑已刺破腹部，流出许多鲜血，上前将手指放在鼻息间，已无气息，不满道：“真晦气！老子还没玩儿，人就死了！”
[EN] The soldier moved the body aside and turned to find the beauty on the floor, the sword buried in her belly and blood spreading out. He knelt, put fingers to her nose, and felt no breath. “What rotten luck! I hadn't even had my fun and she's dead!”
[ZH] 金人拔出插在乔梦容身上的剑，用她身上的衣裳将血迹擦干，嘟囔着离开。
[EN] He pulled the blade from her body, wiped it clean on her clothes, and walked off muttering.
[ZH] 白风身上的伤不少，还好都不在要害处，他在地上躺了一会儿，缓得差不多时，爬起来。
[EN] Bai Feng had taken many cuts, but none in a vital spot. He lay on the ground a while, and when he had recovered enough, he got up.
[ZH] 周围已没有金兵，他踉跄着往回走，也不知走了多久，发现喜兰的尸体。白风一阵紧张，快走几步，结果倒在地上。他爬到喜兰跟前，晃着她：“喜兰！你醒醒！醒醒啊！”
[EN] There were no Jin left nearby. He staggered back. He had no idea how long he walked before he found Xilan's body. His heart clenched. He hurried forward and fell to the ground. He crawled to her and shook her. “Xilan! Wake up! Wake up!”
[ZH] 喜兰毫无反应，白风差点儿哭出来：“仪王妃？仪王妃在哪里？”
[EN] Xilan did not move. Bai Feng was close to tears. “Princess Yi? Where is Princess Yi?”
[ZH] 白风向四周瞧，并未看到仪王妃的身影。他再次努力爬起来，继续踉跄着向前寻找。见有一户人家门大开着，正屋的地上躺着一女子，身上衣裳的颜色与仪王妃的很像。
[EN] He looked all around. There was no sign of her. He forced himself up again and staggered on, searching. Then he saw a house with its door wide open, and on the floor of the main room lay a woman in garments much like the Princess's.
[ZH] 白风担忧不已，想立刻冲进去看个究竟。因身上有伤，再着急，也需一步一步走进。
[EN] His fear grew. He wanted to rush straight in, but his wounds slowed him even in his haste. Step by step he went.
[ZH] 当他进入后，看清倒在地上的女子正是仪王妃时，他再也站不住，倒在地上，哭道：“呜呜，仪王妃遭遇不测，我可怎么向王爷交代？”
[EN] When he saw the woman on the floor was Princess Yi, his legs gave way. He fell and wept. “Wuwu... If something has happened to the Princess, how can I face the Prince?”
[ZH] 话说白风带着乔梦容和喜兰进入巷子时，完颜池骑在马上带领金人的将领和士兵而来。他边走边观察，对众人说：“据本王推测，他们走不远，肯定躲在附近，给本王把这条街守牢了，让他们插翅难飞！”
[EN] While Bai Feng led Qiao Mengrong and Xilan into the alley, Wanyan Chi came riding at the head of Jin officers and soldiers. He scanned the street as he went. “As I see it, they can't have gone far. They must be hiding nearby. Seal this street tight, all of you. Don't let them grow wings and fly away!”
[ZH] 金人的将领分别带士兵将整条街围得水泄不通，也将街道上的巷子包围。
[EN] Jin officers led their men around the whole street until it was hemmed in, and they ringed the alleys off as well.
[ZH] 完颜池并未下马，抬头瞧向街道两旁的屋顶：“逃走的人会轻功，有可能走屋顶，都警惕些！”
[EN] Wanyan Chi did not dismount. He looked up at the roofs on either side. “The ones who got away know light-footed skill. They may have taken the rooftops. Keep your eyes open!”
[ZH] 完颜池跳下马，陆远峰也跟着跳下，陪小王爷巡视。
[EN] Wanyan Chi swung down from his horse. Lu Yuanfeng dismounted too and went with the young prince on his inspection.
[ZH] 来到废弃的屋子外，完颜池停下：“随我进去瞧瞧。”
[EN] Outside the abandoned house, Wanyan Chi stopped. “Come in with me.”
[ZH] 千里寻和申简辰还有那位被救的大宋将领没来得及离开时，金人已到，他们先在屋内躲好。
[EN] Qianli Xun, Shen Jianchen, and the rescued Song general had not had time to leave. The Jin arrived, and they hid inside.
[ZH] 大宋将领忍着背部的伤痛感激道：“感谢英雄和女侠救我，你们还是走吧，不要因为我受到牵连。”
[EN] The Song general swallowed the pain in his back. “Thank you, heroes, for saving me. You should go. Don't get dragged into this because of me.”
[ZH] 申简辰说：“我们二人既然敢出面救你，就不怕被牵连。”
[EN] “If we dared step in to save you, we weren't afraid of being dragged in,” Shen Jianchen said.
[ZH] 千里寻说：“像你这等敢于怒怼金人的英雄不多，既然被我二人碰到，无论有多困难我们都会救。”
[EN] “There aren't many men with the courage to talk back to the Jin as you did,” Qianli Xun said. “Since we happened on you, we'll see you through, however hard it gets.”
[ZH] 屋子的门被推开，发出响声，三人立刻警惕起来。
[EN] The door was pushed open with a creak. The three went on alert at once.
[ZH] 申简辰小声说：“走后门，再用轻功翻墙出去。”
[EN] “Out the back door, then over the wall,” Shen Jianchen whispered.
[ZH] 千里寻点头认可，三人迅速从后门逃出，两人分别抓住将领的手臂用轻功跳出后院，当三人落在后院的巷子时，已被十几个金军包围。
[EN] Qianli Xun nodded. The three slipped out the back. Each of the two took an arm of the general and leapt over the rear wall. They landed in the alley behind the house—and found themselves surrounded by a dozen Jin soldiers.
[ZH] 两人与十来个金人打斗起来，大宋将领身负重伤也不闲着，区区十来个人对付起来倒也轻松。
[EN] The two fought the dozen. The Song general, badly wounded as he was, did not idle either. A mere ten-odd men were nothing they could not handle.
[ZH] 完颜池进入院落时，听到有人从后院出去的声音，立刻带着陆远峰转身出了屋子，来到街上，迅速从他骑的马上取下弓箭，飞快地朝后面拐去。
[EN] As Wanyan Chi entered the courtyard, he heard movement from the back. He turned at once with Lu Yuanfeng, left the house, and went into the street. He snatched a bow and arrows from his horse and raced around to the rear.
[ZH] 陆远峰及其他将领都快速跟上。
[EN] Lu Yuanfeng and the other officers hurried after him.
[ZH] 三人将十几个金兵打趴下，接着听到急促而来的脚步声，从声音判断，逼近的人不少。
[EN] The three had beaten the dozen Jin to the ground when they heard quick footsteps closing in. From the sound, many men were coming.
[ZH] 千里寻与申简辰互相对视，两人点点头，抓起大宋将领，启动轻功。当他们飞身而上快要落到屋顶时，一支箭“嗖”的一声飞来，声音消失后，大宋将领“啊”的一声，向下沉去……
[EN] Qianli Xun and Shen Jianchen exchanged a glance and nodded. They grabbed the Song general and sprang up. They were nearly at the roof when an arrow whistled—and with the sound gone, the general cried out and sank downward...

#CH 336
[ZH] 两人不忍撒手，跟着向下落去……
[ZH] 一到地面，大宋将领倒在地上，已无生命体征。
[ZH] 眼下的形势容不得二人悲叹。
[ZH] 金军逼近，人数众多，从飞来的箭判断射箭之人不仅速度快，而且又狠又准，若再次启动轻功，恐怕到半空对方还会冷不丁拉弓射箭，到时他们其中一人必然会像那位将领一样倒下。
[ZH] 千里寻说：“看来今日逃不走，你我只能正面迎敌。”
[ZH] 申简辰问：“怕不怕？”
[ZH] 千里寻冷笑道：“老娘从小到大还没怕过。”
[ZH] 申简辰说：“你不怕，我也不怕。”
[ZH] 二人以勇敢的姿态面向正在逼近的金军，当金军走近停下脚步时，传来一个声音。
[ZH] “师傅？怎么是你？”
[ZH] 金军中竟然有人称呼她师傅？声音好像在哪里听到过？千里寻纳闷之余，朝那声音瞧去，她的嘴角浮现一丝冷笑。
[ZH] 原来是曾经跟自己对决的高手，被打败后死皮赖脸认她当师傅，叫什么来着？对，陆远峰，一副金人的装扮，难怪没一眼认出。
[ZH] 千里寻记得两年前，麻烦他救了位名唤“延迟”的人。想到“延迟”两个字，总觉得陆远峰身旁的人有些眼熟。
[ZH] 她将目光移向那人，果然是“延迟”，他身着金军的铠甲，手中握着弓箭，看来射杀大宋将领的人正是他。他的身后跟着的人应该是金国的将领，由此判断此人在金国地位不低。早知他是金人，当初就不该救他。
[ZH] 燕京偶遇曾动过心思的美人，完颜池紧绷的神经松弛下来，将弓箭递给一旁的将领，以高傲的姿态瞧向美人，他倒要瞧瞧此等境遇，美人会作何反应。
[ZH] 申简辰从未见过完颜池和陆远峰，他们看到千里寻时的表现，令他既疑惑又担忧。
[ZH] 千里寻大声说：“陆远峰！你既唤我一声师傅，就该听我的，师傅现在命令你不要跟金人同流合污，到我这边来！”
[ZH] 听到“陆远峰”这个名字，申简辰想起在临安城的迷魂巷寻找金人之事。
[ZH] 当时引路之人说他的朋友名叫陆远峰，家里来了一位亲戚五官长得不像汉人。等找到陆远峰的家时，门已上锁，原来陆远峰跟着金人远走他乡。
[ZH] 申简辰从邵正邺口中得知金人名唤完颜池，是金国现任皇帝完颜吴乞买与邵月婷所生。难道眼前这位高高在上，一直盯着千里寻看的人正是完颜池？
[ZH] 陆远峰为难之余坚定地说：“我心甘情愿认你当师傅，也心甘情愿跟着小王爷。既然在燕京碰上，师傅不如一起跟随小王爷？”
[ZH] 申简辰心想：“看来此人的确是邵正邺口中提到的完颜池，他的拳法与申拳如出一辙，不如今日会会他，探探拳法的虚实。”
[ZH] 原来“延迟”是小王爷，身份地位确实不低。面对陆远峰的说辞，千里寻十分不屑，大骂道：“你当叛徒，还想让老娘跟你一样，永远不可能！老娘从未教过你一招一式，只因你死皮赖脸认我做师傅，老娘不得已才答应。今日当着金人的面断绝与你的师徒关系，从今往后你我毫不相干！”
[ZH] 陆远峰十分赖皮：“师傅想怎么说随你，反正在我心里你永远是我师傅。”
[ZH] 完颜池开口道：“你我再次偶遇，还是在燕京，你说我们是不是很有缘分？”
[ZH] 千里寻冷笑一声，并未回答对方。
[ZH] 申简辰担忧地问：“你认识他？”
[ZH] “不过是在临安街头偶遇过一次，还是我的手下败将。那时不知他是金人，若知道又岂会放过？”
[ZH] “只是如此？”
[ZH] “还能是什么？难道你担心老娘会喜欢一个金人？”
[ZH] “我担心他。”
[ZH] 完颜池见美人不回答问题，反而与身边器宇轩昂的男子说悄悄话，他们看上去关系不一般，质问道：“你身边那人是谁？”
[ZH] 千里寻回道：“老娘没有义务告诉你！”
[ZH] “哦，我想起来，你已成亲，难道他是你的夫君？”
[ZH] “老娘为何要告诉你？”
[ZH] 完颜池并不生气：“你嫁人也没关系，今日就是你与夫君的婚姻关系结束之日。”
[ZH] 申简辰趁机说：“只要我在一日，你别想得逞！”
[ZH] “嘴倒挺硬，还是让你尝尝我的拳头。都不许动！本王要亲自收拾他！”
[ZH] 完颜池率先发难，身形如猛虎下山，一拳直击申简辰的面门。这一拳带着呼啸的风声，仿佛能撕裂空气，正是申拳中的“风云变”。
[ZH] 申简辰心想：“果然使出申拳的看家本领，”
[ZH] 拳头快要靠近时，申简辰不慌不忙，脚步轻盈一挪，竟如游鱼般滑出攻击范围，反手一招“龙腾四海”拳风如龙，直取完颜池的腰间要害。
[ZH] 完颜池用一招“地龙翻身”迅速躲过，心中不免大惊：“他怎么会我学的拳法？师傅独创此拳法，只传授于我，并无第二人，而且他的拳法比我更加纯熟，甚至比师傅还要厉害。”
[ZH] 完颜池继续攻击而去，他的拳法刚中带柔；而申简辰的拳法则柔中带刚，如绵绵细雨连绵不绝，又似惊雷般突然爆发。二人你来我往，拳影交错，申拳的精髓在他们身上展现得淋漓尽致。
[ZH] 千里寻一直观察两人的招式，自语道：“我向来瞧不起申拳，今日你们用申拳对决，才看出精妙之处，的确有些招式千里拳自愧不如。”
[ZH] 两人打得甚是激烈，生死存亡之际，申简辰表现出冷静与智慧，他将身形骤然下沉，仿佛融入大地之中，凝聚全身的力量，身形暴起，使出一拳，以不可思议的角度和力度冲向完颜池……
[ZH] 完颜池已来不及躲避，一拳下来他不死也伤。
[ZH] 靠近完颜池的一刻，申简辰手下留情，他还没搞清楚他会的申拳来自何人，完颜池还不能死。
[ZH] 对方的拳头快要到达太阳穴时突然停下，完颜池庆幸命是保住，不由脱口而出：“你怎么会这套拳法？最后一招‘天地同寿’我学了很久都没学会，可你却会？你到底是谁？”
[ZH] 申简辰心想，完颜池的拳法也许是申拳的老祖宗。可他竟然对千里寻起歹心，让他既担心又瞧不起，干脆压他一头。
[ZH] “我的拳法叫申拳，既然将你打败，应该是你那拳法的老祖宗，作为长辈不好与晚辈计较，今日暂且放过你。”
[ZH] 申简辰收起拳头，露出胜利的笑容走向千里寻。
[ZH] 完颜池站稳后，看着比自己年纪小几岁的年轻人竟然口称他的拳法是老祖宗，心中气愤。此气不出，何以为人？
[ZH] “众人听令！女的交给我对付！其他人全部围攻那男的！直到他精疲力尽为止！”
[ZH] 还未走到千里寻身边，一群金人围攻而来，申简辰收起胜利的笑容开始应战。
[ZH] 完颜池见美人打算上去帮忙，一挥手，另一群金人将美人包围，陆远峰也在其中。
[ZH] 完颜池走入包围圈中，半戏谑半认真地说：“燕京府已归金国，叔父让我留下负责管理，他则要从河北出发直取汴梁，大宋的天下快要保不住，你跟那小子的婚姻又岂能保住？不如跟我待在燕京？你想要什么，本王都给你，哪怕要天上的星星，本王也会想办法给你摘下来。”
[ZH] 千里寻见过不要脸的，这么不要脸的还是头一回见，不如逗逗他。她装作信以为真的样子：“你说的可是真话？我要什么都给？”
[ZH] 完颜池以为美人心动，露出真诚的表情：“临安一眼，终生难忘。本王身边的女人虽不少，还是头一次遇见一女子怦然心动，感觉实在美妙，自然挂心你，对你与众不同。只可惜本王事务繁忙，金国又离临安路途遥远，才没有再去打扰你。本想着等我金国夺得天下，一切安定下来，再去临安找你，没想到你竟主动来到燕京，又与本王相遇，你说我们是不是天作之合？”
[ZH] 千里寻并不理会那么多表达情感的话语，只想戏弄他：“既如此，老娘想要你的命！还想让金人滚出大宋的地界！你可给得？”
[ZH] 完颜池被噎住，后悔为了得到美人心，稍不理智将话说满，搞得自己下来台，不由恼羞成怒：“别敬酒不吃吃罚酒！都给我上！”
[ZH] 金人一起围攻而来，完颜池则退出圈外。
[ZH] 千里寻使出拳法与众人打斗起来。
[ZH] 陆远峰也退出圈外，来到完颜池身边：“小王爷，她毕竟是我师傅，我不能打她，还请您不要介意。”
[ZH] “你师傅不好搞定，她有没有什么软肋？”
[ZH] “我唤她一声师傅是因为她拳法比我好，其他一概不知。”
[ZH] 完颜池懒得再理会陆远峰，站在一旁欣赏手下的将士一波一波围攻美人，如此打下去不信她没有疲惫的时候，等她打累了，再将她拿下。
[EN] Unwilling to let go, both dropped back to the ground beside him, where the Song general lay motionless without breath.
[ZH] ……
[EN] No time to mourn. Hundreds of Jin troops closed in, and whoever loosed that arrow shot fast, lethal, and true—one more leap skyward would pick either of them off mid-air.
[ZH] 深秋时节白天开始变短，夜色不知不觉来临。金人一拨打累又换一拨，千里寻和申简辰却没有任何喘息的机会，他们身上的力气随着天色变暗，像是渐渐从身上剥离一般，两人变得力不从心。
[EN] “Looks like no running today,” Qianli Xun said. “Head-on it is.”
[ZH] 申简辰觉得自己的身体变得越来越沉，拳头很难再使出，他快要倒在地上，用最后残存的意志硬撑着。他喘着粗气，微抬眼眸，无力地瞥向不知已多少次后又围过来的金兵。
[EN] “Scared?” Shen Jianchen asked.
[ZH] 千里寻的状态与申简辰差不多，可她依然毫不示弱，硬撑着对围攻而来的金兵说：“老娘……今日……即使死……也值，起码……我……打败不少……金人，呵呵，也打死……不少……”
[EN] “Never knew the word growing up.”
[ZH] 完颜池穿过金兵，走到美人身边：“都这样还嘴硬？你要是懂得向我示弱，我会很开心。”
[EN] “If you’re not scared, neither am I.”
[ZH] “呸……”
[EN] Standing shoulder to shoulder, they braced as Jin ranks parted—and a familiar voice rang out in disbelief.
[ZH] 千里寻差点倒下，完颜池一把扶住，趁机点了她的晕穴，美人倒在他的怀里。
[EN] “Master?! Is that you?!”
[ZH] “现在的样子看上去才乖巧。”完颜池面带微笑，抬头向陆远峰吩咐，“人交给你，把她送到王府，好生安顿。”
[EN] Out stepped Lu Yuanfeng in Jin armor, grinning sheepishly as Qianli Xun recognized the stubborn fighter she had beaten years ago. Right beside him rode “Yan Chi” in full Jin battle mail, holding the bow—the very man she had rescued two years earlier, clearly no ordinary traveler, rather a highborn Jin royal!
[ZH] 陆远峰过去从小王爷的怀里接过师傅。
[EN] Relaxing at the sight of the beauty he had never forgotten, Wanyan Chi handed his bow to an officer and looked down coolly to see how she would handle this trap.
[ZH] 完颜池说的话申简辰一字不落听进耳中，见千里寻要被人带走，心中焦急，怎奈双腿不听使唤，人倒在地上。他稍微爬起，使出力气，大声说：“不准……带走……她！”
[EN] “Lu Yuanfeng!” Qianli Xun snapped. “If you still call me Master, obey me! Leave these Jin dogs and step over here!”
[ZH] 完颜池走到跟前，在他身上踢一脚：“就你现在这样，还想跟我抢人？省省吧！都给我上！今日让他死在乱剑之下！”
[EN] That name triggered Shen Jianchen’s memory at once—Confusing Soul Alley in Lin’an, and Shao Zhengye’s secret about Wanyan Chi, son of the Jin Emperor Wanyan Wuqimai and Shao Yueting.
[ZH] 金人手举利剑向他刺来……
[EN] Torn between loyalty and pride, Lu Yuanfeng begged her to join the young prince instead. Disgusted, Qianli Xun disowned him on the spot, snapping she had never taught a traitor a single move and wanted nothing more to do with him.
[ZH] 申简辰没有心力理会靠近他的许多利剑，用尽仅有的力气抬头盯着陆远峰将千里寻扶进一辆刚刚驶入的马车里。他在心中说：“我这么努力，还是无法得到你，今日我要命丧在此，希望我死后，你能在心里念着我。”
[EN] Unruffled, Wanyan Chi smiled down. “Meeting again in Yanjing—wouldn’t you call that fate?”
[ZH] 申简辰闭上双眼，准备享受即将到来的疼痛和死亡……
[EN] “You know him?” Shen Jianchen asked low.
[ZH] 话说白风伤心过后，不忍仪王妃的尸身躺在冰冷的地上，他爬起来，将仪王妃的尸身努力抱起。因用力过猛，背后的伤口流出许多血来。
[EN] “Met him once on a Lin’an street—beat him flat too. Had no idea he was Jin, or I’d never have let him go.”
[ZH] 白风顾不上伤口疼痛，迈着艰难的步伐靠近床榻。他第一次觉得近在咫尺的距离像是隔了千山万水。
[EN] “Nothing else?”
[ZH] 将仪王妃的尸身在床榻上放好后，他倒在榻边。背上流出太多血，他感到头有些晕，休息片刻应该会好些。
[EN] “What else? Scared I’d fall for a Jin?”
[ZH] 白风边休息边哭：“呜呜……，王爷，你在哪里？我没帮你看好仪王妃，她好可怜，呜呜……”
[EN] “I’m worried about *him*.”
[ZH] 哭了一会儿，白风想起喜兰：“还有喜兰，她还躺在巷子里，我也需将她抱进来，呜呜，陪仪王妃，呜呜……”
[EN] Annoyed watching the beauty whisper with a tall, striking stranger, Wanyan Chi demanded who he was—and grew sharper guessing he might be her husband. “No matter if you married,” Wanyan Chi declared. “Today ends your marriage. Nobody move! I’ll deal with this fellow myself!”
[ZH] 白风又哭一会儿，感觉头不晕时，站起来，踉跄着走出去，来到喜兰的尸身旁，把她抱起。
[EN] Like a tiger bounding downhill, Wanyan Chi lunged at Shen Jianchen’s face with Shen Fist’s signature blow, *Shifting Winds and Clouds*. Sliding clear like a darting fish, Shen Jianchen countered with *Dragon Soaring Across the Four Seas* straight at his ribs. Wanyan Chi flipped away with *Earth Dragon Turning Over*, stunned that this stranger wielded his master’s secret fist art smoother and sharper than he did himself!
[ZH] 背上的伤口又涌出一股血来，白风觉得眼前变黑。他稳了会儿，睁开眼时，又是一片明亮。
[EN] Soft-within-hard clashed against hard-within-soft, fist shadows blurring like wind and thunder while Qianli Xun murmured in surprise that Shen Fist actually held moves even Qianli Fist had to respect.
[ZH] 他迈开步伐，一步一步向前走。人好着时，迅速走完的路程对于现在身负重伤的他来讲十分艰难。
[EN] At the height of the duel, Shen Jianchen sank low as if rooted into stone, coiled every ounce of force, and exploded upward in a devastating arc toward Wanyan Chi’s temple—freezing his knuckles a hair’s breadth from bone.
[ZH] 他边走边回想喜兰说过的话，尤其是问他，若是没有苦荞，你会不会喜欢上我之类的话。要是能预知今日之事，他绝对会说些违心的话哄喜兰开心。
[EN] Spared at death’s door, Wanyan Chi gasped. “How do you know *Shared Span of Heaven and Earth*? I trained for months and never mastered that last move! Who are you?!”
[ZH] 不知走了多久，白风才把喜兰放在仪王妃的身边，这时秋日的太阳已偏西。
[EN] Wanting answers about Shen Fist’s origin, Shen Jianchen smirked coldly. “My art is called Shen Fist. Since I beat you flat, that makes it the grand ancestor of whatever you practice. An elder shouldn’t bully a junior—consider yourself spared today.”
[ZH] 他又倒在榻边休息了一会儿，无意中瞧见靠墙的地方躺着一个金人将领的尸体。
[EN] Walking back to Qianli Xun with a victorious grin, he left Wanyan Chi trembling with humiliation in front of his officers. Nobody could call him a junior and walk away!
[ZH] 白风带着碰运气的心态爬到尸体跟前，在他怀里摸摸，果然摸出两瓶疗伤的药。白风仔细分辨后，将其中一个瓶子打开，取出一丸药服下。
[EN] “All troops, listen up!” Wanyan Chi roared. “Leave the woman to me! Surround that man and wear him down till he drops!”
[ZH] 接着把上衣脱下，露出背部，将另一瓶药粉往背上倒些。他根本看不到伤口，仅凭蛰痛的感觉判断药粉应该已落在伤口处。接着，又给其他伤口上好药。
[EN] As soldiers swarmed Shen Jianchen, another ring sealed off Qianli Xun, Lu Yuanfeng standing inside. Stepping into her circle half-joking, half-earnest, Wanyan Chi boasted his uncle was marching straight through Hebei for Bianliang while leaving him to govern Yanjing. The Song stood on the brink of ruin—stay with him, and he would pluck the stars from the sky for her, confessing one glance in Lin’an had haunted him ever since.
[ZH] 金人疗伤的药效果不错，两刻钟后，白风感觉到伤口的疼痛感减弱，因服下那丸药脑袋也不再沉闷。
[EN] Playing along with an innocent blink, she asked if he would give her *anything* she asked—then snapped, “Good! I want your life, and I want every Jin soldier off Song soil! Hand those over!”
[ZH] 他穿好上衣，爬起来，浑身的确比先前轻松许多。拿出火折子，点着屋内的油灯，走到榻前跪下：“仪王妃，您和喜兰在这里等着，我去找王爷。”
[EN] Choking on his own boast, Wanyan Chi snarled in fury and ordered the troops to attack while stepping clear himself.
[ZH] 天色已变黑，白风顺着巷子寻找王爷，越是靠近废弃的屋子，越能听到打斗的声音。
[EN] Late autumn dusk bled into darkness. Jin squads rotated fresh men in wave after wave, denying Shen Jianchen and Qianli Xun a single breath. Heavy limbs turned to lead; sweat soaked their robes. Gasping as stars swam before his eyes, Shen Jianchen held upright on sheer grit, while Qianli Xun still spat defiance between blows that even if she died today, she had flattened plenty of Jin dogs first.
[ZH] 到达废弃的屋子所在的街道时，依然有声音传来，却未见打斗之人。
[EN] Stepping through the circle, Wanyan Chi caught her swaying form as she spat at him, tapped the fainting acupoint at her neck, and smiled as she collapsed limp into his arms. Handing her over to Lu Yuanfeng to carry back to the manor, he turned and kicked Shen Jianchen flat into the dirt, ordering his soldiers to hack the Song man to pieces under a storm of blades.
[ZH] 白风感到奇怪，抬头观察四周，发现屋子后面的街道上有火把的光芒，声音应该是从那里传来。
[EN] Too spent to rise, Shen Jianchen watched through blurring eyes as Lu Yuanfeng lifted Qianli Xun into a waiting carriage, shutting his eyes silently as steel closed in, hoping only that once he died, she might keep him in her heart.
[ZH] “王爷肯定在那里！”
[EN] Hours earlier, after weeping himself dry over Mengrong and Xilan, Bai Feng had gathered every scrap of strength to carry both women off the freezing stones onto the bed inside the abandoned home—collapsing beside them dizzy from blood loss.
[ZH] 白风朝后面的街道绕去……
[EN] As the western sun faded, his eyes fell on a dead Jin officer slumped against the wall. Crawling over on a prayer, he searched the corpse’s robe and pulled out two medicine bottles—swallowing a pill from one, pouring powder blind across the stinging cuts on his back.
[ZH] 到达时，发现王爷趴在地上毫无反抗之力，而周围的金人手举利剑向王爷刺去。
[EN] Within half an hour, the pain dulled and his head cleared. Lighting the oil lamp with his fire-striker, he knelt beside the bed, promising Princess Consort Yi and Xilan to wait while he found their prince, and limped down the darkening alleys toward the sounds of battle—reaching the rear street just as raised blades hovered above Shen Jianchen’s chest.
[ZH] 白风情急之下，忘记身上有伤，启动轻功落在中间，使出连环腿将拿利剑的金人踢倒，然后扑向仪王，用自己的身体护住他：“王爷！我来救你！”
[EN] Forgetting his own wounds, Bai Feng leaped square into the ring with *Thunderbolt Chain Kicks*, knocking the nearest killers flying, and threw himself flat over Shen Jianchen. “Your Highness! I’m here to save you!”

#CH 337
[ZH] 白风使出连环腿时，完颜池一脸惊讶，轻声自语：“雷霆连环踢！师傅花了整整一年时间才研究出这套拳脚功夫，还没来得及教我，他怎么会？”
[EN] When Bai Feng unleashed that chain of kicks, Wanyan Chi's face filled with surprise. He murmured to himself, 'Thunderbolt Chain Kick! My master spent a whole year working out this set of fist and foot techniques. He never had time to teach it to me. How could he know it?'
[ZH] 听到白风称地上趴着的人为“王爷”，完颜池向没有被白风打倒，想要用剑刺去的将领命令道：“都退下！”
[EN] When he heard Bai Feng call the man on the ground 'Your Highness,' Wanyan Chi ordered the generals who had not been knocked down and were about to thrust with their swords, 'All of you, back off!'
[ZH] 将领们收起剑退到一边。
[EN] The generals sheathed their swords and stepped aside.
[ZH] 完颜池走到跟前：“你称他为‘王爷’，难道他是大宋皇帝的儿子？”
[EN] Wanyan Chi walked up. 'You call him "Your Highness." Is he a son of the Song emperor?'
[ZH] 白风见对方听到“王爷”的称谓不再动手，想着金人应该也畏惧皇家之人，便照实说：“王爷乃官家的十三子仪王，大名赵朴。”
[EN] Bai Feng saw that the mere title had stopped their hands. The Jin, it seemed, feared royalty too. So he told the truth. 'His Highness is the thirteenth son of the Emperor, Prince Yi, named Zhao Pu.'
[ZH] “哈哈哈……，没想到你和我一样都是位王爷。既如此，让你多活些时日，好回汴梁告诉大宋的官家，等我金军到达东京，他最好乖乖送上降书，也免得老百姓跟着遭受战争之苦。”完颜池说完，向金军命令道，“撤！”
[EN] 'Ha! I never expected you to be a prince like me. Then I'll let you live a while longer. You can go back to Bianliang and tell the Song emperor this: when my Jin army reaches the Eastern Capital, he had better hand over a letter of surrender, quietly. That will spare his people the misery of war.' Wanyan Chi turned to his troops. 'Withdraw!'
[ZH] 申简辰揪心千里寻，用手撑着地，扶起身：“站……住！”
[EN] Shen Jianchen's heart was locked on Qianli Xun. He pushed himself up with his hands, struggling to rise. 'Stay...!'
[ZH] 完颜池没走出几步，听到大宋王爷的声音，轻松转过身：“怎么？你还想送死？”
[EN] Wanyan Chi had taken only a few steps. Hearing the Song prince's voice, he turned easily. 'What? You want to die?'
[ZH] “放了……她！”
[EN] 'Let... her go!'
[ZH] “哼！你算老几，本王为何要听你的？她已跟你没关系，还是好好操心自己。”
[EN] 'Hmph. Who are you to order me? Why should I listen? She is nothing to you now. Worry about yourself.'
[ZH] 完颜池不想继续跟手下败将啰唆，带着金国将领和士兵迅速离开。
[EN] Wanyan Chi saw no point in arguing with a man he had already beaten. He led his generals and soldiers away at once.
[ZH] 命算是保住，申简辰才真正放松身心趴在地上，他唯一揪心之人只有千里寻。
[EN] His life was safe. Only then did Shen Jianchen let his body go slack and lie flat on the ground. One person still twisted his heart: Qianli Xun.
[ZH] 白风担心地问：“王爷，您没事吧？”
[EN] Bai Feng asked, worried. 'Your Highness, are you all right?'
[ZH] “没事，就是累，金人……轮番上阵，体力……透支，多休息会儿……会好。”
[EN] 'I'm fine. Just tired. The Jin... came in waves. My strength... ran out. A bit more rest... and I'll be good.'
[ZH] 白风想提仪王妃之事，又不敢开口。
[EN] Bai Feng wanted to speak of Princess Yi, but he did not dare.
[ZH] 正犹豫间，听王爷问：“你把……王妃和喜兰……送回了客栈？”
[EN] As he hesitated, the prince asked, 'Did you... take the princess and Xilan... back to the inn?'
[ZH] 话还未说，白风先哭起来：“呜呜……”
[EN] Bai Feng started crying before he said a word. 'Waaah...'
[ZH] “怎么像……女人一样……哭起来？”
[EN] 'Why are you... crying... like a woman?'
[ZH] “呜呜，我没保护好王妃，她，她，她和喜兰被金人杀害，呜呜……！”
[EN] 'Waaah, I failed to protect the princess. She, she, she and Xilan were killed by the Jin. Waaah...!'
[ZH] 申简辰已无心趴在地上休息，抓住白风：“你说……什么？人……死了？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen forgot all about resting. He grabbed Bai Feng. 'What did you... say? They're... dead?'
[ZH] 白风满脸泪痕点着头：“我们本来要从巷子出去，结果出现许多金兵，我一个人对付起来困难，后来又被砍伤，才没保护好仪王妃和喜兰，呜呜……！”
[EN] Tear streaks covered Bai Feng's face as he nodded. 'We were going out through the alley. Jin soldiers appeared everywhere. I couldn't handle them alone. Then I was slashed. That's why I failed Princess Yi and Xilan. Waaah...!'
[ZH] 申简辰顾不上担忧千里寻，扶着白风站起来。
[EN] Shen Jianchen forgot his fear for Qianli Xun. He helped Bai Feng to his feet.
[ZH] “她们……现在何处？”
[EN] 'Where... are they now?'
[ZH] 白风扶好王爷：“我把她们的尸体放在一个空屋里，就在那边的巷子。”
[EN] Bai Feng steadied the prince. 'I put their bodies in an empty room. It's in the alley over there.'
[ZH] “带我……过去。”
[EN] 'Take me... there.'
[ZH] 一个身上有伤，一个浑身无力，互相搀扶着朝巷子走去。
[EN] One was wounded. One had no strength left. They leaned on each other and headed for the alley.
[ZH] 进入巷子，又走了好长一截，才来到空屋所在的院子。院门敞开着，走进后，因白风离开时将屋内的油灯点着，申简辰在院中能清晰地瞧见正屋的榻上躺着两个人，从衣裳判断其中一人定是梦容。
[EN] They entered the alley and walked a long way before reaching the courtyard with the empty room. The gate stood open. Bai Feng had lit an oil lamp before leaving, so from the yard Shen Jianchen could clearly see two figures lying on the bed in the main room. From their clothes, one had to be Mengrong.
[ZH] 因悲痛，申简辰推开白风独自向前走，走出几步，一条腿软了一下，差点摔倒。
[EN] Grief drove him. Shen Jianchen pushed Bai Feng aside and walked on alone. A few steps later, one leg gave way. He nearly fell.
[ZH] 白风上前扶好，他看到王爷流下两行泪。
[EN] Bai Feng rushed in and caught him. He saw two lines of tears on the prince's face.
[ZH] 申简辰不再拒绝白风的搀扶，一步一步走向屋内，他回想着与梦容曾经在一起的时光，竟然也觉出许多美好来。
[EN] Shen Jianchen no longer pushed him away. Step by step, he went into the room. He thought of the times he had spent with Mengrong, and found, to his surprise, that some of them had been good.
[ZH] 梦容与喜兰并排躺在榻上，面色苍白，她的嘴角似乎有一抹笑容，看来她死的那一刻并不难过，申简辰的内心不再像刚才那么愧疚。
[EN] Mengrong and Xilan lay side by side on the bed, their faces pale. A small smile seemed to rest on Mengrong's lips. She had not suffered at the end. The guilt in Shen Jianchen's chest eased.
[ZH] “梦容，无论你以前做过什么，哥哥都不再怪你，希望你到另一个世界能遇到与你心心相印之人。”
[EN] 'Mengrong, whatever you did before, I won't blame you again. In the next world, I hope you find someone whose heart beats with yours.'
[ZH] ……
[EN] ...
[ZH] 两人守着尸体难过了一晚，第二天申简辰吩咐白风到街上买两口棺材，再租几个脚夫，买些贡品和纸钱。
[EN] The two kept watch over the bodies through a long night of grief. The next day Shen Jianchen sent Bai Feng into the street to buy two coffins, hire a few porters, and get offerings and paper money.
[ZH] 白风问：“难道要将仪王妃葬在燕京？”
[EN] Bai Feng asked, 'Are we to bury Princess Yi in Yanjing?'
[ZH] “尸体不宜存放，需尽快安葬，也好让她们入土为安。等一切理顺后，我会将梦容的尸骨迁回汴梁……”想到汴梁不久之后也保不住，申简辰立刻改口，“不，迁回临安。”
[EN] 'Bodies cannot wait. We must bury them soon, so they may rest in the earth. When things are settled, I'll move Mengrong's bones back to Bianliang. He thought of how soon Bianliang would be lost, and changed his words at once. No. To Lin'an.'
[ZH] 白风不再说什么，出去操办王爷布置的事。
[EN] Bai Feng said no more. He went out to do what the prince had ordered.
[ZH] 金军刚刚进入燕京，没有逃走的百姓担心碰到金人后被杀害，都躲着不敢出来，街上只有白风一人走动。
[EN] The Jin army had only just entered Yanjing. The people who had not fled hid in fear of being killed. No one dared come out. Bai Feng was the only soul moving in the street.
[ZH] 好不容易瞧见一家棺材铺，白风上前拍打着门：“店家，开门，我需要买两口棺材。”
[EN] At last he spotted a coffin shop. He went up and pounded on the door. 'Shopkeeper! Open up. I need two coffins.'
[ZH] 如此喊了好几声，店家才小心把门打开：“进来说。”
[EN] He called several times before the shopkeeper opened the door a cautious crack. 'Come in and speak.'
[ZH] 白风侧身进入铺子内，瞧见里面放着好几口已打制好的棺材。
[EN] Bai Feng slipped inside. Several finished coffins stood in the shop.
[ZH] 店家问：“金人已进燕京，你不怕被杀？还跑出来买棺材？”
[EN] 'The Jin are in Yanjing,' the shopkeeper said. 'Aren't you afraid of being killed? Coming out to buy coffins?'
[ZH] 白风回道：“家里有人被金人杀害，我才来买棺材。”
[EN] 'Someone in my house was killed by the Jin,' Bai Feng answered. 'That's why I'm here.'
[ZH] “可怜啊，可怜，这世道人活着不易。”店家慨叹着，指着棺材，“都在那里，随便挑。”
[EN] 'Pity, poor soul. Life is hard in these times.' The shopkeeper sighed and pointed. 'They're all over there. Take your pick.'
[ZH] 白风选中两口棺材，不着急付钱，继续说：“我是外乡人，在燕京没有亲人，身边的人死了，需尽快入土为安，还需店家帮忙找些脚力，将棺材抬出城。”
[EN] Bai Feng chose two coffins but did not pay yet. 'I'm a stranger here. No kin in Yanjing. My people are dead. They need to be laid in the earth at once. I also need you to find some men to carry the coffins out of the city.'
[ZH] 店家为难道：“城里都是金人，没人敢出去。”
[EN] The shopkeeper looked troubled. 'The city is full of Jin. No one will go out.'
[ZH] “我刚从街上来，并未看到金人，麻烦店家帮忙寻些人来。”白风掏出银票，递上去，“钱只会多给你。”
[EN] 'I just came along the street. I saw no Jin. Please find some men for me.' Bai Feng took out a banknote and held it out. 'You'll be paid extra.'
[ZH] 店家接过银票，上面的数额够自己卖十几口棺材，高兴接下：“我的棺材铺也常帮人办丧事，正好有几个身强力壮的人因金人入城没敢回家，躲在后面的屋里，让他们负责抬棺材，我再免费送你些纸钱。”
[EN] The shopkeeper took the note. It was worth more than a dozen coffins. He agreed at once. 'My shop often handles funerals. A few strong fellows hiding in the back don't dare go home with the Jin in the city. I'll have them carry the coffins. I'll throw in some paper money for free.'
[ZH] 白风带着从棺材铺借来的脚力，抬着两口棺材来到巷子，将仪王妃和喜兰的尸体放进去。
[EN] Bai Feng led the borrowed porters to the alley with the two coffins. They placed Princess Yi and Xilan inside.
[ZH] 钉好棺材后，申简辰与白风走在前面，带着抬棺材的人向燕京城外而去。
[EN] Once the lids were nailed shut, Shen Jianchen and Bai Feng walked ahead of the carriers, heading out of Yanjing.
[ZH] 一路上倒也安全，只是燕京城从昨日起变得冷冷清清，仿佛是座空城一般。
[EN] The way was quiet enough. But Yanjing had turned cold and empty since the day before, like a city with no one left in it.
[ZH] 来到城门口才看到把守的金军，抬棺材的几人有些害怕，一人带头说：“你们两个需保证我们的安全。”
[EN] Only at the gate did they see the Jin guards. The carriers grew nervous. One spoke for the rest. 'You two must guarantee our safety.'
[ZH] 白风说：“不会有事，等出了城，把人安葬好，还会给你们报酬。”
[EN] 'Nothing will happen,' Bai Feng said. 'Once we're outside and the burial is done, you'll be paid.'
[ZH] 还能有银子赚，抬棺材的人也都壮起胆儿。
[EN] Silver still waited at the end. The carriers found their courage again.
[ZH] 申简辰并不害怕，休息一晚上，他的体力恢复不少，大不了再杀几个金人，就当是给梦容和喜兰报仇。他并不理会守城门的金兵，径直朝外走。
[EN] Shen Jianchen was not afraid. A night's rest had brought much of his strength back. Worst case, he would kill a few more Jin and call it revenge for Mengrong and Xilan. He ignored the guards and walked straight out.
[ZH] 守门的金兵拔出剑挡在他面前：“统统不许出去！”
[EN] A guard drew his sword and blocked his path. 'Nobody leaves!'
[ZH] 申简辰本想一把扭断伸在他面前的那只手，还是克制住情绪，决定先礼后兵：“没看到后面抬着死人？总得入土为安。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen itched to snap the hand thrust at him. He held back. Words first, force later. 'Do you see the dead behind me? They have to be buried.'
[ZH] 金兵说：“我管你活人死人，一个都不能出城。”
[EN] 'I don't care if you're alive or dead,' the guard said. 'Not one of you leaves.'
[ZH] 申简辰刚想一拳将金兵打倒，城楼上传来一位将领的声音：“小王爷有令！放他出去！”
[EN] He was about to knock the man down with one punch when a voice came from the tower. 'Order from the young prince! Let him pass!'
[ZH] 金兵抽回宝剑，申简辰松开已攥紧的拳头，继续向城门外走去。
[EN] The guard pulled back his sword. Shen Jianchen unclenched his fist and walked on through the gate.
[ZH] 第二口棺材被抬出后，城楼上的将领喊道：“大宋的王爷！小王爷让我叮嘱你！别再惦记美人！还是快些回汴梁给大宋的官家捎口信，让他夹道欢迎我大金的魏王！哈哈哈……！”
[EN] When the second coffin was carried out, the officer on the tower shouted, 'Prince of Song! The young prince says: stop dreaming of beauties! Go back to Bianliang and tell your emperor to line the streets and welcome the Wei Prince of great Jin! Hahaha...!'
[ZH] 城楼上所有人跟着大笑起来：“哈哈哈……！”
[EN] Everyone on the tower joined in. 'Hahaha...!'
[ZH] 要搁往常他绝对会飞身而上将所有人嘲笑他的人拿下，梦容还未入土，死者为大，至于那些人，往后有的是机会收拾。
[EN] On any other day he would have leapt up and dragged every mocking face down. Mengrong was not yet in the ground. The dead come first. As for those men, there would be time to settle with them later.
[ZH] 申简辰继续朝前走，将金人的笑声甩在身后。
[EN] Shen Jianchen walked on, leaving the Jin laughter behind him.
[ZH] 在脚夫的指引下，来到城外一片野地。几位脚夫很有经验，到不远处的村庄借来工具，挖了一个刚好能容下两口棺材的大坑，将人掩埋好，再帮忙撒些纸钱，拿了白风给的银子，高高兴兴找城外的亲戚暂住。
[EN] The porters led him to a stretch of wild land outside the walls. They knew their work. They borrowed tools from a nearby village, dug a pit just wide enough for two coffins, buried the dead, and scattered paper money over the mound. Then they took Bai Feng's silver and went to stay with relatives outside the city, well pleased.
[ZH] 白风见王爷伫立在坟头，像是用心与埋在地下的仪王妃说话。从昨晚到现在他算是看出来，王爷虽不爱王妃，却也关心她，把她当亲人一样看待。
[EN] Bai Feng saw the prince standing at the grave, as if speaking with his heart to the woman beneath the earth. Since last night he had understood something: the prince had not loved his wife, but he had cared for her, and treated her like family.
[ZH] 白风走到王爷身边，小心说：“王爷，王妃和喜兰已入土，幽栖居士又被金人抓去，我们是回汴梁？还是留下救幽栖居士？”
[EN] Bai Feng came close and said carefully, 'Your Highness, the princess and Xilan are in the ground. Youqi Jushi has been taken by the Jin. Do we return to Bianliang, or stay and save her?'
[ZH] 其实白风已知答案，不过是担心王爷太过悲伤，才说些话转移他的注意力而已。
[EN] He already knew the answer. He only spoke to pull the prince out of his grief for a moment.
[ZH] “自然留下救人。”
[EN] 'We stay, of course. We save her.'
[ZH] “城门有金人把守，不好光明正大进去救人，等天黑后，我和王爷偷偷溜进去。”
[EN] 'Jin hold the gate. We can't walk in openly. After dark, you and I will slip inside.'
[ZH] “只能如此。”
[EN] 'There's no other way.'
[ZH] 白风的肚子叫了两声，从昨晚到现在还未吃一口热乎饭：“王爷，要不到周围的村子里找些吃的？”
[EN] Bai Feng's stomach growled twice. Not one warm meal since the night before. 'Your Highness, shall we find something to eat in the villages nearby?'
[ZH] 因为悲伤，申简辰并不觉得腹中饥饿，却不能不顾白风。他向梦容做最后的道别：“梦容，你安心待在这里，总有一日哥哥会带你回家。”
[EN] Grief had taken his hunger, but he could not ignore Bai Feng. He said his last goodbye to Mengrong. 'Rest here, Mengrong. One day I'll take you home.'
[ZH] 两人恋恋不舍离开坟头，找到一户农家借住。农家的庭院和屋子十分简陋，好在能吃上一口热乎饭。
[EN] Reluctantly they left the grave and found a farmhouse to stay the night. The yard and the rooms were bare. But at least they would eat something warm.
[ZH] 农家人把饭菜端上，不过是两个粗面馒头、两碗粥和一碟腌制的老咸菜。特殊时期，顾不上讲究，两人凑合着吃。
[EN] The farmer set out the meal: two coarse buns, two bowls of porridge, and a dish of old pickled vegetables. These were hard times. No one cared about finer points. The two ate what was put before them.
[ZH] 到了晚上，向农家人讨来两块黑布，蒙上脸。来到城墙之下，两人用轻功跳上城墙，干净利落打晕守卫的金兵，然后跳进城内，沿着无人的主街向内穿梭。
[EN] When night came, they asked the farmer for two pieces of black cloth and covered their faces. At the foot of the wall, they leapt up with qinggong, knocked out the guards clean and quick, and dropped inside. They slipped along the empty main street, deeper into the city.
[ZH] 听到有脚步声，两人迅速用轻功跳上了屋顶。到高处才看清，原来有一队金兵正在巡逻。
[EN] Footsteps sounded. They sprang onto the rooftops. From up high they saw a patrol of Jin soldiers moving below.
[ZH] 白风小声说：“也不知幽栖居士被关在哪里？总不能像无头苍蝇一样瞎找？”
[EN] Bai Feng whispered, 'Where can they be holding Youqi Jushi? We can't stumble around like flies with no heads.'
[ZH] 申简辰朝别处瞧了瞧：“你下去把最后一名金兵抓来，找他问问。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen glanced down the street. 'Go down. Grab the last man in the patrol. Ask him.'
[ZH] 白风启动轻功，迅速从巡街的队伍中点了最后一位金兵的穴位，在其他人发现之前，已用轻功迅速拽着金兵向另一个方向而去。
[EN] Bai Feng moved like a shadow. He struck the acupoint of the last soldier in line, and before the others noticed, he had hauled the man off in another direction.
[ZH] 其他巡逻的金兵感觉到身后有声音，回身向后瞧，任何人影都没有。有人提醒少了一人，其他人才意识到，四处寻找毫无结果。
[EN] The patrol heard a sound behind them and turned. Nothing there. One man said someone was missing. Only then did the others notice. They searched everywhere and found nothing.
[ZH] 有人说，肯定是那金兵偷懒不想巡街偷偷躲起来。其他人无不认可，大家继续安心巡街。
[EN] Someone said the soldier must have slipped off to avoid patrol duty. Everyone agreed. They went back to their rounds, easy in mind.
[ZH] 在另一条街头的犄角旮旯处，白风又在金兵身上的穴位处点了一下，那人醒来，本想反抗，却无法动弹。
[EN] In a dark corner of another street, Bai Feng pressed another acupoint. The soldier woke, tried to fight, and could not move.
[ZH] 申简辰问：“完颜池住在哪里？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen asked, 'Where does Wanyan Chi live?'
[ZH] 金兵嘴硬道：“凭什么告诉你？”
[EN] The soldier stuck to his guns. 'Why should I tell you?'
[ZH] “不说是吧？”白风抡起拳头一阵乱打，直到金人求饶。
[EN] 'You won't talk?' Bai Feng swung his fists again and again until the Jin man begged for mercy.
[ZH] “饶了我吧！饶了我吧！我说！我说！”
[EN] 'Spare me! Spare me! I'll talk! I'll talk!'
[ZH] 申简辰又追问：“住在哪里？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen pressed him again. 'Where does he live?'
[ZH] “小王爷住在离燕京府不远的王府里。”
[EN] 'The young prince lives in a mansion not far from the Yanjing government offices.'
[ZH] “具体在哪个方位？”
[EN] 'Which direction, exactly?'
[ZH] “沿着主街向北走。”
[EN] 'Follow the main street north.'
[ZH] 白风拔出藏在身上的匕首刺在金兵的要害，金兵一命呜呼，然后将匕首拔出，把上面的血迹用金兵的身上的衣裳擦干净：“就当是为仪王妃和喜兰报仇。”
[EN] Bai Feng drew the dagger hidden on him and drove it into the soldier's vital spot. The man died at once. He pulled the blade out and wiped the blood clean on the dead man's coat. 'Call it revenge for Princess Yi and Xilan.'
[ZH] 两人悄悄沿着主街向北走，时不时有金兵巡逻，看来经过一日，金人已在全城布下兵力。
[EN] They slipped north along the main street. Patrols passed now and then. One day had been enough for the Jin to spread their men across the whole city.
[ZH] 两人巧妙躲过的同时变得愈加谨慎。
[EN] They dodged them neatly, but grew more careful with every step.
[ZH] 到达王府时已是深夜，所有人入睡。他们跳进王府内，分头行动，躲开里面巡逻的侍卫，几乎将所有可能关人的屋子查了个遍，也找到到千里寻的身影。
[EN] It was deep night when they reached the mansion. Everyone slept. They vaulted inside and split up, slipping past the guards on patrol. They searched nearly every room that could hold a prisoner. Not a trace of Qianli Xun.
[ZH] 申简辰甚至从屋顶看到完颜池躺在榻上搂着一位女子呼呼大睡的情景，他瞪大眼睛仔细瞧过，完颜池怀中的女子并非千里寻。
[EN] From a roof, Shen Jianchen even saw Wanyan Chi sprawled on a bed, snoring, an arm around a girl. He stared hard. The girl in those arms was not Qianli Xun.
[ZH] 白风与王爷在王府外汇合，两人见面后都失落地摇摇头。
[EN] Bai Feng met the prince outside the mansion. They looked at each other and both shook their heads, disappointed.
[ZH] 这时有马车经过，看那样子应该是从燕京府出来。从马车后面跟的人判断，里面坐的官员应是汉人。
[EN] A carriage rolled by, heading away from the Yanjing government offices, by the look of it. The men escorting it suggested the official inside was Han.
[ZH] “去会会此人。”
[EN] 'Let's pay this man a call.'
[ZH] 两人用轻功落在马车前挡住官员的去路。
[EN] They landed with qinggong in front of the carriage and blocked its way.
[ZH] 车夫勒住缰绳，马车后跟着的士兵冲到前面，厉声道：“你们是何人？怎敢拦截蔡大人的车马？”
[EN] The driver pulled hard on the reins. The soldiers behind rushed forward. 'Who are you? How dare you stop Lord Cai's carriage?'
[ZH] 白风用目光征求王爷后，报上身份：“站在你们面前的人是官家的十三子仪王，前来会会这位大人。”
[EN] Bai Feng glanced at the prince for permission, then gave their names. 'Before you stands the thirteenth son of the Emperor, Prince Yi. He has come to meet your lord.'
[ZH] 马车里的蔡大人听到，推开车门，探出脑袋：“你是仪王？”
[EN] Lord Cai heard him from inside. He pushed the door open and stuck out his head. 'You are Prince Yi?'
[ZH] 申简辰肯定地答道：“如假包换。”顺手取下腰牌。
[EN] 'No doubt about it,' Shen Jianchen answered. He took off his waist token as he spoke.
[ZH] 士兵接过，将腰牌送至马车内。
[EN] The soldier took it and passed it inside the carriage.
[ZH] 很快听到马车里传出蔡大人的声音：“既是仪王，请马车上一叙。”
[EN] Soon Lord Cai's voice came from within. 'If you are Prince Yi, come aboard and talk.'
[ZH] 申简辰走上马车坐好。
[EN] Shen Jianchen climbed in and sat down.
[ZH] 蔡大人向跟着的人说：“都给我记下，今日之事不许向外透露。”
[EN] Lord Cai said to his followers, 'Remember this: not a word of today goes outside.'
[ZH] 然后吩咐车夫：“回府！”
[EN] Then he ordered the driver, 'Home!'
[ZH] 申简辰取下脸上的黑布：“不知蔡大人如何称呼？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen pulled the black cloth from his face. 'May I ask your honorable name, Lord Cai?'
[ZH] 蔡大人看到这张脸，正是昨日在街上救走大宋将领之人，他将腰牌送上：“下官蔡靖，贱名不足仪王挂怀。”
[EN] When Lord Cai saw the face, he knew it: the man who had saved a Song officer on the street the day before. He handed back the token. 'Cai Jing, your humble servant. My poor name is not worth Your Highness's notice.'
[ZH] 申简辰接过腰牌，奚落道：“蔡大人已归顺金人，岂能在我面前称下官？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen took the token and said with a sneer, 'You serve the Jin now, Lord Cai. "Humble servant" does not sit well in my presence.'
[ZH] 蔡靖挤出几滴泪，装作擦拭的样子：“仪王有所不知，我本不愿投降，都是郭药师，他将我扣押，逼迫我投降。下官乃一介文官，手无缚鸡之力，只能任人摆布。”
[EN] Cai Jing squeezed out a few tears and pretended to wipe them away. 'You do not know, Prince. I never wanted to surrender. It was Guo Yaoshi. He held me and forced me. I am only a civil official. I could not lift a chicken, let alone a sword. I had no choice but to do as I was told.'
[ZH] 官家昏庸，朝廷腐败，有缺乏风骨的官员实在正常，申简辰不愿过分批判，再说他有事相求：“蔡大人愿意让我上马车，说明心中还有大宋。”
[EN] The emperor was dull, the court rotten. Men without backbone were only to be expected. Shen Jianchen did not care to judge him too harshly. Besides, he needed a favor. 'You let me into your carriage, Lord Cai. That says the Song is still in your heart.'
[ZH] “岂能没有？”蔡靖又流出一滴泪，“仪王还是不要待在燕京，快些回临安吧。据我估计金人想立刻拿下汴梁还有难度，可燕京已入金人囊中，殿下在此恐怕会遭遇不测。”
[EN] 'How could it not be?' Another tear slipped from Cai Jing. 'Prince, do not stay in Yanjing. Return to Lin'an soon. As I judge it, the Jin cannot take Bianliang at once. But Yanjing is already in their pocket. Your Highness would be in danger here.'
[ZH] “因有一事暂时绊住，等事情处理完，我自会离开。”
[EN] 'A lifetime and I still can't remember it...'
[ZH] 蔡靖敏锐地感觉出仪王找他应该是为他口中之事：“不知仪王因何事绊住？”
[EN] Cai Jing sensed at once that the prince had stopped him for that very matter. 'May I ask what holds Your Highness here?'

#CH 338
[ZH] 申简辰直言不讳：“完颜池抓走我的人……”
[ZH] “可是与仪王一起救大宋将领的女子？”
[ZH] “正是。”
[ZH] “仪王不必忧心，我听说小王爷……不对，完颜池，他喜欢那姑娘，不会轻易杀她。”
[ZH] “正因如此，我才更要救她。”
[ZH] 听得出仪王与那女子关系不一般，蔡靖问：“仪王想怎么救？”
[ZH] “本想今晚将人救走，可在王府查了个遍也没找到人，正好蔡大人出现，想跟你打听打听。”
[ZH] 蔡靖想了想说：“王府是辽国人建造的，听说地下还有一个府邸，称地府。宣和五年金从辽手中夺了燕京，将其归还大宋，童贯大人来此居住过，差人到处寻找地府，竟然没找到。再后来童贯大人听风水先生说王府里有个地府不吉利，才将宅子闲置起来。金军前日一来，完颜池主动选了这座府邸。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen spoke without hesitation. “Wanyan Chi took someone of mine…”
[ZH] “难道金人知道地府？”
[EN] “You mean the woman who helped you rescue the Song general?” Cai Jing asked.
[ZH] “毕竟辽是被金打败，恐怕完颜池知道。”
[EN] “The very same.”
[ZH] “感谢蔡大人，我现在前去抓几个王府的下人问问。”申简辰说着就要下车。
[EN] “Set your mind at ease, Your Highness. I heard the young prince—no, Wanyan Chi—fancies that girl. He will not kill her lightly.”
[ZH] 蔡靖拦住：“仪王且慢！”
[EN] “Which is the exact reason I must rescue her all the sooner.”
[ZH] “蔡大人为何拦我？”
[EN] Catching the depth of Prince Yi’s bond with her, Cai Jing asked how he meant to get her out.
[ZH] “恐怕王府的下人也不知，只有完颜池知道。殿下去了徒劳无益，再遭遇危险得不偿失？”
[EN] “I meant to slip her out tonight, yet I searched the entire royal manor and found nobody. Running into you was pure luck, Lord Cai—I hoped to ask what you know.”
[ZH] “可我总不能坐以待毙？”
[EN] Cai Jing thought a moment. “The Liao built that manor, and rumor says they constructed another estate underground beneath it—what people call the Under-Manor. Back in the fifth year of Xuanhe, after the Jin seized Yanjing from the Liao and returned it to the Song, Grand Preceptor Tong lived there. He sent men hunting everywhere for that underground estate and never found it. Later, a geomancer warned him an underground chamber beneath the manor brought ill luck, so Tong Guan left the compound empty. When Jin troops rode in two days ago, Wanyan Chi picked that estate of his own accord.”
[ZH] “若仪王信得过下官，先在府中住下，下官替王爷打探。”
[EN] “So the Jin know about the Under-Manor?”
[ZH] 申简辰想想，如此也好：“多谢蔡大人。”
[EN] “Having defeated the Liao, Wanyan Chi likely knows.”
[ZH] 蔡靖又流下眼泪：“我作为大宋臣子投降金人，恐被后人耻笑，借此机会替殿下做些事，我的良心也能苟安片刻。”
[EN] “Thank you, Lord Cai. I’ll go seize a few manor servants and wring the truth out of them.” Shen Jianchen moved to step out of the carriage.
[ZH] 申简辰才觉出蔡靖的眼泪源自真心：“蔡大人为何半夜从府衙出来？”
[EN] Cai Jing caught his sleeve. “Hold a moment, Your Highness!”
[ZH] 蔡靖擦干眼泪：“完颜池限我两日之内将大宋接手燕京后的管理过程事无巨细禀报于他，我才不得不在府衙忙碌，刚刚忙完，明日一早还需向他汇报。”
[EN] “Why stop me?”
[ZH] “完颜宗望可在燕京？”
[EN] “Manor servants are unlikely to know—only Wanyan Chi would. Going back now brings danger for nothing.”
[ZH] “已南下准备攻打汴梁。”说着蔡靖双眼又变红，“听说还有一路金兵围攻太原，只怕大宋要毁在金人手中。”
[EN] “I can’t sit here waiting for disaster either.”
[ZH] 申简辰安慰道：“金军虽英勇，我大宋也不乏好男儿，汴梁没那么容易拿下，即使最终拿下，大宋也不会轻易灭亡。大人是有学问之人，应该知道历史不过是一个朝代取代另一个朝代而已，若哪一日大宋被某个朝代取而代之，也不过代表着历史向前发展而已。你我如同蝼蚁，既然无法左右历史，不如欣然接受。据我大胆预测，即使大宋灭亡，也轮不上金人取而代之。”
[EN] “If Your Highness trusts me, stay at my residence while I make inquiries for you.”
[ZH] 蔡靖佩服不已：“仪王一番言论，令下官倾佩。只要金人灭不了我大宋，下官也可心安。我身在金，心却在宋，投降是被逼迫，也是为自保，两日来每每想起羞愧不已，只能期望大宋安然度过此劫难。”
[EN] Weighing the odds, Shen Jianchen agreed and thanked him.
[ZH] 申简辰虽不大了解蔡靖，可通过一路交谈，多少感受出他是真心帮忙，不如信他一回。再说任凭自己功夫再高，也敌不过完颜池手下众多。
[EN] Tears welled in Cai Jing’s eyes again. “As a Song subject who bowed to the Jin, I dread the scorn of future ages. Doing this small service for Your Highness lets my conscience catch its breath.”
[ZH] 第二日，蔡靖带着目的来到王府，呈上他加班加点整理出的小册子。
[EN] Sensing his tears came from real remorse, Shen Jianchen asked why he had stayed at the yamen past midnight.
[ZH] 完颜池打开浏览几眼，很是满意：“辛苦蔡大人，内容较多，本王需慢慢消化。”
[EN] Wiping his eyes, Cai Jing explained Wanyan Chi had given him two days to write a thorough report on every detail of Song governance in Yanjing, due first thing tomorrow morning.
[ZH] 蔡靖趁机问：“小王爷刚住进王府不知可住得习惯？”
[EN] “Is Wanyan Zongwang still in Yanjing?”
[ZH] “王府什么都有，本王自然住得习惯。”
[EN] “Already marching south to strike Bianliang.” Cai Jing’s eyes reddened anew. “I heard another Jin column has surrounded Taiyuan. I fear Great Song may perish at Jin hands.”
[ZH] “小王爷可能还不知，王府内藏有一个地府。”蔡靖之所以主动提到地府，无非是为了确认完颜池是否知道地府的存在。
[EN] “Jin troops fight fierce, yet Song has plenty of brave sons too,” Shen Jianchen said in calm reassurance. “Bianliang will not fall so easy—and even if it falls, Song will not vanish overnight. A learned man like you knows history is just one dynasty giving way to the next. If another dynasty ever replaces the Song, it only marks history moving forward. Ants like us cannot steer the tide; better to take it as it comes. Mark my bold guess: even if Song falls, it will not be the Jin who take its place.”
[ZH] 完颜池警惕之后，装作不知的样子：“地府？藏在哪里？”
[EN] Moved to deep respect, Cai Jing murmured that as long as the Jin could not wipe out Great Song, his heart could rest. Surrender had been forced upon him to stay alive, shaming him day and night.
[ZH] 蔡靖由此断定，完颜池多半已知，说不定仪王想找的人被藏在地府里，不如继续探测：“下官听说辽国人在地府里藏有许多宝贝，当年大宋的童贯大人来此，差人到处寻找地府，没有成功，不得已放弃。既然小王爷住下，下官愿协助小王爷将地府找出来，挖出宝贝……”
[EN] Though barely acquainted with Cai Jing, Shen Jianchen judged him sincere after their talk—and knew one man’s fists alone stood poor odds against an army. He decided to trust him.
[ZH] 完颜池的脸上露出狡黠的笑容：“怎么找？”
[EN] The next morning, Cai Jing presented his neat report booklet at the royal manor. Scanning a few pages, Wanyan Chi nodded in satisfaction. “Well done, Lord Cai. There is much here to digest at leisure.”
[ZH] “据说设计宅子的人姓贾，还有后人在世，只要把他的后人找来，十有八九可以找到藏在地下的府邸。”
[EN] Seizing his chance, Cai Jing asked politely how the young prince liked his new manor.
[ZH] 完颜池抓住一支箭刺向蔡靖。
[EN] “It lacks nothing. Quite comfortable.”
[ZH] 蔡靖本能地向后闪去：“小王爷这是何意？”
[EN] “Perhaps Your Highness does not yet know—a hidden Under-Manor lies beneath these grounds.” Cai Jing dropped the bait to test whether Wanyan Chi knew of it.
[ZH] 完颜池用箭指着蔡靖：“别以为本王不知，昨晚大宋的王爷带人来王府找人，可惜什么都没找到，后来你把他带回府中，今日又跑来打听地府，难道不是为了帮他寻人？”
[EN] A flicker of wariness crossed Wanyan Chi’s face before he feigned ignorance. “An Under-Manor? Hidden where?”
[ZH] 蔡靖吓出一身冷汗，跪在地上：“小王爷饶命！下官只是看他可怜才答应帮忙……”
[EN] Certain now that Wanyan Chi knew—and very likely kept the poetess locked down there—Cai Jing pressed further. “Rumor says the Liao stashed vast treasures below. Years ago, Grand Preceptor Tong searched high and low for it without luck and had to give up. Since you live here now, I could help you locate it and dig up the treasure…”
[ZH] “可怜？”完颜池收回箭扔进箭筒，“一个王爷怎么会可怜？”
[EN] A sly smile curved Wanyan Chi’s lips. “How would you find it?”
[ZH] “小王爷有所不知，他的母妃在他很小的时候离开人世，又因长期病着，官家不怎么待见他。也不知怎的，他的病后来奇迹般好了，因蹴鞠不错官家给了个临安节度使当，没当多久惹怒官家，又没了官职，现在只是个闲散王爷，跟普通百姓中的富家公子哥没什么区别。”
[EN] “The architect who designed this manor was surnamed Jia, and his descendants are said to live still. Track them down, and nine times out of ten they’ll reveal the entrance.”
[ZH] 完颜池念叨着：“原来他也没有娘，跟本王一样是个没人疼的可怜虫。”
[EN] In a flash, Wanyan Chi snatched an arrow and thrust the point toward Cai Jing.
[ZH] 蔡靖见以情动人很有效果，继续替自己开解：“下官见他是个没人疼的可怜虫，才让他住在府中，答应帮他打听那姑娘的事。依下官之见，他从小没有娘疼，好不容易有位姑娘疼他，才会想方设法救那姑娘，跟小王爷没法比。小王爷身边女子众多，多一个不多，少一个不少，依下官之见，不如把那姑娘还给他，再说那姑娘对大金来讲微不足道。”
[EN] Jumping back by reflex, Cai Jing stammered, “What does Your Highness mean by this?”
[ZH] 完颜池坐回位置，看上去并不不生气：“你很会替他说话，可本王偏不听。看在他与本王一样从小没有娘，本王再放他一马，若他能在两个月内拿出一千万两银子，本王便将人还给他。你劝他别再瞎找人，赶快想办法筹钱吧，哈哈哈……！”完颜池大笑着起身拐进里屋。
[EN] Pointing the arrowhead at his chest, Wanyan Chi snapped, “Think I don’t know? Last night the Song prince broke into my manor searching for someone and left empty-handed. Then you hid him in your house and came fishing around about the Under-Manor today—all to help him find her!”
[ZH] 蔡靖擦擦额头的汗，庆幸总算听到准确的话。一千万两银子不是小数目，也不知仪王能否筹到？还是回去劝劝他别再惦记那位姑娘。
[EN] Cold sweat broke down Cai Jing’s back; he hit his knees at once begging mercy, pleading that he only helped out of pity.
[ZH] 完颜池进入里屋，站在一处，先将右脚跺三下，又将左脚跺三下，只见木榻自动掀起，露出一个方形的洞来。
[EN] “Pity?” Wanyan Chi tossed the arrow back into its quiver. “How is a prince pitiable?”
[ZH] 完颜池自语道：“当年从辽国手中夺下燕京，辽国的王爷已将开启地府的方法告诉本王，本王又岂会让宋人知道？”
[EN] “You may not know, Your Highness: his mother died when he was tiny, and because he stayed sick for years, the Song Emperor paid him little mind. Somehow he recovered by sheer miracle, won the post of Military Commissioner of Lin’an for his skill at cuju, then offended the Emperor and lost his office in short order. Now he’s just a powerless idle prince—no different from any rich commoner’s son.”
[ZH] 完颜池从洞口走下去，木榻很快恢复原样，像什么都没发生一般。
[EN] “Motherless from childhood too…” Wanyan Chi murmured. “A poor wretch nobody loved, just like me.”
[ZH] 地下的府邸除了光线暗到处点着灯外，其余与地面上的府邸布置均无两样。
[EN] Seeing sympathy strike home, Cai Jing pressed his plea. “Seeing how nobody ever loved him until one girl finally cared for him—how could I turn him away? Compared with you, surrounded by beauties, one woman more or less means nothing to Great Jin. Why not hand her back?”
[ZH] 一位丫鬟拜道：“小王爷。”
[EN] Leaning back into his seat without anger, Wanyan Chi smirked. “You spin a fine plea for him, yet I refuse. Out of kinship for growing up motherless just like me, I’ll cut him one break: let him bring ten million taels of silver within two months, and I’ll give her back. Tell him to quit hunting blind and go scrape up the silver! Hahaha!” Laughing aloud, he walked into his inner chamber.
[ZH] “美人可好？”
[EN] Wiping sweat off his brow, Cai Jing sighed in relief that at least he had a straight answer—though where on earth anyone could scrape up ten million taels of silver was another matter. Best advise Prince Yi to let the girl go.
[ZH] “不怎么听话，吵着要出去。”
[EN] Inside his inner room, Wanyan Chi halted over one spot, stomped his right foot three times, and stomped his left foot three times. With a low click, the wooden bed lifted upward, revealing a square shaft downward.
[ZH] 完颜池挥手示意丫鬟离开，他独自一人走进一个屋子，刚走几步，千里寻的拳头出现在面前。
[EN] “When we seized Yanjing from the Liao, the Liao prince handed me the secret to this Under-Manor himself,” Wanyan Chi muttered. “Think I’d ever share it with a Song rat?”
[ZH] “赶紧放老娘出去！否则今日打得你跪地求饶！”
[EN] Descending the shaft, the bed lowered shut behind him without leaving a trace.
[ZH] 完颜池对付两拳后说：“你若乖乖跟我在一起，立刻放你出去。”
[EN] Though dim and lit throughout by oil lamps, the underground estate matched the rooms above in every detail.
[ZH] “想得美！老娘跟谁都不会跟你！”千里寻继续打去。
[EN] A maid bowed at his approach. “Young Prince.”
[ZH] 两人打了几个回合，完颜池处于下风，自知打不过，向后退去：“美人，别见我就打，要是打死我，更没人带你出去。”
[EN] “How is our beauty?”
[ZH] 千里寻收起拳头：“少这么称呼老娘！老娘有名有姓！”
[EN] “Hardly obedient. She keeps shouting to be let out.”
[ZH] “我知道，叫朱淑真，是位女词人。按说女词人应该温婉可人，你怎么跟个母老虎似的？”
[EN] Waving the maid away, Wanyan Chi stepped into the chamber—and met Qianli Xun’s fist flying straight at his nose.
[ZH] “对待你这种人，老娘只能开启母老虎模式。”
[EN] “Let me out right now, or I’ll beat you till you kneel and beg!”
[ZH] “这么说你也有温婉可人的一面？”
[EN] Parrying two blows, he smirked. “Stay nicely with me, and I’ll take you straight upstairs.”
[ZH] 面前的小王爷明显没有害她的意思，千里寻尽量缓和态度：“完颜池，你人长得不赖，在金国身份地位也不低，喜欢你的女人应该不少。我听丫鬟说你已娶王妃，身边还有好几个侧妃，又何必在我身上浪费时间？”
[EN] “In your dreams! I’d take anyone before you!”
[ZH] “本王对你一见钟情。”
[EN] After a few exchanges, clearly outmatched, Wanyan Chi stepped back. “Easy, beauty! Beat me to death and nobody opens the doors for you.”
[ZH] “哼！老娘对你一点儿都不钟情，再说老娘绝不会喜欢金人。”
[EN] Dropping her fists, she snapped, “Quit calling me ‘beauty’! I have a name!”
[ZH] “现在不喜欢不等于以后不会喜欢，本王有的是耐心。”
[EN] “I know—Zhu Shuzhen, a female ci poet. Poets are supposed to be gentle and sweet. How did you turn into such a tigress?”
[ZH] “老娘永远不会喜欢你。”
[EN] “For scum like you, tigress mode is the only setting I use.”
[ZH] “你喜欢大宋的仪王？”
[EN] “Meaning you *do* have a sweet, gentle side?”
[ZH] “没错！我就是喜欢他！”
[EN] Seeing no intent to kill her in his eyes, she eased her stance to reason with him. “Wanyan Chi, you’re decent-looking and hold high rank in Jin. Plenty of women throw themselves at you. Your maid told me you already have a primary consort and several secondary consorts. Why waste time on me?”
[ZH] 完颜池被激怒，一拳将旁边装饰的花瓶砸碎：“我得不到你，他也别想得到！”
[EN] “Because I fell for you at first sight.”
[ZH] “哈哈……！”千里寻一阵大笑，“他永远住在我心里，我也永远住在他的心里，谁也无法将我们拆散。”
[EN] “Tch! I feel zero attraction to you—and I would never fall for a Jin.”
[ZH] 完颜池一把卡住美人的脖子：“我要把他从里心里抹去。”
[EN] “Not now doesn’t mean never. I have all the patience in the world.”
[ZH] 千里寻脸上露出一抹冷笑：“妄想。”
[EN] “I will never like you.”
[ZH] 离得这么近，完颜池情不自禁向美人吻去，结果脸上重重挨了一拳，他松开卡住美人的手，退出几步。
[EN] “Do you like that Song Prince Yi?”
[ZH] “带刺的花本王更喜欢。”
[EN] “Yes! I like him!”
[ZH] 千里寻冷笑一声：“哼！迟早扎死你！”
[EN] Stung into rage, Wanyan Chi shattered a porcelain vase on a nearby stand with one punch. “If I can’t have you, neither will he!”
[ZH] 被打的脸上肿了起来，完颜池忍着面部的疼痛继续说：“你既然喜欢他，本王帮你检验一下他的真心。本王派人告诉他若能拿出一千万两银子本王就把你还给他。我倒要瞧瞧在他心里到底是一千万两银子重要还是你更重要。”
[EN] “Haha!” Qianli Xun laughed bright and defiant. “He lives in my heart forever, and I live in his. Nobody can tear us apart!”
[ZH] “无耻！”
[EN] Wanyan Chi lunged and clamped a hand round her throat. “I’ll scrub his name clean out of your heart!”
[ZH] “怎么？怕他嫌银子多放弃你？”
[EN] Her lips curved in a cold sneer. “Keep dreaming.”
[ZH] 千里寻还真有些担心感情在金钱面前毫无抵抗之力，很快她不再担心：“不妨告诉你，我巴不得他因一千万两银子放弃我。我已嫁人，他已娶妻，他的王妃还是我最好的朋友，他应该放弃救我，与梦容好好过日子。”
[EN] Drawn by her proximity, Wanyan Chi dipped down to kiss her—and took a thunderous punch square across the face that knocked him staggered backward.
[ZH] “本王想起来，你嫁到临安的陈家。你们之间的事本王不想知道，本王只想让你的心里没有他，很期待看到他两个月内拿不出一千万两或是主动放弃时，你会如何表现。”
[EN] “A rose with thorns,” he rasped, releasing her throat. “I like it even better.”
[ZH] 完颜池说完，转身离开。
[EN] “Watch those thorns prick you dead one day!”
[ZH] 千里寻悄悄跟上，她想偷偷观察完颜池如何离开地府。
[EN] Nursing a swelling cheek, Wanyan Chi shot back through his pain, “Since you love him so much, let me test his devotion for you. I sent word offering to trade you back for ten million taels of silver. Let’s see what weighs heavier in his heart—ten million taels or you.”
[ZH] 跟到出口，她悄悄躲起来，试着探出半个脑袋，发现完颜池从身上取出一支精巧的竹笛，吹了一曲，出口便自动打开。
[EN] “Shameless!”
[ZH] 千里寻想趁机冲出去，结果一堵墙自上而下挡在面前。
[EN] “Scared he’ll decide ten million taels is too steep and dump you?”
[ZH] 墙的另一边传来完颜池的声音：“本王虽打不过你，可你想出来没那么容易。”
[EN] A flicker of doubt crossed her mind before vanishing. “Truth be told, I *hope* he gives me up over ten million taels! I have a husband, and he has a wife—his consort happens to be my best friend. He ought to drop me and build a good life with Mengrong.”
[ZH] 千里寻听到出口被盖住的声音，很是无奈：“我刚出狱不久，又被关在地府，真是流年不利。”
[EN] “Ah, right—you married into the Chen family of Lin’an. Spare me your tangled history. All I want is him wiped out of your heart. I can’t wait to see your face when two months pass without a single silver note—or when he walks away on his own.”
[ZH] 丫鬟走过来：“姑娘，赶紧回屋吧。”
[EN] With that, Wanyan Chi turned and strode out.
[ZH] 千里寻没好气地说：“你整日待在地下，也不嫌闷？”
[EN] Stealthy on light feet, Qianli Xun shadowed him to the exit, peeking just half a head round a pillar in time to see him pull a slender bamboo flute from his sash, play a short tune, and watch the shaft swing open.
[ZH] 丫鬟说：“以前我在上面，姑娘来后，小王爷才派我到地府伺候。”
[EN] She bolted out to rush the opening—only for a solid stone wall to slam down from above right in her face!
[ZH] 千里边走边试探：“地府有没有其他出口？”
[EN] “I can’t beat your fists,” Wanyan Chi’s voice drifted from beyond the wall, “yet getting out isn’t so simple!”
[ZH] 丫鬟摇摇头。
[EN] Trapped as the ceiling sealed shut above, she sighed in exasperation. “Barely out of prison and straight into an underground vault. What rotten luck.”
[ZH] “是不知道还是没有？”
[EN] The maid hurried over urging her back to her room.
[ZH] “不知道。”
[EN] “Sitting buried underground all day—doesn’t it suffocate you?” Qianli Xun grumbled.
[ZH] 向丫鬟打听一点儿用处都没有，她决定待会儿趁丫鬟不注意时，再到处查探，说不定还会有其他出口。
[EN] “I worked upstairs before, Miss,” the maid answered. “His Highness sent me down only after you arrived.”
[ZH] 蔡靖回到府中，将完颜池的话告知仪王后，劝道：“下官觉得，天下任何女子都没有一千万两银子值钱，莫说仪王搞不来一千万两，即使能搞来，干什么都比买一个女子强。”
[EN] Walking back, Qianli Xun probed casually whether the Under-Manor had any other exit. The maid shook her head—she didn’t know. Deciding to search every wall the moment the maid looked away, Qianli Xun held her peace.
[ZH] 申简辰听出来，完颜池并不是真要一千万两，而是想将他一军。若他搞来银子，完颜池也绝不会将千里寻还给他；若搞不来，完颜池想在千里寻面前如何编排他都可以。
