[EN] Back at Cai Manor, Cai Jing relayed the ransom demand and counseled against it. No woman alive was worth ten million taels of silver—even if Prince Yi could raise such a sum, anything else made better sense than buying one woman back.
[ZH] 衡量片刻后，申简辰毫不犹豫地说：“辛苦蔡大人转告完颜池，两个月后一千万两按时送到。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen read the trap clear as day: Wanyan Chi never intended to release her, money or not; yet failing to try gave Wanyan Chi ammunition to slander him before her.
[ZH] 他不想再耽搁下去，与蔡靖辞别，带着白风离开。
[EN] Weighing it for a single heartbeat, Shen Jianchen answered without blinking. “Trouble yourself to carry word back to Wanyan Chi, Lord Cai: in two months, all ten million taels will arrive on time.”
[ZH] 蔡靖自语道：“仪王还是太年轻，把男女感情看得太重，以后有他后悔的时候。”
[EN] Refusing to waste another hour, he bade Cai Jing farewell and walked out with Bai Feng.
[ZH] 走出蔡府后，白风问：“王爷，我们去哪里搞钱？”
[EN] Watching him leave, Cai Jing shook his head. “Too young, valuing a woman’s heart above heaven and earth. He’ll regret it soon enough.”
[ZH] “回汴梁，把仪王府卖掉，再卖掉我名下所有田产和铺子，不相信凑不够一千万两。”
[EN] Outside the gate, Bai Feng gasped, “Where on earth do we get ten million taels, Your Highness?”
[ZH] “啊！王爷岂不成穷光蛋？”
[EN] “Back to Bianliang. Sell Prince Yi’s Manor, sell every acre of farmland and every shop in my name—I refuse to believe it won’t add up to ten million taels.”
[ZH] “钱财乃身外之物。”
[EN] “Then Your Highness becomes a pauper!”
[ZH] “王爷，要不到临安找陈家帮忙？陈家有钱。”
[EN] “Silver is just worldly dust.”
[ZH] “这是我的事，与陈家无关。”
[EN] “Why not ask the Chen family in Lin’an for help? They’re rolling in silver!”
[ZH] 白风不再说什么。
[EN] “This is my business. It has nothing to do with the Chen family.”
[ZH] ﻿仪王妃已死，又有一道圣旨，关键是陈公子已主动解除婚姻，从道理上来讲幽栖居士是王爷的人，自然与陈家无关。
[EN] Bai Feng held his tongue. Princess Consort Yi lay dead, an Imperial Decree stood written, and Young Master Chen had annulled his marriage. By every right, Youqi Jushi belonged to his prince—never the Chen family.

#CH 339
[ZH] 回汴梁途中，一路都是逃亡的百姓。
[ZH] 申简辰仔细推算时间，完颜宗望正带人攻打河北，再过三个月会渡过黄河直逼汴梁。
[ZH] 他必须快马加鞭，赶在金军攻打到汴梁前快些卖掉所有财产。
[ZH] 申简辰与白风日夜兼程，回到汴梁时，金人还未到，消息早已传遍汴京城，城中百姓人心惶惶，有条件的都向南逃亡。
[ZH] 朝廷不允许城中的达官贵人离开汴梁，也不断派兵支援黄河以北。
[ZH] 申简辰回到仪王府，向邹嬷嬷说了梦容和喜兰之事，邹嬷嬷好一阵伤心。伤心过后她十分支持王爷的做法，告诉他放开手做自己想做的事。
[ZH] 申简辰开始售卖宅子和名下所有财产，局势不稳的状况下，出售起来不容易，一个月过去无一售出。
[ZH] 战事紧张，官家及朝臣自顾无暇，没有心情理会一个王爷卖家产之事，对申简辰来讲再好不过。
[ZH] 幸运的是，汴梁下起第一场雪时，有人上门要买仪王府，此人正是魏夫人。
[ZH] 魏夫人听说仪王是为救人，救的还是幽栖居士，便不吝钱财，没有讲一分一毫价，买下王府。
[ZH] 此后又有许多人买下其他产业，一打听才知都是魏夫人介绍的人，申简辰感激不尽。
[ZH] 银两凑够后，吩咐白风保护邹嬷嬷回临安，先暂住在竹屋里；他则带着一千万两银票开拔燕京。
[ZH] 一路上战事愈加紧张，几经辗转终于到达燕京。
[ZH] 燕京城的百姓已能走出家门，其中不乏金国百姓的身影。如此看来金人应采取了相应政策，允许百姓恢复正常生活。
[ZH] 街头人影穿梭，可战乱时期的热闹根本无法涤荡留存在人内心的凄凉，加上冬季的寒冷，街上每个人都蜷缩着，像现在的大宋一般蜷缩着，努力抵抗正在延续的战事。
[ZH] 申简辰顾不上感慨太多，他找到蔡靖，请他向完颜池禀明一千万两已筹够，劝他快些放人。
[ZH] 蔡靖惊叹之余来到王府，向完颜池禀明情况。
[ZH] 完颜池心中自语：“她在他心中竟如此重要！若是我，可愿用一千万两换一个女人？”
[ZH] 完颜池自愧不如，嫉妒之心使得他根本不愿将美人拱手相让。可一千万两又极富吸引力，已送上门不要白不要。
[ZH] “让他明日来王府，一手交钱，一手交人。”
[ZH] 蔡靖根本猜度不出完颜池的心思，以为对方真的要放人，顺口夸道：“小王爷比大宋的仪王明智许多，人怎么能跟钱比？何况还是一千万两？下官这就回去给大宋的仪王传话。”
[ZH] 第二日，完颜池安排妥当后，在正厅接见仪王，看到地上放着一个很大的木箱，面露满意之色。
[ZH] “你果真筹到一千万两？”
[ZH] 申简辰打开木箱：“都在此，请小王爷过目，还望小王爷尽快将人放出来。”
[ZH] 里面整整齐齐放着厚厚的好几摞银票，看得完颜池双目放光：“你真舍得把这么多钱送给本王？”
[ZH] 申简辰强调道：“不是送，是换人。”
[ZH] “看来你比本王更爱她。”完颜池端起茶盏，“只可惜她昨晚已死，不过是想让她陪本王睡一晚，谁知她用匕首捅在自己的要害，当场毙命，本王为此伤心了好一阵。”
[ZH] 申简辰根本不相信：“你骗我！”
[ZH] “美人与银子比起来本王更爱银子，何况还是一千万两？”完颜池将手中的茶盏扔在地上，“哐当”一声，茶盏摔成两半。
[ZH] 早已藏好的金兵听到响声，手举刀剑攻击而来。
[ZH] 对方不想给人，还想讹钱财，今日激战无法避免，申简辰立刻出拳迎战。
[ZH] 完颜池手下的人根本不是申简辰的对手，他挥舞拳脚打了一刻钟后，都成为他的手下败将。
[ZH] 完颜池早已预料到这一点，他还有其他准备。
[ZH] 仪王按照预想的向他冲来，一拳将他从座位上打倒，抓住他胸前的衣裳，用近似命令的口吻说：“把她还给我！”
[ZH] “本王刚说过，她已死。”
[ZH] 申简辰一拳打在完颜池的脸上，再次上前抓住他：“告诉我，她还活着！”
[ZH] 完颜池的一半脸被打得乌青，为了让仪王因痛苦分散注意力，继续瞎编：“本王跟你不一样，钱和美人两相对比更爱钱，一千万两银票在此，本王又何必不交出美人？是实在交不出来，你若想找她，去城外的乱葬岗，昨晚我已让人把她的尸体抬去扔在那里。”
[ZH] 对方说的像真的一样，申简辰依然难以相信，却也忍不住难过起来。
[ZH] 完颜池趁机向金兵使眼色，只见一人手执利剑迅速刺来。
[ZH] 完颜池则继续用话语分散仪王的注意力：“关押她这段日子，本王算是看出来，她虽嫁到临安陈家，心里却一直想着一个人？你想知道此人是谁吗？”
[ZH] 申简辰用悲痛的声音问：“是谁？”
[ZH] 完颜池回道：“正是你。”
[EN] All along the road back to Bianliang, streams of refugees fled southward. Calculating the days, Shen Jianchen reckoned Wanyan Zongwang’s armies sweeping through Hebei would cross the Yellow River within three months to hammer the capital itself. Every day counted if he meant to sell off his holdings before war closed in.
[ZH] 听到他人肯定的转述，泪水夺眶而出，申简辰悲痛地抓紧完颜池：“既然她因你而死，我今日杀了你替她报仇。”
[EN] Riding day and night with Bai Feng, they reached Bianliang ahead of the Jin vanguard, though panic already gripped the Eastern Capital as wealthy households escaped south. Court bans barred noble officials from leaving while fresh regiments marched north toward the Yellow River.
[ZH] 申简辰的拳头还未砸到完颜池的要害时，身后中了一剑，鲜血直流，他倒在地上晕死过去。
[EN] Back at Prince Yi’s Manor, Shen Jianchen broke the news of Mengrong and Xilan’s deaths to Matron Zou. After weeping through her grief, Matron Zou backed him without reserve, urging him to follow his heart.
[ZH] 完颜池站直，整理好衣裳，很不屑地盯着躺在地上的仪王：“比我厉害又能如何？还不是得不到美人又折了银子？来人！把他拖出去扔到城外！”
[EN] Putting the manor and every estate on the block proved brutal during wartime panic; an entire month dragged past without a single buyer. Busy scrambling against invasion, neither Emperor nor ministers cared what an idle prince sold off—one small mercy.
[ZH] 两名金兵上前将受伤的仪王拖了出去……
[EN] At last, as winter’s first snow drifted over Bianliang, a buyer knocked at the manor gates—Lady Wei! Learning Prince Yi was liquidating everything to ransom Youqi Jushi, she bought Prince Yi’s Manor on the spot without bargaining down a single copper. Soon other buyers snapped up his lands and shops too, every last one sent by Lady Wei, leaving Shen Jianchen overwhelmed with gratitude.
[ZH] 申简辰醒来时，发现自己躺在一家农户的屋内，伤口被处理过。蔡靖身着普通衣衫站在一旁，应该是他救了自己。
[EN] Once every note of the ten million taels lay packed, he ordered Bai Feng to escort Matron Zou safely back to Lin’an and wait at his bamboo house. Alone, carrying ten million taels in silver notes, Shen Jianchen rode straight north into worsening war toward Yanjing.
[ZH] 蔡靖见仪王醒来，上前说：“终于醒了，说明殿下已无生命危险。殿下暂且住在这里养伤，等伤好后，快些回汴梁。”
[EN] By twists and back roads, he reached Yanjing at last. Jin decrees had coaxed locals back onto the streets alongside Jin civilians, restoring surface commerce—yet winter’s biting wind and silent dread hung over everyone huddled in thick coats, much like Great Song itself braced against ruin.
[ZH] 申简辰问：“蔡大人，麻烦你帮我打听一下，她是不是真的……。”
[EN] Waste no breath on sorrow, Shen Jianchen sought out Cai Jing to notify Wanyan Chi that all ten million taels waited ready.
[ZH] 蔡靖以为仪王因一个女人搞得钱财丢失，还差点儿丧命，实在不值。不如欺骗他一回，好让仪王死心，早些离开。
[EN] Dazzled, Cai Jing hurried to the royal manor to report. Hearing it, Wanyan Chi thought jealously of how precious she must be to the Song prince—would he himself trade ten million taels for one woman? Pride and envy burned away any thought of surrendering her, yet ten million taels delivered to his doorstep was too sweet to turn down.
[ZH] “下官私下向王府的侍卫打听过，那姑娘的确已死，尸首被丢在乱葬岗，我派人帮殿下找过一回，的确找到尸首，下官自作主张将人安葬在城外的荒地里。”
[EN] “Tell him to come tomorrow,” Wanyan Chi smirked. “Silver in one hand, the person in the other.”
[ZH] 本有的希望被蔡靖的一席话浇灭，申简辰失声痛哭：“呜呜呜……！”
[EN] Unable to read his mind and convinced a release was set, Cai Jing flattered him on choosing ten million taels over one woman, and hurried back to tell Shen Jianchen.
[ZH] 哭声中既有不舍又有对自己的埋怨，听得蔡靖差点将实情说出。
[EN] The next day, after setting his trap, Wanyan Chi received Prince Yi in the main hall. At the sight of the massive wooden chest set down on the floor, his eyes shone. “You actually raised ten million taels?”
[ZH] 申简辰哭够后努力爬起：“我要杀了完颜池为她报仇！”
[EN] Throwing back the lid, Shen Jianchen gestured inside. “Every tael is here. Inspect them—and release her now.”
[ZH] 因身上的伤太重，他又不得不倒下。
[EN] Stacks upon neat stacks of thick silver notes gleamed inside, making Wanyan Chi’s breath catch. “You’d hand all this over to me without blinking?”
[ZH] 蔡靖劝道：“完颜池为防止殿下前去寻事，加强戒备，王府周围不仅增加一成兵力，还设下天罗地网，只怕前去性命不保。”
[EN] “Not handing it over. Buying her freedom.”
[ZH] 申简辰继续落泪：“她已不在，我活着有何意义？”
[EN] “You love her even deeper than I thought.” Wanyan Chi raised a tea bowl and sipped unhurried. “What a pity—she died last night. All I asked was one night in my bed, and she drove a dagger straight into her heart and dropped dead on the spot. Broke my heart all morning.”
[ZH] 蔡靖仍旧好心劝说：“殿下能活过来已是上天恩赐，千万不能轻生，若真去送死，恐怕那姑娘在天之灵也不好受。依我看，您养好伤去坟头看看她，就赶快回汴梁，此地不宜久留。”
[EN] “Lies!”
[ZH] “你把她葬在哪里？现在就带我去。”申简辰再次艰难地爬起，硬撑着没有倒下。
[EN] “Beauties or silver, I prefer silver—especially ten million taels!” Wanyan Chi smashed the porcelain bowl against the flagstones with a loud crash.
[ZH] 蔡靖见仪王态度坚决，显然已将个人的身体置之脑后，哪怕即刻死了都心甘情愿。他不再劝说，吩咐随身的下人将仪王扶到外面的马车上。
[EN] On that cue, armed Jin soldiers poured out from behind screens, swords raised.
[ZH] 新坟是蔡靖安排人伪造的，他也是一番好心。仪王因为一个女人搞得人财两空，他不希望好好的王爷就此沉迷下去。
[EN] Realizing Wanyan Chi meant to rob him blind and keep her hidden, Shen Jianchen met the steel with bare fists. These manor guards were no match for him; within fifteen minutes, every attacker lay groaning across the floor.
[ZH] 来到新坟旁，光秃秃的一个土堆，显然是刚刚添上去的新土。申简辰已受不了，双腿一软，人向下倒去。
[EN] Just as Wanyan Chi braced for his contingency, Shen Jianchen bolted across the hall, punched him out of his chair, and hauled him upright by his lapels. “Give her back to me!”
[ZH] 蔡靖带的两个下人一左一右扶好仪王，他才没倒在地上。此时的他哭不出声来，任由泪水不断往下掉，一步一步走向坟头。
[EN] “I told you already. She’s dead.”
[ZH] “放开……我。”
[EN] A brutal punch flattened Wanyan Chi’s cheek purple before Shen Jianchen dragged him closer. “Tell me she’s alive!”
[ZH] 两个下人用目光向蔡靖请示，蔡靖点点头。
[EN] Using the lie to distract him while waiting for his hidden striker, Wanyan Chi spat through swollen lips, “Silver beats beauty any day—if I had her alive, why wouldn’t I trade her for ten million taels right here? Look at the mass burial mound outside the city if you don’t believe me. I had her corpse tossed there last night!”
[ZH] 下人松开手后，申简辰因身心失去力气倒在坟头，大声痛哭：“呜呜……！”
[EN] Despite how convincing it sounded, Shen Jianchen refused to swallow it—yet dread clutched his throat. Catching the eye of a lurking Jin soldier raising a sword behind the prince’s back, Wanyan Chi twisted the knife deeper.
[ZH] 蔡靖心想：“哭吧，哭够了，赶紧离开，保命要紧。”
[EN] “Locking her up all these months taught me one thing, though. Married into the Chen family of Lin’an, yet every day her heart pined for one man alone. Want to know who?”
[ZH] 申简辰哭到眼泪流不出时才作罢，他的情绪也稳定许多：“请蔡大人派人把她的尸身挖出来，装殓好后我要亲自带回临安。”
[EN] Pain tightened Shen Jianchen’s chest. “Who?”
[ZH] 本就是一座空坟，蔡靖哪敢挖？还需继续编瞎话：“万万不可！人离世后需尽快入土为安，既然已入土哪有立刻挖出来的道理？即使要挖也要等七七四十九日后再说……”
[EN] “You.”
[ZH] “为何？”
[EN] Tears spilled down Shen Jianchen’s face at that confirmation. Clenching Wanyan Chi’s collar tighter, he swore, “If she died because of you, I’ll kill you right here to avenge her!”
[ZH] “人死亡后有中阴身，到处游荡，等待七七四十九日后投胎，若时候未到，将尸身挖出必然会使中阴身受到影响。再说尸身化为白骨也需三四个月，若仪王想将尸骨带回临安还是等三四个月后再说。”
[EN] Before his fist struck bone, steel pierced deep into Shen Jianchen’s back. Blood gushed across his robes, and he pitched forward unconscious.
[ZH] 听到中阴身之说，申简辰想起穿越之事，他担心千里寻离开回到他们来的那个时代。既然七七四十九日后投胎，他要尽快回临安找神柳。
[EN] Straightening his tunic, Wanyan Chi looked down in contempt. “So what if your fists beat mine? Still lost the girl and lost your silver! Guards—drag him out and dump him beyond the city gates!”
[ZH] 申简辰爬起来，坐在地上：“麻烦蔡大人帮我找辆马车送我回临安。”
[EN] When Shen Jianchen drifted back to consciousness, he lay inside a peasant cottage with bound wounds, Cai Jing standing beside the bed in plain commoner clothes.
[ZH] 仪王要离开，蔡靖满心欢喜，可他身上有重伤，不易路途颠簸：“要不殿下等身上的伤好后再离开？”
[EN] “Thank Heaven you woke,” Cai Jing breathed. “Your life is out of danger. Stay here and heal, then hurry back to Bianliang.”
[ZH] “我必须现在离开。”仪王的声音不大，却很坚定。
[EN] “Lord Cai… find out for me… is she…”
[ZH] 在蔡靖的安排下，申简辰只身一人乘坐马车，一路颠簸驶向临安。他随身携带疗伤的药物，需要换药时，均由车夫帮忙。有药护体，身上的伤口逐渐愈合。
[EN] Thinking the prince had ruined his fortune and nearly lost his life over one woman—and hoping to break his fixation so he’d flee south alive—Cai Jing lied smoothly. “I asked manor guards in secret. She really died, tossed onto the mass burial mound. My men searched till they found her body, and I took the liberty of burying her in waste ground outside the city.”
[ZH] ……
[EN] That lie snuffed out the last spark in Shen Jianchen’s chest. Tears burst forth in ragged wails mixed with self-blame, almost moving Cai Jing to blurt the truth—until Shen Jianchen dragged himself upright swearing to kill Wanyan Chi, collapsing right back onto the bed from agony.
[ZH] 在路上大约走了将近一个月，终于到达临安。申简辰并未去找亲朋好友，直奔神柳而去。
[EN] “Wanyan Chi doubled his guards around the manor and laid traps everywhere waiting for you,” Cai Jing pleaded. “Going back is suicide! Living through that blade was Heaven’s grace. Even her spirit in heaven would weep to see you throw your life away. Visit her grave once you heal, then leave Yanjing at once.”
[ZH] 早已入冬，神柳的枝丫变得光秃秃，看上去毫无生气，就像申简辰的心情一般。他踩着地上的枯草走向神柳，回忆着他与千里寻在这个时代相遇相处的情景。
[EN] “Where did you bury her? Take me there right now.” Propping himself upright through sheer will, Shen Jianchen refused to budge. Seeing him past persuasion, Cai Jing bade two servants help him out to a carriage.
[ZH] 当他走到神柳跟前时，双眼已完全湿润：“两年多我和她没来看你，你可好？”
[EN] Out at the fresh mound Cai Jing had ordered piled up on empty ground, raw yellow earth offered no tombstone. Shen Jianchen’s legs gave out; two servants caught his arms before he hit the dirt. Too broken for sound, silent tears streamed down his face as he staggered forward and waved them back, collapsing face-down onto the bare soil in bitter sobs until no tears remained.
[ZH] 神柳默不作声。
[EN] Exhausted and steadied, he sat up on the grass. “Have your men dig up her body and coffin it. I’m taking her home to Lin’an myself.”
[ZH] “她死了，你知道吗？既然是你把我和她带来，能不能再次把她带到我面前？”
[EN] Terrified his empty grave would be exposed, Cai Jing invented a rule on the spot: opening a fresh grave violated the dead—if he must move her, wait forty-nine days!
[ZH] 神柳依然默不作声。
[EN] “Why?”
[ZH] “你为什么不说话？”申简辰大声质问，他靠近神柳粗壮的树干，紧紧抱住，“我求求你，趁七七四十九日还未到，你把她带到我身边来，若她能再次活过来，我愿用仪王的身份，我拥有的财富，甚至我的性命来换。”
[EN] “After death, the intermediate spirit wanders forty-nine days before seeking rebirth,” Cai Jing improvised. “Dig up the body early, and you disturb her spirit’s crossing. Besides, flesh takes three or four months to turn to clean bone.”
[ZH] 神柳还是默不作声。
[EN] Hearing “forty-nine days” and “rebirth,” Shen Jianchen panicked that Qianli Xun’s soul might pass back through the Divine Willow into their modern world! Sitting up sharp, he demanded a carriage straight to Lin’an right now—ignoring Cai Jing’s warning about bumpy roads tearing open his stitches.
[ZH] “难道只有我死了，才能再次找到她？”
[EN] Over nearly a month of grueling travel south, the driver helped change dressings along the way until his wounds closed, and at last Shen Jianchen stepped back into Lin’an.
[ZH] 神柳仿佛没听见一般。
[EN] Skipping friends and manor alike, he walked straight to the Divine Willow—stripped bare by deep winter, lifeless as his own heart. Treading dead grass to its thick trunk, eyes brimming, he murmured, “Two years since we came to see you. How have you been?”
[ZH] “你不说话就是默认，既然你同意，今日我便死在你面前，希望你能带我见到她。”
[EN] Only wind answered.
[ZH] 申简辰放开神柳，握紧拳头，对准自己的胸口：“等着我，很快我就能见到你。”
[EN] “She’s dead—did you know? You brought us both here. Can’t you bring her back to me one more time?”
[ZH] 他将全身的力气汇聚到拳心，朝自己的胸口冲击而去……
[EN] The old willow stood mute.
[ZH] “施主千万不可轻生！自杀之人难入轮回！”
[EN] “Why won’t you answer?!” Wrapping both arms tight round the rough bark, he pleaded aloud. “Forty-nine days aren’t up yet! Give her back to me! Bring her back alive, and you can have my royal title, every copper left to my name—even my life!”
[ZH] 拳头还未撞到胸口，身后传来净光法师的声音。
[EN] Silence.
[ZH] 听到自杀难入轮回，申简辰的拳头停在半空：“法师莫要骗我。”
[EN] “Is my death the only way to find her again? Your silence means yes!” Stepping back, Shen Jianchen drew back his fist and gathered every ounce of strength to smash his own chest. “Wait for me—I’ll see you soon!”
[ZH] “阿弥陀佛，修行之人不打妄语。”
[EN] “Stop, benefactor! Never throw away your life—one who takes his own life cannot enter the cycle of rebirth!”
[ZH] “可我若不死，又怎会见到她？”
[EN] Frozen with his fist inches from his ribs, Shen Jianchen spun round to see Master Jingguang standing behind him.
[ZH] “施主死了就一定能见到想见之人？”
[EN] “Don’t lie to me, Master!”
[ZH] “起码我不用忍受独自活着的痛苦。”
[EN] “Amitabha. Monks speak no falsehood.”
[ZH] “我佛认为人身难得，是修行的重要法器，若自行了断，会造下新的恶业，旧业未除，又添新业，只怕生生世世难以解脱。”
[EN] “If I live, how do I ever see her again?”
[ZH] “我虽尊重佛法，可法师所说难以令人信服。”
[EN] “And does dying guarantee you meet the one you seek?”
[ZH] “刚刚我在一旁无意听到施主自语，你之所以轻生，不过是因心中之人离你而去，你妄想自我了断前去追随。施主的想法甚是可笑，先不说你为自己平添新的恶业，这恶业带来的恶果会令已死之人不得安息……”
[EN] “At least I won’t endure living alone in agony!”
[ZH] 申简辰放下拳头，转过身，湿润的双眼中充满询问：“为何会令她不得安息？”
[EN] “Our Buddha teaches how rare a human birth is—our precious vessel for spiritual practice,” Master Jingguang answered evenly. “Destroy it by your own hand, and you forge heavy fresh karma atop old debts, trapping yourself across lifetimes without release.”
[ZH] 净光法师见对方听进去，耐心开解：“若无她死，便无你亡，她是因你是果，因果循环互相牵绊，你轻视法身所造恶业，她必然会遭受恶业反噬。你口口声声说是为她，不过是怕自己活着痛苦而已，说到底你很自私，只考虑自己痛快，殊不知你的行为会为她在另一个世界带来劫难。”
[EN] “I respect the Dharma, yet that brings me no comfort.”
[ZH] 净光法师的话如晨钟暮鼓，申简辰幡然醒悟。
[EN] “Unwittingly I overheard your words. You seek death solely to chase after one who departed—yet how selfish that wish is! Leaving aside the dark karma you heap upon yourself, its backlash falls on her too, leaving the dead no rest!”
[ZH] 他想起曾执着于对千里寻感情前去抢亲，给她和自己的生活造成极大困扰，最终导致她入狱将近两年，如此看来法师所说甚有道理。
[EN] Lowering his fist, Shen Jianchen stared through wet eyes. “Why would my death keep her from resting?”
[ZH] 申简辰行礼道：“法师一番言论振聋发聩，令我如梦初醒。从此后我一定好好活着，不是为自己而是为她。”
[EN] “Without her death, there would be no suicide of yours. She is cause, and you are effect; karma binds you both. Desecrate your living vessel, and the foul fruit rebounds onto her soul. You call it devotion, yet you simply flee your own pain—caring only for quick release while bringing torment upon her in the world beyond.”
[ZH] 净光法师赞道：“施主乃有福之人才能听懂我一番劝解，看在施主慧根深重的份上我多说两句。”
[EN] Like morning bells shattering sleep, Master Jingguang’s words struck Shen Jianchen awake. Remembering how clinging to her had sparked his wedding crash and landed her in prison for two years, he saw the truth clear at last.
[ZH] “法师请讲。”
[EN] Bowing deep, Shen Jianchen swore he would live on—not for himself, yet for her.
[ZH] “施主既然放不下心中之人，不妨为她积德行善，既可安活着人之心，亦可为死去之人积累功德，她在另一个世界也会受益匪浅。”
[EN] Pleased by his sudden clarity, Master Jingguang offered one parting counsel: since he could not put her out of mind, do good deeds and build merit in her name, bringing peace to the living and blessings to the dead in the world beyond.
[ZH] 申简辰的双眼再次湿润，他终于找到在没有千里寻的日子里度过余生的方法，他决定以千里寻的名义行善，让别人都来赞美她，这样他会觉得她并未离他而去。
[EN] Warm tears filled Shen Jianchen’s eyes once more; at last he had a path through the years ahead without her—living in her name until the world kept speaking of her as if she stood right beside him.
[ZH] “感谢法师。”
[EN] “Thank you, Master.”
[ZH] “阿弥陀佛！”净光法师露出慈祥的笑容，转身离开。
[EN] “Amitabha.” With a gentle smile, Master Jingguang turned away down the hill.

#CH 340
[ZH] 净光法师一番开解，申简辰没有了轻生的念头，他带着悲伤来到陈府外。
[ZH] 陈浩宇听下人来报仪王找他，人就站在门外，他带着愤怒和不安匆忙走出陈府，顾不上仪王一副落寞失魂的样子，上前就是一拳：“我听白风说我娘子在燕京被金人抓走，都怨你！谁让你把她带去燕京？”
[ZH] 申简辰并不还手，任由陈浩宇打，又是几拳落在他身上。
[ZH] 陈浩宇抓起仪王的衣领：“白风说你去救她，人呢？为何只有你一人回来？”
[ZH] “她……死了。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇一把推开仪王：“你胡说！”
[ZH] “她被葬在燕京城外，我亲眼见过……”
[ZH] “你胡说！一定是你不想将她还给我，才骗我！”
[ZH] “我没骗你，我比谁都希望她活着。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇见仪王双眼红肿，情绪低沉，原来的一张俊脸仿佛一下子苍老了许多，不得不相信他所说，举起的拳头再也打不下去，蹲在地上哭起来：“呜呜……！”
[ZH] 哭了一会儿，陈浩宇起身往回走，边走边说：“我回去准备一下，亲自去趟燕京，把娘子的尸骨带回临安，葬在陈家的墓地……”
[ZH] “她已不是陈家人，你没有资格带回她的尸骨。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇停下脚步转过身，迅速上前，再次抓住仪王的衣领：“我是被圣旨所逼不得已解除婚姻，在我心里她永远是我娘子！”
[ZH] “不管你心里怎么想，她已是我的人。”
[ZH] “什么意思？难道你已经把圣旨的事告诉她？”
[ZH] 申简辰希望死后的千里寻只属于他一人：“是的，我已告诉她。她如释重负，对我说，因有婚姻，她不得不一再委屈跟着你，婚姻没了，终于可以和喜欢的人在一起。她是心甘情愿跟我去燕京，而不是回临安找你。”
[ZH] “你骗我！告诉你，金军正在南下，很快会攻打汴梁，朝堂上那位还不知会如何，到时圣旨作废，她照样是我娘子！”
[ZH] “无论官家如何，都晚了，因为……因为在燕京时，我已与她圆房……”
[ZH] 话还未说完，陈浩宇一拳打在仪王的脸上，本就因痛苦而沧桑的脸上又多出一处瘀青。
[ZH] “你胡说！你胡说！”陈浩宇的声音近似歇斯底里。
[ZH] “该说的话我已说，你比我幸运，起码她生前陪过你一段时日，而我与她单独相处的时间太短。你竟然还不知足？还要在她死后跟我抢？你不觉得我很可怜吗？”申简辰的眼角有泪水滚落下来。
[ZH] 陈浩宇被触动，不想再与仪王争论什么，转身像霜打的茄子一般向陈府内走。
[ZH] 到门口时，他停住脚步，背对着仪王说：“我不再跟你抢，抢又有何用？她又不会活过来。”
[ZH] 直到陈浩宇落寞的背影消失在眼前，申简辰才想到离开。该去哪里？他忽然觉得人生失去方向。以前有千里寻的地方便有人生，现在她不在，方向没了，前面的路不知该如何走？
[ZH] 汴梁的王府已变卖，他不会再回去，过不了多久，汴梁也会被金人霸占。想到幽林别居，虽在那里住过，可幽林别居依然不是他的家。
[ZH] 唯有竹屋可称作家，从萌生建造竹屋的想法时，那竹屋便是为他和千里寻建造的。
[ZH] 申简辰以徒步的方式向城外走，没有了心爱之人，他何必追赶时间？时间需慢慢消磨，消磨的过程可排遣掉内心空落落的不适感。
[ZH] 刚走出城外，一辆马车停在面前，乔文仙从车上下来。
[ZH] “听说你回来，正准备去找你。”
[ZH] “你怎知我回来？”
[EN] Stepping away from Master Jingguang’s counsel with suicide put behind him, Shen Jianchen walked heavy-hearted to the gates of Chen Manor.
[ZH] “幽林别居的下人在街上看到你，说你往陈府方向去。我准备去陈府找你，还好没白跑，半路上遇见。”
[EN] Hearing Prince Yi waited outside, Chen Haoyu rushed out full of dread and rage. Barely glancing at the prince’s gaunt, haunted face, Haoyu swung a fist straight at him. “Bai Feng told me my wife was captured by Jin troops in Yanjing! It’s all your fault—who told you to take her there?!”
[ZH] 面对文渊，申简辰不想绷着，整个人松懈下来，上前抱住文渊，哭起来：“呜呜！文渊，她没了，我永远不可能再见到她，呜呜……！”
[EN] Shen Jianchen stood still and took blow after blow without raising a hand. Haoyu grabbed his lapels and shook him hard. “Bai Feng said you went to save her! Where is she? Why did you come back alone?!”
[ZH] 文渊一边安慰一边说：“看到你的样子我已猜出是这等结果，要是我当初不顾家人反对带她远走他乡也不会有今日之事。别难过，随我回幽林别居。”
[EN] “She… is dead.”
[ZH] 自仪王与仪王妃回汴梁后，乔文仙携带一妻一妾住进幽林别居。不久苗知瑜生下一个儿子，夏果生下一个女儿，两个孩子均两岁多，已经能够满地跑。
[EN] Haoyu shoved him backward. “Liar!”
[ZH] 乔家的长辈见儿子儿女双全甚是满意，更庆幸当年没有选择朱家姑娘，否则今日跟着揪心的会是乔家。
[EN] “She lies buried outside Yanjing’s walls. I saw her grave with my own eyes.”
[ZH] 乔文仙不这么想，他的心里挂念着淑真，希望她一切顺利。
[EN] “You’re lying just to keep her from me!”
[ZH] 来到幽林别居，沐浴后，换上文渊准备的衣裳，简单用过饭后，整个人精神焕发许多，可申简辰的眼睛里依然写着“落寞”二字。
[EN] “I didn’t lie. Nobody wanted her alive more than I.”
[ZH] 乔文仙没有让苗知瑜和夏果以及孩子前来拜见仪王，他情绪不佳，看到自己儿女双全肯定会更难过，等缓过几日再说。
[EN] Seeing Prince Yi’s swollen red eyes, sunken cheeks, and face aged years overnight, Haoyu felt his raised fist go limp. Slowly crouching onto the flagstones, he buried his face and wept aloud.
[ZH] 两人在屋内的桌边对面而坐，一旁的火盆烧得很旺，屋里暖烘烘的。
[EN] After a long moment, Haoyu pushed himself up and turned back toward the gate. “I’ll pack right now and ride to Yanjing myself to bring my wife’s bones home to the Chen family graveyard.”
[ZH] 从燕京一路走来体验过的寒冷在这一刻已被暖意驱散。身体的寒冷是没了，心里的却还在。
[EN] “She belongs to the Chen family no more. You have no right to fetch her bones.”
[ZH] 乔文仙为仪王斟满酒酿，也为自己满上：“白风带邹嬷嬷回到临安时听他说，你把汴梁的王府和名下的财产全部变卖才凑够银两，唉！我自愧不如。”
[EN] Whirling back, Haoyu seized Shen Jianchen’s collar again. “I only dissolved our marriage under an Imperial Decree! In my heart, she is my wife forever!”
[ZH] “没有她要钱财何用？”申简辰端起酒杯一饮而尽，“现在她没了，钱财对我来讲更是毫无意义。”
[EN] “No matter what’s in your heart, she became mine.”
[ZH] “你我两年多没见，很多事情发生变化，朝廷眼看着大势已去，只怕天下要沦为金人的。”
[EN] “What do you mean? Did you tell her about the decree?!”
[ZH] “金人没有资格夺得天下，最多与大宋分庭抗争，最终照样被灭，只可惜你我等不到那一日。”
[EN] Wanting Qianli Xun to belong solely to him even in death, Shen Jianchen lied straight to his face. “Yes. I told her. Relief washed over her—she confessed she had only suffered staying with you out of marital duty, and once free, she could finally be with the man she truly loved. She followed me to Yanjing of her own accord, never intending to come back to Lin’an for you.”
[ZH] “你怎知？”
[EN] “You’re lying! Jin armies are marching south on Bianliang right now—who knows what becomes of the man on that throne? Once that decree turns into scrap paper, she’s my wife all over again!”
[ZH] 申简辰露出难得的笑容：“猜的。”
[EN] “No matter what becomes of His Majesty, it’s too late. In Yanjing… she and I had already consummated our union.”
[ZH] 乔文仙端起酒杯：“不管你猜的是对是错，反正猜到我心里，既然那一日你我等不到，不如现在与我干了这杯，提前为那一日到来庆贺。”
[EN] Before the final word fell, Haoyu cracked a fist across Shen Jianchen’s jaw, painting fresh purple over his weary face. “Liar! Liar!” Haoyu’s voice broke near hysteria.
[ZH] 申简辰为自己斟满，端起酒杯与文渊干了。
[EN] “I’ve said my piece,” Shen Jianchen whispered as tears spilled down his own cheeks. “You’re luckier than me—at least you had her beside you while she lived. My time alone with her was far too brief. Still not satisfied? Still fighting me for her even after death? Don’t you feel even a shred of pity for me?”
[ZH] 乔文仙放下酒杯问道：“往后你如何打算？”
[EN] Stung silent, Haoyu dropped his fists and trudged back through Chen Manor’s gates like frostbitten stalks. Stopping just across the threshold with his back turned, he muttered, “I won’t fight you anymore. What’s the use fighting when she can’t come back alive?”
[ZH] 申简辰沉默一会儿说：“我会回到属于我和她的竹屋，在那里了此残生。”
[EN] Only after Haoyu’s slumped back vanished inside did Shen Jianchen turn away—suddenly adrift with nowhere to go. His Bianliang manor was sold, Secluded Forest Villa belonged to another, and soon Bianliang might fall to Jin hands anyway. Only the bamboo house outside town felt like home—every bamboo beam built for him and Qianli Xun.
[ZH] 想起净光法师的话，又强调道：“我会以她的名义行善积德，当大家说起她时，仿佛她一直在我身边。”
[EN] Walking out past the city gates with nowhere to hurry, letting empty miles eat away the hollow ache inside, he spotted a carriage stopping ahead—and Qiao Wenxian stepped down.
[ZH] 乔文仙很感动：“算我一个好吗？”
[EN] “I heard you returned and was just coming to find you.”
[ZH] “你已夺走她的初恋，这等事还要来跟我抢？”
[EN] “How did you know?”
[ZH] “夺走她初恋的人不是我，是徐公子。”
[EN] “Servants from Secluded Forest Villa spotted you heading toward Chen Manor. I drove straight there and caught you on the road.”
[ZH] “在我看来就是你。”
[EN] Before Wenyuan, Shen Jianchen dropped all pretense and collapsed onto his friend’s shoulder sobbing aloud. “Wenyuan… she’s gone! I’ll never see her again!”
[ZH] “好吧，我不跟你抢。”
[EN] Clapping his shoulder gently, Wenxian blamed himself for not eloping with her back then, then guided the prince back to Secluded Forest Villa.
[ZH] “现在多好，既没有人跟我抢，梦容也不会前来阻拦，我可以毫无顾忌跟她在一起，可她……”申简辰说着又流下泪水。
[EN] Since Prince Yi had returned to Bianliang two years ago, Wenxian had moved into the villa with his wife and concubine. Miao Zhiyu had borne him a son and Xiaguo a daughter—both now lively toddlers past two racing across the yards. Elder Qiaos couldn’t have been happier, thanking Heaven they hadn’t chosen Zhu Shuzhen and brought this heartbreak upon Qiao Manor—though Wenxian himself had prayed for her safety every day.
[ZH] “仪王妃和喜兰的事我也听白风说起过，你要节哀。”
[EN] After a hot bath, fresh clothes, and supper, Shen Jianchen looked less haggard, though his eyes remained hollow. Knowing better than to parade his own happy toddlers before a grieving man, Wenxian kept his family tucked away for a few days and sat across a table beside a glowing brazier pouring warm fermented rice wine.
[ZH] 申简辰抬起头，忍住再次要流下的泪水：“等明年开春时，我会再去趟燕京，把她们的尸骨全部带回临安。”
[EN] Winter’s road-chill melted out of Shen Jianchen’s bones, leaving only the frost inside his chest.
[ZH] 乔文仙不愿仪王太过伤心，打算将话题扯到临安之事：“自从梁内侍将临安搞得乌烟瘴气后，东方诗会没举办过几次被新任的知府大人取缔。东方尘泽只能像从前一样私下搞些小型诗会。后来东方尘泽和安明轩各自成亲，有了家室后，办诗会的次数变得更少。加之政局不稳，人心惶惶，文人们都无心附庸风雅，临安诗坛再无往日气象。”
[EN] “When Bai Feng brought Matron Zou back, he told me you sold your Bianliang manor and everything in your name for ten million taels,” Wenxian sighed, filling both cups. “I couldn’t hold a candle to you.”
[ZH] “没有了她，临安的诗会失去不少风采，又何必再搞？”
[EN] “Without her, what good was silver?” Shen Jianchen drained his cup in one swallow. “And now she’s gone, silver means even less.”
[ZH] 转移话题失败，乔文仙继续提及其他：“倒有件高兴事，今年中秋节曹班带叶安雅回到临安，两人的儿子已两岁多，看在孙子的份上，曹家人接纳了叶安雅。几位好友又能聚到一起，实乃人生乐事，改日我把大家约来陪你说说话。”
[EN] “Over two years apart, and everything has shifted. Our court looks spent—I fear the whole realm may fall to the Jin.”
[ZH] “若中秋节那日把她从狱中接出，立刻送她回临安，也没有后面的事，都怪我贪恋与她单独在一起的日子。”
[EN] “The Jin aren’t fit to rule the whole realm,” Shen Jianchen said. “At best they’ll split the map with Song for a time, then meet ruin themselves. Shame neither of us will live to see that day.”
[ZH] 转移话题再次失败，乔文仙仍旧不放弃：“你知道吗？蒋爷的夫人去年生孩子时难产，蒋爷本想保住大人，他夫人不同意，硬要保住孩子。儿子是有了，夫人却撒手人寰，想来也挺可怜。”
[EN] “How can you be so sure?”
[ZH] 想到天目山之事，申简辰可怜不起来：“佛家讲善恶有报，还真没错。”
[EN] A faint, rare smile touched Shen Jianchen’s lips. “Just a guess.”
[ZH] 乔文仙以为转移话题成功，脸上刚露出笑容，却听仪王说：“她那么善良，处处为他人着想而委屈自己，为何落得如此下场？善恶有报又在哪里？”
[EN] “Right or wrong, you spoke straight to my heart!” Raising his cup, Wenxian toasted that distant day, and Shen Jianchen drank with him.
[ZH] 第三次转移话题失败，乔文仙收起笑容，又想到一个话题：“善恶有报还真有，林觉的妾室春金巧去年生了怪病，怎么都医不好，今年夏天离开人世。大家私底下议论，商妓出身本就卑贱，仗着出卖姿色被临安的贵妇参拜，结果无福消受，一命呜呼。”
[EN] Setting his cup down, Wenxian asked what he planned next.
[ZH] 申简辰批评道：“什么时候临安才子变成长舌妇？竟议论起别家的家长里短来？”
[EN] “Go back to the bamboo house built for her and me, and live out the rest of my days there,” Shen Jianchen answered softly, remembering Master Jingguang’s counsel. “I’ll do charity and build merit in her name, so whenever folks speak of her, it’ll feel like she’s still right beside me.”
[ZH] “哪是我想议论，不过是因为你离开临安的时间长，给你说说两年多的变化。”
[EN] Moved, Wenxian asked, “Count me in?”
[ZH] “林觉现在如何？”
[EN] “You already stole her first love. Trying to steal charity from me too?”
[ZH] 转移话题终于成功，乔文仙开玩笑道：“还有脸说我？你不也关心别人家的家长里短？”
[EN] “Young Master Xu took her first love, not me.”
[ZH] “不说，我可走了。”申简辰装作起身的样子。
[EN] “As far as I’m concerned, it was you.”
[ZH] 乔文仙赶紧拦住：“别着急走，两年多没见，你我需多聊会儿。今日不许走，晚上住在幽林别居，明日我亲自送你回乡下的竹屋。”
[EN] “Fine, I won’t fight you over it.”
[ZH] 见仪王继续坐好，乔文仙说：“林觉最善于巴结上面的官员，诸军都统制的位置坐得相当安稳，还好本性不算坏，做事倒也知分寸。春金巧的死对他影响极大，不仅不再纳妾，也没去过青楼，安心跟白梅若过日子。白梅若给他生下一个女儿，跟我那两个孩子一般大。”
[EN] “Think how absurd life is,” Shen Jianchen sighed as fresh tears brimmed. “Nobody left to fight me now, Mengrong no longer there to block us, nothing standing in our way—and she’s gone.”
[ZH] “真羡慕你们，都已为人父母，可我和她却……”
[EN] “Bai Feng told me about Princess Consort Yi and Xilan too. Accept my condolences.”
[ZH] 仪王又将话题扯了回去，乔文仙转移话题完全失败，他还是努力在脑中搜索新的话题。
[EN] Blinking tears back, Shen Jianchen promised himself that come spring, he would ride back to Yanjing and bring their bones home to Lin’an.
[ZH] 还未想出时，南双进来说：“仪王、公子，林都统前来拜见。”
[EN] Eager to pull him out of grief, Wenxian shifted to Lin’an gossip: after Liang Shicheng tainted the city, the new Prefect shut Dongfang’s poetry banquet down, leaving Dongfang Chenze holding rare quiet gatherings; then Chenze and An Mingxuan both married, political dread killed everyone’s appetite for poetry, and Lin’an’s literary circle lost all its old sparkle.
[ZH] 乔文仙说：“他肯定是听到你回来的消息才来拜见，要不要见？”
[EN] “Without her, Lin’an’s poetry world lost half its soul anyway,” Shen Jianchen murmured. “Why bother hosting?”
[ZH] 申简辰说：“见见又何妨？大家都是老相识，再说那会儿他跟着我也做过不少好事。”
[EN] Attempt one failed. Wenxian tried happier news: at Mid-Autumn, Cao Ban brought Ye Anya back to Lin’an with their two-year-old son, winning the Cao family’s acceptance for the grandson’s sake—and their old group could gather again soon.
[ZH] 乔文仙向南双说：“请林都统进来。”
[EN] “If I’d sent her straight back to Lin’an on Mid-Autumn when she walked out of prison, none of this would have happened,” Shen Jianchen choked. “I got greedy wanting her to myself.”
[ZH] 南双出去后，林觉掀开厚厚的帘子进来。外面冷，满面凉气，一进屋子热气扑面而来，冷热交替的过程中，林觉的脸颊和鼻头变得通红。
[EN] Attempt two failed. Wenxian dug deeper: Master Jiang’s wife died in childbirth last year insisting the midwives save her baby boy—pitiable in its own way.
[ZH] 林觉上前行礼：“拜见仪王殿下。”
[EN] “Buddhism calls it karma—good and evil both get repaid,” Shen Jianchen muttered coldly, recalling Mount Tianmu.
[ZH] 申简辰淡淡地说：“今日没有仪王，只有朋友，坐下喝两杯。”
[EN] Thinking he had succeeded at last, Wenxian broke into a smile—until Shen Jianchen added, “She stayed gentle and put everyone before herself, suffering every wrong. Why did she end up like this? Where was karma for her?”
[ZH] 林觉不敢，跪在地上：“下官今日前来当着仪王的面亲自忏悔，哪怕要了下官的命也在所不惜。”
[EN] Attempt three failed. Wiping away his smile, Wenxian tried one more piece: karma *did* strike—Lin Jue’s concubine Chun Jinqiao caught a strange sickness last year that no doctor could cure, and died this summer. People whispered a courtesan’s low birth couldn’t bear the blessing of Lin’an noblewomen bowing to her.
[ZH] “何悔之有？”
[EN] “Since when did Lin’an’s young scholars turn into gossiping busybodies?” Shen Jianchen chided.
[ZH] “若下官当日没有将她从陈府带走，就没有后面许多事，都是下官的错，要杀要剐任凭仪王处置。”
[EN] “Only catching you up on two years away!”
[ZH] 申简辰冷笑两声：“哼哼！若她不愿意，就凭你能轻易将她带走？你的命对我来讲一文不值，若你舍得性命，不如去汴梁，帮你的官家抵抗金人，死在战场总比死在我的手上更有价值。”
[EN] “How is Lin Jue doing?”
[ZH] “仪王不说，下官也这么打算。下官已向知府大人请命，明日带兵开拔汴梁前去支援。”
[EN] Success at last! Wenxian grinned. “Calling me a busybody when you’re curious about other folks’ homes yourself?”
[ZH] “既如此又何必假惺惺前来让我要了你的命？”
[EN] “Keep teasing and I’m walking out.”
[ZH] “下官不过是为表达忏悔之意。”
[EN] Laughing him back into his chair to stay the night before riding to the bamboo house tomorrow, Wenxian told him Lin Jue kept his post as Commander-in-Chief of All Garrisons by currying favor smoothly, though he knew boundaries. Jinqiao’s death sobered him completely: no more brothels, no more concubines, living steadily with Bai Meiruo and their newborn daughter—the same age as Wenxian’s two kids.
[ZH] “她从来没怪过你，起来吧，坐下说话。”
[EN] “Envious of all of you becoming fathers… while she and I…”
[ZH] “谢仪王不杀之恩。”林觉起身，端把椅子，在桌边坐好。
[EN] Back to square one. Before Wenxian could hunt for a new topic, Nan Shuang stepped in announcing Commandant Lin at the gate. Since he’d heard Prince Yi was back and had done good work under him before, Shen Jianchen agreed to see him.
[ZH] 乔文仙为林觉放好一只酒杯，把酒斟满。
[EN] Pulling back the thick winter door curtain, Lin Jue entered with cheeks and nose bright red from outside cold meeting indoor brazier warmth. Dropping straight to his knees, he confessed his guilt.
[ZH] 三人一边聊往事，一边饮酒，直到天色暗下，仪王要休息，林觉才离开。
[EN] “No prince here today, just old acquaintances—sit and drink,” Shen Jianchen said flatly.
[ZH] 乔文仙吩咐南双伺候仪王，他则回到自己屋中。
[EN] Lin Jue refused to rise. “If I hadn’t marched to Chen Manor to take her away that day, none of what followed would have happened! Take my life if you wish!”
[ZH] 刚踏进门，被一妻一妾堵住。
[EN] “Your life isn’t worth a copper to me,” Shen Jianchen sneered. “If you’re so eager to die, march to Bianliang and fight the Jin for your Emperor. Dying on a battlefield beats dying by my hand.”
[ZH] 苗知瑜问：“仪王都回来了，幽栖居士为何没见人影？”
[EN] “Even without Your Highness saying so, I already petitioned the Prefect—I lead troops north to relieve Bianliang tomorrow!”
[ZH] 夏果问：“我听南双说小姐人没了，是不是真的？”
[EN] “Then spare me the hollow act begging for death.”
[ZH] 乔文仙埋怨道：“我刚把仪王哄好，你们又来。”
[EN] “I came only to ask forgiveness.”
[ZH] “看来是真的。”夏果哭道，“呜呜，小姐是好人，为何好人不长命？”
[EN] “She never blamed you. Get up and sit down.”
[ZH] 苗知瑜比夏果好些，上前把夏果扶到椅子跟前坐好：“妹妹别难过，文渊不是还没给出答案？”
[EN] Thanking him for sparing his life, Lin Jue pulled up a chair while Wenxian poured wine, and the three drank over old days until dusk called them to rest.
[ZH] 夏果又充满希望：“到底如何？”
[EN] When Wenxian finally returned to his own room, Miao Zhiyu and Xiaguo cornered him at the door, demanding why Prince Yi had come back alone—was the rumor from Nan Shuang true?
[ZH] 乔文仙叹口气：“仪王说淑真和仪王妃被葬在汴京城外，明年春天会把她们的尸骨迁回临安。”
[EN] “I barely calmed Prince Yi down, and now you two start!” Wenxian groaned.
[ZH] 得到确切答复，夏果更加伤心：“小姐，你怎么就这么没了？呜呜……！”
[EN] Xiaguo burst into sobs over good people dying young while Zhiyu helped her into a chair hoping Wenxian would deny it—only for Wenxian to sigh out the truth that Shuzhen and Princess Consort Yi lay buried outside Yanjing until spring.
[ZH] 苗知瑜也流下几滴泪：“没了幽栖居士，从此后临安的诗坛再无生气。”
[EN] Xiaguo wailed uncontrollably; Zhiyu shed quiet tears for Lin’an’s dead poetry scene.
[ZH] 文渊发誓道：“从今日起我不会再参加诗会，也不再参加任何文人的活动，除非淑真死而复生。”
[EN] Standing straight, Wenxian swore a solemn oath: “From this day on, I will attend no more poetry banquets and no literary gatherings—unless Shuzhen rises from the dead.”
[ZH] 听到这话，两个女人都哭出声来，既为离世的人悲伤，亦为离世的人在文渊心中的地位而悲伤。
[EN] At that vow, both women wept harder still—grieving both for the dead poetess and for how deep she still stood in their husband’s heart.

#CH 341
[ZH] 完颜池让人将申简辰拖到城外后，来到地府，欺骗道：“时间已到，大宋仪王迟迟未来，别再幻想他会拿钱换你。”
[ZH] 一千万两不是小数目，仪王要凑齐需砸锅卖铁搞得家产尽失，千里寻不希望仪王如此，否则她欠他的生生世世都无法偿还。
[ZH] “老娘从未幻想过，只想着什么时候你能死在老娘的拳头下。”
[ZH] “你虽比我厉害，可也不能将我怎样。”
[ZH] 完颜池靠过来，千里寻看到他脸上的淤青，冷笑道：“都被人打成这样，还有脸在我面前逞能？”趁机伸出一拳攻击而去。
[ZH] 完颜池与美人对打几下，自知打不过，有意向后退去，在地上跺两下脚，从空中落下一个网，将千里寻套住。
[ZH] 被关的时日里好几次被完颜池用机关控制，千里寻在地府中认真观察研究过，也学完颜池用同样的动作，总是不奏效。现在又被机关困住，她气愤道：“像你这种没本事的人才会用机关对付老娘！”
[ZH] 完颜池靠近被网子套住的美人：“你若乖些，本王也不会用机关对付你。”
[ZH] “呸！”
[ZH] 完颜池并不生气：“等我师傅从会宁赶来，给我带来巫医的药，到时给你服下，你会变得温顺可人。”
[ZH] 千里寻平生从未怕过，此时不由害怕起来。
[ZH] “别怕，本王不会害你，只是想拔掉你身上的刺，本王好容易靠近，才有机会把你变成本王的女人。”
[ZH] “你混蛋！”
[ZH] “无论你怎么骂，本王都不会生气。等你成为本王的女人，本王会带你走出地府，欣赏我大金的壮丽山河。”
[ZH] 对方提到“壮丽山河”，千里寻想到金人应该已向汴梁进发，故意说：“金军正在攻打大宋，你怎么像缩头乌龟一样躲在燕京享福？真不像个男人！”
[ZH] 完颜池并不上当：“你激我？哼！没用的。我金军两面夹击，会轻轻松松拿下汴梁，不缺我一个。再说占领燕京总需有人守着，守城有时比攻城还要困难。恐怕你想激我离开，伺机逃走，本王才不会上你的当。奉劝你还是想想如何讨好本王，你也好活得开心些。”
[ZH] 激将法不成，不如以情动人：“完颜池，老娘救过你的命，救命之恩当涌泉相报，没有你这样对待恩人的道理。”
[ZH] “正因为你救过本王的命，本王才要以身相许，让你成为本王的女人。”
[ZH] 说了半天又绕回去，千里寻提醒道：“强扭的瓜不甜。”
[ZH] 完颜池却有他的一番道理：“强扭的瓜甚合本王心意，新鲜吃着也爽口，又得来不易，最令本王魂牵梦绕，倍加珍惜；若等瓜熟透了，自己掉落到本王手中，甜是甜，软塌塌的，吃着没劲儿，本王吃不过两口便会弃置一旁。”
[ZH] 千里寻第一次见如此难缠又不要脸的男人，再怎么劝都没用：“老娘不好惹，你最好放弃，大不了老娘死在你面前，让你得不偿失。”
[ZH] “本王没得到你之前不会让你轻易死，好好活着，等着享受本王带给你的快乐。”
[ZH] 完颜池说完，走了出去，在门外轻轻跺脚，网住千里寻的网子又升到屋顶。
[ZH] 千里寻站起来，走到完颜池跺脚的地方，也将脚跺了几下，毫无反应，又走到门口，又将脚跺几下，还是没用。
[ZH] “地府内的机关甚是奇怪，为何我怎么跺脚都没用？难道机关只听完颜池的？”
[ZH] 丫鬟过来劝道：“姑娘今后不要再跟小王爷置气，地府内机关重重，您很容易吃亏。”
[ZH] 千里寻趁机问：“地府里的机关都是完颜池安装的吗？”
[ZH] 丫鬟说：“应该不是，小王爷才住进王府不久，可能是以前的主人安装好的。”
[ZH] 千里寻纳闷：“为何地府的机关只听得懂完颜池的脚步声？”
[ZH] 丫鬟摇摇头：“奴婢不知？”
[ZH] 地府的机关实在精妙，她一个外行根本搞不明白。她不愿再继续研究，走进屋内，躺在榻上，想自己的事。
[ZH] 丫鬟则乖乖站立一旁。
[ZH] 千里寻并不知梦容已死，仍旧在心里祝福仪王与梦容。算算时间，他们应该回到汴梁，希望梦容服用了刘妙手的药后能痊愈。
[ZH] 她想起陈浩宇，她迟迟未归，他肯定等得焦急，若知道自己被金人抓走，会不会跑到燕京来？希望他别来，好好经营陈家的生意，若她无法回到临安，希望他能另娶他人。
[ZH] 最让千里寻担心的是家中长辈，她又是入狱又是被困燕京，长辈们肯定担心不已。希望他们保重身体，若她无法安全回去，都不要过分挂怀。
[ZH] 回顾一路走来的经历，完全可用“坎坷”二字形容，说得难听点儿是命不好。
[ZH] 穿越前写小说遭网暴，穿越后成为她人，命依然不好。再次想起净光法师说的“正思正念”，她坚守自己认为正确的选择走到今日，本以为业障消尽，可以过上安稳的日子，谁知又落入完颜池手中。
[ZH] 当下她什么都做不了，何谈“正思正念”？千里寻不由叹口气：“唉！”
[ZH] 一旁的丫鬟听到叹息声，主动说：“姑娘如此幸运，怎么叹起气来？”
[ZH] 千里寻坐起来责备道：“你哪只眼睛瞧见我幸运？”
[ZH] “奴婢从未见小王爷对哪位女子如此上心，姑娘难道不觉得自己很幸运？”
[ZH] “这样的幸运我宁可不要！”
[ZH] “姑娘有所不知，在会宁时，许多贵族家的姑娘想给小王爷当侧妃，他都瞧不上，却对姑娘费尽心思，我们下人看了都感动。”
[ZH] “哼！”千里寻很不以为然地冷笑一声，很快转变态度，不如从丫鬟口中多了解了解完颜池，说不定能找到对付他的方法，“喂，既然你被他感动，不如给我说说完颜池的情况，说不定我了解后会喜欢上他。”
[EN] After having Shen Jianchen dragged unconscious beyond Yanjing’s gates, Wanyan Chi strolled down into the Under-Manor and lied smoothly. “Time’s up. Your Song prince never showed. Stop dreaming he’ll buy you back.”
[ZH] “姑娘想了解什么？尽管问。”
[EN] Ten million taels was no pocket change; raising it meant bankrupting every estate he owned, leaving Qianli Xun relieved instead. “Never dreamed of it once! Only dreamed of flattening your face under my fists!”
[ZH] “他身边有几个女人？”
[EN] “Better than me with your hands, maybe—still powerless against me.”
[ZH] “小王爷的府中有一个王妃，三个侧妃。”
[EN] Stepping closer, he revealed a fresh bruise on his cheek, drawing a cold laugh from her before she threw a punch straight at him. Trading two blows before dropping back, Wanyan Chi stomped the floor twice—and a heavy net snapped down from the ceiling, pinning her tight!
[ZH] “都有这么多女人陪他，怎么还这么花心？”
[EN] Furious over falling for his hidden traps again despite mimicking his exact steps in secret, she roared, “Only a spineless coward hides behind tricks and traps!”
[ZH] “什么是花心？”
[EN] Stepping right up to the net, he smiled. “Act sweet, and I wouldn’t need traps. Wait till my master arrives from Huining bringing shaman medicine—one dose turns you gentle and docile as a lamb.”
[ZH] “是说他已经有老婆，却还喜欢其他女子。”
[EN] For the first time in two lifetimes, cold dread pricked Qianli Xun’s heart.
[ZH] 丫鬟想了想解释道：“姑娘冤枉小王爷。”
[EN] “Don’t be scared,” he coaxed. “I wouldn’t hurt you—just pluck off your thorns so I can keep you close and make you mine.”
[ZH] 千里寻瞪大眼睛：“冤枉？事实摆在面前，怎么能说我冤枉？”
[EN] “Bastard!”
[ZH] 丫鬟继续解释：“王妃和三个侧妃是长辈给安排的，小王爷都不喜欢，又不得不娶，唉，小王爷表面上看着风光，其实挺可怜。”
[EN] “Curse all you want. Once you’re my woman, I’ll bring you out into the sun to tour the grand rivers and mountains of Great Jin.”
[ZH] “他可怜？他要是可怜，天下人岂不都成可怜鬼？”
[EN] Hearing “grand mountains,” she seized the opening to goad him. “Jin armies are fighting Song right now, and you hide here in Yanjing enjoying soft living like a turtle pulling in its head! Call yourself a man?”
[ZH] “姑娘不知，小王爷从小没有娘亲，长大后好不容易遇到喜欢的女子，陛下却逼迫他娶臣子的女儿，听说是为笼络人心。小王爷死活不同意，陛下便……”
[EN] Unbothered, Wanyan Chi smirked. “Trying to bait me? Save your breath. Our twin columns will take Bianliang with ease without missing me—and guarding a captured city takes sharper hands than storming it. Nice try baiting me out so you can slip away! Better spend your time figuring out how to please me.”
[ZH] 丫鬟靠近千里寻，悄悄说：“派人杀了小王爷所爱，还骂他是汉人生的野种，想要在大金站住脚，必须娶臣子的女儿，小王爷的身边不得已有了一位王妃和三个侧妃。”
[EN] Since goading failed, she tried appeal to gratitude. “Wanyan Chi, I saved your life once! Nobody treats a lifesaver like this!”
[ZH] 千里寻没好气地说：“活该！”
[EN] “And precisely because you saved my life, I’ll repay you with my own body and make you my woman.”
[ZH] 丫鬟站好继续说：“还别说，仔细瞧，姑娘跟小王爷喜欢的女子有些相似，难怪小王爷会对您不一样。”
[EN] “A forcefully twisted melon is never sweet!”
[ZH] “你见过她？”
[EN] “A forcefully twisted melon suits *my* taste best,” Wanyan Chi chuckled. “Crisp, fresh, hard-won—makes a man crave and treasure it twice as hard! Wait till a melon ripens mushy and drops in your lap, and you take two bites and toss it aside.”
[ZH] “本人我没见过，只见过画像，眉眼处跟姑娘有几分相似。”
[EN] Confronted with logic that shameless, she snapped she’d die before giving in and leave him empty-handed.
[ZH] “相似又能如何？又不是同一个人。”千里寻继续问，“为何完颜池是汉人所生？”
[EN] “I won’t let you die before I have you,” Wanyan Chi answered lightly, strolling back outside the door and tapping his heel twice so the net lifted back to the rafters.
[ZH] 丫鬟竖起食指，放在口边“吁”了一声，四处瞧瞧，确定无人后，回到千里寻身边，压低声音说：“听说陛下还是王爷那会儿，到江南游玩，遇到一汉人女子，动了情，才生下小王爷。小王爷出生不久，被人带到会宁，扔进王府侍卫的怀里。长大后，小王爷多次去江南寻找生母，都失望而归。”
[EN] Testing his exact spot and the threshold with frantic stomps that did nothing, Qianli Xun slumped onto her bed while the maid stood silently by. Could the traps only recognize Wanyan Chi’s own stride?
[ZH] 千里寻终于明白为何会在临安遇见完颜池，难道他的生母是临安人？
[EN] Staring up at the lamp-lit stone ceiling, she assumed Mengrong was healed back in Bianliang by now, prayed Haoyu stayed safe running the Chen business and found a new wife if she never returned, and hoped her elders stayed strong. Looking back over her life—cyberbullied before transmigration, battered by fate after—she remembered Master Jingguang’s teaching on “Right Thought and Right Mindfulness.” What good had clinging to right paths done her here? Out slipped a weary sigh.
[ZH] 丫鬟继续压低声音强调道：“小王爷甚至放出话来，只要此生能见到生母，哪怕丢掉性命他都愿意。这等有情有义之人，姑娘有什么可拒绝的？”
[EN] Hearing her sigh, the maid spoke up gently. “So lucky as you are, Miss—why sigh?”
[ZH] 听了半天没找出对付完颜池的办法，反而对他产生同情之心。千里寻斜眼盯着丫鬟：“你这丫鬟挺有本事，应该让你家小王爷好好奖励你。”
[EN] Qianli Xun sat up sharp. “Which eye of yours sees me lucky?!”
[ZH] 丫鬟赶紧说：“千万不要告诉小王爷，奴婢背地里说主子的隐私，要被打板子的。”
[EN] “Never once have I seen the young prince care over any woman like you. Doesn’t that make you lucky?”
[ZH] “哼，说到底他就不是好人。”
[EN] “Luck like that I’d gladly throw away!”
[ZH] “小王爷是好人！”
[EN] “You don’t know, Miss—back in Huining, half the noble daughters begged to become his secondary consorts and he looked past every one, yet pours his whole heart into you!”
[ZH] “好人还为这点儿小事打你板子？”
[EN] Realizing the maid might hold useful clues, Qianli Xun swallowed her scoff and softened her tone. “Well then, since he moves you so much, tell me about him. Who knows—maybe I’ll warm up to him once I know him better.”
[ZH] “常言道国有国法，家有家规。”
[EN] “Ask anything, Miss!”
[ZH] “你倒是挺能为他开脱，不管你怎么看，在我眼里他就不是个好人。”
[EN] “How many women does he keep?”
[ZH] ……
[EN] “One primary consort and three secondary consorts.”
[ZH] 带着对完颜池的不满，千里寻在地府中又住了五日。开心的是这五日完颜池并未前来打扰，不开心的是始终走不出地府。
[EN] “Four wives already and still chasing after others—what a playboy!”
[ZH] 越是一个人百无聊赖地待着，仪王的身影越容易浮现在她的脑海。空闲时想一想应该无伤大雅，这么无聊的日子也只能靠回忆往事消磨时光。
[EN] The maid frowned in confusion at the modern slang; once Qianli Xun explained, she rushed to defend him. “You wrong him! Elders forced every marriage on him—he dislikes all four. Outwardly grand, His Highness is actually pitiful.”
[ZH] 回忆起与仪王在燕京的街头寻找神医的经历时，千里寻露出笑容。刚开心些，听到地府被打开的声音，脸上的笑容立刻消失。
[EN] “*Him* pitiful?! Then everyone alive is a miserable ghost!”
[ZH] 不一会儿，完颜池走来，身后跟着个丫鬟，端着一壶酒和两只酒杯。
[EN] Lowering her voice and glancing round, the maid whispered how the Emperor killed the girl Wanyan Chi truly loved in order to bind ministers through political marriages—even sneering at Wanyan Chi as a “Han-born bastard” unless he obeyed! What was more, Qianli Xun shared a striking resemblance round the eyes and brows to a portrait of that murdered girl!
[ZH] 千里寻质问道：“你来做什么？”
[EN] “Serves him right,” Qianli Xun muttered.
[ZH] “我来看你，顺便给你带来一个好消息。”
[EN] “And his birth mother,” the maid whispered lower still, leaning close. “When His Majesty traveled south to Jiangnan years ago as a prince, he loved a Han maiden. Soon after our young prince was born, someone carried him straight to Huining and dropped him in a manor guard’s arms. Growing up, he rode south to Jiangnan again and again hunting his mother, swearing he’d trade his life for one look at her—and came home heartbroken every time. How can you reject a man that loyal?”
[ZH] 完颜池坐到桌前，丫鬟将酒壶和酒杯摆好后乖乖出去。
[EN] Annoyed that instead of finding leverage she had almost caught herself feeling sorry for him, Qianli Xun shot the maid a sideways glare. “Smooth tongue you’ve got—your master ought to reward you.”
[ZH] 千里寻警惕地问：“什么好消息？”
[EN] “Please never repeat a word to His Highness—talking behind masters gets us beaten with bamboo!”
[ZH] “今日我会带你走出地府，地下阴暗，见不到阳光，时间久了对你的身体不好。”
[EN] “Hmph! Beats his own servants over idle chatter—proves he’s rotten after all.”
[ZH] “你会这么好？”
[EN] “States have laws and households have rules,” the maid defended stubbornly, leaving Qianli Xun grumbling that no excuse washed him clean.
[ZH] “我对你的好，你何时才能真正体会到？”完颜池似笑非笑又近似调侃。
[EN] Five peaceful days passed without Wanyan Chi setting foot downstairs. With nothing to do in the dim stone rooms, thoughts of Shen Jianchen kept drifting back—especially strolling Yanjing’s autumn streets looking for the doctor. Just as a smile warmed her lips, heavy clicks echoed from the entrance.
[ZH] 千里寻露出厌恶的表情：“少给老娘来这一套，既要带我出去，现在就走。”
[EN] Wanyan Chi strolled in followed by a maid carrying a tray with one pot of wine and two cups, dismissing her at once.
[ZH] “不急，陪我喝两杯再出去不迟。”
[EN] “What do you want now?” Qianli Xun snapped.
[ZH] “不好意思，老娘从不饮酒。”
[EN] “Came to see you—with good news. Today I’m bringing you out of the Under-Manor. Underground damp isn’t good for you.”
[ZH] “不过是汉人做的桂花酿而已，算不上真正的酒。”
[EN] “Since when did you turn generous?”
[ZH] 千里寻盯着那壶桂花酿，想起五天前完颜池提到巫医的药，更加警惕：“你该不会给桂花酿里放了什么药想骗我喝下，好控制我？”
[EN] “When will you ever recognize my good intentions?” Wanyan Chi teased with half a smile.
[ZH] “你是说巫医的药？我编出来骗你的，没想到你竟然当真。”完颜池见美人不上道，故意露出些许悲戚，说出早已想好的理由，“其实，今日是我娘的诞辰，我从小没见过她，也不知她到底是哪天出生，就把我出生的日子作为她的诞辰，每年这个时候我都要独自饮酒，怀念从未见过的娘亲。”
[EN] “Drop the act. If you’re taking me out, let’s walk right now.”
[ZH] 千里寻多少有些感动，依然警惕地说：“哼，谁知道你说的是真是假？”
[EN] “No rush. Share two cups with me first.”
[ZH] “我又何必拿娘的事欺骗你？我娘是我这辈子心头最大的痛。不妨告诉你，我娘跟你一样是临安人，她是邵家镖局邵正邺的亲妹妹。我去临安就是为寻她。邵正邺告诉我她已不在人世，还让我不要再去邵家随便打扰。我问他我娘到底被葬在何处？邵家人不愿告诉我。于是我潜入邵家墓地，并未找到有我娘的名字的墓碑，也寻遍临安所有坟地，仍旧没有找到。我有种强烈的预感，我娘还活着，只是她不愿见我而已……”
[EN] “Sorry. I don’t drink.”
[ZH] 千里寻发现完颜池说到娘亲时眼睛变红，看来他是真情使然，警惕之心不由减弱许多：“你是金人，你娘不愿见你再正常不过。”
[EN] “Just Han osmanthus wine—barely counts as liquor.”
[ZH] “等我大金攻克汴梁，大事已成，我定要再去趟临安，将邵家翻个底朝天，不相信找不到我娘亲。”
[EN] Remembering his threat days earlier, she narrowed her eyes. “Did you spike it with shaman medicine to control me?”
[ZH] “我劝你还是算了，你娘不想见你，又何必打扰她？”
[EN] “The shaman medicine? Made it up to scare you—never thought you’d buy it.” Dropping his playful grin into quiet melancholy, Wanyan Chi spoke softly. “Truth is, today is my mother’s birthday. Never met her, never knew her birth day, so I celebrate hers on the day I was born—drinking alone each year to remember a mother I’ve never seen.”
[ZH] “你根本体会不到一个从小没娘的孩子内心之苦。”
[EN] Touched a fraction yet still wary, she scoffed, “Who knows if that’s true or another lie?”
[ZH] 千里寻想到穿越前的自己，也是小时候没了娘，不过她比完颜池幸运，起码得到过母爱：“我当然能体会到。”
[EN] “Why lie about my mother? She’s the sharpest ache in my chest. She comes from Lin’an just like you—younger sister to Shao Zhengye of the Shao Family Escort Agency. That’s why I went to Lin’an. Shao Zhengye told me she was dead, warned me off his gates, and refused to name her grave. I slipped into the Shao graveyard at night, combed every grave hill round Lin’an, and never found her stone. I’m certain she’s still alive—she just refuses to see me.”
[ZH] “既然能体会到，坐下陪我喝两杯，就当是可怜我这个没娘要的孩子。”
[EN] Seeing genuine redness rim his eyes, Qianli Xun lowered her guard halfway. “You’re a Jin prince. No surprise she won’t see you.”
[ZH] 对方以情动人的攻势下，千里寻失去所有防备：“好，我陪你喝两杯，说好了，只喝两杯，就放我出去。”
[EN] “Once Great Jin conquers Bianliang and things settle, I’m riding back to Lin’an and turning the Shao estate upside down till I find her.”
[ZH] “好，只喝两杯，喝完我带你走出地府。”完颜池伪装出真诚的笑容。
[EN] “Take my advice and drop it. Why disturb her peace if she doesn’t want to meet?”
[ZH] 见美人毫无戒备坐到桌旁，完颜池拿起酒壶为二人斟满，然后端起酒杯：“一起干了。”
[EN] “You could never know the ache of growing up without a mother.”
[ZH] 千里寻小心端起：“这杯敬你的娘亲，希望你和她能早日相见。”
[EN] Thinking of her own childhood before transmigration—losing her mother young too, though at least she had tasted maternal love—Qianli Xun softened. “I *do* know.”
[ZH] 完颜池已等不及，迅速碰杯，一饮而尽。
[EN] “Then sit and drink two cups with me—out of pity for a boy nobody wanted.”
[ZH] 千里寻见完颜池毫不犹豫饮下，确定酒酿没有问题，放心喝下。味道不错，比她喝过的所有桂花酿都要好。
[EN] Disarmed completely by his grief, she agreed: two cups only, then straight upstairs.
[ZH] 千里寻自斟一杯，端起来：“一个从小念着娘亲的人应该不是坏人，既然你不是坏人，早些把我放了，我是临安人，对那地方比你熟，你放我回去，帮你找娘亲的事交给我，保准圆满完成……”
[EN] Pouring both cups full, Wanyan Chi toasted cleanly and drained his cup first without hesitation. Satisfied the wine held no poison, Qianli Xun drank hers—finding it sweeter and smoother than any osmanthus wine she had tasted.
[ZH] 千里寻还没说完，一阵头晕，手中的酒杯掉落在桌上，酒酿洒了一桌。
[EN] Pouring herself a second cup, she smiled gently. “Nobody who remembers his mother like this can be all bad. Tell you what—let me go home to Lin’an. I know every alley there far better than you; I’ll help track down your mother for you—”
[ZH] 眼看着人要倒下，完颜池迅速过去，美人正好倒在他怀里。
[EN] Halfway through her sentence, the room spun violently. Her cup clattered onto the table, spilling amber wine across the wood. Pitching sideways, she fell straight into Wanyan Chi’s waiting arms.
[ZH] 千里寻朦胧着双眼：“你……骗我。”然后晕了过去。
[EN] “You… tricked me…” she slurred before darkness claimed her.
[ZH] 完颜池露出满意的笑容：“我不骗你，你又岂能变乖？”
[EN] “If I didn’t trick you,” Wanyan Chi murmured with a satisfied smile, “how would you ever behave?”

#CH 342
[ZH] 千里寻醒来时，眼前是一片自然的亮光，终于从地府出来，刚要开心，想起自己是喝了完颜池的桂花酿才晕过去，桂花酿中一定放了什么药物，否则怎么会一杯就倒？
[ZH] 她从床上爬起来， 警惕地观察屋子，到处是红色，她躺的床榻铺着红色的褥子，连枕头和被子也是红色。显然她正躺在婚房里，难道完颜池想逼她成亲？
[ZH] 千里寻很气愤，使劲儿掀被子，浑身的肌肉像是在醋中泡过一般，又酸又软，只能慢慢地从被子里溜下床。
[ZH] 刚在地上站稳，听到门被推开，传来完颜池的声音：“人可醒？”
[ZH] 丫鬟回道：“应该还睡着，奴婢刚要进去瞧，小王爷就来了。”
[ZH] “出去吧，把门关好，免得冷气进来冻到美人。”
[ZH] “是。”
[ZH] 千里寻又听到丫鬟出去关门的声音，声音刚结束，完颜池出现在她面前，脸上挂着笑容。
[ZH] “醒了，别站在地上，小心冻着。”
[ZH] 千里寻并不领情，将酸软的拳头伸到完颜池面前。他伸出手轻松推回：“别瞪着本王，本王不会害你，只想让你变得温顺些。”
[ZH] “你给桂花酿里放了什么？”
[ZH] “巫医特制的药。”
[ZH] “卑鄙无耻！”
[ZH] “别这么说你未来的夫君。”
[ZH] “呸！我有夫君，他正在临安等我回去。”
[ZH] “不管你以前有多少夫君，从今往后只会有本王一个。”
[ZH] 千里寻忍着怒气劝道：“我不爱你，又何必为难我？”
[ZH] “你爱不爱本王不重要，重要的是本王喜欢你。”完颜池霸道的同时，露出回忆往事的表情，“临安街头，一眼万年，你是第二个令本王心动的女子。怪只怪你长成本王喜欢的样子，只是性情差些，拳头也比本王厉害。还好有巫医的药，只要今晚洞房花烛夜之后，你成为本王的女人，会慢慢喜欢上本王。”
[ZH] “你敢动老娘一根毫毛，我就死在你面前。”
[ZH] 完颜池大胆靠近美人，在他眼中，此刻的她如同一块刚出炉的嫩豆腐，既软嫩又美味。
[ZH] 千里寻被他眼中的欲望之火吓到，向后退去。她的身后只有床榻，不小心向下倒去……
[ZH] 完颜池趁机扶住她：“你现在无力反抗的样子实在令本王心动。”
[ZH] 千里寻用手去推完颜池，他纹丝不动，她不得已罢手：“你若敢碰我，我就咬舌自尽。”
[ZH] 话音刚落，完颜池的嘴巴已堵在她的唇上。
[ZH] 千里寻厌恶至极，攥紧拳头，只能无力地在他背上拍打，一点儿用处没有。对方吻得越投入她越厌恶，拍打不成，使出嘴上的功夫，一口咬破完颜池的嘴唇，一股血腥味儿传到她的舌尖。
[ZH] 完颜池冷不丁被美人咬一口才停下，放开手，美人倒在床榻上。
[ZH] “别以为我浑身酸软就拿你没办法，只要我能说话，咬人的力气还是有的。”
[ZH] 完颜池站好后用舌头舔去嘴唇上的血迹，笑道：“越难驯服的野兽，本王越感兴趣，今晚你会尝到本王的厉害。”
[ZH] “滚！你给我滚！”
[ZH] “好好休息，晚上本王再来看你。”
[ZH] 完颜池出去后，从外面进来五六个丫鬟，进入屋内到处查看。
[ZH] 千里寻坐起来不解地问：“你们要做什么？”
[ZH] 一个丫鬟回道：“小王爷让我们进来把尖锐之物统统拿出去。”
[ZH] “为何？”
[ZH] “小王爷担心姑娘寻短见。”
[ZH] 千里寻冷笑道：“我要是想寻短见有的是办法。”
[ZH] 丫鬟们查找半天，找出匕首和剪刀带了出去，还把门从外面锁上。
[ZH] 想到今晚有可能失身于完颜池，千里寻又急又气，她不想坐以待毙。屋内没有丫鬟，只好亲自把衣裳从衣桁上取下穿好，披上厚厚的斗篷，向外喊：“来人！”
[ZH] 有开锁的声音，门被打开后，进来一个丫鬟。
[ZH] “姑娘有何吩咐？”
[ZH] “我想出去走走。”
[ZH] “小王爷说姑娘今日不能出去，明日小王爷会亲自带姑娘出去。”
[ZH] 一计不成，再施一计：“我饿了，给我弄些吃的来。”
[ZH] “姑娘稍候，吃的一会儿就到。”
[ZH] 门又被锁上。
[ZH] 千里寻起身走到门口等待，她发现身上的肌肉不再酸软，试着使出一拳，力度跟没有练过拳法的人没有任何区别。自语道：“巫医的药到底是什么，搞得我功力全无，也不知什么时候能恢复？”
[ZH] 外面有脚步声传来，应该是送饭的丫鬟过来。千里寻警惕起来，她要趁门打开的一刻冲出去，需要瞅准时机。
[ZH] 门锁被打开，送饭的正是在地府时伺候过她的丫鬟，走进的过程中，千里寻趁机从她身边溜了出去。
[ZH] 两柄剑挡在面前，一队侍卫如铜墙铁壁般堵住她的去路。
[ZH] 要搁以前她根本不会把他们放在眼里，拳法使不出，只能认怂：“我不过想出去走走，透透气，你们堵我干吗？”
[ZH] 侍卫中有一人说：“小王爷吩咐，姑娘今日不能走出屋子！”
[ZH] 千里寻骂句：“可恶！”转身回到屋内，眼睁睁看着门被继续锁上。
[ZH] 丫鬟把饭菜从食盒里拿出在桌上摆好：“姑娘已从地府出来，不急这一日。”
[ZH] 丫鬟又上前帮千里寻脱掉厚厚的斗篷：“屋里有火盆，暖和着，姑娘披着它会热，我给您脱下，您快坐下吃饭。”
[ZH] 看着桌上的饭菜，千里寻根本没胃口，还是装模作样坐下。
[ZH] “认识你这么久，还不知道你叫什么？”
[ZH] 丫鬟挂好斗篷回到姑娘身旁伺候：“奴婢叫会兰娜。”
[ZH] “会兰娜，名字不错。看到你想起我的丫鬟苦荞，两年多没见，我好想她。”
[ZH] “只要姑娘乖乖跟小王爷在一起，提任何要求小王爷都会答应，到时您让小王爷把苦荞接来，你们不就见着了？”
[ZH] “我才不会让苦荞来金国，她有喜欢的人，我只希望她跟白风尽快成亲，不要再耽搁下去。”
[ZH] 会兰娜见姑娘不吃东西，只顾说话，提醒道：“姑娘快些吃，要不一会儿凉了。”
[ZH] 千里寻拿起筷子象征性吃了一口，问道：“会兰娜，你可知巫医是干什么的？”
[ZH] “巫医是金国的神医，主要负责给皇室医病。”
[ZH] “制作毒药害人，根本当不起‘神医’的美名。会兰娜，你能不能给我帮个忙？”
[EN] Natural sunlight greeted Qianli Xun’s eyes as she woke—free of the underground vault at last! Delight lasted one heartbeat before memory slammed back: one cup of osmanthus wine, a spinning room, and blackness.
[ZH] “姑娘请说。”
[EN] Bolting upright, she froze. Red bed-curtains draped overhead, red silk padded the mattress, and red pillows and quilts wrapped around her—a bridal chamber! Every muscle in her body felt soaked in sour vinegar, weak and rubbery as she slid slowly off the edge onto the floorboards.
[ZH] “完颜池心肠歹毒，在桂花酿里下了巫医制作的毒药，害得我使不出拳法，你能不能帮我找来解药？”
[EN] Barely steadying her feet, she heard footsteps outside the door.
[ZH] 会兰娜吓得跪在地上：“姑娘饶命！奴婢要是帮了您会立刻没命，况且奴婢也不知道解药放在哪里。”
[EN] “Is she awake?” Wanyan Chi’s voice asked.
[ZH] 千里寻失落道：“我只是随口一说，看把你吓的，起来吧，我不会为难你。”
[EN] “Still asleep, likely—I was just about to peek in,” a maid answered.
[ZH] 会兰娜起身宽慰道：“姑娘别急，等您与小王爷洞房花烛之后小王爷自然会给您解药。”
[EN] “Dismiss yourselves and shut the door tight so cold drafts don’t chill her.”
[ZH] “那我岂不是羊入虎口？”
[EN] As the door clicked shut behind the maids, Wanyan Chi walked into view smiling broadly. “Awake? Don’t stand barefoot on the floor—you’ll catch cold.”
[ZH] 会兰娜在一旁偷笑起来。
[EN] Swinging a limp, aching fist toward his jaw, she watched him swat it aside with one finger. “Stop glaring at me. I won’t hurt you—just wanted you gentle for once.”
[ZH] “你笑什么？”
[EN] “What did you put in that wine?!”
[ZH] 会兰娜忍住笑：“姑娘怎能将洞房之夜说成羊入虎口？您不知道，小王爷今天心情特别好，大家都说小王爷嘴角一直挂着笑容。”
[EN] “A special medicine mixed by our shaman.”
[ZH] “哼！将个人的幸福凌驾于他人痛苦之上，有什么好高兴的？”
[EN] “Despicable bastard!”
[ZH] “只要姑娘接受小王爷，您就不会痛苦。”
[EN] “Careful how you speak to your future husband.”
[ZH] “我最讨厌像他这等霸道之人，想让老娘接受他根本不可能。”
[EN] “Bah! I have a husband waiting for me in Lin’an!”
[ZH] 会兰娜见劝说不下，便不再说话。
[EN] “Forget how many husbands you had before—from tonight on, I’m the only one.”
[ZH] 千里寻没有胃口吃饭，起身回里屋躺下。
[EN] Swallowing fury, she pleaded through clenched teeth, “I don’t love you! Why torture me like this?”
[ZH] 会兰娜把没怎么动的饭菜收回食盒，提着食盒在门口敲了敲门，门被打开后，又继续被锁上。
[EN] “Whether you love me doesn’t matter—what matters is I like you.” Memories softened his tone for a moment. “One glance on a Lin’an street, worth a thousand years. You’re only the second woman ever to stir my heart. Too bad your fists hit harder than mine—thank goodness for shaman medicine. Once tonight’s wedding candles burn down and you become my woman, you’ll grow to love me bit by bit.”
[ZH] 千里寻听着锁门的声音，情绪很不好，她长这么大头一次感受到人生无奈，也头一次认识到自己实在弱小，被人轻易拿捏住。
[EN] “Touch one hair on my head, and I swear I’ll die right in front of you!”
[ZH] 她的人生仿佛进入死局，难以突破，该如何是好？还没找到答案，却迷迷糊糊进入梦乡。
[EN] Stepping closer, eyes burning with hunger at how soft and defenseless she looked, he backed her step by step straight against the bedframe until her knees buckled. Catching her waist, he murmured how intoxicating her helpless look made him. Pushing against his chest moved him not an inch.
[ZH] 梦中她来到神柳下，神柳枝叶繁茂，上面挂着一些红绸，微风下柳枝和红绸随风摆动。
[EN] “Touch me,” she hissed, “and I’ll bite off my own tongue!”
[ZH] “好久不见，你可想我？”
[EN] His mouth covered hers before the threat finished. Revolted down to her bones, she beat weakly at his back with useless fists—then sank her teeth straight through his lip until warm iron blood flooded her tongue!
[ZH] 神柳竟然开口说话：“对你来讲是好久，在我看来不过是瞬息之间。”
[EN] Jerking back with a sharp gasp, he let her drop onto the mattress.
[ZH] “带我离开好吗？”
[EN] “Think turning me soft makes me harmless?” she panted. “As long as my mouth works, I can still draw blood!”
[ZH] “我无法带走你。”
[EN] Licking the cut on his lip with a grin, Wanyan Chi chuckled. “The fiercer the beast, the sweeter the taming! Tonight you’ll taste what kind of man I am. Rest up—I’ll come back tonight.”
[ZH] “明明是你把我带来，为何带不走我？”
[EN] Once he left, five or six maids swept into the room rummaging every chest and drawer for daggers and scissors—to keep her from harming herself—before locking the door heavy from outside.
[ZH] “你之所以能来，不过是个人业力借助了我的神力而已，要想回去，也需靠个人的业力。”
[EN] Dreading nightfall, Qianli Xun pulled on her robes from the rack, draped a thick cloak over her shoulders, and hammered the door. A maid unlocked it only to say Wanyan Chi forbade her stepping out until tomorrow.
[ZH] “业力？什么是业力？”
[EN] Switching tactics, she claimed she was starving and waited right behind the door. Testing a punch at empty air confirmed the worst: not a shred of martial power remained—her punches packed no more sting than an ordinary girl’s.
[ZH] “累生累世所造之业凝聚在一起形成无穷无尽之力，这股力量将你带到各处，只有业力消除，方可获得自由。”
[EN] When footsteps returned carrying a food box, she waited till the door swung inward and darted past the maid—straight into twin crossed blades and an iron wall of royal guards.
[ZH] 梦中的千里寻听不明白，大声说：“我坚持正思正念，做事问心无愧，何来业力？”
[EN] “His Highness forbade you leaving this room today,” a guard barked.
[ZH] 神柳沉默不语。
[EN] Cursing under her breath, she retreated while the lock clicked shut once more. Setting dishes onto the table and helping slip off her heavy cloak in the brazier-warmed room, the maid from underground coaxed her to eat.
[ZH] 身后传来申简辰的声音：“千里寻！”
[EN] Too sick with dread to swallow, Qianli Xun sat down anyway and asked the maid’s name.
[ZH] 千里寻转过身，兴奋地跑到申简辰身边，抱住他：“我什么都不想管，只想和你在一起，永远在一起。”
[EN] “Huilanna, Miss.”
[ZH] 申简辰在她怀中消失，陈浩宇出现。
[EN] “Nice name. Seeing you makes me think of my maid Kuqiao—over two years since I saw her. I miss her terribly.”
[ZH] “娘子，我等着你回来。”
[EN] “Obey the young prince, and he’ll grant anything—even fetching Kuqiao here to see you!”
[ZH] 接着梦容出现，手中拿着一把匕首对准胸口：“你若敢抢走我哥哥，我就死在你面前！”
[EN] “I’d never drag Kuqiao here. She has someone waiting for her—I only hope she and Bai Feng marry soon.”
[ZH] 千里寻被吓醒，发现屋内已点上红烛。
[EN] Asking about the Jin “shamans,” she learned they were royal miracle doctors—and pleaded desperately for the antidote. Huilanna dropped straight to her knees begging for her life, whispering afterward with a shy giggle that Wanyan Chi had been smiling ear to ear all day and would give her the antidote right after their wedding night.
[ZH] 会兰娜上前将她扶起：“姑娘，我伺候您换上新娘装。”
[EN] “Walking straight into a tiger’s jaws,” Qianli Xun muttered darkly, refusing food and lying back in the inner chamber while Huilanna cleared the untouched tray.
[ZH] 千里寻坐起，揉揉眼睛，才看清多了两名丫鬟，一个人手里捧着一身金国的新娘装，另一个捧着装饰精美的发冠。
[EN] Listening to the iron lock turn again, despair closed round her like stone walls—helpless for the first time in her life, trapped in a dead end until exhaustion dragged her into sleep.
[ZH] “统统拿走！老娘才不会穿那玩意儿！”
[EN] In her dream, she stood beneath the Divine Willow—thick with fresh green leaves and fluttering red silk ribbons.
[ZH] 会兰娜好言相劝：“姑娘别再任性，您现在身上无力，根本打不过小王爷。奴婢还听说大宋的仪王不会来救您，说明他是薄情之人，您又何必为他长情？即使您在汉人中还有亲人，他们只要听说您被金人抓走，肯定都不敢前来，说不定只当您已死，人家该怎么过日子还怎么过。小王爷现在是您唯一的依靠，还是面对现实穿上嫁衣吧？”
[EN] “Long time no see,” she whispered. “Did you miss me?”
[ZH] 会兰娜一番劝说，倒也戳中千里寻的内心。尽管她不希望仪王筹钱来救她，可听到别人说他不会来救她，心中多少有些凄凉，也许所有感情面对现实总是不堪一击。
[EN] The willow spoke inside her mind. “Long to you; to me, a single blink.”
[ZH] 还有陈浩宇，曾一口一个娘子唤着她，听着十分亲热，若知道她被金人抓走，会不会愿意赶来救她？也许不会，他来了只有死路一条，说不定伤心过后又娶新欢。
[EN] “Take me home, please?”
[ZH] 多年之后，大家都会忘记还有她这么一个人。
[EN] “I cannot take you away.”
[ZH] 尽管伤感，千里寻还是不愿穿上嫁衣：“告诉完颜池，我绝不会穿上嫁衣与他成亲，死也不会。”
[EN] “You brought me here! Why can’t you take me back?”
[ZH] 会兰娜无法，走出洞房向小王爷禀报。
[EN] “Your own karma borrowed my power to bring you here; to return, you must rely on your own karma too.”
[ZH] 不一会儿完颜池身着新郎装而来，向捧着新娘妆的丫鬟挥挥手：“退下吧。”
[EN] “Karma? What karma?”
[ZH] 丫鬟退出后，完颜池开始脱身上的新郎装。
[EN] “Deeds woven across lifetimes gather into boundless force that carries you from place to place. Only when karma is cleared does freedom come.”
[ZH] 千里寻胆怯地问：“你要做什么？”
[EN] “I held to Right Thought and Right Mindfulness!” she cried out in the dream. “My conscience is clear—what karma do I owe?!”
[ZH] 完颜池边脱边说：“你既然不愿穿新娘装，本王穿着还有什么意义？衣裳不过是形式，关键在于洞房花烛之夜。”
[EN] The willow gave no reply. Behind her, Shen Jianchen called her name! Turning joyfully, she flung her arms around him swearing she only wanted to stay with him forever—only for Shen Jianchen to fade into Chen Haoyu waiting quietly, and then Mengrong pointing a gleaming dagger at her heart: “Steal my brother, and I die right before your eyes!”
[ZH] 千里寻从来没有像现在一样害怕过：“你别过来！你要是过来我就咬舌自尽！”
[EN] She gasped awake in cold sweat—and saw thick red wedding candles blazing across the room.
[ZH] 完颜池并不理会，将脱下的新郎装扔在地上，走到床边坐下：“你都是成过亲之人，有什么好害怕的？”
[EN] Huilanna stepped up with two maids cradling Jin bridal robes and an ornate headdress.
[ZH] 千里寻向床榻里边靠去：“别过来！你要是今天动我！等我功力恢复定要杀了你！”
[EN] “Get those things out of my sight! I’m never putting them on!”
[ZH] “呵呵……！”完颜池笑起来，笑声停止后，继续说，“看来你不会咬舌自尽，本王就放心了。”
[EN] “Stop being stubborn, Miss!” Huilanna urged gently. “Your strength is gone; you can’t fight him. Word outside says the Song Prince Yi never came for you—he’s abandoned you. Even your family in Lin’an assumes you dead and moves on with their lives. His Highness is your only shelter left. Face reality and put on the wedding gown!”
[ZH] 千里寻拿起枕头扔向完颜池：“你混蛋！”
[EN] Cold reality cut deeper than any blade. Even hoping Shen Jianchen hadn’t bankrupted himself, hearing he hadn’t come chilled her heart—and Haoyu too might mourn briefly and marry someone new, until everyone forgot she ever lived. Still, pride refused to bend: “Tell Wanyan Chi I’ll die before putting on his bridal gown!”
[ZH] 枕头正好打在完颜池的脸上，软软的，对他毫无影响。他把掉落在身边的枕头扔在地上，袭击而去。
[EN] Minutes later, Wanyan Chi walked in wearing crimson groom’s silks, dismissed the maids, and calmly stripped off his own wedding coat.
[ZH] 千里寻用脚去踢，却被他抓住脚踝，她厌恶地看着对方用贪婪的目光盯着她的脚。
[EN] “What are you doing?!” she stammered, backing onto the bed.
[ZH] “就不怕今晚我再咬伤你。”
[EN] “Since you hate bridal robes, why wear them? Clothes are empty form—tonight’s wedding bed is what counts.”
[ZH] 完颜池放下美人的脚，用手指摸摸唇上的伤：“这点儿伤对本王来讲不算什么，今晚你即使咬得本王全身是伤，本王也不怕。”
[EN] Cold terror gripped her throat. “Stay back! One step closer and I bite off my tongue!”
[ZH] 完颜池再次袭击而来，千里寻已做好准备，瞅准他的软肋，向下身踢去。
[EN] Tossing his coat onto the floor, he sat on the edge of the bed. “You’ve been married twice—what is there to fear?”
[ZH] 这招挺管用，完颜池停下袭击的动作，捂起裤裆：“你的心是石头做的吗？怎么往本王这里踢？”
[EN] “Keep back! Touch me tonight, and the moment my strength returns I’ll kill you!”
[ZH] “活该！这就是欺负老娘的下场！只可惜我现在力气不大，伤不到你，否则非让你断子绝孙不可！”
[EN] “Haha! Good—if you’re waiting for strength to return, you won’t bite off your tongue after all.”
[ZH] 完颜池已无碍，仍旧袭击而来：“你既然想伤本王的命根子，今晚本王就用命根子征服你……”
[EN] Grabbing her pillow, she swung it straight into his face—useless against him. As he lunged forward, she kicked hard at his ankle, watched him catch it, waited till his grip eased, then lashed her foot straight up between his legs!
[ZH] 千里寻还想踢时，完颜池已点了她的穴位，她无法动弹，连话也说不出。
[EN] Doubled over clutching himself, Wanyan Chi groaned through clenched teeth, “Is your heart made of stone?!”
[ZH] 美人无法再反抗， 完颜池温柔地说：“你放心，今晚本王对你会很温柔。”
[EN] “Serves you right! Shame I’m weak, or I’d end your bloodline right here!”
[ZH] 千里寻眼睁睁看着他将她的外衣脱去，把她在床上放好，她的眼角流出屈辱的泪水……
[EN] Straightening up, eyes dark with determination, he lunged before she could kick again, tapped her acupoints silent and frozen, and promised gently to be tender—unfastening her outer robe as tears of pure humiliation slid silently down her temples.

#CH 343
[ZH] 身上的穴位被解开时，完颜池早已得手。
[EN] When the seal on her acupoints was released, Wanyan Chi had already had his way.
[ZH] 天还没亮，千里寻只穿着贴身衣物，缩在床榻的角落哭泣，伤心程度不亚于第一次失恋时。不同的是那时她不会哭出声来，而现在她一直出声哭泣：“呜呜……！”
[EN] Dawn had not come. Qianli Xun sat in her underthings, huddled in a corner of the bed, crying. Her grief was no less than it had been after her first heartbreak. Then she had not cried aloud. Now she could not stop: "Wah...!"
[ZH] 她努力守护的贞洁既没有给她所爱之人，也没有给与她有婚姻关系的陈浩宇，却被一个金人夺走。她越想越委屈，越想越恨，哭的也越发厉害。
[EN] The chastity she had guarded so hard had gone neither to the man she loved nor to Chen Haoyu, the man she was married to. A Jin man had taken it. The more she thought, the more wronged she felt, the more she hated, and the harder she cried.
[ZH] 完颜池坐在床榻上傻乐：“嘿嘿，没想到本王竟是你第一个男人，嘿嘿……”
[EN] Wanyan Chi sat on the bed, grinning like a fool. "Heh, heh. Who would have thought I was your first man? Heh, heh..."
[ZH] “呜呜！滚！滚！呜呜……！”千里寻边哭边喊。
[EN] "Wah! Get off! Get off! Wah...!" Qianli Xun shouted between sobs.
[ZH] 完颜池并不滚，这一晚，美人的心依旧没被他征服，反而是他，成为美人的第一个男人而倍加珍惜。他把被子盖在美人的身上：“小心冻着。”
[EN] He did not get off. All night, the beauty's heart had stayed unconquered. Instead, he had become her first man, and that made him cherish her all the more. He drew the quilt over her. "Don't catch cold."
[ZH] 千里寻掀开盖在身上的被子：“我就是冻死，也不想再见到你！呜呜……！”
[EN] Qianli Xun flung the quilt back. "I'd rather freeze to death than see you again! Wah...!"
[ZH] 完颜池既开心又担心：“别哭了，从今往后你是本王的人，本王会对你加倍好，等大金拿下汴梁，带你回会宁，本王发誓，有了你以后，身边不会再多一个女人。”
[EN] Wanyan Chi was pleased and worried at once. "Stop crying. From now on you're mine. I'll treat you twice as well. When Jin takes Bianliang, I'll take you back to Huining. I swear it: once I have you, there'll be no other woman by my side."
[ZH] “呜呜！我不会跟你去会宁，我要回临安，呜呜……！”
[EN] "Wah! I won't go to Huining with you. I want to go back to Lin'an. Wah...!"
[ZH] “好好，你不想去会宁，本王陪你待在燕京，等战事结束后，再带你去临安，正好拜见岳父岳母。”
[EN] "All right, all right. If you don't want Huining, I'll stay with you in Yanjing. When the fighting ends, I'll take you to Lin'an. We can call on your father and mother then."
[ZH] 千里寻委屈到说不出一句话，大家若知道她与一个金人圆房，不知该如何看待她？还是不回去得好。
[EN] Qianli Xun was too wounded to speak. If people learned she had consummated with a Jin man, what would they think of her? Better not to go back at all.
[ZH] 完颜池见怎么哄都没用，干脆躺在床上：“你继续哭，本王睡会儿。”
[EN] Wanyan Chi saw that coaxing did no good. He lay down. "Cry all you like. I'll sleep a while."
[ZH] 完颜池躺下继续说：“真想不通，本王这么优秀，多少女人想跟本王，她们要被本王这样，都不知乐成什么样，你却委屈，好像本王做错了什么事……”
[EN] He lay there and went on. "I can't understand it. I'm such a fine catch. So many women want me. If I did this to them, they'd be over the moon. But you're wronged, as if I'd done something wrong..."
[ZH] 完颜池说着困意袭来，伴着美人的哭声进入梦乡。
[EN] As he spoke, sleep came over him. He drifted off to the sound of the beauty's crying.
[ZH] 千里寻哭着哭着感到冷意袭来，拽过被子盖在自己身上。
[EN] Qianli Xun cried until cold crept over her. She dragged the quilt onto herself.
[ZH] 哭声越来越小，她开始思考接下来的人生。
[EN] Her sobs died down. She began to think about the life ahead of her.
[ZH] 她怨自己没有勇气结束生命，既如此还要生活下去，难道必须当完颜池的妃子？她根本不乐意。
[EN] She hated herself for not having the courage to end it. Since she had to go on living, did that mean she must become Wanyan Chi's consort? She wanted no part of it.
[ZH] 她的身体还清晰地记着他对待她的感觉，毫无快乐可言，只有屈辱，还有一种珍惜了多年的宝贝被贼人夺走的恨！因恨千里寻把被子全部拉到自己身上：“冻死你！”
[EN] Her body still remembered how he had handled her. No pleasure in it. Only shame. And the hatred of a treasure she had guarded for years, stolen by a thief! Hate made her yank the whole quilt to her side. "Freeze, then!"
[ZH] 被子带来的暖意像微醺的酒酿，她忍不住打起盹来，靠在榻边，带着屈辱的情绪睡去。
[EN] The quilt's warmth was like wine going to her head. She dozed off against the edge of the bed, still wrapped in shame.
[ZH] 天快亮时，完颜池被冻醒，发现身上的被子被美人拉去裹着，她靠在床榻边睡得正酣。
[EN] Near dawn, cold woke Wanyan Chi. The beauty had pulled the quilt off him and wrapped herself in it. She was deep asleep, propped against the side of the bed.
[ZH] 完颜池坐起来，面带笑容欣赏了一会儿美人的睡姿，自语道：“你睡着的样子也这么美，不哭不闹多好，本王扶你躺下。”
[EN] Wanyan Chi sat up and watched her sleep, smiling. "Even asleep you're lovely. Much better when you're not crying or fighting. Let me lay you down properly."
[ZH] 完颜池伸手去抱美人，想将她平放在床上，好睡得舒服些。
[EN] He reached to lift her, meaning to lay her flat on the bed so she would sleep more comfortably.
[ZH] 千里寻在梦中感受到有一双万恶之手向她袭击而来，立刻惊醒，发现完颜池已将她抱住。
[EN] In a dream, Qianli Xun felt a pair of wicked hands come at her. She woke with a start to find Wanyan Chi holding her.
[ZH] “你要做什么？”
[EN] "What are you doing?"
[ZH] “这样睡不舒服，本王想帮你躺好。”
[EN] "This position isn't comfortable. I want to help you lie properly."
[ZH] “别碰我！”千里寻挣脱开来，继续靠在床榻边。
[EN] "Don't touch me!" She broke free and leaned back against the bed's edge.
[ZH] 完颜池很无奈：“放心，以后不经过你同意，本王不会随便碰你。本王去别的屋子睡，好好休息。记着，本王是爱你才迁就你，不是怕你。”
[EN] Wanyan Chi had no answer to that. "Don't worry. From now on I won't touch you without your leave. I'll sleep in another room. Rest well. Remember: I give in to you because I love you, not because I fear you."
[ZH] “哼！等我功力恢复，你只会怕我。”
[EN] "Hah! Once my skill comes back, you'll be the one afraid."
[ZH] “恢复了本王也不怕，因为本王是你的第一个男人。”
[EN] "Even then I won't fear you. I'm your first man."
[ZH] “还不快滚！”
[EN] "Then why don't you get out!"
[ZH] “这就滚，你跟本王有了第一次，应该还会想有第二次甚至无数次，想本王了差人来唤，本王随时奉陪。”
[EN] "I'm going. We've had a first time. You'll want a second, and then countless more. When you miss me, send someone to call. I'll come whenever you wish."
[ZH] “滚！滚！”千里寻大喊着，声音近似歇斯底里。
[EN] "Get out! Get out!" Qianli Xun screamed, her voice near hysteria.
[ZH] 完颜池则心花怒放，吹着口哨离开。
[EN] Wanyan Chi's heart bloomed. He whistled as he left.
[ZH] 确定完颜池离开后，千里寻才安心躺下，眼泪流个不停。
[EN] Only when she was sure he was gone did Qianli Xun lie back. Tears kept running.
[ZH] 睡着后，还有一滴泪从眼角滚落下来，若有人看到会认为梦中的她正在哭泣。
[EN] After she fell asleep, another tear rolled from the corner of her eye. Anyone seeing it would have thought she was crying in her dream.
[ZH] 睡到午时醒来，在丫鬟的伺候下梳洗完毕，简单用过午饭，她要求道：“我想出去走走。”
[EN] She woke near noon. The maids helped her wash and dress. She ate a simple lunch, then asked, "I want to go out for a walk."
[ZH] 会兰娜说：“小王爷吩咐过，姑娘已自由，想去哪里都可以。”
[EN] Huilanna said, "The Young Prince gave orders. You're free, miss. You may go wherever you like."
[ZH] 被关了许久，每日都渴望自由，可这自由是用她的贞洁换来，想着都心痛。
[EN] She had been shut up so long, had longed for freedom every day. And this freedom had cost her chastity. The thought made her chest ache.
[ZH] “今日阳光不错，晒晒太阳，我的心情也许会好些。”
[EN] "The sun's good today. A little sun might lift my mood."
[ZH] 会兰娜取来厚厚的斗篷给她披上，陪她走出屋外。
[EN] Huilanna brought a thick cloak and draped it over her, then went out with her.
[ZH] 眼前的冬季既熟悉又陌生。熟悉的是冬日的寒冷，连高空挂着的红日都无法驱赶走寒意；陌生的是，北方的冬日冷得刺骨，也更显萧瑟，王府里全是光秃秃的树木，连一株梅花都没有，毫无生气。
[EN] The winter before her was familiar and strange. Familiar: the cold, which the red sun hanging high in the sky could not drive off. Strange: the northern cold bit to the bone and looked more barren still. The manor was all bare trees, not one plum branch, nothing alive.
[ZH] 千里寻踩踏着落叶，想起幽林别居，也到处栽种着树木，比燕京王府的树木还多，她曾在冬日踩踏着落叶走过，此时回忆起却比这里的树木更令她心动。
[EN] Qianli Xun walked over fallen leaves. She thought of Youlin Retreat, where trees grew everywhere, more than in this prince's manor in Yanjing. She had once walked through fallen leaves there in winter. Remembering those trees now stirred her more than these did.
[ZH] 她带着情绪自语道：“王府看着挺大，景致却不怎么样。”
[EN] She said to herself, bitterness in her voice, "The manor looks big enough. There's nothing to see."
[ZH] 会兰娜说：“现在冬季，没有好景致也正常。奴婢听说姑娘是从南边来的，自然见过不少好景致，王府里光秃秃的树木居多，肯定与江南水乡没法比。”
[EN] Huilanna said, "It's winter. No wonder there's no fine view. I hear you come from the south, miss. You must have seen many fine views. The bare trees here can't compare with the water towns of Jiangnan."
[ZH] 提到“水乡”，会兰娜想起一处景致来：“我带姑娘去王府的另一处，姑娘绝对喜欢。”
[EN] At the words "water towns," Huilanna remembered a place. "Let me take you to another part of the manor. You'll love it, miss."
[ZH] “哪一处？”
[EN] "Which part?"
[ZH] 会兰娜怕照实说姑娘不乐意去，只说：“姑娘随我去便知。”
[EN] Afraid the truth would make the girl refuse, Huilanna said only, "Come with me and see."
[ZH] 为了心情好些，千里寻点头答应，跟在会兰娜身后。
[EN] To lift her mood, Qianli Xun nodded and followed.
[ZH] 燕京的王府很大，绕了一大圈，会兰娜才停下：“姑娘，到了。”
[EN] The prince's manor in Yanjing was vast. They walked a long way around before Huilanna stopped. "We're here, miss."
[ZH] 千里寻瞧去，有一圆形的池子，池子里是枯萎的荷花，池水的表面散落着枫叶。岸边栽种着几棵枫树，满树原本红彤彤的枫叶已尽显凋零。
[EN] Qianli Xun looked. A round pond, withered lotus in it, maple leaves scattered across the water. A few maples grew on the bank; their red leaves, once a blaze, were now falling away.
[ZH] 离枫树不远处，栽种着一片菊花，可惜也枯萎。菊花的旁边是三棵橘树，绿意盎然，为毫无生命气息的冬季增色不少。绿色的叶子中间时不时挂着一颗颗黄橘。橘在北方应为枳，难以下咽，王府栽种橘树应该仅仅为观赏。
[EN] Not far from the maples stood a bed of chrysanthemums, also withered, poor things. Beside them, three orange trees, green and thriving, gave the lifeless winter some color. Yellow oranges hung here and there among the leaves. In the north the fruit should have been the bitter trifoliate orange, barely edible. The manor must have planted these trees only to look at.
[ZH] 此情此景，加上无所安放的心境，千里寻想起朱淑真的一首诗《初冬书怀》，有感而发：
[EN] The sight, and a heart with nowhere to rest, brought Zhu Shuzhen's poem "Writing My Heart in Early Winter" to Qianli Xun's mind. It fitted, and she recited it:
[ZH] “触目圆池景，荷枯菊已荒。风寒侵夜枕，霜冻怯晨妆。江上风翻赤，庭前橘带黄。题诗欲排闷，对景倍悲伤。”
[EN] Before my eyes, the round pond: lotus withered, chrysanthemums gone to waste. Wind and cold bite through the night pillow; frost makes me dread the morning mirror. On the river, wind rolls up red leaves; before the courtyard, the oranges are yellow. I write a poem to push the gloom away, but facing this scene, sorrow doubles.
[ZH] 传来一阵拍手的声音，千里寻顺着声音看去，完颜池一边拍手一边朝她走来。
[EN] A burst of clapping came. Qianli Xun turned toward it. Wanyan Chi was walking to her, clapping.
[ZH] 她向会兰娜露出询的目光。
[EN] She shot Huilanna a questioning look.
[ZH] 会兰娜解释道：“此处景致好些，小王爷就住在旁边。”
[EN] Huilanna explained, "The view is better here. The Young Prince lives just next door."
[ZH] 完颜池走近后，停下拍手的动作：“没想到本王喜欢的人还会作诗，更令本王刮目相看。”
[EN] Wanyan Chi came close and stopped clapping. "I never guessed the woman I like could write poetry. You've made me see you in a new light."
[ZH] 千里寻不由自主想起昨晚他在床上对待自己的情景，既厌恶又羞愧，转身打算离开。
[EN] Qianli Xun could not help remembering how he had used her in bed the night before. Revulsion and shame. She turned to leave.
[ZH] 完颜池堵在面前：“既然想逛，本王陪你。”
[EN] Wanyan Chi stepped into her path. "Since you wanted a walk, I'll go with you."
[ZH] “有你陪伴，我会更难受，盼着药劲儿尽快消失，好恢复如初，杀了你再回临安。”
[EN] "With you beside me I'll feel worse. I only wish the drug would wear off soon, so I can be myself again, kill you, and go back to Lin'an."
[ZH] “你这么恨本王？”
[EN] "You hate me that much?"
[ZH] “我恨不得扒了你的皮！吃了你的肉！啃了你的骨！”
[EN] "I want to flay you! Eat your flesh! Gnaw your bones!"
[ZH] 完颜池伤感道：“你竟如此恨本王？既如此，本王把解药给你，现在就动手！”
[EN] Wanyan Chi looked hurt. "You hate me so? Then I'll give you the antidote. Right now!"
[ZH] 完颜池从怀里掏出一瓶药，倒出一粒：“会兰娜，把药丸拿给她吃。”
[EN] He took a bottle from his robe and tipped out one pill. "Huilanna, give her the pill."
[ZH] 会兰娜掏出干净的巾帕，接过小王爷手中的药丸，送至姑娘面前。
[EN] Huilanna took a clean handkerchief, accepted the pill from the Young Prince, and held it out to the girl.
[ZH] 千里寻盯着那丸药，甚是怀疑，完颜池该不会又使什么奸诈计谋害她？
[EN] Qianli Xun stared at it, suspicious. Was Wanyan Chi up to another trick?
[ZH] 完颜池见美人犹豫要不要吃下，宽慰道：“本王不会再骗你。”
[EN] He saw her hesitate. "I won't lie to you again."
[ZH] 千里寻依然不信：“你会愿意早些送死？”
[EN] She still did not believe him. "You'd want to die sooner?"
[ZH] “能死在你的拳下，乐意之至。杀了本王后，替本王找到娘亲，把她带到本王坟前，也好让本王死而无憾。”
[EN] "To die under your fist? Gladly. After you kill me, find my mother and bring her to my grave. Then I can die without regret."
[ZH] “好！老娘定会满足你！”
[EN] "Good! I'll see you get your wish!"
[ZH] 千里寻拿起药丸，放入口内吞下。
[EN] Qianli Xun took the pill, put it in her mouth, and swallowed.
[ZH] 没过多久，全身的肌肉变得松快起来，她试着攥紧拳头，完颜池果然没骗她，她的功力已恢复。
[EN] Before long, her muscles loosened. She tried clenching her fist. He had told the truth. Her skill had returned.
[ZH] 想到昨晚的屈辱，千里寻将力量集中在拳头，使出一拳，向完颜池冲去。
[EN] The shame of the night before rose in her. She drove power into her fist and lunged at Wanyan Chi.
[ZH] 完颜池出拳做出应战的准备，当美人的靠近他时，他却收起动作，迎接美人袭击而来的拳头。
[EN] Wanyan Chi threw up a fist, ready to fight. But as the beauty closed in, he dropped his guard and met her incoming fist with his chest.
[ZH] 对方突然收起所有防备，千里寻感到意外，她袭击而出的拳头已收不回，关键她从未想过要收回，一拳落在完颜池的胸口。
[EN] He had given up all defense without warning. Qianli Xun was taken aback. Her fist was already on its way. The truth was, she had never thought of pulling it back. It landed on his chest.
[ZH] 完颜池口吐鲜血，跪在地上，却面带笑容：“一拳……还要不了……本王的命，再……再来……再来一拳。”
[EN] Blood sprayed from Wanyan Chi's mouth. He fell to his knees, smiling. "One punch... won't... kill me. Again... come again... one more."
[ZH] 千里寻从来没遇到过主动求死之人，她变得犹豫。
[EN] Qianli Xun had never met a man who begged for death. She faltered.
[ZH] 王府的侍卫聚拢而来，手执刀剑准备保护小王爷。
[EN] The manor guards gathered, blades drawn, ready to protect the Young Prince.
[ZH] 完颜池命令道：“都……都……退到一边！她是……本王的人，杀……本王……本王……心甘情愿……不许……拦……”
[EN] Wanyan Chi gave the order. "All... all of you, stand aside! She's... mine. If she kills me... I... consent... don't... stop her..."
[ZH] 如此好的机会杀死夺走自己贞洁之人，千里寻不再犹豫：“既然你主动送死，老娘现在送你归西！”
[EN] Here was her chance to kill the man who had taken her chastity. She faltered no longer. "You ask for death. I'll send you on your way now!"
[ZH] 完颜池说：“能成为……你的第一个……男人，本王……死而无憾。”
[EN] Wanyan Chi said, "To be... your first man... I die... content."
[ZH] 千里寻更加气愤：“现在就去死！”
[EN] That made her angrier. "Then die!"
[ZH] 千里寻继续出拳向完颜池袭击而去，拳头快要击打在完颜池的脑门上时，她的腿被会兰娜从身后抱住。
[EN] She struck again. Her fist was inches from his forehead when Huilanna caught her leg from behind.
[ZH] “姑娘！您不能杀小王爷！他是好人！”
[EN] "Miss! You can't kill the Young Prince! He's a good man!"
[ZH] 拳头的力度冲击着气流扑面而来，完颜池不由闭上眼睛。听到会兰娜的为他求情，他睁开眼，美人的拳头就在眼前。
[EN] The force of the punch pushed air into his face. Wanyan Chi shut his eyes. Hearing Huilanna plead for him, he opened them. The beauty's fist hung right before him.
[ZH] “会兰娜……别……拦着，牡丹花下死……做鬼也……风流，我……求之不得。”
[EN] "Huilanna... don't... stop her. To die beneath the peonies... even as a ghost, to die a lover. I... ask for nothing better."
[ZH] “不！小王爷！没有您，我阿爹早已病死，是您请巫医救了他，还分文不取，又让我到王府当下人，每月挣的钱够养活爹娘，您就是我全家的恩人，我不能眼睁睁看着恩人死在我面前。姑娘，您有任何气，尽管撒在我身上，一拳打死我也行，我不过是个奴婢，死不足惜，可若您杀了王爷，天下就少了一位有能力帮助可怜人的王爷。您不知道，在会宁小王爷乐善好施，救济穷人，老百姓都对小王爷夸赞有加，若姑娘杀了小王爷，只怕会被人唾骂。”
[EN] "No! Young Prince! Without you, my father would have died of sickness long ago. You fetched a shaman-healer and saved him, and took no payment. You let me work here in the manor. The wages feed my parents. You're the blessing of my whole family. I can't stand by and watch my benefactor die before me. Miss, if you're angry, take it out on me. Kill me with one punch if you like. I'm only a slave; my life is worth nothing. But if you kill the prince, the world loses a man with the power to help the poor. You don't know: back in Huining, the Young Prince gave to the needy and relieved the poor. People sang his praises. If you kill him, they'll curse you for it."
[ZH] 会兰娜的一番言论直击千里寻的肺腑，她的拳头不由软下来，她仍旧撑住，继续用拳头对准完颜池：“被金人唾骂是宋人的荣耀。”
[EN] Huilanna's words struck home. Qianli Xun's fist softened. She kept it raised, still pointed at Wanyan Chi. "Being cursed by Jin people is an honor to a Song."
[ZH] 周围的侍卫见状全部放下刀剑，跪在地上，齐声求道：“求姑娘放了小王爷！”
[EN] The guards saw it and laid down their blades. They knelt and said with one voice, "Please spare the Young Prince, miss!"
[ZH] 千里寻环顾四周，厉声问道：“你们为何跪下？”
[EN] Qianli Xun looked around. "Why are you kneeling?" she demanded.
[ZH] 带头的侍卫说：“姑娘若杀了小王爷，我等会为他报仇！”
[EN] The guard in front said, "If you kill the Young Prince, we'll avenge him."
[ZH] 千里寻十分不屑：“哼！老娘岂会怕你们？”
[EN] She sneered. "Hah! As if I feared you?"
[ZH] 带头的侍卫继续说：“我等的确不是姑娘的对手，但我等会将报仇之事牢记在心，代代相传，杀不了姑娘，只要您的家人和后辈在，此仇亦可报得。姑娘拳头厉害，不敢保证您的家人和后辈也会像您一样厉害。”
[EN] The guard went on. "We're no match for you, it's true. But we'll pass the duty down, from son to son. If we can't kill you, we'll have your kin and your descendants. Your fist is strong. There's no guarantee theirs will be."
[ZH] “老娘今日偏要杀了他！看你们能将我怎样？”千里寻口中说着狂言，拳头却还停留在原地。
[EN] "I'll kill him today! Let's see what you can do!" The boast left her mouth, but her fist stayed where it was.
[ZH] 完颜池趁机说：“别……吓唬她，我死了……谁都不许……报仇！”
[EN] Wanyan Chi seized the moment. "Don't... frighten her. If I die... nobody... is to avenge me!"
[ZH] 千里寻的拳头彻底泄气，她收了回去：“你的命先给老娘留着，哪一日老娘能下得去狠手，再来索取！”
