[EN] All the strength went out of her fist. She drew it back. "Keep your life for now. When I can bring myself to strike hard, I'll come and claim it."
[ZH] 小王爷的命保住，会兰娜松口气放开手，见姑娘往回走，爬起来向小王爷行礼后迅速跟上。
[EN] With the Young Prince's life safe, Huilanna let go. She saw the girl walk away, scrambled up, bowed to the Young Prince, and hurried after her.
[ZH] 完颜池整个人松懈下来，笑着自语道：“就知道……你……不忍心杀本王。”
[EN] Wanyan Chi went limp. "I knew... you... couldn't bring yourself to kill me," he said, smiling.
[ZH] 千里寻恨自己，既不忍心杀掉别人眼中的好人，又不愿接受他的感情，还让对方夺去她的贞洁，接下来她该如何是好？千里寻打算离开燕京的王府。
[EN] Qianli Xun hated herself. She could not kill a man others called good. She could not accept his love. And she had let him take her chastity. What was she to do now? She decided to leave the prince's manor in Yanjing.
[ZH] 离开后该去哪里？回临安的陈家？她已失去贞节，回去后如何面对陈浩宇？
[EN] Where would she go? Back to the Chen family in Lin'an? She had lost her virtue. How could she face Chen Haoyu?
[ZH] 要不去汴梁找仪王？不，她去了会打扰梦容的好日子，说不定又惹得她旧病复发，再说她已失去贞洁，已经没有资格念着心中的感情。
[EN] Or to Bianliang, to Prince Yi? No. She would only disturb Mengrong's good life, and might bring on her old illness again. Besides, she had lost her chastity. She had no right to nurse the feelings in her heart.
[ZH] 实在不行回临安的朱府，她立即否认，回朱府与回陈家有何区别？
[EN] If worst came to worst, back to Zhu Manor in Lin'an? She rejected the thought at once. How was Zhu Manor any different from the Chen house?
[ZH] 到底该去哪里？她无法做决定，反正她不想再见到完颜池，只要看不到他即可，也许她会渐渐忘记不愉快的经历。
[EN] Where then? She could not decide. All she knew was that she did not want to see Wanyan Chi again. As long as she never saw him, perhaps the ugly memory would fade.
[ZH] 千里寻回到临时住的屋子，想要收拾包袱就此离去，四下环顾，没有一样东西属于她，有什么好收拾的？
[EN] Qianli Xun went back to the room she had been given and started to pack so she could leave. She looked around. Nothing there belonged to her. What was there to pack?
[ZH] 她转身向屋外走去。
[EN] She turned and walked out.
[ZH] 会兰娜跟上去问：“姑娘要去哪里？”
[EN] Huilanna hurried after her. "Where are you going, miss?"
[ZH] “告诉完颜池，老娘要离开王府，让他不许拦着。”
[EN] "Tell Wanyan Chi I'm leaving the manor. He's not to stop me."
[ZH] 千里寻说完大踏步往门口走。
[EN] She finished speaking and strode toward the gate.
[ZH] 会兰娜则急忙赶着去向王爷禀报。
[EN] Huilanna rushed off to report to the prince.

#CH 344
[ZH] 走到王府的门口时，被一群侍卫挡住。
[EN] At the gate of the prince's mansion, a troop of guards blocked her way.
[ZH] 千里寻二话不说与侍卫打起来，她把每个侍卫看作完颜池，将所有怒气发泄出去。
[EN] Qianli Xun didn't waste a word. She waded into them, every guard she saw becoming Wanyan Chi in her eyes, every ounce of her rage poured out.
[ZH] 全部侍卫被打趴下时，完颜池赶来，他服用过疗伤的药物，看上去精神许多。
[EN] By the time the last of them lay flat on the ground, Wanyan Chi came hurrying up. He had taken his medicine and looked much better.
[ZH] “你要离本王而去？”
[EN] "So you mean to leave me?"
[ZH] 千里寻背对着完颜池：“老娘既然不忍心杀你，干脆眼不见心为净，奉劝你别拦着老娘。”
[EN] Qianli Xun kept her back to him. "Since I can't bring myself to kill you, I'll just stay out of your sight. Do yourself a favor and don't block my way."
[ZH] “你难道对本王没有一丝留恋？”
[EN] "Have you no attachment to me at all?"
[ZH] “哼！笑话！老娘为何要留恋你？”
[EN] "Hah! Don't make me laugh. What would I have to cling to you for?"
[ZH] 完颜池心酸不已：“好，既然本王留不住你，放你走，你须记着，本王之所以放你走，是因为爱你。”
[EN] Wanyan Chi's heart twisted. "Fine. If I can't keep you, I'll let you go. But remember this: I'm letting you go because I love you."
[ZH] 千里寻转过身：“呸！少拿爱说事！老娘不吃这一套！你打着爱的名义欺负老娘，别以为你得手，老娘会像其他女人一样黏着你，小鸟依人喊你一声‘小王爷’，做你的春秋大梦！老娘只会选择自己认为正确的人，而你，不在老娘选择的范围之内，即使你将老娘折磨得遍体鳞伤，老娘也不会屈服！”
[EN] Qianli Xun spun around. "Pah! Spare me your talk of love. I don't buy it. You bully me in love's name, and don't flatter yourself that I'll turn soft and clingy like the other women, cooing 'my little prince' and hanging on your sleeve. Dream on! I choose only the man I judge to be right. You're not in my range of choices. Even if you beat me black and blue, I will never bend."
[ZH] 完颜池被骂醒，嘴上仍旧不服软：“无论你走到哪里，都改变不了本王是你第一个男人的事实。”
[EN] The scolding jolted Wanyan Chi awake, though his mouth still refused to yield. "Wherever you go, one thing won't change: I was your first man."
[ZH] 对方再次戳痛她的软肋，千里寻本想打一架，为了顺利离开，她忍了，默无声息地转过身，向大门外走去。
[EN] He had found the soft spot again. She itched to fight him, but to get out cleanly she swallowed it, turned without a sound, and walked toward the gate.
[ZH] 完颜池后悔方才说出的话，一时善心大发，提醒道：“喂！大宋的仪王曾带着一千万两来过，本王骗他你死了，还讹了他一千万两，银票放在我屋里，不信我带你去瞧？”
[EN] Regret washed over him. A sudden fit of kindness seized him. "Hey! The Prince Yi of Great Song came here once with ten million taels. I told him you were dead and took him for another ten million. The banknotes are in my room. Don't believe me? I'll take you to see."
[ZH] 千里寻停下脚步，泪水夺眶而出。他真的来救她，恐怕汴梁的王府和他名下的财产难保。他为她付出太多，而她已不是昨日的她，根本不值得他如此。
[EN] Qianli Xun stopped short. Tears burst from her. He really had come to save her. The mansion in Bianliang and all his holdings would likely be forfeit now. He had done so much for her, and she was no longer the woman she had been—she was worth none of it.
[ZH] 既然完颜池说她死了，就让他以为她已死，才能安心过自己的日子，再说昨日的她的确已死。
[EN] Since Wanyan Chi believed her dead, she would let him go on believing it. Only then could he live his life in peace. Besides, the woman she had been truly was dead.
[ZH] 完颜池继续说：“本王既然喜欢你，希望你离开后一切都好，去找他吧，看得出他对你是真心，你跟他在一起，本王也可安心。”
[EN] Wanyan Chi went on. "Because I love you, I want the best for you once you're gone. Go to him. I can see his feelings for you are real. If you're with him, I can rest easy."
[ZH] 千里寻并未理会，径直走出王府。
[EN] Qianli Xun didn't answer. She walked straight out of the mansion.
[ZH] 完颜池呆呆地盯着门口不愿离去，好不容易喜欢一个人，不得已放她离去，他再不甘心也无可奈何。
[EN] Wanyan Chi stood staring at the empty gate, unwilling to move. He had finally cared for someone, and now he had no choice but to let her go. However much he resented it, there was nothing he could do.
[ZH] 陆远峰从外面进来，见小王爷一直瞧着门口，十分纳闷：“小王爷为何一直站在这里？”
[EN] Lu Yuanfeng came in from outside and found the young prince still staring at the gate. "Why are you standing here, my lord?"
[ZH] “你师傅走了，悄悄跟上她，别让她发现，等她安顿好再向我报告。”
[EN] "Your master has gone. Follow her quietly, don't let her notice, and report back when she's settled."
[ZH] “她恐怕要回临安。”
[EN] "She's likely heading for Lin'an."
[ZH] “不管她去哪里，你都跟着，直到她安顿下来。”
[EN] "Wherever she goes, follow her until she's settled."
[ZH] “是。”
[EN] "Yes, sir."
[ZH] ……
[EN] …
[ZH] 听到淑真已死的消息，夏老太太再也等不下去，咽了气，陈家人痛哭之余上上下下忙着办理丧事。
[EN] When word reached Old Madam Xia that Shuzhen was dead, she could hold on no longer and breathed her last. The Chen family wept, then everyone busied themselves with the funeral.
[ZH] 既失去娘子，又没了奶奶，陈浩宇受到双重打击，心力交瘁，依然坚持给奶奶守棂。
[EN] Bereft of his wife and then his grandmother, Chen Haoyu took the double blow hard. He was worn to the bone, yet he kept his vigil at her coffin.
[ZH] 丧事有他父亲陈江林和两个叔叔操办，陈浩宇省心许多，他只需和堂弟陈浩飞跪在灵堂两侧接待前来吊唁之人。
[EN] His father Chen Jianglin and two uncles handled the arrangements, which lifted a weight off him. All he had to do was kneel with his cousin Chen Haofei on either side of the mourning hall and receive those who came to pay their respects.
[ZH] 两年前，陈浩飞参加了朝廷的殿试，考了个三甲。因夏老太太与梁师慧的关系，梁师成并未因女词人之事牵连陈家。
[EN] Two years earlier, Chen Haofei had sat the palace examination and placed in the third rank. Because of Old Madam Xia's connection to Liang Shihui, Liang Shicheng had not dragged the Chen family into the woman poet's case.
[ZH] 怎奈陈浩飞是耿直之人，看不惯朝廷之人趋炎附势，拉帮结派，更看不惯官家昏庸，在朝中得罪不少人，经过一番思量后辞官回乡。
[EN] But Chen Haofei was a straight-backed man. He could not stomach the way courtiers curried favor and formed cliques, still less His Majesty's muddle-headedness. He made enemies at court and, after much thought, resigned and went home.
[ZH] 刚回来时陈江木和崔氏总是埋怨儿子，问他读了这么多年书只为考功名，好不容易考上，为何轻易辞官？
[EN] When he first returned, Chen Jiangmu and Mrs. Cui never stopped grumbling. All those years of study, they said, just to pass the exam—and now that he had, he threw it away?
[ZH] 陈浩飞不愿多加解释，一心一意跟随大伯和浩哥学起做生意来，他时常慨叹早知如此应该早些放弃考取功名的想法。
[EN] Chen Haofei didn't care to explain. He threw himself into learning the trade from his elder uncle and his cousin Haoyu, often sighing that he should have given up the idea of an official career sooner.
[ZH] 在临安新知府的管理下，陈家的生意虽没有以前好，总比整日混在官场勾心斗角来得实在，陈浩飞越来越安于忙碌陈家的生意。
[EN] Under Lin'an's new prefect, the family business wasn't what it had been, but it beat day after day of court intrigue. Chen Haofei grew content with the busy work.
[ZH] 女儿蹲了将近两年的牢狱，又被金人抓去，现在连人都没了，朱晞颜比谁都痛心，把自己锁在屋里不愿见人。
[EN] His daughter had spent nearly two years in prison, then been seized by the Jin, and now she was gone. No one grieved more than Zhu Xiyan. He shut himself in his room and refused to see anyone.
[ZH] 向夫人本来已好些，听到淑真没了，再次病倒。
[EN] Madam Xiang had been improving. The news of Shuzhen's death laid her low again.
[ZH] 陈文心既要伺候婆婆，还得揪心奶奶的丧事。安排好丫鬟后，才放心离开朱府带着朱景元去陈家奔丧。
[EN] Chen Wenxin had to tend her mother-in-law and worry over Old Madam Xia's funeral besides. Only after she had settled the maids did she leave Zhu Manor with Zhu Jingyuan for the Chen house.
[ZH] 夏老太太被葬两日后，朱家的老祖宗也圆寂。
[EN] Two days after Old Madam Xia was buried, the Zhu family's old ancestor passed away as well.
[ZH] 秀珍嬷嬷前来报信时，朱晞颜才走出屋子，整个人看上去十分憔悴。
[EN] It was Matron Xiuzhen who came with the news. Only then did Zhu Xiyan come out of his room, looking utterly haggard.
[ZH] 秀珍嬷嬷说：“老祖宗走得十分安详，她是预知时至，让我告诉朱府老爷，不用为她大操大办，尸身火化后将骨灰葬在朱家的墓园即可。”
[EN] Matron Xiuzhen said, "The old ancestor went very peacefully. She knew her hour had come. She told me to tell the master of Zhu Manor not to make a great show of it—cremate the body and bury the ashes in the Zhu family cemetery, that's all."
[ZH] 朱晞颜一切照办，葬了老祖宗后，想到女儿还葬在他乡，不免老泪纵横。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan did as she asked. After the old ancestor was buried, he thought of his daughter lying in a strange land, and tears ran down his old face.
[ZH] 调整两日后，朱晞颜亲自到陈府拜访，告诉陈江林和姚轻琰，自家女儿没福分，希望他们劝说陈浩宇放下淑真，再娶个姑娘进门。
[EN] After two days of quiet, Zhu Xiyan called at the Chen residence in person. He told Chen Jianglin and Yao Qingyan that his daughter had been denied a good fate, and asked them to persuade Haoyu to let Shuzhen go and take another wife.
[ZH] 陈江林和姚轻琰点头答应，等朱晞颜走后，都不好开口劝说儿子，两人商量等春节过后再说。
[EN] Chen Jianglin and Yao Qingyan nodded and agreed, but once he was gone neither could find the words. They settled on waiting until after the New Year.
[ZH] 自从夏老太太的丧事办完，陈浩宇待在墨香苑把自己关进素心草堂，身边只有青瓜陪伴。
[EN] Once Old Madam Xia's funeral was over, Chen Haoyu shut himself in the Suxin Study in Moxiang Courtyard with only Qinggua for company.
[ZH] 他躺在娘子睡过的床榻上，回想着他们的过往，一遍一遍回忆娘子在狱中吻他的场景，想着想着，他抱着枕头哭起来。
[EN] He lay on the bed his wife had slept in, going over their past again and again—the way she had kissed him in prison. He hugged a pillow and cried.
[ZH] 青瓜并不安慰，公子心里苦，哭出来会好些。
[EN] Qinggua said nothing. His master's heart was aching; a good cry would help.
[ZH] 一连几日，陈浩宇除吃饭上茅厕，只知躺在娘子的床上，连陈家的生意都没有心情不理会。
[EN] For days on end, Chen Haoyu did nothing but lie on his wife's bed, aside from eating and using the privy. He couldn't even be bothered with the family business.
[ZH] 家人能理解浩宇的心情，都不会主动打扰他。
[EN] His family understood and left him alone.
[ZH] 姑娘入狱时，韦嬷嬷就开始发愁，将近两年的时间，她的黑发中已能明显看到白发。听到姑娘被金人害死，仅仅两日，有一半头发变白，整个人看上去老了许多。
[EN] Matron Wei had begun to worry the day the young mistress was imprisoned. In nearly two years, white hairs had crept plainly into her black. When the word came that the Jin had killed her, half her hair turned white in two days, and she looked years older.
[ZH] 姑娘与姑爷的婚姻早已取消，她之所以和苦荞以及十个下人能继续待在陈府，是因大家都知道取消婚姻之举不过是瞒天过海。
[EN] The young mistress's marriage had long been annulled. She and Kuqiao and the ten servants were still at the Chen house only because everyone knew the annulment was a blind to fool the Jin.
[ZH] 现在姑娘不在，韦嬷嬷认为没有理由在陈府待下去，她先向大老爷和夫人请示，希望他们理解并允许她带着姑娘的下人离开。
[EN] Now that she was gone, Matron Wei saw no reason to stay. She asked the master and madam first, hoping they would understand and let her take the young mistress's servants away.
[ZH] 姚轻琰瞧着韦嬷嬷憔悴样子，鼻子一酸，眼泪不由流出来：“此事还需浩宇同意。”
[EN] Yao Qingyan looked at her drawn face, her nose stung, and tears came before she could stop them. "This needs Haoyu's consent."
[ZH] 陈江林也说：“浩宇的意思就是我们的意思，你还是去问问他。他若同意尽可带人离开，他若不同意，留在陈家我和轻琰也高兴。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin added, "What Haoyu says goes. Ask him. If he agrees, take your people and go. If not, you're welcome to stay—Qingyan and I would be glad of it."
[ZH] 姚轻琰擦干眼泪说：“老太太走了，家里冷清许多，你要是留下，也能多个和我说话的人。”
[EN] Yao Qingyan dried her eyes. "The old madam is gone; the house is so quiet. If you stay, I'll have someone to talk to."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷谢过老爷和夫人后回到墨香苑，站在素心草堂外面，向里说：“姑爷可还在里面？”
[EN] Matron Wei thanked the master and madam and went back to Moxiang Courtyard. She stood outside the Suxin Study and called in, "Is the young master still inside?"
[ZH] 青瓜打开门，小声问：“嬷嬷有事？”
[EN] Qinggua opened the door and asked softly, "Matron, what is it?"
[ZH] “我有事请示姑爷。”
[EN] "I need to ask the young master something."
[ZH] “公子在里面躺着，谁都不见。”
[EN] "He's lying inside. He sees no one."
[ZH] “我在外面声音放大些说，姑爷肯定能听到。”
[EN] "I'll speak louder out here. He's bound to hear me."
[ZH] 青瓜无奈，默认了韦嬷嬷的想法，为了让公子清楚地听到，他有意把门开大些。
[EN] Qinggua had no choice but to let her, silently accepting the idea. He opened the door wider so his master could hear clearly.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷朝里面大声说：“姑爷……现在唤您一声姑爷已不合适，可我一时改不了，先这么唤着。姑娘没了，婚姻也没了，我与姑娘的下人继续待在陈府不合适，想向姑爷请示，过几日带着苦荞和十个下人回朱府。”
[EN] Matron Wei raised her voice. "Young master—'young master' no longer fits, I know, but I can't change my habit overnight, so forgive me. The mistress is gone, the marriage is gone. It wouldn't be right for me and her servants to stay on. I've come to ask permission: in a few days, I'd like to take Kuqiao and the ten servants back to Zhu Manor."
[ZH] 苦荞在悦宁屋听到韦嬷嬷的声音，从屋内出来，她的两只眼睛哭得又红又肿。
[EN] Kuqiao heard her from Yuening's room and came out, her eyes red and swollen from crying.
[ZH] 她来到韦嬷嬷身边：“我们真的要回朱府？我不相信小姐没了，她肯定还活着。”
[EN] She went to Matron Wei's side. "Are we really going back to Zhu Manor? I don't believe the young mistress is gone. She has to be alive."
[ZH] “傻丫头，我也希望姑娘活着。消息是仪王带回来的，他比谁都希望姑娘活着，难道他说的会有假？”
[EN] "Silly girl. I wish she were alive too. The word came from Prince Yi, and no one wanted her alive more than he did. Would he lie about it?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇从里屋出来，站在门口，整个人看上去气色比韦嬷嬷和苦荞更差。
[EN] Chen Haoyu stepped out of the inner room and stood in the doorway. He looked worse than Matron Wei or Kuqiao.
[ZH] “安心待在墨香苑，一个都不许走，她永远是我娘子，哪怕人不在也是我娘子。”
[EN] "Stay in Moxiang Courtyard. Not one of you leaves. She is my wife forever—gone or not, she is my wife."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷劝道：“姑爷此言差矣，墨香苑迟早会有新主人进来，我们继续待下去不合适。”
[EN] Matron Wei coaxed him. "You're wrong, young master. Moxiang Courtyard will have a new mistress sooner or later. It isn't right for us to go on here."
[ZH] “不会有人进来，永远不会……”陈浩宇嘟囔着继续进屋躺下。
[EN] "No one is coming. No one, ever…" He mumbled it and went back inside to lie down.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷对苦荞说：“本想我们主动离去，姑爷也好快些从痛苦中走出来开启新的生活，可姑爷他……唉，算了，先住着吧。”
[EN] Matron Wei said to Kuqiao, "I thought if we left of our own accord, it might help him climb out of his grief and start a new life. But he… ah, well. Let it be. We'll stay a while longer."
[ZH] 苦荞一边流泪一边说：“嬷嬷，红豆他们已经够难受，他们看到我的样子会更难过，能不能让我去你屋里坐会儿？”
[EN] Kuqiao cried as she spoke. "Matron, Hongdou and the others are hurting enough. Seeing me like this will only hurt them more. Could I sit in your room for a while?"
[ZH] “也好，陪我说说话，省得我老觉得姑娘在唤我。”
[EN] "Come, then. Keep me company. I keep thinking I hear the mistress calling me."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷的话更令苦荞伤感，她抹着眼泪跟在韦嬷嬷身后去了静宜堂。
[EN] Her words cut Kuqiao deeper. She wiped her eyes and followed Matron Wei to Jingyi Hall.
[ZH] ……
[EN] …
[ZH] 金人大举南下入侵大宋，直逼汴梁，大宋的朝廷乱作一团，官家急忙派人赴金营议和。完颜宗望根本不吃这一套，一心想攻下汴梁，继续迅速南下。
[EN] The Jin poured south in force, driving straight at Bianliang. The Song court was in chaos, and His Majesty hurriedly sent envoys to their camp to sue for peace. Wanyan Zongwang would hear none of it; he meant to take Bianliang and press south without pause.
[ZH] 赵佶手足无措，在大臣的建议下，传位于太子赵桓。
[EN] Zhao Ji, at his wits' end, followed his ministers' advice and passed the throne to the crown prince, Zhao Huan.
[ZH] 赵桓临危受命根本高兴不起来，父皇显然是撂挑子。他先是坚决不接受，又几次装作晕死过去，要么是大哭，都不奏效，最终在父皇的威逼之下走向皇位，大宋正式进入靖康元年。
[EN] Zhao Huan took the throne at the worst of moments and could hardly rejoice. His father was plainly washing his hands of it. He refused outright, then several times pretended to faint dead away, or broke into great tears—none of it worked. Under his father's pressure he finally mounted the throne, and Great Song entered the first year of Jingkang.
[ZH] 靖康元年正月，完颜宗翰率金兵东路军进至汴京城下。
[EN] In the first month of Jingkang, Wanyan Zonghan led the Jin eastern army to the walls of Bianliang.
[ZH] 太常少卿李纲亲自督战，几次打败攻城的金军；加之河北、山东等地义军奋起反抗，形势对金军十分不利，金军伤亡惨重，不得不撤退。
[EN] Li Gang, vice minister of the Court of Sacrificial Worship, directed the fighting in person and beat back the besiegers several times. Volunteer armies in Hebei and Shandong rose up as well. The tide turned against the Jin, who took heavy losses and had to withdraw.
[ZH] 完颜宗望不死心，施行诱降的计谋。北宋的朝堂立刻分为两派，一派主张割地求和，一派则主战。李纲坚决反对割地求和，结果被赵桓罢官。赵桓此举引起军民不满，在大家的抗议下再次启用李纲。
[EN] Wanyan Zongwang would not give up. He tried to lure the Song into surrender. The court split at once: one faction wanted to cede territory for peace, the other to fight. Li Gang opposed the cession tooth and nail, and Zhao Huan stripped him of office. The soldiers and people protested, and Zhao Huan reinstated him.
[ZH] 李刚带兵守护汴梁，完颜宗望无法得逞，最终大宋提出割让中山、河间、太原三镇后金军撤退。汴梁保卫战获胜，可抵不住投降派得势，李刚逼不得已离开汴梁。
[EN] Li Gang commanded the defense of Bianliang, and Wanyan Zongwang could make no headway. In the end Song offered the three towns of Zhongshan, Hejian, and Taiyuan, and the Jin withdrew. The defense of Bianliang was won—but the appeasers gained the upper hand, and Li Gang was forced to leave the city.
[ZH] 摇摇欲坠的朝廷得到喘息的机会，老百姓也暂时过上平凡的日子。
[EN] The teetering court caught its breath, and the common people found a few more ordinary days.
[ZH] 今年的春节过得毫无气氛，好在稀里糊涂也就过去。终于熬到开春，万物复苏，人的心情随之变得好些。
[EN] The New Year came and went without a spark of celebration; luckily, it passed in a daze. At last spring arrived, the world woke again, and hearts lifted a little.
[ZH] 整个冬季，陈浩宇都没理会过陈家的生意，他一直颓废落寞，要么一个坐着喝闷酒，要么把娘子写的诗词拿出来读。
[EN] All winter Chen Haoyu had ignored the business. He dragged about, low and listless—sitting alone with cup after cup of bitter wine, or taking out his wife's poems to read.
[ZH] 每读到娘子写下的一首诗词，仿佛能感受到娘子站在他身边一般。为了天天有这种感觉，他天天读娘子写下的诗词，竟然把大部分熟记在心。
[EN] Every poem she had written seemed to put her beside him again. To keep that feeling each day, he read her poems daily, until most of them were fixed in his memory.
[ZH] 他不用再借助纸张或诗集，随口便能念出。
[EN] He no longer needed paper or a collection. The lines came at a word's notice.
[ZH] 他边饮酒边念着娘子写的一首《卜算子·咏梅》：“竹里一枝斜，映带林逾静。雨后清奇画不成，浅水横疏影。吹彻小单于，心事思重省。拂拂风前度暗香，月色侵花冷。”
[EN] As he drank, he recited one of her *Busuanzi: Ode to the Plum Blossom*: "One bough leans sideways deep in the bamboo grove, / lending the quiet woods a stillness deeper still. / After rain, pure beauty no brush could ever paint— / sparse shadows stretched across the shallow water. / A flute plays the Little Chanyu tune clean through, / stirring old sorrows back to mind once more. / Faint fragrance drifts across the gentle breeze, / while chill moonlight wraps the blossoms cold." [TN: Translator's rendering]
[ZH] 青瓜很担心公子，自从知道少夫人离世，公子从未走出过墨香苑，长此以往下去不是个办法，得想方设法劝公子出去散散心。
[EN] Qinggua worried about his master. Ever since the young madam's death, he had not set foot outside Moxiang Courtyard. This couldn't go on. He had to find a way to get him out, if only for a change of air.
[ZH] “公子，我们不如去瓦子里听听说书？”
[EN] "Master, why don't we go to the tiles and listen to a storyteller?"
[ZH] “有什么好听的？哪有娘子的诗词好？”陈浩宇又饮下一杯，他猛然想起娘子曾说过想了解她，还有一个途径，便是去听千里寻才子写的故事。陈浩宇放下酒杯，站起来往出走。
[EN] "What's to hear? What could beat my wife's poems?" He drained another cup. Then he remembered: she had once said there was another way to know her—to hear the tales the scholar Qianli Xun wrote. He set down his cup and stood to go.
[ZH] 青瓜见公子要出去，很开心，快步跟上。
[EN] Qinggua saw him heading out and was delighted. He hurried after him.
[ZH] 他们来到桥头瓦，找到说书的勾栏，一打听才知说话艺人最近生病，好几天都没来。
[EN] They reached Qiaotou Tile and found the storytelling house, only to learn the performer had been ill and hadn't come for days.
[ZH] 陈浩宇很失望，打算回府。
[EN] Chen Haoyu was disappointed and turned to go home.
[ZH] 青瓜建议道：“既然出来，公子不如到处逛逛散散心？”
[EN] Qinggua suggested, "Since we're out, why not wander a bit, master? Take your mind off things."
[ZH] “有什么好逛的？临安城的大街小巷哪一处我没去过？”陈浩宇提不起精神，懒洋洋地走出桥头瓦。
[EN] "What's to see? What lane or street of Lin'an haven't I been down?" He had no heart for it, and drifted listlessly out of the tile-house.
[ZH] 青瓜跟在身后，思忖着用什么办法可以让公子心甘情愿逛街。他边想边感慨，以前公子从不着家，现在却想方设法劝他走出家门。唉，公子的变化都因少夫人。
[EN] Qinggua followed, turning over ways to get him to stroll willingly. He couldn't help marveling: once his master could hardly stay home; now he had to be coaxed out of the door. All of it, because of the young madam.
[ZH] 想到少夫人，青瓜终于想到一个好办法：“有一处公子肯定没去过。”
[EN] Thinking of her gave him an idea at last. "There's one place I bet you've never been, master."
[ZH] 陈浩宇并未搭理青瓜的话，来到停马车的地方，径直走上马车。
[EN] Chen Haoyu didn't answer. He went to where the carriage waited and stepped aboard.
[ZH] 青瓜也跟着上了马车，坐下继续说：“花云楼公子可曾去过？”
[EN] Qinggua climbed in after him and went on as they sat down. "Have you ever been to Huayun Pavilion, master?"
[ZH] 凡是临安城的娱乐场所，陈浩宇没有一处落下过，花云楼他自然去过，只不过不是那里的常客。花云楼的姑娘个个才艺绝佳，交往的均是文人才子，最令以前的陈浩宇不齿，他去过几次后便再没光顾过。
[EN] There was no house of pleasure in Lin'an Chen Haoyu had missed; of course he had been to Huayun Pavilion, though never a regular. The girls there were all accomplished, and the men they kept company with were scholars and poets—the old Chen Haoyu had looked down on the whole place. He had gone a few times and never returned.
[ZH] 公子虽不言语，那表情显然是去过。青瓜早料到这一点，好在他有准备。
[EN] His master said nothing, but the look on his face said yes. Qinggua had expected as much, and he was ready for it.
[ZH] “公子虽去过，却不晓得花云楼有位姑娘最擅长唱少夫人写的诗词，听说曲子是东方公子谱的。公子与其在家里一个人念少夫人的诗词，不如去听听花云楼的姑娘是如何把少夫人的诗词唱出来？”
[EN] "You may have been, master, but you don't know Huayun Pavilion has a girl who sings my young madam's poems better than anyone. The tunes, they say, were written by Master Dongfang. Rather than recite her verses here alone, why not hear how the girl at Huayun Pavilion sings them?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇想起参加东方诗会时花云楼的想绒姑娘唱的正是娘子的诗词，的确唱得好，估计青瓜说的便是她。
[EN] Chen Haoyu remembered Xiangrong at Huayun Pavilion singing his wife's words at the Dongfang poetry gathering—and singing them beautifully. This must be the girl Qinggua meant.
[ZH] “你怎知道得如此清楚？难道去过花云楼？”
[EN] "How do you know all this? Have you been to Huayun Pavilion?"
[ZH] “公子有所不知，我跟您在永泰茶楼忙时，有几位花云楼的姑娘来品茶，她们聊天儿时说起过，我无意中听到。”
[EN] "You didn't know, master. When I was busy with you at the Yongtea Teahouse, a few girls from Huayun Pavilion came in for tea. I overheard them talking."
[ZH] 既是唱娘子的诗词，陈浩宇决定前去听听：“去花云楼。”
[EN] If she sang his wife's poems, he would go. "To Huayun Pavilion."
[ZH] 终于劝动公子，青瓜很开心，对外面的车夫说：“去花云楼！”
[EN] He had finally talked him around. Qinggua beamed and called to the driver outside, "To Huayun Pavilion!"

#CH 345
[ZH] 春节前后北边的战事多少影响到江南一带，也多少波及花云楼，致使里面的客人几乎减少一大半。
[EN] The fighting in the north around the New Year had touched Jiangnan somewhat, and Huayun Pavilion felt the ripple too. Its regular crowd had all but halved.
[ZH] 陈浩宇一走进直接点名要听想绒姑娘唱曲儿，被伙计请入雅间。
[EN] The moment Chen Haoyu walked in, he named the singer he wanted: Xiangrong. An attendant showed him into a private room.
[ZH] “陈公子稍候片刻，想绒姑娘一会儿就来。”
[EN] "Young Master Chen, wait a moment. Xiangrong will be with you shortly."
[ZH] 伙计离开后，青瓜为公子斟满酒：“公子，听说想绒姑娘人长得不错。”
[EN] Once the man left, Qinggua filled his master's cup. "Sir, they say Xiangrong is a fine-looking girl."
[ZH] 陈浩宇没有回应，端起酒杯一口喝下。
[EN] Chen Haoyu said nothing. He lifted the cup and drained it.
[ZH] 当青瓜刚为公子斟满第二杯时，想绒从外面进来：“不知陈公子想听我唱什么？”
[EN] Qinggua was pouring a second when Xiangrong came in. "What would you like me to sing, Young Master Chen?"
[ZH] “只要是我娘子写的诗词即可。”
[EN] "Anything written by my wife."
[ZH] 想绒已知幽栖居士在燕京的遭遇，也曾黯然落泪，见陈公子一脸悲戚落寞，便知他内心有多难过。
[EN] Xiangrong had heard what had happened to Youqi Jushi in Yanjing and had wept over it in private. One look at the sorrow and emptiness on his face told her how much he was hurting.
[ZH] 她坐到琴旁，用纤细的手指拨弄琴弦，口中唱道：“斜风细雨作春寒。对尊前，忆前欢，曾把梨花，寂寞泪阑干。……”
[EN] She sat down at the zither. Slim fingers touched the strings, and she sang: *"Slanting winds and fine rain bring a spring chill. / Before the wine cup, / I recall old joys. / Once beside the pear blossoms, / Lonely tears streamed down..."* [TN: Translator's rendering]
[ZH] 想绒的歌喉优美动听，在歌声的加持下，陈浩宇更能体会到诗词中的情感。他边听边饮酒，回忆过往……
[EN] Her voice was lovely to hear, and the music let him feel the words all the more. He drank as he listened, and the past came back to him...
[ZH] 想绒一连唱了五首，见陈公子意犹未尽，没有让她停下的意思。她便主动停下拨弄琴弦的手指：“陈公子若喜欢听，明日再来。”
[EN] She sang five songs in a row. He showed no sign of wanting her to stop, so she lifted her fingers from the strings. "If you enjoy them, Young Master Chen, come again tomorrow."
[ZH] 陈浩宇怅然若失：“这就完了？我娘子写过好多诗词，怎么才唱五首就完了？你该不会只会唱五首？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu stared at her, at a loss. "That's it? My wife wrote so many poems. Why stop at five? You can't tell me those are the only ones you know."
[ZH] 想绒照实说：“幽栖居士的诗词我都会唱，美好的事物一下子享受完岂不没趣？再说花云楼有规定，我一日之内唱曲儿不能超过五首。”
[EN] Xiangrong told him the truth. "I can sing every poem Youqi Jushi ever wrote. Isn't it dull to finish all the good things at once? Besides, Huayun Pavilion has a rule. I can't sing more than five songs in a single day."
[ZH] 不能继续感受娘子仿佛还在他身边的感觉，陈浩宇失望中带着些许悲伤：“好吧，我明日再来。”
[EN] He could not keep feeling that his wife was still beside him, and disappointment carried a thread of grief. "All right. I'll come tomorrow."
[ZH] 想绒被触动：“陈公子对幽栖居士一往情深，令想绒既感动又羡慕，我破例再为你多唱一曲。”
[EN] It touched Xiangrong. "Young Master Chen, your love for Youqi Jushi moves me and makes me envy her too. I'll break the rule and sing one more for you."
[ZH] 陈浩宇刚抬起的屁股继续放下，悲伤的神情中多出些许喜色：“有劳。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu had half risen; he settled back down, his grief tinged with sudden light. "My thanks."
[ZH] 想绒说：“我接下来要唱的是幽栖居士新写的《圈儿词》……”
[EN] Xiangrong said, "This next one is a new ci by Youqi Jushi, the *Circle Ci*..."
[ZH] “新写的？”陈浩宇的脸上露出渴望的表情。
[EN] "New?"Hope showed on Chen Haoyu's face.
[ZH] 想绒猜到陈公子不愿承认幽栖居士已死，才会如此发问。
[EN] Xiangrong guessed he could not bear to admit Youqi Jushi was dead. That was why he asked.
[ZH] “是幽栖居士在汴梁时所写，传言因为这首词官家才豁免她的死罪。”
[EN] "She wrote it in Bianliang. They say His Majesty spared her life because of that ci."
[ZH] 陈浩宇失望道：“都过去两年多，为何要说是我娘子新写的来哄我？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu's face fell. "It's been over two years. Why tell me she wrote it recently, just to humor me?"
[ZH] “之所以如此说，是因我不愿承认幽栖居士离我们而去。在我看来，幽栖居士这首词应是写给陈公子的。”
[EN] "I said that because I won't admit she's gone from us. To my mind, she wrote that ci for you."
[ZH] “写给我的？”
[EN] "For me?"
[ZH] 想绒肯定地点点头：“是，我这就唱给陈公子听。”
[EN] Xiangrong nodded. "Yes. Let me sing it for you now."
[ZH] 想绒继续拨弄琴弦，展开歌喉：“相思欲寄无从寄，画个圈儿替。话在圈儿外，心在圈儿里。单圈儿是我，双圈儿是你。……”
[EN] Her fingers moved over the strings again. "*I wish to send my longing, yet find no way to send it, so I draw these circles in its stead. / The words lie outside the circle; my heart lies inside the circle. / A single circle is me; a double circle is you...*" [TN: Translator's rendering]
[ZH] 陈浩宇仅知仪王长跪不起才救下娘子的性命，根本不清楚娘子还写下过这首词。从词句中能清晰地感受到娘子对他的思念之情，他已泪眼模糊，怨自己平日不关注文人之事，致使两年多之后才得知娘子的心意。
[EN] Chen Haoyu knew only that Prince Yi's kneeling had saved his wife's life. He had no idea she had written this too. He could feel her longing in every line. His eyes blurred with tears, and he berated himself for never caring about literary matters. Two years had passed before he learned what was in her heart.
[ZH] 待想绒唱完后，陈浩宇提出要求：“想绒姑娘能否将词句写出来？我好拿出回去再仔细品味。”
[EN] When the song ended, he asked, "Could you write the words down for me? I'd like to take them home and savor them properly."
[ZH] “陈公子稍候。”想绒起身走到放有纸笔的桌前，提起笔开始忙碌。
[EN] "Wait here, Young Master Chen." Xiangrong got up and went to a table with paper and brush, then set to work.
[ZH] 写完后让丫鬟送到陈公子面前。
[EN] When she had finished, she had a maid carry the sheet over to him.
[ZH] 陈浩宇接过，除了“圈儿词”三个字，其他均是些圈圈点点。
[EN] He took it. Apart from the three characters of the title, *Circle Ci*, the page held nothing but circles and dots.
[ZH] “想容姑娘刚才唱得明明是句子，为何画些圈圈点点来糊弄我？”
[EN] "You were singing words just now, Xiangrong. Why fob me off with circles and dots?"
[ZH] “陈公子有所不知，这才是词本有的内容，刚才我唱的不过是幽栖居士对圈圈点点的解释而已。”
[EN] "You see, Young Master Chen, this is the ci as it was written. What I sang was only Youqi Jushi's explanation of the circles and dots."
[ZH] 陈浩宇再瞧一眼圈圈点点，终于有所领悟，脸上露出笑容：“感谢想绒姑娘，告辞。”
[EN] He looked again at the marks on the page, and understanding slowly dawned. A smile broke through. "Thank you, Xiangrong. I'll take my leave."
[ZH] 马车里，陈浩宇一直捧着“圈儿词”，心想：“娘子，你一定是担心我看不懂诗词的句子，才画些圈圈点点，好让我能从中感受到你的心意。我已感受到。‘圈儿词’是娘子留给我最后的念想，我要把它好好珍藏起来。”
[EN] In the carriage, Chen Haoyu held the *Circle Ci* in both hands. *Wife,* he thought, *you must have known I wouldn't understand the words, so you drew circles and dots to let me feel what you meant. I feel it now. The Circle Ci is the last thing you left me. I'll keep it safe.*
[ZH] 青瓜没有心情关心公子，他将厚厚的车窗帘子掀开一些，欣赏街上的景况。无意中瞧见阿美怀里抱着个一岁多的孩子在街边站着，一脸焦急。
[EN] Qinggua had no heart for his master's thoughts. He lifted a corner of the thick carriage curtain and watched the street. By the roadside stood Amei, a child of a year and more in her arms, worry written all over her face.
[ZH] 青瓜放下帘子，征求公子的意思：“公子，阿美在街边抱着个孩子，看上去像是有事，我下去问问？”
[EN] He dropped the curtain and asked, "Sir, Amei's on the street with a child. She looks like she's in trouble. Mind if I go down and ask?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇的心思放在圈圈点点上，无意识地点点头。
[EN] Chen Haoyu was still lost among his circles and dots. He nodded without thinking.
[ZH] 青瓜让车夫将马车停好，急忙下车招呼阿美：“你怎么站在这儿？怀里的孩子是谁家的？”
[EN] Qinggua told the driver to pull over, jumped down, and called out to her. "What are you doing here? Whose child is that?"
[ZH] 阿美解释道：“我也不知道是谁家的孩子，刚学会走路，就跑到街上，也没个大人管，我把他抱着，站在这儿等，说不定他家大人会找来。”
[EN] "I don't know whose, sir," Amei said. "He'd only just learned to walk. He wandered into the street with no grownup watching. I picked him up and waited here. Maybe his family will come looking."
[ZH] 青瓜瞧着这孩子怪眼熟：“好像在哪里见过这孩子。”
[EN] Qinggua studied the boy. "I've seen him somewhere before."
[ZH] 阿美急道：“好好想想。”
[EN] "Think hard," Amei urged.
[ZH] 青瓜一时想不起来，他看到孩子胸前挂着一个长命锁，下意识把锁子翻过去瞧，后面有个“蒋”字，青瓜恍然大悟：“对了，是蒋爷的孩子，去年满月时公子带我参加过他的满月宴，我还逗过这孩子，有些印象，难怪觉得眼熟。”
[EN] He couldn't place him, then spotted a longevity lock hanging on the boy's chest. He turned it over without thinking. On the back was the character *Jiang*. It came to him. "That's it. He's Master Jiang's boy. Sir brought me to the full-month banquet last year and I played with him. I remember now. No wonder he looked familiar."
[ZH] “原来是蒋爷的孩子，你可知他家在哪个方向？”
[EN] "So he's Master Jiang's. Do you know which way his house is?"
[ZH] 青瓜指着一个方向：“沿着这条街一直走，遇到第二个路口，向北走，看到‘蒋府’两个字就是他家。”
[EN] Qinggua pointed. "Straight down this street. Take the second turning north, and when you see the words Jiang Manor, you're there."
[ZH] 阿美来不及谢青瓜，抱着孩子就走。
[EN] Amei did not even stop to thank him. She carried the boy off at once.
[ZH] 青瓜又上了马车，见公子手中的纸张已没有，估计是叠起来放进怀里。他坐好后，马车继续行走在街道上。
[EN] Qinggua climbed back into the carriage. The paper had gone from his master's hands, folded away into his robe, he guessed. He settled in his seat and the carriage moved on down the street.
[ZH] 公子一直沉浸在自己的世界里，青瓜不好打扰，干脆闭目养神。
[EN] His master had retreated into his own world and Qinggua did not like to disturb him, so he shut his eyes and rested.
[ZH] 走了一阵，外面传来嘈杂的人声。青瓜睁开眼睛，掀起窗帘向外瞧。一家卖馒头的铺子外挂着个牌子，上写“千里馒头千里粥。”许多乞丐围拢过来准备领馒头和粥。
[EN] After a while, a noisy crowd came from outside. Qinggua opened his eyes and peered through the curtain. A sign hung outside a steamed-bun shop: *Qianli buns, Qianli congee.* Beggars had gathered to collect their buns and congee.
[ZH] 青瓜放下帘子随口议论：“自从仪王不当节度使后临安的乞丐越来越多。仪王人好，一回到临安又开始免费给乞丐们提供馒头和粥，只是不明白为何把馒头叫‘千里馒头’，粥又称‘千里粥’？”
[EN] He let the curtain fall and remarked idly, "Ever since Prince Yi gave up his military post, there are more beggars in Lin'an every day. He's a good man. Back in Lin'an, he started handing out free buns and congee again. I can't see why he calls them 'Qianli buns' and 'Qianli congee,' though."
[ZH] 青瓜提到仪王，陈浩宇活泛些：“你怎知是仪王做的好事？”
[EN] At the mention of Prince Yi, Chen Haoyu roused a little. "How do you know it's Prince Yi doing this?"
[ZH] “公子整日待着墨香苑，许多事情不清楚。听说仪王每个月给足馒头铺银子，让馒头铺每日给乞丐们免费提供一定数量的粥和馒头，跟陈家以前的施粥棚一个道理？”
[EN] "You shut yourself up in Moxiang Courtyard all day, sir, so you miss things. I hear he gives the bun shop enough silver each month to hand out a set number of buns and congee to beggars every day. Same idea as the Chen family's old congee stalls."
[ZH] “你可知仪王住在哪里？”
[EN] "Do you know where he lives?"
[ZH] “听苦荞说住在竹里村，真想不通一个王爷和乡下老百姓住一个村子，也不知图什么？”
[EN] "Kuqiao says Zhuli Village. I can't work out why a prince would live in the same village as common farmers. What's he after?"
[ZH] 青瓜不知，陈浩宇却知。竹林是娘子的产业，仪王住在那里不过是因为无法忘记她。
[EN] Qinggua did not know, but Chen Haoyu did. The bamboo grove was his wife's property. Prince Yi lived there because he could not forget her.
[ZH] 陈浩宇对仪王没有了嫉妒和埋怨之情，娘子已不在，多一个人思念她也是好的。
[EN] The jealousy and resentment he had felt toward Prince Yi were gone. His wife was gone too; one more person missing her was a good thing.
[ZH] 话说自从春金沫难产离世后，蒋爷为孩子考虑，在临安城新置办了宅子，请一位奶娘负责照顾。
[EN] After Chun Jinmo died in childbirth, Master Jiang, thinking of the child, had bought a house in Lin'an and hired a wet nurse to look after him.
[ZH] 这日奶娘带孩子到街上逛，本是抱着，可孩子贪玩儿，想自己走，奶娘才牵起小手。来到一处卖荷包的摊位，奶娘很喜欢，领着孩子到跟前瞧。因被荷包吸引，一时松手，孩子便自己走入人群中。
[EN] One day the nurse took the boy out. She carried him at first, but he was restless and wanted to walk, so she took his little hand. They stopped at a pouch stall. She liked the look of the pouches and led him closer. She let go for a moment to look, and the boy walked off into the crowd on his own.
[ZH] 等奶娘放下手中的荷包，发现孩子不见，急得满街找，怎么都找不到。只能回家报告蒋爷。
[EN] When she put the pouch down and found the boy gone, she searched the whole street frantically and could not find him. She had to go home and tell Master Jiang.
[ZH] 蒋爷顾不上惩罚奶娘，吩咐人尽快到街上寻。
[EN] Master Jiang had no time to punish her. He sent men out to search the streets at once.
[ZH] 出去的人还未回来时，有下人来报，一位姑娘将小公子送回来，人就在门外。
[EN] Before they returned, a servant came to report that a young woman had brought the little master back. She was at the gate.
[ZH] 蒋爷喜出望外，来到大门口，瞧见姑娘怀里抱着的的确是自己的儿子。
[EN] Master Jiang's heart leapt. He went out and saw the girl holding his son, no doubt about it.
[ZH] 奶娘赶快上前从阿美手中接过孩子：“多谢姑娘。”
[EN] The nurse hurried forward and took the child from Amei's arms. "Thank you, miss."
[ZH] 蒋爷仔细瞧好心的姑娘，人长得面若桃花，一身丫鬟装扮难掩她俏丽的姿容。
[EN] Master Jiang looked the girl over carefully. Her face was like a peach blossom, and her maid's clothes could not hide her lively beauty.
[ZH] “感谢姑娘救了小儿，不知姑娘如何称呼？”
[EN] "Thank you for saving my boy. May I ask your name?"
[ZH] “我叫阿美。”
[EN] "Amei."
[ZH] “真是人如其名。总觉得姑娘面善，好像在哪里见过？”
[EN] "A name to fit you. I feel I've seen your face before. Where was it?"
[ZH] “当然见过，以前我还打过蒋爷的手下。”
[EN] "You have seen me. I once beat up some of Master Jiang's men."
[ZH] 蒋爷恍然大悟，与他手下交过手的女子只有幽栖居士的下人：“姑娘是幽栖居士的丫鬟？”
[EN] It dawned on him. The only woman who had ever fought his men was a servant of Youqi Jushi's. "You're Youqi Jushi's maid?"
[ZH] “正是。”
[EN] "I am."
[ZH] “难怪看上去与众不同。”
[EN] "No wonder you seem different."
[ZH] 蒋爷吩咐身边的人将一袋银子拿来，他亲自送到阿美面前：“不成敬意，请姑娘笑纳。”
[EN] He told a servant to bring a bag of silver and pressed it into her hands himself. "A small token. Please accept it."
[ZH] “举手之劳，蒋爷又何必给我银子？我若接了，反而显得我是为银子而来。”阿美拒绝完，笑着离开。
[EN] "It was nothing. Why give me silver, Master Jiang? If I took it, it would look as if I came for the money." She refused, smiled, and walked away.
[ZH] 蒋爷手拿钱袋，望着阿美的背影自语道：“人长得美，心地又好，还会功夫，真是天赐良缘。”
[EN] Master Jiang stood with the purse in his hand, watching her go. "Beautiful face, kind heart, and skilled with her fists," he murmured. "This is a marriage sent from heaven."
[ZH] 第二日，蒋爷亲自去陈府提亲，陈江林和姚轻琰不敢轻易答应，问儿子的意思。陈浩宇也不敢点头，阿美是娘子带过来的丫鬟，需尊重她的想法。
[EN] The next day he went to Chen Manor to ask for her hand. Chen Jianglin and Yao Qingyan would not decide lightly and asked their son. Chen Haoyu dared not nod either. Amei had come with his wife, and her wishes had to be respected.
[ZH] 阿美思来想去，认为自己作为丫鬟能嫁蒋爷做正室也不错，便点头答应。
[EN] Amei thought it over. As a maid, marrying Master Jiang as his proper wife was no bad thing. She consented.
[ZH] 得到允诺后，蒋爷兴冲冲回到家，准备下个月举办婚礼将阿美娶进门。
[EN] With that settled, Master Jiang hurried home, planning the wedding for the following month.
[ZH] 陈浩宇连着半个月天天泡在花云楼听想绒唱曲儿，陈江林和姚轻琰担忧不已。天黑后，差人把青瓜唤来。
[EN] For half a month Chen Haoyu spent every day at Huayun Pavilion listening to Xiangrong sing. Chen Jianglin and Yao Qingyan grew worried. After dark, they sent for Qinggua.
[ZH] 陈江林板着脸问：“听说最近你常陪公子去花云楼，难不成他又回到从前的样子？”
[EN] Chen Jianglin's face was stern. "I hear you've been taking the boy to Huayun Pavilion a lot lately. Has he slipped back into his old ways?"
[ZH] 青瓜解释道：“大老爷误会，花云楼的想绒姑娘会唱少夫人的诗词，公子才天天去听。”
[EN] "No, sir, you've got it wrong. Xiangrong sings Youqi Jushi's poems. That's why he goes to hear her every day."
[ZH] 姚轻琰问：“只因如此？”
[EN] Yao Qingyan asked, "That's the only reason?"
[ZH] 青瓜肯定道：“只因如此。”
[EN] "That's the only reason," Qinggua said firmly.
[ZH] 两人松口气，吩咐青瓜退去。
[EN] The two of them breathed easier and told him he could go.
[ZH] 姚轻琰说：“听说花云楼的姑娘接触的都是文人才子，不过是陪他们吟诗作赋，展示才艺而已，身世倒也清白……”
[EN] Yao Qingyan said, "They say the girls at Huayun Pavilion only meet scholars and men of letters. All they do is exchange verses and show their talents. Her background is clean enough..."
[ZH] 陈江林不解：“什么意思？”
[EN] Chen Jianglin did not follow. "What are you getting at?"
[ZH] “自从淑真没了，浩宇一直提不起精神，现在愿意走出家门接触其他姑娘已是进步，也证明想绒姑娘能吸引到他，不如你出面到花云楼替她赎身，把她放在浩宇身边，或许他慢慢会从痛苦中走出，开始新的生活。”
[EN] "Ever since Shuzhen died, Haoyu has had no life in him. Now he's willing to leave the house and be around another woman. That's progress, and it proves Xiangrong can reach him. Why don't you go to Huayun Pavilion and buy out her contract? Put her beside Haoyu. With any luck he'll come out of this grief and start living again."
[ZH] “不妥不妥，虽卖艺不卖身，也被许多男子欣赏过。再说浩宇只是因她会唱淑真的诗词才去找她，又不是因为她自己。”
[EN] "No, no. Even if she only sells her songs and not her body, plenty of men have admired her. And Haoyu only goes because she can sing Shuzhen's poems, not for her own sake."
[ZH] “我问你，是儿子重要，还是你那老观念重要？”
[EN] "Tell me this: which matters more, your son or your old-fashioned ideas?"
[ZH] “当然是儿子重要。”
[EN] "My son, of course."
[ZH] “那不就结了？不能让浩宇一直这么下去，他可是陈家的产业继承人，肩负重任。他因淑真总去找想绒姑娘，我总觉得这姑娘能让浩宇好起来。”
[EN] "Then there's nothing to decide. We can't let him go on like this. He's the heir to the Chen family business. He has duties. He keeps going to Xiangrong because of Shuzhen, and I keep thinking this girl is the one who can bring him back."
[ZH] 陈江林不再说反对的话：“依夫人的，明儿我亲自去为想绒姑娘赎身，不过你需答应我一个条件。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin stopped arguing. "Have it your way, wife. I'll go buy out Xiangrong's contract tomorrow. But you must grant me one condition."
[ZH] “什么条件？”
[EN] "What condition?"
[ZH] “先把她放浩宇身边做个妾室，以后有好人家的姑娘再劝说浩宇重新娶房妻室。”
[EN] "Put her in Haoyu's house as a concubine first. Later, when a girl from a good family comes along, we'll talk about taking a proper wife again."
[ZH] 姚轻琰点头认可：“我也是这个意思。”
[EN] Yao Qingyan nodded. "That's what I had in mind."
[ZH] 陈江林叹道：“我陈家的男人本不纳妾，这倒好，到浩宇这儿破了例。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin sighed. "The men of our family have never taken concubines. Haoyu will be the first."
[ZH] 姚轻琰说：“计划赶不上变化，也是不得已之事。收拾睡吧，明儿还要忙。”
[EN] "Plans can't keep up with changes. We have no choice. Finish your tea and get some sleep. Tomorrow will be busy."
[ZH] 第二日，陈浩宇用过午饭打算继续去花云楼听想绒姑娘唱曲儿，刚走出墨香苑，想绒姑娘出现在眼前。
[EN] The next day, Chen Haoyu had finished lunch and was about to go back to Huayun Pavilion when Xiangrong appeared as he stepped out of Moxiang Courtyard.
[ZH] 陈浩宇瞧着想绒姑娘身后抱着琴的丫鬟，问道：“我昨天并未请你来，为何今日亲自登门？”
[EN] He noticed the maid carrying a zither behind her. "I didn't send for you yesterday. Why come to the house today?"
[ZH] 想绒行礼道：“陈老爷已为我赎身，让我留在陈公子身边伺候。”
[EN] Xiangrong curtsied. "Old Master Chen has bought out my contract. He has asked me to stay and serve you, Young Master Chen."
[ZH] “胡闹！”陈浩宇脱口而出，从想绒身边走过，打算找爹娘问问清楚。
[EN] "Nonsense!"The word burst out. He walked past her, meaning to ask his parents what was going on.
[ZH] 想绒大声说：“陈公子不必找长辈质问，木已成舟问了又有何用？想绒心甘情愿来陈家，替淑真照顾公子，也算是全了我和她的友情。”
[EN] Xiangrong called after him. "There's no need to question your elders. What's done is done. I came to Chen Manor of my own free will, to look after you for Shuzhen. It settles the debt I owed her friendship."
[ZH] 陈浩宇停住脚步：“我心里只有她，恐怕容不下他人。”
[EN] He stopped. "My heart holds only her. I'm afraid there's no room for anyone else."
[ZH] 想绒走到陈浩宇面前：“我知道，我不奢望你能忘记淑真，只希望我每日唱曲儿，能与你一起想起她。”
[EN] Xiangrong came up to him. "I know. I don't ask you to forget Shuzhen. I only hope that when I sing each day, you'll remember her with me."
[ZH] 陈浩宇被感动：“墨香苑是她的地儿，不适合你住，我会给你另外安排院子。”
[EN] He was moved. "Moxiang Courtyard was her place. It isn't right for you. I'll find you a courtyard of your own."
[ZH] 想绒毫不介意：“都听你的。”
[EN] "Anything you say," Xiangrong said, untroubled.
[ZH] 想绒住进属于她的院子，虽不大，却无比温馨。她从心底感谢淑真，因为她的诗词，她悄悄爱上陈浩宇；也因为她，才有人主动替她赎身，她才过上正常人的日子。
[EN] Xiangrong moved into her courtyard. It was small but wonderfully warm. She thanked Shuzhen from the bottom of her heart. Because of Shuzhen's poems, she had quietly fallen in love with Chen Haoyu. Because of Shuzhen, someone had bought out her contract, and she had a normal life now.
[ZH] 她扫视着屋里的一切，自语道：“淑真，不要怪我，我会替你照顾好陈公子，替你好好爱他。”
[EN] She looked around the room and said softly, "Don't blame me, Shuzhen. I'll look after him for you, and love him well for you."
[ZH] 陈浩宇每日都会去想绒的屋里听曲儿，陈府里充满悠扬的琴声，让一度沉寂的府邸再次充满生气。
[EN] Every day Chen Haoyu went to her courtyard to hear her sing. Music filled Chen Manor again, and the house that had been so quiet came back to life.
[ZH] 有天晚上，陈浩宇梦见娘子后醒来，再也睡不着，穿好衣裳，独自饮酒，直到醉意袭来。在酒精的刺激下，他更加思念娘子，决定去找想绒听曲儿。白天虽已听过，可此刻他比任何时候都想听。
[EN] One night he woke from a dream of his wife and could not sleep again. He dressed, drank alone until the wine took hold, and the drink only sharpened his longing. He decided to go to Xiangrong for a song. He had heard her that day, but now he wanted it more than he had ever wanted anything.
[ZH] 他踉跄着推开素心草堂的门，独自一人出了墨香苑，来到想绒的院子，借着酒劲儿使劲敲门。
[EN] He pushed open the door of Suxin Thatched Hall, unsteady on his feet, left Moxiang Courtyard alone, and reached Xiangrong's courtyard. He hammered on the door with all the strength the wine gave him.
[ZH] 丫鬟打开门，见是公子，问道：“这么晚，公子为何过来？”
[EN] The maid opened up and saw who it was. "Sir, why come at this hour?"
[ZH] “我来……听曲儿。”
[EN] "I've come... to hear a song."
[ZH] “公子白天已听过。”
[EN] "You heard one today, sir."
[ZH] “少废话，让想绒……起来……唱曲儿。”
[EN] "Less talk. Get Xiangrong... up... to sing."
[ZH] 屋里传来想绒的声音：“让他进来！”
[EN] From inside came her voice. "Let him in."
[ZH] 丫鬟打开屋门，陈浩宇踉跄着走进屋内。想绒已坐在琴旁，身着睡觉的衣裙，披散着头长发，脸上未施粉黛，看上去像一朵含苞待放的荷花。
[EN] The maid opened the inner door and Chen Haoyu staggered into the room. Xiangrong was already at the zither, in her sleeping clothes, her long hair loose, her face bare of powder and paint. She looked like a lotus bud about to open.
[ZH] 陈浩宇带着微醺的状态坐在他常坐的桌边：“唱《减字木兰花·春怨》。”
[EN] He sat down at the table he always used, still warm with drink. "Sing *Jianzi Mulanhua: Spring Resentment*."
[ZH] 想绒拨动琴弦，唱道：“独行独坐，独唱独酬还独卧。……”
[EN] Xiangrong plucked the strings and sang: *"I walk alone, sit alone, sing alone, drink alone, and sleep alone..."* [TN: Translated by Tony Barnstone and Chou Ping]
[ZH] 诗句将陈浩宇带入东方诗会的场景，娘子在东方诗会中的一颦一笑尽入眼帘……
[EN] The lines carried him back to the Eastern Poetry Society. He saw his wife's every smile and every small movement there...
[ZH] 歌曲唱完后，陈浩宇自语道：“娘子，有我在，你永远不会孤独……”
[EN] When the song was over, he said to himself, "Wife, I'm here. You'll never be lonely again..."
[ZH] 他抬起眼，想绒出现在他身边，深情地看着他：“你也不会孤独，因为有我陪着你，我愿意做你的娘子。”
[EN] He raised his eyes. Xiangrong had come to stand beside him, looking at him with all her heart. "You won't be lonely either. I'm here with you. I want to be your wife."
[ZH] “做我的娘子？”
[EN] "My wife?"
[ZH] “你可以把我当成你的娘子。”
[EN] "You can think of me as your wife."
[ZH] “我的娘子？”
[EN] "My wife?"
[ZH] 在酒精的作用下，陈浩宇似乎看到娘子就在他的眼前，想起在狱中与娘子亲吻的场景，不由向想绒吻去……
[EN] The wine made it seem that his wife stood right before him. He remembered kissing her in the prison cell, and he leaned in and kissed Xiangrong...

#CH 346
[ZH] 天亮后，陈浩宇从想绒的榻上醒来，昨晚他把想绒想象成娘子放肆了一回。他心里明白，从此后没有资格再念着娘子。
[EN] Chapter 346: A Storyteller Brings Good News
[ZH] 想绒依偎到他身边：“浩宇，能成为你的女人，我感到很幸福。”
[EN] At daybreak, Chen Haoyu woke from Xiangrong's bed. The night before, he had let himself loose, pretending she was his wife. He knew it plainly: from now on, he had no right to long for his wife again.
[ZH] 与娘子相比，想绒温柔许多；与其他的漂亮姑娘相比，她最能将娘子的诗词唱得婉转动人，他被想绒拿下再正常不过。
[EN] Xiangrong nestled up to him. "Haoyu, I'm so happy to be your woman."
[ZH] “想绒，原谅我不能与你举办婚礼，我这辈子的婚礼只能有一次。”
[EN] Compared with his wife, Xiangrong was far gentler; compared with other pretty girls, she sang his wife's poems the sweetest. It was only natural that she had won him over.
[ZH] “不用解释，我什么都明白，只要能陪着你，为你生儿育女我便心满意足。”
[EN] "Xiangrong, forgive me for not holding a wedding with you. I can have only one wedding in this life."
[ZH] “我虽不能给你婚礼，却可以向你保证，我的身边不会再有其他女人。”
[EN] "No need to explain. I understand everything. If I can stay by your side and bear your children, I'll be content."
[ZH] 想绒明白，陈浩宇之所以与她同房不过是因思念幽栖居士，他的保证也并非因对她钟情。尽管如此，她依然感动。
[EN] "I can't give you a wedding, but I promise you this: there'll be no other woman in my life."
[ZH] 自从有了第一晚，陈浩宇完全放开，几乎天天晚上留宿在想绒的屋子。
[EN] Xiangrong knew he had slept with her only because he missed Youqi Jushi, and that the promise came from obligation, not love. Even so, she was moved.
[ZH] 有一日想绒觉得没胃口直恶心，请来大夫一瞧，原来已有身孕，全家上下无不高兴。
[EN] From that first night on, Chen Haoyu stopped holding back. He spent nearly every night at Xiangrong's rooms.
[ZH] 快要当父亲，陈浩宇不再沉迷下去，决定肩负起产业继承人的职责。
[EN] One day Xiangrong lost her appetite and kept retching. The doctor they called confirmed it: she was pregnant. The whole household rejoiced.
[ZH] 姚轻琰和陈江林终于松口气，庆幸用三千两银子为想绒赎身实在值得。
[EN] With a child on the way, Chen Haoyu put aside his indulgence and decided to take up his duties as heir to the business.
[ZH] 某一日天色刚刚暗下，陈浩宇从绣庄回来，走进墨香苑，走入娘子的素心草堂，躺在她的闺床上。
[EN] Yao Qingyan and Chen Jianglin finally breathed easy. Paying three thousand taels of silver to buy Xiangrong's freedom had been worth every coin.
[ZH] “娘子，我跟想绒连孩子都有了，你会不会怪我？我最想有我们的孩子，却永远不可能……”一滴眼泪从眼角滚落下来。
[EN] One evening, just after dusk, Chen Haoyu came back from the embroidery shop, walked into Moxiang Garden, entered his wife's Suxin Thatched Hall, and lay down on her bed.
[ZH] 门外传来韦嬷嬷的声音：“姑爷，我有事与你商议。”
[EN] "Wife, Xiangrong and I even have a child now. Will you blame me? What I wanted most was a child of our own, and that can never be…" A tear rolled from the corner of his eye.
[ZH] 陈浩宇擦干眼泪，起身走到外间，亲自将门打开：“嬷嬷进屋说。”
[EN] From outside came Matron Wei's voice. "Young master, there's something I need to discuss with you."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷走进，见姑爷的眼圈发红，猜他刚刚哭过，如此看来他还是放不下姑娘。
[EN] Chen Haoyu wiped his eyes, got up, walked into the outer room, and opened the door himself. "Come in, Matron, and tell me."
[ZH] 她感动的同时，不得不说：“姑爷，想绒姑娘已有身孕，我等下人还霸占着墨香苑不甚合适，不如趁此机会搬回朱府，好让想绒姑娘住进来。”
[EN] Matron Wei stepped in and saw the young master's eyes were red. She guessed he had been crying. So he still couldn't let the young mistress go.
[ZH] 陈浩宇果断拒绝：“嬷嬷莫要再提，除非娘子活过来亲口对我说要带你们离开，我才会同意。”
[EN] Moved as she was, she had to speak. "Young master, Miss Xiangrong is with child. It isn't right for us servants to keep occupying Moxiang Garden. Why not take this chance to move back to the Zhu residence, so Miss Xiangrong can live here?"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷叹口气：“唉，姑爷与姑娘的婚姻已解除，下人们待在这里名不正言不顺，恐传出去被人笑话。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu refused flatly. "Don't bring it up again, Matron. Unless my wife comes back to life and tells me with her own lips to take you all away, I won't hear of it."
[ZH] “解除婚姻是不得已之举，新官家端坐朝堂，以前的圣旨可以作废，嬷嬷休要再提离开之事。”
[EN] Matron Wei sighed. "Alas, the young master's marriage to the mistress has been dissolved. We servants have no proper place here. Word will get out, and people will laugh."
[ZH] “好吧，往后我不会再提。”
[EN] "Dissolving the marriage was a last resort. A new emperor sits on the throne; the old edict can be voided. Say no more about leaving, Matron."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷的确没有再提过同样的话题，与十个下人安心住在墨香苑，近期还要忙阿美出嫁之事。
[EN] "Very well. I won't mention it again."
[ZH] ……
[EN] Matron Wei indeed never raised the subject again. She lived quietly in Moxiang Garden with the ten other servants, and had her hands full with Amei's upcoming wedding.
[ZH] 申简辰挂着王爷的名头，过着乡间百姓的生活，倒也乐得自在。
[EN] …
[ZH] 与百姓不同的是，他不必劳作，自有临安知府李克亲自将钱财奉上。
[EN] Shen Jianchen bore the title of prince but lived the life of a country villager, and he found it rather pleasant.
[ZH] 申简辰来者不拒，照单全收。贪官的银子不收白不收，拿去做善事也算替他们积累功德。
[EN] Unlike the villagers, he never had to work. Li Ke, the prefect of Lin'an, saw to it that money was delivered to him in person.
[ZH] 李克之所以对仪王态度大变，主要因朝中的靠山梁师成被新任官家赵桓派人缢杀，全部家产也被抄没。旧靠山没了，他才想到仪王。
[EN] Shen Jianchen turned no one away and accepted every gift. A corrupt official's silver was there for the taking; spending it on good deeds would count as merit for them.
[ZH] 仪王尽管是个没有实权的皇子，可他毕竟是皇子，跟朝堂上那位哥哥多少能说上话。
[EN] Li Ke's change of heart toward the Prince of Yi had one main cause: his patron at court, Liang Shicheng, had been strangled on the new emperor Zhao Huan's orders, and every last bit of his property confiscated. With his old backer gone, he had thought of the Prince of Yi.
[ZH] 向上巴结贿赂时，李克听说当今官家对流浪在外的弟弟甚是同情，还给身边的臣子说，因朝廷内忧外患暂时顾不上弟弟，等忧患解除，定会将他召回京城。
[EN] The Prince of Yi was a prince without real power, but a prince all the same. He could get a word in with that brother of his on the throne.
[ZH] 此等景况，李克不会轻易放过身边的贵人，时不时亲自来到竹林拜见，每次来都带着丰厚的礼物和钱财。
[EN] While currying favor with bribes, Li Ke heard that the emperor pitied his brother wandering outside the capital. He had even told his ministers that the court was too busy with troubles at home and abroad to fetch him now, but once the troubles were over, he would surely summon him back to the capital.
[ZH] 竹里村的百姓从未见过大官，知府大人来时大家都会围观。士兵们将百姓阻挡在外，一个个伸长脖子向竹里村最精美的竹屋瞧去。
[EN] With such signs in the air, Li Ke would not let a powerful man under his nose slip away. He came to the bamboo grove to pay his respects in person now and then, never without rich gifts and money.
[ZH] 这日李克送完钱财和礼物带人满意离开，百姓们目送完知府大人，都站在竹屋外等待白风发话。
[EN] The people of Zhuli Village had never seen a high official. Whenever the prefect came, they all crowded around to watch. Soldiers held them back while every neck craned toward the finest bamboo house in the village.
[ZH] 竹屋内，申简辰一身普通百姓装扮，满脸胡茬，整个人看上去苍老许多，他手中拿着酒葫芦边饮酒边说：“出去告诉大家，今日又有酒席吃。”
[EN] One day Li Ke delivered his silver and gifts and left, well pleased. The villagers watched him go, then stood outside the bamboo house waiting for Bai Feng to speak.
[ZH] 白风也是普通百姓装扮，头发却梳得整齐，脸也白净，显得比王爷精神许多。他想起自己的终身大事，不着急出去告知村民，而是说：“王爷，您先答应我件事。”
[EN] Inside the house, Shen Jianchen was dressed as a common villager, his face stubbled, looking much older than his years. He raised a wine gourd, drank, and said, "Go tell everyone there's another feast today."
[ZH] “哼，什么时候学会跟我谈条件？”
[EN] Bai Feng was dressed as a villager too, but his hair was neatly combed and his face clean; he looked far sharper than the prince. He thought of his own marriage and, instead of hurrying off to tell the villagers, said, "Your Highness, promise me one thing first."
[ZH] “不是谈条件，是想求王爷应允。上周阿美都嫁给了蒋爷，我和苦荞什么时候是个头？”
[EN] "Hmph. Since when do you bargain with me?"
[ZH] “因为我的事耽误你跟苦荞，真是过意不去。我答应你，先给你建好竹屋，再把苦荞娶过来，省得她名不正言不顺待在陈家。”
[EN] "It isn't a bargain, Your Highness, it's a request. Amei married Master Jiang last week. When will Kuqiao and I have our day?"
[ZH] 白风兴奋道：“谢谢王爷！”
[EN] "I'm sorry my trouble has kept you and Kuqiao waiting. I promise you this: I'll have your bamboo house built first, then bring Kuqiao here as your bride, so she won't have to stay in the Chen house with no standing."
[ZH] “改日我要找陈浩宇好好谈谈，把邹嬷嬷等人接来，她是我的人，她的下人也应跟着我。建竹屋的事还由你负责，银子不够尽管找贪官要。”
[EN] Bai Feng beamed. "Thank you, Your Highness!"
[ZH] “是！”
[EN] "I'll have a proper talk with Chen Haoyu in a day or two and bring Matron Zou and the others back. She belongs to me; her servants should follow me too. The bamboo house is still your job. If the silver runs short, just ask the corrupt officials for more."
[ZH] 白风走出竹屋，向围在外面的村民发话：“大家听好了，今日又有千里席可吃，还是老规矩，所有人大声喊十遍‘千里寻’！”
[EN] "Yes, sir."
[ZH] 村民们不知所以，大声喊着：“千里寻！千里寻！千里寻！……”
[EN] Bai Feng walked out of the bamboo house and called to the villagers gathered outside. "Listen up, everyone. There's another Qianli feast today. Same rule as always: every one of you shout 'Qianli Xun' ten times, loud!"
[ZH] 听到外面的喊声，申简辰喝完一口酒自语道：“只有这样，我才会觉得你还活着。这个冬季你在燕京城外度过会不会很冷？春天已到，该去燕京城外把你带回临安，顺便也带回梦容和喜兰。我已为你找好住处，离我很近，我会陪着你了却残生。”
[EN] The villagers had no idea why, but they shouted: "Qianli Xun! Qianli Xun! Qianli Xun!…"
[ZH] 村民喊完后，白风继续说：“还像往常一样，负责采买的人到我这里领银子！”
[EN] Hearing the shouts from outside, Shen Jianchen drained a mouthful of wine and murmured to himself, "Only this makes me feel you're still alive. Was it cold for you outside Yanjing this winter? Spring is here. It's time I went to Yanjing and brought you back to Lin'an, and Mengrong and Xilan too. I've found a place for you, close to me. I'll stay with you to the end of my days."
[ZH] 苦蕨带领几个负责采购的人领完银子各自忙活。
[EN] When the shouting was done, Bai Feng went on. "Same as before. Whoever's doing the shopping, come to me for silver."
[ZH] 中午时千里席开始，安排在村长家的院子。
[EN] Ku Jue led the buyers forward, collected the silver, and went off about their tasks.
[ZH] 申简辰简单吃了几口，一个人提着酒葫芦离开。
[EN] At noon the Qianli feast began, laid out in the village head's courtyard.
[ZH] 村长本想上前劝仪王多吃些，被白风拦住：“王爷心里有事，别打扰他。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen ate a few mouthfuls, then took his wine gourd and went off alone.
[ZH] 村长继续坐下招呼村民享用千里席，顺便问出心中疑惑：“白侍卫，我多嘴问一问，为何仪王要将宴席称为‘千里席’？还要让大家喊‘千里寻’？我去城里时听说千里寻是位才子，故事写得极好，说话艺人都喜欢讲他写的故事，仪王跟千里寻才子是什么关系？”
[EN] The village head was about to urge the Prince of Yi to eat more when Bai Feng stopped him. "His Highness has something on his mind. Don't disturb him."
[ZH] “不该你问的别瞎问，只需记着喊这个名字王爷高兴，王爷一高兴大家就有席吃。”
[EN] The village head sat back down, saw to the guests at the Qianli feast, and asked the question that had been bothering him. "Captain Bai, forgive my prying. Why does the prince call this a 'Qianli feast'? And why make everyone shout 'Qianli Xun'? When I went into the city, I heard Qianli Xun was a gifted writer whose stories are wonderful—the storytellers all love to tell them. What is the prince to this Master Qianli Xun?"
[ZH] “记下了，记下了，不再瞎问，白侍卫与我干一杯。”
[EN] "Don't ask about what's none of your business. Just remember: when the prince hears that name, he's happy. When the prince is happy, we all get a feast."
[ZH] 申简辰拎着酒葫芦来到村口，村口正好停着一辆马车，他坐上后，让车夫带他到城里的桥头瓦。
[EN] "I've got it, I've got it. No more prying. Captain Bai, let's drink a cup together."
[ZH] 来到桥头瓦，直奔说书的勾栏，他比陈浩宇幸运，擅长说千里寻话本的说话艺人正好登场。
[EN] Shen Jianchen carried his wine gourd to the village gate, where a carriage happened to be waiting. He climbed in and told the driver to take him to Qiaotouwa in the city.
[ZH] 申简辰找位置坐下，没有要茶，象征性地要了一盘蜜饯，只顾喝着葫芦里的酒。
[EN] At Qiaotouwa he headed straight for the storytelling house. He was luckier than Chen Haoyu: a storyteller who specialized in tales by Qianli Xun was just taking the stage.
[ZH] 说话艺人在台上站好后，按照惯例抿口茶，开始聊天儿：“诸位今日有耳福，鄙人新得了千里寻才子的话本，还热乎着呢，今日给各位说上一说……”
[EN] Shen Jianchen found a seat, ordered no tea, asked for a token plate of preserved fruit, and kept drinking from his gourd.
[ZH] 申简辰停下喝酒的动作，迷离的眼神中充满期待。
[EN] Once the storyteller had settled himself on the stage, he sipped tea as custom required and began. "Friends, you're in luck today. I've come by a brand-new storybook by the gifted Qianli Xun—still warm from the press. Listen, and I'll tell it to you now…"
[ZH] 台下有人说：“别唬人！谁不知道千里寻才子两年多前去了汴梁，你说的故事恐怕汴梁人都听腻了才拿来哄骗我们！”
[EN] Shen Jianchen stopped drinking. His hazy eyes were full of anticipation.
[ZH] 申简辰收回充满希望的眼神，小声自语：“她已经不在，怎么可能还有新的话本？”他继续拿起葫芦喝酒。
[EN] Someone in the audience called out, "Don't fool us! Everyone knows the gifted Qianli Xun left for Bianliang more than two years ago. That story of yours is probably old hat in Bianliang, and now you're peddling it to us!"
[ZH] 说话艺人解释道：“这位客官太小瞧人，不热乎我也不敢拿出来说，再说话本的内容是我的朋友从燕京偷偷给我寄来。燕京被金人占领，要从燕京寄东西出来十分不易。我那朋友说燕京现在无人敢说话本，千里寻才子托他将话本寄出，辗转几次才到我的手中。我那朋友在信中说了，千里寻才子还会继续写话本，到时仍旧给我寄来。”
[EN] The hope faded from Shen Jianchen's eyes. "She's gone. How could there be a new story?" he murmured, and lifted the gourd to drink again.
[ZH] 申简辰的眼中再次燃起希望之火，大声说：“请问，新话本是何时寄来的？”
[EN] The storyteller explained, "You wrong me, sir. I wouldn't dare bring it out if it weren't fresh. The book was sent to me secretly from Yanjing by a friend. The Jin hold Yanjing; getting anything out of there is no small feat. My friend says nobody in Yanjing dares tell stories now, so the gifted Qianli Xun asked him to send the books out. They changed hands several times before they reached me. He wrote in his letter that the gifted Qianli Xun will go on writing and will keep sending them to me."
[ZH] 说话艺人回道：“十日前。”
[EN] Hope flared in Shen Jianchen's eyes again. "When did the new book arrive?" he called out.
[ZH] 申简辰站起来：“十日前？从燕京到临安少说需一个月左右，话本在一个月内寄来，说明是她一个月前写的。现在是四月，这么说话本很有可能是她三月份所写，不！哪怕是二月、一月也可，她还活着？”
[EN] "Ten days ago," the storyteller answered.
[ZH] 众人都纳闷地看着申简辰，不知他口中嘟囔的话是何意？
[EN] Shen Jianchen stood up. "Ten days? From Yanjing to Lin'an is a month at the least. If it got here within a month, she wrote it a month ago. It's April now, so she likely wrote it in March—no! Even February or January would do. She's alive?"
[ZH] 说话艺人回道：“千里寻才子自然活着，否则我的朋友不可能告诉我还会继续给我寄来他的话本。”
[EN] The crowd stared at him, wondering what he was muttering about.
[ZH] “她竟然还活着，她竟然还活着，我要去找她，要去找她……”申简辰已待不下去，含着兴奋的泪水转身离开。
[EN] The storyteller replied, "The gifted Qianli Xun is alive, of course. Otherwise my friend couldn't have told me he'd keep sending me his stories."
[ZH] 有人议论道：“此人肯定是千里寻才子的追随者，以为他离开临安去了汴梁会因为金军南下遇难，现在听说人在燕京，激动到要亲自去找他，燕京都是金人，只怕有去无回。”
[EN] "She's alive. She's alive. I have to go find her, I have to go…" Shen Jianchen couldn't sit still a moment longer. He turned and left, tears of joy in his eyes.
[ZH] 说话艺人叹道：“诸位可不要学那位年轻人，千里寻才子写的故事好，听听也就罢了，没必要狂热到人去哪里就追随到哪里。闲话少说，言归正传……”
[EN] Someone in the crowd observed, "He must be a follower of the gifted Qianli Xun. He thought the writer might have died when the Jin army came south after he left Lin'an for Bianliang. Now that he hears he's in Yanjing, he's so worked up he wants to go find him in person. Yanjing is full of Jin soldiers. He'll likely never come back."
[ZH] 一路上申简辰激动得在马车上四肢都不知如何安放，口中一直念叨着：“她还活着，她还活着……”
[EN] The storyteller sighed. "Don't be like that young man, friends. The gifted Qianli Xun writes fine stories; listening is enough. No need to follow him wherever he goes. But enough chatter—back to the tale…"
[ZH] 回到竹屋时，本来要立刻收拾行囊去燕京，却发现文渊正在屋里等他。
[EN] All the way back, Shen Jianchen was too excited to know what to do with his limbs in the carriage. "She's alive, she's alive," he kept saying under his breath.
[ZH] 申简辰激动地抱住文渊：“你知道吗？她还活着！还活着！”
[EN] When he got back to the bamboo house, he meant to pack at once and leave for Yanjing, but found Wenyuan waiting for him inside.
[ZH] 乔文仙也抱住仪王：“我来正是为此事，没想到你已知。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen threw his arms around him, trembling. "Do you know? She's alive! Alive!"
[ZH] 申简辰放开文渊：“你是如何得知？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian hugged the prince back. "That's exactly why I came. I see you've heard already."
[ZH] 乔文仙扶仪王坐下：“今日一早东方兄来找我，说他昨日举办诗会，诗会中来了一位新朋友。去年这位朋友被困燕京，直到当今官家即位，大宋割让土地与金人谈和，金人从汴梁城外撤退后，困在燕京的百姓才可自由出入。自由后这位新友想目睹金人统治下的燕京会变成什么样，便在燕京住下，后因朋友盛情难却，留在燕京度过元日。有一日他在街上瞎逛，来到一家茶肆，里面挂着字画，他一一浏览，竟看到淑真写的一首新词，你可知落款的时间是哪一日？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen let go of him. "How did you learn it?"
[ZH] “哪一日？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian guided him to a seat. "Early this morning, Brother Dongfang came to me. He held a poetry gathering yesterday, and a new acquaintance was there. This friend was trapped in Yanjing last year. It was only after the present emperor took the throne, and the Song ceded land to make peace with the Jin and the Jin withdrew from outside Bianliang, that the people stuck in Yanjing could come and go freely. Once free, this friend wanted to see what Yanjing looked like under Jin rule, so he stayed on. Then, unable to refuse a friend's kindness, he spent New Year's Day there. One day he was wandering the streets and stepped into a tea house hung with calligraphy and paintings. He went through them one by one, and there, to his amazement, was a new lyric in Shuzhen's hand. Do you know what date it was signed?"
[ZH] “靖康元年正月初六。”
[EN] "What date?"
[ZH] 申简辰更加激动：“我看到她的新坟时还是宣和年间，既是正月初六所写，她必然还活着。我，我现在就去燕京找她。”
[EN] "The sixth day of the first month of the Jingkang era."
[ZH] 乔文仙说：“不要心急，心急反而坏事。你仔细想想，以淑真的本事若得自由肯定会回到临安，她没回来说明什么？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen was more agitated than ever. "When I saw her new grave, it was still the Xuanhe era. If she wrote it on the sixth day of the first month, she must be alive. I—I'm going to Yanjing now, to find her."
[ZH] 被文渊一劝，申简辰的心绪平静许多：“说明她还被完颜池关着不得自由。”
[EN] "Don't rush," Qiao Wenxian said. "Haste only spoils things. Think carefully. With Shuzhen's gifts, if she were free, she'd have come back to Lin'an at once. She hasn't come back. What does that tell you?"
[ZH] 乔文仙点点头：“所以你冒失赶去，只怕完颜池又会使坏，再问你要一千万两，又该去哪里筹？知道淑真活着你我可安心，需想到一个法子逼得完颜池不得不交出人，再去燕京不迟。”
[EN] Wenyuan's words steadied him. "That Wanyan Chi is still holding her, and she isn't free."
[ZH] 申简辰十分认可，到底什么办法可一招致命，逼得完颜池心甘情愿将人放出？
[EN] Qiao Wenxian nodded. "So if you charge off rashly, Wanyan Chi may play his tricks again and demand another ten million taels of silver. Where would you find that? Knowing Shuzhen is alive is reason enough for us to breathe easier. Think of a way to force Wanyan Chi to hand her over before you go to Yanjing."
[ZH] 想到完颜池为寻母曾来到临安，申简辰立刻有了主意：“我已有办法，定能让他乖乖放人。不过，有一事需麻烦你。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen saw the sense in it—but what move could strike in one blow and make Wanyan Chi let her go willingly?
[ZH] “跟我何必客气，尽管说。”
[EN] He remembered how Wanyan Chi had once come to Lin'an searching for his mother, and at once he had a plan. "I have a way. I'll make him release her, willingly. But I need a favor from you."
[ZH] “叮嘱三位公子，不要把她还活着的消息告诉陈浩宇，我不想节外生枝。”
[EN] "Why stand on ceremony with me? Just say it."
[ZH] “早知道你的心思，我已给东方兄他们叮嘱过。听说想绒姑娘已有身孕，陈浩宇有他的小日子过，大家都不愿前去破坏。主要还是因为你，一路走来，千辛万苦，付出极大，再不让你与她在一起，连老天都看不下去，何况是我们？”
[EN] "Tell those three young masters not to breathe a word to Chen Haoyu that she's alive. I don't want any complications."
[ZH] 申简辰含泪而笑，站在一旁的白风和南双也为仪王感到开心。
[EN] "I knew your mind before you spoke. I've already warned Brother Dongfang and the others. And with Miss Xiangrong pregnant, Chen Haoyu has his own life to live; no one wants to disturb it. It's mainly for you. You've come through so much, traveled so far, paid so dearly. If they still kept you from her, even heaven would find it hard to bear—let alone us."
[ZH] 白风本想将幽栖居士活着的消息告诉苦荞，听到王爷嘱咐乔公子的话，决定先不告诉，省得苦荞忍不住告诉韦嬷嬷，韦嬷嬷再忍不住告诉其他下人，其他下人再忍不住说漏嘴传到陈浩宇耳中，说不定会坏了王爷的好事。
[EN] Shen Jianchen smiled through tears. Bai Feng and Nan Shuang, standing to one side, were happy for the prince too.
[ZH] 乔文仙走后，申简辰开始梳洗。白风先帮王爷刮掉脸上的胡茬，又伺候王爷把脸洗干净，然后帮他梳发髻。
[EN] Bai Feng had meant to tell Kuqiao that Youqi Jushi was alive. But hearing what the prince asked of Qiao Wenxian, he decided to hold his tongue. Kuqiao would tell Matron Wei, Matron Wei would tell the other servants, one of them would slip up, and word would reach Chen Haoyu—and that might ruin everything the prince had planned.
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷从外面进来：“我来伺候王爷。”
[EN] After Qiao Wenxian left, Shen Jianchen began to wash and groom. Bai Feng first shaved the stubble from the prince's face, then washed it for him, and finally combed his hair into a topknot.
[ZH] 白风闪到一边：“嬷嬷的手艺比我好，还是你来。”
[EN] Matron Zou came in from outside. "Let me attend to Your Highness."
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷从白风手中接过木梳，仔细帮仪王梳理头发。
[EN] Bai Feng stepped aside. "Your hands are better than mine, Matron. You do it."
[ZH] “刚才乔公子来，你们说的话我在外面听到一些，幽栖居士活着我比谁都高兴。王爷从小是个苦孩子，长大了还要受苦……，希望把幽栖居士救出来后，王爷和幽栖居士不再经受任何苦难，平平安安顺顺利利在一起，永远不分开。”
[EN] Matron Zou took the wooden comb from Bai Feng and carefully combed the prince's hair.
[ZH] 申简辰没有回应，他用心感受着充满感情的话语，能确定的是他与千里寻之间没有任何障碍，可以毫无顾忌地走向她。
[EN] Shen Jianchen made no reply. He listened with his whole heart to the words so full of feeling. One thing was certain: nothing stood between him and Qianli Xun now. He could go to her without a shadow of hesitation.
[ZH] 收拾完毕后，整个人精神许多。
[EN] When he was done, he looked a new man.
[ZH] ﻿申简辰吩咐白风不必跟着，想要早日娶苦荞过门，赶快将竹屋建起来，他则独自一人则骑马向城里的邵家镖局而去。
[EN] Shen Jianchen told Bai Feng not to follow him. Bai Feng wanted to bring Kuqiao into the house as soon as possible and hurried off to get the bamboo house built. Shen Jianchen rode alone toward the Shao family escort agency in the city.

#CH 347
[ZH] 邵家镖局门外，申简辰匆忙跳下马，快速进入镖局。
[EN] Shen Jianchen sprang from his horse outside the Shao escort compound and hurried inside.
[ZH] 镖局的手下认得仪王：“殿下来了，我这就禀报邵当家。”
[EN] One of the escorts recognized Prince Yi. "Your Highness has come. I'll announce you to Master Shao at once."
[ZH] “无须禀报，只需告诉我邵当家在哪里？”申简辰加快步伐。
[EN] "No need to announce me. Just tell me where Master Shao is." Shen Jianchen quickened his pace.
[ZH] 镖局的手下紧跟上：“在后院操练。”
[EN] The escort hurried after him. "Training in the back yard."
[ZH] 申简辰继续加快步伐向后院走。
[EN] Shen Jianchen strode faster toward the back yard.
[ZH] 邵正邺正在亲自操练几名刚入镖局的手下，冷不丁眼前跪下一人，仔细一瞧原来是仪王。邵正邺惶恐至极，上前去扶：“仪王这是何意？折煞我也！”
[EN] Shao Zhengye was drilling a few new escorts himself when a man dropped to his knees right in front of him. He looked closer and saw Prince Yi. Shao Zhengye was struck with alarm and stepped forward to help him up. "What is Your Highness doing? You'll be the death of me!"
[ZH] 申简辰坚决不起：“邵当家不答应，我便长跪不起。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen refused to rise. "Until Master Shao agrees, I shall stay on my knees."
[ZH] “仪王快起来，你不起来我可什么都不答应。”
[EN] "Get up, Prince Yi. If you stay down, I'll promise you nothing."
[ZH] “好，我起来。”
[EN] "Very well. I'll stand."
[ZH] 待仪王起身后，邵正邺对手下说：“都退下吧，我与仪王有话要说。”
[EN] Once Prince Yi was on his feet, Shao Zhengye said to his men, "All of you, go. I need a word with His Highness."
[ZH] 估摸着手下走远，邵正邺向仪王行礼：“殿下今日此举不知因何缘故？”
[EN] When he judged them out of earshot, Shao Zhengye bowed to Prince Yi. "May I ask what brings Your Highness here today?"
[ZH] “想请邵当家的妹妹出面救人。”
[EN] "I've come to ask your sister to save a life."
[ZH] “为何要请胞妹出面？”
[EN] "Why my sister?"
[ZH] “我想救的人在完颜池手中……”
[EN] "The woman I want to save is in Wanyan Chi's hands..."
[ZH] 邵正邺犹豫道：“可以想想其他办法……”
[EN] Shao Zhengye hesitated. "Surely there's another way..."
[ZH] “所有办法已想过，均无法撼动完颜池，无路可走才想到邵家。”
[EN] "I've tried every way. Nothing shakes Wanyan Chi. I came to the Shao family because I have nowhere left to turn."
[ZH] “不知仪王所救何人？”
[EN] "Who is it Your Highness wants to save?"
[ZH] “幽栖居士。”
[EN] "Youqi Jushi."
[ZH] “听说人死在金军刀下。”
[EN] "I heard she died under the Jin blades."
[ZH] “我刚刚得到消息她没死，应该还在完颜池手中。”
[EN] "I learned only now that she's alive. She must still be in Wanyan Chi's hands."
[ZH] 仪王与幽栖居士的事邵正邺听人议论过，仪王如此深情，实在令人感动。他刚才不顾王爷身份跪下，足以证明今日得不到他的应允绝不离开。
[EN] Shao Zhengye had heard people talk of Prince Yi and Youqi Jushi. Such devotion moved him. The fact that the prince had knelt despite his rank proved he would not leave without a promise.
[ZH] “既是救仪王的人邵家义不容辞，只是我妹妹出家已久，恐怕难以说动。”
[EN] "If it's to save Prince Yi's woman, the Shao family can't refuse. But my sister has been a nun for years. I doubt I can persuade her."
[ZH] “所以我才请邵当家出面。”
[EN] "That's why I came to you."
[ZH] 邵正邺犹豫片刻，果断答应：“请仪王随我去山上的尼姑庵。”
[EN] Shao Zhengye thought it over, then made up his mind. "Come with me to the convent up the mountain, Your Highness."
[ZH] 邵正邺独自陪同仪王来到山脚下，拴了马后，步行上山。
[EN] Shao Zhengye went alone with Prince Yi to the foot of the mountain. They tied their horses and walked up.
[ZH] 申简辰心里明白，邵正邺之所以单独陪他，是不想邵家与金人的关系让更多人知道。
[EN] Shen Jianchen understood why Shao Zhengye had come alone: he did not want word of the Shao family's ties to the Jin spreading.
[ZH] 到达尼姑庵外，负责传话的小尼姑出来后，邵正邺说：“小师傅，请转告净悟法师，我想见见她。”
[EN] At the convent gate, the young nun who handled messages came out. Shao Zhengye said, "Sister, please tell Reverend Jingwu I'd like to see her."
[ZH] 小尼姑认得邵正邺：“邵当家稍候。”
[EN] The young nun knew him. "Wait here, Master Shao."
[ZH] 没多久，小尼姑又来到门口：“净悟法师说让邵当家到客堂等候，二位请跟我来。”
[EN] Before long she returned to the gate. "Reverend Jingwu says to wait in the guest hall. Follow me, both of you."
[ZH] 二人来到客堂，小尼姑上好茶自行离去。
[EN] They entered the guest hall. The young nun served tea and left them alone.
[ZH] 两人品完一盏茶后，一位四十开外的女尼进入客堂。
[EN] They had finished one cup when a nun in her forties walked in.
[ZH] 申简辰站起来行礼：“阿弥陀佛。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen stood and bowed. "Amitabha."
[ZH] 邵月婷回礼道：“阿弥陀佛，施主请坐。”
[EN] Shao Youting returned the bow. "Amitabha. Please sit, benefactor."
[ZH] 邵月婷坐下主动问：“哥哥许久不来看我，好不容易来一趟却带一位陌生人，不知是何缘故？”
[EN] Shao Youting sat down and spoke first. "Brother, it's been ages since you visited. You finally come, and you bring a stranger. What's the reason?"
[ZH] 邵正邺解释道：“你也知镖局事务繁忙，再说哥哥不好上山打扰你清修。”
[EN] Shao Zhengye explained, "You know how busy the escort business is. Besides, I didn't wish to climb the mountain and disturb your meditation."
[ZH] 邵月婷看向仪王：“哥哥还是给我介绍一下你带来的年轻人。”
[EN] Shao Youting looked at Prince Yi. "Brother, introduce the young man you've brought."
[ZH] 邵正邺介绍道：“这位是仪王殿下。”
[EN] Shao Zhengye said, "This is His Highness Prince Yi."
[ZH] 邵月婷并未起身行礼，依旧端坐，态度平和：“原来是皇家人，难怪哥哥会带来见我。我佛向来主张众生平等，我乃出家人，恕我不能遵从俗世礼仪向贵人行礼。”
[EN] Shao Youting did not rise or bow. She sat straight, her tone even. "So, royalty. No wonder you brought him to see me. Buddhism teaches that all beings are equal. I'm a nun, so forgive me for not observing worldly customs and bowing to a noble."
[ZH] 申简辰并不介意：“我虽身是皇家人，却自视为普通百姓，法师只需将我看作普通众生即可。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen didn't mind. "Though I was born royal, I think of myself as an ordinary man. Reverend, you may treat me as just another being."
[ZH] 邵月婷赞道：“仪王乃智慧之人。”
[EN] Shao Youting praised him. "Prince Yi is a wise man."
[ZH] 两人说话间，邵正邺犹豫如何道出实情，也不知妹妹经过二十多年修行是否能超脱人间感情，从而原谅自己。
[EN] As they spoke, Shao Zhengye weighed how to tell the truth. He wondered whether twenty years of practice had freed his sister from earthly feeling enough to forgive him.
[ZH] 为救人只好鼓起勇气：“妹妹，有一事藏在哥哥心里，本想一直隐瞒下去，却事与愿违。我想应是妹妹修行感动上天，才逼得哥哥不得不上山向你道明实情，希望妹妹不要怪罪哥哥。”
[EN] To save the woman, he had to summon his courage. "Little sister, there's something I've kept in my heart. I meant to hide it forever, but things went otherwise. I think your practice moved Heaven, and it has forced me to climb this mountain and tell you the truth. Please don't blame me."
[ZH] 申简辰深感愧疚，自己的事逼得邵当家不得不说出隐瞒了二十多年的事。
[EN] Shen Jianchen felt deeply ashamed. His own trouble had forced Master Shao to reveal a secret kept for over twenty years.
[ZH] 邵月婷先是疑惑，紧接着品出些滋味来：“哥哥难道想跟我提二十多年前的事？”
[EN] Shao Youting was puzzled at first, then caught his meaning. "Brother, are you speaking of what happened more than twenty years ago?"
[ZH] “正是。”
[EN] "Exactly."
[ZH] “我修的是六根清净，哥哥又何必再提当年之事？”
[EN] "I practice detachment of the six senses. Why bring up the past now?"
[ZH] “佛家讲救人一命胜造七级浮屠，若不是为救人，我又何必再提当年之事？”
[EN] "The Buddha says saving a life is better than building a seven-tiered pagoda. If it weren't to save a life, why would I speak of it again?"
[ZH] “哥哥这话说得甚是蹊跷。”
[EN] "That's a strange thing to say, brother."
[ZH] 邵正邺站起来，踱步走到门口，把客堂的门关好。
[EN] Shao Zhengye stood, walked to the door, and shut the guest hall tight.
[ZH] “哥哥这是何意？”
[EN] "What do you mean, brother?"
[ZH] 邵正邺转过身：“妹妹，哥哥骗了你，你的儿子还在人世，我也没有杀死完颜吴乞买，他现在是金国的皇帝……”
[EN] Shao Zhengye turned around. "Sister, I lied to you. Your son is alive. And I didn't kill Wanyan Wobuyan. He's the emperor of Jin now..."
[ZH] 突如其来的消息的确令邵月婷震惊，她紧紧捏住手中的佛珠，努力用平静的表情压服内心的情绪：“哥哥既然骗我，为何不继续欺骗？却要道出实情？”
[EN] The news stunned Shao Youting. She gripped her prayer beads, forcing her face to stay calm while her heart churned. "If you lied, brother, why not keep lying? Why tell me the truth?"
[ZH] “我刚说过是为救人。”
[EN] "I just said: to save a life."
[ZH] “与我何干？”
[EN] "What has that to do with me?"
[ZH] “你的儿子现在是金国小王爷，名唤完颜池，他多次来临安找亲生娘亲，都被我打跑，有一次他甚至说只要能见娘亲一面，哪怕即刻死了也心甘情愿……”
[EN] "Your son is a young Jin prince now, named Wanyan Chi. He's come to Lin'an more than once looking for his birth mother, and each time I drove him away. Once he said that if he could only see his mother for a moment, he'd die content..."
[ZH] 眼泪从邵月婷的眼角滚落下来。
[EN] Tears rolled down Shao Youting's cheeks.
[ZH] “仪王所爱之人被完颜池抓去，他想尽办法都无法从完颜池手中夺回爱人，才求到邵家镖局，跪在我面前。我被感动，更不希望身上流淌着邵家血液的人干出伤天害理之事，才决定说出实情，”
[EN] "The woman Prince Yi loves was seized by Wanyan Chi. He's tried every means and can't win her back, so he came to the Shao escort compound and knelt before me. I was moved. And I didn't want a man with Shao blood in his veins doing something so cruel. That's why I decided to tell you the truth."
[ZH] “哥哥想让我做什么？”
[EN] "What does my brother want me to do?"
[ZH] “跟仪王去趟燕京，见见完颜池，劝他放人。”
[EN] "Go with Prince Yi to Yanjing. See Wanyan Chi. Persuade him to let her go."
[ZH] 邵月婷脸上挂着泪痕沉默不语。
[EN] Shao Youting sat silent, tear streaks on her face.
[ZH] 申简辰豁出去，起身跪下，直接给邵月婷磕三个响头：“求法师帮我。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen threw caution aside. He stood, knelt, and knocked his head three times against the floor. "I beg you, Reverend, help me."
[ZH] 邵月婷略微平静一会儿，站起来：“我乃修行之人，向来不问世俗，仪王还是另寻他法，告辞。”
[EN] Shao Youting composed herself after a moment and stood. "I've left the world behind. I don't involve myself in secular matters. Prince Yi must find another way. Good day."
[ZH] 邵月婷既不顾仪王跪着，也不顾哥哥挡在面前，绕过邵正邺，推开客堂的门，走了出去，刚跨出门，忍了许久的泪水喷涌而出。
[EN] She paid no heed to the kneeling prince or to her brother blocking the way. She walked around Shao Zhengye, pushed open the door, and stepped out. The moment she crossed the threshold, the tears she'd held back for so long poured out.
[ZH] 邵正邺扶起仪王：“妹妹怨我欺骗了她，等她消气后自然会答应。”
[EN] Shao Zhengye helped Prince Yi up. "My sister resents my deception. Once her anger cools, she'll agree."
[ZH] 申简辰倍感惭愧：“为了我的事害邵当家不得已说出实情，我深感愧疚，这份恩情我会牢记在心。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen was filled with shame. "I'm sorry that my affair forced you to speak out. I'll remember this kindness all my life."
[ZH] “仪王言重，瞒着妹妹二十多年对她太不公平，我早已想将实情告知，苦于没有好的契机，仪王所求之事算是让我找到借口。”
[EN] "Prince Yi, you honor me. Keeping the truth from my sister for twenty years was unfair to her. I've long wanted to tell her. I just lacked the right moment. Your request gave me the excuse."
[ZH] “邵当家不担心与金人扯上关系连累邵家？”
[EN] "Aren't you afraid ties to the Jin will bring trouble on the Shao family?"
[ZH] “当然怕。金人攻打大宋，多少大宋官员不战而降，邵家的事与他们比起来不值一提，他们都不怕百姓唾骂，我邵家更没资格怕。何况说出真相是为救人，若百姓知道说不定还会夸赞邵家义薄云天。”
[EN] "Of course I am. When the Jin attacked Song, how many Song officials surrendered without a fight? What our family did is nothing beside theirs. They weren't afraid of the people's scorn, so how can the Shaos claim to be? Besides, telling the truth is to save a life. If the people knew, they might even praise the Shao family for its loyalty."
[ZH] “邵当家如此想，我会心安些。”
[EN] "If you see it that way, I can rest easier."
[ZH] 两人来到尼姑庵门外，邵正邺打算下山，申简辰则立足不前。
[EN] They came out to the convent gate. Shao Zhengye meant to head down the mountain, but Shen Jianchen stayed put.
[ZH] “殿下先下山，等妹妹想通自然会答应。”
[EN] "Go down first, Your Highness. Once my sister has thought it through, she'll agree."
[ZH] “也不知净悟法师何日能想通？我急着赶去燕京救人，必须今日得到答复。邵当家尽管离去，我要留在尼姑庵外耐心等待，希望用我的诚心打动法师。”
[EN] "When will Reverend Jingwu think it through? I must reach Yanjing to save her, and I need an answer today. Go back without me. I'll wait here at the gate and hope my sincerity moves her."
[ZH] “唉，没有其他更好的办法，只能委屈仪王。”
[EN] "Ah. There's no better way. I'll leave Prince Yi to it."
[ZH] “邵当家的忙已帮，剩下的交给我，你尽管下山。”
[EN] "You've done your part, Master Shao. Leave the rest to me and go down."
[ZH] 正是夕阳西下之时，尼姑庵的大门上染上夕阳的余晖，申简辰坐在门口的台阶上，享受着夕阳的温度。千里寻还活着，他的人生一下子充满希望。
[EN] The sun was setting. Its last light stained the convent gate. Shen Jianchen sat on the stone steps, warming himself in the glow. Qianli Xun was alive. His life was suddenly full of hope.
[ZH] 蔡靖将他骗的好苦，等到了燕京定要当面斥责。
[EN] Cai Jing had deceived him bitterly. When he reached Yanjing, he would confront the man to his face.
[ZH] 转念一想，要不是蔡靖欺骗他，他又如何能在陈浩宇面前表现得真实，让他相信他的娘子已不在，然后另觅新欢，现在连孩子都有了。
[EN] Then he thought again: if Cai Jing hadn't lied, how could he have acted so convincingly before Chen Haoyu, letting him believe his wife was gone and find someone new—a woman who now carried his child?
[ZH] 也许一切冥冥中注定，蔡靖欺骗的行为不过是为他清理掉障碍而已。神柳下女尼的话传入耳边：“守得云开见月明”，“随缘而不攀缘”。
[EN] Perhaps it was all fated. Cai Jing's lie had merely cleared the path for him. The nun under the willow came to mind: "When the clouds part, the moon shines," and "Follow fate, don't chase it."
[ZH] 那时的他看不到任何希望，无法理解女尼所说。回头想想过往的经历，猛然顿悟，人生只有随缘才可跨越一道道坎儿，最终拨云见日。
[EN] Back then he had seen no hope and could not grasp her words. Looking back now, it struck him: only by following fate could a man cross one hurdle after another and finally see the clouds break.
[ZH] 天黑时，小尼姑前来关门，见台阶上坐着个人，上前问好：“阿弥陀佛，施主为何还不下山？”
[EN] At dusk the young nun came to close the gate. Seeing someone on the steps, she went over and greeted him. "Amitabha. Why hasn't the benefactor gone down?"
[ZH] 申简辰起身有礼道：“请小师傅转达净悟法师，我在此等候她回话。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen rose and bowed. "Please tell Reverend Jingwu I'm waiting here for her answer."
[ZH] 小尼姑叹口气关上大门，将申简辰在外之事告知慈空，慈空又到山洞中说给住持。
[EN] The young nun sighed, shut the gate, and told Cikong about the man outside. Cikong went to the cave and told the abbess.
[ZH] 净光法师说：“请净悟到山洞来一趟。”
[EN] Reverend Jingguang said, "Ask Jingwu to come to the cave."
[ZH] 慈空出去不久，邵月婷独自一人进入山洞内，上了石炕，盘腿而坐。
[EN] Cikong left. Soon Shao Youting entered the cave alone, climbed onto the stone bed, and sat cross-legged.
[ZH] “师姐唤我何事？”
[EN] "What does Sister want with me?"
[ZH] 净光法师边盘佛珠边说：“听说今日邵家人来看你，走时留下一位年轻人，他一直守在门外等你回话。”
[EN] Reverend Jingguang fingered her beads. "I hear your family came to see you today. They left a young man behind. He's been outside the gate ever since, waiting for your answer."
[ZH] “该说的我已说，师姐不必为此费心。”
[EN] "I've said all I have to say. Sister needn't trouble herself."
[ZH] “各人造下的业由个人承受，我自然不必费心。既然你唤我一声师姐，我有义务提醒你，出家人修行是要消去累生累世所造之业。既然业已找上门来，没有理由拒之门外，应该想方设法了结，方可清净。”
[EN] "Each man bears his own karma. You're right, I needn't. But since you call me sister, it's my duty to remind you: a nun practices to burn away the karma of this life and lives before. Now karma has come to your door. You can't shut it out. You must find a way to settle it, and only then will you be clean."
[ZH] “师姐有所不知，我本以为自己的孩子不在人世，才看破红尘出家为尼，现在却知道他还活着，我怕见到他，二十多年修行的功夫化为泡影。”
[EN] "Sister doesn't understand. I thought my child was dead. That's why I saw through the world and became a nun. Now I learn he's alive. I'm afraid that if I see him, twenty years of practice will crumble."
[ZH] “我问你，可放得下男女之情？”
[EN] "Tell me this: can you let go of desire between man and woman?"
[ZH] “早已放下。”
[EN] "Long ago."
[ZH] “最难的已做到，其他的不在话下，要相信自己。”
[EN] "The hardest is done. The rest is nothing. Trust yourself."
[ZH] “我以为最难放下的是母子之情。”
[EN] "I thought the hardest was a mother's love for her child."
[ZH] “既然难放下不如勇敢面对，面对了才真正能放下，总是躲避，又如何消去业障？”
[EN] "If it's hard to let go, face it. Only by facing it can you truly let go. Keep avoiding it, and how will you clear your karma?"
[ZH] “我怕……”
[EN] "I'm afraid..."
[ZH] “修行之人没有畏惧之心。”
[EN] "A person in practice knows no fear."
[ZH] 邵月婷被说服，终于答应：“师姐的境界师妹难及，一番开悟令我茅塞顿开，我这就让人告诉门外的王爷，同意随他前去救人。”
[EN] Shao Youting was won over. At last she agreed. "Sister, I can't match your understanding. Your words have opened my eyes. I'll tell the prince outside the gate that I'll go with him to save her."
[ZH] 净光法师露出笑容：“此业消除，师妹的境界自然会得到提升，我圆寂之后，尼姑庵可放心交到师妹手中。”
[EN] Reverend Jingguang smiled. "Once this karma is cleared, your understanding will deepen. After I pass from this world, you may take charge of the convent."
[ZH] 尼姑庵的大门被打开，邵月婷走出来。
[EN] The convent gate opened. Shao Youting walked out.
[ZH] 申简辰激动地站起来：“法师可答应与我一同前去救人？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen rose in excitement. "Reverend, will you come with me to save her?"
[ZH] 邵月婷点点头：“阿弥陀佛！让施主久等，明日一早请施主在山下等我。”
[EN] Shao Youting nodded. "Amitabha! I've kept you waiting. At first light tomorrow, wait for me at the foot of the mountain."
[ZH] 申简辰开心不已：“多谢法师！多谢法师！”
