[EN] Shen Jianchen was overjoyed. "Thank you, Reverend. Thank you."
[ZH] 一大早两辆马车候在山下，邵月婷果不食言，收拾好包袱，跟随仪王前往燕京。
[EN] Early the next morning two carriages waited at the foot of the mountain. Shao Youting kept her word. She packed her things and set off with Prince Yi for Yanjing.
[ZH] 宋金的战事暂时搁置，大宋表面上恢复平静，连那位临阵逃脱的父皇听说已从镇江回到汴梁。他的回归会让本就千疮百孔的朝廷更加千疮百孔，他恐怕还没意识到几个月后汴梁也无法保住。
[EN] Fighting between Song and Jin had paused. On the surface Song was calm again. Even the emperor who had fled the battlefield—his father—was said to have returned from Zhenjiang to Bianliang. His return would make an already broken court more broken still. He likely had no idea that in a few months even Bianliang would be lost.
[ZH] 表面上的祥和也无法掩盖难民的数量，金军第一次南下，大宋的可怜人增加不少，一路上不断遇到因战乱失去家园之人，他们凭借双脚向南走，远离家乡乞讨生活。
[EN] The outward peace could not hide the number of refugees. The Jin's first push south had added to the ranks of Song's poor. All along the road they met families driven from home by war, walking south on foot, begging far from the places they called home.
[ZH] 两个难民说话时，申简辰无意中在马车上听到。
[EN] Shen Jianchen overheard two refugees talking from the carriage.
[ZH] 女的说：“都不打仗了，还是回去吧？到南边人生地不熟的，怎么过日子？”
[EN] The woman said, "The fighting's over. Why not go back? Down south we know nobody. How will we live?"
[ZH] 男的说：“头发长见识短，没听说金人不会善罢甘休吗？迟早还会打到汴梁，再回去，到时打起来刀剑无眼，吃亏的只能是老百姓。早些往南走，早些安顿下来，听我的准没错。”
[EN] The man said, "Short hair, short sight. Haven't you heard the Jin won't stop? Sooner or later they'll reach Bianliang. If we go back and fighting starts again, blades have no eyes. It's ordinary folk who get hurt. Go south early, settle early. Listen to me."
[ZH] 申简辰心想：“百姓看得明明白白的事，也不知朝堂的新旧官家是否明白。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen thought, "What common people see so clearly—do the court's new and old rulers see it too?"
[ZH] ……
[EN] ...
[ZH] 路上行走了一个多月，终于来到燕京城外，城门口和城墙上均是把守的金人。
[EN] More than a month on the road, they finally reached the outskirts of Yanjing. Jin soldiers guarded the gate and lined the walls.
[ZH] 申简辰走下马车，用轻功跳上墙，打倒几个围攻的金兵，向上次嘲笑他的将领袭击而去，几拳将其打倒：“告诉完颜池，我带了他想见的人，他若愿意见，必须用我想要的人交换！”
[EN] Shen Jianchen stepped down, used his lightness skill to leap onto the wall, knocked down several Jin soldiers closing in, and struck the officer who had mocked him last time. A few punches brought the man down. "Tell Wanyan Chi I've brought the person he wants to see. If he'll see her, he must trade me the woman I want!"
[ZH] 被打伤的将领嘴上并不示弱：“你等着！小王爷来会要了你的命！”
[EN] The injured officer didn't yield in words. "Just you wait! When the young prince comes, he'll take your life!"
[ZH] 完颜池正在王府练习拳法，他的师傅纳河谷正在一旁指导。
[EN] Wanyan Chi was practicing boxing in the prince's residence. His master Na He Gu stood by, guiding him.
[ZH] 城墙上的将领在侍卫的带领下前来报告：“小王爷，大事不好，大宋的仪王又跑来要人，在城外候着。”
[EN] An officer led by a guard came to report. "Young Prince, bad news. Prince Yi of Song is here again, demanding the woman. He's waiting outside the city."
[ZH] 完颜池收起拳法：“真是好玩儿，他竟然还以为美人在我府中，那我就逗他一逗。”
[EN] Wanyan Chi drew back his fists. "This is fun. He still thinks the beauty is in my residence. I'll toy with him a little."
[ZH] 纳河谷说：“小王爷，既然大宋仪王的拳法与我创制的拳法一样，甚至很多招数比我的还精妙，不如趁此机会将他留下。”
[EN] Na He Gu said, "Young Prince, since Prince Yi of Song uses the same boxing I created—and many of his moves are finer than mine—why not take the chance to keep him here?"
[ZH] “师傅既有此想法，徒儿定会满足。”完颜池又问哪位将领，“他还说了什么？”
[EN] "If that's your wish, Master, I'll see it done." Wanyan Chi turned to the officer. "What else did he say?"
[ZH] 将领回道：“他说他带了小王爷想见的人，小王爷若愿意见，需用他想要的人交换。”
[EN] The officer replied, "He says he's brought the person the Young Prince wants to see. If you'll see her, he wants the woman he wants in exchange."
[ZH] “我想见的人？”完颜池轻声重复着，难道是他的娘亲？完颜池变得激动，“带上人随我去城外！”
[EN] "The person I want to see?" Wanyan Chi repeated under his breath. Could it be his mother? His pulse quickened. "Bring men and horses. We're going outside the city!"
[ZH] 陆远峰大声回道：“是！”
[EN] Lu Yuanfeng called out, "Yes!"
[ZH] 王府外，完颜池跳上马后，想起一事，向陆远峰吩咐：“你不必随我前去，去看着她，以防她与大宋的仪王碰上，坏我的大事。”
[EN] Outside the residence, Wanyan Chi mounted and remembered something. He turned to Lu Yuanfeng. "Don't come with me. Go and watch her. Make sure she doesn't run into Prince Yi of Song and ruin this."
[ZH] “是。”陆远峰扭转马头朝另一个方向走去。
[EN] "Yes." Lu Yuanfeng turned his horse toward the other direction.

#CH 348
[ZH] 离开完颜池那日，千里寻孤身一人在燕京街头逛荡，她犹豫是离开燕京还是去别处？能肯定的是她不会回临安或者去汴梁。
[EN] The day she left Wanyan Chi, Qianli Xun wandered the streets of Yanjing alone, torn between leaving the city and going somewhere else. One thing was certain: she would not return to Lin'an or go to Bianliang.
[ZH] 尽管这两个地方随便选一个她都十分乐意，可想到自己失身完颜池，她没有勇气去找她想要找的人，最终决定一个人在燕京度过。
[EN] She would have been glad to pick either place, but the thought of having given herself to Wanyan Chi stripped her of the courage to seek the people she wanted to see. In the end she decided to stay in Yanjing, on her own.
[ZH] 她身无分文，走出王府时什么都没带，该如何在燕京讨生活？想到自己一身功夫，不如卖艺先赚些钱，再租个地方住下来。
[EN] She had no money. She had walked out of the prince's mansion with nothing. How was she to make a living in Yanjing? She thought of her martial arts. She would perform in the street, earn some cash, then rent a place to sleep.
[ZH] 她在街头选了一块空地，把斗篷脱掉，叠整齐放在墙根的石头上，向来来往往的人大声说：“南来的北往的！向这儿瞧了！……”
[EN] She picked a patch of open ground, took off her cloak, folded it neatly, and set it on a stone at the foot of the wall. Then she called out to everyone passing by, 'Travelers from south and north! Look over here!...'
[ZH] 一些逛街的人被吸引，靠拢而来。
[EN] A few people out shopping were drawn by her voice and drew closer.
[ZH] “小女初来乍到，身无分文，好在会些拳脚功夫，在此给各位表演一套拳法，若觉得好，赏小女几文，若觉得不好分文不取！”
[EN] 'This girl is new here and hasn't a coin. Luckily I know a few moves. I'll perform a set of fist forms for you. If you like it, toss me a few coppers. If not, I won't take a single one.'
[ZH] 围观的人越来越多，都想目睹美娇娘功夫如何。
[EN] The crowd grew. Everyone wanted to see what a pretty young woman could do with her fists.
[ZH] 千里寻摆好姿势，她打算表演配合朱淑真的《念奴娇·冬晴无雪》创制的拳法。她边吟诵诗词边使出拳法，诗词吟诵得饱含情感，拳法招招与诗词相配，阳刚与阴柔之美交织在一起，既赏心悦目，又令人惊叹拳技之高超。
[EN] Qianli Xun set her stance. She would perform the fist sequence she had created to go with Zhu Shuzhen's 'Nian Nu Jiao: Clear Winter, No Snow.' She chanted the lines as she struck, her voice thick with feeling, every move fitted to a line of the poem. Hard strength and soft grace wound together, lovely to watch and astonishing in their skill.
[ZH] 诗词念完时，拳法也跟着结束，周围的百姓使劲儿鼓掌。千里寻掬起双手上前讨钱，许多人一文两文地放进她的手中，一圈儿下来，挣了一把钱，临时找个地方住应该不成问题。
[EN] When the last line faded, the last fist fell. The onlookers clapped hard. Qianli Xun cupped her hands and went among them for coins. People dropped in one and two at a time. By the time she had made the round she held a fistful of cash—enough for a bed for the night.
[ZH] 正当她高兴时，陆远峰过来，将围着的百姓驱散：“都走吧，别看了，她不会再表演。”
[EN] She was still smiling over it when Lu Yuanfeng came up and waved the crowd away. 'Go on, all of you. Nothing more to see. She's done performing.'
[ZH] 百姓们纷纷离开。
[EN] The people drifted off.
[ZH] 千里寻掬着钱，生气地质问道：“陆远峰！你太过分！凭什么赶走我的客人？”
[EN] Qianli Xun cupped her coins and glared at him. 'Lu Yuanfeng! How dare you? What right have you to chase away my customers?'
[ZH] 陆远峰转过身：“师傅，你是何等身份？怎能在此卖艺？”
[EN] Lu Yuanfeng turned around. 'Master, who are you? How can you perform in the street like this?'
[ZH] “我不过是普通百姓一个，为什么不能卖艺？我卖艺既不违背良心也不违背律法，你管得着吗？”
[EN] 'I'm an ordinary commoner. Why shouldn't I earn a living? I break no law and wrong no conscience. Since when is it your business?'
[ZH] 陆远峰见师傅掬着钱十分辛苦，到旁边的摊位上买来一个荷包，把师傅手中的钱往荷包里装：“师傅何必如此？跟着小王爷多好？偏偏跑出来在街头卖艺，小王爷要是知道心里肯定不好受。”
[EN] Lu Yuanfeng saw how hard she had worked for those coins. He went to a nearby stall, bought a small purse, and began pouring her money into it. 'Why do this, Master? Life with the young prince would be good. Instead you run out here to perform. If he learns of it, his heart will ache, I promise you.'
[ZH] “是完颜池派你来的？”
[EN] 'Did Wanyan Chi send you?'
[ZH] 陆远峰将钱全部装进荷包：“跟小王爷没关系，我出来办事，偶然看到师傅卖艺，才过来劝说。”
[EN] Lu Yuanfeng tucked the last coin into the purse. 'The young prince has nothing to do with it. I was out on an errand, saw you performing, and came over to talk sense into you.'
[ZH] 千里寻从陆远峰手中拿过荷包：“跟他没关系就好，你也不必劝说，我的事不许告诉完颜池。”
[EN] Qianli Xun took the purse from his hand. 'If it has nothing to do with him, good. Save your breath. Don't you dare tell Wanyan Chi a word of this.'
[ZH] 陆远峰假装道：“师傅和小王爷之间的事我不想参与，不过我得说句公道话，小王爷对师傅没的说，我在他身边也有一段时日，他从未对哪个姑娘像对师傅一样上心过。”
[EN] Lu Yuanfeng put on an innocent face. 'I don't want to meddle in what's between you and the prince. But I will say this much in fairness: the young prince has never been anything but good to you. I've served at his side a long time. He's never cared for a girl the way he cares for you.'
[ZH] “那又如何？对我好的人多了，难道我要个个喜欢？像完颜池那种喜欢霸王硬上钩的人老娘最讨厌，他打着喜欢别人的旗号，采取强硬手段，纯粹是为满足个人私欲，别以为老娘是瞎子，什么都看不明白。”
[EN] 'So what? Plenty of people are good to me. Must I love them all? A man like Wanyan Chi, who forces himself on a woman and calls it love—he's the sort I despise most. He waves a banner of affection while using brute force, all to feed his own desire. Don't think I'm blind. I can see straight through it.'
[ZH] 陆远峰竖起大拇指：“师傅是女中豪杰，跟一般女子不一样，佩服！”
[EN] Lu Yuanfeng raised his thumb. 'You're a heroine among women, Master. Not like other girls. I admire you.'
[ZH] “少奉承我！”千里寻把荷包装好，到墙根的石头跟前取了斗篷披好，准备离开。
[EN] 'Stop flattering me.' She tucked the purse away, went to the stone at the wall, picked up her cloak, and put it on, ready to leave.
[ZH] 陆远峰上前拦住：“师傅打算离开燕京？”
[EN] Lu Yuanfeng stepped in front of her. 'Are you leaving Yanjing, Master?'
[ZH] “你管得着吗？少拦我！”千里寻从一旁闪开。
[EN] 'What business is it of yours? Get out of my way.' She slipped past him.
[ZH] 陆远峰在身后喊：“师傅！你要是不打算离开燕京，我帮你找地方住！”
[EN] He called after her. 'Master! If you're not leaving Yanjing, I'll find you a place to stay!'
[ZH] 千里寻正为住的地方发愁，不如听他如何说。她转过身：“真的不是完颜池派你来的？”
[EN] She had been worrying about exactly that. She turned. 'You swear Wanyan Chi didn't send you?'
[ZH] 陆远峰继续撒谎：“真的不是，我称你一声师傅，总不能看着你沦落街头，伸手帮一把也是应该的。”
[EN] Lu Yuanfeng kept up the lie. 'He truly didn't. I call you Master. I can't stand by and watch you sleep in the street. Lending a hand is only right.'
[ZH] “住的地方在哪里？带我去吧。”千里寻的语气缓和下来，此时的她急需找到住的地方，有人送上门来，不如先住下。即使与完颜池有关，她也不怕，他若敢出现，不是他死便是她亡。
[EN] 'Where is this place? Take me there.' Qianli Xun's tone softened. She needed a roof badly. Here was one handed to her, and she might as well take it. Even if it had something to do with Wanyan Chi, she wasn't afraid. If he dared show his face, one of them would not walk away.
[ZH] 陆远峰见师傅松口，很开心：“不远处的巷子里，有个闲置的屋子，正好给师傅住。”
[EN] Lu Yuanfeng was delighted she had relented. 'Not far. There's an empty room in the lane nearby. Perfect for you, Master.'
[ZH] 千里寻怀疑道：“你整日跟完颜池住在王府，怎么会在外面有屋子？”
[EN] Qianli Xun eyed him suspiciously. 'You live in the prince's mansion with Wanyan Chi day and night. How do you come to have a room out here?'
[ZH] 陆远峰“嘿嘿”笑道：“我是男人，也喜欢养个把女人，在王府不太合适，临时租了间屋子，人还没安置进去，先给师傅住。”
[EN] Lu Yuanfeng chuckled. 'I'm a man. A man likes to keep a woman on the side. Can't do that in the mansion, so I rented a room. Hadn't moved her in yet. You can have it first, Master.'
[ZH] “我岂不是坏了你的好事？”
[EN] 'Am I ruining your little arrangement?'
[ZH] “哪里话，给师傅住是我的荣幸。”
[EN] 'Don't say that. It's an honor to have you there.'
[ZH] 千里寻把荷包掏出来，走上前，递给陆远峰：“今晚的租金，有些少，别介意，等我安顿下来挣到更多银子再补上。”
[EN] Qianli Xun pulled out her purse, stepped forward, and held it out to him. 'Rent for tonight. It's not much, don't mind that. Once I'm settled and earn more silver, I'll make it up to you.'
[ZH] 陆远峰推回师傅的手：“我怎能跟师傅要租金？师傅放心住下。”
[EN] Lu Yuanfeng pushed her hand back. 'How could I take rent from you, Master? Stay with an easy mind.'
[ZH] 千里寻坚持道：“你若不收，我便不住。”
[EN] Qianli Xun insisted. 'If you won't take it, I won't stay.'
[ZH] 陆远峰说：“我收我收，师傅还需钱生活，你先把钱拿回去，等以后挣到钱再补给我不迟。”
[EN] 'All right, all right, I'll take it. You need money to live on. Keep your coins now and pay me later, when you've earned more.'
[ZH] 千里寻心想，也成，便收回荷包：“谢了，带我去吧。”
[EN] Qianli Xun thought that made sense and drew the purse back. 'Thanks. Lead the way.'
[ZH] 陆远峰租的屋子尚可，对于急需容身之地的人来讲已是不错，她安心住下。
[EN] The room Lu Yuanfeng had rented was passable—more than passable for someone who just needed somewhere to be. She settled in with a quiet mind.
[ZH] 后来陆远峰以能尽快还房租为理由，给她介绍帮人写书信以及给茶馆提供新的诗词供茶客品鉴的活儿，每个月能挣一两银子。虽不多，也足以令她心安。
[EN] Later, saying she would need to pay the rent back quickly, Lu Yuanfeng found her work writing letters for people and supplying new poems to a teahouse for the guests to enjoy. It came to a tael or two of silver a month. Not much, but enough to put her mind at rest.
[ZH] 千里寻自然没有忘记继续写话本，可惜在燕京说书不被允许。几经辗转才找到一位说话艺人，免费将话本的书稿送给他，假装千里寻才子认识她留给她的。说话艺人听后高兴不已，答应她想办法将话本寄出燕京城。
[EN] Qianli Xun had not forgotten to keep writing story scripts, but storytelling was forbidden in Yanjing. After some searching she found a professional storyteller and gave him the manuscripts for free, claiming a scholar named Qianli Xun, an acquaintance, had left them with her. The storyteller was delighted and promised to find a way to send the stories out of the city.
[ZH] 为了以后写的故事能被百姓听到，千里寻告诉说话艺人，后续千里寻才子还会提供话本。
[EN] So that the stories might one day reach ordinary listeners, Qianli Xun told him the scholar would be providing more.
[ZH] 说话艺人急切地说：“燕京城只有我有门路能将话本送出，后面的统统给我，来者不拒。”
[EN] The storyteller spoke eagerly. 'I'm the only one in Yanjing with a way to get scripts out. Send me every one you have. I'll take as many as come.'
[ZH] 千里寻欣然答应。
[EN] Qianli Xun gladly agreed.
[ZH] 大宋已进入靖康元年，一个耻辱即将来临的年份。
[EN] The Song had entered the first year of Jingkang—a year of shame about to arrive.
[ZH] 过元日时，千里寻一个人在他乡度过，心中充满对亲人和爱人的思念。恐怕大家都以为她不在人世，她不想回去打扰每个人平静的生活。
[EN] New Year's Day came and Qianli Xun spent it in a strange land, her heart full of thoughts of family and of the man she loved. No doubt they all believed her dead. She had no wish to go back and disturb the quiet lives of everyone she knew.
[ZH] 正月初六这晚，天空晴朗，新月升起。千里寻披上斗篷，来到院中，抬头仰望新月的模样，心中怀念旧人，也怀念她常常去看望的神柳。
[EN] On the sixth night of the first month the sky was clear and a new moon rose. Qianli Xun wrapped her cloak around her, went into the courtyard, and looked up at the crescent. She missed old friends, and she missed the sacred willow she used to visit.
[ZH] 时代的悲哀令身在异乡的她更有一种悲戚之情，明日该给茶馆提供诗词，茶馆的老板说春节期间最好不要写太过悲伤的句子，可此时的她只回忆起悲伤的诗词。
[EN] The sorrow of the age weighed heavier on her in a foreign place. Tomorrow she had to deliver poems to the teahouse. Its owner had said to avoid lines too sad during the New Year holidays. But all she could recall were sorrowful verses.
[ZH] 正为难间，想到今日是正月初六，正好朱淑真有首《忆秦娥·正月初六日夜月》，与今晚的夜景相合，还符合茶馆的要求，随即进屋提笔写下：
[EN] She was stuck, then remembered today was the sixth of the first month. Zhu Shuzhen had a poem, 'Yi Qin E: Moon on the Night of the Sixth Day of the First Month,' that suited the night and would please the teahouse. She went inside, took up her brush, and wrote:
[ZH] 弯弯曲，新年新月钩寒玉。钩寒玉，凤鞋儿小，翠眉儿蹙。
[EN] Crooked, so crooked, the new moon of the new year hooks cold jade. Hooks cold jade—small phoenix shoes, brows of green knit tight.
[ZH] 闹蛾雪柳添妆束，烛龙火树争驰逐。争驰逐，元宵三五，不如初六。
[EN] Moth and willow ornaments add to her dress, candle-dragon and fire-tree race along. They race along—the Lantern Festival on the fifteenth is not as good as this sixth day.
[ZH] 落款时，千里寻犹豫起来，金人已下令必须用金的年号，若不用便是杀头之罪。
[EN] When she came to the signature, Qianli Xun hesitated. The Jin had ordered everyone to use their reign title. To refuse was punishable by death.
[ZH] 片刻犹豫后，千里寻果断写下“靖康元年正月初六”，她偏不用金的年号，大不了死在金人刀下，反正她心里念着的人都以为她已不在。
[EN] She hesitated only a moment, then wrote firmly, 'First year of Jingkang, sixth day of the first month.' She would not use the Jin calendar. Let them kill her with their swords, if it came to that. Everyone she cared about already believed her gone.
[ZH] 结果她一点儿事没有，诗词照样挂在茶馆里，她猜测应是陆远峰帮忙，毕竟他在完颜池身边混得不错。
[EN] Nothing happened. The poem hung in the teahouse as before. She guessed Lu Yuanfeng had smoothed things over. He stood high enough in Wanyan Chi's favor, after all.
[ZH] 进入春季千里寻的日子会好过些，令她庆幸的是，完颜池没有打扰过她，她更相信陆远峰说的话，他所做的一切的确与完颜池没有任何关系。
[EN] Spring would make her life easier, and to her relief Wanyan Chi left her alone. She believed more strongly what Lu Yuanfeng had said—that everything he did truly had nothing to do with the prince.
[ZH] 桃花刚刚开放时，陆远峰又给她带来新的差事，给金人的孩子教汉人的文字。
[EN] When the peach blossoms first opened, Lu Yuanfeng brought her a new job: teaching Jin children to read and write Chinese.
[ZH] 开始千里寻不愿接受，事后一想，学习汉文化是将金人汉化的绝佳途径。金人发动战争伤害了多少汉人，不如趁机把他们的孩子汉化，让他们的思想变得与汉人一致。
[EN] At first she refused. Then she thought it over. Learning Han culture was the perfect way to turn the Jin into Han. How many Han people had their wars harmed? Better to win over their children while there was still time, to make their thoughts the same as Han thoughts.
[ZH] 千里寻为自己的计谋感到开心，等陆远峰再来时，告诉他愿意接下此事。
[EN] Qianli Xun was pleased with her little scheme. When Lu Yuanfeng came again, she told him she would take the work.
[ZH] 屋子的外厅变成学堂，每日会有六名金人的孩子前来学习。为了改造他们，千里寻夜以继日翻阅买回的典籍。
[EN] The outer room became a schoolroom. Six Jin children came every day. To reshape them, Qianli Xun pored over the books she bought, day and night.
[ZH] 在她的努力下，教学的效果不错，六名孩子都认为金汉乃一家，不应发动战争互相残杀。
[EN] Her efforts paid off. All six came to believe that Jin and Han were one family and that war between them was senseless killing.
[ZH] ……
[EN] ...
[ZH] 今日，正在教孩子们学习《论语》，陆远峰跑来，坐在一旁听了一会儿。
[EN] Today she was teaching the children the Analects when Lu Yuanfeng came in and sat to one side to listen for a while.
[ZH] 待孩子们离开后，千里寻取来一袋银子给陆远峰：“感谢你一直以来帮我，一年的房租已挣到，你拿着。”
[EN] When the children had gone, Qianli Xun fetched a bag of silver and handed it to him. 'Thank you for all your help. A year's rent is covered. Take this.'
[ZH] 陆远峰装模作样接过：“师傅今日有什么打算？”
[EN] Lu Yuanfeng accepted it with a show of reluctance. 'What are your plans for today, Master?'
[ZH] “这话问得奇怪，我能有什么打算？每日这个时候我会逛街，笔用了好久，开始掉毛，墨条也快用完，正好出去买些回来。”
[EN] 'A strange question. What plans could I have? I go shopping at this hour every day. This brush has shed half its hairs, and the ink stick is nearly gone. Time to buy more.'
[ZH] 陆远峰担心师傅跑到主街，若碰到仪王，或是听到百姓议论，恐怕坏了小王爷的大事。建议道：“我知道有一家笔墨甚好，我带师傅去。”
[EN] Lu Yuanfeng worried she might wander onto the main street and run into Prince Yi, or overhear people talking, and spoil the young prince's plan. He suggested, 'I know a shop with very good brushes and ink. Let me take you there.'
[ZH] 千里寻并未多想：“你推荐的应该不错，在外面等会儿，我换件衣裳就来。”
[EN] Qianli Xun gave it no thought. 'If you recommend it, it must be good. Wait outside a moment. I'll change and come out.'
[ZH] 师傅进去换衣裳的空档，陆远峰走到一旁的柜子跟前，拉开抽屉，把一袋银子放进去，自语道：“租金都是小王爷出的，我怎敢要你的银子？”
[EN] While she changed, Lu Yuanfeng went to a cabinet, pulled open a drawer, put the bag of silver inside, and muttered to himself, 'The prince paid the rent. How could I take your silver?'
[ZH] 千里寻换好衣裳后，跟着陆远峰一起逛街。
[EN] Qianli Xun finished changing and went shopping with him.
[ZH] 陆远峰专挑偏僻的街道走，不仅人少，卖东西的铺子也不多。千里寻十分纳闷：“你怎么把我往偏僻的街道带？半天没看到一家卖笔墨的铺子。”
[EN] Lu Yuanfeng chose quiet back streets, where there were few people and few shops. Qianli Xun wondered at it. 'Why are you leading me into the back lanes? We haven't passed a single shop selling brushes.'
[ZH] “别着急，再往前走就到。”陆远峰解释完，趁机说，“师傅，最近我想一直陪你。”
[EN] 'Patience. Just a little farther.' He explained, then seized the moment. 'Master, lately I'd like to stay by your side.'
[ZH] 千里寻边走边说：“不用，还是好好陪你偷偷养在外面的女人。”
[EN] She walked on. 'No need. Go keep company with that woman you've secretly set up outside.'
[ZH] 陆远峰说：“外面的女人哪有师傅重要？我还是陪师傅。”
[EN] 'What woman out there could matter as much as you? I'll stay with you, Master.'
[ZH] 千里寻担心这家伙对自己有想法，停下脚步：“什么意思？”
[EN] Worried he had designs on her, Qianli Xun stopped short. 'What do you mean?'
[ZH] 陆远峰从师傅的表情中看出他的话说得有些重，感情表达得不合适，赶紧解释：“师傅别误会，我哪敢对小王爷喜欢的人动心思？我对师傅只有尊重。”
[EN] Lu Yuanfeng saw from her face that he had gone too far and expressed himself badly. He hurried to explain. 'Don't misunderstand me, Master. How would I dare want the woman the young prince loves? I only respect you.'
[ZH] 千里寻松口气，继续朝前走：“少在我面前提他。”
[EN] Qianli Xun let out a breath and walked on. 'Don't mention him to me again.'
[ZH] “你还没答应我是否让我陪你？”
[EN] 'You haven't said whether I may keep you company?'
[ZH] “不必！老娘现在习惯一个人！”
[EN] 'No! I'm used to being on my own now.'
[ZH] 陆远峰跟上师傅，两人拐了一个弯，终于看到一家售卖笔墨的铺子。还好笔墨不错，否则今日肯定要穿帮。
[EN] Lu Yuanfeng followed her. They turned a corner at last and saw a shop selling brushes and ink. Let the brushes be good, he thought, or he was caught out today.
[ZH] 话说完颜池带人来到燕京城门口，登上高高的城墙，向下俯瞰。金兵已将进出的百姓拦截并驱散，城门口里里外外围满金兵。
[EN] Now Wanyan Chi had brought his men to the gate of Yanjing and climbed the high wall to look down. Jin soldiers had stopped and scattered everyone entering or leaving. The gate was ringed with them, inside and out.
[ZH] 申简辰站在城墙下，盯着高高在上的完颜池：“你怎么一个人出现？我要的人呢？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen stood below the wall, glaring up at him. 'Why are you here alone? Where is the person I asked for?'
[ZH] 完颜池继续撒谎：“去年已告诉过你，她死了。”
[EN] Wanyan Chi kept up the lie. 'I told you last year. She's dead.'
[ZH] 申简辰不会再轻易被欺骗：“你的谎言对我毫无作用，既然你没把我想要的人带来，也别想见到你想见之人。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen would not be fooled again. 'Your lies mean nothing to me. Since you haven't brought the one I want, don't expect to see the one you want.'
[ZH] 完颜池注视着两辆马车：“本王想见的人可在马车里？”
[EN] Wanyan Chi eyed the two carriages. 'Is the person I wish to see in one of those?'
[ZH] 申简辰指着一辆马车：“你想见的人就在那辆马车里。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen pointed. 'The one you want is in that carriage.'
[ZH] 激动的泪水滚落下来，完颜池稍加平静后，问道：“我怎么能知道你是否在欺骗本王？”
[EN] Tears of joy ran down Wanyan Chi's face. When he had steadied himself, he asked, 'How do I know you're not deceiving me?'
[ZH] 马车里传出女人的声音：“仪王，你过来。”
[EN] A woman's voice came from the carriage. 'Prince Yi, come here.'
[ZH] 女人的声音慈祥而温柔，完颜池站在高处，多少听到一些，与他想象中娘亲的声音一模一样。
[EN] The voice was kind and gentle. Standing high on the wall, Wanyan Chi caught enough of it to know it was exactly the voice of the mother he remembered.
[ZH] 大宋的仪王走到马车旁，里面的人好像给他说了什么，只见仪王拱手之后抬头瞧过来。
[EN] The Prince Yi of Song walked to the carriage. Someone inside seemed to speak to him. He cupped his hands, then looked up.
[ZH] “小王爷想见的人告诉我你的左耳朵后面有颗红痣，不知可否属实？”
[EN] 'The man who wishes to see you tells me there is a red mole behind your left ear. Is that true?'
[ZH] 完颜池彻底沦陷，他匆忙跑下城墙，冲出城门，来到马车跟前，已泪流满面。
[EN] Wanyan Chi was undone. He scrambled down the wall, rushed through the gate, and reached the carriage with tears streaming down his face.
[ZH] “您是我娘？……”
[EN] 'Are you my mother?...'
[ZH] 马车里的邵月婷也泪如雨下：“我是你亲娘……”
[EN] Inside the carriage Shao Yueting was weeping too. 'I am your own mother...'
[ZH] 完颜池跪在地上：“娘让我找得好苦！为何躲着一直不见我？”
[EN] Wanyan Chi fell to his knees. 'Mother, I searched so hard for you! Why hide away and refuse to see me?'
[ZH] “见又有何用？只不过徒增烦恼。”
[EN] 'What good would seeing me do? Only more grief.'
[ZH] “娘，您下来，让儿子好好看看您。”
[EN] 'Mother, come down. Let me look at you properly.'
[ZH] “我今日是为救人而来，你不把那姑娘放了，娘绝不走下马车。”
[EN] 'I have come today to save a life. Until you free that girl, I will not step down from this carriage.'
[ZH] “好，我放，只要能见到娘，做什么我都愿意。”
[EN] 'All right. I'll free her. To see you, I'd do anything.'
[ZH] 完颜池答应放人，申简辰再高兴不过：“人在哪里？”
[EN] With his promise given, Shen Jianchen was overjoyed. 'Where is she?'
[ZH] 完颜池骗道：“她在王府，见到娘亲我自然会放她；不过，你还需答应本王一个条件。”
[EN] Wanyan Chi lied. 'She's at the mansion. Once I've seen my mother, of course I'll release her. But first you must grant me one condition.'
[ZH] 申简辰谨慎地问：“什么条件？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen asked carefully, 'What condition?'
[ZH] “本王会履行承诺将她送回临安，而你需在燕京留一年，把拳法尽数教给本王和师傅。还有，她离开时，你不许见她。”
[EN] 'I will keep my word and send her back to Lin'an. But you must stay in Yanjing for a year and teach the fist art to me and my master, every move of it. And when she leaves, you are not to see her off.'
[ZH] 申简辰担心完颜池使诈：“你竟然敢跟我提条件？就不怕我原路返回？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen suspected a trick. 'You dare make demands of me? Have you no fear I'll turn back the way I came?'
[ZH] “睁大眼睛瞧瞧，周围全是本王的人，你根本逃不掉。”
[EN] 'Open your eyes. My men surround you. You cannot escape.'
[ZH] 申简辰早已看到，本以为带邵月婷前来必然顺利换回千里寻，然后他与她一起回临安，因而并未将周围的金兵当回事。
[EN] Shen Jianchen had seen them already. He had assumed bringing Shao Yueting would secure Qianli Xun's release without trouble, and that he and she would go home to Lin'an together. So he had not taken the soldiers seriously.
[ZH] 现在看来完颜池诡计多端，想趁机留下他学习拳法。若真打起来，他一个人难以取胜，说不定会将事情闹得更僵，不仅救不出千里寻，还把自己搭进去。留一年便留一年，也正好趁此机会搞清楚申拳的来历。
[EN] Now it was clear: Wanyan Chi was plotting to keep him here and learn the fist art. If it came to a fight, one man could not win, and might make things worse—he would fail to free Qianli Xun and lose himself as well. A year, then. A year would also give him a chance to learn where Shen-style boxing came from.
[ZH] “你必须答应我老老实实放她回临安，不许诓我。”
[EN] 'You must promise to let her go back to Lin'an in good faith. No tricks.'
[ZH] “本王已得到最珍贵的，其他不敢奢求，她既不喜欢本王，本王放她自由，让她去找她爱的人。”
[EN] 'I have gained the one thing most precious to me. I dare ask for nothing more. She does not love me, so I will set her free, to go to the man she loves.'
[ZH] 马车里再次传出邵月婷的声音：“你若欺骗仪王，娘绝不下车见你。”
[EN] Shao Yueting's voice came from the carriage again. 'If you deceive Prince Yi, I will never step down to see you.'
[ZH] “娘，我说的是真的，您跟儿子回王府，只要让儿子看娘一眼，我一定放人。”
[EN] 'Mother, I mean it. Come home to the mansion with me. Let me set eyes on you, and I will free her at once.'
[ZH] “好，我随你回王府。”
[EN] 'Good. I will go with you to the mansion.'

#CH 349
[ZH] 王府内，完颜池终于见到娘亲。她一身女尼装扮，端坐在椅子上，手持佛珠，双目湿润。
[EN] Inside the prince's mansion, Wanyan Chi finally saw his mother. She sat upright in a chair, dressed as a nun, prayer beads in hand, her eyes wet.
[ZH] 完颜池再次跪在娘亲面前：“娘，难怪我找不到您，原来您出家为尼，现在找到儿子，不如还俗？跟儿子住在燕京，或者回会宁？”
[EN] Wanyan Chi knelt before her again. "Mother, no wonder I could not find you. You had become a nun. Now that you have found your son, why not return to secular life? Come live with me in Yanjing, or go back to Huining?"
[ZH] 曾几何时邵月婷想象过儿子若还在世，会长什么样。每次在尼姑庵的香客中看到年纪相仿的公子，她都会畅享一番。今日终于看到亲生儿子的真实面目，比她想象中更加高大魁梧，五官分明，鼻梁高挺，只是皮肤黑了些。
[EN] Shao Yueting had often wondered what her son would look like, had he lived. Each time she saw a young gentleman among the pilgrims at the convent, she let her imagination run. Now, at last, she saw the real face of her own flesh and blood—taller and broader than she had pictured, with sharp features and a high bridge of his nose, only his skin a little dark.
[ZH] “你上前些，让娘仔细看看。”
[EN] "Come closer. Let me look at you properly."
[ZH] 完颜池朝前跪了些，抬头仔细观察娘亲的容貌和表情，仿佛要将娘的样子刻在心里一般。
[EN] Wanyan Chi shuffled forward on his knees and lifted his head, studying his mother's face and every expression on it, as if to carve her image into his heart.
[ZH] 申简辰站在一旁，被眼前的情景感动，他再讨厌完颜池，不得不承认他长这么大头一次见娘亲，着实可怜。
[EN] Shen Jianch stood to one side, moved by the sight. Much as he disliked Wanyan Chi, he had to admit the man was pitiful: the first time in his life he had ever seen his mother.
[ZH] 邵月婷用手摸着儿子的脸：“原来我的孩子长大后是这样……”
[EN] Shao Yueting touched her son's cheek. "So this is what my child grew up to be..."
[ZH] 完颜池继续追问：“娘可否愿意留下？”
[EN] Wanyan Chi pressed on. "Will Mother stay?"
[ZH] “娘是出家人，见过你之后，还需回到尼姑庵。”
[EN] "I am a woman of the cloth. Having seen you, I must return to the convent."
[ZH] “娘是不是不想见我才出家？”
[EN] "Did you become a nun because you did not want to see me?"
[ZH] “哪有娘不愿见儿子的？我以为你不在人世，还有你爹也已死，才看破红尘出家为尼。”
[EN] "What mother would refuse to see her son? I thought you were dead, and your father too. That is why I saw through the vanity of the world and took the veil."
[ZH] “肯定是邵正邺欺骗了娘。”
[EN] "It must be Shao Zhengye who deceived you."
[ZH] 因心疼儿子，邵月婷说：“你起来，坐下与娘说话。”
[EN] Her heart going out to him, Shao Yueting said, "Get up. Sit and talk with me."
[ZH] 完颜池站起来，吩咐丫鬟端把椅子，他要坐在娘亲身边说话。丫鬟拿来椅子后，他坐下后一直抓着娘的手。
[EN] Wanyan Chi rose and told a maid to bring a chair—he wanted to sit beside his mother as they spoke. When the chair came, he sat down and kept hold of her hand.
[ZH] “怨不得哥哥，要怨只怨娘年轻不懂事，喜欢上不该喜欢的人……”
[EN] "Do not blame your brother. Blame me. I was young and foolish, and I loved someone I should not have loved..."
[ZH] “娘后悔遇见父皇？”
[EN] "Do you regret meeting His Majesty?"
[ZH] “曾经后悔过，现在已云淡风轻。”
[EN] "I did, once. Now it is all water under the bridge."
[ZH] “若父皇见到您，肯定开心。”
[EN] "If His Majesty saw you, he would be overjoyed."
[ZH] “答应娘，不要告诉他你见过我，娘想安静度过此生。”
[EN] "Promise me you will not tell him you found me. I want to spend the rest of my days in peace."
[ZH] 完颜池点头答应：“好，但娘必须与儿子多待些时日。”
[EN] Wanyan Chi nodded. "All right. But you must stay with your son a while longer."
[ZH] “好，我答应与你待上半个月再回临安。”
[EN] "Good. I promise to stay half a month, then return to Lin'an."
[ZH] 完颜池心情大好，向一旁站着的仪王说：“桌上有纸笔，给她写封信，劝她今日离燕京，不要再回来生事。”
[EN] Wanyan Chi was in high spirits. He turned to the Prince of Yi, standing nearby. "There is paper and ink on the table. Write her a letter. Urge her to leave Yanjing today and never come back to stir up trouble."
[ZH] 完颜池的意思显然是要放人，申简辰二话不说坐到桌前，提笔写书信。他无心再理会母子二人互诉衷肠，思忖着完颜池刚才说的话。
[EN] Wanyan Chi clearly meant to let her go. Shen Jianch sat down at once, brush in hand. He had no stomach for the mother and son pouring out their hearts to each other; his mind was on what Wanyan Chi had just said.
[ZH] 千里寻若听说完颜池将他留下，恐怕为救自己不愿离开，甚至会与完颜池大打出手。别说完颜池不愿看到，他也不愿看到。好不容易换回千里寻，不希望她再有事，一定要通过书信劝她乖乖回临安等他。
[EN] If Qianli Xun heard that Wanyan Chi had kept him here, she might refuse to leave for fear of endangering him—might even come to blows with the prince. Wanyan Chi did not want that, and neither did he. He had got Qianli Xun back at last; he could not bear for harm to come to her again. He would write and coax her into going quietly back to Lin'an to wait for him.
[ZH] 想好后，申简辰提笔书写书信。他没有写称谓，只因大家知道的称谓并非他心中之人，而他心中的称谓是他与她之间的秘密。
[EN] Once he had thought it through, Shen Jianch set brush to paper. He wrote no form of address, for the name everyone knew was not the person in his heart, and the name he called her by was a secret between the two of them.
[ZH] 写完时，只在落款处写下一个“申”字，也只有她能明白其中的意思。
[EN] At the end, he wrote only the character Shen. Only she would know what it meant.
[ZH] 丫鬟见信写完，主动上前接过。
[EN] Seeing the letter finished, the maid stepped forward to take it.
[ZH] 完颜池将丫鬟招呼到身边，对她耳语几句，丫鬟只顾点头。
[EN] Wanyan Chi beckoned the maid close and whispered a few words in her ear. She only nodded and nodded.
[ZH] 申简辰警惕地说：“完颜池，不许耍花招。”
[EN] Shen Jianch said warily, "Wanyan Chi, no tricks."
[ZH] 完颜池笑道：“娘在此，我怎敢耍花招？放心，我现在只想成全你们。”
[EN] Wanyan Chi laughed. "With my mother here, how could I play tricks? Rest easy. All I want now is to see you two together."
[ZH] 他的表情中透出诚意，申简辰心安许多。
[EN] There was sincerity in his face, and Shen Jianch felt easier in his mind.
[ZH] ……
[EN] ...
[ZH] 千里寻买完笔墨刚回到住处，王府的丫鬟前来，将信给了陆远峰。
[EN] Qianli Xun had just bought ink and brushes and returned to her lodgings when a maid from the prince's mansion came and handed the letter to Lu Yuanfeng.
[ZH] 那丫鬟千里寻见过，质问道：“陆远峰，你是不是一直在骗我？”
[EN] Qianli Xun knew the maid by sight. "Lu Yuanfeng," she demanded, "have you been lying to me all this time?"
[ZH] 陆远峰老实交代：“的确是小王爷派我帮师傅，住的地方也是小王爷吩咐我租的，包括师傅赚钱的活儿都是小王爷帮忙找的，我只是个跑腿的。”
[EN] Lu Yuanfeng confessed plainly. "It is true. His Lordship sent me to help you, master. He told me to rent this place, and he found the work that earned you money too. I am only the errand boy."
[ZH] 千里寻伸出拳头向陆远峰打去：“让你骗我！”
[EN] Qianli Xun threw out a fist at him. "Take that for lying to me!"
[ZH] 陆远峰边躲避边说：“师傅别生气，丫鬟带来仪王的信，您看后准高兴。”
[EN] Lu Yuanfeng dodged. "Do not be angry, master. The maid brought a letter from the Prince of Yi. Read it and you will be glad."
[ZH] 千里寻打不下去，不由自主收起拳头：“什么？仪王的信？他不是以为我死了吗？为何还会给我写信？”
[EN] Her fist would not land. She lowered it against her will. "What? A letter from the Prince of Yi? Does he not think I am dead? Why would he write to me?"
[ZH] “具体什么原因，我不清楚，师傅看完信后自然明白。”陆远峰将信递到师傅面前。
[EN] "I could not say. Read it, master, and you will understand." Lu Yuanfeng held the letter out to her.
[ZH] 千里寻接过，打开认真浏览……
[EN] She took it, opened it, and read with care...
[ZH] 完颜池与蔡靖骗我你已不在人世，我匆忙回临安来到神柳下，希望它能帮忙让你回到我身边。后得法师开示，我在临安苟延残喘些时日，老天护佑，既听说千里寻才子写了新话本，还有幽栖居士新写的诗词，才知你没死。于是我请来完颜池的娘亲邵月婷，带她来到燕京，希望把你换回。……
[EN] Wanyan Chi and Cai Jing told me you were gone. I hurried back to Lin'an and went to the Sacred Willow, hoping it would bring you back to me. Then a monk showed me a way. I dragged out a few more days in Lin'an, and heaven took pity: I heard that the scholar Qianli Xun had written a new story, and that Youqi Jushi had new poems besides. Only then did I know you were alive. So I fetched Wanyan Chi's mother, Shao Yueting, and brought her to Yanjing, hoping to trade her for you...
[ZH] 千里寻自语道：“你怎么这么傻？我已不在王府，竟然还这么努力地救我。”
[EN] Qianli Xun murmured to herself, "How could you be so foolish? I am no longer in the prince's mansion, and still you work this hard to save me."
[ZH] 完颜池见到他亲娘后，答应放你回临安，我很开心。完颜池的拳法与申拳有渊源，我需在燕京待上一年，与他的师傅互相切磋。原谅我在你离开燕京时不能见你一面。……
[EN] Now that he has seen his own mother, Wanyan Chi has promised to let you go back to Lin'an, and I am glad. His boxing has roots in the Shen style, and I must stay a year in Yanjing to trade pointers with his master. Forgive me that I could not see you off when you left Yanjing...
[ZH] 千里寻感动之余气愤道：“肯定是完颜池的阴谋，我现在就去找他算账！”
[EN] Tears welled in Qianli Xun's eyes, and anger with them. "It is a plot. I am going to find him and settle this now!"
[ZH] 陆远峰提醒道：“师傅先别生气，看完信再决定要不要找小王爷算账。”
[EN] Lu Yuanfeng warned her, "Do not fly off the handle, master. Finish the letter before you decide whether to settle with His Lordship."
[ZH] 千里寻稳住情绪继续向下看：
[EN] Qianli Xun steadied herself and read on:
[ZH] 出了王府别再回头，不要找完颜池闹事，我好不容易把你救出来，不想你再有任何闪失。你若心里有我，乖乖回临安，在我们的竹屋里等我回家，我向你保证，一年后会完好无损回到你身边。
[EN] Once you leave the mansion, do not look back, and do not pick a fight with Wanyan Chi. I went to a great deal of trouble to get you out; I will not have you come to harm. If you have any feeling for me, go quietly back to Lin'an and wait for me in our bamboo hut. I promise I will return to you in one piece, a year from now.
[ZH] 我把梦容和喜兰葬在燕京城外，出了城一直向东边走，便可看到她和喜兰的坟头，墓碑上刻着她们的名字，很容易找到。你帮我把她们的尸骨迁回临安，安葬的地方早已预备好，白风都知道……
[EN] I buried Mengrong and Xilan outside Yanjing. Go out of the city and keep walking east, and you will see their graves. Their names are carved on the stones; you cannot miss them. Move their bones back to Lin'an for me. The burial place is already prepared—Bai Feng knows all about it...
[ZH] 千里寻已泪眼婆娑，本是为梦容医病来到燕京，燕京却成为她的葬身之地。
[EN] Qianli Xun's eyes brimmed with tears. She had come to Yanjing to cure Mengrong's illness, and Yanjing had become the place where she was laid to rest.
[ZH] 她瞪着陆远峰：“是不是金人杀了梦容和喜兰？”
[EN] She glared at Lu Yuanfeng. "Did the Jin kill Mengrong and Xilan?"
[ZH] 陆远峰根本不知此事：“她们是谁我都不认识，怎么知道她们是不是被金人所杀？”
[EN] Lu Yuanfeng knew nothing of it. "I did not even know them. How would I know if the Jin killed them?"
[ZH] “要是承认你是大宋子民，要是还认我这个师傅，给我把杀害梦容和喜兰的人找出来，我要替她们报仇。”
[EN] "If you admit you are a subject of the Song, and if you still call me your master, then find me the ones who killed Mengrong and Xilan. I will avenge them."
[ZH] “找个把人倒不难，需告诉我她们什么时候死的？在哪里？我好查寻。”
[EN] "That is not hard. Tell me when they died and where, so I can look into it."
[ZH] 千里寻仔细推断，梦容应该是在金兵进入燕京那日：“应该是去年我刚遇见你时，她们从一个巷子逃走，估计是在巷子里被害，你只要打听那日是哪群人进入巷子肯定能找到人。”
[EN] Qianli Xun thought it through. It must have been the day the Jin troops entered Yanjing. "It should be last year, just after I met you. They fled from an alley. They were likely killed in that alley. Ask who went into the alley that day, and you will find them."
[ZH] 陆远峰心想进入巷子的那队金人正好是小王爷的手下的人马，找起来应该容易，不过此事还需征求小王爷同意。
[EN] Lu Yuanfeng thought of the Jin squad that had gone into the alley—His Lordship's own men. They would be easy enough to find, but he would need the prince's leave first.
[ZH] “刚才来的丫鬟说，小王爷让师傅尽快出城，若天黑前您还没出去，他便不会放您离开。师傅把握好时间，我这就去替师傅找人，找到后带到城外任由师傅处置。”
[EN] "The maid who came just now said His Lordship wants you out of the city as soon as possible. If you are still inside by nightfall, he will not let you go. Watch the time, master. I will find the men now and bring them outside the city for you to deal with as you see fit."
[ZH] 陆远峰离开后，千里寻继续阅览剩下的内容：
[EN] When Lu Yuanfeng had gone, Qianli Xun read the rest:
[ZH] 以后不必再纠结与陈浩宇的婚姻，在汴梁时官家下旨将你许给我。陈浩宇虽不情愿，可圣意难违，为了家人他只能取消婚姻。我心里高兴，却不想逼迫你成为我的人，向陈浩宇承诺绝不对你说出实情，等你出狱后，尊重你的选择。……
[EN] You need not agonize over the marriage to Chen Haoyu any longer. Back in Bianliang, the emperor decreed that you were to be given to me. Chen Haoyu was unwilling, but the imperial will could not be opposed; for his family's sake he had to call it off. I was glad, yet I would not force you to be mine. I promised Chen Haoyu I would never tell you the truth, and that after you came out of prison I would leave the choice to you...
[ZH] 千里寻终于明白为何他们二人都向她问过同样的问题。
[EN] At last Qianli Xun understood why both of them had asked her the same question.
[ZH] 告诉你一个好消息，陈家为想绒姑娘赎身，把她放在陈浩宇身边，我来燕京前听说陈浩宇快要当爹。他已有着落，你不必再挂心。我的努力终于感动上天，你我可以毫无阻碍地走到一起。记着，什么都别做，回临安等我。
[EN] Good news, too. The Chen family has bought Xiangrong's freedom and installed her at Chen Haoyu's side. Before I came to Yanjing, I heard he was to be a father soon. He is settled; you need not worry about him. My efforts have moved heaven at last, and nothing now stands between us. Remember: do nothing. Go back to Lin'an and wait for me.
[ZH] 千里寻盯着落款处“申”字：“好，我听你的，回临安在竹屋等你。”
[EN] Qianli Xun stared at the character Shen at the foot of the letter. "All right. I will do as you say. I will wait for you in the bamboo hut in Lin'an."
[ZH] 千里寻将信装好，开始收拾东西，不过是些衣物和她写的手稿，再就是所剩无几的银子。
[EN] She folded the letter away and began to pack—a few clothes, the manuscripts she had written, and the little silver she had left.
[ZH] 她拉开抽屉准备将不多的银子带走，发现交房租的一袋银子乖乖躺在里面，定是陆远峰所为。
[EN] She opened the drawer to take her money and found the bag of rent silver sitting meekly inside. Lu Yuanfeng must have put it back.
[ZH] “不要也好，完颜池欠我太多，要了他的命都偿还不清，区区一袋银子算不了什么。”千里寻拿起银子，再次想到她失身完颜池之事，“我努力守住的贞洁被狗吃了，你会不会嫌弃我？……，你我好不容易能走到一起，我想这么多做什么？还是尽快出城。”
[EN] "Let him have his way. Wanyan Chi owes me more than his life could pay, so one bag of silver is nothing." She picked up the silver, and her thoughts turned again to the night she had been forced by Wanyan Chi. "The virtue I fought to keep went to the dogs. Will you still want me? ... We have finally found our way to each other. Why should I dwell on it? Better to leave the city before dark."
[ZH] 千里寻来到城外，找了好久才找到埋葬梦容的地方。
[EN] Qianli Xun reached the outskirts and searched a long while before she found where Mengrong was buried.
[ZH] 她眼中噙着泪水：“梦容，都怪我，若不是我极力要求带你来燕京找神医诊治，你也不会这么早离开，呜呜……”
[EN] Tears in her eyes, she said, "Mengrong, it is all my fault. If I had not insisted on bringing you to Yanjing to see the physician, you would not have left so soon. Oh, oh..."
[ZH] 千里寻哭了一会儿继续说：“今日我会带你一起回临安……”
[EN] She wept a while, then went on. "Today I am taking you back to Lin'an with me..."
[ZH] “什么准备都没有，怎么挖出尸骨回临安？岂不是空口白牙说瞎话？”
[EN] "You have made no preparations. How will you dig up the bones and carry them to Lin'an? Empty words, all of them."
[ZH] 身后传来完颜池的声音，千里寻猛然转过头，发现完颜池押着一金人，身后还跟着些拿工具的人。
[EN] Wanyan Chi's voice came from behind her. She spun around and saw him with a Jin man in his grip, and several men with tools following.
[ZH] “你来做什么？”
[EN] "What are you doing here?"
[ZH] “来帮你。”完颜池将押着的金人推到坟头，“是他逼死了你的朋友，本王把人带来，任由处置。”
[EN] "Helping you." Wanyan Chi shoved the prisoner toward the grave. "He is the one who hounded your friend to death. His Lordship has brought him for you to deal with as you please."
[ZH] 千里寻站起来，露出难以置信的表情：“你会这么好心？”
[EN] Qianli Xun rose, unable to believe her ears. "You would be so kind?"
[ZH] “本王当然没这么好心，若是本王信任之人，绝不会随便带到你面前，可惜此人杀了本王手下一名将领，他必须死，顺便卖你个人情，只可惜找不到杀死丫鬟的人。”
[EN] "Of course I am not kind. I would never bring a man I trusted before you so lightly. But this one killed one of my officers, so he must die. I might as well do you a favor. Pity I could not find the one who killed your maid."
[ZH] “哼！就知道你没这么好心。”
[EN] "Hah! I knew you were not kind."
[ZH] “少啰嗦，快动手吧。”
[EN] "Less talk. Get on with it."
[ZH] 千里寻攥紧拳头：“梦容，今日我要为你报仇。”她用拳向金人打去，却下不去手，拳头停在半空。她从没杀过没有反抗之力的人，还真有些不习惯。
[EN] Qianli Xun clenched her fists. "Mengrong, today I avenge you." She struck at the Jin man, but the blow would not come; her fist hung in the air. She had never killed a man who could not fight back, and the strangeness of it stayed her hand.
[ZH] 被绑的金人不断求饶：“姑娘饶命！小王爷饶命！”
[EN] The bound Jin man begged for mercy. "Spare me, miss! Spare me, Your Lordship!"
[ZH] 完颜池一拳过去，砸在那人的太阳穴，被绑的金人立刻毙命。完颜池收起拳头：“本王帮你，是不是该说些感谢的话？”
[EN] Wanyan Chi drove a fist into the man's temple, and the bound Jin man died on the spot. He drew back his hand. "I helped you. Should you not thank me?"
[ZH] 千里寻冷笑一声，并未理会。
[EN] Qianli Xun gave a cold laugh and said nothing.
[ZH] 完颜池招呼拿工具的人：“可以忙活了！”
[EN] Wanyan Chi called to the men with tools. "Get to work!"
[ZH] 拿工具的人开始刨坟挖尸骨，千里寻则走到一旁耐心等待。
[EN] They began to break the grave and dig out the bones. Qianli Xun walked aside and waited patiently.
[ZH] 完颜池有意靠近美人，美人转过头懒得理他，他并不在意：“你记着，放你走是因为本王爱你，希望你好。”
[EN] Wanyan Chi edged closer to the beauty. She turned her head and would not look at him, but he did not mind. "Remember this. I let you go because I love you and want the best for you."
[ZH] 千里寻冷笑道：“少来迷惑老娘，老娘不吃这一套。警告你，一年后把仪王乖乖送回临安，否则老娘这辈子跟你没完！”
[EN] Qianli Xun sneered. "Save your sweet talk for someone else. I do not buy it. And hear this: send the Prince of Yi quietly back to Lin'an in a year, or I will never leave you alone in this life."
[ZH] “跟本王没完才好，这样你与本王时时有牵绊。”
[EN] "If you never leave me alone, so much the better. Then you and I will always be tied together."
[ZH] “你！……”
[EN] "You! ..."
[ZH] “行了，别生气，本王答应你一年后乖乖把仪王还给你。”
[EN] "Enough. Do not be angry. I promise to return the Prince of Yi to you in a year, quietly."
[ZH] “你就是个卑鄙小人，我明明已不在王府，还设计骗他？”
[EN] "You are nothing but a low, contemptible man. I am no longer in your mansion, and still you trick him?"
[ZH] “仪王看上去也是聪明人，可惜遇到你的事他会变笨，笨到他自己都意识不到，本王略施小计，他便信了。不过你放心，本王只想与他切磋拳法，没别的意思。”
[EN] "The Prince of Yi looks clever enough, but where you are concerned he turns stupid—too stupid to know it. I played a small trick and he believed me. Rest easy, though. All I want is to trade boxing moves with him. Nothing more."
[ZH] “量你也不敢。”
[EN] "I doubt you would dare do more."
[ZH] 完颜池收起戏谑的表情，一脸认真：“你这一走，也不知往后你我还能否相见？”
[EN] Wanyan Chi dropped his teasing and turned serious. "You are going away. Who knows whether we shall meet again?"
[ZH] “最好永远别见！”
[EN] "Best never to see you again!"
[ZH] “本王的娘出家为尼，不愿留在本王身边，以后本王会抽时间去临安看望她，如果到临安，你会不会与本王像朋友一样一起喝茶？”
[EN] "My mother has taken the veil and will not stay by my side. From time to time I will go to Lin'an to see her. If I come to Lin'an, will you drink tea with me as a friend?"
[ZH] 千里寻沉默片刻，用平缓的语气说：“我不想回忆不愉快的往事。”
[EN] Qianli Xun was silent a moment, then said evenly, "I do not want to remember unhappy things."
[ZH] 完颜池明白美人的意思，失落地说：“好吧，以后本王不会再打扰你。”
[EN] Wanyan Chi read her meaning, dashed. "All right. I will not trouble you again."
[ZH] 他很想上前最后拥抱一下美人，看到美人警惕的眼神，只好作罢：“马车已给你预备好，在前面路口等着。”
[EN] He longed to take her in his arms one last time, but the wariness in her eyes stopped him. "The carriage is ready for you. It waits at the crossroads ahead."
[ZH] 完颜池既失落又深情地看了美人最后一眼，转身离开。
[EN] Wanyan Chi gave the beauty one last look, heavy with loss and longing, then turned and walked away.
[ZH] 千里寻对完颜池所做的一切一点儿也感激不起来，只盼望此人永远别出现在她面前。
[EN] Qianli Xun felt not the least gratitude for all Wanyan Chi had done. She only wished the man would never cross her path again.
[ZH] 完颜池回到王府来到一处屋子，外面安排有重兵把守。
[EN] Wanyan Chi returned to the mansion and went to a house guarded by armed men.
[ZH] 守卫门口的人见小王爷前来，主动打开门上的大锁。
[EN] The guards at the door saw the young prince coming and unlocked the heavy lock themselves.
[ZH] 完颜池推门而入，仪王正悠然自得坐在案前饮酒，他走上前在仪王对面坐好。
[EN] Wanyan Chi pushed the door open. The Prince of Yi was sitting at a table, drinking at his ease. He stepped forward and sat down opposite him.
[ZH] “本王已按照你的要求给她安排好一切，你可以安心留下。”
[EN] "I have arranged everything for you as you asked. You may stay in peace."
[ZH] “外面的人就是你的待客之道？”
[EN] "Armed men outside. Is that your idea of hospitality?"
[ZH] “你的拳法在本王和师傅之上，怕你逃走。”
[EN] "Your boxing is better than mine and my master's. I am afraid you will run away."
[ZH] “你我拳法甚有渊源，我是心甘情愿留下。”
[EN] "Our styles share the same roots. I am here of my own accord."
[ZH] “人心难测，还是小心为妙。”
[EN] "People's hearts are hard to read. Better to be careful."
[ZH] “什么时候开始切磋？”
[EN] "When do we start trading moves?"
[ZH] “师傅说明日开始。”
[EN] "My master says tomorrow."
[ZH] “好，那就明日。”
[EN] "Good. Tomorrow, then."
[ZH] 仪王悠然自得应对，还夺走美人的心，完颜多少不舒服，不如刺激刺激他：“有件事你可能还不知。”
[EN] The Prince of Yi answered with perfect composure—and had stolen the beauty's heart besides. Wanyan Chi could not help but feel a sting. He would needle him a little. "There is something you may not know."
[ZH] 申简辰并未当回事，继续饮酒：“什么事？”
[EN] Shen Jianch did not take the bait and went on drinking. "What?"
[ZH] 完颜池大言不惭说出十个字：“本王是她的第一个男人。”
[EN] Wanyan Chi said it shamelessly, ten words: "I was the first man she ever had."
[ZH] 申简辰将杯中未喝完的酒泼在完颜池的脸上。
[EN] Shen Jianch flung the rest of his wine into Wanyan Chi's face.
[ZH] 完颜池笑道：“呵呵……！怎么？是不是知道没办法成为她的第一个男人，后悔自己付出这么多？”
[EN] Wanyan Chi laughed. "Ha! What is it? Sorry you cannot be her first, after all you have given?"
[ZH] 申简辰没有回答，一拳向完颜池打去。完颜池躲避过后，迎拳而上。两人打了几个回合，完颜池逐渐败下阵来。
[EN] Shen Jianch did not answer. He threw a punch. Wanyan Chi dodged, then countered. They traded several rounds, and Wanyan Chi was slowly driven back.
[ZH] 打不过对方只好用言语攻击：“看来你真的后悔，往后少在本王面前表现出你有多爱她。”
[EN] Losing on his feet, he went for words. "You really do regret it. Stop showing me how much you love her."
[ZH] 申简辰一拳将完颜池打倒：“不是后悔，是心痛，心痛被你和蔡靖所骗没有及时救她，让她被你糟蹋，她当时肯定很难过，你就是个畜生！根本不配喜欢她！”
[EN] Shen Jianch knocked him down with one punch. "Not regret. Pain. Pain that you and Cai Jing fooled me, that I did not save her in time, that you defiled her. She must have been miserable. You are a beast. You do not deserve to love her at all!"
[ZH] 玩延迟的嘴角被打裂，鲜血流出，他用手抹去，强调道：“本王因为爱她才会动她，别的女人想让本王动，本王都不稀罕。”
[EN] Wanyan Chi's split lip bled. He wiped it away and insisted, "I touched her because I love her. Other women want me to touch them, and I could not care less."
[ZH] 申简辰又一拳打在完颜池的脸上：“她是我的人，你再敢动他我就杀了你！”
[EN] Shen Jianch hit him in the face again. "She is mine. Touch her again and I will kill you!"
[ZH] 玩延迟向后趔趄，忍着痛笑道：“本王不会再动她，能成为她的第一个男人本王很满足，可惜，你永远不可能，呵呵……！”
[EN] Wanyan Chi staggered back and laughed through the pain. "I will not touch her again. I am content to have been her first. Pity you never can be. Ha!"
[ZH] 申简辰一脚将完颜池踢倒在地，上前抓住他的衣领：“告诉你，我不在乎，以后少用此事激我，再提我就送你归西！”
[EN] Shen Jianch kicked him to the floor, grabbed his collar and hauled him close. "Listen. I do not care. Stop using this to provoke me. One more word and I send you to your grave."
[ZH] 完颜池终于说出心中所想：“本王想听的就是你这句话，本王把她还给你，你答应本王要好好待她。”
[EN] At last Wanyan Chi said what was on his mind. "That is what I wanted to hear. I am giving her back to you. Promise me you will treat her well."
[ZH] 申简辰一把将完颜池推开，站起来：“不必你来叮嘱。”
[EN] Shen Jianch shoved him aside and stood up. "I do not need you to tell me that."
[ZH] 完颜池躺在地上笑起来：“呵呵呵……！”
[EN] Wanyan Chi lay on the floor and laughed. "Ha, ha, ha!"

#CH 350
[ZH] 装殓好梦容和喜兰的尸骨，千里寻连夜回临安。
[EN] Once Mengrong's and Xilan's bones were laid in their coffins, Qianli Xun rode for Lin'an that same night.
[ZH] 一路上倒也顺利，只是与两年多前比起来流浪的百姓变得更多。
[EN] The journey went smoothly enough. But set against two years earlier, there were far more refugees on the roads.
[ZH] 宋金一役，老百姓最苦，因战争失去家园，失去好不容易积攒的家业。
[EN] War between Song and Jin fell hardest on ordinary people. They lost their homes, and the property they had scraped together over years.
[ZH] 与流浪的百姓相比，千里寻认为她经历的一切根本不算什么，没必要纠结失去的，需向前看，真正属于她的未来正等着她。
[EN] Against those wandering families, her own losses seemed small. No use clinging to what was gone. She had to look forward. A future that was truly hers was waiting.
[ZH] 由北向南，一路行进，经过不少陌生的地方，以后恐怕很难有机会再欣赏他乡之景。她有意放慢脚步，每到一处，停下马车，将看到的说给后面马车里放着的红色木箱。
[EN] Traveling north to south, she passed many strange places. Likely she would never see them again. She slowed the pace on purpose. At each stop she halted the carriage and described the view aloud—to the red wooden chest in the carriage behind her.
[ZH] 红色的木箱里装着梦容的尸骨，灰色的则是喜兰。
[EN] The red chest held Mengrong's bones. The gray one held Xilan's.
[ZH] 梦容活着的时候，她的哥哥生病，她待在仪王府照顾，根本没有机会走出家门；后来她的哥哥活过来，却不是她真正的哥哥，心里装着别人，将她忽略一旁。
[EN] When Mengrong was alive, her brother fell ill and she stayed at Prince Yi's palace to nurse him. She never had a chance to leave the house. Later he recovered—but he was not her real brother. His heart belonged to someone else, and he left her at the edge of his life.
[ZH] 现在既然有机会，千里寻想借助自己的双眼告诉梦容大千世界是什么样。
[EN] Now she had the chance. Qianli Xun wanted to lend Mengrong her eyes and show her what the wide world looked like.
[ZH] 走走停停，一个月的路程足足走了一个半月才到达临安的地界。
[EN] She went in fits and starts. A journey of one month took her a full month and a half before she reached the border of Lin'an.
[ZH] 千里寻没有着急进临安城，她让车夫先去竹林。
[EN] She was in no hurry to enter the city. She told the driver to take the bamboo grove first.
[ZH] 来到竹林，马车无法进村，只能停在村外。
[EN] The carriage could not go into the village, so they stopped outside it.
[ZH] 她走下马车时，正好碰见要出村的苦蕨。
[EN] As she stepped down, Ku Jue came walking out of the village.
[ZH] 苦蕨一眼认出东家，有些不信，走上前瞧个仔细，激动地说：“真的是东家，您还活着？我姐姐要是知道还不得高兴坏了，我……我这就去城里告诉姐姐去！”
[EN] Ku Jue knew her mistress at a glance but could hardly believe it. She came closer to look, then cried out, 'It's you, mistress! You're alive? My sister will be beside herself. I—I'll run to the city and tell her this instant!'
[ZH] 千里寻一把抓住苦蕨：“先带我去仪王的竹屋。”
[EN] Qianli Xun caught her arm. 'Take me to Prince Yi's bamboo house first.'
[ZH] 苦蕨不好意思道：“光想着我姐，竟把东家给忘了，还是先安顿好东家再去城里。”
[EN] Ku Jue flushed. 'I was so taken with my sister I forgot my mistress. Let me settle you first, then go to the city.'
[ZH] 在苦蕨的指引下，来到申简辰建造的竹屋。
[EN] Ku Jue led her to the bamboo house Shen Jianchen had built.
[ZH] 竹屋位于村子最里面，占了一大块地方，算得上是村里最豪华的竹屋。主屋和侧屋将整个院子围起来，像座宅子似的。
[EN] It stood at the far end of the village and took up a wide plot—the finest bamboo house in the place. The main hall and side rooms enclosed the whole courtyard, more like a manor than a cottage.
[ZH] 千里寻心中自问：“你怎么知道我想建这样的竹屋？”
[EN] How did you know I wanted a house like this? she wondered.
[ZH] 进入院中，苦蕨兴奋地喊道：“姐夫！快看谁回来了！”
[EN] In the courtyard Ku Jue shouted, 'Brother-in-law! Look who's come back!'
[ZH] 白风从侧屋出来，见到幽栖居士活生生地站在面前，激动不已，上前行礼：“幽栖居士可回来，王爷总算放心。”
[EN] Bai Feng came out of a side room. Seeing Youqi Jushi standing there alive, he was overcome. He stepped forward and bowed. 'You've come back. At last the prince can rest easy.'
[ZH] 没有见到王爷的身影，白风问：“我家王爷怎么没跟您一起回来？”
[EN] No prince in sight. Bai Feng asked, 'Why didn't His Lordship come back with you?'
[ZH] “仪王……仪王他暂留燕京，一年后方可归来。”
[EN] 'Prince Yi... he's staying on in Yanjing for a time. He can return in a year.'
[ZH] 白风虽担心王爷，却也不好继续追问，不用猜便知王爷留下不过是为换回幽栖居士而已。
[EN] Bai Feng worried for his lord, but could not press further. He did not need to guess why the prince had stayed: it was the price of getting Youqi Jushi back.
[ZH] “竹屋是王爷为幽栖居士建的，往后这里就是您的家，旅途劳顿，快进屋休息。”
[EN] 'The prince built this house for you. From now on it's your home. You've come a long way—come in and rest.'
[ZH] “不急，梦容和喜兰的尸骨还在村口，先安葬好她们再休息不迟。”
[EN] 'There's time. Mengrong's and Xilan's bones are still at the village gate. Let's bury them before we rest.'
[ZH] 白风想起在燕京的经历，伤感道：“怪我没保护好她们，每每想起自责不已……”
[EN] Bai Feng thought of Yanjing and his face fell. 'I failed to protect them. Every time I think of it I blame myself...'
[ZH] “白侍卫不必难过，害死梦容的金人已死在她坟前，算是大仇得报，只是杀害喜兰的人没有找到。”
[EN] 'Don't grieve, Captain Bai. The Jin man who killed Mengrong is dead at her grave. That debt is paid. But we still haven't found Xilan's killer.'
[ZH] “让幽栖居士费心，王爷早已将坟墓箍好，我这就找人把尸骨抬过去安葬。”
[EN] 'You've done enough, Youqi Jushi. The prince had the tombs built already. I'll fetch men to carry the boxes over.'
[ZH] “我跟你一起去。”
[EN] 'I'll go with you.'
[ZH] 苦蕨也说：“我去搭把手。”
[EN] 'I'll give a hand,' Ku Jue said.
[ZH] 三人往外走，没走出几步，迎面遇见邹嬷嬷。
[EN] The three started outside. They had not gone far when Matron Zou met them head-on.
[ZH] 她一眼瞧见幽栖居士，鼻子一酸，眼眶跟着变红，上前抓住幽栖居士的手：“能看到你活着回来我真的很开心，王爷终于可以安心。”
[EN] She saw Youqi Jushi and her nose stung, her eyes reddening. She came up and seized her hand. 'I'm so glad to see you alive. Now the prince can rest.'
[ZH] “嬷嬷……我……我没照顾好梦容，您会不会怪我？”
[EN] 'Matron... I... I didn't look after Mengrong properly. Do you blame me?'
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷放开手，取出巾帕，擦掉眼角的泪水：“唉，各人有命，怨不着幽栖居士，你也是好心，要怪只怪这世道。你也受了不少苦，往后一切会好起来，我现在只盼你跟王爷平平安安地走下去。”
[EN] Matron Zou let go, took out a handkerchief and dabbed her eyes. 'Ah, each of us has her fate. No blame to you. Your heart was in the right place. Blame these times. You've suffered enough. Things will get better. All I ask now is that you and the prince live out your days in peace.'
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷向四下瞧：“王爷呢？他可在屋里？”
[EN] Matron Zou looked around. 'Where is the prince? Is he inside?'
[ZH] 千里寻解释道：“金国小王爷见仪王拳法精妙，把他留下切磋拳法，答应一年后放人归来。”
[EN] Qianli Xun said, 'The young Jin prince admired Prince Yi's boxing. He kept him to trade techniques and promised to let him go in a year.'
[ZH] “希望金国小王爷不要食言。”
[EN] 'Let's hope the young prince keeps his word.'
[ZH] “他若敢食言，我会杀了他。”
[EN] 'If he breaks it, I'll kill him.'
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷再次抓住幽栖居士的手：“千万别！你要好好的，再有个闪失王爷可怎么办？我相信王爷会回来，他不是一个随便被人威胁的人，你是他的软肋，你没事王爷才能放开手脚。我想是他自己愿意留在金国一段时日，即使金人不放他，凭王爷的本事也会想方设法归来。”
[EN] Matron Zou grabbed her hand again. 'Never say that! Stay safe. If anything happened to you, what would the prince do? He'll come back—he's not a man to be bullied. You're his one weak spot. As long as you're all right, he's free to act. I think he chose to stay in Jin for a while. And even if they won't let him go, he'll find a way home. He's that capable.'
[ZH] 千里寻从邹嬷嬷一番说辞中体会到，大家以为她死去的那段日子里申简辰不知有多难过，以至于邹嬷嬷为了王爷不愿她再有任何事。
[EN] Listening to her, Qianli Xun understood how much Shen Jianchen must have suffered in the days they thought her dead. That was why Matron Zou could not bear the thought of any harm coming to her—not for the prince's sake.
[ZH] “嬷嬷，随我一起去安葬梦容和喜兰的尸骨。”
[EN] 'Matron, come with us. We're burying Mengrong and Xilan.'
[ZH] 邹嬷嬷泪如雨下，松了手，再次擦着眼泪：“她们终于回来，仪王妃往后不用再待在异国他乡。”
[EN] Tears poured down Matron Zou's face. She let go and wiped them again. 'They're home at last. Prince Yi's consort won't have to stay in a strange land any longer.'
[ZH] 箍好的坟墓在竹林以外的山下，到达后，千里寻发现周围长满各色野花，将梦容葬在此处，与花为伴，再好不过。
[EN] The brick-lined graves lay below the grove, at the foot of the hill. When they arrived, Qianli Xun saw wildflowers of every color growing around them. No better place for Mengrong, with flowers for company.
[ZH] 在苦蕨的协助下，白风将两个木箱顺利搬进早已箍好的坟墓。
[EN] With Ku Jue helping, Bai Feng carried the two chests into the waiting graves.
[ZH] 千里寻拿出半路上买好的纸钱和贡品，在墓前摆好，跟邹嬷嬷一起蹲下把纸钱烧了，两人又在墓前陪梦容说了一会儿话，才回到竹屋。
[EN] Qianli Xun brought out the spirit money and offerings she had bought on the road and laid them before the grave. She and Matron Zou crouched down and burned the paper money, then stayed a while talking to Mengrong before going back to the bamboo house.
[ZH] 竹屋内的布置完全符合千里寻的喜好，有茶桌、书桌，还有一个用竹子打制的书架，也不知从哪里淘来那么书，摆满整整一个书架。里屋也是她喜欢的布置，清雅不俗，唯一缺的人是苦荞。
[EN] The house inside suited her down to the last detail. A tea table, a writing desk, and a bookshelf built of bamboo—where he had found so many books she could not guess, but they filled the whole shelf. The inner room was to her taste as well, quiet and elegant. Only one thing was missing: Kuqiao.
[ZH] 千里寻走到竹屋的外间，白风正好进来。
[EN] She went into the outer room just as Bai Feng came in.
[ZH] “我正要找你，你就来了。”
[EN] 'I was about to look for you.'
[ZH] “我有事求幽栖居士。”
[EN] 'I have a favor to ask, Youqi Jushi.'
[ZH] “先说你的事。”
[EN] 'Tell me first.'
[ZH] 白风大胆说：“我跟苦荞的婚事不能再等下去，王爷去燕京前我开始建竹屋，现在已建好，幽栖居士能不能答应让我尽快娶苦荞过门？”
[EN] Bai Feng spoke up. 'I can't wait any longer to marry Kuqiao. I started building this house before the prince left for Yanjing. It's finished now. Will you let me bring her home soon?'
[ZH] 千里寻笑道：“准了。”
[EN] Qianli Xun smiled. 'Granted.'
[ZH] 白风高兴地说：“我这就去把苦荞接过来。”
[EN] Bai Feng beamed. 'I'll fetch her at once.'
[ZH] 千里寻说：“等等，你跟我一起回朱府接她，顺便看望我爹娘。”
[EN] 'Wait,' Qianli Xun said. 'Come with me to the Zhu house. We'll pick her up and visit my parents while we're there.'
[ZH] 白风解释道：“苦荞还住在陈府。”
[EN] 'Kuqiao is still at the Chen house,' Bai Feng explained.
[ZH] “还住在陈府？”千里寻以为婚姻解除，陈浩宇已和想绒在一起，她身边的下人应该回朱府才对，竟还住在陈府，也不知是陈家的长辈强留，还是陈浩宇，“先回朱府见过我爹娘再去陈府……”
[EN] 'Still at the Chen house?' She had thought that with the marriage ended and Chen Haoyu together with Xiangrong, his servants would have gone back to the Zhu house. Why was Kuqiao still there? Had the Chen elders kept her, or Chen Haoyu himself? 'We'll go to the Zhu house first, see my parents, then on to the Chens...'
[ZH] 朱府门口的下人见到小姐活生生出现，又惊又喜，竟然忘记前去向老爷和夫人禀报。
[EN] The servants at the Zhu gate saw their young mistress standing there alive, and were so startled and glad they forgot to announce her to the master and mistress.
[ZH] 千里寻并不理会，径直朝父亲的院子走。
[EN] She paid them no mind and went straight to her father's courtyard.
[ZH] 白风没有跟进去，站在外面等候，他能想象到朱家人见女儿完好无损回来肯定高兴得说不出话来。
[EN] Bai Feng stayed outside. He could imagine the family's joy at seeing their daughter returned whole—too happy to speak.
[ZH] 正如白风所料，朱晞颜见到女儿时，手中刚端起的茶盏掉落在地，喜泪纵横，半晌才对下人说：“快去告诉夫人！”
[EN] Just as he guessed, Zhu Xiyan dropped the teacup he had just lifted. Tears of joy ran down his face. It was a while before he could say, 'Go tell her mistress—quick!'
[ZH] 千里寻跪在地上：“爹，女儿不孝，让您担心了。”
[EN] Qianli Xun knelt. 'Father, I've been a bad daughter. I worried you.'
[ZH] 朱晞颜上前扶起女儿：“活着就好，活着就好……”
[EN] He came forward and lifted her up. 'Alive is enough. Alive is enough...'
[ZH] 向夫人在陈文心的搀扶下与儿子朱景元匆忙赶来，走入屋内，见女儿活生生的样貌，哭道：“淑真！是你吗？”
[EN] Lady Xiang, helped by Chen Wenxin, hurried in with her son Zhu Jingyuan. She saw her daughter alive and cried, 'Shuzhen! Is it you?'
[ZH] 千里寻转过身上前扶住向夫人：“娘，是我，我没死，活得好好的。”
[EN] Qianli Xun turned and took her arm. 'Mother, it's me. I'm not dead. I'm fine.'
[ZH] 向夫人摸着女儿的脸：“瘦了，就知道我闺女不会死，总有一日会出现在我面前，唤我声娘，呜呜……”
[EN] Lady Xiang stroked her face. 'So thin. I knew my girl wasn't dead. One day she'd stand in front of me and call me Mother. Oh...'
[ZH] 陈文心也高兴落泪，安慰婆婆：“淑真回来，婆婆应该高兴，怎么哭起来？”
[EN] Chen Wenxin was crying too, and comforted her mother-in-law. 'Shuzhen is home. You should be glad. Why these tears?'
[ZH] 朱景元也说：“是啊，娘，妹妹安然无恙归来，我们都应该高兴，快和妹妹坐下好好聊。”
[EN] 'That's right, Mother,' Zhu Jingyuan said. 'Our sister is back safe and sound. We should all be happy. Come, sit with her and talk.'
[ZH] 朱晞颜发话道：“别哭了，坐下说话。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan said, 'No more tears. Sit down and talk.'
[ZH] 向夫人止住哭声，在女儿的搀扶下与家人落座。每个人再次表达对淑真的思念之情，问起她在金国的经历以及为何会有死讯传回？
[EN] Lady Xiang stopped crying. With her daughter's help she sat down with the rest. One after another they said how they had missed Shuzhen, and asked about her time in Jin and how the news of her death had reached them.
[ZH] 为避免家人再次伤心，千里寻挑些可说的说了。
[EN] To spare them more pain, Qianli Xun told only what she could.
[ZH] 家人听后只痛骂金人实在可恶，嘱咐女儿往后再别往北边去，就目前形势来看，还是南边安全。
[EN] They cursed the Jin for their cruelty and told her never to go north again. By the look of things, the south was safer.
[ZH] 又问起是如何安全回来时，千里寻并未交代邵月婷之事，毕竟是邵家的秘密，只说仪王再次赶到燕京换了她，一年后金人才愿意放他回临安。
[EN] When they asked how she had come back safely, she said nothing of Shao Yueting—that was the Shao family's secret. She only said Prince Yi had ridden to Yanjing again to exchange himself for her, and the Jin had agreed to release him for Lin'an a year later.
[ZH] 所有人沉默不语，朱晞颜等人想的是仪王对淑真情深义重，等他回来后，没有理由不让二人在一起。
[EN] The room went quiet. Zhu Xiyan and the others were thinking how deep the prince's feeling for Shuzhen ran. When he came home, there would be no reason to keep them apart.
[ZH] 陈文心则想到她的弟弟，因为忘不了淑真，一直留着墨香苑，若知道她活着，他又该如何？
[EN] Chen Wenxin thought of her brother. He had never forgotten Shuzhen and had kept Moxiang Yuan all this time. If he learned she was alive, what would he do?
[ZH] 白风在外面等得焦急，幽栖居士与家人也不知叙到何时？不如先到陈家告诉苦荞好消息后直接把她带来。
[EN] Outside, Bai Feng grew restless. No telling how long Youqi Jushi would talk with her family. Better to go to the Chen house, tell Kuqiao the good news, and bring her back with him.
[ZH] 正打算离开，朱家的下人前来挽留：“白侍卫，我家小姐要与家人吃团圆饭，让您也在朱府用饭。”
[EN] As he was about to leave, a servant stopped him. 'Captain Bai, our mistress is having a family dinner. The master says you must eat here too.'
[ZH] 白风推辞道：“不了不了，我还有事。”
[EN] 'No, really, I have business to attend to,' Bai Feng said.
[ZH] 下人上前拉住白风不放：“老爷吩咐，今日必须把您留下，小姐好不容易回来，您也沾沾家里的喜气。”
[EN] The servant took hold of him and would not let go. 'The master's orders: you stay today. Our mistress has come back after all this time. Share a little of the family's luck.'
[ZH] 白风不好再推辞，进入朱府，在下人专门为他安排的地方用饭。
[EN] Bai Feng could not refuse again. He went inside and ate at the place the servants had set for him.
[ZH] 朱家人吃过团圆饭后，又坐在一处说话，正说着，陈浩宇带着苦荞、韦嬷嬷还有想绒前来。
[EN] After the reunion dinner the family sat talking. In the middle of it, Chen Haoyu arrived with Kuqiao, Matron Wei, and Xiangrong.
[ZH] 走到门口时，陈浩宇听到屋里传出娘子的声音。
[EN] At the door he heard his wife's voice from inside.
[ZH] “我陪爹娘住段时日，往后会一直住在竹屋，等他回来。”
[EN] 'I'll stay with Father and Mother for a while. After that I'll live in the bamboo house. I'll wait there for him.'
[ZH] 陈浩宇闭上眼睛，几滴泪水经不住挤压，滚落下来。他踏进屋内，所有人停止说话，也停止笑声。
[EN] Chen Haoyu shut his eyes. A few tears slipped out. He stepped into the room, and every voice stopped. Even the laughter died.
[ZH] 朱晞颜向其他人使了使眼色，大家都起身跟着出去。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan signaled to the others. They all rose and went out.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷和苦荞先向老爷和夫人行礼。
[EN] Matron Wei and Kuqiao bowed to the master and mistress.
[ZH] 向夫人心情一好，精神也好许多，拉起韦嬷嬷：“先到我屋里坐会儿。”
[EN] Lady Xiang's spirits had lifted with her mood. She took Matron Wei's hand. 'Come sit in my room a while.'
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷明白向夫人的意思，搀扶她跟着朱晞颜离开。
[EN] Matron Wei understood and helped her out after Zhu Xiyan.
[ZH] 苦荞想进屋，被陈文心拦住：“你去干吗？”
[EN] Kuqiao started toward the inner room, but Chen Wenxin stopped her. 'What are you doing?'
[ZH] 苦荞解释道：“两年多没见小姐，想她。”
[EN] 'I haven't seen my mistress in over two years,' Kuqiao said. 'I miss her.'
[ZH] “不急这一会儿，白风在朱府，你先去找她聊。”陈文心唤来一个丫鬟，“带苦荞去找白风。”
[EN] 'Not now. Bai Feng is here at the Zhu house. Go talk to him first.' She called a maid over. 'Take Kuqiao to see Bai Feng.'
[ZH] 苦荞向屋内瞧了瞧，不舍地离开。
[EN] Kuqiao glanced at the inner room, unwilling to go, but left.
[ZH] 想绒一直盯着陈浩宇走进屋内的背影，心里很不是滋味。
[EN] Xiangrong watched Chen Haoyu's back as he disappeared into the room. Something sat bitter in her chest.
[ZH] 陈文心上前拉起想绒的手：“别介意，浩宇不得已结束婚姻，到汴梁接人又没接着，后又听说淑真没了，现在人好好回来，总得让他们把话说开。”
[EN] Chen Wenxin took her hand. 'Don't take it to heart. Haoyu had no choice but to end the marriage. He went to Bianliang to fetch her and missed her, then heard she was gone. Now she's back, safe. They have to have it out.'
[ZH] 想绒装作不在意的样子：“我懂，浩宇心里一直有淑真，憋在心里的话不说来恐怕会憋坏。”
[EN] Xiangrong made herself smile. 'I understand. Haoyu has always carried Shuzhen in his heart. If he kept those words locked up, they'd make him ill.'
[ZH] 陈文心瞧一眼想绒已凸起的腹部：“都四个月身孕，不能老站着，到我屋里坐坐，陪我说说话。”
[EN] Chen Wenxin glanced at the slight swell of her belly. 'Four months gone. You can't stand about. Come to my room and keep me company.'
[ZH] 想绒点点头，瞅一眼屋内，跟随陈文心离开。
[EN] Xiangrong nodded, stole one look at the room, and left with her.
[ZH] 朱景元也向屋内瞧一眼，叹口气：“唉，淑真的姻缘实在不顺，希望以后不要再出现任何意外。”
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan also glanced in and sighed. 'Ah, Shuzhen's luck in love has never been easy. Let nothing else go wrong for her.'
[ZH] 朱景元慨叹完，跟在陈文心和想绒身后离开。
[EN] With that, he followed Chen Wenxin and Xiangrong out.
[ZH] 众人走出屋后，千里寻站起来。陈浩宇的样子令她感动，感动到不知该如何与他搭话。
[EN] When they were gone, Qianli Xun stood up. The sight of Chen Haoyu moved her so much she did not know how to begin.
[ZH] 陈浩宇慢慢靠近，每一步既有埋怨又有意外之喜，直到足够近时，眼泪滚落下来：“为何回来不第一个找我？要不是苦蕨到陈府来找苦荞，我恐怕是最后一个知道。”
[EN] He came closer, one step at a time, each step carrying both reproach and a joy he had not expected. When he was near enough, the tears fell. 'Why wasn't I the first to know? If Ku Jue hadn't come to the Chen house for Kuqiao, I'd have been the last.'
[ZH] “我准备见过家人后去陈府，谁知你先来……”
[EN] 'I meant to come to the Chen house after seeing my family. I didn't expect you here first...'
[ZH] 陈浩宇再也忍不住，一把上前抱住她：“娘子，我很想你……”
[EN] He could hold back no longer. He stepped in and pulled her into his arms. 'I missed you so...'
[ZH] 千里寻并未去抱陈浩宇，含着泪提醒道：“我已不是……不是你的娘子。”
[EN] She did not return the embrace. With tears in her eyes she said, 'I'm no longer... no longer your wife.'
[ZH] 陈浩宇缓慢地放开手：“仪王骗我，他实在有心机，一步一步把你从我身边抢走……”
[EN] He let her go little by little. 'Prince Yi lied to me. He's a scheming man. Step by step he took you from me...'
[ZH] “不是这样，你误会他。”
[EN] 'That's not it. You've got him wrong.'
[ZH] “误会？你明明活着，他偏偏装作伤心的样子骗我以为你死了，他就是别有用心！”
[EN] 'Wrong? You were alive, and he acted the grieving man to make me think you were dead. He had an agenda!'
[ZH] “那时他真的以为我死了，他也是被人所骗，是我新写的诗词传到临安，他才知道我活着，才赶到燕京将我换回来。”
[EN] 'He truly believed I was dead then. Someone had fooled him too. A poem of mine reached Lin'an. That's how he learned I was alive, and he rode to Yanjing to exchange himself for me.'
[ZH] “换回来？难道他人在燕京？”
[EN] 'Exchange himself? He's in Yanjing?'
[ZH] “完颜池说一年后放他回来。”
[EN] 'Wanyan Chi says he'll be released in a year.'
[ZH] 陈浩宇的泪水中混着苦笑：“哼，他一直很无耻，无耻到抢走所有对你好的机会。在汴梁时长跪不起，害我丢掉婚姻；后来悄悄卖掉所有财产筹够一千万两；现在又一个人跑到燕京用他自己把你换回来。真够无耻！无耻到我想恨他也恨不起来，不愿失去你又不得不失去你，你说我该怎么办？”
[EN] Tears and a bitter laugh mixed on Chen Haoyu's face. 'Hmf. He's always been shameless. Shameless enough to take every chance I had to be good to you. In Bianliang he knelt and wouldn't rise, and I lost my marriage. Then he sold everything he owned without a word and raised ten million taels of silver. Now he's gone alone to Yanjing and traded himself for you. Shameless, I tell you! So shameless I can't even bring myself to hate him. I don't want to lose you, and I have to lose you. Tell me—what am I supposed to do?'
[ZH] “听说……想绒已有了你的孩子……”
[EN] 'I hear... Xiangrong is carrying your child...'
[ZH] “你听他说的？”陈浩宇继续含泪埋怨，“他的确处心积虑，早知如此，我该到燕京去找你，怪我一时相信邹嬷嬷的话，鬼迷心窍跑回临安，一步错步步错。”
[EN] 'He told you that?' Chen Haoyu went on, tears in his voice. 'He's been plotting all along. If I'd known, I would have gone to Yanjing to find you. I was a fool to believe Matron Zou and run back to Lin'an. One wrong step, and then every other.'
[ZH] “陈浩宇，事已至此，你我只能面对。”
[EN] 'Chen Haoyu. It's done. All we can do is face it.'
[ZH] 陈浩宇仍旧不愿放弃：“写圣旨的官家已不是官家，他的圣旨应该可以作废，你若愿意，明日就恢复我们的婚姻……”
[EN] He would not let go. 'The emperor who wrote that edict is no longer emperor. The edict should be void. If you're willing, we can restore our marriage tomorrow...'
[ZH] “圣旨岂可轻易作废？即使可以，恐怕我……我再也回不去……”
[EN] 'An imperial edict isn't so easily voided. And even if it could be—I'm afraid I... I can't go back...'
[ZH] “回不去？什么意思？”
[EN] 'Can't go back? What do you mean?'
[ZH] “我打算住在仪王建造的竹屋里等他回来……”
[EN] 'I intend to live in the bamboo house Prince Yi built, and wait for him.'
[ZH] 刚才在门口时已听到同样的话，再次从她的口中说出，陈浩宇彻底失去希望，他回忆起在狱中两人拥吻的情景，用泪眼紧盯着她：“你老实回答我，可对我动过情？”
[EN] He had heard the same words at the door. Hearing them again from her lips finished him. He thought of that kiss in the prison cell and stared at her through tears. 'Answer me honestly. Did you ever feel anything for me?'
[ZH] “我努力过，想跟你好好过日子，可事与愿违，若没发生这么多事，恐怕我已怀上你的孩子……”
[EN] 'I tried. I wanted us to have a good life. Things went against us. If none of this had happened, I might be carrying your child by now...'
[ZH] “我问的是你有没有爱过我？”
[EN] 'I asked whether you loved me.'
[ZH] “我……我曾一直在努力……”
[EN] 'I... I kept trying...'
[ZH] “在狱中，你吻我时，可对我有爱？”
[EN] 'When you kissed me in that cell, was there love in it?'
[ZH] 千里寻回忆那时的心情，那是她人生的低谷，陈浩宇对她不离不弃，令她感动，加之认为这辈子陈浩宇就是她人生的终点，与他有任何行为都不为过，才有了相拥而吻的画面。
[EN] Qianli Xun remembered how she had felt. It was the lowest point of her life. Chen Haoyu had stayed by her and would not leave, and that moved her. She had thought he would be the end of her road, that nothing between them could be wrong. That was why she had let him hold her, why she had kissed him.
[ZH] 陈浩宇哭红的双眼充满担忧和渴望，一个曾经的浪荡公子因她深受情伤，千里寻不忍心再次伤害他。
[EN] His eyes were red with weeping, full of worry and longing. He had once been a wastrel, and she had hurt him more than he deserved. She could not bear to hurt him again.
[ZH] “那一刻，我应该……应该对你有爱。”
[EN] 'At that moment I... I think I loved you.'
[ZH] 陈浩宇这才露出满意的笑容：“就知道你爱过我……”他带着一份爱，既满意又失落地走出屋子。
[EN] At last Chen Haoyu smiled. 'I knew you had loved me...' He walked out of the room, satisfied and emptied by that love.
[ZH] 千里寻自语道：“陈浩宇，谢谢你在我的人生中出现，谢谢你爱上我，你我的缘分就此终结。”
[EN] Qianli Xun said to herself, 'Chen Haoyu, thank you for coming into my life, thank you for loving me. Our fate ends here.'
